**the staff of bread** the support of bread, pozon de pan in Old French.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 4:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/4:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/4:16:2)


**וּבִדְאָגָה.** דּוֹאֲגִים שֶׁמָּא יִכְלֶה הַ**לֶּחֶם** מֵהֶם:


**and with worry** They will worry that their bread supply will be depleted.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 4:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/4:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/4:16:3)


**דְּאָגָה.** אֵיינְשָׁ״א בְּלַעַ״ז, וּבִלְשׁוֹנֵינוּ טְרוּאָ״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


דְּאָגָה means aynse in Old French, anxiety, and in our language [dialect] doubt, terror, fright.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 4:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/4:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/4:17:1)


**לְמַעַן יַחְסְרוּ.** דַּאֲגָתָם וְשִׁמְמוֹנָם בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁיִּהְיוּ חֲסוּרֵי **לֶחֶם וָמָיִם** בִּימֵי הַמָּצוֹר, וְיִדְאֲגוּ פֶּן יִכְלֶה:


**Because they will lack** Their worry and their bewilderment will be because they will lack bread and water in the days of the siege, and they will worry that the supply will be entirely depleted.



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:1:1)


**חֶרֶב חַדָּה.** סִימָן לְחֶרֶב נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**a sharp sword** symbolizing the sword of Nebuchadnezzar.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:1:2)


**תַּעַר הַגַּלָּבִים.** הָרַצְעָנִים, וְלָשׁוֹן יְוָנִי הוּא. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים ׳פַּרְקְמַצִי״ר׳ בְּלַעַ״ז; וּמְנַחֵם חִבֵּר **תַּעַר הַגַּלָּבִים** – תַּעַר הַגּוֹלְחִים, וְאֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן בַּתּוֹרָה:


**a cobbler’s razor** Heb. הַגַלָבִים, the cobblers. It is Greek, and some say that it is parcheminer in Old French, cobbler, saddler. However, Menachem (p. 56) defines תַעַר הַגַלָבִים as, the barber’s razor. No similar word exists in Scripture.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:2:1)


**בְּתוֹךְ הָעִיר.** אֲשֶׁר חַקּוֹתָ עַל הַלְּבֵנָה, כִּכְלוֹת יְמֵי מְצוֹרְךָ; אוֹת הִיא שֶׁיִּכְלוּ שְׁלִישִׁיתָם בְּזַלְעֲפוֹת רָעָב בְּתוֹכָהּ, שֶׁהֵם כָּאֵשׁ, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״עוֹרֵנוּ כְּתַנּוּר נִכְמָרוּ מִפְּנֵי זַלְעֲפוֹת רָעָב״ (איכה ה:י):


**in the midst of the city** that you inscribed on the brick, as the completion of the days of the siege. This is a sign that a third of them will perish in the heat of hunger in its midst, which is like fire, in the same way that it says (Lam. 5:10): “Our skin is parched as by a furnace because of the heat of hunger.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:2:2)


**תַּכֶּה.** אוֹתָהּ **בַּחֶרֶב**:


**and strike** it.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:2:3)


**סְבִיבוֹתֶיהָ.** סְבִיב אוֹתָהּ לְבֵנָה, סִימָן עַל הַבּוֹרְחִים מִן הָעִיר שֶׁיַּשִּׂיגוּם כַּשְׂדִּים וְיַהַרְגוּם סְבִיב יְרוּשָׁלַיִם:


**with a sword all around it** Around that brick, symbolizing those who flee from the city, whom the Chaldeans will overtake and slay in the environs of Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:2:4)


**וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁית תִּזְרֶה לָרוּחַ.** סִימָן לַגּוֹלִים שֶׁיֵּלְכוּ לְמִצְרַיִם, יוֹחָנָן בֶּן קָרֵחַ וַחֲבוּרָתוֹ; וְשָׁם **אָרִיק חֶרֶב אַחֲרֵיהֶם** – שֶׁהָלַךְ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר וְהֶחֱרִיב אֶת מִצְרַיִם, וְשָׁם נֶהֶרְגוּ (ירמיה מב:טז; מג:ה-ז; מו:יג-כו):


**and a third you shall scatter to the wind** symbolizing the exiles who will go to Egypt, viz. Johanan son of Kareach and his associates, and there I shall unsheathe a sword [in pursuit] after them, for Nebuchadnezzar went and destroyed Egypt, and there they were slain.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:3:1)


**וְצַרְתָּ אוֹתָם בִּכְנָפֶיךָ.** אֵלּוּ מְתֵי מְעַט שֶׁיִּגְלוּ לְבָבֶל וְיִחְיוּ:


**and you shall bind them in your skirts** These are the few people who will be exiled to Babylon and will live. An addendum to Rashi reads: **and you shall bind them** From here is an allusion [to the fact] that whoever cuts his hair should not cast it away, but should hide it, for any act written in these verses has implications of evil.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:4:1)


**וּמֵהֶם עוֹד תִּקַּח.** מֵאוֹתָם מְתֵי **מְעָט**:


**And you shall take more of them** From those few people.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:4:2)


**וְשָׂרַפְתָּ אוֹתָם בָּאֵשׁ.** סִימָן לְאַחְאָב בֶּן קוֹלָיָה וְצִדְקִיָּה בֶּן מַעֲשֵׂיָה, ״אֲשֶׁר קָלָם מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל״ (ירמיה כט:כב):


**and burn them with fire** This represents Ahab son of Kolaiah and Zedekiah son of Maaseiah, whom the king of Babylon burnt with fire (Jer.29: 21f.).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:4:3)


**מִמֶּנּוּ תֵצֵא אֵשׁ.** מִן הָרְמָזִים הָאֵלֶּה שֶׁאֲנִי רוֹמֵז לְךָ תֵּצֵא הַפּוּרְעָנִיּוֹת לְכָל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**therefrom a fire will emerge** From these allusions that I give you, retribution will emerge to all the house of Israel.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:5:1)


**בְּתוֹךְ הַגּוֹיִם שַׂמְתִּיהָ.** בְּאֶמְצַע הָעוֹלָם (תנחומא, קדושים י):


**among the nations I have placed it** In the middle of the world.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:6:1)


**וַתֶּמֶר אֶת מִשְׁפָּטַי.** וַתַּחֲלִיף אֶת מִשְׁפָּטַי:


**But she exchanged My judgments** Heb. וַתֶּמֶר, she exchanged My judgments.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:6:2)


**מִן הַגּוֹיִם.** שֶׁכָּךְ כָּתוּב בִּמְנַשֶּׁה ״וַיַּתַע מְנַשֶּׁה אֶת יְהוּדָה וְיֹשְׁבֵי יְרוּשָׁלַיִם לַעֲשׂוֹת רַע מִן הַגּוֹיִם אֲשֶׁר הִשְׁמִיד ה׳ מִפְּנֵי בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל״ (דברי הימים ב לג:ט); וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים, שֶׁהַגּוֹיִם לֹא קִבְּלוּ תּוֹרָתִי, וְהֵם קִבְּלוּהָ עֲלֵיהֶם וְעָבְרוּ עָלֶיהָ:


**more than the nations** For so it is written concerning Manasseh (II Chron. 33:9): “And Manasseh misled Judah and the inhabitants of Jerusalem to do evil more than the nations that the Lord had destroyed from before the children of Israel.” Some interpret this to mean that the nations did not accept His Torah, but they [Israel] did accept it upon themselves, and transgressed it.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:7:1)


**יַעַן הֲמָנְכֶם.** יַעַן הִזְמִינְכֶם אֶת עַצְמְכֶם לְהֶפְקֵר **מִן הַגּוֹיִם**:


**Because you have prepared yourselves** Because you have prepared yourselves to be more profligate than the nations.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:7:2)


**הֲמָנְכֶם.** לְשׁוֹן ״וַיְמַן לָהֶם הַמֶּלֶךְ״ (דניאל א:ה); וּמְנַחֵם פֵּתְרוֹ לְשׁוֹן ׳הוֹמֶה׳, כְּמוֹ ״הָמוּ גוֹיִם״ (תהלים מו:ז):


[The word] הֲמָנְכֶם is an expression of (Dan 1:5): “And the king allotted (וַיְמַן) [food] for them.” Menachem, though, interpreted it as an expression of stirring, like (Ps. 46: 7): “Nations have stirred (הָמוּ).”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:7:3)


**וּכְמִשְׁפְּטֵי הַגּוֹיִם אֲשֶׁר סְבִיבוֹתֵיכֶם לֹא עֲשִׂיתֶם.** שֶׁהֵם לֹא הֵמִירוּ אֶת אֱלֹהֵיהֶם וְהֵמָּה לֹא אֱלֹהִים, וְאַתֶּם הֲמַרְתֶּם כְּבוֹדִי בְּלֹא יוֹעִיל (ירמיה ב:יא); וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ (סנהדרין לט:): כַּמְתֻקָּנִים שֶׁבָּהֶם לֹא עֲשִׂיתֶם, כַּמְקֻלְקָלִים שֶׁבָּהֶם עֲשִׂיתֶם:


**and like the customs of the nations that were around you, you did not do** For they did not exchange their gods, although they are not gods, but you exchanged My glory for something that avails not. Our Sages (Sanh. 39b) explained: You did not do as the virtuous ones among them, you did as the corrupt ones among them.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:8:1)


**גַּם אֲנִי.** אַתְּ בָּגַדְתְּ עָלַי, וְ**גַם אֲנִי הִנְנִי עָלַיִךְ**:


**I too** You betrayed Me, and I too, behold I am upon you.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:13:1)


**וְהַנִּיחוֹתִי חֲמָתִי.** כְּשֶׁאֶנָּקֵם **בָּם** תָּנוּחַ חֲמָתִי וְלֹא תְצִיקֵנִי רוּחִי:


**and I shall cause My fury against them to subside** When I avenge Myself against them, My fury will subside, and My spirit will not oppress Me.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:13:2)


**וְהִנֶּחָמְתִּי.** וְאֶתְנַחֵם עַל הַצַּעַר שֶׁצִּעַרְתֶּם אוֹתִי:


**and I shall be comforted** Heb. וְהִנֶחָמְתִּי. And I shall be comforted from the pain that you inflicted upon Me.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:14:1)


**וְלְחֶרְפָּה.** דִישְׁטְרוֹיְ״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a ruin** Heb. לְחָרְבָּה, destroyer in Old French, to make desolate, destroy.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:15:1)


**וְגִדּוּפָה.** בִּזָּיוֹן:


**and a taunt** A disgrace.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:15:2)


**מוּסָר.** יִסּוּרִין:


**a chastisement** Heb. מוּסָר torments.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:15:3)


**וּמְשַׁמָּה.** תִּמָּהוֹן:


**and an astonishment** A wonder.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:16:1)


**וְרָעָב אוֹסִיף עֲלֵיכֶם.** הוּא רָעָב הַפְּרוּטָה הַכָּלָה מִן הַכִּיס; כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי:


**and I shall add a famine upon you** This is the famine of the penny depleted from the pocket. So I heard.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 5:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/5:16:2)


**מַטֵּה לָחֶם.** ״מַשְׁעַן לֶחֶם״ (ישעיה ג:א):


**your staff of bread** Support of bread.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:3:1)


**וְלַגְּבָעוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן רָמָה (הושע ה:ח):


**and to the hills** [Heb. [ולגבעות is] a term expressing height.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:3:2)


**וְלַגֵּאָיוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן גַּיְא:


**and to the valleys** [ולגאיות is] a term expressing valleys.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:4:1)


**חַמָּנֵיכֶם.** מִין תַּבְנִית צַלְמֵי עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת הָעֲשׂוּיִין עַל הַגַּגּוֹת לַחַמָּה:


**your sun gods** חַמָּנֵיכֶם, a class of pagan idols made on the roofs [dedicated] to the sun.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:6:1)


**וְיֶאְשְׁמוּ.** יִהְיוּ נִכָּרִים בְּאַשְׁמָתָם:


**and shown to be guilty** Heb. ויאשמו [lit. they be made guilty.] They will be recognized for their guilt.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:9:1)


**וְזָכְרוּ פְלִיטֵיכֶם אוֹתִי.** אֶת חַסְדִּי וְאֶת רַחֲמִי שֶׁהָיִיתִי מְרַחֵם עֲלֵיהֶם בְּהַעֲוֹתָם לְפָנַי בְּאַרְצָם:


**And those of you that escape will remember Me** My kindnesses and My compassion, that I had compassion on them when they committed iniquities before Me in their land.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:9:2)


**אֲשֶׁר נִשְׁבַּרְתִּי אֶת לִבָּם הַזּוֹנֶה.** אֲשֶׁר נִכְנַעְתִּי אֶצְלָם, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁזּוֹנֶה לִבָּם וְ**סָר מֵעָלַי**, הָיִיתִי מְבַקֵּשׁ מֵהֶם עַל יְדֵי נְבִיאַי לָשׁוּב אֵלַי וְאֵיטִיב לָהֶם, וְלֹא אָבוּ:


**that I yielded to their straying heart** That I yielded to them. Although their heart went astray and they turned away from Me, I begged of them, through My prophets, to return to Me, and [promised] that I will [then] do good for them, but they were unwilling.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:9:3)


**וְנָקֹטּוּ בִּפְנֵיהֶם.** מְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ עִם ״רָאִיתִי בוֹגְדִים וָאֶתְקוֹטָטָה״ (תהלים קי״ט, קנח): יִתְקוֹטְטוּ עַל עַצְמָם עַל אֲשֶׁר לֹא שָׁמְעוּ אֵלַי; וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״וִידַנְקוּן וְאִינוּן יְהוֹן חָזָן״, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם ״הַנֶּאֱנָקִים״ (יחזקאל ט, ד): ״דְּמִדַנְקִין״, וּ״בְכִי וַאֲנָקָה״ (מלאכי ב, יג): ״דְּמִדַנְקָן״; ׳דְמוֹנִיר׳ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and they will quarrel with themselves** Heb. וְנָקֹטוּ. Menachem associated it with (Ps. 119:158): “I saw traitors, and I quarreled (וָאֶתְקוֹטָטָה).” They will quarrel with themselves for not listening to Me. Jonathan renders: וְידַנְקוּן and they will see, and so does he translate (below 9:4): “those who groan (הַנֶאֶנָקִים),” דְּמִדַנְקִין, and (Mal. 2:13): “weeping...and groaning (וַאֲנָקָה),” דְּמִדַנְקָן, demonir in Old French, to groan.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:11:1)


**וְרָקַע.** עִנְיָנוֹ יוֹרֶה עָלָיו: מַכֶּה רַגְלוֹ עַל הָאָרֶץ וּמְרַדֵּד אֶת הַטִּיט, וְהוּא דֶּרֶךְ אָבֵל:


**and stamp** Its context implies that it means to stamp one’s foot on the ground to flatten out the mud, and this is a custom of a mourner.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:11:2)


**וֶאֱמָר אָח.** צַעֲקַת יָגוֹן וָאוֹי:


**and say, Ah!** A cry of grief and woe.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:12:1)


**וְהַנָּצוּר.** הַנֶּאֱסָף אֶל הָעִיר וְיָצוּרוּ עָלָיו:


**and who is in the siege** Heb. והנצור, one who is gathered into the city when they besiege it.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:12:2)


**וְכִלֵּיתִי חֲמָתִי.** אַשְׁלִים כָּל חֲרוֹנִי וְחֶפְצִי בְּרָעָתָם:


**and I shall spend My fury** I shall finish all My fury and My desire to do evil to them.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:13:1)


**אֵלָה.** פְּלַנְצוֹ״ן בְּלַעַ״ז:


**terebinth** Heb. אֵלָה, planzon in Old French, a branch.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:13:2)


**עֲבֻתָּה.** לְשׁוֹן ״עֵץ עָבוֹת״ (יחזקאל כ:כח), שֶׁעֲנָפָיו מְרֻבִּין:


**branchy** עֲבֻתָּה, an expression of (below 20: 28): “a branchy (עָבוֹת) tree,” i.e., whose branches are many.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:14:1)


**שְׁמָמָה.** ״מֵאֵין יוֹשֵׁב״ (ירמיה לד:כב):


**a desolation** Heb. שְּׁמָמָה, without an inhabitant.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 6:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/6:14:2)


**מְשַׁמָּה.** תִּמָּהוֹן, שֶׁיִּתְמְהוּ עָלֶיהָ רוֹאֶיהָ:


**and an astonishment** Heb. ומשמה, a wonder, i.e., that all who see it will wonder about it.



## Chapter 7


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:2:1)


**בָּא הַקֵּץ.** שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי ״וְנוֹשַׁנְתֶּם בָּאָרֶץ וְהִשְׁחַתֶּם וְגוֹ׳״, ״הַעִידֹתִי בָכֶם הַיּוֹם כִּי אָבֹד תֹּאבֵדוּן״ (דברים ד:כה-כו); ״וְנוֹשַׁנְתֶּם״ – בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא שְׁמוֹנֶה מֵאוֹת חֲמִשִּׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם, וְהֵם נִשְׁתַּהוּ בָהּ שְׁמוֹנֶה מֵאוֹת וַחֲמִשִּׁים: אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְאַרְבָּעִים מִשֶּׁנִּכְנְסוּ לָהּ עַד שֶׁנִּבְנָה הַבַּיִת (מלכים א ו:א; במדבר יד:לג-לד), וְאַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת וְעֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים עָמַד הַבַּיִת (יומא ט.):


**The end has come** Because I had said (Deut. 4:25): “and you will have grown old (וְנוֹשַּׁנְתֶּם) in the land, and you will practice corruption, etc., so that you will be lost.” [The word] וְנוֹשַּׁנְתֶּם has the numerical value of 852, but they stayed there for [only] 850 years: 440 from the time they entered it until the Temple was built, and [then] the Temple stood 410 years.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:5:1)


**רָעָה אַחַת רָעָה.** רָעָה שְׁלֵימָה וּמְיֻחֶדֶת, לְמַעְלָה מִכָּל הָרָעוֹת, וְהוּא חֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת:


**An evil, a singular evil** Heb. רָעָה אַחַתרָעָה, a total, special evil, worse than all evils; that is the destruction of the Temple.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:5:2)


**הִנֵּה בָאָה.** טַעְמוֹ בָּאָלֶ״ף, הוּא לְשׁוֹן פּוֹעֶלֶת:


**behold it comes** Heb. בָאָה. Its accent is on the ‘aleph.’ It is in the present tense.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:7:1)


**בָּאָה הַצְּפִירָה.** טַעְמוֹ לְמַעְלָה בַּבֵּי״ת, וְהוּא לְשׁוֹן פָּעֲלָה: כְּבָר **בָּאָה הַצְּפִירָה** – כְּמוֹ **בָּא** וְשָׁקַע מְאוֹר הַשַּׁחַר וְהַתִּפְאֶרֶת בָּא:


**The dawn has set** Heb. בָּאָה הַצְפִירָה. Its accent is at the beginning of the word, and it is in the past tense. The צְפִירָה has already set. The light of the dawn and the glory has already set.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:7:2)


**וְלֹא הֵד הָרִים.** ״וְלָא לְאִשְׁתֵּיזָבָא בְּמִצְדֵי טוּרַיָּא״, וְהֵד – לְשׁוֹן צַעֲקַת הַכְרָזָה לָקוּם לִבְרוֹחַ אֶל רָאשֵׁי הֶהָרִים, כְּמוֹ ״הֵידָד הִשְׁבַּתִּי״ (ישעיה טז:י), ״וְעָנוּ עָלֶיךָ הֵידָד״ (ירמיה נא:יד), הַשְׁמָעַת קוֹל נוֹשֵׂא מַשָּׂא עֲנָבִים אֶל הַגַּת וְטוֹעֲנֵי הַקּוֹרָה:


**and not the shout of the mountains** [Jonathan renders:] And not to escape into the strongholds of the mountains. [The word] הֵד means the cry of the announcement to rise and flee to the mountaintops, like (Isa. 16: 10): “the shout [of joy] (הֵידָד) have I abolished.” Also (Jer. 51:1): “and they shall raise a battle-cry (הֵידָד) against you.” The shout of those who carry the load of grapes to the winepress and those who load on the beam.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:8:1)


**מִקָּרוֹב.** בִּמְהֵרָה:


**Speedily** Heb. מקרוב, [lit. from close.] Quickly.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:10:1)


**הִנֵּה הַיּוֹם.** הִנֵּה בָּא הַיּוֹם:


**Behold the day** Behold the day has come.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:10:2)


**הִנֵּה בָאָה.** הָרָעָה:


**behold it is coming** The evil.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:10:3)


**יָצְאָה הַצְּפִירָה.** עָלָה הַשַּׁחַר שֶׁל יוֹם הָרָעָה:


**the dawn has gone forth** The dawn of the day of the evil.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:10:4)


**צָץ הַמַּטֶּה.** לְהַכּוֹת אֶתְכֶם:


**the staff has budded** to strike you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:10:5)


**פָּרַח הַזָּדוֹן.** נִתְגַּלָּה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר הָרָשָׁע:


**the willful wickedness has blossomed** The wicked Nebuchadnezzar has revealed himself.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:11:1)


**הֶחָמָס קָם וְגוֹ׳.** הֶחָמָס אֲשֶׁר בְּיֶדְכֶם הוּא **קָם** עֲלֵיכֶם **לְמַטֵּה רֶשַׁע** לְשַׁחֶתְכֶם:


**The violence has arisen, etc.** The violence in your hands has risen upon you as a staff of wickedness to destroy you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:11:2)


**לֹא מֵהֶם.** כָּךְ אָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לַמַּשְׁחִית: אֵינְךָ זָקוּק לְהַשְׁאִיר נֶפֶשׁ מֵרִשְׁעֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, כִּי אֵין טוֹבָה יוֹצֵאת **לֹא מֵהֶם<b> וְלֹא מִבְּנֵיהֶם <b>וְלֹא מֵהֲמוֹנָם**:


**neither from them** So said the Holy One, blessed be He, to the Destroyer: You are not required to spare a soul [from the wicked of Israel], for no good will come from them or from their children or from their multitude.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:11:3)


**וְלֹא נֹהַּ בָּהֶם.** אֵין בָּהֶם נוֹהֶה אַחֲרַי, מְגַעְגֵּעַ וְנוֹתֵן לֵב אֵלַי; כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּנָּהוּ כָּל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל אַחֲרֵי ה׳״ (שמואל א ז:ב), וְלָשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי הוּא: ״וְאִתְנְהִיאוּ לְפוּלְחָנָא״, לְשׁוֹן תַּאֲוָה וְגִעְגּוּעִין. וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן יְלָלָה, וְכֵן ״וַיִּנָּהוּ כָּל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל״ פֵּרֵשׁ לְשׁוֹן יְלָלָה, וְאֵין בַּתֵּבָה רַק ה״א יְסוֹד:


**and no one among them longs** No one among them longs or gives thought to Me, as in (I Sam. 7:2): “and all the house of Israel longed (וַיִנָהוּ) for the Lord.” This is an Aramaism: “And they longed (וְאִתְנְהִיאוּ) for My worship,” an expression of desire and longing. Menachem (p. 69) associated it with יללה, an expression of wailing, and the word retains only the ‘hey’ as a radical.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:12:1)


**וְהַמּוֹכֵר אַל יִתְאַבָּל.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ הַמּוֹכֵר שָׂדֵהוּ לִהְיוֹת עָצֵב, אַל יִתְעַצֵּב, **כִּי** סוֹפְכֶם לִגְלוֹת:


**neither will the seller mourn** Although one who sells his field usually grieves, he will not grieve because you are destined to go into exile.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:13:1)


**כִּי הַמּוֹכֵר אֶל הַמִּמְכָּר לֹא יָשׁוּב.** כְּשֶׁיִּגְלֶה בַּגּוֹלָה לֹא יָשׁוּב וְיִרְאֶה עוֹד הַשָּׂדֶה שֶׁמָּכַר, וְלָמָּה יִתְאַבֵּל?


**For the seller will not return to that which was sold** Once he goes into exile, he will not return and will no longer see the field that he had sold; so why should he mourn?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:13:2)


**וְעוֹד בַּחַיִּים חַיָּתָם.** כָּל יְמֵי חַיֵּיהֶם לֹא יָשׁוּבוּ אֶל מְקוֹמָם:


**as long as they are alive** All the days of their lives they will not return to their place.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:13:3)


**כִּי חָזוֹן אֶל כָּל הֲמוֹנָהּ וגו׳.** ״אֲרֵי נְבִיָּיא מִתְנַבְּן עַל כָּל אַתְרְגוּשְׁתְהוֹן לְמֵיתַב בִּתְיוּבְתָּא וְלָא תַּיְיבִין״ (תרגום יונתן):


**for when the prophecy was [directed] to all her multitude, etc.** For the prophets prophesy concerning all their multitude to repent, but they do not repent.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:13:4)


**וְאִישׁ בַּעֲוֹנוֹ חַיָּתוֹ.** כָּל אֶחָד מֵהֶם חַיָּתוֹ וְנַפְשׁוֹ דְּבוּקָה בַּעֲוֹנוֹ:


**and each man’s soul is in his iniquity** The life and soul of every one of them is attached to his iniquity.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:13:5)


**לֹא יִתְחַזָּקוּ.** לֹא יְחַזְּקוּ לִבָּם עַל יִצְרָם הָרָע לָשׁוּב לְתוֹרָתָם:


**they do not strengthen themselves** They do not strengthen their heart over their evil inclination, to return to their Torah.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:14:1)


**תָּקְעוּ בַתָּקוֹעַ.** כְּשֶׁיָּבֹא עֲלֵיהֶם הַצַּר הֵכִינוּ עַצְמָם לַמִּלְחָמָה, וְאֵין יוֹצֵא **לְהִלָּחֵם**:


**They sound the shofar** When the enemy comes upon them, they prepare themselves for war, but no one will go out to fight.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:16:1)


**כְּיוֹנֵי הַגֵּאָיוֹת.** דֶּרֶךְ הַיּוֹנִים לְהִקָּבֵץ בַּגֵּאָיוֹת וְהוֹמוֹת שָׁם; וְהָיוּ הוֹמִים אֶל הֶהָרִים אֲשֶׁר יָנוּסוּ שָׁם **אִישׁ בַּעֲוֹנוֹ**, **כְּיוֹנֵי הַגֵּאָיוֹת** אֲשֶׁר **כֻּלָּם הֹמוֹת**:


**like the doves of the valleys** It is customary for doves to congregate in the valleys and moan there. They [the survivors] will moan on the mountains, where they will flee, each one for his iniquity, like the doves of the valleys, which all moan.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:17:1)


**תֵּלַכְנָה מָּיִם.** מִזִּיעַת אֵימָה:


**will go [like] water** from the sweat of fright.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:19:1)


**לְנִדָּה יִהְיֶה.** לְשִׁקּוּץ יִהְיֶה לָהֶם:


**will be repugnant** It will be disgusting for them.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:20:1)


**וְצְבִי עֶדְיוֹ.** שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֲשֶׁר **לְגָאוֹן שָׂמָהוּ** לָהֶם, הוּא בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ שֶׁנִּקְרָא גְּאוֹן עֻזָּם, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״הִנְנִי מְחַלֵּל אֶת מִקְדָּשִׁי גְּאוֹן עֻזְּכֶם״ (יחזקאל כד:כא), הֵם **גִּלּוּלֵי תוֹעֲבוֹת עָשׂוּ בוֹ עַל כֵּן וְגוֹ׳**:


**And the beauty of His adornment** of the Holy One, blessed be He, that He made for their pride. This is the Temple, called the pride of their might, as it is said (24:21): “Behold I profane My sanctuary, the pride of Your might.” They made in it the images of their abominations, therefore, etc.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:22:1)


**אֶת צְפוּנִי.** אֶת הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁהָיִיתִי צָפוּן שָׁם בֵּינֵיהֶם:


**My secret place** The place where I was hidden among them.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:23:1)


**עֲשֵׂה הָרַתּוֹק.** שַׁלְשֶׁלֶת, אוֹת הוּא שֶׁיֵּלְכוּ בַּגּוֹלָה אֲסוּרִים בְּזִיקִים:


**Make the chain** Heb. הָרַתּוֹק, the chain. It is a symbolic sign that they will go into exile bound in fetters.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:23:2)


**מִשְׁפַּט דָּמִים.** ״חַיָּבֵי קְטוֹל״ (תרגום יונתן):


**crimes of bloodshed** [lit. judgment of blood. Targum renders:] Crimes deserving of death.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:24:1)


**רָעֵי גוֹיִם.** רָעִים שֶׁבְּעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת וְאַכְזָרִים:


**the most wicked of the nations** Heb. רָעֵי גוֹיִם. The most wicked and cruel of the nations.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:24:2)


**וְנִחֲלוּ.** וְיִתְנַחֲלוּ לַאֲחֵרִים:


**and they will deliver as an inheritance** Heb. וְנִחֲלוּ, they will give over to others as an inheritance.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:24:3)


**מְקַדְּשֵׁיהֶם.** בָּתֵּי הֵיכַל הַבָּמוֹת שֶׁהָיוּ נוֹעָדִים שָׁם, לְשׁוֹן בֵּית זִמּוּנָם:


**their meeting places** Heb. מְקַדְשֵׁיהֶם. The temples of the high places, where they would gather at appointed times; an expression of a house of their appointment.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:25:1)


**קְפָדָה בָּא.** כְּמוֹ ״קִפַּדְתִּי כָאֹרֵג חַיַּי״ (ישעיהו לח:יב), לְשׁוֹן כְּרִיתָה:


**A cutting off comes** Heb. קְפָדָה like (Isa. 38:12): “I severed (קִפַּדְתִּי) like a weaver, my life”; a term denoting cutting off.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:26:1)


**הוֹוָה עַל הוֹוָה.** מְאוֹרָע עַל מְאוֹרָע, הֲוָיָה אַחַר הֲוָיָה. וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן שֶׁבֶר:


**Happening upon happening** Heb. הוָֹה עַל-הוָֹה, happening upon happening, being after being. Menachem (p. 70) associated it to a term for a breach.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 7:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/7:26:2)


**וְתוֹרָה תֹּאבַד מִכֹּהֵן.** הָרְגִילִים בְּהוֹרָאָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְתוֹרָה יְבַקְשׁוּ מִפִּיהוּ״ (מלאכי ב:ז):


**and instruction will be lost from a priest** who usually gave instruction, as is said (Mal. 2:7): “and teaching should be sought from his mouth.”



## Chapter 8


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:1:1)


**בַּשָּׁנָה הַשִּׁשִּׁית.** לְגָלוּת יְכָנְיָה:


**in the sixth year** of the exile of Jeconiah.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:2:1)


**מִמַּרְאֵה מָתְנָיו וְגוֹ׳.** אָסוּר לְהִתְבּוֹנֵן בְּפָסוּק זֶה (ראה רש״י יחזקאל א:כז):


**from the appearance of its loins, etc.** It is forbidden to reflect on this verse.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:3:1)


**בְּצִיצִית רֹאשִׁי.** בִּשְׂעַר רֹאשִׁי, פְּלוּצִיי״ל בְּלַעַ״ז:


**by a lock of my hair** Heb. צִיצִיתרֹאשִּׁי [lit. fringes of my head.] By the hair of my head, flozel in Old French, a lock of hair.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:3:2)


**בְּמַרְאוֹת אֱלֹהִים.** הֶרְאַנִי כְּאִלּוּ נוֹשֵׂא אוֹתִי וּמְבִיאַנִי לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**in the visions of God** He showed me [a vision looking] as if He were carrying me and bringing me to Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:3:3)


**שַׁעַר הַפְּנִימִית הַפּוֹנֶה צָפוֹנָה.** שַׁעַר הָעֲזָרָה, שֶׁהִיא פְּנִימִית לְהַר הַבַּיִת, וְהָיוּ לָהּ שְׁעָרִים לְכָל רוּחוֹתֶיהָ:


**the inner gate that faces north** The gate to the court, which is within the Temple Mount, [and] which had gates to all its sides.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:3:4)


**סֵמֶל הַקִּנְאָה.** צוּרַת הַקִּנְאָה מְקַנֵּא וּמַקְנִיט לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁנְּתָנוּהָ שָׁם לְהַקְנָאוֹת לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, לְהַרְגִּיזוֹ; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״צֶלֶם דְּקִנְאָתָא דְּמַרְגְּזִין״:


**the image of jealousy** The image of jealousy would make the Holy One, blessed be He, jealous and provoke Him, for they put it there to make the Holy One, blessed be He, jealous; to anger Him, and so did Jonathan render: the image of jealousy, which they provoked.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:5:1)


**שָׂא נָא עֵינֶיךָ דֶּרֶךְ צָפוֹנָה.** לְהַר הַבַּיִת מִחוּץ לָעֲזָרָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּפֶתַח שַׁעַר הַצְּפוֹנִי שֶׁל עֲזָרָה מִבַּחוּץ הָיָה עוֹמֵד, וְדֶרֶךְ צָפוֹנָה לַשַּׁעַר, חוּץ לָעֲזָרָה הוּא:


**“Now lift your eyes northward”** Toward the Temple Mount, outside the court. For he [already] was standing outside the northern entrance of the court, and north of the gate is [farther] outside the court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:5:2)


**מִצָּפוֹן לְשַׁעַר הַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** בְּהַר הַבַּיִת, מִצָּפוֹן לְאוֹתוֹ שַׁעַר הָעֲזָרָה הַצְּפוֹנִי; וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי, שֶׁהָיָה אוֹתוֹ שַׁעַר מְכֻוָּן כְּנֶגֶד הַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


**from the north of the gate of the altar** On the Temple Mount, from the north of that northern gate of the court, and I say that that gate was exactly in line with the gate of the altar.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:5:3)


**בַּבִּאָה.** כְּשֶׁאָדָם בָּא לִיכָּנֵס לָעֲזָרָה, מוֹצְאוֹ שָׁם. **בַּבִּאָה** – אלאנטריא״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**at the entrance** Heb. בַּבִאָה. When a person comes to enter the court, he finds it there; a l’entree in French, at the entrance.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:6:1)


**לְרָחֳקָה מֵעַל מִקְדָּשִׁי.** כְּדֵי שֶׁאֶתְרַחֵק מֵעַל מִקְדָּשִׁי:


**to cause Me to distance Myself from My sanctuary** In order that I distance Myself from My sanctuary.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:7:1)


**אֶל פֶּתַח הֶחָצֵר.** הִקְרִיבַנִי עַד פֶּתַח אוֹתוֹ שַׁעַר עַצְמוֹ (ראה רש״י יחזקאל ח:ה):


**to the entrance of the court** He brought me up until the entrance of that very gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:7:2)


**חוֹר אֶחָד בַּקִּיר.** חוֹמַת הָעֲזָרָה:


**a hole in the wall** The wall of the court.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:8:1)


**חֲתָר נָא בַקִּיר.** וְהַרְחֵב אֶת הַחוֹר:


**dig now in the wall** and widen the hole.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:8:2)


**וְהִנֵּה פֶתַח אֶחָד.** דֶּרֶךְ הַחוֹר רָאִיתִי פֶּתַח לִשְׁכָּה אַחַת:


**and behold an entrance** Through the hole, I saw the entrance of one chamber.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:10:1)


**שֶׁקֶץ.** עֲשׂוּיִין לְשִׁקּוּץ עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה:


**of detestation** Made for the detestation of idolatry.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:10:2)


**מְחֻקֶּה.** חֲקוּקִין **עַל הַקִּיר**:


**engraved** Heb. מְחֻקֶה, engraved on the wall.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:11:1)


**וְיַאֲזַנְיָהוּ בֶן שָׁפָן.** חָשׁוּב הָיָה, וּלְמֵדִין דּוֹרוֹ הֵימֶנּוּ, לְפִיכָךְ הִקְפִּיד עָלָיו:


**and Jaazaniah the son of Shaphan** He was of high esteem, and his generation was learning from him; therefore, He took particular offense at him.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:11:2)


**וַעֲתַר עֲנַן הַקְּטֹרֶת.** תִּימּוֹר קִיטוֹר עֲנַן הַקְּטֹרֶת; וְלָשׁוֹן עֲתַר – ׳מַרְבִּית׳, וְכֵן ״הַעְתַּרְתֶּם עָלַי דִּבְרֵיכֶם״ (יחזקאל לה:יג), וְכֵן ״נַעְתָּרוֹת נְשִׁיקוֹת שׂוֹנֵא״ (משלי כז:ו); וְכֵן חִבְּרָן מְנַחֵם בְּמַחֲלֹקֶת אַחַת שְׁלָשְׁתָּן, וּפֵירֵשׁ: עִנְיָן מְרֻבֶּה הֵמָּה, אַךְ כָּל אֶחָד לְשׁוֹן בְּנֵי אָדָם:


**and a thick cloud of incense** Heb. עֲתַר, thick. The ascent of the smoke of the cloud of the incense [was thick]. The word עַתַר denotes immensity, and so (35:13): “and have magnified (הַעְתַּרְתֶּם) your words against Me,” and so (Prov. 27:6): “whereas kisses of an enemy are burdensome (נַעְתָּרוֹת).” Menachem (p. 140) grouped all three under one heading and they all denote an idea of thickness, but each one is an independent expression.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:12:1)


**בְּחַדְרֵי מַשְׂכִּיתוֹ.** מְשׁוּכָה בְּרִצְפַת אַבְנֵי שַׁיִשׁ, לְשׁוֹן ״וְשַׂכֹּתִי כַפִּי״ (שמות לג:כב):


**in his paved chambers** Heb. מַשְּׂכִּיתוֹ. Covered with a floor of marble. An expression related to (Exod. 32:22): “and I will cover (וְשֲּׂכֹּתִי) you with My hand.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:14:1)


**אֶל פֶּתַח שַׁעַר בֵּית ה׳.** תּוֹךְ חֲלַל הָעֲזָרָה, אֶל פֶּתַח הַהֵיכָל אֲשֶׁר בַּצָּפוֹן; דְּאָמַר מַר (זבחים נה:): שְׁנֵי פִשְׁפְּשִׁין הָיוּ בְּבֵית הַחֲלִיפוֹת, אֶחָד בַּצָּפוֹן וְאֶחָד בַּדָּרוֹם (תמיד ל:):


**to the entrance of the gate of the house of the Lord** Within the inner space of the courtyard [that led] to the entrance of the heichal that is in the north, for the master [R. Jose the son] taught: Two wickets were in the Temple chamber for the knives, one in the north and one in the south (see Zev. 55b, 56a).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:14:2)


**מְבַכּוֹת אֶת הַתַּמּוּז.** דְּמוּת אֶחָד שֶׁמְּחַמְּמוֹת אוֹתוֹ מִבִּפְנִים, וְהָיוּ עֵינָיו שֶׁל עוֹפֶרֶת וְהֵם נִתּוֹכִין מֵחוֹם הַהֶסֵּק וְנִרְאֶה כְּאִלּוּ בּוֹכֶה, וְאוֹמְרוֹת: תִּקְרוֹבֶת הוּא שׁוֹאֵל:


**making the Tammuz weep** There was an image that they would heat up from the inside, and its eyes, which were of lead, would melt from the heat of the fire, and it would appear as though it was weeping, and they would say, “It is asking for an offering.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:14:3)


**תַּמּוּז.** לְשׁוֹן הֶיסֵּק, כְּמוֹ ״עַל דִּי חֲזֵה לְמֵזְיֵהּ״ (דניאל ג:יט), ״וְאַתּוּנָא אֲזֵה יַתִּירָה״ (שם):


“Tammuz” is an expression of heating, like (Dan. 3:19): “more than it was wont to be heated (לְמֵזְיֵהּ)”; (ibid. 22), “and the furnace exceedingly hot (אֵזֵהּ).”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:14:4)


**מְבַכּוֹת אֶת הַתַּמּוּז.** פִּיישְׁנְ״ט פְּלוֹרִי״ר לָא״ל קָאלְפִּי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Making the Tammuz cry** is fesant plorer la chalfez in Old French; making the heated [god] weep.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:16:1)


**וַיָּבֵא אוֹתִי אֶל חֲצַר בֵּית ה׳ הַפְּנִימִית.** הִיא הָעֲזָרָה, כִּי הַר הַבַּיִת נִקְרָא ״הֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה״ (יחזקאל מ:יז), וְעַד עַכְשָׁיו בְּתוֹכָהּ הָיָה עוֹמֵד, אֶלָּא שֶׁהוּא נִכְנַס בְּשַׁעַר הַצְּפוֹנִי, וְהֶרְאָהוּ בְּבֵית הַחֲלִיפוֹת אֶת צוּרַת הַתַּמּוּז, וְחָזַר וְהוֹלִיכוֹ לְמִזְרָח לִפְנֵי הַהֵיכָל וְהַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


**And He brought me to the inner court of the house of the Lord** This is the Temple court, for the Temple Mount is called the outer court, and until now that is where he [already] had been standing, except that he had entered the northern gate when He showed him the image of the Tammuz in the chamber of the knives. Then He brought him to the east, [to stand] before the Temple and the altar.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:16:2)


**וּפְנֵיהֶם קֵדְמָה וגו׳.** מִמַּשְׁמָע שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר **וּפְנֵיהֶם קֵדְמָה** יוֹדֵעַ אֲנִי שֶׁאֲחוֹרֵיהֶם אֶל הַהֵיכָל, אֶלָּא מַגִּיד שֶׁהָיָה אֲחוֹרֵיהֶם מְגֻלֶּה, שֶׁהָיוּ פּוֹעֲרִין עַצְמָם וּמַתְרִיזִין כְּלַפֵּי מַטָּה (סוכה נג:):


**and their faces toward the east** From the implication of the statement, “and their faces toward the east,” I [already] know that “their backs were toward the Temple,” but [Scripture] tells us that their posteriors were exposed, for they would uncover themselves and excrete downward.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:16:3)


**מִשְׁתַּחֲוִיתֶם.** בִּשְׁתֵּי תֵיבוֹת הוּא מְשַׁמֵּשׁ, לְשׁוֹן ׳הַשְׁחָתָה׳ וּלְשׁוֹן ׳הִשְׁתַּחֲוָיָה׳, כֵּן הוּא בִּירוּשַׁלְמִי (סוכה ה, ה): מַשְׁחִיתִים הַהֵיכָל וּמִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לַחַמָּה; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְאִינוּן מִתְחַבְּלִין וְסָגְדִין״. וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן ׳שָׁחוֹחַ׳, כְּמוֹ ״שְׁחִי וְנַעֲבוֹרָה״ (ישעיה נא, כג):


**and they were prostrating themselves** Heb. מִשְּׁתַּחֲוִיתֶם. This serves as two words, an expression of destruction (הַשְּׁחָתָה) and an expression of prostrating oneself (הִשְּׁתַּחֲוָיָה). So it is in Yerushalmi (Suc. 5:5, see Korban Ha’edah): “They were destroying the Temple and bowing to the sun.” Jonathan, too, rendered in that manner: and they were destroying and bowing.” Menachem (p. 171) classified it as an expression of bending down, like (Isa. 51:23): “Bend down (שְּׁחִי) and let us cross.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:17:1)


**הֲנָקֵל לְבֵית יְהוּדָה.** הַמְעַט לָהֶם וְקַל בְּעֵינֵיהֶם שְׁאָר עֲבֵירוֹת שֶׁבְּיָדָם?


**Was it [too] trivial to the house of Judah** Is it trivial and light in their eyes, the other sins that they have committed?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:17:2)


**מֵעֲשׂוֹת אֶת הַתּוֹעֵבוֹת.** אִם הָיוּ חֲדֵלִים **מֵעֲשׂוֹת אֶת כָּל הַתּוֹעֵבוֹת אֲשֶׁר עָשׂוּ** בְּבֵיתִי **פֹּה**:


**to prevent them from performing the abominations** that they should refrain from doing all the abominations that they have done in My house here?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:17:3)


**כִּי מָלְאוּ אֶת הָאָרֶץ חָמָס.** וְנָקֵל בְּעֵינֵיהֶם אוֹתוֹ עָוֹן, וַיָּשׁוּבוּ לְהַכְעִיסֵנִי בְּתוֹךְ בֵּיתִי בַּתּוֹעֵבוֹת הָאֵלֶּה:


**for they have [already] filled the land with violence** and that that iniquity is trivial in their eyes, and they repeatedly provoke Me within My house with these abominations.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 8:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/8:17:4)


**וְהִנָּם שׁוֹלְחִים אֶת הַזְּמוֹרָה אֶל אַפָּם.** וְהִנֵּה שׁוֹלְחִים אֶל חוֹטְמָם אֶת רֵיחַ רַע שֶׁל זְמוֹרָה זוֹ, שֶׁהֵם מְזַמְּרִים בָּרוּחַ הַיּוֹצֵא מִפִּי טַבַּעַת, כְּלוֹמַר: בָּשְׁתָּם זֶה אֶל פְּנֵיהֶם יָשׁוּב. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּירֵשׁ **וְהִנָּם שׁוֹלְחִים אֶת הַזְּמוֹרָה אֶל אַפָּם** עִנְיַן דּוֹפִי וְקָלוֹן הוּא. לְפֵירוּשׁוֹ הִשְׁוָה רֵיחַ מִקְטַרְתָּם וְכַעַס קָרְבָּנָם וְנִיחוֹחִים (יחזקאל כ:כח) לִמְלֶאכֶת דּוֹפִי נְקָבִים, וַ**אֲחוֹרֵיהֶם אֶל הֵיכַל ה׳** יוֹרֶה עַל הָעִנְיָן:


**and behold they send disgrace into their nose** Behold they send to their nose the foul odor of this “song,” which they “sing” with the wind emerging from their anus; i.e., this disgrace of theirs will come back to their faces. Menachem (p. 79) explained: “and behold they send the disgrace into their nose” is a matter of disgrace and shame; and according to his explanation, He compares the aroma of their censers and (Ezek. 20:28) “the anger of their sacrifices” and (ibid.) “their pleasant aroma” to the work of the disgrace of the orifices. “Their backs to the Temple of the Lord” implies this idea.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:1:1)


**קָרְבוּ פְקֻדּוֹת הָעִיר.** הַקְרִיבוּ אֶת הַפְּקוּדִים אֲשֶׁר הִפְקַדְתִּי לְשַׁחֵת אֶת הָעִיר:


**Bring near those appointed over the city** Bring near the appointees whom I appointed to destroy the city.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:2:1)


**שִׁשָּׁה אֲנָשִׁים.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּם בְּמַסֶּכֶת שַׁבָּת (נה.): קֶצֶף, אַף, וְחֵמָה, מַשְׁחִית, מְשַׁבֵּר, מְכַלֶּה:


**six men** Our Rabbis explained them in Tractate Shabbath (55a) as Anger, Wrath, Fury, Destroyer, Breaker, Annihilator.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:2:2)


**מַפָּצוֹ.** שֶׁהוּא מְשַׁבֵּר אֶת הַכֹּל, כְּמוֹ ״וְנִפֵּץ אֶת עֹלָלַיִךְ״ (תהלים קלז:ט):


**his sledgehammer** Heb. כְּלִי מַפַּצוֹ, which smashes everything, as in (Ps. 137: 9): “and dash (וְנִפֵּץ) your infants.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:2:3)


**וְקֶסֶת הַסּוֹפֵר.** פִּנְקָס; הֵם לוּחוֹת שֶׁמְּחֻפִּין בְּשַׁעֲוָה, וְחוֹרְתִין בָּם בְּעֵט:


**with a scribe’s tablet** [Synonymous with] פִּנְקַס. These are wax- covered tablets upon which they engrave with a stylus.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:2:4)


**אֵצֶל מִזְבַּח הַנְּחֹשֶׁת.** הוּא מִזְבַּח אֲבָנִים שֶׁעָשָׂה שְׁלֹמֹה (מלכים א ח:סד; ט:כה) תַּחַת מִזְבַּח הַנְּחֹשֶׁת שֶׁעָשָׂה מֹשֶׁה בַּמִּדְבָּר (שמות לח:א-ב):


**beside the copper altar** This was the stone altar erected by Solomon instead of the copper altar that Moses had made in the desert.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:3:1)


**מֵעַל הַכְּרוּב.** מֵעַל הַכַּפֹּרֶת, שֶׁהַשְּׁכִינָה הָיְתָה שׁוֹרָה שָׁם עַד הֵנָּה, וְהִתְחִילָה לְהִסְתַּלֵּק וְלָצֵאת לַחוּץ מְעַט מְעַט, עֶשֶׂר מַסָּעוֹת (ראש השנה לא.), וְזֶה מַסָּע הָרִאשׁוֹן(עיין עמוס ט פסוק א ברש״י וצריך עיון יוש״ה): מִן הַכְּרוּב לְ**מִפְתַּן** בֵּית קָדְשֵׁי הַקֳּדָשִׁים:


**from upon the cherub** which was on the Ark cover, for the Shechinah had been resting there until then, and it began to withdraw and to go outside little by little in ten steps, and this is the first stepfrom the cherub to the threshold of the Holy of Holies.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:4:1)


**וְהִתְוִיתָ תָּו.** וְרָשַׁמְתָּ רֹשֶׁם:


**and you shall mark a sign** You shall mark a sign.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:4:2)


**עַל מִצְחוֹת הָאֲנָשִׁים.** הַצַּדִּיקִים, כְּדֵי לִהְיוֹת לְאוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יִפְגְּעוּ בָם הַמַּשְׁחִיתִים (להלן ו):


**on the foreheads of the [righteous] men** in order to be for a sign that the destroyers should not strike them.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:5:1)


**וְלָאֵלֶּה אָמַר בְּאָזְנַי.** לְשֵׁשֶׁת הַמְּחַבְּלִים:


**And to these, He said in my ears** To these six destroyers.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:6:1)


**וּמִמִּקְדָּשִׁי תָּחֵלּוּ.** מִן הָעוֹמְדִים לִפְנֵי מִקְדָּשִׁי; וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ: אַל תִּקְרֵי **מִמִּקְדָּשִׁי** אֶלָּא ׳מִמְּקֻדָּשַׁי׳, מִן הַמְקֻדָּשִׁים לִי, הֵם אוֹתָם חֲקוּקֵי הַתָּו שֶׁהִתְרָה בָּהֶם מִלְּהַזִּיקָם, חָזַר וְצִוָּה לְחַבְּלָם, לְפִי שֶׁאָמְרָה מִדַּת הַדִּין לְפָנָיו: מַה נִּשְׁתַּנּוּ אֵלּוּ מֵאֵלּוּ, הֲרֵי לֹא מִחוּ בָהֶם; כִּדְאִיתָא בְּמַסֶּכֶת שַׁבָּת (נה.):


**and you shall commence from My sanctuary** From those standing before My sanctuary. Our Rabbis said: Do not read וּמִמִִִּקְדָשִּׁי, and from My sanctuary, but וּמִמְּקֻדָשַּׁי, and from My sanctified ones, from those sanctified to Me. They are the ones marked with the sign, whom He had warned [them] not to hurt. [Now] He reneged and commanded [the angels] to destroy [even] them because the Divine standard of justice contended before Him, “Why are these different from those? Is it not so that they did not protest?” As is stated in Tractate Shabbath (55a).



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:8:1)


**וְנֵאשֲׁאַר אֲנִי.** וְנִשְׁאַרְתִּי אֲנִי:


**that I remained** Heb. וְנֵאשֲּׁאַר, and I remained.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/9:9:1)


**מָלְאָה מֻטֶּה.** דִּין מְעֻקָּל (חבקוק א:ד):


**full of perversion** Crooked judgments.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:1:1)


**עַל רֹאשׁ הַכְּרוּבִים.** עַל רֹאשׁ אַרְבָּעָה חַיּוֹת הַמֶּרְכָּבָה, וּבְפָרָשָׁה זוֹ חָזַר לִקְרוֹתָם כְּרוּבִים, לְפִי שֶׁנֶּהֶפְכוּ פְּנֵי הַשּׁוֹר לִפְנֵי כְרוּב כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּפָרָשָׁה זוֹ, וְאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ יְחֶזְקֵאל בִּקֵּשׁ רַחֲמִים וְהָפְכוּ לִכְרוּב, אָמַר לְפָנָיו רִבּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם אֵין קָטֵיגוֹר נַעֲשֶׂה סָנֵיגוֹר:


**over the heads of the cherubim** Over the heads of the four living beings of the Chariot. In this chapter, he switches over to calling them [all] cherubim because the face of the ox was transformed into the face of a cherub, as it is written in this chapter. Our Rabbis said (Chag. 13b): Ezekiel begged for mercy and transformed it to a cherub. He said before Him, “Master of the world, an accuser cannot become a defender.”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:2:1)


**בֵּינוֹת לַגַּלְגַּל.** בֵּין הָאוֹפַנִּים. כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר וַיֹּאמֶר... וַיֹּאמֶר אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לִדְרֹשׁ: הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אָמַר לְגַבְרִיאֵל, וְהוּא בִּקֵּשׁ מִן הַכְּרוּב לְתִתָּם לוֹ, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּצְטַנְּנוּ הַגֶּחָלִים וְתֵקַל הַגְּזֵרָה (איכה רבה א:מא); וְכֵן הוּא מְפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן **וַיִּשָּׂא וַיִּתֵּן אֶל חָפְנֵי לְבֻשׁ הַבַּדִּים**


**between the wheels** Heb. (בֵּינוֹת לַגַלְגַל) [lit. between the wheel.] Wherever it says, “And he said...and he said,” it is meant only to be expounded upon. The Holy One, blessed be He, told Gabriel [to take the coals], but he [Gabriel] asked the cherub to [take them first and then] give them to him so that the coals would cool and the decree would be mitigated. And this is clearly stated in the chapter (verse 7): “and he picked up [the fire] and gave [it] into the hands of the one clothed in linen.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:2:2)


**וּזְרֹק עַל הָעִיר.** לֹא זְרָקָם מִיָּד, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁקִּבְּלָם לְבוּשׁ הַבַּדִּים לֹא הִשְׁלִיכָם עַד לְאַחַר שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים לְקַבָּלָתוֹ, שֶׁנִּתְעַמְּמוּ הַגֶּחָלִים בְּיָדוֹ כָּל שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים. וְתֵדַע, שֶׁהֲרֵי נְבוּאָה זוֹ בַּשָּׁנָה הַשִּׁשִּׁית נֶאֶמְרָה (יחזקאל ח:א), וְהָעִיר חָרְבָה בִּשְׁנַת אַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה (מלכים ב כה:ב, ח):


**and cast [them] upon the city** He did not cast them immediately, and although the one clothed in linen received them, he did not cast them until six years after he had received them, for the coals were smoldering in his hand for all the six years. And you should know [that this is true] because this prophecy was said in the sixth year, and the city was destroyed in the eleventh year.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:3:1)


**מִימִין לַבַּיִת.** בַּדָּרוֹם:


**at the right of the House** In the south [of the Temple].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:3:2)


**הֶחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִית.** הִיא הָעֲזָרָה:


**the inner court** This is the Temple court.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:4:1)


**עַל מִפְתַּן הַבַּיִת.** הוּא הַסִּלּוּק עַצְמוֹ, הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה (יחזקאל ט:ג):


**onto the threshold of the House** That is the very [stage of] evacuation mentioned above (9:3).



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:5:1)


**עַד הֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה.** הַר הַבַּיִת. יָכוֹל מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהַקּוֹל נָמוּךְ? תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר: **כְּקוֹל אֵל שַׁדַּי בְּדַבְּרוֹ**! אֶלָּא מִשֶּׁהוּא מַגִּיעַ לֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה הוּא נִפְסָק (במדבר רבה יד:כא):


**until the outer court** The Temple Mount. I may think that [it was heard no further] because the sound was low. Therefore, Scripture states: “like the voice of God Almighty when He speaks.” Yet, when it would reach the outer court, it would [suddenly] stop.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:9:1)


**וְהִנֵּה אַרְבָּעָה אוֹפַנִּים.** לְפִי שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּנֵּית אַחַת מִן הַפָּנִים (יחזקאל א:י), הֻזְקַק לַחֲזוֹר וְלִשְׁנוֹת כָּאן כָּל מַעֲשֵׂה הַחַיּוֹת כְּבָרִאשׁוֹנָה (יחזקאל א), שֶׁלֹּא תֹאמַר: מֶרְכָּבָה אַחֶרֶת הִיא:


**and behold four wheels** Since one of the faces was changed, it was necessary to repeat here the entire account of the living beings as at first, so that you should not say that it is another Chariot.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:12:1)


**וִידֵיהֶם.** שֶׁל אוֹפַנִּים, הֵם הַחוֹר שֶׁהַמֶּרְכָּבָה מִתְגַּלְגֶּלֶת בְּתוֹכָן, שֶׁקּוֹרִין בויי״ל בְּלַעַ״ז. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״וְאַשְׁדַּתְהוֹן״ – וְשִׁפּוּכֵיהֶם:


**and their hubs** Heb. וִידֵיהֶם of the wheels. They are the hole in which the [axle of the] chariot turns, called boyol, nave, hub. Jonathan, however, renders: וְאֲשְּׁדַתְהוֹן, and their pourings.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:12:2)


**לְאַרְבַּעְתָּם אוֹפַנֵּיהֶם.** לְאַרְבַּעַת אוֹפַנֵּי הַחַיּוֹת:


**to their four wheels** To the four wheels of the living beings.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:13:1)


**לָהֶם קוֹרָא הַגַּלְגַּל בְּאָזְנָי.** אֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי שֶׁהַמַּלְאָךְ הָיָה קוֹרֵא אֶת הָאוֹפַן גַּלְגַּל:


**to them was called hagalgal to my ears** I heard that the angel would call the wheel “galgal.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:13:2)


**קוֹרֵא הַגַּלְגַּל.** לָהֶם נִקְרָא שֵׁם גַּלְגַּל:


**was called hagalgal** to them was called the name “galgal.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:14:1)


**פְּנֵי הָאֶחָד פְּנֵי הַכְּרוּב.** הֵם הָיוּ פְּנֵי הַשּׁוֹר (יחזקאל א:י) שֶׁנֶּהֶפְכוּ לִכְרוּב:


**the face of one was the face of the cherub** That was the face of the ox, which was transformed into a cherub.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:19:1)


**פֶּתַח שַׁעַר בֵּית ה׳.** פֶּתַח הַהֵיכָל:


**at the entrance of the...gate of the House of the Lord** The entrance of the heichal.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:19:2)


**הַקַּדְמוֹנִי.** שֶׁבַּמִּזְרָח; וְזֶה הָיָה מַסַּע סִלּוּק הַשֵּׁנִי: מִמִּפְתַּן בֵּית קָדְשֵׁי הַקֳּדָשִׁים לָעֲזָרָה (יחזקאל ט:ג):


**the eastern** Heb. הַקַדְמוֹנִי, that is in the east. This was the second stage of the evacuation, from the threshold of the Holy of Holies to the court.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:20:1)


**הִיא הַחַיָּה.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁרָאִיתִי בַּמֶּרְכָּבָה זוֹ כָּל מַעֲשֵׂה הַמֶּרְכָּבָה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה (יחזקאל א), אֲנִי אוֹמֵר **הִיא אֲשֶׁר רָאִיתִי בִּנְהַר כְּבָר**:


**It was the living being** Since I saw in this Chariot the entire phenomenon of the first Chariot, I say “[It] is the one I saw by the river Chebar.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 10:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/10:20:2)


**וָאֵדַע.** כִּי נֶהְפַּךְ פְּנֵי הַשּׁוֹר לִפְנֵי כְרוּב (חגיגה יג:); וְהָיוּ בָהּ שְׁנֵי פְּנֵי אָדָם: פָּנִים קְטַנִּים שֶׁל יֶלֶד, וּפָנִים גְּדוֹלִים שֶׁל אָדָם:


**and I knew** that the face of the ox was transformed into the face of a cherub, and there were in it two faces of man: a small face of a child and a large face of a man.



## Chapter 11


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:1:1)


**וְהִנֵּה בְּפֶתַח הַשַּׁעַר וגו׳.** הֵם הֵם שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה ״אֲחוֹרֵיהֶם אֶל הֵיכַל ה׳״ (יחזקאל ח:טז), אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא פֵּרֵשׁ שָׁם מִי הָיוּ, וְכָאן פֵּרֵשׁ אֶת שְׁמוֹת שְׁנַיִם מֵהֶם:


**and behold at the entrance of the gate [twenty-five men]** They are the very same ones that he mentioned above (ad loc.): “their backs to the Temple of the Lord” except that he did not explain there who they were, and here he delineates the names of two of them.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:3:1)


**הָאוֹמְרִים לֹא בְקָרוֹב.** יָבוֹאוּ דִּבְרֵי הַנְּבִיאִים עָלֵינוּ:


**Who say, ‘It is not near’** that the words of the prophets will come upon us.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:3:2)


**בְּנוֹת בָּתִּים.** אֵין לָחוּשׁ לָהֶם, אֶלָּא **בְּנוֹת בָּתִּים** וְשֶׁבֶת בְּתוֹכָם, כִּי לֹא נִגְלֶה מִן הָעִיר הַזֹּאת עַד מוֹתֵינוּ:


**build houses** We need not be concerned with them; rather build houses in which to dwell, for we shall not be exiled from this city until we die.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:3:3)


**הִיא הַסִּיר וַאֲנַחְנוּ הַבָּשָׂר.** כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאֵין הַבָּשָׂר יוֹצֵא מִן הַסִּיר עַד גְּמַר בִּשּׁוּלוֹ, כָּךְ לֹא נֵצֵא מִמֶּנָּה עַד שֶׁנָּמוּת:


**it is the pot, and we are the meat** Just as the meat is not taken out of the pot until it has been completely cooked, neither shall we leave it until we die.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:5:1)


**כֵּן אֲמַרְתֶּם.** יָדַעְתִּי כִּי זֹאת אֲמַרְתֶּם, שֶׁיְּרוּשָׁלַיִם הַסִּיר וְאַתֶּם הַבָּשָׂר (יחזקאל יא:ג); וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר לָכֶם, כִּי לַחֲלָלִים שֶׁנְּתַתֶּם בָּהּ הִיא לְסִיר (יחזקאל יא:ז), שֶׁהֵם נִגְמְרוּ בָהּ, אֲבָל לָכֶם לֹא תִהְיֶה לְסִיר, לִקְלוֹט אֶתְכֶם עַד יוֹם מִיתָה, כִּי אֶתְכֶם אוֹצִיא מִתּוֹכָהּ:


**So have you said** I know that you have said this, that Jerusalem is the pot and you are the meat, but I say to you that for the slain whom you have laid in it, it becomes a pot, for they are finished in it; but for you, it will not become a pot to retain you until the day of death, for you I shall take out of it.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:5:2)


**וּמַעֲלוֹת רוּחֲכֶם.** הָעוֹלֶה עַל דַּעְתְּכֶם, בּוֹטְרָ״א טָלַנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and what enters your mind** [lit. what goes up in your spirit.] What goes up on your thoughts, votre talant in Old French, your mind [intentions].



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:7:1)


**הוֹצִיא.** כְּמוֹ לְהוֹצִיא; וְלַשָּׁוְא בְּנִיתֶם הַבָּתִּים (יחזקאל יא:ג), כִּי בַּגָּלוּת תֵּלְכוּ, הָאָבוֹת וְהַבָּנִים, וְאֵין יוֹרֵשׁ אֶת הַבָּתִּים:


**take out** Heb. הוֹצִיא, like לְהוֹצִיא to take out. In vain have you built the houses, for you will go into exile, both the fathers and the sons, and no one will inherit the houses.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:8:1)


**חֶרֶב יְרֵאתֶם.** כְּשֶׁפְּנִיתֶם לְעֶזְרָה לְמֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּצִדְקִיָּהוּ ״וַיִּמְרֹד בּוֹ לִשְׁלֹחַ מַלְאָכָיו מִצְרַיִם״ (יחזקאל יז:טו):


**You feared the sword** when you turned for aid to the king of Egypt, as it is said regarding Zedekiah (17:15): “And he rebelled against him (the king of Babylon) by sending his emissaries to Egypt.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:10:1)


**עַל גְּבוּל יִשְׂרָאֵל.** בְּסוֹף הַגְּבוּל; וְהִיא רִבְלָה אֶרֶץ חֲמָת (ירמיה לט:ה), הִיא אַנְטוֹכְיָא (ויקרא רבה ה:ב); שָׁם נִשְׁפַּט צִדְקִיָּהוּ בְּיַד נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסֵפֶר מְלָכִים (מלכים ב כה:ו-ז), שָׁם שָׁחַט אֶת בָּנָיו, שָׁם נִקֵּר אֶת עֵינָיו; וְהִיא סוֹף הַגְּבוּל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בַּמְרַגְּלִים וּבְכַמָּה מְקוֹמוֹת: ״עַד לְבֹא חֲמָת״ (במדבר יג:כא):


**by the border of Israel** At the end of the boundary; that is Riblah, the land of Hamath, which is Antioch. There Zedekiah was judged by Nebuchadnezzar, as is stated in the Book of (II) Kings (25: 6f.). There he slaughtered his sons, there he put out his eyes. And it is the end of the boundary, as is said concerning the spies and in many places (Num. 13:21): “until... coming to Hamath.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:11:1)


**לֹא תִהְיֶה לָכֶם לְסִיר.** הַמְשַׁמֶּרֶת נְתָחֶיהָ עַד גְּמַר בִּשּׁוּלָם:


**It will not become a pot for you** [A pot,] which retains its pieces until they are completely cooked.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:11:2)


**וְאַתֶּם תִּהְיוּ בְתוֹכָהּ לְבָשָׂר.** שֶׁתִּנָּתְכוּ בְתוֹכָהּ וְתִשָּׁבְרוּ בְתוֹכָהּ כְּבָשָׂר בְּסִיר וּבְתוֹךְ קַלַּחַת:


**but you will become in its midst as meat** for you will be melted in its midst, and you will be broken in its midst as meat in a pot and in a cauldron.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:11:3)


**אֶל גְּבוּל יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶשְׁפֹּט אֶתְכֶם.** וְלֹא בְתוֹכָהּ תָּמוּתוּ:


**by the border of Israel I will judge you** and not in its midst will you die.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:15:1)


**אַחֶיךָ אַחֶיךָ.** גָּלוּת שׁוֹמְרוֹן (מלכים ב יז) וְגָלוּת יְכָנְיָה (מלכים ב כד):


**Your brethren, your brethren** The exile of Samaria and the exile of Jeconiah.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:15:2)


**וְכָל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל.** שֶׁהָלְכוּ בַּגּוֹלָה:


**and the entire house of Israel** who have gone into exile.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:15:3)


**אֲשֶׁר אָמְרוּ לָהֶם.** אֲשֶׁר אָמְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם יוֹשְׁבֵי יְרוּשָׁלַיִם שֶׁלֹּא גָלוּ עִם יְכָנְיָה, שֶׁהִמְלִיךְ עֲלֵיהֶם נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר אֶת צִדְקִיָּהוּ (מלכים ב כד:יז), וְעַתָּה גַּם הֵם מְשַׂחֲקִים עַל אֲחֵיהֶם שֶׁגָּלוּ, וְאוֹמְרִים עֲלֵיהֶם: **רַחֲקוּ מֵעַל ה׳**:


**to whom...said** About whom theythe dwellers of Jerusalem who have not been exiled with Jeconiah, over whom Nebuchadnezzar enthroned Zedekiahsaid. Now they ridicule their brethren who have been exiled, and say about them, “They have distanced themselves from the Lord.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:16:1)


**לְמִקְדָּשׁ מְעַט.** ״לְבָתֵּי כְנִישְׁתָּא דְּאִינוּן יָתְבִין תַּנְיָין לְבֵית מִקְדָּשָׁא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**a minor sanctuary** Synagogues, which are in a position second to the Temple.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:19:1)


**לֵב בָּשָׂר.** לֵב רַךְ וְנוֹחַ לְהִכָּנַע:


**a heart of flesh** A heart that is soft and easily submissive.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:21:1)


**וְאֶל לֵב שִׁקּוּצֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳.** וְאוֹתָם אֲשֶׁר אֶל לֵב שִׁקּוּץ וְתוֹעֵבָה **לִבָּם הוֹלֵךְ**, שֶׁנִּשְׁאֲרוּ בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, שֶׁלֹּא גָלוּ, וְטוֹעִים בַּעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה וְשִׁקּוּצִים:


**But to the heart of their detestations, etc.** And as for those whose hearts go to every detestation and abomination, who remained in Jerusalem, who were not exiled, and who go astray after idolatry and detestations.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:21:2)


**דַּרְכָּם בְּרֹאשָׁם נָתַתִּי.** שֶׁאוֹצִיא עֲלֵיהֶם חֶרֶב וּשְׁבִי וְיֵלְכוּ בַגּוֹלָה:


**I have placed their way upon their heads** for I will draw the sword upon them, and [place them in] captivity, and they will go into exile.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:22:1)


**וַיִּשְׂאוּ הַכְּרוּבִים.** לְאַחַר שֶׁגָּמַר דְּבָרוֹ אֵלַי, נִסְתַּלֵּק:


**Then the cherubim lifted** After He finished His words to me, He left.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:23:1)


**עַל הָהָר אֲשֶׁר מִקֶּדֶם לָעִיר.** הוּא הַר הַזֵּיתִים, שֶׁהוּא בְּמִזְרָחוֹ שֶׁל הָעִיר, וְזֶה מַסָּע שְׁלִישִׁית (ראה רש״י יחזקאל ט:ג):


**on the mountain that was on the east of the city** That is the Mount of Olives, which is east of the city, and this is the third stage [of the Shechinah’s departure].



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 11:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/11:24:1)


**בַּמַּרְאֶה בְּרוּחַ אֱלֹהִים.** נִדְמֶה לִי כְּאִלּוּ עַכְשָׁיו הוּא מַחֲזִירֵנִי מִירוּשָׁלַיִם לְבָבֶל:


**in the vision, with the spirit of God** It seemed to me as though He now was taking me back from Jerusalem to Babylon.



## Chapter 12


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:3:1)


**כְּלֵי גוֹלָה.** כְּלִי לִשְׁתּוֹת, וּמְקִידָה קְטַנָּה (משנה שבת כד,ה) לָלוּשׁ בָּהּ חֲרָרָה (תנחומא בא ט), אוֹת לְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁבִּירוּשָׁלַיִם שֶׁיַּעֲשׂוּ כֵן, וְלֹא יִצְטָרְכוּ לָלוּשׁ בָּצֵק בְּגוּמָא עַל גַּבֵּי קַרְקַע וְהַחֲצָצִים נִכְנָסִים בְּתוֹכוֹ; וְהֵם לֹא שָׁמְעוּ לוֹ, וְהֻצְרְכוּ לְכָךְ, וְהוּא הַכָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר קִינוֹת ״וַיַּגְרֵס בֶּחָצָץ שִׁנִּי״ (איכה ג:טז) – בְּמִדְרַשׁ קִינוֹת (איכה רבה א:כב):


**implements for exile** A vessel from which to drink and a small earthen bowl in which to knead a cake, as a sign for the house of Israel in Jerusalem to do likewise, so that they would not have to knead dough in a hole on the ground with the gravel going into it. But they did not heed him, and they had to do just that. This is what is written in the Book of Lamentations (3:16): “Indeed, He made my teeth grind on gravel.” In Midrash Lamentations.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:3:2)


**וְגָלֵה יוֹמָם.** מִיּוֹם אֶל יוֹם, שְׁנַיִם שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים, נְהוֹג בְּעַצְמְךָ כְּאָדָם הַמֵּכִין צְעָדָיו לְגָלוּת, שֶׁיִּרְאוּ בְּנֵי הַגּוֹלָה וְיִשְׁאֲלוּ: מַה מַּעֲשֶׂיךָ? וְאַתָּה תַּגִּיד לָהֶם: שֶׁסִּימָן הוּא לְצִדְקִיָּהוּ וְעַמּוֹ; וְיִשְׁלְחוּ אֵלֵיהֶם אֵלּוּ אֶת שְׁלוּחֵיהֶם, וְיוֹדִיעוּ לַאֲחֵיהֶם וּקְרוֹבֵיהֶם אֲשֶׁר שָׁם כְּכָל אֲשֶׁר יִשְׁמְעוּ מִפִּיךָ. וְכֵן כָּל ׳יוֹמָם׳ שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא לְשׁוֹן ׳יוֹם יוֹם׳ הוּא, דָּבָר הַנּוֹהֵג יָמִים רַבִּים, כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי מִשְּׁמוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי מְנַחֵם, וֶאֱמֶת דְּבָרָיו:


**and go into exile day by day** Heb. יוֹמָם, from day to day, two or three days, behave like a man preparing his steps for exile, so that the people of the exile will see and ask what you are doing, and you shall tell them that it symbolizes Zedekiah and his people [in Jerusalem], and these people will send their messengers and inform their brothers and relatives who are there, according to everything they hear from your mouth. Likewise, every [occurrence of] יוֹמָם in Scripture is an expression denoting “day by day,” something that goes on [continually] for many days. This I heard in the name of Rabbi Menachem, and his words are true.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:3:3)


**אוּלַי יִרְאוּ.** שֶׁמָּא יָבִינוּ, אֶפְּרְצְבְרוֹנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**perhaps they will see** Perhaps they will understand, apercevront in French, they will realize.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:3:4)


**כִּי בֵית מְרִי הֵמָּה.** לְפִיכָךְ עֲדַיִן הוּא שֶׁמָּא:


**for a rebellious house are they** Therefore, it is still a “perhaps.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:4:1)


**כְּמוֹצָאֵי.** קוֹמָ״א אִישׁוּאָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**like the departures of** Heb. כְּמוֹצָאֵי, come issues, essues in Old French, [comme les issues in modern French] like the departures.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:5:1)


**חֲתָר לְךָ בַקִּיר.** סִימָן לְצִדְקִיָּהוּ שֶׁיָּצָא דֶּרֶךְ מַחְתֶּרֶת מְעָרָה (יחזקאל יב:יג), כִּי יָרֵא לָצֵאת דֶּרֶךְ בְּפַרְהֶסְיָא מִפְּנֵי הַכַּשְׂדִּים:


**dig for yourself in the wall** Symbolic of Zedekiah, who [later] went out through a tunnel [leading into] a cave, for he was afraid to go out in the open because of the Chaldeans.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:6:1)


**בָּעֲלָטָה תוֹצִיא.** לִכְשֶׁתֶּחְשַׁךְ תּוֹצִיא אֶת כֵּלֶיךָ:


**in the dark you shall bring [them] out** When it becomes dark, you shall bring out your implements.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:7:1)


**כֵּלַי הוֹצֵאתִי כִּכְלֵי גוֹלָה יוֹמָם.** יוֹם יוֹם הָיִיתִי מַרְאֶה לָהֶם שֶׁאֲנִי מְתַקֵּן לְעַצְמִי כְּלֵי גוֹלָה:


**I brought out my implements as implements for exile, from day to day** Every day, I would show them that I was preparing for myself implements for exile.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:10:1)


**הַנָּשִׂיא הַמַּשָּׂא הַזֶּה.** עַל הַמֶּלֶךְ צִדְקִיָּהוּ אֲנִי מַרְאֶה **הַמַּשָּׂא הַזֶּה**; לְשׁוֹן נְבוּאָה:


**[Concerning] the prince is this burden** Concerning King Zedekiah, I am showing this מַשָּׂא, burden, an expression denoting prophecy.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:12:1)


**פָּנָיו יְכַסֶּה.** מֵחֲמַת כְּלִימָה, שֶׁהוּא בּוֹרֵחַ מִן הָעִיר לַיְלָה:


**He will cover his face** because of shame, that he is fleeing from the city at night.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:13:1)


**וּפָרַשְׂתִּי אֶת רִשְׁתִּי עָלָיו.** מְעָרָה הָיְתָה לוֹ מִבֵּיתוֹ עַד עַרְבוֹת יְרִיחוֹ, וְיָצָא דֶּרֶךְ הַמְּעָרָה (פסיקתא רבתי כו); מָה עָשָׂה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא? הֵבִיא צְבִי אֶחָד לִפְנֵי חֵיל כַּשְׂדִּים, וְהָלַךְ לִפְנֵיהֶם עַל גַּג הַמְּעָרָה וְהֵם רוֹדְפִים אוֹתוֹ, וּכְשֶׁיָּצָא צִדְקִיָּהוּ מִן הַמְּעָרָה בְּעַרְבוֹת יְרִיחוֹ רָאוּהוּ יוֹצֵא וּתְפָשׂוּהוּ (ראה רש״י מלכים ב כה:ד; ירמיה לט:ד):


**And I shall spread my net over him** There was a cave [extending] from his house to the plains of Jericho, and he went out through the cave. What did the Holy One, blessed be He, do? He brought before the army of the Chaldeans a deer which was walking before them on the roof of the cave, and they [began] chasing it. And when Zedekiah emerged from the cave in the plains of Jericho, they saw him emerging and caught him.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:13:2)


**וְאוֹתָהּ לֹא יִרְאֶה.** כִּי בְרִבְלָה בְּאֶרֶץ חֲמָת דִּבֵּר אִתּוֹ מִשְׁפָּטִים וְעִוֵּר אֶת עֵינָיו, וְאַחַר כָּךְ הֱבִיאוֹ לְבָבֶל, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסֵפֶר מְלָכִים (מלכים ב כה:ה-ז) וּבְיִרְמְיָה (לט:ה-ז):
