**but he will not see it** because in Riblah in the land of Hamath, he [Nebuchadnezzar] called him to account and blinded his eyes. Afterwards, they brought him to Babylon, as is stated in the Book of (II) Kings (25:7 and in Jer. 39:7).



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:14:1)


**וְכָל אֲגַפָּיו.** שָׂרָיו. וּמְנַחֵם פָּתַר בּוֹ לְשׁוֹן כְּנָפַיִם:


**and all his officers** Heb. אֲגַפָּיו, his officers, and Menachem (p. 15) interpreted it as an expression of wings (גף).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:14:2)


**אֱזָרֶה לְכָל רוּחַ.** אֶפַּנְדְרִ״י בְּלַעַ״ז, הֵם יוֹחָנָן בֶּן קָרֵחַ וְשָׂרֵי הַחַיָּלִים שֶׁגָּלוּ לְמִצְרַיִם, וְשָׁם הִשִּׂיגָתָם חַרְבּוֹ שֶׁל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר כְּשֶׁהֶחֱרִיב אֶת מִצְרַיִם (ירמיה מג:ז-יג):


**I will scatter to every direction** Heb. אֱזָרֶה, epandre in Old French, to disperse, and they are Johanan the son of Kareah and the officers of the armies, who were exiled to Egypt, where the sword of Nebuchadnezzar overtook them when he destroyed Egypt.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:16:1)


**לְמַעַן יְסַפְּרוּ אֶת כָּל תּוֹעֲבוֹתֵיהֶם.** וְיֹאמְרוּ: בַּעֲוֹנֵינוּ לָקִינוּ, כִּי כָזֹאת וְכָזֹאת עָשִׂינוּ; וְאוֹדִיעַ כִּי לֹא עַל חִנָּם כִּלִּיתִים:


**so that they will tell of all their abominations** and they will say, “Because of our sins, we have suffered, since such-and-such things we did,” and I shall make known that I had not annihilated you for nothing.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:19:1)


**מִמְּלוֹאָהּ.** מֵעָשְׁרָהּ:


**of its fullness** Of its wealth.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:19:2)


**מֵחֲמַס כָּל הַיּוֹשְׁבִים בָּהּ.** בַּעֲוֹן חֲמַס יוֹשְׁבֶיהָ:


**because of the violence of all who dwell therein** Because of the iniquity of the violence of its dwellers.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:20:1)


**הַנּוֹשָׁבוֹת.** לִי״שׁ אַשֵׁיּיאַ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the inhabited** Heb. הַנוֹשָבוֹת, les assiyes, inhabited.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:22:1)


**מָה הַמָּשָׁל הַזֶּה.** לָמָּה אַתֶּם מוֹשְׁלִים כֵּן תָּמִיד בְּפִיכֶם, לִמְשׁוֹל דָּבָר זֶה:


**what is this proverb** Why do you constantly use this proverb with your mouth, to make a proverb of this matter?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:22:2)


**יַאַרְכוּ הַיָּמִים.** וְלֹא תָבֹא הַפּוּרְעָנוּת, וּבְתוֹךְ כָּךְ יֹאבַד הַחָזוֹן הָרָע שֶׁהַנְּבִיאִים נִבָּאִים לָנוּ, וְיִשְׁתַּכַּח וְיִשְׁתַּקֵּעַ:


**The days will draw on** and the retribution will not come, and meanwhile, the evil prophecy that the prophets are prophesying about us will be lost, and it will be forgotten and disappear.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:23:1)


**יִמְשְׁלוּ.** לְשׁוֹן מָשָׁל:


**use it as a proverb** Heb. יִמְשְּׁלוּ, from the word מָשָּׁל, a proverb.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:24:1)


**כִּי לֹא יִהְיֶה עוֹד כָּל חֲזוֹן שָׁוְא.** לֹא יוּכְלוּ עוֹד נְבִיאֵי הַשֶּׁקֶר לְהַבְטִיחַ אֶתְכֶם שָׁלוֹם עַל שֶׁקֶר, כִּי מְהֵרָה אַקְדִּים אֶת דִּבְרֵי נְבִיאֵי הָאֱמֶת לָבוֹא:


**For there will never again be any false prophecy** The false prophets will no longer be able to promise you peace with falsehood, for speedily I will bring near the [realization of the] words of the true prophets.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:24:2)


**מִקְסַם חָלָק.** דִּבְנַיְּלָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז, קֶסֶם שֶׁל חֲלַקְלַקּוּת:


**glib divination** Heb. מִקְסַם חָלָק, devinaylle in Old French, soothsaying, divination. Divination of smoothness.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:25:1)


**תִּמָּשֵׁךְ.** שר״א אֲלוֹנְגִי״אָ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**will be drawn out** Heb. תִּמָשֵּׁך, sera allongee in French, will be deferred, delayed.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 12:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/12:27:1)


**לְיָמִים רַבִּים.** הַנְּבוּאָה לֹא תִתְקַיֵּים אֶלָּא לִזְמַן מְרֻבֶּה:


**for many days** The prophecy will not be fulfilled except after a long time.



## Chapter 13


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:3:1)


**הַנְּבָלִים.** הַשּׁוֹטִים:


**mad** Heb. הַנְבָלִים, madmen.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:3:2)


**רוּחָם.** כְּמוֹ רְצוֹנָם:


**their desire** Heb. רוּחָם [lit. their spirit,] like רְצוֹנָם, their will.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:4:1)


**כְּשׁוּעָלִים בָּחֳרָבוֹת.** כְּשֶׁיָּבֹא אָדָם עַל פֶּרֶץ הַחוּרְבָּה, בּוֹרֵחַ הַשּׁוּעָל עַל דֶּרֶךְ פֶּרֶץ אַחֶרֶת, וְאֵינוֹ עוֹמֵד בַּפֶּרֶץ לְהִלָּחֵם:


**Like foxes in ruins** When a man comes upon a breach in a ruin, the fox flees through another breach, and it does not stand in the breach to fight.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:5:1)


**לֹא עֲלִיתֶם בַּפְּרָצוֹת.** לֹא עֲשִׂיתֶם מַעֲשִׂים טוֹבִים, שֶׁתּוּכְלוּ לְעַכֵּב אֶת הָרָעָה מִלָּבֹא:


**You have not gone up into the breaches** You did not do good deeds so that you could prevent the evil from coming.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:6:1)


**חָזוּ.** לְשׁוֹן נְבוּאָה:


**They prophesied** Heb. חָזוּ, a term for prophecy.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:6:2)


**וְיִחֲלוּ לְקַיֵּם דָּבָר.** וְאֵיךְ סְבוּרִים לְקַיֵּם דִּבְרֵיהֶם? לְשׁוֹן תּוֹחֶלֶת; וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים אִיקְוִוירִירַ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז; לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן הַמְתָּנָה:


**and expect their words to be fulfilled** Heb. וְיִחֲלוּ . “Now how do they expect that their words will be fulfilled?” [The word is] an expression of hope (תּוֹחֶלֶת). Some say that it is ecuydiyant in Old French, and they hoped. [Other editions read: et croiront, and they believed.] Another explanation: An expression of waiting.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:10:1)


**וְהוּא בּוֹנֶה חַיִץ.** וְהִנֵּה עַמִּי כְּבוֹנִים בִּנְיַן מְחִיצָּה, וְהַנְּבִיאִים **טָחִים אוֹתוֹ תָּפֵל** – קַרְקַע הַדּוֹמֶה לְסִיד, וְאֵינוֹ מִתְקַיֵּם לִפְנֵי הַגְּשָׁמִים:


**and it is building a flimsy wall** And behold My people are like builders of a partition, and the prophets are plastering with daub-earth similar to lime cement, but which does not resist the rains.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:10:2)


**תָּפֵל.** אוֹטוֹמְנָ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, וְיֵ״שׁ אוֹמְרִים בְּלַאשְׁטְנִיְיָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**daub** Heb. תָּפֵל, ajo[s]tamanz in Old French, coating, layer, and others say: plastre, plaster.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:11:1)


**וְאַתֵּנָה.** כְּמוֹ וְאֶתֵּן:


**then you** Heb. וְאַתֵּנָה, like וְאֶתֵּן [the second person pronoun in the feminine plural].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:11:2)


**אַבְנֵי אֶלְגָּבִישׁ.** אַבְנֵי בָּרָד גְּדוֹלוֹת, וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי, לְפִי שֶׁהֵם מַזְהִירוֹת כְּכָל אַבְנֵי גָבִישׁ (ברד) הוּא מְדַמֶּה אוֹתָם אֶל גָּבִישׁ, שֶׁהִיא אֶבֶן טוֹבָה, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״רָאמוֹת וְגָבִישׁ״ (איוב כח:יח). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (ברכות נד:): **אֶל גָּבִישׁ** – שֶׁעָמְדוּ בָאֲוִיר עַל גַּב אִישׁ, וְזֶהוּ מֹשֶׁה רַבֵּינוּ (במדבר יב:ג), שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּמָטָר לֹא נִתַּךְ אָרְצָה״ (שמות ט:לג) – אוֹתָם שֶׁהִתְחִילוּ לִירֵד לֹא הִגִּיעוּ לָאָרֶץ:


**gigantic hailstones** Heb. אַבְנֵי אֶלְגָּבִישּׁ, huge hailstones, and I say that because they shine as do all [crystal] hailstones, he likens them to crystal (גָבִישּׁ), which is a precious stone, as in the way that it is stated (Job 28:18): “Coral and crystal (וְגָבִישּׁ).” Our Sages (Ber. 54b) said: [They are called] אֶלְגָבִישּׁ because they [once] stood in the air over a man עַל גַב אִישּׁ, and this is Moses, our teacher, as it is said (Exod. 9: 33): “and rain did not reach the earth” those that started to fall did not reach the earth.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:11:3)


**תִּפֹּלְנָה.** עַל הַקִּיר:


**will fall** on the wall.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:12:1)


**אַיֵּה הַטִּיחַ.** כָּךְ כְּשֶׁתָּבֹא הָרָעָה, יֵאָמֵר לָכֶם: **אַיֵּה** הַשָּׁלוֹם שֶׁהִבְטַחְתֶּם?


**Where is the plaster** When the calamity comes, such will be said to you: “Where is the peace that you promised?”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:14:1)


**הַקִּיר.** הִיא יְרוּשָׁלַיִם:


**the wall** That is Jerusalem.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:1)


**הוֹי לִמְתַפְּרוֹת כְּסָתוֹת.** קוֹטְרָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז. כְּמִין כְּסָתוֹת קְטַנּוֹת הָיוּ תּוֹפְרוֹת וְתוֹלוֹת לַנִּשְׁאָלִים אֲלֵיהֶם עַל **אֲצִילֵי יְדֵיהֶם**, וְקוֹסְמוֹת לָהֶם בְּאוֹתָן כְּסָתוֹת קֶסֶם שְׁקָרִים, וּלְמִי שֶׁהֵם חֲפֵצוֹת אוֹמְרוֹת קֶסֶם טוֹב, וּלְמִי שֶׁרוֹצוֹת אוֹמְרוֹת קֶסֶם רָע:


**Woe to those who sew cushions** Heb. לִמְתַפְּרוֹת, coseres(es) in Old French, needle-women; coudre, to sew. They would sew something like small cushions and hang them on the armpits of those consulting them, and they would divine with those cushions with a false divination; and to those whom they wished, they would tell a favorable divination, and to whom they wished, they would tell an unfavorable divination.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:2)


**אֲצִילֵי.** אֵיישִׁיל״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**armpits** Heb. אַצִילֵי, aisselles in French.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:3)


**אַצִּילֵי יָדַי.** כְּמוֹ ׳אַצִּילֵי יָדַיִם׳, כְּמוֹ ״תַּלְמֵי שָׂדָי״ (הושע י:ד):


**armpits** Heb. אַצִילֵי יָדַי, like אַצִילֵי יָדַיִם, as in (Hosea 12:12): “the furrows of the fields (שָׂדָי).”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:4)


**וְעוֹשׂוֹת הַמִּסְפָּחוֹת.** עַל רָאשֵׁי הָאֲנָשִׁים, אַף הֵם לִקְסֹם בָּהֶם; וְלֹא יָדַעְתִּי מַה הֵם, וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר **וְקָרַעְתִּי אֹתָם** אוֹמֵר אֲנִי שֶׁהֵן שֶׁל בֶּגֶד; וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגְּמוֹ: ״פַּת כּוּמְרִים״, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם ״בָּמוֹת טְלֻאוֹת״ (יחזקאל טז:טז): ״מְחַפְיָין פַּת כּוּמְרִים״:


**and who make the veils** on the heads of people. They, too, are made to divine with, but I do not know what they are. Since it says (verse 21), “And I shall tear them,” I say that they are of cloth, and Jonathan renders: פַּת כּוּמָרִין, embroidered cloths over the heads of idolatrous statues, and so he renders (16:16): “mounds of striped clothing” בָּמוֹת טְלֻאוֹת covered with פַּת כּוּמָרִין “cloths of idolatrous statues.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:5)


**הַמִּסְפָּחוֹת.** כְּמוֹ ״סְפָחֵנִי נָא״ (שמואל א ב:לו), לִישָׁאגְמַנְ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, וְיֵשׁ לוֹעֲזִים פְּלַטְרַאיִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the veils** Heb. הָמִּסְפָּחוֹת, like (I Sam. 2: 36): “Allow me to join (סָפְחֵנִי).” Faltres in Old French, veils, wraps, coverings.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:6)


**לְצוֹדֵד נְפָשׁוֹת.** בָּהֶן הֵן צָדוֹת הַנְּפָשׁוֹת לְגֵיהִנֹּם, שֶׁמַּחֲזִיקוֹת יְדֵי הָרְשָׁעִים (יחזקאל יג:כב) לִקְסֹם לָהֶם שָׁלוֹם, וּמְרַפּוֹת יְדֵי צַדִּיקִים לִקְסֹם לָהֶם רָעָה:


**to ensnare souls** With them they ensnare souls for Gehinnom, for they encourage the wicked by divining favorably for them, and they discourage the righteous by divining unfavorably for them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:7)


**הַנְּפָשׁוֹת תְּצוֹדֵדְנָה וגו׳.** תְּמִיהָּ הִיא:


**Will you ensnare the souls, etc.** This is a rhetorical question.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:18:8)


**וּנְפָשׁוֹת לָכֶנָה תְחַיֶּינָה.** לָכֶנָה – כְּמוֹ ׳לָכֶן׳, שֶׁאַתֶּנָה נוֹטְלוֹת שָׂכָר עַל כָּךְ וּמִתְפַּרְנְסוֹת בְּכָךְ:


**and sustain your own souls** Heb. לָכֶנָה, like לָכֶן, for you take reward for this and thereby earn a livelihood.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:19:1)


**בְּשַׁעֲלֵי שְׂעוֹרִים.** כְּמוֹ ״מִי מָדַד בְּשָׁעֳלוֹ מַיִם״ (ישעיהו מ:יב), אוֹנְגְיָאלוֹנִיאָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן מִדָּה:


**for fistfuls of barley** Heb. בְּשֳּׁעֲלֵי, like (Isa. 40:12): “Who measured water with his fist (בְּשָּׁעֳלוֹ)?” Jaloynes in Old French, double-handfuls. Another explanation: an expression of a measure.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:19:2)


**וּבִפְתוֹתֵי לֶחֶם.** אִיאַנְפִּיצָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, שֶׁאַתֶּן נוֹטְלוֹת בִּשְׂכַרְכֶן:


**and for morsels of bread** E an piyezes in Old French, and with morsels, that you take as your fee.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:19:3)


**לְהָמִית נְפָשׁוֹת אֲשֶׁר לֹא תְמוּתֶנָה.** כְּלוֹמַר, לֵאמֹר עַל נְפָשׁוֹת אֲשֶׁר לֹא תְמוּתֶנָה: אֵלּוּ יָמוּתוּ, וְלֵאמַר עַל אֲשֶׁר לֹא תִחְיֶינָה: יִחְיוּ; כִּי קְסָמֵיכֶם שֶׁקֶר, וְעַמִּי מְקַבְּלִים אֶת קִסְמֵי כָזָב שֶׁלָּכֶם:


**to slay souls that will not die** i.e., to say about souls that will not die, “These will die,” and to say about souls that will not live, “These will live,” for your divinations are false, and My people accept your false divinations.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:20:1)


**לְפֹרְחוֹת.** לִהְיוֹת פּוֹרְחוֹת עַל יְדֵיכֶן לְגֵיהִנֹּם:


**to fly away** To fly to Gehinnom because of you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:20:2)


**וְשִׁלַּחְתִּי אֶת הַנְּפָשׁוֹת.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״וַאֲשֵׁזִיב יָת נַפְשָׁתָא דְּאַתּוּן מְחַרְשָׁן יָת נַפְשֵׁיהֶן לַאֲבָדוּתָהּ״:


**and I shall free the people whose souls** As the Targum renders: and I shall save the people upon whose souls you cast a spell to destroy them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:20:3)


**אֶת נְפָשִׁים.** אֶת אֲנָשִׁים, לִהְיוֹת הַנְּפָשׁוֹת פּוֹרְחוֹת:


**whose souls** The people [whose souls you ensure] to fly away.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 13:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/13:22:1)


**יַעַן הַכְאוֹת לֵב צַדִּיק שֶׁקֶר.** כְּשֶׁאַתֶּן קוֹסְמוֹת לוֹ רָעָה, הוּא מִצְטַעֵר וְלִבּוֹ נִכְאֶה:


**Because you break the heart of the righteous one with falsehood** When you divine unfavorably for him, and he is anguished, and his heart is broken.



## Chapter 14


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:1:1)


**וַיָּבוֹא אֵלַי אֲנָשִׁים.** כֻּלָּם בְּלֵב אֶחָד לְרָעָה, לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר **וַיָּבוֹא**; וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה אוֹמֵר, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא גִּלָּה אֹזֶן הַנָּבִיא תְּחִלָּה: הִנֵּה יָבוֹאוּ אֵלֶיךָ פְּלוֹנִי וּפְלוֹנִי:


**Now there came to me some men** Heb. וַיָבוֹא. All of them with one accord for evil. Therefore, it is said: וַיָבוֹא, and came [the singular form]. The Midrash Aggadah says that [the word is in the singular] because the Holy One, blessed be He, revealed to the ear of the prophet beforehand, “Behold so-and- so and so-and-so will come to you.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:3:1)


**הֶעֱלוּ גִלּוּלֵיהֶם עַל לִבָּם.** לִבָּם אֶל גִּלּוּלֵיהֶם לַעֲבֹד:


**have set their idols upon their heart** Their heart is toward their idols to worship [them].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:3:2)


**נָתְנוּ נֹכַח פְּנֵיהֶם.** לִהְיוֹת לָהֶם עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה תָּמִיד לְמוֹרָא וְלֵאלוֹהַּ:


**opposite their faces** for the idol to always be for them an object of fear and a deity.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:3:3)


**הַאִדָּרֵשׁ אִדָּרֵשׁ.** הֲתֵרָאֶה כִּי אֲנִי אִדָּרֵשׁ **לָהֶם** וְאָשִׁיב עַל דִּבְרֵיהֶם?! הַרְבֵּה תְּמִיהוֹת מִתְקַיְּמוֹת:


**Shall I respond to their inquiry?** Is it proper that I should respond to them and answer their words? Many rhetorical questions anticipate an affirmative reply.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:4:1)


**נַעֲנֵיתִי לוֹ.** שויי״ש רפונדו״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז. אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא בָּא לְפָנַי **בְּרֹב גִּלּוּלָיו**. כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**shall respond to him** Heb. נַעֲנֵיתִי, suys reponduz in Old French, I was answered, although he comes before me with his many idols. So did Jonathan render it.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:5:1)


**לְמַעַן תְּפֹשׂ אֶת בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ׳.** ״בְּדִיל לְקָרְבָא יָת בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִתַּן תְּיוּבְתָּא בְּלִבְּהוֹן״:


**In order to take hold of the house of Israel, etc.** In order to draw the house of Israel near, to put repentance into their hearts.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:5:2)


**נָזוֹרוּ.** כְּמוֹ ״נְזִיר אֶחָיו״ (בראשית מט:כו), פורינ״ט אשיברי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**who have drawn away** Heb. נָזֹרוּ, like (Gen. 48:26): “the one separated (נְזִיר) from his brothers” furent asevrez in Old French; were severed, separated.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:6:1)


**שׁוּבוּ.** מִדַּרְכֵיכֶם:


**Repent** of your ways.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:6:2)


**וְהָשִׁיבוּ.** אֶת לְבַבְכֶם (דברים ל:א):


**and move away** Heb. וְהָשִּׁיבוּ, [remove] your hearts [as well].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:6:3)


**מֵעַל גִּלּוּלֵיכֶם.** אֲשֶׁר הֶעֱלִיתֶם אֶל לְבַבְכֶם (יחזקאל יד:ג):


**from your idols** which you brought up to your heart.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:6:4)


**הָשִׁיבוּ פְנֵיכֶם.** אֲשֶׁר שַׂמְתֶּם מִכְשׁוֹל עֲוֹנְכֶם לְנוֹכַחְכֶם:


**remove your faces** opposite which you set the stumbling block of your iniquities.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:7:1)


**נַעֲנֶה לוֹ בִּי.** ״מִשְׁתָּאֵיל לֵיהּ בְּמֵימְרִי״ (תרגום יונתן), אֶדְרֹשׁ לוֹ:


**I...shall respond to him by Myself** Shall be inquired of him about Myself. I shall be inquired of to him.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:8:1)


**וְנָתַתִּי פָנַי בָּאִישׁ הַהוּא.** אַחֲרֵי כֵן, אִם לֹא יִשְׁמַע אֶל דִּבְרֵי הַנָּבִיא:


**And I shall direct My anger against that man** afterward, if he does not obey the words of the prophet.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:8:2)


**פָּנַי.** מִישָׁאִירָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז (כַּעֲסִי):


**My anger** Heb. פָּנַי, mes iras, ires in Old French, my anger (as in Psalms 34:17).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:8:3)


**וְהַשִׁמּוֹתִיהוּ.** תַּרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן ״וַאֲשַׁצְיִנֵיהּ״:


**and I shall make him desolate** Jonathan rendered: and I shall make him [a sign].



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:9:1)


**וְהַנָּבִיא כִּי יְפֻתֶּה וְדִבֶּר דָּבָר.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֱמֶת:


**But the prophet who is enticed and speaks a word** that is not true.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:9:2)


**פִּתֵּיתִי.** פָּתַחְתִּי לוֹ פֶּתַח לְאֵיזוֹ שֶׁיִּרְצֶה; וּמִכָּאן יֵשׁ לוֹמַר: בָּא לִטָּמֵא, פּוֹתְחִין לוֹ (שבת קד.):


**have enticed** I opened a doorway for him to [do] whatever he wishes. And from here we can learn that if one wishes to defile himself, they open [a door] for him.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:10:1)


**כַּעֲוֹן הַדֹּרֵשׁ כַּעֲוֹן הַנָּבִיא.** ״כְּחוֹב דְּאָתֵי לְמֵילַף וְלָא אֲלִיף כְּחוֹב נְבִיֵּי שִׁקְרָא יְהִי״ (תרגום יונתן):


**Like the iniquity of the inquirer, so will be the iniquity of the prophet** Like the iniquity of the one who comes to learn and does not learn, so will be the iniquity of the false prophets.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:13:1)


**מַטֵּה לָחֶם.** מִשְׁעַן לֶחֶם (ישעיה ג:א):


**staff of bread** Support [provided by] bread.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:14:1)


**נֹחַ דָּנִיֵּאל וְאִיּוֹב.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵלּוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה רָאוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה עוֹלָמוֹת: נֹחַ רָאָה הָעוֹלָם בָּנוּי וְחָרֵב וּבָנוּי (בראשית ו-ט), וְדָנִיֵּאל אֶת בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ (דניאל א:א-ו), אוֹ אֶת עַצְמוֹ, תְּחִלָּה שַׂר עַל כָּל הַשָּׂרִים (דניאל ב:מח) סוֹף הוּשְׁלַךְ לְגוֹב אֲרָיוֹת וְחָזַר לִגְדֻלָּתוֹ (דניאל ו), וְכֵן אִיּוֹב רָאָה אֶת עַצְמוֹ מְיֻשָּׁב וְחָרֵב (איוב א-ב) וּמְיֻשָּׁב (איוב מב:י-יז; תנחומא נח ה), לְפִיכָךְ הֵבִיאוּ דֻגְמָא לְדוֹרוֹ שֶׁל יְכָנְיָה, שֶׁרָאוּ אֶת הַבַּיִת בְּבִנְיָנוֹ וְחוּרְבָּנוֹ וּבִנְיַן שֵׁנִי (עזרא ג:יב); וְכֵן אָמַר לָהֶם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: חִבָּה יְתֵרָה אֲנִי מַרְאֶה לָכֶם, אַתֶּם יְכָנְיָה וְגָלוּתוֹ: אִלּוּ תֶּחֱטָא לִי אַחַת מֵאַרְצוֹת הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת וְגָזַרְתִּי עֲלֵיהֶם אַחַת מֵאַרְבַּעַת שְׁפָטִים אֵלֶּה, אוֹ רָעָב אוֹ חַיָּה רָעָה אוֹ חֶרֶב אוֹ דֶּבֶר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּסֻדָּרִין בְּפָרָשָׁה זוֹ זֶה אַחַר זֶה, וּשְׁלֹשֶׁת הַצַּדִּיקִים אֵלּוּ בְּתוֹכָהּ, לֹא יַצִּילוּ לֹא בֵּן וְלֹא בַת; וַאֲנִי אָבִיא אַרְבַּעְתָּן עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם וְאַשְׁאִיר לָכֶם מִבְּנֵיכֶם אֲשֶׁר שָׁם, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינְכֶם בְּתוֹכָם; וְלֹא שֶׁהֵם רְאוּיִים לְהַצָּלָה, אֶלָּא לְנַחֵם אֶתְכֶם, כְּשֶׁיִּגְלוּ אֶצְלְכֶם וְתִרְאוּ מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם הָרָעִים, וְתִתְנַחֲמוּ עַל הָרָעָה אֲשֶׁר הֵבֵאתִי עֲלֵיהֶם, כִּי תִרְאוּ שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ כְּדַי לְסוֹבְלָם עוֹד. הֲרֵי זֶה מַהֲלַךְ סֵדֶר פָּרָשָׁה זוֹ כֻּלָּהּ. לָשׁוֹן זֶה אֵין לְיַשְּׁבוֹ וְנִרְאֶה שֶׁאֵינוֹ לְשׁוֹן הָרַב, וְעוֹד נִרְאֶה שֶׁבְּסֵפֶר זֶה חָסֵר אַחַת מֵאַרְבַּע שְׁפָטִים מֵאָז וְעַד אָז, סֵפֶר אַחֵר מֵאוֹ וְעַד אוֹ. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **אֶרֶץ כִּי תֶחֱטָא לִי וְגוֹ׳ וְהָיוּ שְׁלֹשֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁים הָאֵלֶּה בְּתוֹכָהּ נֹחַ דָּנִיֵּאל וְאִיּוֹב** לְפִי שֶׁשְּׁלֹשֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁים הָאֵלֶּה הִצִּילָם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מִשְּׁלֹשֶׁת (מארבעת) שְׁפָטִים הָאֵלֶּה, הִזְכִּיר הַכָּתוּב אֶת אֵלּוּ. נֹחַ נִמְלַט מִשְּׁלָשְׁתָּן: מֵחַיָּה רָעָה מִנַּיִן? אַתָּה אוֹמֵר: בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁנִּכְנַס לַתֵּיבָה בִּקְּשׁוּ כָּל הַחַיּוֹת לִיכָּנֵס וְלֹא הִנִּיחָן הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, דִּכְתִיב ״וַיִּסְגֹּר ה׳ בַּעֲדוֹ״ (בראשית ז:טז), אֵין ״וַיִּסְגֹּר״ אֶלָּא מֵחַיּוֹת, כְּמָא דְאַתְּ אָמַר ״וּסְגַר פֻּם אַרְיָוָתָא וְלָא חַבְּלוּנִי״ (דניאל ו:כג); אֲרִי בָּא לִיכָּנֵס וְהָיוּ שִׁנָּיו כֵּהוֹת, דֹּב בָּא לִיכָּנֵס וְהָיוּ רַגְלָיו מִתְעַרְסְלוֹת וְכוּ׳ (בראשית רבה לא:יח). מִן הָרָעָב מִנַּיִן? שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מִן הָאֲדָמָה אֲשֶׁר אֵרְרָהּ״ (בראשית ה:כט), צֵא וּלְמַד בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה, בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁחוֹשֵׁב עֲשָׂרָה רְעָבוֹן שֶׁיָּרְדוּ לָעוֹלָם (בראשית רבה כה:ג), וְתִמְצָא שֶׁבִּימֵי נֹחַ הָיָה אֶחָד מֵהֶם, וּמֵבִיא רְאָיָה ״מִן הָאֲדָמָה אֲשֶׁר אֵרְרָהּ״. מִן הַחֶרֶב – זֶה הַמַּבּוּל, וְעוֹד: שֶׁהָיוּ דּוֹרוֹ מְבַקְּשִׁין לִזְדַּוֵּג לוֹ, וְהָיוּ אוֹמְרִים: אֵין אָנוּ מַנִּיחִין אוֹתוֹ לִיכָּנֵס, אֶלָּא שֶׁהִצִּילוֹ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא; אָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: אֵין אֲנִי מַנִּיחוֹ לִיכָּנֵס אֶלָּא בַּחֲצִי הַיּוֹם, וְדַרְגִּישׁ לֵיהּ יְמַלֵּל (בראשית רבה לב:ח); הֲדָא הוּא דִּכְתִיב ״בְּעֶצֶם הַיּוֹם הַזֶּה בָּא נֹחַ״ (בראשית ז:יג), בְּעִצּוּמוֹ שֶׁל יוֹם. דָּנִיֵּאל: בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁנִּכְנַס נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר לַהֵיכָל בִּימֵי יְהוֹיָקִים, וְהָרַג יְהוֹיָקִים וְהִנִּיחַ לְדָנִיֵּאל, וְהִגְלָה לְדָנִיֵּאל הַגְלָת שְׁלוֹמִים (ירמיהו יג:יט), לְפִי שֶׁמְּצָאוֹ מָלֵא רוּחַ חָכְמָה. מִן הָרָעָב – שֶׁהֲרֵי עַד שֶׁלֹּא נִכְבְּשָׁה יְרוּשָׁלַיִם בִּימֵי יְהוֹיָקִים צָר עָלֶיהָ נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר שָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיְהִי הָרָעָב גָּדוֹל בָּעִיר״ (מלכים ב ו:כה) וְגַם ״וַיֶּחֱזַק הָרָעָב״ (ירמיהו נב:ו). מִן הַחַיּוֹת מִנַּיִן? ״וּסְגַר פֻּם אַרְיָוָתָא וְלָא חַבְּלוּנִי״ (דניאל ו:כג). וְכֵן אַתָּה מוֹצֵא אִיּוֹב שֶׁנִּמְלַט מִשְּׁלָשְׁתָּן: מִן הָרָעָב מִנַּיִן? לְדִבְרֵי הָאוֹמֵר אִיּוֹב בִּימֵי יַעֲקֹב הָיָה (בבא בתרא טו:ב), מָצִינוּ שֶׁהָיָה רָעָב בְּיָמָיו (בראשית מא:נד). מִן הַחַיָּה מִנַּיִן? דִּכְתִיב ״וּמִקְנֵהוּ פָּרַץ בָּאָרֶץ״ (איוב א:י), פָּרַץ גִּדְרוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם (בבא בתרא טו:ב). מִן הַחֶרֶב מִנַּיִן? ״יְפַלַּח כִּלְיוֹתַי וְלֹא יַחְמוֹל״ (איוב טז:יג):


**Noah, Daniel, and Job** Because each of these three saw three worlds: Noah saw the world built up, destroyed, and rebuilt; and Danielthe Temple. Or himself: first a prince over all the princes, eventually cast into a lions’ den, and [ultimately] restored to his greatness. Likewise, Job saw himself well-established, destroyed, and [again] well-established. Therefore, they were brought as a parallel to the generation of Jeconiah, which saw the Temple while it was built and while it was destroyed, and the [Temple’s] second building. And the Holy One, blessed be He, said the following to them: “I am showing you great affectionyou, Jeconiah and [those of] his exile. Should one of the lands of the nations sin against Me and had I decreed upon it one of these four punishmentseither “famine” (verse 13), “wild beast” (verse 15), “the sword” (verse 17), “or pestilence” (verse 19) as they appear in this chapter, consecutively, and these three righteous men were in its midst, they would save neither son nor daughter. Yet I will bring [all] four of them on [your brethren in] Jerusalem, and [still] leave over for you some of your sons who are there although you are not in its midst. And not because they are worthy of being saved, but to console you when they are exiled [to an area] near you and you will see their evil deeds and be consoled about the evil that I brought upon them. For you will see that it could no longer be tolerated. [The annotator of our ms. remarks:] (This is the theme of this entire chapter. This [following] explanation cannot be made fit, and it appears that it is not the wording of the rabbi [viz. Rashi]. It appears further that this edition [of Rashi in which this comment was found] is missing one of the four punishments, from אוֹ, or, until כִּי or.) Another explanation: **If a land sins against Me, etc.** and had these three men been in its midst [namely] Noah, Daniel, and Job because the Holy One, blessed be He, saved these three men from these three [four] judgments, Scripture mentioned these. Noah was saved from the three of them. How do we know [that he was saved] from wild beasts? When he entered the ark, all the beasts attempted to enter, but the Holy One, blessed be He, did not allow them, as it is written (Gen. 7:16): “and the Lord shut around him.” Now the word “shut” refers only to protection from beasts, as Scripture states (Dan. 6:23): “and he shut the lions’ mouths, and they did not hurt me.”A lion came to enter [the ark] and its teeth were set on edge; a bear came to enter, and its feet would totter, etc. (see Gen. Rabbah 31:12). How do we know [that he was saved] from famine?For it is said (Gen. 5:29): “from the soil, which the Lord has cursed.” Go out and learn Genesis Rabbah (25:3) where it enumerates the ten famines that descended upon the world, and you will find that one of them was in the time of Noah, and it brings proof from “from the soil, which... [He] cursed.” From the swordthis is the Flood, and moreover, the people of his generation sought to attack him, saying, “We will not allow him to enter,” but the Holy One, blessed be He, saved him: said the Holy One, blessed be He, “I will not allow him to enter except at midday [in broad daylight], and whomever it hurtslet him protest.” That is what is written (Gen. 7:13): “In this very (עֶצֶם) day Noah came [into the ark]” at the strong point (עֱצוּמוֹ) of the day. Danielwhen Nebuchadnezzar entered the Temple in the days of Jehoiakim, he slew Jehoiakim and spared Daniel. He expelled Daniel [into] a peaceful exile because he found him to be full of a spirit of wisdom. From faminefor until Jerusalem was conquered in the days of Zedekiah, Nebuchadnezzar besieged it for three years [during which it suffered a famine], as is said (II Kings 25:3): “the famine became severe in the city,” and also (in Jer. 52:6): “and the famine became severe.” How do we know [that he was saved] from beasts?”and shut the lions’ mouths (Dan. ibid.), and they did not hurt me.” Likewise, you find that Job was saved from the three of them. How do we know [that he was saved] from famine?According to the one who says (Baba Bathra 15b) that Job lived in the time of Jacob, we find that there was a famine in his days. How do we know [that he was protected] from beasts?For it is written (Job 1:10): “and his cattle has broken out into the land.” It has breached the [natural] limitations of the world. [Usually wolves kill goats, but Job’s goats killed wolves; B. B. 15b.] How do we know [that he survived] the sword?(ibid. 16:13) “He splits my kidneys and does not have compassion.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:14:2)


**וְהָיוּ שְׁלֹשֶׁת הָאֲנָשִׁים הָאֵלֶּה בְּתוֹכָהּ נֹחַ דָּנִיֵּאל וְאִיּוֹב הֵמָּה בְצִדְקָתָם יְנַצְּלוּ נַפְשָׁם.** מִפְּנֵי מָה לֹא הוּזְכְּרוּ בְּמִיתַת הָרָעָב בָּנִים וּבָנוֹת, שֶׁלֹּא נֶאֱמַר בָּהֶם אִם בָּנִים אִם בָּנוֹת יַצִּילוּ כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּזְכְּרוּ בִּשְׁנֵי שְׁפָטִים הָאַחֲרוֹנִים? דּוּגְמָא לְדָנִיֵּאל, שֶׁהוּא אֶחָד מֵהֶם, וְהוּא הָיָה מִדּוֹרוֹ שֶׁל יְכָנְיָה (דניאל א:א,ו; מלכים ב כד:א,ו), וְלֹא נִמְלְטוּ עִמּוֹ לֹא בָנִים וְלֹא בָנוֹת, וְעָלָיו נִתְנַבֵּא יְשַׁעְיָה ״כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ לַסָּרִיסִים אֲשֶׁר יִשְׁמְרוּ אֶת שַׁבְּתוֹתַי״ (ישעיה נו:ד; סנהדרין צג:):


**Now should these three men be in its midst[namely] Noah, Daniel, and Job, they would save themselves with their righteousness.** Why are sons and daughters not mentioned [in verse 14] in regard to the death by faminei.e., it is not stated about them, “[as I lived] if they will save sons or daughters!”as they are mentioned in the last two punishments (verses 16, 18)? This is to depict Daniel, for he was one of those three saved from famine, he was of the generation of Jeconiah, and neither sons nor daughters were rescued with him. Concerning him Isaiah prophesied (56:4): “[For] so says the Lord to the to the eunuchs who will keep My Sabbaths.”

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 14:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/14:21:1)


**אַף כִּי.** לְשׁוֹן קַל וָחֹמֶר וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן הוּא, וְכֵן בְּכָל הַמִּקְרָא, כְּגוֹן: ״הִנֵּה אֲנַחְנוּ פֹה בִיהוּדָה יְרֵאִים וְאַף כִּי נֵלֵךְ קְעִילָה״ (שמואל א כג:ג), וְכֵן בְּפָרָשָׁה זוֹ ״הִנֵּה בִּהְיוֹתוֹ תָמִים לֹא יֵעָשֶׂה לִמְלָאכָה אַף כִּי אֵשׁ אֲכָלַתְהוּ וְכוּ״ (יחזקאל טו:ה) (בראשית רבה צב:ז):


**How much more** Heb. אֲף כִּי, an expression of an a fortiori and ‘how much more so.’ It is the same throughout Scripture, like (I Sam. 23:3): “Behold we are afraid here in Judah; how much more so (אֲף כִּי) if we go to Keilah...?” Likewise, in this chapter (15:5): “Behold when it was whole, it could not be used for work; how much less (אֲף כִּי) when fire has consumed it, etc.?”

**שְׁפָטַי.** מִישְׁגוּשְׁטִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**My judgments** Mes justises in Old French, my punishments.

## Chapter 15


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 15:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:2:1)


**מַה יִּהְיֶה עֵץ הַגֶּפֶן מִכָּל עֵץ.** מַה סּוֹפוֹ מִכָּל שְׁאָר עֵצִים?


**what will become of the vine tree out of all [other] trees** How will its end be different from that of all other trees?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 15:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:2:2)


**הַזְּמוֹרָה אֲשֶׁר הָיָה בַּעֲצֵי הַיָּעַר.** לֹא עַל גֶּפֶן הַכְּרָמִים שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה פְּרִי אֲנִי אוֹמֵר לְךָ, אֶלָּא עַל זְמוֹרַת הַגֶּפֶן הַגְּדֵלָה בַּיְּעָרִים:


**the branch that was among the trees of the forest** Not about the vine of the vineyards, which produces fruit, am I asking you, but about the branch of a vine that grows in the forest.

### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 15:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:4:1)


**הִנֵּה לָאֵשׁ נִתַּן לְאָכְלָה.** אַרְבוֹרִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז. הִנֵּה שְׁאַלְתִּיךָ עַל הַתָּמִים, וְאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לִכְלוּם, עַתָּה עוֹד אֲנִי מוֹסִיף עַל הַשְּׁאֵלָה: הִנֵּה אִם נִתְּנוּ זְמוֹרָה מִמֶּנּוּ בָּאֵשׁ וְאָכְלָה הָאֵשׁ בִּשְׁנֵי קְצוֹת הֵיקִיפָה עַד כִּי **נָחַר תּוֹכוֹ**:


**Behold, [if] it were given to the fire to be consumed** Adevorer in Old French, to be devoured, consumed. Behold I asked you about a whole one, which is not fit for anything. Now I shall add more to the question: Behold if they put a branch of it into the fire, and the fire consumed it at its two ends of its outer surface until its inside became charred...


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 15:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:4:2)


**נָחַר.** נִשְׁאַר מִן הָאֵשׁ, בְּרוּאִיר בְּלַעַ״ז; **נָחַר** לְשׁוֹן ״וְעַצְמִי חָרָה מִנִּי חֹרֶב״ (איוב ל:ל). וְיֵשׁ לוֹעֲזִין אֶשָׁארְט בְּלַעַ״ז; וְכֵן ״וַיִּחַר עָלַי אַפּוֹ״ (איוב יט:יא) חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם עִם ״עַצְמִי חָרָה״, **הֲיִצְלַח** עוֹד לִמְלָאכָה?


**were charred** Heb. נָחָר, blackened from the fire, brouir in French, to blacken, to scorch. נָחָר derives from the same form found in (Job 30:30): “and my bones dried out (חָרָה) from the heat.” Some translate this as e(s)sart in Old French, calcined, burnt to ashes, as [its sense] in Hosea (8:5) and also in (Job 19:11): “He has kindled (וַיַחַר) His wrath against me.” Menachem (p. 93) [however] related it to “my bones dried out.” **is it fit** [Is it] still fit for work?



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 15:5:1

**אַף כִּי.** לְשׁוֹן קַל וָחֹמֶר וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן הוּא, וְכֵן בְּכָל הַמִּקְרָא, כְּגוֹן הִנֵּה אֲנַחְנוּ פֹה בִּיהוּדָה יְרֵאִים **וְאַף כִּי** נֵלֵךְ קְעִילָה (שמואל א כג:ג):



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 15:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:6:1)


**כֵּן נָתַתִּי.** אֶת **יְרוּשָׁלִַם** שְׁתֵּי קְצוֹתֶיהָ אָכְלָה הָאֵשׁ, שְׁתֵּי גָלִיּוֹת: כְּשֶׁגָּלָה יְהוֹיָקִים גָּלוּ עִמּוֹ מִזֶּרַע הַמְּלוּכָה וּמִן הַפַּרְתְּמִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּדָנִיֵּאל (דניאל א:ג). אַחֲרָיו גָּלָה יְכָנְיָה בְּנוֹ וְהֶחָרָשׁ וְהַמַּסְגֵּר (מלכים ב כד:יד-טו). **וְתוֹכוֹ נָחָר**: צִדְקִיָּהוּ הַנּוֹתָר בָּהּ, כְּבָר גְּזַרְתִּי עָלָיו ״מְלֹא חָפְנֶיךָ גַּחֲלֵי אֵשׁ וּזְרֹק עַל הָעִיר״ (לעיל י:ב):


**have I placed** Jerusalem. Its two ends were consumed by the fire, [i.e.,] the two exiles: When Jehoiakim was exiled, some of the royal seed and of the nobles were exile with him, as it is said in Daniel (1:3). After him were exiled his son Jeconiah and the craftsmen and the gate sentries. “And its inside was charred” [based on above, verse 4] Concerning Zedekiah, the one who remained therein, I have already decreed (10:2): “and fill your hands with fiery coals...and cast [them] onto the city.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 15:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/15:7:1)


**מֵהָאֵשׁ יָצָאוּ.** ״עַל פִּתְגָּמֵי אוֹרַיְתָא דִּיהִיבִין מִגּוֹ אֶשְׁתָּא עֲבָרוּ, וְעַמְמַיָּא דְּתַקִּיפִין כְּאִישְׁתָּא יְשֵׁיצִינוּן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**From the fire they have emerged** They transgressed the words of the Torah, which were given from fire, and nations as strong as fire will destroy them.



## Chapter 16


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:2:1)


**הוֹדַע.** פֵּיישְׁבוֹיְי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**let...know** Heb. הוֹדַע, fais savoir in French, inform.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:3:1)


**מְכוֹרוֹתַיִךְ.** כְּמוֹ מְגוּרוֹתַיִךְ. וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן ׳תּוֹלָדָה׳, וְכֵן פֵּירֵשׁ ״כְּלֵי חָמָס מְכֵרֹתֵיהֶם״ (בראשית מט:ה); ״אִישׁ מֵאֵת מַכָּרוֹ״ (מלכים ב יב:ו); ״לְבַד מִמְּכָרָיו״ (דברים יח:ח) – כֻּלָּם לְשׁוֹן ׳תּוֹלָדוֹת׳:


**your dwelling place** Heb. מְכֹרֹתַיִך, like מְגוּרוֹתַיִךְ, your dwelling place [interchanging כ and ג], and Menachem (p. 110) connected the word to the idea of birth. He similarly explained (Gen. 49: 5): “weapons of violence are their birth (מְכֵרוֹתֵיהֶם)” (Deut. 18:8); “besides what he has from his birth (מִמְכָּרָיו)” (II Kings 12:6); “each one from his kinsman (מַכָּרוֹ).” They all mean birth.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:3:2)


**אָבִיךְ וְאִמֵּךְ.** אַבְרָהָם וְשָׂרָה (סנהדרין מד:), מִשָּׁם נָטְלוּ גְדֻלָּתָם, וּבְנֵי חֵת נָתְנוּ לָהּ קֶבֶר (בראשית כג:כ):


**your father, etc., and your mother** Abraham and Sarah took their greatness from there, and the children of Heth gave her a grave.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:4:1)


**בְּיוֹם הוּלֶּדֶת אֹתָךְ.** פושניא״ה בְּלַעַ״ז; כְּשֶׁבָּחַרְתִּי בָּךְ בְּמִצְרַיִם, הָיִית בְּלִי שׁוּם תִּקּוּן; וּלְפִי שֶׁדִּמָּה הַדָּבָר לְלֵידָה, הִזְכִּיר כָּאן תִּקּוּנֵי הַוָּלָד:


**on the day you were born** Heb. הוּלֶדֶת, fus nee in French. When I chose you in Egypt, you were without any preparation, and because he compares the matter to birth, he mentions here the preparations of the newborn.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:4:2)


**שָׁרֵךְ.** כְּמוֹ ״שָׁרְרֵךְ״ (שיר השירים ז:ג):


**your navel** Heb. שָּׁרֵך, like שָּׁרְרֵך.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:4:3)


**לְמִשְׁעִי.** לְצִחְצוּחַ, ״צְחִיחַ הַסֶּלַע״ (יחזקאל כו:ד) תַּרְגּוּמוֹ ״שְׁעִיעוּת כֵּיפָא״, אפלצמנ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז. וּמְנַחֵם חִבֵּר לְמִשְׁעִי לְשׁוֹן שַׁעֲשׁוּעִים:


**for cleansing** Heb. לְמִשְּׁעִי, to make smooth, [as] (below 24:7),”the smoothness of the rock (צְחִיחַ סֶלַע)” is translated שְּׁעִיעוּתכֵּיפָא, apla[ni] ment in Old French, clearing, smoothing. Menachem (p. 177) associates it to the word for dandling (שַּׁעַשּׁוּעַ).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:4:4)


**לֹא הֻמְלַחַתְּ.** מִכָּאן שֶׁמּוֹלְחִין אֶת הַוָּלָד כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְקַשֶּׁה בְּשָׂרוֹ:


**nor were you salted** From here it is derived that we salt the newborn so that his flesh hardens.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:4:5)


**וְהַחְתֵּל.** לְשׁוֹן כְּרִיכָה בִּבְגָדִים, וְכֵן ״וַעֲרָפֶל חֲתֻלָּתוֹ״ (איוב לח:ט), הוּא שֶׁמְּלַפְּפִין אֶת הַוָּלָד:


**nor swaddled** Heb. וְהָחְתֵּל, a word denoting enwrapping with garments, and likewise (Job 38: 9): “and the dark cloud its wrapping (חֲתֻלָּתוֹ)” It is [the cloth with] which they swathe the infant.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:5:1)


**לְחֻמְלָה.** לָחוּס:


**to have mercy** Heb. לְחֻמְלָה, to pity.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:5:2)


**בְּגֹעַל.** אַנְבוֹרִישְׁמַנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**in the loathsomeness** Heb. בְּגֹעַל an [a]borissemant, in abhorrence, loathing.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:6:1)


**מִתְבּוֹסֶסֶת.** נִרְפֶּסֶת, כְּמוֹ ״יָבוּס קָמֵינוּ״ (תהלים מד:ו):


**downtrodden** Heb. מִתְבּוֹסֶסֶת, downtrodden, as (Ps. 60:14): “and He will trample (יָבוּס) our adversaries.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:6:2)


**בְּדָמַיִךְ חֲיִי.** אַף בְּנִוּוּלֵיךְ זֶה לֹא תָמוּתִי; וְשֶׁשִּׁנָּה לוֹמַר שְׁנֵי פְּעָמִים, לְפִי שֶׁבְּדַם פֶּסַח וְדַם מִילָה נִגְאֲלוּ (פרקי דרבי אליעזר כט). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **מִתְבּוֹסֶסֶת** – שוייליא״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**With your blood, live** Even with this loathsomeness of yours you shall not die. He repeats this a second time because they were redeemed with the blood of the Passover sacrifice and the blood of circumcision (Pirke d’Rabbi Eliezer, chapter 29). Another explanation of מִתְבּוֹסֶסת: souillee in French, soiled, sullied.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:7:1)


**רְבָבָה כְּצֶמַח.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל פָּרוּ וַיִּשְׁרְצוּ וַיִּרְבּוּ״ (שמות א:ז):


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:7:2)


**בַּעֲדִי.** לְשׁוֹן ״מִכְלוֹל״ (יחזקאל כג:יב); וּמְנַחֵם פֵּירֵשׁ **בַּעֲדִי עֲדָיִים** לְשׁוֹן ׳עַד׳, כְּמוֹ ״בִּטְחוּ בַה׳ עֲדֵי עַד״ (ישעיה כו:ד); אַנְגוּק״א אַשִׁיגְל״א בְּלַעַ״ז; אֲבָל דּוֹנָשׁ פָּתַר אוֹתוֹ לְשׁוֹן ״עֲדִי״ (יחזקאל טז:יא), וּמִשְׁקַל **עֲדִי עֲדָיִים** הוּא מִן עֵשֶׂר שֵׁמוֹת: אֲרִי – ״אֲרָיִים״ (מלכים א י:כ), צְבִי – ״צְבָיִים״ (שמואל ב ב:יח), גְּדִי – ״גְּדָיִים״ (שמואל א י:ג):


**with perfect beauty** Heb. בַּעֲדִי עֲדָיִם, [a form] expressing perfection. But Menachem (p. 131) explained בַּעֲדִי עֲדָיִם as related to the word עַד, eternity, as (Isa. 26:4): “Trust in the Lord forever (עֲדֵי עַד)”; enjusque a s(i)egle in Old French, to eternity [lit. for centuries]. Dunash (p. 83), however, interpreted it as a word for ornaments. The declension of עַדִי עֲדָיִם is one of the ten nouns: lion, lions (אֲרִי אֲרָיִם) ; deer [sing.], deer [pl.] (צְבִי צְבָיִם) ; kid, kids (גְדִי גְּדָיִם).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:7:3)


**נָכוֹנוּ.** פוֹרִינְ״ט אַפִייטִיאַ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**fashioned** Heb. נָכֹנוּ, furent efeties in Old French, were fashioned, developed.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:7:4)


**וּשְׂעָרֵךְ צִמֵּחַ.** סִימָנֵי נַעֲרוּת:


**and your hair grown** Signs of puberty.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:8:1)


**דּוֹדִים.** אַמִיטִיאָ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**love** Heb. דֹדִים, amities in French; friendship, love.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:1)


**וָאֲסוּכֵךְ.** לְשׁוֹן מְשִׁיחָה. וְכָל הָעִנְיָן אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן מָשָׁל, וְיוֹנָתָן פֵּירֵשׁ אֶת כֻּלָּהּ, וְאֵין לְהוֹסִיף עַל הַתַּרְגּוּם; וְכֵן פֵּירְשָׁהּ:


**and I anointed you** Heb. וָאֲסֻכֵך, a word expressing anointing. Now the entire episode is only a parable, and Jonathan explained it all, leaving nothing to add to the Targum, and he explained it in this manner:


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:2)


**מְכוּרֹתַיִךְ וּמוֹלַדְתַּיִךְ וְגוֹמֵר.** ״תּוֹתְבוּתְכוֹן וִילְדוּתְכוֹן מֵאַרְעָא כְּנַעֲנָאֵי, תַּמָּן אִתְגְּלִיתִי אֶל אַבְרָהָם אֲבוּכוֹן בֵּין בְּתָרַיָּא (בראשית טו:יז-יח), וְאוֹדַעְתֵּיהּ דְּאַתּוּן נָחֲתִין לְמִצְרַיִם (בראשית טו:יג), וּבִדְרַע מְרוֹמָם אֲנָא פָּרִיק יָתְכוֹן, וּבִזְכוּת אֲבָהַתְכוֹן אֲנָא מְתָרִיךְ יָת אֱמוֹרָאֵי וּמְשֵׁיצִי יָת חִתָּאֵי״:


**[3] Your dwelling place and your birthplace, etc.** Your dwelling place and your birthplace are from the land of the Canaanites; there I revealed Myself to your father Abraham [in the covenant] between the parts, and I let him know that you are going down to Egypt. With an arm raised on high, I redeemed you, and through the merit of your fathers, I drove out the Amorites and destroyed the Hittites.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:3)


**וּמוֹלְדוֹתַיִךְ בְּיוֹם הוּלֶּדֶת אוֹתָךְ וגו׳.** ״וְאַף כַּד נְחָתוּ אֲבָהָתְכוֹן לְמִצְרַיִם, תּוֹתְבִין בְּאַרְעָא דְּלָא דִילְהוֹן, מְשַׁעְבְּדִין וּמְעַנָּן לְהוֹן, הֲוַת כְּנִישְׁתָּא דְיִשְׂרָאֵל דְּמִיָא לְוַלְדָּא דְּרְטִישׁ עַל אַפֵּי בָּרָא, דְּלָא אִיתְפְּסִיק שׁוּרֵיהּ, וּבְמַיָּא לָא אִישְׁתְּטִיף לְאִיתְנַקָּהָא, וּבְמֶלַח לָא אִיתְמְלַח, וּבְאִיסּוּרִין לָא אִיתְאַסָּא״:


**[4] And as for your birth, on the day you were born, etc.** And also, when your forefathers went down to Egypt, sojourners in a land that was not theirs, they subjugated them and oppressed them. The assembly of Israel was like a newborn infant abandoned in the open field, whose navel was not cut, and who was not rinsed with water to be cleansed, and not salted with salt or swaddled with swaddling clothes.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:4)


**לֹא חָסָה עָלֶיךְ עַיִן וגו׳.** ״לָא חָסַת עֲלֵיכוֹן עֵינָא דְּפַרְעֹה רְשִׁיעָא לְמֶעְבַּד לְכוֹן טָבָא חֲדָא, לַאֲנָחָא לְכוֹן מִשִּׁעְבּוּדְכוֹן, לְרַחֲמָא עֲלֵיכוֹן, וּגְזַר עֲלֵיכוֹן גְּזֵירַת גְּמִירָא, לְמִרְמֵי יָת דְּכוּרֵיכוֹן בְּנַהֲרָא (שמות א:כב), לְאוֹבְדָא יָתְכוֹן, בְּעִידָּן דַּהֲוֵיתוֹן בְּמִצְרַיִם״:


**[5] No eye pitied you, etc.** The eye of the wicked Pharaoh did not pity you to do you any favor, to lighten your bondage, [or] to have pity on you, and he decreed upon you a decree of annihilation, to cast your males into the river to destroy you at the time you were in Egypt.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:5)


**וָאֶעֱבֹר עָלַיִךְ וגו׳.** ״וְעַל דּוּכְרָן קְיָם אֲבָהָתְכוֹן קֳדָמִי אִיתְגְּלִיתִי לְמִפְרְקְכוֹן, אֲרֵי גְּלֵי קֳדָמִי אֲרֵי אַתּוּן מְעַנָּן בְּשִׁיעְבּוּדִיכוֹן, וַאֲמָרִית לְכוֹן: בְּדָמָא דִּמְהוּלְתָא אָחוּס עֲלֵיכוֹן, וַאֲמָרִית לְכוֹן: בְּדַם פִּסְחָא אֶפְרוֹק יָתְכוֹן״:


**[6] And I passed by you, etc.** And the remembrance of the covenant of your forefathers came before Me. I revealed Myself to redeem you because it was known to Me that you were being oppressed in your bondage, and I said to you, “With the blood of the circumcision I shall spare you,” and I said, “With the blood of the Passover, I shall redeem you.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:6)


**רִבְבָה כְּצֶמַח הַשָּׂדֶה נְתַתִּיךְ.** ״רִיבְּבַת כְּצִמְחֵי חַקְלָא יְהַבְתִּיכוֹן, וּסְגִיתוֹן וְתַקִּיפְתּוֹן וַהֲוִיתוֹן לְזַרְעִין וּלְשִׁבְטִין, וּבְעוֹבְדֵי אֲבָהָתְכוֹן תַּקְנִיָּא, עִידָן פּוּרְקָן כְּנִישַׁתְכוֹן מְטָא, אֲרֵי אַתּוֹן מְשַׁעְבְּדִין וּמְעַנָּן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**[7] Myriads, like the plants of the field I have made you** Myriads, as the plants of the field, I have made you, and you have increased and become strong, and you have become families and tribes, and with the good deeds of your forefathers, the time of the redemption of your assembly arrived because you were subjugated and oppressed.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:7)


**רְבָבָה כְּצֶמַח.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל פָּרוּ וַיִּשְׁרְצוּ וַיִּרְבּוּ״ (שמות א:ז):


**Myriads, like the plants of the field I made you** as it is said (Exod. 1:7): “And the children of Israel were fruitful and increased.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:8)


**וְאַתְּ עֵרֹם וְעֶרְיָה.** מִן הַמִּצְוֹת (מכילתא בא ה):


**but you were naked and bare** of the commandments.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:9)


**וָאֶעֱבֹר עָלַיִךְ וָאֶרְאֵךְ.** ״וְאִתְגַּלֵּיתִי עַל מֹשֶׁה בְּאַסְנָא, וַאֲרֵי גְּלֵי קֳדָמַי אֲרֵי מְטָא זְמַן פּוּרְקַנְכוֹן, וַאֲגֵנִית בְּמֵימְרִי עֲלֵיכוֹן, וְאַעְדִיתִי חוֹבֵיכוֹן, וְקַיָּימִית בְּמֵימְרִי לְמִפְרַק יָתְכוֹן כְּמָה דְּקַיָּימִית לַאֲבָהָתְכוֹן, אָמַר ה׳ אֱלֹהִים לְמֶהֱוֵיכוֹן עַם מְשַׁמֵּשׁ קֳדָמַי״:


**[8] And I passed by you, etc** And I revealed Myself to Moses in the thorn bush because it was revealed to Me that the time of your redemption had arrived. I shielded you with My word, and I removed your sins, and I swore by My word to redeem you as I swore to your forefathers, said the Lord God, that you should be a people serving before Me.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:10)


**וָאֶפְרֹשׂ כְּנָפִי.** כְּנַף בִּגְדִּי:


**and I spread My skirt** The skirt of My garment.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:11)


**וָאֶשָּׁבַע לָךְ.** ״לָכֵן אֱמֹר לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲנִי ה׳ וְהוֹצֵאתִי אֶתְכֶם״ (שמות ו:ו), וְאֵין ׳לָכֵן׳ אֶלָּא שְׁבוּעָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״לָכֵן נִשְׁבַּעְתִּי לְבֵית עֵלִי״ (שמואל א ג:יד):


**and I swore to you** (Exod. 6: 6): “Therefore (לָכֵן), say to the children of Israel: I am the Lord! I shall take you out, etc.” “Therefore” means only an [introduction to an] oath, as it is said (I Sam. 3:14): “therefore, I have sworn to the house of Eli...”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:12)


**וָאָבוֹא בִבְרִית אוֹתָךְ.** ״הִנֵּה דַם הַבְּרִית אֲשֶׁר כָּרַת ה׳ עִמָּכֶם״ (שמות כד:ח):


**and came into a covenant with you** (Exod. 24:8): “Behold the blood of the covenant that the Lord has made with you.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:9:13)


**וָאֶרְחָצֵךְ בַּמַּיִם.** ״וּפְרִיקִית יָתְכוֹן מִשִּׁעְבּוּד מִצְרָאֵי, וְאַעֲדִיתִי תְּקוֹף מָרְוָותָא מִנְּכוֹן, וּדְבָרִית יָתְכוֹן בְּחֵירוּתָא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**[9] and I washed you with water** And I redeemed you from the bondage of the Egyptians, and I removed the strength of their lordship from you, and I led you to freedom.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:10:1)


**וָאַלְבִּישֵׁךְ רִקְמָה.** ״וְאַלְבִּישִׁית יָתְכוֹן לְבוּשֵׁי צִיּוּרִין, מֵחֲמַדַּת סָנְאֵיכוֹן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**And I clothed you with embroidered garments** “And I clothed you with embroidered garments of the spoils of your enemies.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:10:2)


**וָאַנְעֲלֵךְ תָּחַשׁ.** אִיקַאלְצֵייטוֹיִ״י טִיישׁוֹ״ן בְּלַעַ״ז, ״וִיהִיבִית מְסָן דִּיקָר בְּרַגְלִיכוֹן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and I shod you with badger** [Jonathan renders:] And I put shoes of glory on your feet.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:10:3)


**וָאֶחְבְּשֵׁךְ בַּשֵּׁשׁ.** אִיפּוֹצִיֵּינִישְׁטוֹ״י בְּלַעַ״ז, ״וְקַדִּישִׁית מִנְּכוֹן כָּהֲנַיָּא לְמֶהֱוֵי מְשַׁמְּשִׁין קֳדָמַי בְּמִצְנְפָן דְּבוּץ״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and I girded you** Heb. וָאֶחְבְּשֵּׁךּ. [Jonathan renders:] and I hallowed priests of you to be serving before Me with turbans of fine linen.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:10:4)


**וָאֲכַסֵּךְ מֶשִׁי.** שווייא״ה בְּלַעַ״ז, ״וְיָת כַּהֲנָא רַבָּא בִּלְבוּשֵׁי צִבְעוֹנִים״ (תרגום יונתן). וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (תנחומא, תצוה א): **וָאֲכַסֵּךְ מֶשִׁי** אֵלּוּ שִׁבְעַת עַנְנֵי כָבוֹד, דִּכְתִיב ״לֹא יָמִישׁ עַמּוּד הֶעָנָן יוֹמָם״ (שמות יג:כב):


**and I covered you with silk** Heb. מֶשִּׁי, soie in French. And the High Priest with colored raiment, and [according to] Midrash Aggadah (Mid. Song 4:2), these are the seven clouds of glory, as it is written (Exod. 13:22): “He did not move (יָמִישּׁ) the pillar of cloud by day.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:11:1)


**וָאֶעְדֵּךְ עֶדִי.** איפאריטו״י בְּלַעַ״ז; ״וְתַקִּינִית יָתְכוֹן תִּיקוּן פִּתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא, כְּתִיבִין עַל תְּרֵין לוּחֵי אַבְנַיָּא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**And I adorned you with ornaments** E pare toy, and I adorned you. [Jonathan renders:] And I adorned you with the adornment of the words of the Torah, written on the two stone tablets.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:11:2)


**וָאֶתְּנָה.** עַל יְדֵי מֹשֶׁה:


**and I put** Heb. וָאֶתְּנָה [lit. and I gave,] through Moses.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:11:3)


**צְמִידִים.** חִבַּרְתִּים דִּבּוּר כְּנֶגֶד חֲבֵרוֹ, חֲמִשָּׁה כְּנֶגֶד חֲמִשָּׁה (מכילתא יתרו בחדש ח):


**bracelets** Heb. צְמִידִים [from צמד, to join]. I joined one commandment opposite the other, five opposite five.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:12:1)


**וָאֶתֵּן נֶזֶם עַל אַפֵּךְ.** ״וִיהָבִית אֲרוֹן קַיָּמִי בֵּינֵיכוֹן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**And I put a nose ring on your nose** [Jonathan renders:] And I placed the ark of My covenant among you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:12:2)


**וַעֲגִילִים עַל אָזְנָיִךְ.** לְשׁוֹן נֶזֶם, ״וְעָנָן יְקָרִי מְטַל עֲלוֹיכוֹן״ (תרגום יונתן); עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהָיוּ מֻקָּפִין בְּעִגּוּל:


**and earrings on your ears** Heb. וַעֲגִילִים, a word for an earring. [Jonathan interprets:] And the cloud of My glory covering over you. [The clouds of glory are called וַעֲגִילִים] because they surrounded them in a circle (עִגּוּל).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:12:3)


**וַעֲטֶרֶת תִּפְאֶרֶת בְּרֹאשֵׁךְ.** ״וּמַלְאָךְ שָׁלִיחַ מִן קֳדָמַי, מְדַבֵּר בְּרֵישֵׁיכוֹן״, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְיַעֲבֹר מַלְכָּם לִפְנֵיהֶם וַה׳ בְּרֹאשָׁם״ (מיכה ב:יג):


**and a crown of glory on your head** [Jonathan renders:] And an angel was sent from before Me leading at your head, as it is said (Micah 2:13): “and their king passed before them, and the Lord was at their head.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:13:1)


**וַתַּעְדִּי זָהָב וָכֶסֶף.** ״וִיהַבִית מִשְׁכָּנִי בֵּינֵיכוֹן, מְתַקֵּן בִּדְהַב וְכֶסֶף וִירִיעַת בּוּץ וְצִבְעוֹנִין וְצִיּוּרִין, וּמָנָא דְטָב כְּסוֹלְתָּא וְכִדְבַשׁ וְכִמְשַׁח אוֹכִילִית יָתְכוֹן, וַעֲתַרְתּוּן וְתַקִּיפְתּוּן לַחֲדָא, וְאַצְלַחְתּוּן וְשַׁלִּיטְתּוּן בְּכָל מַלְכְווֹתָא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**And you adorned yourself with gold and silver** [Jonathan renders:] And I placed My Tabernacle among you, adorned with gold, silver, curtains of linen, colored tapestries and embroidery, and mannawhich was as good as fine flour, honey and oil—I fed you, and you became exceedingly rich and strong, and you prospered and ruled over all the kingdoms.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:13:2)


**סֹלֶת וּדְבַשׁ וָשֶׁמֶן אָכַלְתְּ.** הַמָּן שֶׁהָיָה מִשְׁתַּנֶּה לְכָל הַטְּעָמִים לְסֹלֶת וּדְבַשׁ וָשֶׁמֶן אָכַלְתְּ (השוה תנחומא שמות כה). וּמִדְרָשׁ אַגָּדָה (תנחומא, תרומה ה): שְׁלוֹשָׁה עָשָׂר דְּבָרִים יֵשׁ כָּאן, וּכְנֶגְדָּן אָמַר לָהֶם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְהָבִיא שְׁלוֹשָׁה עָשָׂר דְּבָרִים הַמְּנוּיִין בִּתְרוּמַת הַמִּשְׁכָּן, וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן הוּא פּוֹרֵעַ לָהֶם כְּנֶגְדָּם לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא שְׁלוֹשָׁה עָשָׂר דְּבָרִים: ״וּבָרָא ה׳ עַל מְכוֹן הַר צִיּוֹן וְגוֹ׳ וְסֻכָּה תִּהְיֶה לְצֵל יוֹמָם מֵחֹרֶב מִזֶּרֶם וּמִמָּטָר וְגוֹ׳״ (ישעיה ד:ה-ו):


**fine flour, honey, and oil you ate** The manna, which would change to any flavorto fine flour, honey, and oilyou ate. The Midrash Aggadah (Mid. Song 4:2) [points out that] thirteen items are enumerated here, and corresponding to them, the Holy One, blessed be He, ordered them to bring the thirteen items enumerated in the donation for the Tabernacle (Exod. 25:1-7). Nevertheless, He is destined to repay them in the future with thirteen items (Isa. 4:5f.): “And the Lord shall create over every dwelling of Mount Zion, etc. And a tabernacle shall be for shade by day from the heat, etc., from stream and from rain, etc.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:13:3)


**וַתִּיפִי.** לְשׁוֹן יֹפִי:


**became exceedingly beautiful** An expression of beauty.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:14:1)


**כִּי כָלִיל הוּא.** לְשׁוֹן מִכְלוֹל. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **כִּי כָלִיל הוּא** כִּי שָׁלֵם הָיָה. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **כִּי כָלִיל** קְרוֹנָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״נֵזֶר״ דִּמְתַרְגְּמִינָן ״כְּלִילָא״:


**for it is all inclusive** Heb. כָּלִיל, an expression of all-inclusive beauty (23:12). Another explanation: כִּי כָּלִיל הוּא, for it was perfect. Another explanation: כִּי כָּלִיל couronne in French, like (Lev. 21: 12): “crown (נֵזֶר),” which is translated כְּלִיל by Targum.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:15:1)


**וַתִּזְנִי עַל שְׁמֵךְ.** בָּטַחְתְּ עַל שֵׁם הַיֹּפִי שֶׁיָּצָא לָךְ:


**and you went astray because of your fame** You relied on the fame of the beauty that spread concerning you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:15:2)


**וַתִּשְׁפְּכִי אֶת תַּזְנוּתַיִךְ.** הֶרְאֵית יָפְיֵךְ לְכָל עוֹבֵר, עַד נִשְׁפְּכָה עֲלֵיהֶם תַּאֲוַת זְנוּתֵךְ, לְפַתּוֹת אוֹתָךְ לִזְנוֹת עִמָּם:


**and you poured out your harlotries** You displayed your beauty to every passerby until the lust for your harlotry gushed upon them [arousing them] to seduce you into fornicating with them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:15:3)


**לוֹ יְהִי.** וּמִשֶּׁנִּתְאַוּוּ לְךָ אַתָּה הִפְקַרְתָּ לָהֶם עַצְמְךָ וּלְכָל אֲשֶׁר שָׁאַל זְנוּתֵךְ לוֹ הָיָה, וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם ״וְלָא כָּשֵׁר לִיךְ לְמֶעְבַּד כֵּן״, כְּאִלּוּ הָיָה כְּתִיב לֹא יְהִי, וְכָל מָשָׁל הַזֶּה עַל עֵגֶל הַמִּדְבָּר וְעַל שְׁאָר עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת שֶׁעָבְדוּ שִׁבְטוֹ שֶׁל דָּן בַּמִּדְבָּר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב ״כָּל הַנֶּחֱשָׁלִים אַחֲרֶיךָ״ (דברים כה:יח), וּמָצִינוּ בַּפְּסִיקְתָּא זֶה שִׁבְטוֹ שֶׁל דָּן שֶׁהֶעָנָן פּוֹלְטוֹ, שֶׁהָיוּ כֻּלָּם עוֹבְדֵי עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**to him it would be** And once they desired you, you surrendered yourself to them; and to anyone who requested your harlotry, it would be his. Jonathan renders: and it was not fitting for you to do so, as though it were written לֹא יְהִי. This entire allegory represents the Calf of the desert and the other idolatry that the tribe of Dan worshipped in the desert, as it is written (Deut. 25:18): “all those who trailed after you,” and we find in the Pesikta (d’Rav Kahana p. 27b): This is the tribe of Dan, for the cloud would reject them, because the were all idolators.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:16:1)


**בָּמוֹת טְלֻאוֹת.** מְכֻסּוֹת בִּבְגָדִים מְנֻמָּרִין שֶׁל מִינֵי צִבְעוֹנִין פלטריא״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**mounds of colored garments** Covered with spotted garments of various colors, pla[t]rees in Old French, multi-colored.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:16:2)


**לֹא בָאוֹת.** לֹא הָיְתָה רְאוּיָה זֹאת לָבֹא, נוֹ״ן אַבְנִי״ר אִינוֹ״ן אֵייטְרָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**this should not have come** It was not proper for this to come, non a venir et non a etre in French, [which was] not to come nor to be.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:17:1)


**צַלְמֵי זָכָר.** לְפִי שֶׁדִּמָּה אוֹתָהּ לִנְקֵבָה זוֹנָה, דִּמָּה אֶת הַגְּלוּלִים לְ**זָכָר** הַנּוֹאֵף עִמָּהּ:


**male images** Since he compares her to a female harlot, he compares the image to a male who fornicates with her.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:19:1)


**וְלַחְמִי אֲשֶׁר נָתַתִּי לָךְ וְגוֹ׳.** יוֹם שֶׁעָשׂוּ אֶת הָעֵגֶל, יָרַד לָהֶם הַמָּן, וְנָתְנוּ מִמֶּנּוּ לִפְנֵי הָעֵגֶל, וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן **וַיֶּהִי** – בְּיוֹם מָחָר, וְלֹא פָּסַק; וְאַף כֵּן מְפֹרָשׁ בְּסֵפֶר עֶזְרָא (נחמיה ט:יח-כ): ״אַף כִּי עָשׂוּ לָהֶם עֵגֶל מַסֵּכָה וְגוֹ׳ וְאַתָּה בְּרַחֲמֶיךָ הָרַבִּים לֹא עֲזַבְתָּם וְגוֹ׳ וּמָנְךָ לֹא מָנַעְתָּ מִפִּיהֶם״ – כְּדַאי הוּא לִמְנוֹעַ מֵהֶם וְלֹא מָנַעְתָּ; אַף כָּאן **וַיֶּהִי נְאֻם אֲדֹנָי יְהוִה** – הָיָה הַלֶּחֶם, וְלֹא פָּסַק מִלֵּירֵד לְיוֹם מָחָר; כָּךְ דָּרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, כי תשא יד):


**And My bread that I gave you** The day they made the Calf, the manna descended for them and they placed some of it before the Calf. Nonetheless, ‘so it was’ on the morrow, and it did not cease. Even this is delineated in the Book of Ezra (Neh. 9:18ff.): “Even when they made for themselves a molten calf, etc. And You, with Your manifold mercies, did not forsake them, etc., and You did not withhold Your manna from their mouth.” It was fitting to withhold it from them, yet You did not withhold it. [Scripture writes] here, too, “so it was, says the Lord God” the bread was, and it did not stop falling on the next day. So did Rabbi Tanchuma expound on it (Buber, Re’eh 13a).



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:20:1)


**אֲשֶׁר יָלַדְתְּ לִי.** הָיוּ לוֹ חֲמִשָּׁה בָּנִים, אַרְבָּעָה מֵהֶם לַעֲבוֹד עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת וְאֶחָד מוּקְצֶה לְבֵית הַסֵּפֶר, וּבָא לְהַקְרִיב אֶחָד מִבָּנָיו לַמּוֹלֶךְ – מַקְרִיב לוֹ אֶת זֶה שֶׁהִקְצָה לִלְמוֹד תּוֹרָה, בְּמִדְרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא; וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר: **אֲשֶׁר יָלַדְתְּ לִי** אֵלּוּ הַבְּכוֹרוֹת, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר בַּסֵּפֶר הַזֶּה (לקמן כ:כו) ״וָאֲטַמֵּא אוֹתָם בְּמַתְּנוֹתָם בְּהַעֲבִיר כָּל פֶּטֶר רָחֶם״:


**that you bore for Me** If one had five sons, four of them were devoted to worshipping idols, and one he would set aside to attend school; and when he comes to offer up one of his sons to Molech, he offers up to it this one whom he had set aside to study Torah in the Midrash of Rabbi Tanchuma. But I say that “that you bore for Me” refers to the firstborn. Likewise, it is stated in this Book (20:26): “And I defiled them with their gifts, when they caused every one who opened the womb to pass.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:22:1)


**אֶת יְמֵי נְעוּרָיִךְ.** אֶת חֲסָדַי אֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתִי עִמָּךְ בְּמִצְרַיִם (שמות יב:כט-לו):


**the days of your youth** My acts of kindness that I performed for you in Egypt.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:23:1)


**אוֹי אוֹי לָךְ.** ״וַי לִיךְ עַל דְּחָבַת, וַוי לִיךְ עַל דְּלָא תָבַת״ (תרגום יונתן):


**Woe, woe is to you** Woe is to you because you have sinned! Woe is to you because you have not repented!



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:24:1)


**וַתִּבְנִי לָךְ גֶּב.** גּוֹבַהּ; כְּעֵין בִּימוֹס וּמִזְבֵּחַ. **וַתִּבְנִי** – לְשׁוֹן בִּנְיָן:


**You built yourself an eminent place** Heb. גָב, a height such as pedestal or an altar. **You built** Heb. וַתִּבְנִי, an expression for building.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:25:1)


**וַתְּפַשְּׂקִי אֶת רַגְלַיִךְ.** לְפִי שֶׁדִּמָּה אוֹתָהּ לְזוֹנָה, דִּבֵּר בִּלְשׁוֹן תַּשְׁמִישׁ, שֶׁיֵּשׁ פִּסּוּק הָרַגְלַיִם, אִיאַקִיישְׁמַ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**you spread your legs** Since He compares her to a harlot, He speaks in terms of sexual activity, in which there is spreading of the legs, a cesmas in Old French, you spread wide apart.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:26:1)


**גִּדְלֵי בָשָׂר.** בַּעֲלֵי אֵבֶר תַּשְׁמִישׁ:


**great of flesh** Having a large male organ.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:27:1)


**חֻקֵּךְ.** מְזוֹנוֹתַיִךְ, בִּימֵי שְׁפֹט הַשּׁוֹפְטִים (רות א:א):


**your ration** Your food, in the days that the judges judged.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:27:2)


**וָאֶתְּנֵךְ בְּנֶפֶשׁ שֹׂנְאוֹתַיִךְ.** בִּימֵי שִׁמְשׁוֹן (שופטים יג-טז):


**and I delivered you to the will of those who hate you** in the days of Samson.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:27:3)


**הַנִּכְלָמוֹת מִדַּרְכֵּךְ זִמָּה.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״דְּאִלּוּ לְוָתְהוֹן שְׁלָחִית נְבִיַּי, אִתְכְּנָעוּ״, וְלֹא הָיוּ הוֹלְכִין בְּדֶרֶךְ זִמָּתֵךְ:


**who were embarrassed by your lewd way** Jonathan [paraphrases]: For had I sent My prophets to them, they would have humbled themselves, and they would not have gone in the way of your lewdness.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:28:1)


**מִבִּלְתִּי שָׂבְעָתֵךְ.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁלֹּא שָׂבַעַתְּ בְּתוֹעֲבוֹת מִצְרַיִם:


**because you were not sated** Because you were not sated with the abominations of Egypt.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:28:2)


**וַתִּזְנִים.** לְשׁוֹן זְנוּת, אהאנשאר״ה אאל״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and you played the harlot with them** Heb. וַתִּזְנִים, from the term for harlotry (זְנוּת), e ansera[s] a els.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:29:1)


**אֶל אֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן כַּשְׂדִּימָה.** כַּשְׂדִּים לֹא מֵאֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן הֵם, וְכָךְ פֵּרוּשׁוֹ: הוֹסַפְתְּ צַלְמֵי כַשְׂדִּים עַל שֶׁל כְּנַעֲנִים, שֶׁהָיוּ סְמוּכִים לִיךְ וְזָנִית לָהֶם כְּבָר; וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם **וַתַּרְבִּי תַּזְנוּתֵךְ כַּשְׂדִּימָה** – וְנִדְמֵית לַכְּנַעֲנִים הַשְּׁטוּפִים בַּעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת: ״וְאַסְגֵית יָת טָעֲוָותִיךְ לְאִתְחַבְּרָא לְעַמָּא דְכְנַעַן, לְמֵהַךְ בְּנִימוּסֵי כַשְׂדָּאֵי״. וְלִי נִרְאֶה: **כְּנַעַן** – כְּמוֹ סוֹחֵר, כְּמוֹ ״כְּנַעַן בְּיָדוֹ מֹאזְנֵי מִרְמָה״ (הושע יב:ח), שֶׁכֵּן קוֹרֵא אֶרֶץ כַּשְׂדִּים אֶרֶץ סוֹחֲרִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיְבִיאֵהוּ אֶל אֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן בְּעִיר רֹכְלִים שָׂמוֹ״ (יחזקאל יז:ד):


**with the land of Canaan, with the Chaldeans** The Chaldeans were not from the land of Canaan, but this is its meaning: You added the images of the Chaldeans to those of the Canaanites, who were near you, and you played the harlot with them. However Jonathan rendered: You increased your harlotries with the Chaldeans and You became like the Canaanites, who were steeped in idolatry, and you increased your gods, joining the people of Canaan who follow the customs of the Chaldeans. But to me it appears that כְּנַעַן is like סוֹחֵר, a merchant, like (Hosea 12:8): “A trafficker (כְּנַעַן) who has deceitful scales in his hand.” He thus calls the land of the Chaldeans the land of the merchants, as it is said [concerning Babylon, too,] (17:4): “and he brought it to the land of merchants (כְּנַעַן), in a city of traffickers he placed it.”



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:30:1)


**מָה אֲמֻלָּה.** מְקֻלְקֶלֶת, וְכֵן ״הַיְּהוּדִים הָאֲמֵלָלִים״ (נחמיה ג:לד), וְכֵן ״כִּי אֻמְלַל אָנִי״ (תהלים ו:ג):


**How degenerate** Heb. אֲמֻלָה, spoiled. Similarly (Neh. 3:34): “the feeble (הָאֲמֵלָלִים) Jews,” and similarly (Ps. 6:3), “because I languish (אֻמְלַל).”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:30:2)


**לִבָּתֵךְ.** כְּמוֹ לִבֵּךְ:


**your heart** Heb. לִבַָּתֵךְ, like לִבֵּךּ.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:30:3)


**שַׁלָּטֶת.** שֶׁיִּצְרָהּ שַׁלִּיט עָלֶיהָ:


**dominated** Heb. שַּׁלָּטֶת, whose evil inclination dominates her.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:31:1)


**בִּבְנוֹתַיִךְ גַּבֵּךְ.** כְּמוֹ בִּבְנוֹתֵךְ, לְשׁוֹן בִּנְיָן:


**When you build your eminent place** Heb. בִּבְנוֹתַיִךּ, like בִּבְנוֹתֵךּ, from the word for building.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:31:2)


**לְקַלֵּס אֶתְנָן.** לְדַבֵּר וּלְהִתְהַלֵּל בְּאֶתְנָן יָפֶה שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין לָהּ, וְכֵן כָּל לְשׁוֹן קִלּוּס לְשׁוֹן דִּבּוּר, פארלי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**to boast of a hire** Heb. לְּקַלֵס, to speak and to boast of the fine hire that they give her. Similarly, every form of קֶלֶס is an expression of speech, parliz in Old French, boastful, empty talk.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:31:3)


**אֶתְנָן.** הוּא לְשׁוֹן שְׂכִירוּת, לויאי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**hire** Heb. אֶתְנַן, an expression for hire, loyez in old French.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:32:1)


**הָאִשָּׁה הַמְּנָאָפֶת.** אַתְּ **הָאִשָּׁה הַמְּנָאָפֶת**:


**the adulterous wife** You are the adulterous wife.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:32:2)


**תַּחַת אִישָׁהּ וְגוֹ׳.** בִּהְיוֹתָהּ תַּחַת בַּעְלָהּ, נוֹתֶנֶת עֵינֶיהָ בַּאֲחֵרִים:


**who, under her husband, takes strangers** While she is under her husband, [she] casts her eyes on others.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:33:1)


**נֵדֶה.** מֹהַר:


**gifts** Heb. נֵדֶה, a dowry.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:33:2)


**בְּתַזְנוּתָיִךְ.** מֵרוֹב זְנוּת שֶׁהָיָה בָּךְ עָשִׂית כֵּן:


**because of your harlotries** Because of the great [lust for] harlotry that was in you, you did so.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:34:1)


**לֹא זוּנָּה.** לֹא נִזְנָה לְכָל זוֹנָה כִּזְנוּתֵךְ:


**there was no such harlotry** No harlotry was done with any harlot like your harlotry.



### Verse 36
