###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:36:1)


**הִשָּׁפֵךְ נְחֻשְׁתֵּךְ.** הוּא לְשׁוֹן שׁוּלַיִם, שֶׁקּוֹרִין פונ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז; וְכֵן הַרְבֵּה בְּסֵדֶר טָהֳרוֹת (משנה כלים ח, ג; ט, א, ג): נְחוּשְׁתּוֹ שֶׁל תַּנּוּר. **הִשָּׁפֵךְ נְחֻשְׁתֵּךְ** מֵרֹב תַּאֲוַת זְנוּת הָיָה מְקוֹרֵךְ זָב:


**your bottom was poured out** Heb. נְחֻשְּׁתֵךּ. This is a term for a bottom, which is called fonz in old French, bottom. And so [is this word] many [times] in the [Mishnaic] order of Tohoroth (Kelim 8: 3): “the base (נְחֻשְּׁתוֹ) of a stove.” **your bottom was poured out** Because of the great lust for harlotry. your ‘fountain’ would flow.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:36:2)


**וְעַל כָּל גִּלּוּלֵי תוֹעֲבוֹתָיִךְ.** וְיַעַן כָּל גִּלּוּלֵי תוֹעֲבוֹתָיִךְ:


**and because of all your abominable idols** Heb. וְעַל.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:36:3)


**וְכִדְמֵי בָנָיִךְ.** וּבַעֲוֹן דְּמֵי בָנַיִךְ אֶעֱשֶׂה לְךָ הָרָעָה הַזֹּאת הַמְפֹרֶשֶׁת בָּעִנְיָן: **הִנְנִי מְקַבֵּץ וְגוֹ׳**:


**and according to the blood of your sons** And for the iniquity of the blood of your sons I will do to you this evil delineated in the chapter: “Behold I gather etc.”



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:37:1)


**עָרַבְתְּ.** מאלא״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**you mingled** Heb. עָרַבְתְּ, mellas in Old French.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:38:1)


**וּנְתַתִּיךְ דַּם חֵמָה וְקִנְאָה.** וְאַסְגִּירֵךְ לְהוֹרְגֵי בְּחֵמָה וְקִנְאָה וְכַעַס:


**and I shall deliver you [to those who shed] blood with fury and zeal** And I shall deliver you to those who slay with fury and zeal and anger.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:39:1)


**וְהָרְסוּ גַבֵּךְ.** בִּנְיָנֵי גוֹבְהִי; בִּימוֹסֵיךְ (ראה רש״י יחזקאל כד:כא):


**and they will demolish your eminent place** Your tall ceremonial buildings.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:39:2)


**רָמוֹתַיִךְ.** לְשׁוֹן בָּמוֹת:


**and your lofty places** An expression used for high altars.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:40:1)


**וּבִתְּקוּךְ.** אֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן; וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם ״וִיבַזְּעוּנִיךְ״, איטרופ״ר נרונטו״י בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and pierce you** Heb. וּבִתְּקוּךְ. There is no similar word elsewhere in Scripture, and Jonathan rendered וְיִבְזְעוּנִיךּ, and they will pierce you, e tre[s]perzeront toy in Old French.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:41:1)


**וְהִשְׁבַּתִּיךְ מִזּוֹנָה.** מֵהֱיוֹת עוֹד זוֹנָה:


**and I shall stop you from a harlot** From being a harlot.



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:43:1)


**הֵא.** כְּמוֹ **הִנֵּה**:


**behold** Heb. הֵא, like הִנֵה.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:43:2)


**וְלֹא עָשִׂית אֶת הַזִּמָּה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֲנִי נָתַתִּי דַּרְכֵּךְ בְּרֹאשֵׁךְ **לֹא עָשִׂית** עֵצָה לָתֵת לֵב **עַל תּוֹעֲבוֹתַיִךְ** לָשׁוּב מֵהֶם, כָּל זִמָּה שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא לְשׁוֹן עֵצָה הוּא, יֵשׁ לְטוֹבָה וְיֵשׁ לְרָעָה:


**yet you did not make a plan** Although I have given your way on your head, you have not made a plan to ponder your abominations, to repent of them. Every זִמָה in Scripture is an expression of counsel, sometimes for good and sometimes for bad.



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:44:1)


**כָּל הַמּוֹשֵׁל.** לְשׁוֹן מָשָׁל וְחִידָה הוּא:


**whoever uses proverbs** Heb. הַמֹשֵּׁל. This is an expression of a proverb and a riddle


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:44:2)


**כְּאִמָּה בִּתָּהּ.** הֵ״א שֶׁל כְּאִמָּהּ אֵינָהּ מֵפִיק הֵ״א, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת כְּלוּם; אֶלָּא: כְּאֵם, כֵּן בַּת:


**“Like mother, like daughter”** Heb. כְּאִמָה בִּתָּה. The ‘hey’ of כְּאִמָה is not a ‘mappiq [aspirate] hey,’ [to read her mother; i.e. it is not the third person possessive] because it does not serve for any noun but it means, as the mother, so is her daughter.



### Verse 45

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:45:1)


**בַּת אִמֵּךְ.** אֻמָּה שֶׁל כְּנַעַן, אֲשֶׁר יְרַשְׁתֶּם אֶת אַרְצָם:


**your mother’s daughter** The nation of Canaan, whose land you inherited.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:45:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:45:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:45:2)


**גֹּעֶלֶת אִישָׁהּ וּבָנֶיהָ.** אֲשֶׁר הִרְבּוּ תּוֹעֲבוֹתָם עַד כִּי גָּעֲלָה וְהֵקִיאָה הָאָרֶץ אֶת יוֹשְׁבֶיהָ (ויקרא יח:כה), הָאָבוֹת וְהַבָּנִים יַחַד:


**spewing forth her husband and her children** Who so increased their abominations that the land spewed forth its inhabitants, the fathers and the children together.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:45:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:45:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:45:3)


**וַאֲחוֹת אֲחוֹתֵךְ.** סְדוֹם, הַסְּמוּכָה לְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and you are your sister’s sister** Sodom, which is next to the land of Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:45:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:45:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:45:4)


**אִמְּכֶן חִתִּית וְגוֹ׳.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״הֲלָא אִמְּכוֹן שָׂרָה בֵּינֵי חִתָּאֵי הֲוַת וְלָא עַבְדַת כְּעוֹבְדֵיהוֹן, וַאֲבוּכוֹן אַבְרָהָם בֵּינֵי אֱמוֹרָאֵי, וְלָא הֲלִיךְ בְּעֵיצַתְהוֹן״:


**your mother was a Hittite, etc.** Jonathan renders: Is it not so that your mother Sarah was among the Hittites, yet did not do as their deeds, and your father Abraham was among the Amorites, yet did not follow their counsel!



### Verse 46

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:46:1)


**עַל שְׂמֹאולֵךְ.** לְפִי שֶׁנַּחֲלַת יְהוּדָה בִּגְבוּל דְּרוֹמִי שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל הָיָה, נִמְצְאוּ כָּל שְׁאָר הַשְּׁבָטִים **עַל שְׂמֹאלָם**:


**at your left** Since the territory of Judah was at the southern border of Eretz Israel [and one is considered facing east], all the other tribes were situated at their left [since they are north of Judah].



### Verse 47

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:47:1)


**וְלֹא בְדַרְכֵיהֶן הָלַכְתְּ.** וְהַלְוַאי בְּדַרְכֵיהֶן הָלַכְתְּ, וְלֹא הִתְעַבְתְּ יוֹתֵר מֵהֶם; וְכֵן ״וְלֹא יֻתַּן נָא לְעַבְדֶּיךָ״ (מלכים ב ה:יז), וְכֵן ״וְלֹא אִם עוֹדֶנִּי חַי״ דִּיהוֹנָתָן (שמואל א כ:יד) אַפְּלִיָּ״אוֹט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**If only you had gone in their ways** Heb. וְלֹא, if only you had gone in their ways and not committed abominations more than they. Likewise (II Kings 5:17): “Now if only (וָלֹֹא) your servant to be given.” Likewise (I Sam. 20:14): “And please, if (וְלֹא) I am still alive,” concerning Jonathan, et plut a in French, would that..., if only!


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:47:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:47:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:47:2)


**כִּמְעַט קָט.** הָיוּ תוֹעֲבוֹתַיִךְ לְפָנַי אִילּוּ לֹא הִתְעַבְתְּ מֵהֶן, אֲבָל אַתְּ **וַתַּשְׁחִיתִי מֵהֶן**; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִין **וְלֹא בְדַרְכֵיהֶן הָלַכְתְּ** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ: **כִּמְעַט קָט** הָיוּ תוֹעֲבוֹתֵיהֶן בְּעֵינַיִךְ, **וַתַּשְׁחִיתִי מֵהֶן. קָט** – פִּיקוֹ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**so very little** Would your abominations have been before Me, had you not committed abominations more than they, but you dealt more corruptly than they. This is also Jonathan’s rendering. Others interpret וְלא בָּדֳרְכֵהֶן הָלֳכְתְּ as its apparent meaning, “and you did not go in their ways,” [and thus follows the reason:] So very little were their abominations in your eyes that you dealt more corruptly than they, pochet in Old French, a little.



### Verse 50

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:50:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:50:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:50:1)


**וַתִּגְבְּהֶינָה.** הִיא וּבְנוֹתֶיהָ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְפֹרָשׁ בְּאִיּוֹב ״אֶרֶץ מִמֶּנָּה יֵצֵא לָחֶם״ (איוב כח:ה), וְכָל הָעִנְיָן, עַד שֶׁאָמְרוּ: נִשְׁכַּח תּוֹרַת רֶגֶל מֵאַרְצֵנוּ, שֶׁאֵין בָּאִים אֶלָּא לְחַסֵּר (תנחומא, אחרי מות א; במדבר רבה ט:כד):


**And they became haughty** She and her daughters, as is delineated in Job (28:5): “A land from which bread emerged,” and the entire piece (ibid. 4-9). [The Sodomites grew in haughtiness] until they said, “Let us cause the habit of traveling [lit. the foot] to be forgotten from our land, for they come only to cause us loss.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:50:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:50:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:50:2)


**וָאָסִיר אֶתְהֶן כַּאֲשֶׁר רָאִיתִי.** דַּרְכָּן, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בִּסְדוֹם ״אֵרְדָה נָּא וְאֶרְאֶה וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית יח:כא):


**and I removed them when I saw** their way, as the matter that is stated regarding Sodom (Gen. 18:21): “I shall go down and see.”



### Verse 51

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:51:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:51:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:51:1)


**וַתְּצַדְּקִי אֶת אֲחוֹתֵךְ.** קוֹדֶם שֶׁהִתְעַבְתְּ יוֹתֵר מֵהֶן, הָיוּ נִדּוֹנִים לְחוֹבָה; וְכֵיוָן שֶׁהִרְשַׁעְתְּ יוֹתֵר מֵהֶן, עָשִׂית אוֹתָן כְּצַדְקָנִיּוֹת, וְנָתַתְּ לָהֶן פִּתְחוֹן פֶּה לוֹמַר: יֵשׁ שֶׁהִכְעִיסוּךְ יוֹתֵר מִמֶּנִּי:


**and you vindicated your sisters** Before you had committed abominations more than they, they would have been a judged guilty, but since you became more wicked than they, you made them [appear] as righteous women, and you gave them an excuse to say, “There are some who have provoked you more than I.”



### Verse 52

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:52:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:52:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:52:1)


**אֲשֶׁר פִּלַּלְתְּ.** אֲשֶׁר שָׁפַטְתְּ לָהֶם; קוֹדֶם שֶׁהִרְשַׁעְתְּ מֵהֶן הָיִית אוֹמֶרֶת עֲלֵיהֶן: רְאוּיוֹת הֵן לְפֻרְעָנוּת:


**that you judged** That you judged them; before you behaved more wickedly than they, you used to say about them that they are fit for punishment.



### Verse 53

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:53:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:53:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:53:1)


**אֶת שְׁבוּת סְדֹם.** אֶרְפָּא אֶת הָאָרֶץ מִגָּפְרִית וָמֶלַח, וְאוֹשִׁיב בָּהּ יוֹשְׁבִים:


**the captivity of Sodom** Heb. שְּׁבוּת. I shall cure the land of brimstone and salt, and I shall settle it with inhabitants.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:53:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:53:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:53:2)


**בְּתוֹכָהְנָה.** כְּמוֹ **בְּתוֹכָן**:


**in their midst** Heb. בְּתוֹכָהְנָה, like בְּתוֹכָן.



### Verse 54

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:54:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:54:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:54:1)


**בְּנַחֲמֵךְ אוֹתָן.** כְּשֶׁהִרְשַׁעְתְּ מֵהֶן, וְנָתַתְּ לָהֶן פִּתְחוֹן פֶּה; דָּבָר אַחֵר: **בְּנַחֲמֵךְ אוֹתָן.** כְּשֶׁתָּבֹא עָלַיִךְ הָרָעָה, יִנּוֹחֲמוּ הֵנָּה, לוֹמַר: לֹא לְבַדֵּנוּ לָקִינוּ:


**in that you consoled them** when you became more wicked than they, and you gave them an excuse [to neglect righteousness]. Another explanation: **in that you consoled them** When the evil comes upon you, they will be consoled, saying, We have not suffered alone.



### Verse 56

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:56:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:56:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:56:1)


**וְלֹא הָיְתָה סְדֹם אֲחוֹתֵךְ וְגוֹ׳.** בִּתְמִיהָּ:


**Now, was not Sodom your sister, etc.** This is a [rhetorical] question.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:56:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:56:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:56:2)


**לִשְׁמוּעָה בְּפִיךְ.** לְהוֹכִיחַ רַבִּים וְלוֹמַר: הִזָּהֲרוּ שֶׁלֹּא תֵהָפְכוּ כִּסְדוֹם, כְּאִלּוּ הָיְתָה לָךְ שְׁמוּעָה חֲדָשָׁה:


**a report in your mouth** [by which] to reprove many and to say, Beware that you should not be overturned like Sodom, as though it were recent news to you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:56:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:56:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:56:3)


**בְּיוֹם גְּאוֹנָיִךְ.** כְּשֶׁהָיִית בִּגְדֻלָּתֵךְ:


**on the day of your pride** When you were in your greatness.



### Verse 57

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:57:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:57:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:57:1)


**כְּמוֹ עֵת חֶרְפַּת בְּנוֹת אֲרָם.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּגְלֵית בְּעֵת מָסַרְתִּי אֶתְכֶם בְּיַד מַלְכֵי אֲרָם, שֶׁבָּאוּ עַל אָחָז: ״בָּעֵת הַהִיא הֵחֵל ה׳ לְהַשְׁלִיחַ בִּיהוּדָה אֶת גְּדוּדֵי אֲרָם וְגוֹ׳״ (מלכים ב טו:לז), וּבְדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים: ״וַיִּתְּנֵהוּ ה׳... בְּיַד מַלְכֵי אֲרָם וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב כח:ה), ״וּפְלִשְׁתִּים פָּשְׁטוּ בְּעָרֵי הַנֶּגֶב וְהַשְּׁפֵלָה וְגוֹ׳״ (דברי הימים ב כח:יח):


**like [at] the time of the taunt by the daughters of Aram** As it was revealed when I delivered you into the hands of the kings of Aram, who attacked Ahaz (II Kings 15:36); “In those days, the Lord began to incite Rezin the king of Aram. etc.”; (II Chron. 28:5), “And the Lord his God delivered him into the hands of the king of Aram, etc.”; (ibid. verse 18), “And the Philistines invaded the cities of the plain and the south, etc.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:57:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:57:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:57:2)


**הַשָּׁאטוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן בִּזָּיוֹן, וְלֹא קְרִי אָלֶ״ף. ״וַיִּבֶז״ (בראשית כה:לד), מְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״וְשָׁט״ (תרגום אונקלוס):


**who disgraced you** Heb. הֳשָּׁאטוֹת, an expression for disgrace. The ‘aleph’ is not sounded. וַיִבֶז, despised (Gen. 25:34), is translated as וְשָּׁט [in Targum Onkelos].



### Verse 58

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:58:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:58:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:58:1)


**נְשָׂאתִים.** נָשָׂאת אוֹתָם, וְלָשׁוֹן נְקֵבָה הִיא בְּכַמָּה מְקוֹמוֹת בְּסֵפֶר זֶה:


**you bore them** Heb. נְשָּׂאתִים, you bore them. This is a feminine form [used] in many places in this Book.



### Verse 59

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:59:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:59:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:59:1)


**וְעָשִׂיתִי אוֹתָךְ וְגוֹ׳.** עָשִׂיתִי לְךָ רָעָה כִּגְמוּלְךָ **אֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתָ**:


**I have done with you, etc.** I have done evil to you commensurate to your [ungrateful] repayment [to Me] which you made.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:59:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:59:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:59:2)


**אֲשֶׁר בָּזִית אָלָה וְגוֹ׳.** אוֹתָהּ שֶׁקִּבַּלְתְּ עָלֶיךָ בְּחוֹרֵב: ״לְעָבְרְךָ בִּבְרִית ה׳ אֱלֹהֶיךָ וְגוֹ׳״ (דברים כט:יא); אַךְ לֹא אָפִיר אֲנִי אֶת הַבְּרִית מִלְּזוֹכְרוֹ, כִּי אֵין אֲנִי כְּמוֹתֵךְ:


**that you despised an oath, etc.** That one that you accepted upon yourself in Horeb (Deut. 29:11): “That you pass into the covenant of the Lord your God, etc.” But I shall not violate the covenant by not remembering it, because I am not like you.



### Verse 60

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:60:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:60:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:60:1)


**וְזָכַרְתִּי אֲנִי אֶת בְּרִיתִי.** אֲשֶׁר הָיָה עִמָּךְ **בִּימֵי נְעוּרָיִךְ**:


**But I shall remember My covenant** That was with you in the days of your youth.



### Verse 61

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:61:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:61:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:61:1)


**וְזָכַרְתְּ.** בַּהֲטִיבִי לָךְ **אֶת דְּרָכַיִךְ** הָרָעִים, **וְנִכְלַמְתְּ** מִלְּפָנַי עַל שֶׁגָּמַלְתְּ לִי רָעָה וַאֲנִי גּוֹמְלֵךְ טוֹבָה:


**And you will remember** when I improve you evil ways for you, and you will be humiliated before Me for repaying Me with evil whereas I paid you with good.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:61:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:61:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:61:2)


**בְּקַחְתֵּךְ אֶת אֲחוֹתַיִךְ.** כְּשֶׁתִּכְבְּשִׁי לִירַשׁ אֶת מְקוֹמוֹת אֲשֶׁר סְבִיבוֹתַיִךְ, **הַקְּטַנּוֹת** וְ**הַגְּדֹלוֹת**:


**when you take your sisters** When you conquer to inherit the nations that are around you, the small and the great.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:61:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:61:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:61:3)


**לְבָנוֹת.** לִפְרְוָרִים לְךָ מְשֻׁעְבָּדִים לְךָ, כְּמוֹ **סְדוֹם וּבְנוֹתֶיהָ** ״וְכַפְרָנָהָא״:


**for daughters** For your suburbs, subordinate to you, like (verse 55): “Sodom and her daughters,” [translated by Jonathan as] וְכָפְרָנָהָא, its suburbs.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:61:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:61:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:61:4)


**וְלֹא מִבְּרִיתֵךְ.** וְלֹא מִשָּׁמְרֵךְ אֶת הַבְּרִית אֲשֶׁר כָּרַתִּי אִתָּךְ, אֶלָּא בְּחַסְדִּי וּבְרַחֲמִי שֶׁאֲנִי שׁוֹמֵר אֶת בְּרִיתִי; וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה דְּרַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא: **וְלֹא מִבְּרִיתֵךְ** לֹא מִפַּטְרְמוֹנִי״א שֶׁלִּיךְ, לֹא נְתַתִּיהָ לְאַבְרָהָם אָבִיךְ בֵּין הַבְּתָרִים (בראשית טו:יז-יח), וְזֶה הַלָּשׁוֹן קָרוֹב לַלַּעַ״ז, שֶׁכֵּן קוֹרִין עַתָּה לַקְּרָבוֹת פַּטְרָ״מוֹנִיָא בְּלַעַ״ז, וְכֵן קוֹרִין כָּל דָּבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ לָאָדָם מִנַּחֲלַת אֲבוֹתָיו:


**but not from your covenant** But not because of your observance of the covenant that I made with you, but because of My kindnesses and My mercies, [namely] that I observe My covenant. Midrash Aggadah of Rabbi Tanchuma (Buber, Devarim 3a) states: but not from your covenant (מִבְּרִיתֵךְ) not from your ‘patromonia’: I did not grant them to Abraham your father [in the covenant] ‘between the segments’ (Gen. 15:1). This term is close to the French, for now, kinship is called patremone, patrimony, and this is what they call anything a person has from the inheritance of his fathers.



### Verse 63

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:63:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:63:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:63:1)


**לְמַעַן תִּזְכְּרִי.** דַּרְכֵּךְ, בְּהֵיטִיבִי לָךְ:


**In order that you remember** your way when I do good for you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:63:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:63:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:63:2)


**וָבֹשְׁתְּ.** לְהָרִים פָּנֶיךָ אֵלַי:


**and be ashamed** to raise your face to Me.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 16:63:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:63:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/16:63:3)


**בְּכַפְּרִי לָךְ.** בְּסָלְחִי לָךְ, אֶת **אֲשֶׁר עָשִׂית**, תֵּבוֹשִׁי וְתֵדְעִי, כִּי לֹא מִגְּמוּלָךְ הַטּוֹב הֵטַבְתִּי לָךְ:


**when I forgive you** Heb. בְּכַּפְרִי לָךְ [lit. in my atoning to you]. When I forgive you for what you have done, you will be ashamed, and you will know that I did not benefit you because of the good that you bestowed upon Me.



## Chapter 17


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:2:1)


**חוּד חִידָה.** הַנְּבוּאָה שֶׁבָּעִנְיָן בִּלְשׁוֹן חִידָה הִיא, שֶׁהוּא מְדַמֶּה אֶת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר לְנֶשֶׁר וְאֶת מַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה לְצַמֶּרֶת אֲרָזִים:


**propound a riddle** The prophecy in this chapter is expressed as a riddle, [in] which he compares Nebuchadnezzar to an eagle and the kings of Judah to the lofty top of cedars.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:3:1)


**אֶרֶךְ הָאֵבֶר.** כְּמוֹ: כָּנָף, אֵילָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז; לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ, לְשׁוֹן אֵבָרִים מַמָּשׁ:


**long pinions** Heb. אֵבֶר like כָּנָף, wing, aile in French. Another explanation: as its apparent meaning, an expression of actual limbs.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:3:2)


**הָרִקְמָה.** נוֹצָתוֹ נָאָה וּמְצֻיֶּרֶת:


**diverse colors** Heb. קָרְמָה [lit. embroidered]. Its feathers are beautiful and ornate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:3:3)


**אֶת צַמֶּרֶת הָאָרֶז.** כָּל נוֹף הָאִילָן, וּבְלַעַ״ז צִימָ״א; הוּא מָשָׁל, שֶׁהִגְלָה אֶת יְהוֹיָקִים וְגִבּוֹרָיו:


**the lofty top of the cedar** Heb. צַמֶרֶת, all the foliage of the tree, cime in French. This is a parable representing Jehoiakim and his mighty men.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:4:1)


**אֶת רֹאשׁ יְנִיקוֹתָיו קָטָף.** גְּדֻלָּתוֹ וּמַלְכוּתוֹ הִשְׁפִּיל. **יְנִיקוֹתָיו** כְּמוֹ עֲנָפָיו:


**He plucked the top of its branches** Heb. יְנִיקוֹתָיו. He humbled his [Jehoiakim’s] greatness and his monarchy. יְנִיקוֹתָיו is like עַנָפָיו, its branches.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:4:2)


**וַיְבִיאֵהוּ אֶל אֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן.** אֶל אֶרֶץ תַּגָּרִים, הִיא בָּבֶל:


**and brought to a land of merchants** Heb. כְּנַעַן, to a land of merchants; that is Babylon.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:5:1)


**וַיִּקַּח מִזֶּרַע הָאָרֶץ.** אֶת צִדְקִיָּהוּ בְּנוֹ:


**Then he took of the seed of the land** Zedekiah his son.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:5:2)


**בִּשְׂדֵה זֶרַע.** בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, בְּעִיר מְלוּכָה:


**in a fertile field** [lit. in a field of seed.] In Jerusalem, in the capital city.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:5:3)


**קַח עַל מַיִם רַבִּים.** נִשְׁרַשׁ עַל מַיִם רַבִּים, כְּלוֹמַר: נָתַן לוֹ גְּדֻלָּה וּמֶמְשָׁלָה עַל סְבִיבָיו:


**it took [root] beside abundant waters** Heb. קַח, it took. It became rooted beside abundant waters, i.e., he gave him greatness and dominion over his surroundings.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:5:4)


**צַפְצָפָה.** מִין אִילָן עֲרָבָה, שֶׁעֲנָפָיו מְרֻבִּים שאלק״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a mountain willow he made it** Heb. צַפְצָפָה, a species of willow tree which has many branches, saice in Old French, a willow.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:1)


**סוֹרַחַת.** כְּמוֹ ״וְסֶרַח הָעוֹדֵף״ (שמות כו:יב), גְּדוֹלָה:


**spreading** Heb. סוֹרַחַת, like (Exod. 26:12): “And the overhanging length (וְסֶרַח),” large.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:2)


**שִׁפְלַת קוֹמָה.** לַעֲבֹד לְמֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל:


**of low stature** to serve the king of Babylon.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:3)


**דָּלִיּוֹתָיו.** זְמוֹרוֹתָיו:


**its tendrils** Heb. דָּלִיוֹתָיו, its branches.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:4)


**אֵלָיו.** אֶל מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל:


**to him** to the king of Babylon.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:5)


**תַּחְתָּיו.** תַּחַת מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל:


**under him** under the king of Babylon


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:6)


**בַּדִּים.** מוֹטוֹת:


**branches** Heb. בַּדִים,staves.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:7)


**וַתְּשַׁלַּח.** וַתִּגְדַּל:


**and sent forth** It grew.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:6:8)


**פֹּארוֹת.** עֲנָפִים רַבִּים:


**boughs** many leaves.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:7:1)


**וַיְהִי נֶשֶׁר אֶחָד.** מֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם:


**Now there was one...eagle** The king of Egypt.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:7:2)


**כָּפְנָה שָׁרֳשֶׁיהָ.** לְשׁוֹן רְעָבוֹן, כְּמוֹ ״לְשֹׁד וּלְכָפָן״ (איוב ה:כב); רָעֲבוּ וְנִתְאַוּוּ שָׁרְשֵׁי הַגֶּפֶן הַזֹּאת לְאוֹתוֹ הַנֶּשֶׁר. וְיֵשׁ מַשְׁמָע **כָּפְנָה** כְּמוֹ ׳אָסְפָה׳, וְהוּא הָפוּךְ; וּבְדִבְרֵי רַבּוֹתֵינוּ יֵשׁ מִן הַדּוֹמֶה לוֹ, שֶׁקּוֹרִין לַאֲסִיפַת הָעָם ״כְּנוּפְיָא״ (שבת ס:); וְכָפְנָה הוּא כְּמוֹ כָּנְפָה; אמאש״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**gathered its roots** Heb. כָּפְנָה, an expression of hunger, like (Job 5:22): “plunder and famine (וּלְכָפָן).” The roots of this vine hungered and longed for that eagle. (Addendum:) Some explain כָּפְנָה like אָסְפָה, gathered, the letters [of כָּפְנָה] being transposed. In the words of our Sages there is something similar to it, for they call a public gathering כְּנוּפְיָא. כָּפְנָה is like כָּנְפָה, amassa in French, brought in, gathered.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:7:3)


**וְדָלִיּוֹתָיו שִׁלְּחָה לוֹ.** וּשְׁלוּחֶיהָ שִׁלְּחָה לוֹ לָבֹא אֵלֶיהָ וּ**לְהַשְׁקוֹת** אוֹתָהּ:


**and its tendrils it sent forth to him** And her emissaries she sent to him, to come to her and water her.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:7:4)


**מֵעֲרֻגוֹת מַטָּעָהּ.** מֵהָעוּגִיּוֹת מַיִם (משנה מועד קטן א:א) שֶׁעָשָׂה לוֹ הַנֶּשֶׁר הָרִאשׁוֹן:


**from the furrows of its planting** From the water ditches that the first eagle had made for it.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:8:1)


**אֶל שָׂדֶה טוֹב וְגוֹ׳.** כְּלוֹמַר: וְעַל חִנָּם עָשְׂתָה זֹאת, כִּי **אֶל שָׂדֶה טוֹב הָיְתָה שְׁתוּלָה**:


**In a good field, etc.** i.e., she did this for no purpose, because she had been planted in a good field.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:8:2)


**לַגֶּפֶן אַדָּרֶת.** ״לַגֶּפֶן תַּקִּיף״ (תרגום יונתן):


**a sturdy vine** Heb. אַדָרֶת, a sturdy vine.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:1)


**אֱמֹר.** כָּל הָאַלְפָּא בֵּיתָא בְּמִקְרָא זֶה, וְתָמֵיהַּ אֲנִי כִּי אֵינוֹ נִמְנֶה בַּמָּסוֹרֶת:


**Say** The entire Hebrew alphabet is found in this verse, and I am puzzled that it is not listed in the Masorah.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:2)


**יְנַתֵּק.** הַנֶּשֶׁר הָרִאשׁוֹן (לעיל, ג):


**[Will he not] pull up its roots** The first eagle.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:3)


**אֶת פִּרְיָהּ יְקוֹסֵס.** כְּמוֹ יְקוֹצֵץ, הֵם בְּנֵי צִדְקִיָּהוּ שֶׁשְּׁחָטָן לְעֵינָיו (מלכים ב כה:ז):


**and cut off its fruits** Heb. יְקוֹסֵס, like יְקוֹצֵץ, will cut off. They are Zedekiah’s sons, whom he slaughtered before his eyes.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:4)


**טַרְפֵּי.** עֲלֵי, פוייליי״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the leaves of** Heb. טַרְפֵי, leaves, feuilles in French.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:5)


**וְלֹא בִזְרוֹעַ גְּדוֹלָה וּבְעַם רָב.** יָבֹא הַנֶּשֶׁר הַשֵּׁנִי (לעיל, ז) לְעֶזְרָתָהּ:


**And not with great power and many people** will the second eagle come to their assistance.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:9:6)


**לְמַשְׂאוֹת אוֹתָהּ מִשָּׁרָשֶׁיהָ.** לְנֹכַח הָעוֹקְרוֹת וּמְסִיעוֹת אוֹתָהּ מִשָּׁרָשֶׁיהָ:


**against those who rip it out by its roots** Against those who uproot and pull it out by its roots.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:10:1)


**וְהִנֵּה.** עַתָּה שְׁתוּלָה כִּרְצוֹנָהּ:


**And behold** now it is planted as they wished.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:10:2)


**הֲתִצְלָח.** אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁתִּצְלַח זוֹ, שֶׁבָּגְדָה בְּמִי שֶׁנְּטָעָהּ רִאשׁוֹן?!


**will it prosper** Is it possible that it will prosper, this one that betrayed the one who planted it originally?



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:12:1)


**מָה אֵלֶּה.** מָה הַחִידָה הַזֹּאת; וּפֵירְשָׁהּ לָהֶם:


**what these are** What this riddle is, and he explained it to them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:12:2)


**וַיִּקַּח אֶת מַלְכָּהּ וְאֶת שָׂרֶיהָ.** הֵם **צַמֶּרֶת הָאֶרֶז**, יְהוֹיָקִים וְשָׂרָיו:


**took away its king and its princes** They are the “lofty top of the cedar,” Jehoiakim and his princes.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:12:3)


**בָּבֶלָה.** הִיא **עִיר הָרוֹכְלִים**:


**to Babylon** That is the “city of traffickers.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:13:1)


**מִזֶּרַע הַמְּלוּכָה.** צִדְקִיָּהוּ:


**of the royal seed** Zedekiah.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:13:2)


**אֵילֵי הָאָרֶץ.** שָׂרֵי הָאָרֶץ:


**the mighty of the land** The princes of the land.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:14:1)


**לְעָמְדָהּ.** לְמַעַן יִתְקַיֵּים:


**that it might stand** That it might endure.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:17:1)


**בִּשְׁפֹּךְ סוֹלְלָה.** כְּשֶׁיָּבֹא נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר וְיִשְׁפֹּךְ סוֹלְלָה עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם:


**when he cast up mounds** When Nebuchadnezzar comes and casts up mounds against Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:17:2)


**לְהַכְרִית נְפָשׁוֹת רַבּוֹת.** לַהֲרוֹג אֶת בְּנֵי הָעִיר:


**to cut off many souls** To slay the people of the city.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:18:1)


**נָתַן יָדוֹ.** הִבְטִיחַ בֶּאֱמוּנָה, וְנָשָׂא יָדוֹ בִּ**בְרִית**:


**he gave his hand** He promised in good faith and raised his hand in a covenant.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:19:1)


**וּנְתַתִּיו בְּרֹאשׁוֹ.** הוּא סִימּוּי עֵינָיו (מלכים ב כה:ז):


**I will bring it upon his head** [lit. put it in his head.] That is the blinding of his eyes.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:20:1)


**וּפָרַשְׂתִּי עָלָיו רִשְׁתִּי.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּירַשְׁתִּיו לְמַעְלָה בִּתְחִלַּת הַסֵּפֶר (יחזקאל יב:יג), שֶׁזִּמֵּן צְבִי לַהֲלוֹךְ עַל גַּג הַמְּעָרָה:


**And I will spread My net over him** as I explained above at the beginning of the Book (12:13): that He set up a deer to walk on the roof of the cave.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:20:2)


**וְנִשְׁפַּטְתִּי.** וְאֶתְוַכַּח עִמּוֹ:


**and contend** And I will debate with him.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:21:1)


**וְאֵת כָּל מִבְרָחָיו.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״וְיָת כָּל גִּבָּרוֹהִי״; וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ עִם ״בְּרַח דּוֹדִי״ (שיר השירים ח:יד); וְכֹה פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: וְאֵת כָּל הַבּוֹרְחִים:


**And all his fugitives** Heb. מִבְרָחָיו. According to Targum, and all his mighty men. But Menachem (p. 48) associated it with (Song 8:10): “Flee (בְּרַח) my beloved,” and this is its interpretation: and all those who fled.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:21:2)


**בְּכָל אֲגַפָּיו.** בְּכָל כַּנְפֵיהֶם, כְּלוֹמַר בְּכָל כֹּחָם, **בַּחֶרֶב יִפֹּלוּ**:


**with all his wings** Heb. אַגַפָּיו with all their wings, i.e., with all their strength they will fall by the sword.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:21:3)


**יִפָּרֵשׂוּ.** שרונאפנדו״ן בְּלַעַ״ז:


**will disperse** Seront repandus, will be scattered.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:22:1)


**וְנָתַתִּי.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ ״וַאֲקַיְּמִינֵיהּ״. **וְלָקַחְתִּי אֲנִי** אֶת מֶלֶךְ הַמָּשִׁיחַ:


**and I will place [it]** As the Targum renders: and I will establish him. **And I Myself will take** And I will take the King Messiah.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:22:2)


**רַךְ אֶקְטֹף.** אֵין דֶּרֶךְ לְהַרְכִּיב, אֶלָּא מִן הָרַךְ שֶׁגָּדַל אֶשְׁתָּקַד, שֶׁקּוֹרִין ׳גְּרַפְיָיא׳ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a tender one** It is customary to graft only from the tender [sprigs] that grew the previous year, called grayfe in Old French, a shoot, graft.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:22:3)


**וְתָלוּל.** לְשׁוֹן ״תֵּל״ (דברים יג:יז), שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ תֵּל לִהְיוֹת גָּבוֹהַּ וְתָלוּל; אמונצלי״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and lofty** Heb. וְתָלוּל, from the word mound, תֵּל, for a mound is usually high and lofty; amonceler in French, to heap up.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:23:1)


**תִּשְׁכֹּנָּה.** דַּגְשׁוּת הַנּוּ״ן מְשַׁמֵּשׁ תַּחַת נוּ״ן שְׁנִיָּה, כְּאִלּוּ כָּתוּב תִּשְׁכֹּנְנָה:


**shall they dwell** Heb. תִּשְּׁכֹּנָה. The ‘dagesh’ in the ‘nun’ serves instead of a second ‘nun,’ as though it were written תִּשּׁכֹּנְנָה.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:24:1)


**הִשְׁפַּלְתִּי עֵץ גָּבֹהַּ.** אֶת הַכַּשְׂדִּים שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹשְׁלִים בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


**I...have lowered the high tree** The nations that were ruling over Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:24:2)


**הוֹבַשְׁתִּי עֵץ לָח.** צִדְקִיָּהוּ וְזַרְעוֹ:


**I have dried up the green tree** Zedekiah and his seed.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 17:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/17:24:3)


**וְהִפְרַחְתִּי עֵץ יָבֵשׁ.** יְהוֹיָכִין שֶׁגָּלָה בְּלֹא בָנִים (מלכים ב כד:טו-טז) יוֹלִיד בְּבָבֶל אֶת זְרֻבָּבֶל, וְהוּא יִהְיֶה פַּחַת יְהוּדָה לִמְשׁוֹל (חגי א:א; דברי הימים א ג:יז):


**and have made the dry tree blossom** Jehoiachin, who was exiled without children, will beget Zerubbabel in Babylon, and he will be the governor of Judea, [appointed] to rule.



## Chapter 18


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:2:1)


**וַיְהִי דְבַר ה׳ אֵלַי לֵאמֹר. מַה לָּכֶם אַתֶּם מֹשְׁלִים אֶת הַמָּשָׁל.** אשנפלאנ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**And the word of the Lord came to me, saying:** **What do you mean that you use this parable** Asanplanz in Old French, comparison, parable.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:2:2)


**אָבוֹת יֹאכְלוּ בֹסֶר.** פְּרִי עַד שֶׁלֹּא נִתְבַּשֵּׁל, בֵּיירְגוּ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**The fathers have eaten sour grapes** Heb. בוֹסֶר, fruit before it has ripened. Verjus in—French, verjuice.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:2:3)


**וְשִׁנֵּי הַבָּנִים תִּקְהֶינָה.** אגאצירונ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, כָּךְ דַּרְכּוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: הָאָבוֹת חוֹטְאִים וְהַבָּנִים לוֹקִים, שֶׁהֲרֵי כַּמָּה שָׁנִים חָטְאוּ מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל קֹדֶם שֶׁגָּלוּ, וְאַף אָנוּ לֹא נִדְאַג לִלְקוֹת בַּעֲוֹנֵינוּ:


**and the children’s teeth are set on edge** Heb. תִּקְהֶינָה, agazeront in Old French, will be set on edge. So is the way of the Holy One, blessed be He: the fathers sin and the children are punished, for the kings of Israel sinned for many years before they were exiled, and we too need not be concerned that we shall be punished for our iniquities.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:3:1)


**מְשׁוֹל.** כְּמוֹ לִמְשׁוֹל:


**shall no longer use this parable** Heb. מְשֹּׁל, like לִמְשֹּׁל [the infinitive].



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:4:1)


**כָּל הַנְּפָשׁוֹת לִי הֵנָּה.** וְלָמָּה יִלְקֶה הַבֵּן שֶׁלֹּא חָטָא לִי?! הֲלֹא שֶׁלִּי הוּא!


**Behold, all souls are Mine** So why should the son who did not sin against Me suffer? Is he not Mine?



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:6:1)


**אֶל הֶהָרִים לֹא אָכַל.** ״בְּטוּרַיָּא לֹא פְּלַח לְטָעֲוָותָא״ (תרגום יונתן), לֶאֱכוֹל זִבְחֵי מֵתִים:


**And does not eat on the mountains** “On the mountains, he did not worship pagan deities” [Targum Jonathan] by eating “sacrifices of the dead.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:6:2)


**אֶל גִּלּוּלֵי.** שְׁאָר עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים, כְּגוֹן הַבַּעַל שֶׁבְּשׁוֹמְרוֹן וְהָעֲגָלִים שֶׁבְּבֵית אֵל וְשֶׁבְּדָן (מלכים א יב:כט):


**to the idols** Referring to other idolatry, e.g., Baal in Samaria and the calves in Bethel and Dan.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:7:1)


(**יוֹנֶה.** לְשׁוֹן אוֹנָאָה כְּלוֹמַר שׁוּם **אִישׁ לֹא** הוֹנָה):


**wrongs** Heb. יוֹנֶה, an expression of wronging (אוֹנָאָה) ; i.e., he wronged no man.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:7:2)


**חֲבוֹלָתוֹ חוֹב יָשִׁיב.** אִם חָבַל שַׂלְמַת רֵעֵהוּ בְּחוֹבוֹ, יָשִׁיב אוֹתָהּ לוֹ כְּבֹא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ (שמות כב:כה):


**what has been pledged for a debt he returns** If he took his friend’s garment as a pledge for a loan, he would return it to him at sunset.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:10:1)


**וְעָשָׂה אָח מֵאַחַד מֵאֵלֶּה.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְיַעֲבִד לַאֲחוֹהִי מֵחֲדָא מֵאִילֵּין״:


**and he commits upon his brother any of these** Heb. אָח. Jonathan renders: and he does to his brother any one of these.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:11:1)


**וְהוּא אֶת כָּל אֵלֶּה.** אֶת כָּל הַטּוֹבוֹת הָאֵלֶּה, שֶׁעָשָׂה אָבִיו:


**And he does not do all these** All these good deeds that his father did.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:12:1)


**תּוֹעֵבָה עָשָׂה.** מִשְׁכַּב זָכוּר (ויקרא כ:יג):


**committed abomination** Homosexual relations.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:14:1)


**וַיִּרְאֶה וְלֹא יַעֲשֶׂה כָּהֵן.** רָאָה בְּלִבּוֹ שֶׁאֵין טוֹב לַעֲשׂוֹת כֵּן, אִיאַפְרְצוֹיְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and sees and does not do likewise** He sees with his heart that it is not good to do so, et il apercut in French, he realized.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:17:1)


**מֵעָנִי הֵשִׁיב יָדוֹ.** מֵעֲשׂוֹת לוֹ רָעָה:


**From the poor he kept his hand back** from harming him.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:24:1)


**כָּל צִדְקוֹתָיו לֹא תִזָּכַרְנָה.** פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ: בְּמִתְחָרֵט עֲלֵיהֶם (קידושין מ:):


**All his righteous deeds, etc., shall not be remembered** Our Sages qualified this as referring to one who regrets them.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:29:1)


**לֹא יִתָּכֵן.** כְּמוֹ: לֹא יִתָּקֵן, אַפְיֵיאַטְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**is not right** Heb. יִתָּכֵן, like יִתָּקֵן, afetes in Old French; just, correct, right.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 18:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/18:30:1)


**שׁוּבוּ וְהָשִׁיבוּ.** שׁוּבוּ אַתֶּם, וְהָשִׁיבוּ אֶת אֲחֵרִים רְטוֹרְנִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**repent and cause others to repent** Heb. שּׁוּבוּ וְהָשִּׁיבוּ, you repent and you cause others to repent (tourner, retourner in French), turn [others], return.



## Chapter 19


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:2:1)


**מָה אִמְּךָ לְבִיָּא.** כַּמָּה הָיְתָה חֲשׁוּבָה אִמְּךָ, אַתָּה צִדְקִיָּהוּ, שֶׁנִּמְשְׁלָה לִלְבִיאָה:


**How was your mother a lioness!** How esteemed was your mother you, Zedekiah—that she was compared to a lioness!


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:2:2)


**בֵּין אֲרָיוֹת.** שָׂרֵי יְהוּדָה:


**Among the lions** The princes of Judah.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:3:1)


**וַתַּעַל אֶחָד מִגּוּרֶיהָ.** עַל בְּנֵי יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ נֶאֶמְרָה קִינָה זוֹ, שֶׁהָרִאשׁוֹן הוּא יְהוֹאָחָז:


**And she raised up one of her cubs** This lamentation was said regarding the sons of Josiah. The first is Jehoahaz.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:3:2)


**אָדָם אָכָל.** גּוֹזֵל אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל, כִּי יִשְׂרָאֵל נִקְרְאוּ ״אָדָם״, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְאַתֵּנָה צֹאנִי צֹאן מַרְעִיתִי אָדָם אַתֶּם״ (יחזקאל לד:לא; קהלת רבה ג:כד); וּלְפִי הַמָּשָׁל, כָּל חַיָּה שֶׁטָּעֲמָה טַעַם בְּשַׂר אָדָם מִתְגָּרֶה בָּאֲנָשִׁים לְעוֹלָם:


**he devoured men** Heb. אָדָם. He robbed Israel, for Israel is called אָדָם, man, as is stated (below 34:31): “And you are My flock, the flock of My pasture; you are men,” and according to the allegory, any beast that tasted human flesh will always provoke humans.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:4:1)


**וַיִּשְׁמְעוּ אֵלָיו גּוֹיִם.** פַּרְעֹה מֶלֶךְ מִצְרָיִם:


**Nations gathered around him** Pharaoh the king of Egypt.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:4:2)


**בְּשַׁחְתָּם נִתְפָּשׂ.** גּוּמִין חוֹפְרִין וּמְכַסִּין פִּיהֶם בְּקַשׁ, וְהַחַיָּה נוֹפֶלֶת בְּתוֹכָהּ:


**in their pit he was caught** Heb. בְּשַּׁחְתָם. They dig pits and cover their openings with straw, and the beast falls into them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:4:3)


**חַחִים.** כְּמִין טַבְּעוֹת בַּרְזֶל, נוֹקְבִין חוֹטְמוֹ וּמוֹשְׁכִין אוֹתָן בָּהֶן:


**and they brought him with hooks** A sort of iron ring. They puncture his nose and pull him with them.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:5:1)


**וַתֵּרֶא.** אִמּוֹ, כְּנֶסֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל (ראה רש״י יחזקאל יט:ב):


**Now she saw** His mother, the congregation of Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:5:2)


**כִּי נוֹחֲלָה.** לְשׁוֹן חוֹלִי:


**that she had taken ill** Heb. נוֹחַלָה, an expression of illness (חֹלִי).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:5:3)


**וַתִּקַּח אֶחָד מִגּוּרֶיהָ.** יְהוֹיָקִים:


**and she took one of her cubs** Jehoiakim.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:5:4)


**כְּפִיר שָׂמָתְהוּ.** מֶלֶךְ; כָּל כְּפִיר שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא: אֲרִי בָּחוּר וְגִבּוֹר:


**and made him a young lion** [i. e.,] a king. Every כְּפִיר in Scripture is a young and mighty lion.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:7:1)


**וַיֵּדַע אַלְמְנוֹתָיו.** מְעַנֶּה אֶת הַנָּשִׁים וְהוֹרֵג אֶת בַּעֲלֵיהֶן (ויקרא רבה יט:ו); וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִין **אַלְמְנוֹתָיו** כְּמוֹ אַרְמְנוֹתָיו. **וַיֵּדַע** לְשׁוֹן פִּגּוּר וְהֶרֶס, כְּמוֹ ״וְגָבֹהַּ מִמֶּרְחָק יְיֵדָע״ (תהלים קלח:ו):


**And he was intimate with their widows** He would rape the women and kill their husbands. Some interpret אַלְמְנוֹתָיו like אַרְמְנוֹתָיו [their palaces] and וַיֵדַע as an expression of destruction and ruin, as in (Ps. 138:6): “and He chastises (יְיֵדָע) the haughty from afar.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:7:2)


**וְעָרֵיהֶם הֶחֱרִיב.** שֶׁנָּתַן מַס עַל הָאָרֶץ לָתֵת לְפַרְעֹה בְּכָל שָׁנָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (מלכים ב כג:לה):


**and their cities he laid waste** For he levied a tax on the land to give Pharaoh annually, as it is said in II Kings (23:35).



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:8:1)


**וַיִּתְּנוּ עָלָיו גּוֹיִם סָבִיב מִמְּדִינוֹת.** ״וַיִּשְׁלַח ה׳ בּוֹ אֶת גְּדוּדֵי אֲרָם וְאֶת גְּדוּדֵי כַשְׂדִּים וְאֶת גְּדוּדֵי מוֹאָב וְאֶת גְּדוּדֵי בְנֵי עַמּוֹן״ (מלכים ב כד:ב):


**And nations gathered around against him from provinces** (II Kings 24:2): “And the Lord incited against him bands of Chaldeans, bands of Arameans, and bands of Moabites, and bands of the children of Ammon.” “And nations gathered against him.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:8:2)


**וַיִּתְּנוּ עָלָיו גּוֹיִם.** ״וְאִתְכְּנָשׁוּ״ (תרגום יונתן):


וַיִתְּנוּ [is rendered by Jonathan as:] and they gathered.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:9:1)


**בַּסּוּגַר.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״בְּקוֹלָרִין״:


**into a collar** Heb. בַּסוּגַר. Jonathan rendered: into a collar.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:9:2)


**בַּמְּצֹדוֹת.** לִהְיוֹת כָּלוּא שָׁם:


**into strongholds** to be imprisoned there.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:10:1)


**אִמְּךָ כַגֶּפֶן.** עוֹד זֶה מִן הַקִּינָה (יחזקאל יט:א):


**Your mother is like a vine** This is still part of the lamentation.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:10:2)


**בְּדָמְךָ.** לְשׁוֹן דִּמְיוֹן וּמָשָׁל; כְּלוֹמַר: דּוֹמֶה אַתָּה לְמִי שֶׁהָיְתָה אִמּוֹ **כַגֶּפֶן שְׁתוּלָה עַל מַיִם**:


**in your likeness** Heb. בְּדַמְךָ, a term denoting a likeness and an analogy; i.e., you are like one whose mother was like a vine planted by water.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:10:3)


**פּוֹרִיָּה.** כְּמוֹ ״פּוֹרֶה רֹאשׁ״ (דברים כט:יז), לְשׁוֹן עוֹשָׂה פְּרִי:


**fruitful** Heb. פֹּרִיָה, like (Deut. 29:17): “that bears (פּוֹרָה) gall,” [a word meaning:] produces fruit.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:10:4)


**מִמַּיִם רַבִּים.** עַל יְדֵי מַיִם רַבִּים:


**from many waters** Through many waters.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:11:1)


**מַטּוֹת עֹז אֶל שִׁבְטֵי מוֹשְׁלִים.** ״שָׁלְטוֹנִין תַּקִּיפִין וּמַלְכִין גִּבָּרִין״:


**sturdy rods for the scepters of the rulers** Powerful rulers and mighty kings.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:11:2)


**עֲבוֹתִים.** אִילָנוֹת שֶׁעֲנָפֵיהֶן מְרֻבִּין:


**over between the interwoven branches** The trees whose branches were many.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:12:1)


**וַתֻּתַּשׁ.** כָּל נְתִישָׁה וּנְטִישָׁה הוּא דָּבָר הַמֻּטָּל בְּפִזּוּר עַל פְּנֵי כָּל הָאָרֶץ:


**And it was scattered** Heb. וַתֻּתֳּשּׁ. Every [expression of] נְתִישָּׁה or נְטִישָּׁה means something that lies scattered upon the face of the whole earth.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:12:2)


**וְרוּחַ הַקָּדִים.** נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**and the east wind** Nebuchadnezzar.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:12:3)


**הוֹבִישׁ פִּרְיָהּ.** שָׁחַט אֶת בְּנֵי צִדְקִיָּהוּ (מלכים ב כה:ז):


**dried up its fruit** He slaughtered the sons of Zedekiah.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:14:1)


**וַתֵּצֵא אֵשׁ מִמַּטֵּה בַדֶּיהָ.** מֵעֲוֹן מְלָכֶיהָ וְשָׂרֶיהָ בָּאַת לָהּ הָרָעָה:


**And fire came out of the staff of its sturdy branches** From the iniquity of its kings and princes, the evil came to it.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 19:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/19:14:2)


**קִינָה הִיא וַתְּהִי לְקִינָה.** ״אֵילְיָא הִיא נְבוּאָתָא וּתְהֵא לְאֵילְיָא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**It is a lamentation, and it will be a lamentation** The prophecy is a lamentation, and it will be a lamentation.



## Chapter 20


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:1:1)


**לִדְרוֹשׁ אֶת ה׳.** עַל צָרְכֵיהֶם; וְאִם אֵינוֹ שׁוֹמֵעַ לָנוּ, גַּם אָנוּ לֹא נֵעָנֵשׁ עַל עֲבֵירוֹת שֶׁבְּיָדֵינוּ, שֶׁהֲרֵי כְּבָר מְכָרָנוּ וְאֵין לוֹ עָלֵינוּ כְּלוּם; עֶבֶד שֶׁמְּכָרוֹ רַבּוֹ וְאִשָּׁה שֶׁגֵּרְשָׁהּ בַּעְלָהּ, כְּלוּם יֵשׁ לָזֶה עַל זֶה כְּלוּם? וּמִסּוֹף הָעִנְיָן אַתָּה לָמֵד, מִתְּשׁוּבָה שֶׁהֵשִׁיבָם: ״וְהָעוֹלָה עַל רוּחֲכֶם וְגוֹ׳״ (לקמן, לב):


**to inquire of the Lord** concerning their needs, and if He does not listen to us, neither will we be punished for the sins in our hands, for He has already sold us, and He has nothing on us. If a master has sold his slave, or a husband has divorced his wife, does one have anything on the other? And from the conclusion of the matter you learn [this], from the reply that he gave them (verse 32): “What enters your thoughts, etc.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:3:1)


**אִם אִדָּרֵשׁ לָכֶם.** בְּבַקָּשַׁתְכֶם; וּבְסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר הַזֶּה הוּא אוֹמֵר ״אִדָּרֵשׁ לְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל״ (יחזקאל לו:לז)! זוֹ אַחַת מִן הַמְּקוֹמוֹת הַמְלַמְּדִים אוֹתָנוּ שֶׁהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא חוֹזֵר בּוֹ עַל הָרָעָה, וְעַל כַּיּוֹצֵא בָזֶה ״הַהוּא אָמַר וְלֹא יַעֲשֶׂה״ (במדבר כג:יט); מִדְרַשׁ תַּנְחוּמָא (וירא יג):


**I will not be inquired of by you!** in your request. Yet at the end of this Book (36:37): He says: “I will...be inquired of by the house of Israel.” This is one of the places that teach us that the Holy One, blessed be He, recants on evil. And regarding matters similar to this, it is said (Num. 23:19): “He says but He does not do.” Midrash of Rabbi Tanchuma (Vayera 13).



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:4:1)


**הֲתִשְׁפֹּט אוֹתָם.** לְשׁוֹן וִכּוּחַ:


**Will you contend with them** Heb. הֲתִישְׁפֹּט, an expression of contending.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:5:1)


**בְּיוֹם בָּחֳרִי בְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** הֲרֵי שֶׁהָיְתָה שִׂנְאָה זוֹ כְּבוּשָׁה לִפְנֵי הַמָּקוֹם קָרוֹב לִתְשַׁע מֵאוֹת שָׁנָה, מִשֶּׁהָיוּ בְּמִצְרַיִם עַד יְחֶזְקֵאל, וְהָיְתָה אַהֲבָתוֹ מְחַפָּה עֲלֵיהֶם; וְעַל יְדֵי שֶׁהָיוּ עַתָּה מַרְבִּים לִפְשֹׁעַ – נִתְעוֹרְרָה, וְעַל זֶה נֶאֱמַר ״שִׂנְאָה תְּעוֹרֵר מְדָנִים וְגוֹ׳״ (משלי י:יב):


**On the day I chose Israel** This hatred was pent up before the Omnipresent close to nine hundred years from [the days] that they were in Egypt until [those of] Ezekiel but His love had shielded them. Now that they had increased their transgressions, though, it was awakened, and concerning this it is stated (Prov. 10:12): “Hatred arouses quarrels [but love covers up all transgressions].”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:7:1)


**וָאֹמַר אֲלֵיהֶם.** אַהֲרֹן נִיבָּא לָהֶם נְבוּאָה זוֹ, קוֹדֶם שֶׁנִּגְלָה הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עַל מֹשֶׁה בַּסְּנֶה, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לְעֵלִי (שמואל א ב:כז) ״הֲנִגְלֹה נִגְלֵיתִי אֶל בֵּית אָבִיךָ בִּהְיוֹתָם בְּמִצְרַיִם וְגוֹ׳״ (תנחומא שמות כז):


**And I said to them** Aaron proclaimed this prophecy to them before the Holy One, blessed be He, revealed Himself to Moses in the thorn bush. This is what was said to Eli (I Sam. 2:27): “Did I appear to the house of your father when they were in Egypt, etc.?”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:8:1)


**וַיַּמְרוּ בִי.** הֵם הָרְשָׁעִים, רוּבָּם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁמֵּתוּ בִּשְׁלֹשֶׁת יְמֵי אֲפֵלָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַחֲמֻשִׁים עָלוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ׳״ (שמות יג:יח) – אֶחָד מֵחֲמִשִּׁים, וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: אֶחָד מֵחֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת (מכילתא בא יב):


**But they rebelled against Me** They are the wicked, the majority of Israel, who died in the three days of darkness, as it is said (Exod. 13:18): “and the children of Israel went out of Egypt, (חֲמֻשִּׁים)”one out of fifty, and some say, one out of five hundred.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:9:1)


**לְבִלְתִּי הֵחֵל.** שֶׁלֹּא יִתְחַלֵּל שְׁמִי; מֵאַחַר שֶׁנּוֹדַעְתִּי לָהֶם וְהִבְטַחְתִּים לְהוֹצִיאָם וְהִכִּירוּ מִצְרַיִם שֶׁהֵם עַמִּי, אִלּוּ הָיִיתִי מַשְׁחִיתָם הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים אוֹיְבֵיהֶם: מִבִּלְתִּי יְכֹלֶת בְּיָדוֹ לְהוֹצִיאָם (במדבר יד:טז):


**that it should not be desecrated** That My Name should not be desecrated; since I became known to them and I promised to take them out, and the Egyptians recognized that they are My people, if I were to destroy them their enemies would say, “Because He has not the ability to take them out.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:12:1)


**לִהְיוֹת לְאוֹת.** אוֹת גָּדוֹל הוּא לָהֶם, שֶׁנָּתַתִּי אֶת יוֹם מְנוּחָתִי לָהֶם לִמְנוּחָה; הֲרֵי הֶיכֵּר גָּדוֹל שֶׁקִּדַּשְׁתִּים לִי:


**to be for a sign** It is a great sign for them, that I gave the day of My resting to them for a resting. Behold this is a phenomenal manifestation that I hallowed them to Myself.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:13:1)


**בְּחֻקּוֹתַי לֹא הָלָכוּ.** נִסּוּ אוֹתִי בָּעֵגֶל (שמות לב) וּבִרְפִידִים (שמות יז:א-ז) מִלְּקַבֵּל אֶת הַתּוֹרָה (מכילתא בשלח מסכתא דעמלק א), וְהוֹתִירוּ מִן הַמָּן (שמות טז:כ):


**they walked not in My statutes** They tested Me with the calf, and in Rephidim [i.e., showing unwillingness] to receive the Torah, and they left over some of the manna.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:13:2)


**וְאֶת שַׁבְּתוֹתַי חִלְּלוּ.** יָצְאוּ מִן הָעָם לִלְקוֹט מָן (שמות טז:כז):


**and My Sabbaths they desecrated** Some of the people went out to gather manna.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:21:1)


**וַיַּמְרוּ בִי הַבָּנִים.** שִׁבְטוֹ שֶׁל דָּן (שופטים יח:ל-לא); וְכֵן הַנִּצְמָדִים לְבַעַל פְּעוֹר (במדבר כה:א-ה), לְאַחַר שֶׁמֵּתוּ דּוֹר הַמִּדְבָּר (דברים ב:יד-יח):


**But the children rebelled against Me** The tribe of Dan, and likewise those who became attached to Baal Peor after the generation of the wilderness had died out.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:23:1)


**לְהָפִיץ אוֹתָם.** מֵאָז הִבְטַחְתִּים לַהֲפִיצָם: ״כִּי תוֹלִיד בָּנִים וּבְנֵי בָנִים וְגוֹ׳״ (דברים ד:כה-כז):


**to scatter them** Back then, I promised them to scatter them (Deut. 4:25ff.): “When you will beget children and children’s children, etc. [the Lord will scatter you among the nations, etc.].”



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:25:1)


**נָתַתִּי לָהֶם חֻקִּים לֹא טוֹבִים.** מְסַרְתִּים בְּיַד יִצְרָם לְהִכָּשֵׁל בַּעֲוֹנָם; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּמְסַרְתִּינוּן בְּיַד יִצְרֵיהוֹן טַפְשָׁא, וַאֲזַלוּ וַעֲבַדוּ גְּזֵירָן דְּלָא תַקְנָן וְנִימוּסִין דְּלָא יִתְקַיְּמוּ בְּהוֹן״:


**statutes that were not good** I delivered them into the hands of their temptation to stumble over their iniquity, and so did Jonathan render: And I delivered them into the hands of their foolish temptation, and they went and made decrees that were not good, and practices by which they cannot live.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:26:1)


**וָאֲטַמֵּא אוֹתָם בְּמַתְּנוֹתָם.** אוֹתָם מַתָּנוֹת שֶׁחָקַקְתִּי לָהֶם, לְקַדֵּשׁ לִי כָּל בְּכוֹר (שמות יג:ב), מְסַרְתִּים בְּיַד יִצְרָם לְהַעֲבִירָם, לְאוֹתָם בְּכוֹרוֹת, לַמּוֹלֶךְ (ויקרא יח:כא); הֲרֵי: **חֻקִּים לֹא טוֹבִים**:


**And I defiled them with their gifts** Those gifts that I legislated for themto hallow for Me every firstborn—I delivered them into the hands of their temptation: to pass those firstborn to the Molech. Hence the statutes that are not good.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:26:2)


**לְמַעַן אֲשִׁמֵּם.** אֲשִׂימֵם וְאַחֲרִיב אוֹתָם, אֲשִׁמֵּם לְשׁוֹן ״שְׁמָמָה״ (יחזקאל ו:יד); וְהַמְפָרְשׁוֹ לְשׁוֹן ״אַשְׁמָה״ (ויקרא כב:טז) טוֹעֶה הוּא, שֶׁהָאָלֶ״ף אֵינָהּ מִשָּׁרְשֵׁי הַתֵּיבָה:


**in order that I lay them desolate** Heb. אֲשְּׁמֵם. I shall lay them desolate and destroy them. אֲשְּׁמֵם, is an expression of שְּׁמָמָה, desolation. Whoever explains it as an expression of אַשְּׁמָה, guilt, errs, because the ‘aleph’ is not part of the radical.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:26:3)


**אֲשֶׁר יֵדְעוּ.** כְּשֶׁאָבִיא עֲלֵיהֶם הָרָעָה:


**that they know** when I will bring upon them the evil.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:27:1)


**עוֹד זֹאת.** הוֹסִיפוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת כְּשֶׁהֲבִיאוֹתִים אֶל הָאָרֶץ:


**this too** they continued to do when I brought them into the land.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:29:1)


**וַיִּקָּרֵא שְׁמָהּ בָּמָה.** לְשׁוֹן גְּנַאי, כְּלוֹמַר: בַּמֶּה הִיא נֶחְשֶׁבֶת?


**So its name was called Bamah** A pejorative: i.e., of what (בַּמֶה) importance is it?



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:30:1)


**הַבְּדֶרֶךְ אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם וגו׳.** לְשׁוֹן תֵּימַהּ: וְכִי כָּךְ הָגוּן, שֶׁ**בְּדֶרֶךְ אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם אַתֶּם נִטְמְאִים**, וַאֲנִי אֶדָּרֵשׁ לָכֶם לְהָשִׁיב עַל שְׁאֵלַתְכֶם?!


**in the way of your forefathers, etc.** [This is] a rhetorical question. Is this proper, that you should have defiled yourselves in the way of your forefathers, and I should be inquired of by you to reply to your question?



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:31:1)


**וּבִשְׂאֵת מַתְּנוֹתֵיכֶם.** וּבִלְקִיחַת מַתְּנוֹתֵיכֶם, שֶׁהָיָה לָכֶם לָתֵת לִי, וְאַתֶּם לוֹקְחִים אוֹתָם מִמֶּנִּי לְהַעֲבִיר בְּכוֹרוֹתֵיכֶם לַעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָָבִים:


**And when you take your gifts** And with the taking of your gifts that you should have given Me, and you take them from Me to cause your firstborn to pass to idols.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:32:1)


**וְהָעוֹלָה עַל רוּחֲכֶם.** שֶׁעוֹלֶה עַל דַּעְתְּכֶם:


**But what enters your mind** Heb. והעלה על-רוחכם [lit. what ascends upon your spirit], what ascends upon your mind.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:32:2)


**נִהְיֶה כַגּוֹיִם.** נִפְרוֹק עֻלּוֹ מֵעַל צַוָּארֵנוּ, מֵאַחַר שֶׁשִּׁלְּחָנוּ מֵעָלָיו (תנחומא, נצבים ג):


**‘Let us be like the nations’** Let us break off His yoke from our necks since He sent us away from Him.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:32:3)


**הָיוֹ לֹא תִהְיֶה.** כִּי כְּבָר קִבַּלְתֶּם עֲלֵיכֶם מִדַּעְתְּכֶם בִּימֵי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (יהושע כד:א) ״וַיֶּאֱסֹף יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אֶת כָּל שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל וְכָל הָעִנְיָן״:


**shall not come about** For you already accepted upon yourselves of your own free will in the days of Joshua, as it is written (Josh. 24:1): “And Joshua gathered all the tribes of Israel,” and the entire episode.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:35:1)


**מִדְבַּר הָעַמִּים.** הוּא הַמִּדְבָּר שֶׁהָלְכוּ בּוֹ אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה (במדבר לב:יג); וְכֵן נִיבָּא הוֹשֵׁעַ ״וְהוֹלַכְתִּיהָ הַמִּדְבָּר״ (הושע ב:טז):


**the wilderness of the peoples** That is the wilderness through which they [the children of Israel] traveled for forty years, and so did Hosea prophesy (2:16): “and I will...lead her into the desert.” That is the desert of Sihon and Og. Ezekiel called it the wilderness of the peoples because many nations bordered upon it.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:37:1)


**תַּחַת הַשָּׁבֶט.** שֶׁתִּהְיוּ כְּפוּפִים לִי וּלְמוּסָרִי:


**under the rod** That you shall be subordinate to Me and to My chastisement.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:37:2)


**בְּמָסֹרֶת הַבְּרִית.** בַּבְּרִית שֶׁמָּסַרְתִּי לָכֶם:


**into the transmission of the covenant** The covenant that I transmitted to you.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:38:1)


**וּבָרוֹתִי.** לְשׁוֹן בְּרִירָה:


**And I shall separate** Heb. וּבָרוֹתִי, an expression of ברר [selecting, separating].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:38:2)


**מֵאֶרֶץ מְגוּרֵיהֶם.** מֵאֶרֶץ גָּלוּתָם **אוֹצִיא אוֹתָם**, וַאֲמִיתֵם בַּמִּדְבָּר:


**from the land of their sojournings** From the land of their exile I shall take them and slay them in the wilderness.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:39:1)


**לְכוּ עֲבֹדוּ.** וְאַל תַּרְאוּ לָאוּמּוֹת שֶׁאַתֶּם נִקְרָאִים עַל שְׁמִי בְּבוֹאֲכֶם אֶל הַנָּבִיא לִשְׁאוֹל לוֹ תּוֹרָה, וּמֵאַחַר שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר לָכֶם דְּבָרַי, **אֵינְכֶם שֹׁמְעִים אֵלָי**, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְהִנְּךָ לָהֶם כְּשִׁיר עֲגָבִים וְגוֹ׳, שׁוֹמְעִים אֶת דְּבָרֶיךָ וְאֵינָם עוֹשִׂים אוֹתָם״ (יחזקאל לג:לב):


**go worship** and do not show the nations that you are called by My Name by coming to the prophet to inquire of him [about the] Torah, and, after he tells you My words, not hearkening to Me, as the matter that is stated (33:32): “And behold you are to them like the song of the flutes, etc.” They hear your words, but they do not carry them out.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:39:2)


**בְּמַתְּנוֹתֵיכֶם וּבְגִלּוּלֵיכֶם.** שֶׁאַתֶּם מַפְרִישִׁים הַבְּכוֹרוֹת בְּשֵׁם בְּכוֹרָה, כַּאֲשֶׁר צִוִּיתִי אֶתְכֶם (שמות יג:יא-טז), וּמִשֶּׁנִּקְרָא שְׁמִי עַל הַדָּבָר, אַתֶּם מַקְרִיבִים אוֹתָם לַעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים:


**with your gifts and with your idols** For you separate the first born to be designated as a firstborn as I commanded you, but once My Name is called upon the thing, you sacrifice them to idols.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:40:1)


**מַשְׂאוֹתֵיכֶם.** כְּמוֹ מִשְׁאֲרוֹתֵיכֶם, לְשׁוֹן מָזוֹן, כְּמוֹ ״שְׁאֵרָהּ כְּסוּתָהּ וְעֹנָתָהּ לֹא יִגְרָע״ (שמות כא:י); וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**and the first of your food** Heb. מַשְׂאוֹתֵיכֶם, like מִשְׁאַרוֹתֵיכֶם your kneading troughs, an expression of food, like (Exod. 21:10): “her food (שְּׁאֵרָה), her clothing, or her conjugal rights shall not be diminished.” Menachem also (p. 168) associated it in this manner.



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 20:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/20:43:1)


**וּנְקֹטֹתֶם.** לְשׁוֹן קְטָטָה; לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר – כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״וְתִידַנְקוּן״, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״הַנֶּאֱנָחִים״ (יחזקאל ט:ד); דְּמוֹנִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and you will quarrel** Heb. וּנְקֹטֹתֶם, an expression of quarreling (קְטָטָה). Another explanation, following the Targum: וְתִּידַנְקוּן, and so does Jonathan render (9:4) הַנֶאֱנָחִים: “and those who groan,” demonir in Old French, to melt away.



## Chapter 21


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:2:1)


**דֶּרֶךְ תֵּימָנָה.** אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, בִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל בָּבֶל:


**southward** The land of Israel is south of Babylon.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:2:2)


**אֶל יַעַר הַשָּׂדֶה.** אֶל בֵּית מִקְדָּשִׁי, שֶׁעָתִיד לִהְיוֹת כְּיַעַר וּכְשָׂדֶה, לְהֵחָרֵשׁ, לְהַלֵּךְ בּוֹ שׁוּעָלִים (מיכה ג:יח; איכה ה:יח):


**to the forest of the field in the south** To My Temple, which is destined to be like a forest and like a field, to be plowed in order to be overrun by foxes.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:3:1)


**וְנִצְרְבוּ.** לְשׁוֹן ״צָרֶבֶת הַשְּׁחִין״ (ויקרא יג:כג), ״עַל שְׂפָתוֹ כְּאֵשׁ צָרָבֶת״ (משלי טז:כז); לְשׁוֹן כְּוִיָּה:


**shall be scorched** Heb. וְנִצְרְבוּ, an expression related to (Lev. 13:23): “a scar (צָרֶבֶת) from the boil”; (Prov. 16:27), “and on his lips is a kind of searing (צָרֶבֶת) fire,” an expression of a burn.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:3:2)


**כָּל פָּנִים מִנֶּגֶב צָפוֹנָה.** כָּל הָאֲנָשִׁים שֶׁבִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל בָּבֶל, שֶׁהִיא בַּצָּפוֹן. לְשׁוֹן צָרַב – רַטְרֵיי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**all faces from the south to the north** All the people south of Babylon, which is to the north. וְנִצְרְבוּ, shall be scorched, comes from צרב (as Lev. 13:2), retrere in Old French, to shrivel up.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:5:1)


**מְמַשֵּׁל מְשָׁלִים הוּא.** כְּשֶׁאֲנִי מִתְנַבֵּא לָהֶם בִּלְשׁוֹן מָשָׁל וְחִידָה, כְּגוֹן: יַעַר וְאֵשׁ אוֹכֶלֶת לַח וְיָבֵשׁ (יחזקאל כ:מז):


**‘Is he not an inventor of parables’** when I prophesy to them in language of parable and riddle, such as a forest and a fire consuming [trees] moist and dry.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:6:1)


**וַיְהִי דְבַר ה׳ אֵלַי לֵאמֹר.** הִנָּבֵא לָהֶם נְבוּאָה מְפֹרֶשֶׁת; שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לְךָ ״שִׂים פָּנֶיךָ דֶּרֶךְ תֵּימָנָה״ (יחזקאל כ:מו), אֲנִי מְפָרֵשׁ לְךָ: **שִׂים פָּנֶיךָ אֶל יְרוּשָׁלִַם**:


**Then the word of the Lord came to me, saying** Prophesy to them a clear prophecy. What I said to you, “Set your face southward,” I explain to you: “Set your face toward Jerusalem.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:7:1)


**וְהַטֵּף.** וְהִנָּבֵא; וְכֵן ״אַל תַּטִּיפוּ״ (מיכה ב:ו) ״וְאַל תַּטִּיף עַל בֵּית יִשְׂחָק״ (עמוס ז:טז):


**and prophesy** Heb. וְהַטֵף, and prophesy. Similarly (Micah 2:6): “Preach (תַטִיפוּ) not”; (Amos 7: 16), “and do not prophesy (תַטִיף) concerning the house of Isaac.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:7:2)


**אֶל מִקְדָּשִׁים.** הוּא ״יַעַר הַשָּׂדֶה״ (יחזקאל כא:ב):


**about the sanctuaries** That is the forest of the field.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:7:3)


**מִקְדָּשִׁים.** שְׁנֵי חֻרְבָּנִים:


**Sanctuaries** denotes the two destructions.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:8:1)


**צַדִּיק וְרָשָׁע.** הוּא הַ״לַּח״ וְהַ״יָּבֵשׁ״ (יחזקאל כ:מז):


**righteous and wicked** That is “the moist and the dry.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:9:1)


**יַעַן אֲשֶׁר הִכְרַתִּי מִמֵּךְ.** וְיָדַעְתִּי כִּי יִשְׂמְחוּ הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים לְאֵידְכֶם, אֶקְצֹף עֲלֵיהֶם וַאֲגָרֶה אֶת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר בְּכֻלָּם; הוּא שֶׁכָּתַבְתִּי ״וְנִצְרְבוּ בָהּ כָּל פָּנִים״ הַדְּרוֹמִיִּים (יחזקאל כ:ג):


**Since I cut off from you** and I knew that the nations would rejoice at your misfortune, I shall be wroth with them and incite Nebuchadnezzar against all of them, and that is what I wrote, “and all faces from the south will be scorched.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:14:1)
