**חֶרֶב חֶרֶב.** שְׁתֵּי חֲרָבוֹת: חֶרֶב מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל רִאשׁוֹנָה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ חֶרֶב בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן בְּיַד יִשְׁמָעֵאל בֶּן נְתַנְיָה, שֶׁיַּהֲרֹג אֶת גְּדַלְיָה וְאֶת הַנֶּאֱסָפִים אֵלָיו מִשְּׁאֵרִית הַחֶרֶב, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּיִרְמְיָהוּ (ירמיהו מא:ב-ג):


**A sword, a sword** Two swords: the sword of the king of Babylon first, and afterwards the sword of the children of Ammon in the hand of Ishmael the son of Nethaniah, who will slay Gedaliah and those gathered to him of the survivors of the sword, as is delineated in Jeremiah (41:1-10).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:14:2)


**מְרוּטָה.** פּוֹרְבִּיאָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז. מְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן שְׁלִיפָה, וְכֵן ״וַיִּתֵּן אוֹתָהּ לְמָרְטָה״ (יחזקאל כא:טז), וְכֵן כֻּלָּם:


**furbished** Heb. מְרוּטָה, forbide in Old French, furbished, polished. Menachem (p. 120), however, associated it as an expression of drawing. Likewise (verse 16): “and He gave it to draw (לְמָרְטָה),” and so all of them.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:15:1)


**הֱיֵה לָהּ בָּרָק.** לִהְיוֹת לָהּ בָּרָק, זַהֲרוּרִית, פלנדור״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**that it may glitter** Heb. בָּרָק, to have a glitter, a shine, flandours, a flash, flashing (as in Deut 32: 41).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:15:2)


**אוֹ נָשִׂישׂ.** לְשׁוֹן תְּמִיהָּ הוּא: שֶׁמָּא יֵשׁ לָנוּ לִשְׂמוֹחַ עוֹד?


**Shall we rejoice?** This is a rhetorical question. Do we have anything more to be happy about?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:15:3)


**שֵׁבֶט בְּנִי מֹאֶסֶת כָּל עֵץ.** שֵׁבֶט שֶׁאֲנִי מַכֶּה בָּהּ אֶת בְּנִי, לְיַסְּרוֹ קָשֶׁה מִכָּל הַשְּׁבָטִים, וּמוֹאֶסֶת הִיא מַכַּת כָּל מַטֶּה וְכָל עֵץ, כִּי אֵינָם אֶצְלָהּ לִכְלוּם:


**The rod for My son disdains every stick** The rod with which I strike My son to chastise him is the hardest of all rods, and it disdains the blow of every staff and every stick, for they are like nothing to it.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:16:1)


**וַיִּתֵּן אוֹתָהּ.** אֶת הַשֵּׁבֶט:


**Then he had it** the rod.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:16:2)


**לְמָרְטָה לִתְפֹּשׂ בַּכָּף.** לְצַחְצְחָהּ, שֶׁתְּהֵא נוֹחָה וַהֲגוּנָה לְתָפְשָׂהּ בַּכָּף:


**furbished to grasp in the hand** To furbish it so that it be easy and fit to grasp with the hand.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:16:3)


**הִיא הוּחַדָּה חֶרֶב.** הִיא, הַשֵּׁבֶט, חֶרֶב חַדָּה וּמְרוּטָה הִיא, **לָתֵת אוֹתָהּ בְּיַד** נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, **לְהָרֹג** בָּהּ:


**that is the sword that was sharpened** That is the rod; it is a sharp and furbished sword, to give it into the hand of Nebuchadnezzar to slay with it.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:17:1)


**וְהֵילֵל.** לְשׁוֹן יְלָלָה:


**and wail** Heb. וְהֵילֵל, a term for wailing (יְלָלָה).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:17:2)


**כִּי הִיא.** הַחֶרֶב, תִּהְיֶה **בְעַמִּי**, וּ**בְכָל** נְשִׂיאֵיהֶם וְשָׂרֵיהֶם:


**for it** The sword will be upon My people and upon their princes and officers.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:17:3)


**מְגוּרֵי אֶל חֶרֶב.** קְבוּצוֹת עַמִּים בָּאִים לַחֶרֶב הָיוּ סְבִיבוֹת עַמִּי, כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן. **מְגוּרֵי** לְשׁוֹן ״אוֹגֵר בַּקַּיִץ בֵּן מַשְׂכִּיל״ (משלי י:ה), ״אָגְרָה בַקָּצִיר״ (משלי ו:ח), ״נֶהֶרְסוּ מַמְּגוּרוֹת״ (יואל א:יז), ״הַעוֹד הַזֶּרַע בַּמְּגוּרָה״ (חגי ב:יט). וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן יִרְאָה, כְּמוֹ ״וַיָּגָר מוֹאָב״ (במדבר כב:ג):


**those gathered to the sword** Heb. מְגוּרֵי, a gathering of nations coming to the sword were around My people. So did Jonathan render מְגוּרֵי, related to the word in (Prov. 10: 5): “An intelligent son gathers (אוֹגֵר) in the summer”; (ibid. 6:8), “She gathers (אָגְרָה) her food in the harvest”; (Joel 1:17), “garners (מַמְּגֻרוֹת) are demolished”; (Haggai 2:19), “The seed is still in the granary (בַּמְגוּרָה).” Menachem, however, connected it to the word for fear, like (Num. 22:3): “And Moab became terrified (וַיָגָר).”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:17:4)


**לָכֵן סְפֹק אֶל יָרֵךְ.** לְנֶהִי וְלִקִינָה:


**therefore, clap upon your thigh** as a wailing and a lament.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:1)


**כִּי בֹחַן.** כִּי בֹחַן רַב, בְּצָרוֹת שְׁאָר פֻּרְעָנִיּוֹת הַרְבֵּה, נִבְחֲנוּ בָהֶן: שָׁפוֹט וְדֶבֶר וְרָעָב וְחַיָּה (דברי הימים ב כ:ט):


**For a test** For a great test, with torments in many other forms of retribution, they were tested: the rod, pestilence, famine, and wild beasts.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:2)


**וּמָה אִם גַּם שֵׁבֶט מֹאֶסֶת לֹא יִהְיֶה.** וּמָה תְּהִי עוֹד עַל בְּנִי, אִם גַּם שֵׁבֶט מֹאֶסֶת זוֹ תָּבֹא עָלָיו עִם שְׁאָר הַ**בֹּחַן**:


**and what of it, if there be also a rod that disdains—He will not be** And what more will be on My son if also this disdainful rod comes upon him with the rest of the test?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:3)


**לֹא יִהְיֶה.** עוֹד בְּנִי בָּעוֹלָם; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לָא יִתְקַיְּמוּן״, אֲבָל שְׁאָר הַמִּקְרָא לֹא תִרְגֵּם כָּךְ; וְכֵן נִרְאֶה בְּעֵינַי. וְשָׁמַעְתִּי בּוֹ לְשׁוֹנוֹת רַבִּים, וְרָאִיתִי בְּסִפְרֵי פִּתְרוֹנוֹת, וְלֹא נִתְיַשְּׁבוּ לִי; וְהַפַּיָּט שֶׁיִּסֵּד בַּחֲנֻכָּה (בשבת ראשונה ביוצר): ״הַמּוֹאֶסֶת כָּל עֵץ הִדְקִיר הַמְּזַנֶּה״, מְסַיְּעֵנִי, שֶׁקָּרָא אֶת הַחֶרֶב מוֹאֶסֶת כָּל עֵץ (יחזקאל כא:טו). וְיוֹנָתָן כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם: ״אוֹ נָשִׂישׂ וְגוֹ׳״ (יחזקאל כא:טו) – ״עַל דַּחֲדִיאוּ שִׁבְטָא דְבֵית יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין לְשִׁבְטֵי דְיִשְׂרָאֵל, כַּד גְּלוֹ עַל דִּפְלָחוּ לְטָעֲוָותָא, וְתָבוּ אִינוּן לְמִטְעֵי בָּתַר צַלְמֵי אָעָא״; אַף הוּא מְפָרֵשׁ ״אוֹ נָשִׂישׂ״ לְשׁוֹן תְּמִיהָא:


**He will not be** My son will no longer be in the world. Jonathan also rendered it “they will not exist,” but the rest of the verse he did not render in this [i.e., Rashi’s] way, but so it appears to me. I heard many explanations for it, and I saw [others] in books of interpretations, but they did not appear correct to me, and the ‘payat’ who composed for Chanukah [for the morning of the first Sabbath]: “That which disdained all wood stabbed the adulterer,” supports me, for he called the sword “that which disdained all wood.” Jonathan rendered in this manner: (verse 15) אוֹ נָשִׂישׂ. because the tribe of the house of Judah and Benjamin rejoiced at the tribes of Israel when they were exiled for worshipping idols, and they returned to stray after wooden images. He, too, explains אוֹ נָשִׂישׂ as a rhetorical question.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:4)


**כִּי בֹחַן וּמָה אִם גַּם שֵׁבֶט מֹאֶסֶת לֹא יִהְיֶה.** ״אֲרִי אִתְנַבִּיאוּ עֲלֵיהוֹן נְבִיָּיא וְלָא תָבוּ, כְּלוֹמַר: כְּבָר הַדָּבָר בָּחוּן שֶׁלֹּא יָשׁוּבוּ, וּמָה יְהֵא בְּסוֹפֵיהוֹן?״ (תרגום יונתן):


**For a test** and what if also a rod that disdains He will not be [Jonathan renders:] “For the prophets prophesied about them but they did not repent.” That is to say, the matter is already determined that they will not repent. And what will be their end?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:5)


**אִם גַּם שֵׁבֶט מֹאֶסֶת לֹא יִהְיֶה.** ״אָמַר וְאַף שִׁבְטַיָּא דְּבֵית יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִין מִגְלָא יִגְלוֹן וּבְעוֹבְדֵיהוֹן בִּישָׁא לֹא יִתְקַיְּמוּן״: מַלְכוּת שֵׁבֶט שֶׁהָיְתָה מוֹאֶסֶת בַּחֲבֵרְתָּהּ וּמְלַעֶגֶת עֲלֵיהֶם וְעַל הָרָעָה הַבָּאָה עֲלֵיהֶם, אַף הִיא לֹא תִהְיֶה וְלֹא תָקוּם. עוֹד אֲפָרְשֶׁנּוּ לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר **אוֹ נָשִׂישׂ וְגוֹמֵר** אוֹ שֶׁמָּא נָשִׂישׂ כִּי תֹאמַר אֵלַי שֵׁבֶט בְּנִי מֹאֶסֶת כָּל עֵץ, מַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה מוֹאֲסֵי עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים הֵם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמָּצִינוּ בְּאַבִיָּה שֶׁאָמַר (דברי הימים ב יג:ח) ״וְעִמָּכֶם עֶגְלֵי הַזָּהָב״, וְאָסָא וִיהוֹשָׁפָט וְחִזְקִיָּה וְיֹאשִׁיָּהוּ שֶׁבִּעֲרוּ כָּל עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים שֶׁבָּעוֹלָם שֶׁתַּחַת יָדָם, וְאַף אֵלּוּ יָשׁוּבוּ בִּתְשׁוּבָה:


**if also a disdainful rod? He will not be** He [Jonathan] says: “And also the tribes of the house of Judah and Benjamin will be exiled, and with their evil deeds, they not [continue to] exist.” The kingdom of the tribe that disdained its fellow and scorned them and the evil that befell themshe too will not be and will not endure. I shall further interpret it in another manner: **Or shall we rejoice, etc.** Or perhaps we should rejoice, for you say to Me that the tribe of My son disdains all wood: The kings of Judah disdain idolatry, as we find with Abijah, who said (II Chron. 13:8): “and with you are the golden calves,” and Asa, Jehoshaphat, Hezekiah, and Josiah, who abolished all the idolatry in the world that was under their control, and these [the remaining tribes] too will repent.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:18:6)


**זְעַק וְהֵילֵל.** וְאַל תָּשִׂישׂ עַל זֹאת, כִּי הִיא תִהְיֶה בְעַמִּי, וְלָמָּה **כִּי בֹחַן** כְּבָר בֹּחַן הַדָּבָר לְפָנַי שֶׁלֹּא יָשׁוּבוּ תְּשׁוּבָה הוֹגֶנֶת. **וּמָה אִם גַּם שֵׁבֶט מוֹאֶסֶת לֹא יִהְיֶה.** וּמָה אַמְתִּין עוֹד אֲפִלּוּ אִם בָּאוּ לָשׁוּב וְלִמְאֹס בַּעֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים, לֹא יִתְקַיֵּם הַדָּבָר בְּיָדָם, כִּי יְמַהֲרוּ לָשׁוּב לְרִשְׁעָתָם, וְהוּא שֶׁאָמַר לְיִרְמְיָה ״אִם יָשׁוּב לֹא יָשׁוּב״ (ירמיה ח:ד):


**Cry and wail** and do not rejoice over this, for it will be upon My people. And why? **Because it is tested** The matter is already tested and determined before Me that they will not repent properly. **And what if even they disdain wood, it will not be** And what if I wait longer? Even if they came to repent and to disdain idolatry, the matter would not last with them because they would hasten to revert to their wickedness. That is what is said in Jeremiah (8: 4): “if he repents, will he not return?”



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:19:1)


**הִנָּבֵא וְהַךְ כַּף אֶל כָּף.** כְּדֶרֶךְ הַסּוֹפְדִים; וּמַה הִיא הַנְּבוּאָה?


**prophesy and clap your hands one upon the other** in the manner of those who lament, and what is the prophecy?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:19:2)


**וְתִכָּפֵל חֶרֶב שְׁלִישִׁתָה.** אֲנִי אָמַרְתִּי **חֶרֶב חֶרֶב**, חַרְבּוֹ שֶׁל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר לְהַחֲרִיב יְרוּשָׁלַיִם (דברי הימים ב לו:יז-כ), וְחֶרֶב בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן בִּגְדַלְיָה (ירמיה מ:יג-יד; מא:ב); עוֹד יֵשׁ חֶרֶב שֶׁל שְׁלִישִׁית, כְּפוּלָה כִּי שְׁתֵּיהֶן הַלָּלוּ **חֶרֶב חֲלָלִים** תִּהְיֶה, וְהִיא **חֶרֶב הֶחָלָל הַגָּדוֹל** – הַמַּפֶּלֶת חֲלָלִים רַבִּים:


**and a third sword shall be doubled** I said, “A sword, a sword”; Nebuchadnezzar’s sword to destroy Jerusalem and the sword of the children of Ammon upon Gedaliah. There is yet a third sword, doubled as much as these two, and it shall be a sword of the slain; that is the sword of the great slaughter, which causes many slain to fall.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:19:3)


**הַחוֹדֶרֶת לָהֶם.** בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר תֵּצְאוּ, תִּרְדֹּף אֶתְכֶם בְּחַדְרֵי חֲדָרִים; הֲרֵי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן קָרֵחַ וְשָׂרֵי הַחֲיָלִים יֵלְכוּ לְמִצְרַיִם, שָׁם תַּשִּׂיגֵם חֶרֶב (ירמיה מב:טו-כב), וְכֵן בְּכָל הַמְּקוֹמוֹת אֲשֶׁר יִמָּלְטוּ מֵהֶם אֶל עָרִים בְּצוּרוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בָּעִנְיָן: **עַל כָּל שַׁעֲרֵיהֶם נָתַתִּי אִבְחַת חֶרֶב**:


**which penetrates to them** Wherever you go out, it will pursue you into the innermost chambers. Behold, Johanan the son of Kareah and the generals of the armies will go to Egypt; there the sword will overtake them, and so in all the place from which they will flee, to the fortified cities, as it is stated in this piece: “by all your cities I gave the scream of the sword.”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:20:1)


**לָמוּג.** לְשׁוֹן הֲנָעָה; לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן דָּבָר נָמֵס:


**In order to melt** Heb. לָמוּג, an expression of moving. Another explanation: an expression of something melting.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:20:2)


**וְהַרְבֵּה הַמִּכְשׁוֹלִים.** וְהַרְבּוֹת הַמִּכְשׁוֹלִים, וְכֵן כָּל הַרְבֵּה שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא לְשׁוֹן הַרְבּוֹת הוּא; לְשׁוֹן פָּעוֹל:


**and to increase the stumbling blocks** Heb. וְהַרְבֵּה, and to increase the stumbling blocks, and so every הַרְבֵּה in Scripture is an expression of increasing, an infinitive.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:20:3)


**אִבְחַת חָרֶב.** יֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִין טִבְחַת, טִי״ת תַּחַת אָלֶ״ף בְּא״ט ב״ח; וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִין כְּמוֹ אִבְעַת, לְשׁוֹן ״בְּעָתָה״ (ירמיה ח:טו); וְלֹא יִתָּכֵן, כִּי הַתָּי״ו בְּאִבְחַת אֵינָהּ יְסוֹד, וְלֹא בָּאָה אֶלָּא לִסְמִיכָה, וְהַתִּי״ו בְּבְעָתָה מִן הַיְּסוֹד. וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר: **אִבְחַת חָרֶב** הַשְׁמָעַת קוֹל הֲרוּגֵי חֶרֶב, לְשׁוֹן נְבִיחָה, גְּלָאפִ״י שְׁמִינ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז; כִּי נוּ״ן בִּנְבִיחָה יְסוֹד נוֹפֵל הוּא, כְּמוֹ נוּ״ן בִּנְשִׁיכָה, וְתָבֹא אָלֶ״ף בִּמְקוֹמָהּ, כְּמוֹ ״וְאַחֲוָתִי בְאָזְנֵיכֶם״ (איוב יג:יז); ״אָסוּךְ שָׁמֶן״ (מלכים ב ד:ב). וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ כְּמוֹ אֵימַת חֶרֶב, וְאֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן, אֶלָּא פִּתְרוֹנוֹ לְפִי עִנְיָנוֹ:


**the scream of the sword** Heb. אִבְחֳת. Some interpret [this as] טִבְחַת, the slaughter of, the ‘teth’ in place of the ‘aleph,’ by the code of א"ט ב"ח [whereby ‘aleph’ is interchangeable with ‘teth,’ ‘beth’ with ‘cheth,’ etc.] [This appears to be Jonathan’s interpretation.] Others interpret it as אִבְעַת an expression of בְּעָתָה [fright], but this cannot be because the ‘tav’ in אִבְחֳת is not part of the radical, and it comes only for the construct state, whereas the ‘tav’ in בְּעָתָה is a radical. Therefore, I say that אִבְחֳתחֶרֶב means the sounding of the voice of those slain by the sword, an expression of barking (נְבִיחָה) glapissement in French, yelping, for the ‘nun’ in נְבִיחָה is a defective radical, like the ‘nun’ in נְשִּׁיכָה biting, and the ‘aleph’ comes in its place, like (Job 13:17): “and...my speech (אַחְוָתִי) in your ears”; (II Kings 4:2), “except a jug (אָסוּךְ) of oil.” Menachem (p. 12) explained it as fear of the sword. It has no counterpart, but is interpreted according to the context.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:20:4)


**אָח עֲשׂוּיָה לְבָרָק.** אוֹי וְאָח, כִּי עֲשׂוּיָה הִיא לְלַהַט, מְרוּטָה וּמֻבְרֶקֶת:


**Woe, it is made to glitter** Alas and alack! For it is made into a furbished and polished blade.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:20:5)


**מְעֻטָּה לְטָבַח.** וַעֲטוּיָה בְּשִׂמְלָה עַד תִּתָּפֵשׂ בַּכַּף לְטֶבַח בְּיוֹם הַמִּלְחָמָה. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִין **מְעֻטָּה** עַל לְשׁוֹן מִיעוּט, שֶׁמִּתְמַעֶטֶת כְּשֶׁמְּשַׁנְּנִין אוֹתָהּ; וְטָעוּת הוּא בְּיָדָם, כִּי חֶסְרוֹן הַוָּי״ו וְדַגְּשׁוּת הַטִּי״ת יוֹרֶה עָלֶיהָ, כִּי אֵין הַמֵּ״ם שֹׁרֶשׁ בַּתֵּיבָה כְּמֵ״ם שֶׁל ״מְעוּנָה״ וּמְשׁוּנָה וּמְעוּטָה וּ״מְעֻלֶּפֶת סַפִּירִים״ (שיר השירים ה:יד). וּמְנַחֵם חִבֵּר **מְעֻטָּה** לְשׁוֹן מַחֲשָׁבָה, כְּמוֹ (דניאל ב:יד) ״הֵתִיב עִיטָה וְטַעַם״; אֲבָל דּוּנַשׁ פָּתַר אוֹתוֹ כְּמוֹ ״עֹטֶה אוֹר כַּשַּׂלְמָה״ (תהלים קד:ב):


**enwrapped for the slaughter** And enwrapped in a cloth until it would be grasped in the hand for slaughter on the day of war. Some interpret מְעֻטָה as a form of the word for diminishing (מִעוּט), for it diminishes when they sharpen it, but it is an error that they have made, for the absence of the ‘vav’ and the ‘dagesh’ in the ‘teth’ indicate about it that the ‘mem’ is not a radical, [but,] like the ‘mem’ of מְעֻנָה, afflicted, מְעֻטָה, enwrapped, and (Songs 5:14) מְעֻלֶפֶת, “overlaid with sapphires.” Menachem (p. 132) associated it as an expression of thought, like (Dan. 2:14): “answered with counsel (עֵטָא) and discretion.” Dunash, however, interprets it (p. 78) like (Ps. 104:2): “[You] enwrap (עֹטֶה) Yourself with light like a garment.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:21:1)


**הִתְאַחֲדִי.** אֶל מָקוֹם אֶחָד, אוֹ לְיָמִין אוֹ לִשְׂמֹאל:


**Get yourself to one side** To one place, either to the right or to the left.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:21:2)


**מוּעָדוֹת.** מְזֻמָּנוֹת:


**directed** Heb. מֻעָדוֹת, prepared.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:22:1)


**וְגַם אֲנִי אַכֶּה כַפִּי אֶל כַּפִּי.** אֲנִי אוֹמֵר לְךָ הַכֵּה **כַּף אֶל כַּף**, וְגַם **אֲנִי** כָּמוֹךָ מְקוֹנֵן עֲלֵיהֶם **וְאַכֶּה כַפִּי אֶל כַּפִּי**, אַךְ חֲמָתִי בּוֹעֶרֶת בִּי **וַהֲנִיחֹתִי** אוֹתָהּ:


**I, too, shall clap My hands** I say to you, “Clap your hands one upon the other,” and “I, too,” like you, lament over them, and I “shall clap My hands” one upon the other, but My fury burns within Me, “and I shall give it rest.”



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:24:1)


**שִׂים לְךָ שְׁנַיִם דְּרָכִים.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא מְסַיֵּם הַמִּקְרָא:


**make for yourself two roads** as He concludes the [next] verse.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:24:2)


**וְיָד בָּרֵא בְּרֹאשׁ דֶּרֶךְ עִיר.** פַּנֵּה לְךָ מָקוֹם בְּרֹאשׁ דֶּרֶךְ שֶׁאַתָּה בָּהּ, וַעֲשֵׂה לְךָ כְּמִין דֶּרֶךְ מְפֻצֶּלֶת לְפָרָשַׁת דְּרָכִים, אֶחָד פּוֹנֶה לְיָמִין וְאֶחָד פּוֹנֶה לִשְׂמֹאל:


**and clear a place; at the head of the road to the city** Clear for yourself a place at the head of the road you are on, and make for yourself a sort of road opening into the crossroads, one facing right and one facing left.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:24:3)


**יֵצְאוּ שְׁנֵיהֶם.** שְׁנֵי הַדְּרָכִים:


**both of them will emanate** The two roads.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:24:4)


**וְיָד בָּרֵא.** מָקוֹם פַּנֵּה מִן הַקּוֹצִים וְהַבַּרְקָנִים, אֵישְׁרְטִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז; וְדֻגְמָתוֹ בְּסֵפֶר יְהוֹשֻׁעַ: ״עֲלֵה לְךָ הַיַּעְרָה וּבֵרֵאתָ לְךָ שָׁם״ (יהושע יז:טו), וְעוֹד: ״כִּי הַר יִהְיֶה לָּךְ כִּי יַעַר הוּא וּבֵרֵאתוֹ״ (יהושע יז:יח). וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרָם כֻּלָּם לְשׁוֹן בְּרִירָה, כְּמוֹ ״בְּרוּ לָכֶם אִישׁ״ (שמואל א יז:ח); **בָּרֵא** – לִישְׁנְ״ט בְּלַעַז:


**and clear a place** A place cleared of the thorns and the thistles; [בָּרִא is like] essarter in French, to clear. A word similar to it appears in the Book of Joshua (17:15): “then go up to the forest, and clear it (וּבֵרֵאתָ) for yourself there.” Again (ibid. verse 18): “But the mountain shall be yours, for it is a forest, and you shall cut it down (וּבֵרֵאתוֹ).” Menachem connected them all (p. 48) as an expression of choosing, like (I Sam. 17:8): “Choose (בְּרוּ) for yourselves a man”; בָּרֵא meaning lisant in Old French, choosing, appointing.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:25:1)


**דֶּרֶךְ תָּשִׂים לָבוֹא וגו׳.** סִימֵּן שְׁתֵּי דְרָכִים הַלָּלוּ לְמֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל הַיּוֹצֵא מֵאַרְצוֹ עַל מְנָת לֵילֵךְ אוֹ לִירוּשָׁלַיִם אוֹ לְרַבַּת בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן, לְאֵיזֶה מִשְּׁתֵּיהֶן שֶׁיִּפּוֹל לוֹ גּוֹרָל בִּקְסָמָיו עָלֶיהָ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפוֹרָשׁ ״כִּי עָמַד מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל וגו׳״ (יחזקאל כא:כו):


**You shall make a road for the coming, etc.** These two roads symbolize the king of Babylon, who is leaving his country with the intention of going either to Jerusalem or to Rabbah of the children of Ammon. To whichever of the two of them the lot will fall for him by his divinations, he will march, as is delineated (verse 26): “For the king of Babylon stood, etc.”



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:26:1)


**אֵם הַדֶּרֶךְ.** קַארְפוּ״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the crossroads** Heb. אֵם הַדֶרֶךְ, carrefour in French, crossroads.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:26:2)


**קִלְקַל בַּחִצִּים.** לִיהֵט בְּלַהַט חִצִּים; זָרַק חֵץ כְּלַפֵּי מַעְלָה, וְהִיא פּוֹנָה מֵאֵלֶיהָ לְיָמִין אוֹ לִשְׂמֹאל, וְלִמְקוֹם שֶׁהִיא פּוֹנָה – הוּא הַגּוֹרָל:


**he furbished the arrows** Heb. קִלְקַל, he furbished the arrows with a glitter. He shot an arrow upward, and it turns by itself right or left, and to the place it turns—that is the lot.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:26:3)


**קִלְקַל בַּחִצִּים.** חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם עִם ״נְחֹשֶׁת קָלָל״ (יחזקאל א:ז) וְהוּא ״לֹא פָנִים קִלְקַל״ (קהלת י:י). וְיֵשׁ מַשְׁמָע קִלְקַל – לְשׁוֹן קִלְקוּל, כְּמוֹ ״וְכָל הַגְּבָעוֹת הִתְקַלְקָלוּ״ (ירמיה ד:כד); וְיֵשׁ לוֹעֲזִים קִלְקַל טְרָאאִי״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן **קִלְקַל בַּחִצִּים**: ״קְשִׁית בְּגִירְרְיָא״:


**he flashed with the arrows** Menachem (p. 155), connected it with (above 1:7): “burnished (קָלָל) copper”; (Ecc. 10:10): “and he did not sharpen (קִלְקֵל) the edge.” It may also be explained as an expression of deterioration, as in (Jer. 4:24): “and all the hills deteriorated (הִתְקַלְקָלוּ).” Some translate קִלְקַל into French, as trait, he shot, and Jonathan as well renders קִלְקַל בֲּחִצִים as קְשֲּׁתבְּגִרְרֲיָא.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:26:4)


**שָׁאַל בַּתְּרָפִים.** דְּמוּת שֶׁמְּדַבֵּר בִּמְכַשְּׁפוּת; וְיֵשׁ שָׁעָה שֶׁמְּכֻוֶּנֶת, שֶׁאִם עוֹשֵׂהוּ בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה, יֵשׁ בּוֹ דִּבּוּר לְעוֹלָם:


**he inquired of the teraphim** An image that talks through magic, and there is a set time, that if one makes it at that time, it possesses speech forever.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:26:5)


**רָאָה בַּכָּבֵד.** יֵשׁ בְּקִיאִים קוֹסְמִים בַּכָּבֵד:


**looked into the liver** There are some experts who divine with liver.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:27:1)


**בִּימִינוֹ הָיָה הַקֶּסֶם יְרוּשָׁלִַם.** כָּל גּוֹרָלוֹתָיו קָסְמוּ לוֹ לָבֹא יְרוּשָׁלִַם:


**At his right was the divination to Jerusalem** All his lots gave divinistic indication to come to Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:27:2)


**לָשׂוּם כָּרִים.** שַׁלְטוֹנִים מְמֻנִּים, רָאשֵׁי גְּיָסוֹת:


**to appoint officers** Heb. כָּרִים, appointed officers, heads of troops.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:27:3)


**לָשׂוּם כָּרִים עַל שְׁעָרִים.** אַתָּה וְגַיִּס שֶׁלְּךָ, שֵׁב עַל שַׁעַר פְּלוֹנִי:


**to station officers over the gates** You and your troop, take position by such-and-such a gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:27:4)


**לִשְׁפֹּךְ סוֹלְלָה.** שׁוֹפְכִין עָפָר וְסוֹלְלִין וְצוֹבְרִין תֵּל גָּבוֹהַּ, וְכוֹבְשִׁין אוֹתָהּ כְּעֵין שֶׁכּוֹבְשִׁין תִּלֵּי הַמִּגְדָּלִים, לַעֲמוֹד עָלָיו וּלְהִלָּחֵם בִּבְנֵי הָעִיר:


**to pour a siege mound** They pour earth and tread it down and pile up a high mound and press it down, in a fashion similar to the way they press down the mound of the towers, to stand on it and to wage war against the people of the city.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:28:1)


**וְהָיָה לָהֶם.** לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, מַה שֶּׁעָשָׂה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**And it was to them** To Israel what Nebuchadnezzar did.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:28:2)


**כִּקְסַם שָׁוְא.** וְלֹא יַאֲמִינוּ שֶׁיַּצְלִיחַ; אַךְ בֶּאֱמֶת **שְׁבֻעֵי שְׁבֻעוֹת לָהֶם** – אַרְבָּעִים וְתִשְׁעָה פְּעָמִים קָסַם לָהֶם נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר (איכה רבה פתיחתא כג), וְכָל הַקְּסָמִים כִּוְּנוּ זֶה כָּזֶה:


**like a false divination** and they will not believe that he would succeed, but indeed, they had seven sevens. Nebuchadnezzar divined forty-nine times, and all the divinations coincided exactly.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:28:3)


**וְהוּא מַזְכִּיר עָוֹן לְהִתָּפֵשׂ.** נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר בְּקָסְמוֹ אֶת קְסָמָיו הָיָה מַזְכִּיר עֲוֹנוֹת שֶׁבְּיַד יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁהָיוּ מוֹדִיעִים לוֹ עַמּוֹן וּמוֹאָב שְׁכֵנֵיהֶם, שֶׁהָיוּ שׁוֹמְעִין אֶת תּוֹכְחוֹת הַנְּבִיאִים:


**and he brings iniquity to be remembered in order that they be seized** And Nebuchadnezzar, in his divinations, would cause the iniquities that Israel committed to be remembered, for Ammon and Moab, their neighbors, would make them known to him because they would hear the reproofs of the prophets.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:28:4)


**לְהִתָּפֵשׂ.** שֶׁיִּהְיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל נִתְפָּשִׂים בְּיָדוֹ:


**in order that they be seized** [lit. to be seized.] That Israel be seized in his hand.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:29:1)


**יַעַן הַזְכַּרְכֶם עֲוֹנְכֶם.** שֶׁאַתֶּם מְחַדְּשִׁין עֲבֵירוֹת מִיּוֹם אֶל יוֹם, וַעֲוֹנוֹתֵיכֶם הָרִאשׁוֹנִים נִזְכָּרִים עַל יְדֵיהֶם:


**Because you have made your iniquity to be remembered** For you commit new transgressions daily, and your earlier iniquities are remembered through them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:29:2)


**בְּהִגָּלוֹת פִּשְׁעֵיכֶם.** הַחֲדָשִׁים:


**in that your transgressions are revealed** [i.e.,] the new ones.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:29:3)


**לְהֵרָאוֹת חַטֹּאותֵיכֶם.** הַיְשָׁנִים:


**so that your sins appear** [i.e.,] the old ones.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:29:4)


**יַעַן הִזָּכֶרְכֶם.** לְפָנַי לְרָעָה, לְפִיכָךְ:


**because of your being remembered** before Me for evil, therefore...


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:29:5)


**בַּכַּף תִּתָּפֵשׂוּ.** ״בְּיַד מַלְכָּא דְבָבֶל תִּתְמַסְרוּן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**you shall be seized in the hand** You shall be delivered into the hand of the king of Babylon.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:30:1)


**חָלָל רָשָׁע.** חַיָּב לִהְיוֹת חָלָל בְּרִשְׁעֲךָ:


**wicked man, sentenced to be slain** Liable to be slain for your wickedness.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:30:2)


**נְשִׂיא יִשְׂרָאֵל.** צִדְקִיָּהוּ:


**prince of Israel** Zedekiah.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:30:3)


**בְּעֵת עֲוֹן קֵץ.** בְּעֵת נִתְמַלֵּאת הַסְּאָה, וְהִגִּיעַ הָעָוֹן הָאַחֲרוֹן הַמְמַלֵּא אֶת הַסְּאָה:


**at the time of the final iniquity** At the time the measure has become filled and the final iniquity, which fills the measure, has arrived.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:31:1)


**הָסִיר הַמִּצְנֶפֶת וגו׳.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״אַעְדִּי מִצְנֶפְתָּא מִשְּׂרָיָה כַּהֲנָא רַבָּא וַאֲבַטֵּל כִּתְרָא מִצִּדְקִיָּה מַלְכָּא, אָמַר דֵּין וְדֵין בְּאַתְרֵיהּ לָא יִתְקַיֵּים״:


**I shall remove the turban and lift off the crown, etc.** This is to be explained as the Targum renders: I shall remove the turban from Seraiah the High Priest, and I shall abolish the crown from King Zedekiah. He said: Neither this nor that will remain in its place.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:31:2)


**הַגְבֵּהַּ וְגוֹ׳.** ״גְּדַלְיָה בַר אֲחִיקָם דְּלָאו הֲוָה דִילֵיהּ יִסְבּוּנָהּ, וְצִדְקִיָּהוּ דְּדִילֵיהּ הִיא תַּעְדִּי מִינֵּיהּ״:


**the humble will be uplifted** Gedaliah the son of Ahikam, to whom it did not belong, will take it, and Zedekiah, to whom it did belong it will be taken from him.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:32:1)


**עַוָּה עַוָּה.** לְשׁוֹן ״עִי הַשָּׂדֶה״ (מיכה א:ו), לְשׁוֹן חֻרְבָּה אַחַר חֻרְבָּה; פְּעָמִים וְשָׁלֹשׁ **אֲשִׂימֶנָּה**: כְּבָר נִטְּלָה מִיכָנְיָה וְנִתְּנָה לְצִדְקִיָּה (מלכים ב כד:ח-יז), וּמִצִּדְקִיָּה תִּנָּתֵן לִגְדַלְיָה (מלכים ב כה:א-כב), הֲרֵי שְׁתַּיִם. וּמְנַחֵם חִבֵּר עַוָּה עִם ״עָוְתָה וַשְׁתִּי הַמַּלְכָּה״ (אסתר א:טז); ״הֶעֱוִינוּ וְהִרְשָׁעְנוּ״ (תהלים קו:ו):


**A ruin, a ruin** Heb. עַוָה, an expression of (Micah 1:6): “a heap (עִי) in the field,” an expression of ruin after ruin, two and three times, I shall make it: It was already taken from Jeconiah and given to Zedekiah, and from Zedekiah it will be given to Gedaliah; this makes twice. Menachem (p. 131) connected עַוָה with (Esther 1:16): “has Vashti the queen done wrong (עָוְתָה)?”; (Ps. 106:5), “we have committed iniquity (הֶעֱוִינוּ) and wickedness.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:32:2)


**גַּם זֹאת לֹא הָיָה.** גַּם לִגְדַלְיָה לֹא תִתְקַיֵּם, **עַד בֹּא** יִשְׁמָעֵאל בֶּן נְתַנְיָה (מלכים ב כה:כה-כו):


**this too will not be** Also, to Gedaliah it will not remain, [only] until Ishmael the son of Nethaniah [the assassin] comes.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:32:3)


**אֲשֶׁר לוֹ הַמִּשְׁפָּט.** אֲשֶׁר עָלָיו שַׂמְתִּי לְהִפָּרַע נִקְמָתִי וּמִשְׁפָּטִי:


**until he who has the judgment** Upon whom I placed [the mission] to exact My vengeance and My judgment.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:32:4)


**וּנְתַתִּיו.** בְּיָדוֹ:


**and I will deliver him** into his hands.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:33:1)


**אֶל בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן וְאֶל חֶרְפָּתָם.** אֲשֶׁר שָׂמְחוּ בִּנְפֹל גּוֹרָלוֹ שֶׁל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר בִּקְסָמָיו לָבֹא עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, וְחֵרְפוּ אוֹתִי וְאֶת עַמִּי לֵאמֹר, יַד עֲבוֹדַת כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת שֶׁלָּהֶם רָמָה:


**concerning the children of Ammon and concerning their blasphemy,** [in] that they rejoiced when Nebuchadnezzar’s lot in his divinations fell out to come upon Jerusalem, and they blasphemed Me and My people, saying that their deity was triumphant.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:33:2)


**לְהָכִיל.** חֲלָלִים רַבִּים, **לְמַעַן בָּרָק** לְמַעַן לֹא יֶחְשַׁךְ מַרְאֵה לַהַט שֶׁלָּהּ בַּחֲלָלֶיהָ:


**to consume** Heb. לְהָכִיל, to cause many slain to fall. **in order to glitter** In order that its shiny appearance should not be darkened by its slain ones.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:34:1)


**בַּחֲזוֹת לָךְ שָׁוְא.** קוֹסְמֵי עַמּוֹן חָזוּ לָהֶם שָׁוְא, לֵאמֹר: לֹא יָבֹא עוֹד עָלֵינוּ:


**When you prophesy vanity for yourself** The diviners of Ammon prophesied vanity for themselves, saying, “Nothing more will come upon us.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:34:2)


**לָתֵת אוֹתְךָ אֶל צַוְּארֵי חַלְלֵי רְשָׁעִים.** לְהַטְעוֹתְךָ וְלָתֵת אֶת צַוָּארְךָ כְּצַוְּארֵי חַלְלֵי רְשָׁעִים שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**to place you with the necks of the slain of the wicked** To mislead you and to place your neck like the necks of the slain of the wicked of Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:34:3)


**אֲשֶׁר בָּא יוֹמָם וְגוֹ׳.** יוֹם שֶׁלָּהֶם:


**whose day has come, etc.** Heb. יוֹמָם, their day.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:35:1)


**הָשַׁב.** כְּמוֹ הָשֵׁב, שֶׁהֲרֵי הַשִּׁי״ן רָפָה; הַמְתִּינִי עַד שׁוּב חֶרֶב שֶׁעַל יִשְׂרָאֵל **אֶל תַּעְרָהּ**, וְאַחַר כָּךְ, כְּשֶׁיִּגְמֹר נִקְמָתוֹ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, יָשׁוּב עָלֶיךָ, וּ**בִמְקוֹם אֲשֶׁר נִבְרֵאת וְגוֹמֵר**:


**It has been returned** Heb. הָשֵּׁב, like שָּׁבָה, it has returned, because the ‘shin’ has no ‘dagesh.’ [The missing ‘dagesh’ indicates that no letter is missing; hence, the root is שוּב, return, rather than ישב, sit or dwell.] Wait until the sword that is upon Israel returns into its sheath, and afterward, when he has finished His revenge upon Israel, He will return upon you, and in the place you were created, etc.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:1)


**אָפִיחַ עָלָיִךְ.** לְשׁוֹן נְפִיחַת אֵשׁ בְּמַפּוּחַ:


**I shall blow upon you** Heb. אָפִּיחָ, an expression for blowing fire with a bellows.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:2)


**אֲנָשִׁים בֹּעֲרִים.** מַנְהִיגִין עַצְמָן בְּשִׁגָּעוֹן לְחַבֵּל וּלְהַשְׁחִית:


**brutish men** Who behave madly to ruin and to destroy.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:3)


**חָרָשֵׁי מַשְׁחִית.** ״אוּמָנִין לְקַטְלָא״ (זֶה יָמִים רַבִּים שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי פָּרָשָׁה זוֹ כַּאֲשֶׁר כָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה עַכְשָׁו אֲנִי רוֹאֶה בְּשִׁנּוּי לְשׁוֹנוֹת שֶׁבָּהּ שֶׁאֵין הַנָּבִיא מְדַבֵּר אֶלָּא עַל הַחֶרֶב), **לָתֵת אוֹתְךָ אֶל צַוְּארֵי חַלְלֵי רְשָׁעִים וְגוֹ׳, הָשֵׁב אֶל תַּעְרָהּ וְגוֹ׳, אָפִיחַ עָלֶיךָ וְגוֹ׳** אֵינוֹ נוֹפֵל לָשׁוֹן זֶה אֶלָּא עַל מַתֶּכֶת, וְכֵן **חָרָשֵׁי מַשְׁחִית**. וְנִרְאֶה בְּעֵינַי שֶׁעַל נְבוּכַדְנֶאצַּר נֶאֶמְרָה נְבוּאָה זוֹ, וְכֵן מַשְׁמַע (לג) **הִנָּבֵא וְאָמַרְתָּ כֹּה אָמַר ה׳ אֱלֹהִים עַל בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן וְעַל חֶרְפָּתָם**, כְּלוֹמַר (שֶׁחֶרְפָּתִי, שֶׁמְּחָרְפִים הָיוּ) עַל נְבוּכַדְנְצַר (בִּשְׁבִיל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר) שֶׁבְּנֵי עַמּוֹן מְחָרְפִים אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל שֶׁהֶחֱרִיבָם נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר:


**craftsmen of destruction** Artisans of murder. (Already many days [have passed] since I explained this chapter as it is written above. Now I see from the peculiarities of its wording that the prophet is talking only about the sword.) “To place you upon the necks of the slain of the wicked,” “It has been returned to its sheath, etc.” “I shall blow upon you.” This wording is appropriate only for metal, and so “craftsmen of destruction.” It seems to me that this prophecy was stated about Nebuchadnezzar, and so it appears [from]: “prophesy and say: So said the Lord God concerning the children of Ammon and concerning their taunts.” That is to say, [their taunts] over Nebuchadnezzar [because of Nebuchadnezzar], for the children of Ammon were taunting Israel because Nebuchadnezzar destroyed them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:4)


״**חֶרֶב חֶרֶב פְּתוּחָה.** חֶרֶב נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר הַכְּפוּלָה לָצֵאת בִּשְׁנֵי הַדְּרָכִים, עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם וְעַל בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן, כְּמוֹ בַּפָּרָשָׁה הָעֶלְיוֹנָה (לעיל כג). הֲרֵי פֻּרְעָנוּת לִבְנֵי עַמּוֹן:


**Sword, drawn sword** The sword of Nebuchadnezzar, which is double, to go out on the two waysagainst Jerusalem and against the children of Ammon, as is delineated in the above chapterthus the retribution on the children of Ammon. [34]


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:5)


(לד) **בַּחֲזוֹת לְךָ שָׁוְא בִּקְסָם לְךָ כָּזָב.** אַתָּה סָבוּר כִּי עַל יְדֵי קְסָמֶיךָ שֶׁל הֶבֶל וְשָׁוְא וְכָזָב אַתָּה מַצְלִיחַ, שֶׁחַרְבְּךָ נְתוּנָה **אֶל צַוְּארֵי חֲלָלִים הָרְשָׁעִים שֶׁבָּא יוֹמָם** בִּגְזֵרָה מִלְּפָנַי, בְּעֵת תַּשְׁלוּם אֶת קִצָּם:


**When you prophesy for yourself, when you divine lies for yourself** You think that through your divinations of vanity, futility, and lies, you will prosper, for your sword is placed upon the necks of the slain of the wicked, whose day has come by decree from before Me at the time of the payment at the time of their end. [35]


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 21:36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/21:36:6)


(לה) **הָשֵׁב אֶל תַּעְרָהּ.** כְּלוֹמַר, הָעֵת יָבֹא שֶׁתָּשׁוּב אֶל תַּעְרָהּ וְתְקַבֵּל הַחֶרֶב פּוּרְעָנוּתָהּ מִיַּד מַלְכֵי מָדַי, וְאָז **אָפִיחַ עָלֶיךָ בְּאֵשׁ עֶבְרָתִי וְתִהְיֶה נְתוּנָה בְּיַד** נַפָּחִים שׁוֹטִים שֶׁהֵם **חָרָשֵׁי מַשְׁחִית** שֶׁיָּבוֹאוּ לְחַדֶּדְךָ וּלְמוֹרְטֶךָ כַּאֲשֶׁר מֵאָז וְיַשְׁחִיתוּךָ:


**It has been returned to its sheath** That is to say, the time will come that the sword will return to its sheath, and the sword will receive its own retribution, at the hands of the kings of Media, and then I shall blow upon you with the fire of My wrath, and you shall be delivered into the hands of mad blacksmiths, who are craftsmen of destruction, who will come to sharpen and to furbish it as of yore, and they will destroy you.



## Chapter 22


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:2:1)


**וַיְהִי דְבַר ה׳ אֵלַי לֵאמֹר. הֲתִשְׁפֹּט אֶת עִיר הַדָּמִים.** יְרוּשָׁלַיִם:


**will you judge the city of bloodshed** Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:2:2)


**וְהוֹדַעְתָּהּ.** מֵפִיק הֵ״א וְהוֹדַעְתָּהּ, אוֹתָהּ:


**and let her know** The “heh” in והודעתה is pointed, so that the meaning is, “and let her know.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:3:1)


**עִיר שׁוֹפֶכֶת דָּם בְּתוֹכָהּ לָבוֹא עִתָּהּ.** הֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא מְסֹרָס, וְזֶהוּ פֵּירוּשׁוֹ: **עִיר שׁוֹפֶכֶת דָּם בְּתוֹכָהּ**, אָמַר ה׳ אֱלֹהִים, **לָבוֹא עִתָּהּ**:


**O city that sheds blood in her midst, her time is approaching** This is a transposed verse, and this is its meaning: A city that sheds blood in her midst the Lord God said that its time is coming.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:3:2)


**וְעָשְׂתָה גִלּוּלִים.** כְּמוֹ וַאֲשֶׁר עָשְׂתָה גִלּוּלִים וְגוֹ׳:


**and makes idols** Like “and which makes idols, etc.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:4:1)


**עַד שְׁנוֹתָיִךְ.** גְּזֵרַת ״וְנוֹשַׁנְתֶּם״ (דברים ד:כה) בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא שֶׁאָמַר מֹשֶׁה:


**up to your years** The decree of (Deut. 4:25): “and you will have grown old,” with its numerical value, which Moses said.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:4:2)


**וְקַלָּסָה.** דִּבָּה; שֶׁהַכֹּל נִדְבָּרִים בָּךְ; פַּרְלְרִי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and [a subject of] gossip** Heb. וְקַלָסָה, gossip, that everyone will speak about you, parlediz in Old French, [subject of] gossip.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:5:1)


**יִתְקַלְּסוּ בָךְ.** וּמָה הִיא הַקַּלָּסוּת? **טְמֵאַת הַשֵּׁם**. זוֹ הִיא שֶׁטִּמֵּאת אֶת שְׁמָהּ; שֶׁהָיוּ נִקְרָאִים ״עַם קָדוֹשׁ״, ״בָּנִים״ לְיוֹצְרָם (דברים יד:א-ב), וְגָרְמוּ עַצְמָם לְהִקָּרְאוֹת ״עַם טְמֵא שְׂפָתַיִם״ (ישעיהו ו:ה):


**will gossip about you** And what is the gossip?...**whose name is so befouled** This is she who befouled her name, for they were called a holy people, sons of their Creator, and they caused themselves to be called (Isa. 6:5) “a people of unclean lips.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:6:1)


**אִישׁ לִזְרוֹעוֹ.** כָּל דְּאַלִּים גָּבַר (בבא בתרא לד:):


**each one by his own arm** Whoever was stronger overpowered [the other].



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:7:1)


**אָב וָאֵם הֵקַלּוּ בָךְ.** כָּל הַתּוֹעֵבוֹת שֶׁבְּפָרָשַׁת קְדֹשִׁים תִּהְיוּ (ויקרא יט-כ), שֶׁהוּזְהֲרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם, נִמְנוּ כָאן:


**Father and mother are held light in you** All the abominations in the section of (Lev. 19, 20) “You shall be holy,” about which they were warned, are enumerated here.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:8:1)


**קָדָשַׁי בָּזִית.** וְלֹא כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי ״לִרְצֹנְכֶם תִּזְבָּחוּ״ (ויקרא יט:ה):


**My holy things have you despised** And not as I said (Lev. 22:29): “you shall slaughter it as an expression of your good will.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:9:1)


**וְאֶל הֶהָרִים אָכְלוּ בָךְ.** ״וּבְטוּרַיָּא פְּלָחוּ בֵּיךְ לְטָעֲוָותָא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and in you they have eaten upon the mountains** and on the mountains they have worshipped in you to pagan deities.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:9:2)


**זִמָּה עָשׂוּ.** עֵצוֹת שֶׁל רְשָׁעָה:


**they have held wicked plans** Heb. זִמָה, plans of wickedness.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:12:1)


**וַתְּבַצְּעִי רֵעַיִךְ בַּעֹשֶׁק.** הֶעֱשַׁרְתְּ אֶת אוֹהֲבַיִךְ בָּעֹשֶׁק שֶׁעָשַׁקְתְּ מִן הָעֲנִיִּים. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **וַתְּבַצְּעִי** – לְשׁוֹן גֶּזֶל, כְּמוֹ ״וּבֹצֵעַ בֵּרֵךְ נִאֵץ ה׳״ (תהלים י:ג); וְכֹה פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: וַתִּגְזְלִי לְרֵעַיִךְ בְּעֹשֶׁק:


**and enriched your neighbors with dishonest gain** You made your friends rich with the dishonest gain that you gained dishonestly from the poor. Another explanation: וַתְּבַצְעִי is a form of a word for robbery, like (Ps. 10:3): “and the robber (וּבֹצֵעַ) blesses himself for having blasphemed the Lord.” Accordingly, its interpretation is: and you robbed your friends with affliction.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:13:1)


**הִכֵּיתִי כַפִּי.** צָעַקְתִּי וַוי וְקִינָה:


**I clapped My hands together** I cried out, “Woe!” and lamentation.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:13:2)


**אֶל בִּצְעֵיךְ.** כָּאן לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁהַגָּזֵל קָשֶׁה מִן הַכֹּל, שֶׁעָלָיו נִתְחַתֵּם גְּזַר דִּין (קהלת רבה ג:יב):


**over your dishonest gain** Here we learn that robbery is more severe than all of them, for their verdict was sealed because of it.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:14:1)


**אֲשֶׁר אֲנִי עוֹשֶׂה אוֹתָךְ.** פּוֹעֵל לָךְ כְּפָעֳלֵךְ:


**that I will deal with you** That I will deal with you according to your deeds.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:15:1)


**וְהַתִּמּוֹתִי.** וְכִלִּיתִי:


**and I will make...completely disappear** And I will end.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:16:1)


**וְנִחַלְתְּ בָּךְ.** תִּתְחַלְחֲלִי בְּעַצְמֵךְ עַל מַה שֶּׁעָשִׂית:


**And you shall be profaned within yourself** Heb. וְנִחַלְתָּ בָּךְ, you shall be profaned within yourself for what you have done.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:18:1)


**לְסִיג.** תַּעֲרֹבֶת פְּסֹלֶת הַמְּעֹרָב בְּכֶסֶף וְזָהָב, וְצָרִיךְ לְהַתִּיכוֹ בָּאֵשׁ לְצָרְפוֹ:


**dross** A mixture of dross mixed in silver and gold, and it is necessary to smelt it in fire to refine it.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:18:2)


**כּוּר.** שֶׁצּוֹרְפִין בּוֹ זָהָב:


**a furnace** in which gold is refined.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:20:1)


**קְבֻצַת כֶּסֶף.** כַּאֲשֶׁר יְקַבֵּץ הַצּוֹרֵף אֶת כָּל הַסִּיגִים **אֶל תּוֹךְ כּוּר**:


**As they gather silver** As the silversmith gathers all the dross into the furnace.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:20:2)


**לָפַחַת.** לְנַפֵּחַ:


**to blow** Heb. לָפַחַת, to blow (לְנַפֵּחַ).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:20:3)


**לְהַנְתִּיךְ.** ״וְיָצַקְתָּ״ (שמות כה:יב) מְתַרְגֵּם: ״וְתַתִּיךְ״:


**to melt** Heb. לְהַנְתִּיךְ. (Exod. 25:12) “and cast (וְיָצַקְתָּ)” is translated into Aramaic as וְתַתִּיךְ.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:24:1)


**לֹא גֻשְׁמָהּ בְּיוֹם זָעַם.** לֹא נִתַּן גֶּשֶׁם הָרָאוּי לָהּ לְטַהֵר **בְּיוֹם זָעַם**; וּפֵירוּשׁ הַדָּבָר כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״לָא אִיתְעֲבִידוּ בָהּ עוֹבְדִין טָבִין דְּיִגְנוּן עֲלָהּ בְּיוֹם לְוָוט״:


**it was not rained upon on the day of fury** The rain fit to cleanse it on the day of fury was not given it, and the explanation of the matter is as the Targum paraphrases: Good deeds were not performed in her which would protect her on the day of curse.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:25:1)


**קֶשֶׁר נְבִיאֶיהָ.** נִקְשָׁרִים יַחַד בְּעֵצָה רָעָה:


**There is a conspiracy of her prophets** Heb. קֶשֶׁר [lit. a knot]. They are joined together in an evil counsel.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:25:2)


**נֶפֶשׁ אָכָלוּ.** שֶׁהָיוּ נוֹטְלִין שֹׁחַד וּמַחֲזִיקִין יְדֵי מְרֵעִים, לַהֲרֹג וְלִשְׁלֹל וְלָבֹז, וְנִבָּאִין לָהֶם שָׁלוֹם:


**they devoured souls** For they would take bribes and strengthen the hands of evildoers to kill and plunder and rob, and then they would prophesy peace for them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:25:3)


**חֹסֶן.** מָמוֹן:


**riches** Heb. חֹסֶן, money.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:26:1)


**חָמְסוּ תוֹרָתִי.** עֲלֵיהֶם הָיָה לְהוֹכִיחַ וּלְהוֹרוֹת וּלְהוֹדִיעַ חֹק וּמִשְׁפָּט, וְלֹא עָשׂוּ, הֲרֵי זֶה **חָמָס**, שֶׁחֲמָסוּהָ מִן הַצְּרִיכִין לִלְמֹד:


**robbed My Torah** It was incumbent upon them to reprove, to instruct, and to make known statutes and ordinances, but they did not do so. This is robbery, for they robbed it from those who needed to learn.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:26:2)


**וָאֵחַל.** וְנִתְחַלַּלְתִּי:


**and I was profaned in their midst** Heb. וָאֵחַל, and I was profaned.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:27:1)


**לְמַעַן בְּצֹעַ בָּצַע.** לַחֲטֹף מָמוֹן:


**for the sake of dishonest gain** To seize money.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 22:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/22:28:1)


**טָחוּ לָהֶם תָּפֵל.** דּוֹמִין לְטָחֵי בִּנְיָן בְּטִיט שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְעֻבָּד כָּל צָרְכּוֹ, שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ תֶּבֶן, וְהוּא נִפְשָׁר מֵאֵלָיו בְּבֹא עָלָיו גְּשָׁמִים; וְלָשׁוֹן **תָּפֵל** – כָּל דָּבָר הַמְחֻסָּר תִּקּוּן:


**plastered for them with daub** They are like those who plaster a building with plaster that is not completely processed, which has no straw; it dissolves by itself when rain falls on it. The expression תָּפֵל means anything that is lacking preparation.



## Chapter 23


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:2:1)


**שְׁתַּיִם נָשִׁים.** ״תַּרְתֵּי מְדִינָן, דְּאִינוּן כְּתַרְתֵּין נְשִׁין״ (תרגום יונתן):


**two women** Two states, which are like two women.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:2:2)


**בְּנוֹת אֵם אֶחָת.** שְׁתֵּיהֶן מִכְּנֶסִיָּה אַחַת, נֶחְלְקוּ לִשְׁתַּיִם בִּימֵי רְחַבְעָם (מלכים א יב:א-יט):


**the daughters of one mother** The two of them were from one nation, separated into two in the days of Rehoboam.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:3:1)


**שָׁמָּה מֹעֲכוּ.** בְּנֵי מִצְרַיִם אֶת שְׁדֵיהֶם, לְשׁוֹן ״וּמָעוּךְ״ (ויקרא כב:כד):


**There...were pressed** [Heb. מֹעְכוּ]. The Egyptians [pressed] their bosoms, an expression of (Lev. 22: 24): “and anything that has been crushed (וּמַעוּךְ).”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:3:2)


**וְשָׁם עָשׂוּ.** הַמִּצְרִיִּים אֶת **דַּדֵּי בְּתוּלֵיהֶן**; כְּלוֹמַר: לִמְּדוּם אֶת דַּרְכֵי תּוֹעֲבוֹתָם. **עָשׂוּ** – אִיטִיינִירִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and there they squeezed** The Egyptians [squeezed] their virgin breasts; i.e., they taught them the ways of their abominations. [Heb. עִשׂוּ,] eteyn[d]irent in Old French, they squeezed



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:4:1)


**וּשְׁמוֹתָן.** בְּכִנּוּי: **אָהֳלָה וְאָהֳלִיבָה**:


**And their names** i.e., their nicknames were Oholah and Oholibah.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:4:2)


**וּשְׁמוֹתָן.** הָרִאשׁוֹנִים: **שֹׁמְרוֹן** הִיא שֶׁאֲנִי מְכַנֶּה **אָהֳלָה**, שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית אֹהֶל מִתְּחִלָּה לְעֶגְלֵי יָרָבְעָם (מלכים א יב:כח-כט) וּבֵית הַבַּעַל לְאַחְאָב (מלכים א טז:לב); וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁשּׁוֹמְרוֹן לֹא נִבְנְתָה אֶלָּא בִּימֵי עָמְרִי (מלכים א טז:כג-כד), קוֹרֵא אֶת מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל שְׁמָהּ, שֶׁמִּשֶּׁנִּבְנֵית נַעֲשֵׂית רֹאשׁ:


**and their names** The original ones. Samaria is the one I nickname Oholah, for from the beginning she became a tent (אֹהֶל) for the calves of Jeroboam and for Ahab’s temple of Baal. Although Samaria was not built until the days of Omri, He calls the kings of Israel by its name, because as soon as it was built, it became the capital.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:4:3)


**אָהֳלִיבָה.** עַל שֵׁם שֶׁאָהֳלִי הָיָה בָהּ וּמִקְדָּשִׁי:


**Oholibah** Because My tent (אֹהֹלִי) was in her (בָה), and My sanctuary.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:5:1)


**וַתַּעְגַּב.** וַתִּתְאַוֶּה; אִישְׁוִויי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז; לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: דִּישְׁשִׁירִי״ר:


**and she lusted** [Heb. וַתַּעְגַּב,] and she lusted, esvayer or eswaier in Old French, to covet, to lust after. Some editions read: desirer, to desire.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:5:2)


**אֶל אַשּׁוּר קְרוֹבִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ שְׁכֵנִים לָהּ, וְשָׁלְחוּ לָהֶם לִהְיוֹת לָהֶם לְעֵזֶר; זֶה הָיָה מְנַחֵם בֶּן גָּדִי (מלכים ב טו:יט) שֶׁנָּתַן מַס לְפוּל מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר ״לִהְיוֹת יָדוֹ אִתּוֹ לְהַחֲזִיק הַמַּמְלָכָה בְּיָדוֹ״. וְהַנָּבִיא מְדַמֶּה הַדָּבָר לְאִשָּׁה מְנָאֶפֶת, שֶׁאִם הָיוּ עוֹבְדִים לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, לֹא נִצְטָרְכוּ לְמַלְכֵי אַשּׁוּר:


**for the Assyrians, who were nearby** Who were her neighbors, and they sent to them [requesting that they] aid them. This is Menachem the son of Gadi (II Kings 15: 19f.), who gave tribute to Pul king of Assyria in order “that his hands might be with him to strengthen the kingdom in his hand.” The prophet likens the matter to an adulterous woman, for, had they worshipped the Holy One, blessed be He, they would not have needed the kings of Assyria.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:7:1)


**וַתִּתֵּן תַּזְנוּתֶיהָ עֲלֵיהֶם.** לֵב נִיאוּפִים וְרוּחַ זְנוּנִים נָתְנָה עֲלֵיהֶם, שֶׁהָיְתָה מִתְאַוָּה לָהֶם:


**And she bestowed her harlotries upon them** Her adulterous heart and her promiscuous spirit she bestowed upon them, for she desired them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:7:2)


**וּבְכֹל אֲשֶׁר עָגְבָה וְגוֹ׳.** בְּכָל עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת אֲשֶׁר נִשְׁתַּתְּפָה עִמָּהֶם לָמְדָה דְּרָכֵיהֶם וְ**נִטְמְאָה בְּגִלּוּלֵיהֶם**:


**and in all that she lusted** In all the nations with whom she collaborated, she learned their ways and became defiled with their idols.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:8:1)


**וְאֶת תַּזְנוּתֶיהָ מִמִּצְרַיִם לֹא עָזָבָה.** גַּם עַל מַלְכֵי מִצְרַיִם סָמְכוּ, שֶׁמָּצִינוּ בְּהוֹשֵׁעַ בֶּן אֵלָה ״אֲשֶׁר שָׁלַח מַלְאָכִים אֶל סוֹא מֶלֶךְ מִצְרָיִם״ (מלכים ב יז:ד):


**And her harlotries from Egypt she did not abandon** They relied also upon the kings of Egypt, for we find (II Kings 17:4), concerning Hoshea the son of Elah, that he sent messengers to So the king of Egypt.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:8:2)


**כִּי אוֹתָהּ שָׁכְבוּ וגו׳.** וּמֵאָז **וַיִּשְׁפְּכוּ תַזְנוּתָם עָלֶיהָ** – תַּאֲוַת זְנוּנִים נִכְנַס בָּהּ מִימֵי נְעוּרֶיהָ:


**for they had lain with her, etc.** And since then, “they poured out their harlotry upon her” - her promiscuous spirit entered her from the days of her youth.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:9:1)


**לָכֵן נְתַתִּיהָ וְגוֹ׳.** שֶׁבָּא סַנְחֵרִיב וְהִגְלָה אוֹתָם (מלכים ב יז:ה-ו):


**Therefore, I delivered her, etc.** For Sennacherib came and exiled them.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:10:1)


**וַתְּהִי שֵׁם לַנָּשִׁים.** לוּקַח מֵהֶן קְלָלָה לְכָל הַנּוֹאֲפוֹת לְהִוָּסֵר מִמֶּנָּה, רְאוּ מַה עָלְתָה לִפְלוֹנִית עַל זִמָּתָהּ:


**and she became a name for the women** to take from them a curse for any adulteress to learn a lesson from her: See what happened to so and so because of her lewdness.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:10:2)


**וּשְׁפוּטִים.** כְּמוֹ ״שְׁפָטִים״ (יחזקאל ה:י), יוּשְׁטִישָׁא״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and... judgments** [Heb. וּשְּׁפוּטִים,] like שְּׁפָטִים, judgments, justises, jostises in Old French, judgments, punishments.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:11:1)


**וַתֵּרֶא אֲחוֹתָהּ אָהֳלִיבָה.** אֶת כָּל הָרָעָה אֲשֶׁר בָּא לַאֲחוֹתָהּ עַל זְנוּנֶיהָ, וְלֹא יָרְאָה:


**And her sister Oholibah saw** all the evil that befell her sister because of her harlotries, but she was unafraid.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:11:2)


**וַתַּשְׁחֵת עַגְבָתָהּ.** תַּאֲוָתָהּ; זוֹיְט״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**she became... corrupt in her lust** [Heb. עַגְבָתָה,] her lust, wai(y)ter in Old French, unbridled passion.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:12:1)


**אֶל בְּנֵי אַשּׁוּר עָגָבָה.** זֶה אָחָז, שֶׁשָּׁלַח אֶל מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר לְעֶזְרָה (מלכים ב טז:ז):


**For the sons of Assyria she lusted** This is Ahaz, who sent to the king of Assyria for aid (II Kings 16:7).



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:14:1)


**וַתּוֹסֶף אֶל תַּזְנוּתֶיהָ.** לְהִתְאַוּוֹת לַצְּלָמִים שֶׁלֹּא רָאֲתָה אוֹתָם אֶלָּא דְּמוּתָם:


**And she increased her harlotries** to lust for images that she had not seen [in actuality], only their likeness,


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:14:2)


**מְחֻקָּה עַל הַקִּיר וְגוֹ׳ בַּשָּׁשַׁר.** כָּזֹאת לֹא עָשְׂתָה אֲחוֹתָהּ:


**engraved on the wall with types of drawings**, whereas her sister did not do anything like this.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:14:3)


**צַלְמֵי כַשְׂדִּים.** דְּמוּת אַנְשֵׁי כַשְׂדִּים, שֶׁמְּשֻׁנִּין בִּלְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן:


**images of Chaldeans** The likeness of Chaldean men, who were unusual in their attire.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:14:4)


**בַּשָּׁשַׁר.** מִינֵי צִיּוּר:


**with types of drawings** [Heb. בַּשָׁשַׁר,] types of drawings.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:15:1)


**חֲגוֹרֵי אֵזוֹר.** כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ לְבוּשֵׁי כַשְׂדִּים:


**Girded with girdles** This was the way of the Chaldeans’ attire.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:15:2)


**סְרוּחֵי טְבוּלִים.** יְתֵירֵי כּוֹבָעִים גְּדוֹלִים. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּירֵשׁ כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּטְבְּלוּ הַכֻּתֹּנֶת בְּדָם״ (בראשית לז:לא), וּפִתְרוֹן **סְרוּחֵי טְבוּלִים** כֵּן הוּא: ״סֶרַח הָעוֹדֵף״ (שמות כו:יב) מִן הַמִּצְנֶפֶת צְבוּעָה. **טְבוּלִים** אֵין לוֹ דִּמְיוֹן, וּפִתְרוֹנוֹ לְפִי הָעִנְיָן; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״מְחַתִּין קוֹלְסִין״:


**hanging turbans** [Heb. סְרוּחֵי טְבוּלִים.] [סְרוּחֵי means] oversized, large hats. Menachem (p. 97) explains [טְבוּלִים] like (Gen. 37: 31) “and dipped (וַיִטְבְּלוּ) the shirt in the blood.” Thus the interpretation of סְרוּחֵי טְבוּלִים is as follows (Exod. 26:12): “The overhanging (וְסֵרַח) length” of the dyed turban. There is no [other occurrence of a word] similar to טְבוּלִים, and its meaning is derived from the context, and so did Jonathan render: helmets placed on their heads].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:15:3)


**שָׁלִישִׁים.** גִּבּוֹרִים:


**mighty men** [Heb. שָׁלִישִׁים,] mighty men.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:16:1)


**לְמַרְאֵה עֵינֶיהָ.** בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁרָאֲתָה דְּמוּת צוּרָתָן:


**at the sight [that reached] her eyes** Because she saw the likeness of their form.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:17:1)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ אֵלֶיהָ בְּנֵי בָבֶל לְמִשְׁכַּב דּוֹדִים.** כָּרְתוּ יַחַד בְּרִית. וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי שֶׁזֶּה נֶאֱמַר עַל חִזְקִיָּה, שֶׁשָּׂמַח עַל מַלְאֲכֵי מְרֹדַךְ בַּלְאֲדָן (ישעיהו לט:א) וְהֶאֱכִילָן עַל שֻׁלְחָנוֹ וְהֶרְאָם אֶת כָּל בֵּית נְכֹתֹה, וְדוּגְמָא שֶׁאָמַר הַנָּבִיא אַנְשֵׁי מְחֻקֶּה עַל הַקִּיר, לְפִי שֶׁמֵאֶרֶץ רְחוֹקָה בָּאוּ וְלֹא הָיוּ בְּנֵי כַשְׂדִּים רְגִילִים אֶצְלָם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר שָׁם ״מֵאֶרֶץ רְחוֹקָה בָּאוּ אֵלַי״, וּבִשְׁבִיל רְאוּת פְּנֵיהֶם פַּעַם אַחַת נִתְחַבְּבוּ עָלָיו וְהִקְפִּיד הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עַל הַדָּבָר, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״הִנֵּה יָמִים בָּאִים וְנִשָּׂא אֶת כָּל אֲשֶׁר בְּבֵיתֶךָ וְגוֹ׳״ (שם), ובדברי הימים (ב לב:כה) ״וְלֹא כִגְמוּל עָלָיו הֵשִׁיב חִזְקִיָּהוּ כִּי גָבַהּ לִבּוֹ וַיְהִי עָלָיו קֶצֶף גָּדוֹל וְגוֹמֵר״:


**And the children of Babylon came to her for a bed of love** They made a covenant together, and I say that this is said concerning Hezekiah, who rejoiced about the emissaries of Merodach-baladan (Isa. 39, II Kings 20), and he fed them at his table and showed them his entire treasure house. This resembles the prophet’s statement (verse 14), “men engraved on the wall,” because they came from a distant land, and the Chaldeans were not frequent visitors to them, as it is said (Isa. 39:3): “They have come to me from a distant country,” and from seeing their faces once, they became endeared to him. And the Holy One, blessed be He, became wroth with him because of this matter, as it is written (Isa. 36:6, II Kings 20:17): “Behold a time will come when everything in your palace...will be carried off, etc.,” and in (II) Chronicles (32:25): “But Hezekiah did not pay back according to the benefit they had done for him, but his heart became haughty; therefore, wrath came upon him and upon Judah, etc.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:17:2)


**וַתֵּקַע נַפְשָׁהּ מֵהֶם.** שֶׁמָּרַד בָּהֶם יְהוֹיָקִים וְצִדְקִיָּהוּ (מלכים ב כד:א, כ), וַתֵּקַע אשלוקי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, **וַתֵּקַע** כְּמוֹ ״וַתֵּקַע כַּף יֶרֶךְ יַעֲקֹב״ (בראשית לב:כו), הוּקְעָה, לְשׁוֹן חִבּוּר וּתְקִיעַת עֵץ בָּאָרֶץ הוּא, וְכֵן בְּהִשְׁתַּלְפוֹ מִן הָאָרֶץ יִפּוֹל בּוֹ לְשׁוֹן קְעִיָּה, כַּאֲשֶׁר תֹּאמַר ״וַתַּשְׁרֵשׁ שָׁרָשֶׁיהָ״ (תהילים פ:י) לְשׁוֹן קְלִיטָה, ״וּבְכָל תְּבוּאָתִי תְשָׁרֵשׁ״ (איוב לא:יב) לְשׁוֹן עִקּוּר, וְכֵן ״בִּסְעִיפֶיהָ פֹּרִיָּה״ (ישעיהו יז:ו) לְשׁוֹן עֲנָפִים, ״וּמְסָעֵף פָּארָה״ (ישעיהו י:לג) לְשׁוֹן קוֹצֵץ עַנְפֵי הָעֵץ וּפְאֵרוֹתָיו:


**her soul was disgusted with them** [Heb. וַתֵּקַע.] That Jehoiakim and Zedekiah rebelled against them. וַתֵּקַע is esloched in Old French, dislocated. וַתֵּקַע is like (Gen. 32: 26): “and the upper joint of Jacob’s thigh was dislocated (וַתֵּקַע).” הוֹקָעָה is an expression of joining and inserting a pole into the earth, and also when it is withdrawn from the earth, is the expression קְעִיָה appropriate for it, just as you say (Ps. 80:10): “it took root (וַתַשְׁרֵשׁ),” as an expression of taking root, and (Job 31:12), “and it uproots (תְּשָׁרֵשׁ) all my grain,” as an expression of uprooting. Similarly (Isa. 17:6): “on its branches (בִּסְעִפֶיהָ) when it produces fruit,” is an expression of branches, and (ibid. 10:33), “lops off (מְסָעֵף) the branches,” is an expression of cutting off the branches of the tree and its boughs.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:19:1)


**לִזְכֹּר אֶת יְמֵי נְעוּרֶיהָ.** עָלְתָה עַל לִבָּהּ תַּאֲוַת בְּנֵי מִצְרַיִם, שֶׁשָּׁלַח צִדְקִיָּהוּ אֲלֵיהֶם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וַיִּמְרֹד בּוֹ לִשְׁלֹחַ מַלְאָכָיו מִצְרַיִם״ (יחזקאל יז:טו):


**to remember the days of her youth** The desire for the Egyptians rose in her heart, for Zedekiah sent to them, as it is said (above 17:15): “But he rebelled against him by sending to Egypt.”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:20:1)


**וַתַּעְגְּבָה עַל פִּלַגְשֵׁיהֶם.** עַל פִּלַּגְשׁוּתָם, לִהְיוֹת לָהֶם לְפִילֶּגֶשׁ:


**And she lusted for their concubinage** [Heb. פִּלַּגְשֵׁיהֶם,] for their concubinage, to be a concubine to them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:20:2)


**אֲשֶׁר בְּשַׂר חֲמוֹרִים בְּשָׂרָם.** אֵבֶר תַּשְׁמִישׁ:


**those whose flesh is the flesh of donkeys** [i.e.,] the phallus.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:20:3)


**וְזִרְמַת סוּסִים.** לְשׁוֹן רִבּוּי תַּשְׁמִישׁ; שֶׁמַּרְבִּים תַּשְׁמִישׁ יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר כָּל הַזְּכָרִים שֶׁבַּבְּהֵמָה:


**and...is the issue of horses** An expression of a profusion of sexual intercourse, for they engage in sexual intercourse more than any other male animals.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:20:4)


**זִרְמַת.** קִלּוּחַ הַזֶּרַע, כְּמוֹ ״זֶרֶם מַיִם עָבָר״ (חבקוק ג:י):


זִרְמַת refers to the stream of semen, like (Habakkuk 3:10): “A stream (זֶרֶם) of water passed.”



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:21:1)


**וַתִּפְקְדִי.** וַתִּזְכְּרִי:


**And you remembered** [Heb. וַתִּפְקְדִי,] and you remembered.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:21:2)


**בַּעֲשׂוֹת.** לְשׁוֹן כְּבִישָׁה:


**were squeezed** [Heb. בַּעֲשׂוֹת,] an expression of pressing.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:22:1)


**אֶת מְאַהֲבַיִךְ.** אֶת כַּשְׂדִּים, אֲשֶׁר עָרְבוּ עַל חִזְקִיָּהוּ (ישעיה לט:א-ב):


**your lovers** The Chaldeans, who pleased Hezekiah.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:22:2)


**אֶת אֲשֶׁר נָקְעָה נַפְשֵׁךְ מֵהֶם.** אֲשֶׁר חָזַר צִדְקִיָּהוּ וּשְׂנָאָם וּמָרַד בָּהֶם (מלכים ב כד:כ):


**those with whom your soul has become disgusted** Whom Zedekiah [in a change of heart] subsequently hated and against whom he rebelled.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:23:1)


**פְּקוֹד וְשׁוֹעַ וְקוֹעַ.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן שֵׁם מְדִינוֹת, וּמִקְרָא מְסַיְּעוֹ (ירמיהו נ:כא) ״עַל הָאָרֶץ מְרָתַיִם עֲלֵה עָלֶיהָ וְעַל יוֹשְׁבֵי פְּקוֹד״, וְהַפּוֹתְרִים אוֹמְרִים לְשׁוֹן פְּקִידִים וְשָׂרִים וְשַׁלְטוֹנִים:


**Pekod, Shoa, and Koa** Jonathan rendered [these as] the names of states, and Scripture supports him in the Book of Jeremiah (50:21): “Upon the rebellious land, march upon her, and upon the inhabitants of Pekod.” [But] the Commentators say that they are an expression of appointees, princes, and rulers.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:23:2)


**וּקְרוּאִים.** שְׁמוֹנְ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and summoned ones** Somonz, semonz in Old French, summoned (top people).



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:24:1)


**הֹצֶן.** פֵּרַשׁ דּוּנַשׁ: אֶחָד מִכְּלֵי הַמִּלְחָמָה הוּא; וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר: לְשׁוֹן הַקָּפוֹת מַחֲנוֹת, כְּמוֹ ״וְלִצְנִינִים בְּצִדֵּיכֶם״ (במדבר לג:נה) – ״וּלְמַשְׁרְיָין מַקְּפַנְכוֹן״. **הֹצֶן** – (מְנַחֵם פֵּרְשׁוּ כְּמוֹ ״צִנָּה וְסֹחֵרָה אֲמִתּוֹ״ (תהלים צא:ד); וְאֵין דּוּנַשׁ מוֹדֶה לוֹ; וְהַתַּרְגּוּם שֶׁמְּתַרְגֵּם ״וְיֵיתוֹן עֲלָךְ בְּמָאנֵי זַיְנָא״ מְסַיֵּעַ לְדוּנַשׁ): הֹצֶן כְּמוֹ בְּהֹצֶן. וְיֵשׁ מַשְׁמָע הֹצֶן כְּמוֹ ׳חֹצֶן׳, מִן ״וְהֵבִיאוּ בָנַיִךְ בְּחֹצֶן״ (ישעיהו מט:כב), שֶׁפֵּרוּשׁוֹ: זְרוֹעַ, וְכֵן **וּבָאוּ עָלַיִךְ הֹצֶן** (כאן), וְעִנְיָנוֹ: חַיִל, כְּמוֹ ״וּזְרֹעוֹת הַנֶּגֶב״ (דניאל יא:טו), שֶׁפֵּרוּשׁוֹ: וְחַיָּלוֹת הַנֶּגֶב:


**weapons** [Heb. הֹצֶן]. Dunash (p. 59) explained it as one type of weapon, but I say that it is an expression of the surroundings of camps like (Num. 33:55): “and camps (וְלִצְנִינִים) in your sides,” [which Onkelos renders:] and camps surrounding you. Menachem (p. 150) explains הֹצֶן like (Ps. 91:4): “His truth is an encompassing shield (צִנָה וְסֹחֵרָה), but Dunash does not agree with him, and the Targum supports Dunash, rendering: they will come upon you with weapons. הֹצֶן is like בְּהֹצֶן, with weapons, and הֹצֶן could mean the same as חֹצֶן, from (Isa. 49:22): “and they shall bring your sons בְּחֹצֶן,”which means with the arm, and so too: “And they will come upon you הֹצֶן,” meaning [as with] an army, as (Dan. 11:15), “and the arms (וּזְרוֹעוֹת) of the south,” meaning the armies of the south.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:24:2)


**וְנָתַתִּי לִפְנֵיהֶם מִשְׁפָּט.** מַה שֶּׁעָלַי לִשְׁפּוֹט, אֶעֱשֶׂה אוֹתָם שְׁלוּחַי לַעֲשׂוֹת הַמִּשְׁפָּט:


**and I shall place judgment before them** What is incumbent upon Me to judge. I will make them My agents to execute the judgment.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:24:3)


**בְּמִשְׁפְּטֵיהֶם.** מִשְׁפָּט שֶׁלֹּא כָתַבְתִּי בְּתוֹרָתִי, וְהוּא מִמִּשְׁפְּטֵי נִימוּסֵיהֶם; וְאֵיזֶה זֶה? זֶה סִימוּי עֵינַיִם, שֶׁעִוְּרוּ אֶת עֵינֵי צִדְקִיָּהוּ (מלכים ב כה:ז):


**with their judgments** A judgment that I did not write in My Torah, but which is one of the judgments of their customs, and what is it? Blinding the eyes, that they blinded Zedekiah’s eyes.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:25:1)


**אַפֵּךְ וְאָזְנַיִךְ.** הֵם תִּפְאֶרֶת הַפַּרְצוּף, וְלָהֶם הִמְשִׁיל הַכְּהוּנָּה וְהַמַּלְכוּת:


**your nose and your ears** They are the beauty of the countenance, and to them he compared the priesthood and the kingship.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:25:2)


**וְאַחֲרִיתֵךְ.** שְׁאָר הָעָם:


**and your survivors** The rest of the people.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:25:3)


**וְאַחֲרִיתֵךְ תֵּאָכֵל בָּאֵשׁ.** ״וּשְׁפַר אַרְעִיךְ״ (תרגום יונתן), שֶׁהֵן ׳אַחֲרִיוּת׳ לִישָּׁעֵן עֲלֵיהֶם לְפַרְנָסָה:


**and your residue will be consumed by fire** And the best of your land, which is the security upon which to rely for a livelihood.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:26:1)


**אֶת בְּגָדַיִךְ.** בִּגְדֵי כְהֻנָּה:


**of your garments** The priestly raiment.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:28:1)


**אֲשֶׁר שָׂנֵאת.** כַּשְׂדִּים שֶׁמָּרַדְתָּ בָּהֶם (מלכים ב כד:כ):


**those you hate** The Chaldeans, against whom you rebelled.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:30:1)


**עָשֹׂה אֵלֶּה.** לְשׁוֹן פָּעוּל, פיישנ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**These will be done** [Heb. עָשׂה,] an expression of continuous action, faisant in French, doing (gerundive).



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:32:1)


**מִרְבָּה לְהָכִיל.** יְתֵרָה מִלְּהָכִיל, כְּמוֹ מִלִּסְבּוֹל:


**too much to bear** Too much to contain, like to bear.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:33:1)


**וּשְׁמָמָה.** תִּמָּהוֹן:


**and bewilderment** [Heb. וּשְׁמָמָה] bewilderment.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:34:1)


**וּמָצִית.** לְשׁוֹן מוֹצֵץ עַד הַתַּמְצִית, אישוצרא״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:
