**and drain** [Heb. וּמָצְת,] an expression of sucking to the last drop, et tu suceras in French, and you will suck.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:34:2)


**וְאֶת חֲרָשֶׂיהָ.** דִּמָּה כּוֹס הַמַּשְׁקֶה פֻּרְעָנוּת לְיִשְׂרָאֵל לִכְלִי חֶרֶשׂ, שֶׁמִּשֶּׁנִּשְׁתַּבֵּר אֵין לוֹ תַּקָּנָה, כָּךְ מִשֶּׁתָּבֹא תְּשׁוּעָתָם וְיִשָּׁבֵר הַכּוֹס, לֹא יָשׁוּב לְהַשְׁקוֹתָם עוֹד; וּכְשֶׁהוּא מְדַבֵּר בְּהַשְׁקָאַת הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת, קוֹרֵא אוֹתוֹ זָהָב, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״כּוֹס זָהָב בָּבֶל בְּיַד ה׳ הַמְשַׁכֶּרֶת כָּל הָאָרֶץ״ (ירמיהו נא:ז):


**and its shards** He compares the cup that gives Israel the drink of retribution to an earthenware vessel, which, once broken, can no longer be repaired. So will it be that once their salvation comes and the cup breaks, He will not return to give them any more to drink. When He speaks of giving drink to the nations, however, He calls it gold, as it is said (Jer. 51:7): “Babylon is a golden cup in the Lord’s hand, it intoxicates all the earth.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:34:3)


**וְאֶת חֲרָשֶׂיהָ תְּגָרֵמִי.** כְּמוֹ ״וְעַצְמֹתֵיהֶם יְגָרֵם״ (במדבר כד:ח), ״לֹא גָרְמוּ לַבֹּקֶר״ (צפניה ג:ג), רוֹנְגִיי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, גּוֹרֵר בְּשִׁנָּיו אֶת סְבִיבוֹת הָעֶצֶם אוֹ הַחֶרֶשׂ, לִרְדּוֹת מֵעָלָיו בָּשָׂר הַדָּבוּק בּוֹ. וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן שְׁבִירָה, וְגַם כֵּן פָּתַר ״וְעַצְמֹתֵיהֶם יְגָרֵם״ (במדבר כד:ח), וְכֵן ״לֹא גָרְמוּ לַבֹּקֶר״ (צפניה ג:ג):


**you shall scrape** [Heb. תְּגָרֵמִי,] like (Num. 24:8): “and he scrapes (יְגָרֵם) their bones”; (Zeph. 3:3), “they did not leave over the bones (גָרְמוּ) for morning,” ronger in French, to gnaw. He scrapes with his teeth around the bone or the shard to detach from it the meat adhering to it. Menachem (p. 59) connects it to an expression of breaking, and he interprets: “and he breaks their bones,” and “they did not break the bones for morning,” in like manner.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:35:1)


**שְׂאִי זִמָּתֵךְ.** ״קַבִּילִי חוֹבֵי עֲצַת חֲטָאָיִךְ״ (תרגום יונתן):


**bear your lewdness** Accept the liabilities of the counsel of your sins.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:36:1)


**הֲתִשְׁפּוֹט.** לְשׁוֹן וִכּוּחַ:


**will you judge** [Heb. הֲתִשׁפֹט,] an expression of debate.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:37:1)


**וְדָם בִּידֵיהֶן.** בְּנִאוּפֵיהֶם שָׁפְכוּ דָּם:


**and blood is on their hands** Through their adulteries, they shed blood.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:37:2)


**וְאֶת גִּלּוּלֵיהֶן נִאֵפוּ.** נִאוּף שֶׁלָּהֶם עִם גִּלּוּלֵיהֶם הָיָה:


**and with their idols they have committed adultery** Their adultery was with their idols.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:37:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:37:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:37:3)


**וְגַם אֶת בְּנֵיהֶן וְגוֹ׳.** וְזֶהוּ שְׁפִיכוּת הַדָּם שֶׁבִּידֵיהֶם (כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁהוֹצִיאוּ זֶרַע לְבַטָּלָה בִּידֵיהֶם וְלֹא עָשׂוּ תְּשׁוּבָה):


**and also their children, etc.** And this is the bloodshed that is on their hands (i.e., they masturbated with their hands and they did not repent).



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:38:1)


**טִמְּאוּ אֶת מִקְדָּשִׁי בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא.** בַּיּוֹם שֶׁשּׁטוּ אֶת בְּנֵיהֶם לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, בָּאוֹת לְפָנַי בְּמִקְדָּשִׁי לַעֲשׂוֹת בּוֹ תּוֹעֵבָה לְהַכְעִיסֵנִי:


**they defiled My sanctuary on that day** On the day they slaughter their children for idolatry, they come before Me in My sanctuary to commit abominations therein, to provoke Me.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:38:2)


**וְאֶת שַׁבְּתוֹתַי חִלֵּלוּ.** שֶׁהָיוּ שׁוֹחֲטִין אֶת בְּנֵיהֶם בְּשַׁבָּת:


**and they profaned My Sabbaths** For they would slaughter their children on the Sabbath.



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:40:1)


**וְאַף כִּי תִשְׁלַחְנָה.** וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן קָשֶׁה מִן הַכֹּל, כִּי **תִשְׁלַחְנָה** לְהָבִיא אֲלֵיהֶן מִשָּׂרֵי הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת אוֹיְבֵיהֶן, וְרוֹאִין הַחִלּוּל שֶׁמְּחַלְּלוֹת מִקְדָּשִׁי:


**And furthermore, that they would send** [Heb. וְאַף כִּי.] And surely, worst of all is that they would send to bring to them [invite] some of the princes of the nations, their lovers, and these see the profanation with which they profane My sanctuary.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:40:2)


**שָׁלוּחַ אֲלֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳.** מֵאֵת אָהֳלָה וְאָהֳלִיבָה לָבֹא אֲלֵיהֶם:


**to whom an emissary is sent, etc.** from Oholah and Oholibah to come to them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:40:3)


**וְהִנֵּה בָאוּ לַאֲשֶׁר רָחַצְתְּ כָּחַלְתְּ וְגוֹ׳. לַאֲשֶׁר** – תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לַאֲתַר״; אֵל לוּ״אָ בְּלַעַ״ז, אֶל מָקוֹם אֲשֶׁר יִעַדְתְּ לָהֶם לֵאמֹר: בְּמָקוֹם פְּלוֹנִי נֵצֵא לִקְרַאתְכֶם, מְקֻשָּׁטוֹת בְּמֶרְחָץ וְכָחוֹל:


**and behold they came to the place where you bathed, daubed, etc.** [Heb. לַאֲשֶׁר,] to which. Jonathan renders: לַאֲתַר, to the place, au lieu in Modern French. To the place that you designated for them, saying, “In such and such a place, we will come out to meet you, beautified by the bathing and the daubing.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:40:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:40:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:40:4)


**כָּחַלְתְּ.** מִין צֶבַע וּשְׁמוֹ כָּחוֹל, וְצוֹבְעוֹת בּוֹ עֵינֵיהֶם, אוֹקְלִיר״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**daubed** [Heb. כָּחַלְתָּ,] a kind of dye named כָּחוֹל, with which they color their eyes, ecler in Old French, to brighten.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:41:1)


**מִטָּה כְבוּדָּה.** מְכֻלֶּלֶת בְּמַצָּעוֹת נָאוֹת, פְּרִידִ״י בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a stately bed** Adorned with all kinds of beautiful bedclothes, parede in Old French, adorned lavishly.



### Verse 42

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:42:1)


**וְקוֹל הָמוֹן.** שֶׁל אוּמּוֹת רַבּוֹת הָיוּ בָהּ, וְלֹא הָמוֹן שֶׁל מִלְחָמָה, אֶלָּא **קוֹל הָמוֹן שָׁלֵו**:


**And the sound of a multitude** of many nanons were in her; not a multitude that came for war, but the voice of a tranquil multitude.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:42:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:42:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:42:2)


**וְאֶל אֲנָשִׁים.** בִּשְׁבִיל אֲנָשִׁים וְשֶׁל אֲנָשִׁים הָיוּ אוֹתוֹ הָמוֹן, וְלֹא שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה:


**for the sake of men** [Heb. וְאֶל אֶנָשִׁים,] for the sake of men. That multitude was of men, not of animals.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:42:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:42:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:42:3)


**מֵרֹב אָדָם.** שֶׁהָיוּ בְתוֹכָהּ, הַמּוּבָאִים לְנִיאוּף מִן הַמִּדְבָּר:


**of the many people** who were in her midst, who were brought to commit adultery, from the desert.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:42:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:42:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:42:4)


**סָבָאִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ דָּרִין סָבִיב לָהּ, מִשְּׁכֵנֶיהָ עַמּוֹן וּמוֹאָב; וְכֵן תַּרְגּוּם **סָבָאִים**: ״מִסְּחוֹר סְחוֹר״:


**those dwelling all around** [Heb. סָבָאִים,] people who lived around her. Of her neighbors, Ammon and Moab, and so did [Jonathan] render סָבָאִים “from all around.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:42:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:42:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:42:5)


**וַיִּתְּנוּ.** אָהֳלָה וְאָהֳלִיבָה **צְמִידִים אֶל יְדֵיהֶן.** לְהִתְקַשֵּׁט לְנִאוּף, כְּלוֹמַר: עַמּוֹן וּמוֹאָב מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהֵן רַבִּים, הִיא מִתְקַשֶּׁטֶת וּמִתְפָּאֶרֶת לָהֶם; וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהֵם שְׁכֵינֶיהָ, מִתְפָּאֶרֶת לְעֵינֵיהֶם בְּקִשּׁוּטֶיהָ. לְפִיכָךְ וַיִּתְּנוּ אָהֳלָה וְאָהֳלִיבָה צְמִידִים עַל יְדֵיהֶם, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהוּ נָאוֹת בְּעֵינֵי הַנּוֹאֲפִים. וָי״ו שֶׁל וַיִּתְּנוּ יְתֵרָה, כְּמוֹ אוֹתָהּ שֶׁל ״עָזִּי וְזִמְרָת יָהּ וַיְהִי לִי לִישׁוּעָה״ (שמות טו:ב), וּכְמוֹ אוֹתָהּ שֶׁל ״וַאֲשֶׁר לֹא שָׂם לִבּוֹ אֶל דְּבַר ה׳ וַיַּעֲזֹב״ (שמות ט:כא):


**they placed** Oholah and Oholibah [placed] bracelets on their hands to adorn themselves in order to commit adultery; i.e., Ammon and Moab, since they are many, she adorns and beautifies herself for them, and because they are her neighbors, she beautifies herself before their eyes with her ornaments; therefore, Oholah and Oholibah put bracelets on their hands in order to be beautiful in the eyes of the adulterers. The “vav” of וַיִתְּנוּ is superfluous, like that of (Exod. 15:2): “The strength and the cutting of God was (וַיְהִי) my salvation,” and like that of (ibid. 9:21): “But whoever did not take the word of the Lord to heart left (וַיַעֲזֹב).”



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:43:1)


**וָאֹמַר לַבָּלָה נִאוּפִים עַתָּה יִזְנוּ וְגוֹ׳.** אָמַר לִי לִבִּי עַל זוֹ שֶׁבָּלְתָה וְהִזְקִינָה בְּנִאוּפִים, מֵעַתָּה שֶׁהִזְקִינָה, **יִזְנוּ תַזְנוּתֶהָ** מִמֶּנָּה, וְתֵצֵא תַּאֲוַת זְנוּתָהּ מִלִּבָּהּ וְתַחְזֹר בָּהּ, וַהֲרֵי הִיא עוֹדֶנָּה בִּזְנוּנֶיהָ כַּאֲשֶׁר בַּתְּחִלָּה:


**And I said of the one who had aged [in] adulteries, “Now her harlotries will end,” etc.** I said to Myself about this one who had become worn out and aged in her adulteries, “From now on, since she has aged, her harlotries will leave her, and the desires for her harlotry will leave her heart, and she will repent,” but she is still in her harlotries as in the beginning.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:43:2)


**יִזְנוּ.** יֵצְאוּ; כָּל ״זוֹנָה״ תִּרְגּוּם ׳נָפְקַת בָּרָא׳ (תרגום אונקלוס בראשית לד:לא; תרגום יונתן יחזקאל טז:ל). כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**will end** [Heb. יִזְנוּ] will leave. Every “harlot” (זוֹנָה) is translated into Aramaic as נֳפְקֲת בְּרָא, one who goes out. So did I hear, and so did Jonathan render.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:43:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:43:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:43:3)


**לַבָּלָה נִאוּפִים.** כְּמוֹ לַבָּלָה בְּנִאוּפִים; כַּאֲשֶׁר תֹּאמַר ״וַתֵּשֶׁב בֵּית אָבִיהָ״ (בראשית לח:יא) – כְּמוֹ ״בְּבֵית אָבִיהָ״ (במדבר ל:ד); ״יִרְחֲצוּ מַיִם״ (שמות ל:כ) – כְּמוֹ ׳יִרְחֲצוּ בְּמַיִם׳; ״וְיָצְאָה חִנָּם״ (שמות כא:יא); ״וְאִם בֵּית אִישָׁהּ נָדָרָה״ (במדבר ל:יא); וְכֵן פֵּרֵשׁ דּוּנָשׁ. וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ עִם ״תֶּבֶל עָשׂוּ״ (ויקרא כ:יב), וְלֹא יִתָּכְנוּ דְּבָרָיו:


**of the one who had aged [in] adulteries** like: [lit. to the one who had aged adulteries,] the one who had aged in adulteries, as you say (Gen. 38:11): “and she remained her father’s house,” is like “in her father’s house”; (Exod. 30:20), “they shall wash water,” is like “they shall wash with water.” [Likewise,] (ibid.): “And he shall go out freedom;” [and] (Num. 30:11): “But if she made a vow [in] her father’s house.” So did Dunash (p. 17) explain [this]. However, Menachem connected it (p. 45) to (Lev 20:12): “they committed a disgraceful act (תֶּבֶל),” but his words are impossible.



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:44:1)


**וַיָּבוֹא אֵלֶיהָ.** כָּל אֲשֶׁר מִלְּאוֹ לִבּוֹ:


**And he came to her** whoever so desired


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:44:2)


**אִשּׁוֹת הַזִּמָּה.** נְשֵׁי הַזִּמָּה:


**the lewd women** [Heb. אִשֹׁתהַזִמָּה,] [like] נְשֵׁי הַזִמָּה, women of lewdness.



### Verse 45

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:45:1)


**וַאֲנָשִׁים צַדִּיקִים.** אֲנָשִׁים דַּיָּנִים, שָׂרֵי מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל וּמֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״זַכָּאִין בְּקִבְלֵיהוֹן״ – לְפִי מַעֲשֵׂיהֶן שֶׁל אֵלּוּ הַזּוֹנוֹת, **צַדִּיקִים** הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת מֵהֵנָּה:


**And righteous men** Judges, the princes of the king of Babylon and the king of Assyria. But Jonathan renders: righteous, as compared to them. According to the deeds of these harlots, the nations are more righteous than they.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:45:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:45:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:45:2)


**מִשְׁפַּט נֹאֲפוֹת וּמִשְׁפַּט שׁוֹפְכוֹת דָּם.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ אַחֲרָיו (יחזקאל כג:מו-מט):


**the judgment of adulteresses, etc.** as is delineated further.



### Verse 46

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:46:1)


**הַעֲלֵה.** וְנָתֹן, וּבְרָא, כֻּלָּן לְשׁוֹן עֲשֵׂה וּפָעוֹל הֵם:


**Let...be brought up...and let them be submitted** All these are expressions of doing and of a continuous action.



### Verse 47

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:47:1)


**וְרָגְמוּ עֲלֵיהֶן אֶבֶן.** הֲרִיגַת בְּנֵיהֶן וּבְנוֹתֵיהֶן וּשְׂרֵיפַת בָּתֵּיהֶן הוּא **מִשְׁפַּט נוֹאֲפוֹת**:


**shall pelt them with stone** The slaying of their sons and daughters and the burning of their houses is the judgment of adulteresses.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:47:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:47:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:47:2)


**וּבָרֵא אוֹתְהֶן בְּחַרְבוֹתָם.** הוּא מִשְׁפַּט שׁוֹפְכוֹת דָּם, וּבָרֵא לְשׁוֹן קְצִיצָה, וְכֵן ״וּבֵרֵאתוֹ״ דְּ״יַעַר יִהְיֶה לָךְ״ בְּסֵפֶר יְהוֹשֻׁעַ (יהושע יז:יח):


**and slash them with their swords** That is the judgment of murderesses. [The word] וּבָרֵא is an expression of cutting, and so: “and you shall cut it down (וּבֵרֵאתוֹ),” of “the forest shall be yours” in the Book of Joshua (17:18).



### Verse 49

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 23:49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/23:49:1)


**וְנָתְנוּ זִמַּתְכֶנָה עֲלֵיכֶן.** שׁוֹפְטֵיכֶן יִתְּנוּ אֶת מִשְׁפַּט זִמַּתְכֶן בְּרָאשֵׁיכֶן:


**And they shall put their lewdness upon you** Your judges shall put the judgment of your lewdness on your heads.



## Chapter 24


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:1:1)


**הַתְּשִׁיעִית.** לְצִדְקִיָּהוּ:


**in the ninth year** of Zedekiah.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:2:1)


**סָמַךְ מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל.** הַיּוֹם צָר עָלֶיהָ:


**the king of Babylon has besieged** Today he besieged it.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:3:1)


**שְׁפֹת הַסִּיר.** עַל פִּי הַכִּירָה בִּמְקוֹם מוֹשָׁבָהּ; הִיא קְרוּיָה ׳שְׁפִיתָה׳ בִּלְשׁוֹן הַמִּשְׁנָה, שֶׁאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (שבת לח:): מְקוֹם שְׁפִיתַת שְׁתֵּי קְדֵירוֹת; וְעוֹד שָׁנִינוּ (ביצה לד.): אֶחָד שׁוֹפֵת אֶת הַקְּדֵירָה וְאֶחָד נוֹתֵן לְתוֹכָהּ מַיִם וְאֶחָד נוֹתֵן לְתוֹכָהּ תַּבְלִין; לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁהַשְּׁפִיתָה הִיא הוֹשָׁבָתָהּ עַל פִּי הַכִּירָה, עַד שֶׁלֹּא יִתְּנוּ לְתוֹכָהּ כְּלוּם. וְהַדּוּגְמָא זוֹ עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, שֶׁאֲמַרְתֶּם עָלֶיהָ ״הִיא הַסִּיר״ (יחזקאל יא:ג) – עַתָּה בָּא הָעֵת שֶׁתִּהְיֶה לְסִיר וְאַתֶּם בְּתוֹכָהּ לְבָשָׂר (יחזקאל יא:ד), בָּא נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר וְיִתְּנֶנָּה עַל הָאוּר. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ לְשׁוֹן שִׂימָה:


**Set the pot** [Heb. שְׂפֹת, set.] On the mouth of the stove on the place where it is set. That is called שְׂפִיתָה in Mishnaic Hebrew, for our Rabbis said (Shab. 38b): “[A stove has] a place for the setting of ] שְׂפִיתַת[two pots.” We learned also (Beizah 34a): “If one sets the pot, one puts in water, and one puts in the condiments...” We learn that is the placing it on the mouth of the stove before they put anything into it. This figure represents Jerusalem, about which you said, “It is the pot.” Now the time has come for it to become a pot, and you will become the meat inside it, and Nebuchadnezzar is coming and will put in on the fire. Menachem (p. 179) explained it as an expression of placing.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:3:2)


**וְגַם יְצֹק בּוֹ מָיִם.** הֵם צָרְכֵי הַמָּצוֹר, **יְצֹק** כְּמוֹ ״שְׁפוֹךְ״ (שופטים ו:כ):


**and also pour water into it** These [represent] the accessories for the siege. יִצֹק is like שְׁפֹךְ, pour.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:4:1)


**אֱסֹף נְתָחֶיהָ אֵלֶיהָ.** מִיִּרְאַת הַמָּצוֹר אָסְפוּ לְתוֹכָהּ כָּל רָאשֶׁיהָ וְשָׂרֶיהָ:


**Gather its pieces to it** Out of fear of the siege, gather into it all its leaders and its princes.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:4:2)


**כָּל נֵתַח טוֹב.** ״כָּל גְּבַר גִּיבָּר אֵימְתָן וּגְבַר עָבִיד קְרָב״, כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**every good piece** Every mighty, awesome hero and every warrior.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:5:1)


**מִבְחַר הַצֹּאן לָקוֹחַ.** קַח אַתָּה, נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, אֶת מִבְחַר חֲיָלֶיךָ סְבִיבוֹתֶיהָ, כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן; וְאֵינוֹ מְיֻשָּׁב עַל עִנְיַן הַסִּיר. וְכֵן צָרִיךְ לְפוֹתְרוֹ: **מִבְחַר** יִשְׂרָאֵל יָבֹאוּ וְיִכָּנְסוּ בְּתוֹכָהּ:


**Take the choice of the flocks** You, Nebuchadnezzar, take the choicest of your armies around it. So did Jonathan render, but this does not fit with the idea of the pot. This must therefore be interpreted to mean that the choicest of Israel will come and enter into its midst.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:5:2)


**וְגַם דּוּר הָעֲצָמִים תַּחְתֶּיהָ.** סַדֵּר וְעָרוֹךְ הָעֲצָמִים תַּחַת שְׁאָר הָאֵיבָרִים; כְּלוֹמַר: יִלָּחֲמוּ הַגִּבּוֹרִים בִּשְׁבִיל הַחֲלָשִׁים וְיִסְמְכוּ אֵלּוּ עֲלֵיהֶן. **דּוּר** לְשׁוֹן עֲרִיכָה; וְכֵן ״יִצְנָפְךָ צְנֵפָה כַּדּוּר״ (ישעיהו כב:יח) – שׁוּרוֹת הֶיקֵּף גַּיָּסוֹת; וְכֵן ״שׁוּרָה״ תַּרְגּוּם ״דָּרָא״ (תענית כב:). וּמְנַחֵם פֵּירֵשׁ כְּמוֹ ״אֲנִי אַגְדִּיל הַמְּדוּרָה״ (יחזקאל כד:ט):


**and also arrange the limbs under it** [Heb. דוּר,] an expression of arranging, and so (Isa. 22:18): “He shall wind you around like a turban, like a surrounding wall (כַּדוּר),” a row of surrounding troops. A row (דוּרָה) is translated into Aramaic as דַּרָא. However, Menachem explains it (p. 67) as (verse 9): “Even I shall enlarge the fire (הַמְדוּרָה) .”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:5:3)


**רַתַּח רְתָחֶיהָ.** וְאַחַר שֶׁיְּהוּ כֻּלָּם לְתוֹכָהּ, יְקָרְבוּ כְּלֵי הַמָּצוֹר וְהַמִּלְחָמָה אֶל הָעִיר:


**make it boil well** After they all are inside it, the siegeworks and the armaments will come near the city.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:6:1)


**חֶלְאָתָהּ.** זוֹהֲמָתָהּ שֶׁמּוֹצִיאִין מֵעַל פִּיהָ עַל יְדֵי הַרְתָָּחָתָהּ, שֶׁקּוֹרִין ׳אֶשְׁקוּמָ״א׳ בְּלַעַ״ז; כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא יָצְאוּ מִמֶּנָּה רְשָׁעִים שֶׁבָּהּ:


**and its filth** Its filth, which they remove from its surface by boiling it, is called escume in Old French, foam, ecume in Modern French. This means: the wicked within her have not come out.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:6:2)


**לִנְתָחֶיהָ לִנְתָחֶיהָ.** גָּלוּת אַחַר גָּלוּת, הַיּוֹצֵא וְנָפַל אֶל הַכַּשְׂדִּים, אִישׁ אִישׁ לְבַדּוֹ:


**limb by limb** Exile after exile, who went out and defected to the Chaldeans, every man by himself.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:6:3)


**לֹא נָפַל עָלֶיהָ גּוֹרָל.** לֹא כִּשְׁאָר קְדֵרוֹת שֶׁמּוֹצִיאִין נְתָחֵיהֶן יַחַד, לְתִתָּהּ לִפְנֵי כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד חֶלְקוֹ וְגוֹרָלוֹ; וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״מִדְּלָא יִתְעֲבִיד בָּהּ תְּיוּבָא״ – **לֹא נָפַל עָלֶיהָ** גּוֹרָלוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם:


**no lot has fallen on it** Not like other pots, whose pieces they take out all at once in order to place before each one his portion and his lot. Jonathan renders: because they did not repent within it. [Therefore,] the lot of the Omnipresent did not fall on it.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:7:1)


**צְחִיחַ.** שְׁעִיעוּת (ראה רש״י יחזקאל טז:ד):


**smooth** [Heb. צְחִיחַ,] smoothness.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:7:2)


**לֹא שְׁפָכַתְהוּ עַל הָאָרֶץ וְגוֹ׳.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״בְּזִידוֹ וּבִדְרַע מְרוֹמָם שְׁפָכְתִּיהּ, לֹא שְׁפָכְתִּיהּ בְּשָׁלוּ לְמִיעֲבַד עֲלוֹהִי תְּיוּבָא״; וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּהוּ (איכה רבה פתיחתא ה, גטין נז:) בְּדָמוֹ שֶׁל זְכַרְיָה בֶּן יְהוֹיָדָע, לֹא נָהֲגוּ בּוֹ לֹא כְּדַם הַצְּבִי וְלֹא כְּדַם הָאַיִל:


**she did not pour it out on the ground, etc.** Jonathan paraphrased: with willful wickedness and with an arm raised on high did she shed it; she did not shed it unintentionally to repent of it. Our Rabbis (Git. 57b, Pesikta d’Rav Kahana 122a) explained it as referring to the blood of Zechariah the son of Jehoiada. They did not treat it as the blood of the deer or the blood of the gazelle.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:8:1)


**לְהַעֲלוֹת חֵמָה.** לְפָנַי, צִוִּיתִי לְאוֹתוֹ דָּם שֶׁלֹּא יִבָּלַע בַּקַּרְקַע, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּזָּכֵר **לִנְקֹם** נִקְמָתוֹ:


**So that it might bring up fury** before Me. I [therefore (Etz Chaim ms.)] commanded that blood not to be absorbed into the ground, in order that it might be remembered, so that My vengeance might be taken.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:9:1)


**הַמְּדוּרָה.** הֶסֵּק גְּדוֹלָה פואיא״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the fire** [Heb. הַמְדוּרָה,] a great kindling, foaie in Old French, a pyre.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:10:1)


**הַרְבֵּה הָעֵצִים הַדְלֵק הָאֵשׁ.** לְהַרְבּוֹת הָעֵצִים וּלְהַדְלִיק הָאֵשׁ:


**pile on the wood, kindle the fire** And to pile on the wood, to kindle the fire.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:10:2)


**הָתֵם הַבָּשָׂר וְהַרְקַח הַמֶּרְקָחָה.** כְּשֶׁהַבָּשָׂר חַם מֵעַל הָעֲצָמוֹת וְהוּא מִצְטַמֵּק וּמְעֹרָב בָּרוֹטֶב וּמֵגִיס בַּכַּף, דּוֹמֶה לְמִרְקַחַת פִּטּוּם סַמָּמְנִין, שֶׁמְּעָרְבִין אוֹתָהּ יָפֶה יָפֶה, וּמִתּוֹךְ צִימוּק הַבָּשָׂר עֲשָׁנָהּ נֶהְפַּךְ לְתוֹכָהּ, שֶׁקּוֹרִין אאורש״יר בְּלַעַ״ז, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר **וְהָעֲצָמוֹת יֵחָרוּ**, יִבְשׁוּ וְיֵעָשְׁנוּ, לְשׁוֹן ״חָרָה מִנִּי חֹרֶב״ (איוב ל:ל), וְכֵן ״לְמַעַן תֵּחַם וְחָרָה נְחֻשְׁתָּהּ״ (יחזקאל כד:יא) – כְּדֶרֶךְ קְדֵרָה שֶׁתַּמּוּ מֵימֶיהָ, בִּרְתִיחָתָהּ מִתְחַמֶּמֶת וְנִשְׂרָפִין חֲרָסֶיהָ וְהַתַּבְשִׁיל מִתְקַלְקֵל:


**and to consume the meat and prepare the mixture** When the meat is consumed off the bones and it shrivels and becomes mingled with the liquid, and one stirs it with a spoon, it resembles a mixture of a compounding of spices, which they mix very thoroughly; and through the shriveling of the meat, its smoke is turned back into it, which is called aorser in Old French, to burn. And this is what [the following verse] says: **and the bones will be scorched** [Heb. יִחָרוּ.] They will be cooked and smoked, an expression of (Job 30:30): “dried out (חָרָה) from the heat.” Similarly (verse 11), “in order that it be heated, that its base be scorched (וְחַרָה),” as happens with a pot whose water has evaporated through its boiling: it heats up, its shards burn, and the cooked food spoils.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:11:1)


**תִּתֻּם חֶלְאָתָהּ.** תִּכְלֶה זוֹהֲמָתָהּ וְתִבָּלַע בִּנְחֻשְׁתָּהּ – סִיר שֶׁל נְחֹשֶׁת הִיא:


**its filth shall be consumed** [Heb. תִּתֻּם.] Its filth will end and be absorbed into its base. סִיר is [a pot] made of copper.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:12:1)


**תְּאֻנִּים הֶלְאָת.** בְּתוֹאֲנָה וְתַרְמִית הֶלְאָת אֶת הָעֲנִיִּים; לָכֵן **לֹא יֵצְאוּ מִמֶּנָּה** רְשָׁעִים שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ, אֶלָּא **בְּאֵשׁ** פֻּרְעָנוּתָהּ יִכְלוּ:


**With guile she wearied** [Heb. תְּאֻנִים הֶלְאָת.] With pretext and guile, she wearied the poor. Therefore, the wicked in her midst will not leave her, but they will perish in the fire of her retribution.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:13:1)


**בְּטֻמְאָתֵךְ.** בְּטֻמְאָה שֶׁל זִמָּה תִּכְלֶה:


**With your defilement** With defilement of lewdness you will perish.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:13:2)


**יַעַן טִהַרְתִּיךְ.** לְהוֹכִיחַ עַל פִּי הַנְּבִיאִים, **וְלֹא טָהַרְתְּ וְגוֹ׳**:


**Since I purified you** by reproving you through the prophets, and you did not become purified, etc.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:14:1)


**לֹא אֶפְרַע.** לֹא אֲבַטֵּל גְּזֵרָתִי:


**I shall not nullify** [Heb. לֹא אֶפְרַע.] I shall not nullify My decree.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:14:2)


**וְלֹא אֶנָּחֵם.** לָשׁוּב מִמַּחֲשַׁבְתִּי הָרָעָה אֲשֶׁר דִּבַּרְתִּי לַעֲשׂוֹת לָךְ:


**nor shall I renege** [Heb. וְלֹא אֶנָחֵם,] to turn back from My plans of evil, which I said I would execute upon you.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:16:1)


**מַחְמַד עֵינֶיךָ.** אִשְׁתְּךָ; כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָעִנְיָן מוֹכִיחַ בְּסוֹפוֹ (יחזקאל כד:יח):


**the desire of your eyes** Your wife, as the context proves at the end [of the episode].



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:17:1)


**הָאָנֵק.** הִתְמַקְמֵק בִּשְׁתִיקָה, שוא״ש דמוני״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Melt** [Heb. הֵאָנֵק,] melt in silence, soliles demoniz in Old French, to melt.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:17:2)


**פְּאֵרְךָ.** תְּפִלִּין (מועד קטן טו.). כָּל אֵלֶּה מִצְווֹת אֲבֵלִים, וְאַתָּה חִלּוּף אָבֵל: אָסוּר בִּתְפִלִּין וּסַנְדָּל, וְחַיָּב בַּעֲטִיפַת הָרֹאשׁ (מועד קטן טו.-טו:), וּמַבְרִין אוֹתוֹ סְעוּדָה רִאשׁוֹנָה מִשֶּׁל אֲחֵרִים (מועד קטן כז:):


**your glory** Tefillin. All these commandments are for mourners, but you shall do the opposite. A mourner is forbidden to wear tefillin or shoes, he is required to wrap his head, and they serve him the first meal from food belonging to others.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:18:1)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר צֻוֵּיתִי.** שֶׁלֹּא לִנְהוֹג אֲבֵילוּת:


**as I had been commanded** not to observe mourning.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:19:1)


**מָה אֵלֶּה לָּנוּ.** מָה הָרְמָזִים הַלָּלוּ עָלֵינוּ?


**what these mean to us** What are these signs to us?



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:21:1)


**אֲשֶׁר עֲזַבְתֶּם.** בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם, כְּשֶׁיְּצָאתֶם בַּגּוֹלָה:


**whom you have left over** in Jerusalem, when you went out into exile.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:22:1)


**וַעֲשִׂיתֶם כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתִי.** לֹא תִנְהֲגוּ אֲבֵילוּת, כִּי אֵין מְנַחֲמִין לָכֶם, שֶׁאֵין בָּכֶם אֶחָד שֶׁלֹּא יְהְיֶה אָבֵל, וְאֵין אֲבֵילוּת אֶלָּא בִּמְקוֹם מְנַחֲמִים; דָּבָר אַחֵר: שֶׁתִּהְיוּ יְרֵאִים לִבְכּוֹת לִפְנֵי הַכַּשְׂדִּים שֶׁאַתֶּם בְּתוֹכָם:


**And you shall do as I have done** You shall not observe mourning because you have no consolers, for no one among you is not a mourner, and there is no mourning except where there are consolers. Another explanation: For you will be afraid to weep before the Chaldeans in whose midst you are.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:22:2)


**שָׂפָם.** גְרִינוֹ״ן בְּלַעַ״ז:


**upper lip** [Heb. שָּׁפָם,] grenon in Old French, moustache.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:23:1)


**וּנְמַקֹּתֶם.** אֵישְׁרוֹיִי״ץ דְּמוֹנִי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and you will melt** [Heb. וּנְמַקֹתֶם,] esere(y)z demoniz, and you will collapse.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:24:1)


**בְּבוֹאָהּ.** בָּאָה הָרָעָה הַזֹּאת:


**when it comes** When this evil comes.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:25:1)


**מַשָּׂא נַפְשָׁם.** שֶׁנַּפְשָׁם מִתְנַשֵּׂאָה בָּהֶם:


**the pride of their soul** [Heb. מַשָׂא נַפְשָׂם.] Their soul is uplifted through them.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:26:1)


**בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא.** אִי אֶפְשָׁר לָבֹא הַפָּלִיט בְּיוֹם הַחֻרְבָּן; וְכֵן פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: **בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא**, שֶׁיָּבֹא **הַפָּלִיט אֵלֶיךָ** לְהַשְׁמִיעַ אָזְנְךָ, **בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא יִפָּתַח פִּיךָ**:


**On that day** It is impossible for the fugitive to come on the day of the destruction. Therefore this is the meaning: On that day, [on the day] that the fugitive will come to you to let you hear with your own ears, on that day, your mouth will be opened.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:26:2)


**לְהַשְׁמָעוּת אָזְנָיִם.** לְבַשֵּׂר בְּשׂוֹרָה שֶׁהֻכְּתָה הָעִיר:


**to let you hear with your own ears** To announce the news that the city has been struck.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:27:1)


**אֶת הַפָּלִיט.** עִם הַפָּלִיט:


**with the fugitive** [Heb. אֶת הַפָּלִיט,] with the fugitive.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 24:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/24:27:2)


**וּתְדַבֵּר וְלֹא תֵאָלֵם.** לְפִי שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לְךָ ״וְנֶאֱלַמְתָּ וְלֹא תִהְיֶה לָהֶם וְגוֹ׳״ (יחזקאל ג:כו); מֵעַתָּה יִרְאוּ שֶׁדְּבָרֶיךָ אֱמֶת, **וְלֹא תֵאָלֵם עוֹד** מֵהִתְנַבְּאוֹת לָהֶם:


**and you will speak and no longer be mute** Because I said to you (3:26): “and you shall become mute, and you shall not be, etc.” From now on, they will see that your words are true, and you will no longer be mute from prophesying to them.



## Chapter 25


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:3:1)


**הֶאָח.** לְשׁוֹן שִׂמְחָה:


**Aha!** An expression of joy.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:3:2)


**כִּי נִחָל.** כִּי נִנְחַל, וְהָיָה מוֹרָשָׁה לַאֲחֵרִים:


**because it was possessed** [Heb. נִחָל.] Because it was possessed (נִנְחָל) and became an inheritance for others.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:4:1)


**לִבְנֵי קֶדֶם.** כַּשְׂדִּים וַאֲרָם לְצַד מִזְרָח הֵם:


**to the children of the East** The Chaldeans and Aram are to the east.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:6:1)


**יַעַן מַחְאֲךָ יָד וְרַקְעֲךָ בְּרָגֶל.** לְשֵׁם שִׂמְחָה; וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבְּרֹאשׁ הַסֵּפֶר אָמַר כֵּן לְעִנְיַן אֵבֶל, ״הַכֵּה בְכַפְּךָ וּרְקַע בְּרַגְלֶךָ וֶאֱמֹר אָח״ (יחזקאל ו:יא), יֵשׁ דְּבָרִים שֶׁעוֹשִׂין אוֹתָם לְאֵבֶל וּלְשִׂמְחָה, אֲבָל אֵין עִנְיָנָם שָׁוֶה, וְכֵן חֲלִילִים לְכַלָּה וְלַמֵּת (משנה בבא מציעא ו.):


**Because you clapped with your hand and stamped with your foot** for joy. Although he said this at the beginning of the Book in reference to mourning (6:11): “Strike with your hand and stamp with your foot, and say, Ah!” there are things that are done for [both] mourning and joy, but the idea behind them is not the same. Similarly, flutes for a bride and for the dead.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:6:2)


**וְרַקְעֲךָ.** לְשׁוֹן ״מִרְפָּס״ (יחזקאל לד:יט):


**and stamped** [Heb. וְרַקְעֲךָ,] an expression of stamping.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:6:3)


**בְּכָל שָׁאטְךָ.** לְשׁוֹן בִּזָּיוֹן פּוֹתְרִין בּוֹ, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּבֶז עֵשָׂו״ (בראשית כה:לד) שֶׁתַּרְגּוּם אוּנְקְלוֹס ״וְשָׁאט עֵשָׂו״. וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר שָׁאט לְשׁוֹן שֶׁטֶף, גולוזמנ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**with all the disdain** [Heb. שָּׁאטְךָ,] interpreted as an expression of disdain, like (Gen. 25:34): “and Esau despised,” which Onkelos renders: וְשָׁט, but I say that שָּׁאטְךָ is an expression of lust, golozemant or golosement in Old French, ardent desire, gluttony.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:8:1)


**הִנֵּה כְּכָל הַגּוֹיִם בֵּית יְהוּדָה.** בָּטְלָה אַהֲבַת הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא מֵהֶם, וְנִמְאֲסוּ לָתֵת בְּיַד נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר כִּשְׁאָר הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (יומא נד.-נד:), שֶׁנִּכְנְסוּ לַהֵיכָל וְקִפְּלוּ צוּרוֹת הַכְּרוּבִים שֶׁצָּר שְׁלֹמֹה עַל קִיר הַהֵיכָל ״כְּמַעַר אִישׁ וְלֹויוֹת״ (מלכים א ז:לו), וְהוֹצִיאוּם לַחוּץ, אָמְרוּ: רְאוּ אֵלּוּ שֶׁהָיוּ מַרְאִים עַצְמָם כִּצְנוּעִים, בַּמֶּה הָיוּ עוֹסְקִין:


**Behold the house of Judah is like all the nations** The love of the Holy One, blessed be He, has been annulled from them, and they are rejected, to be given into the hands of Nebuchadnezzar like the other nations. The Midrash Aggadah (see Lam. Rabbah, Proem 9) [says] that they entered the Temple and peeled off the pictures of the cherubim that Solomon had painted on the wall of the Temple (I Kings 7:36): “according to the attachment of a man and the joining, etc.” They took them outside and said, “Look at what these [Jews], who were showing themselves as decent people, were occupied with.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:9:1)


**הִנְנִי פוֹתֵחַ.** אֶת מִבְצְרֵי אֶרֶץ מוֹאָב אֲשֶׁר בִּסְפָר, בְּסוֹף גְּבוּלָם, הוּא **הַכָּתֵף**, סוֹף גְּבוּל אַרְצָם; כְּמוֹ ״וְלַכָּתֵף הַשֵּׁנִית״, ״אֶל הַכָּתֵף״ (שמות כז:טו), דִּמְתַרְגֵּם: ״לְעִיבְרָא״:


**behold I will expose** the fortresses of the land of Moab that are on the border at the end of their boundary; that is the flank, the end of the boundary of their land, like (Exod. 27:15): “And on the other side,” to the flank, which Targum [Jonathan] translates לְעִיבְרָא, to the side.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:10:1)


**לִבְנֵי קֶדֶם.** אֲפַתֶּה אוֹתָם שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ עַל **בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן** דֶּרֶךְ אֶרֶץ מוֹאָב:


**To the children of the East** I will expose them so that they should come upon the children of Ammon through the land of Moab.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:13:1)


**וּדְדָנֶה.** ״וְיָתְבֵי דְדָן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and Dedan** [Heb. וּדְדָנֶה,] the inhabitants of Dedan.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:14:1)


**בְּיַד עַמִּי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** הֵם יִהְיוּ שְׁלוּחַי לִנְקוֹם נִקְמָתִי:


**by the hand of My people Israel** They will be My agents to wreak My vengeance.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:15:1)


**יַעַן עֲשׂוֹת פְּלִשְׁתִּים בִּנְקָמָה.** בְּשִׂנְאָה וּבְפֶרֶךְ, כְּאָדָם הַנּוֹקֵם נִקְמָתוֹ בְּאוֹיְבוֹ:


**Because the Philistines acted with vengeance** With hatred and with rigor, like a man who wreaks vengeance upon his enemy.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:16:1)


**אֶת כְּרֵתִים.** ״עַמָּא דְחַיָּיבִין לְאִישְׁתִּיצָאָה״. וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי: לְפִי שֶׁרָאִיתִי בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר קוֹרֵא אֶל פְּלִשְׁתִּים כְּרֵתִים, ״הוֹי יוֹשְׁבֵי חֶבֶל הַיָּם גּוֹי כְּרֵתִים״ (צפניה ב:ה), וְאֵינוֹ קוֹרֵא כֵּן לְאוּמָּה אַחֶרֶת, נִרְאֶה בְּעֵינַי שֶׁיֵּשׁ גְּבוּל בִּפְלִשְׁתִּים שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ כְּרֵתִי, וְכֵן אֲנִי מוֹצֵא בְּסֵפֶר שְׁמוּאֵל (שמואל א ל:יד) בַּגְּדוּד שֶׁשָּׂרַף אֶת צִקְלַג ״אֲנַחְנוּ פָּשַׁטְנוּ עַל נֶגֶב הַכְּרֵתִי״:


**the Cherethites** [Heb. כְּרֵתִים,] a nation deserving of being cut off (Targum). But I say, because I saw elsewhere that Scripture calls the Philistines כְּרֵתִים (Zeph. 2:5): “Woe to the inhabitants of the seacoast, the nation of (כְּרֵתִים),” and it does not give any other nation this appellation, it appears to me that there is a region of Philistia called Chereth, and so I find in the Book of (I) Samuel (30:14), concerning the group that set fire to Ziklag: “We made a raid on the south of the Cherethites.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 25:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/25:16:2)


**חוֹף הַיָּם.** מַאַרְקָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז; הֵם פְּלִשְׁתִּים, שֶׁיּוֹשְׁבִים בְּמִקְצוֹעַ דְּרוֹמִית מַעֲרָבִית שֶׁל גְּבוּל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל הַיָּם הָאַחֲרוֹן (דברים יא:כד):


**the seacoast** [Heb. חוֹף הַיָם,] marche in Old French, frontier, borderland. They are Philistines, who live in the southwestern corner of the land of Israel on the Western (Mediterranean) Sea.



## Chapter 26


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:1:1)


**בְּעַשְׁתֵּי עֶשְׂרֵה.** לְצִדְקִיָּהוּ:


**in the eleventh [year]** of Zedekiah.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:2:1)


**עַל יְרוּשָׁלִַם.** שֶׁהָיְתָה בְּמָצוֹר:


**Jerusalem** which was under siege.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:2:2)


**הֶאָח.** לְשׁוֹן קוֹל שִׂמְחָה:


**Aha!** [Heb. הֶאָח,] an expression of a shout of joy.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:2:3)


**נִשְׁבְּרָה דַּלְתוֹת הָעַמִּים.** עִיר שֶׁכָּל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים הָיוּ נִכְנָסִים לָהּ לִסְחוֹרָה, מֵעַתָּה תִּשָּׁבֵר, כִּי לֹא יֵלֵךְ הַמָּצוֹר מֵעָלֶיהָ עַד תִּלָּכֵד:


**The doors of the peoples have been broken** A city through which all the nations enter for commerce will now be broken, for the siege will not leave it until it is taken.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:2:4)


**נָסֵבָּה אֵלָי.** מֵעַתָּה הוּסְבָּה סְחוֹרַת הָעַמִּים אֵלָי:


**it has turned to me** From now on, the merchandise of the nations has turned on me.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:2:5)


**אִמָּלְאָה הָחֳרָבָה.** אֶתְמַלֵּא אֲנִי מִן הָעִיר יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הַחֲרֵבָה:


**I shall become full from the destroyed [city]** I shall become full from the destroyed city of Jerusalem.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:4:1)


**וְסִחֵיתִי.** וְעָקַרְתִּי, כְּמוֹ ״בֵּית גֵּאִים יִסַּח ה׳״ (משלי טו:כה); ״וְנִסַּחְתֶּם מֵעַל״ (דברים כח:סג):


**and I shall remove** [Heb. וְסִחֵיתִי,] and I shall uproot, like (Prov. 15:25): “The Lord will uproot (יִסַח) the house of the haughty;” (Deut. 28:63); “and you will be uprooted (וְנִסַחְתֶם) from...”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:4:2)


**לְצְחִיחַ סָלַע.** לְפִי שֶׁהָיְתָה בְּנוּיָה בְּסֶלַע בְּתוֹךְ הַיָּם, כְּשֶׁתִּנָּתֵץ וְיִסַּח **עֲפָרָהּ** אֶל הַיָּם נִשְׁאָר הַסֶּלַע חָלָק; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לִשְׁעִיעוּת כֵּיף גַּלִּי״:


**a smooth rock** Since it was built on a rock in the midst of the sea, when it will be demolished and its earth will be uprooted into the sea, the rock will be left bare, and so did Jonathan render: a smooth, bare rock.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:5:1)


**מִשְׁטַח חֲרָמִים.** עַל הַסֶּלַע יִשְׁטְחוּ הַדַּיָּגִים אֶת חֶרְמֵי מִכְמְרוֹתָם, לְנַגְּבָם:


**a place to spread out nets** On the rock the fishermen will spread out their fishing nets, to dry them.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:6:1)


**וּבְנוֹתֶיהָ אֲשֶׁר בַּשָּׂדֶה.** הַכְּפָרִים הַמְשֻׁעְבָּדוֹת לָהּ סְבִיבוֹתֶיהָ:


**And her daughters that are in the field** the villages that are subservient to her, surrounding her.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:8:1)


**וְהֵקִים עָלַיִךְ צִנָּה.** ״וִיקִים עָלַיִךְ דִּי מְזַיְּנִין בְּתַרְסִין״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and will station against you shields** [Heb. צִנָה,] and will station against you those armed with shields.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:9:1)


**וּמְחִי קָבָלוֹ.** הוּא אֶחָד מִתַּחְבּוּלוֹת הַמָּצוֹר, לִזְרוֹק אֲבָנִים גְּדוֹלוֹת, וּמַכֶּה כְּנֶגְדּוֹ אילקולי״אה דשאפורי״ירא בְּלַעַ״ז; וְיֵשׁ לוֹעֲזִים קָבָלוֹ דשארבלי״טא בְּלַעַ״ז; וְיֵשׁ לוֹעֲזִין פורייר״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**And...his catapult** [Heb. וּמְחִי קָבָלוֹ.] This is one of the devices of the siege, to throw huge rocks and to strike opposite it, e la hone de sa pereyre in Old French, and the striking of his stone-throwing machine. Others render קָבָלוֹ as desarbalete in Old French, of his war-machines.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:9:2)


**בְּחַרְבוֹתָיו.** בְּאוּמָנוּת חוּרְבָּן שֶׁיָּבִיא עִמּוֹ:


**with his instruments of destruction** [Heb. בְּחַרְבּוֹתָיו,] with tools of destruction that he will bring with him.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:10:1)


**מִשִּׁפְעַת סוּסָיו.** כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ סוּסִים הַרְבֵּה מַעֲלִים אָבָק בְּרַגְלֵיהֶם, וְעוֹלֶה וְנַעֲשֶׂה כְּעָנָן וּמַחְשִׁיךְ הָאָרֶץ, הוּא קָרוּי ׳שִׁפְעַת׳; וְכֵן ״וְשִׁפְעַת מַיִם תְּכַסֶּךָּ״ (איוב כב:יא) ׳דְלָאפוֹיְשׁוֹן׳ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**From the abundance of his horses** When there are many horses, they raise dust with their feet, which ascends and becomes like a cloud, darkening the earth. This is called שִּׁפְּעַת, an abundance. Similarly, (Job 22:11): “or an abundance of (וְשִּׁפְּעַת) water covers you,” de la foison in French, of abundance.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:10:2)


**כִּמְבוֹאֵי עִיר מְבֻקָּעָה.** ״כְּמָה דְעָלִין לְקַרְתָּא דְמִתְרְעָא מִשּׁוּר״ (תרגום יונתן), שֶׁנִּפְרְצָה חוֹמָתָהּ:


**as men enter a breached city** as they enter a city that has been breached of its wall(Targum), that its wall has been breached.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:12:1)


**רְכֻלָּתֵךְ.** סְחוֹרָתֵךְ:


**your merchandise** [as translated], your merchandise.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:16:1)


**וְחָרְדוּ לִרְגָעִים.** לִשְׁבָרִים, פֶּן יָבֹא עֲלֵיהֶם גַּם הֵם כְּשִׁבְרֵךְ:


**and they will tremble for [fear of] destruction** [Heb. לִרְגָעִים,] for fear of destruction, lest it befall them like your destruction.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:16:2)


**לִרְגָעִים.** לְשׁוֹן שֶׁבֶר, כְּמוֹ ״רוֹגַע הַיָּם״ (ישעיה נא:טו); ״עוֹרִי רָגַע״ (איוב ז:ה); ״עַל רִגְעֵי אָרֶץ״ (תהלים לה:כ) – עַל דַּכָּאֵי אֶרֶץ. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרְשׁוֹ לְשׁוֹן רֶגַע, כְּמוֹ ״כִּי רֶגַע בְּאַפּוֹ״ (תהלים ל:ו):


לִרְגָעִים as is an expression of breaking, like (Isa. 51:15): “Who splits (ַרוֹגַע) the sea;” (Job 7:5), “my skin split (רָגַע);” (Ps. 35:20), “and against the crushed people of (רִגְעֵי) the earth,” [synonymous with] דִכְּאֵי “the crushed people of the earth.” Menachem, however, explained it (p. 162) in the sense of רֶגַע, a moment, as in (Ps. 30:6): “For His wrath lasts but a moment (רֶגַע).”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:16:3)


**וְשָׁמְמוּ.** לְשׁוֹן תִּמָּהוֹן:


**and will be appalled** [Heb. וְשָּׁמְמוּ,] an expression of bewilderment.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:17:1)


**נוֹשֶׁבֶת מִיַּמִּים.** יֵשׁ נוֹקְדִין רָפֵי, וּפוֹתְרִין: מְיוּשֶׁבֶת מִיָּמִים קַדְמוֹנִים; וְיֵשׁ נוֹקְדִים דָּגֵשׁ, וּפוֹתְרִין מְיוּשֶׁבֶת בְּחָזְקָה עַל יְדֵי **יַמִּים** הַסּוֹבְבִים לָהּ; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן, וְכֵן הוּא:


**established from the seas** [Heb. מִיָמִּים.] Some vocalize it without a “dagesh” (מִיָמִים) and interpret it to mean “settled from days of yore,” and others punctuate it with a “dagesh” (מִיַמִּים) and interpret it to mean “settled with strength through the seas surrounding her,” and so it is.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:17:2)


**אֲשֶׁר נָתְנוּ חִתִּיתָם.** סְבִיבוֹת **לְכָל** הַיּוֹשְׁבִים:


**who cast terror** around to all the inhabitants.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:17:3)


**חִתִּיתָם.** לוֹרְדְפְרֵיימְ״נְט בְּלַעַ״ז:


Heb. חִתִּיתָם, lur defre[n]emant in Old French, their terror.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:18:1)


**הָאִין.** כְּמוֹ הָאִיִּים:


**the isles** [Heb. הָאִיִן,] like הָאִייִם.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:18:2)


**מִצֵּאתֵךְ.** מִיצִיאָתֵךְ בַּגּוֹלָה:


**by your departure** by your departure into exile.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:19:1)


**בְּהַעֲלוֹת עָלַיִךְ אֶת תְּהוֹם.** ״בְּאַסְקוּתִי עֲלָךְ מַשְׁרְיָת עַמְמִין דְּסַגִּיאִין כְּמֵי תְהוֹמָא, וִיחַפּוּנָךְ עַמְמִין סַגִּיאִין״, כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן; וּכְמַשְׁמָעוֹ: בִּתְחִלָּה נִתְּנָה בְּיַד נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, וְסוֹף עָלָה הַיָּם וְטָבְעָהּ:


**when [I] bring up the deep upon you** “When I bring upon you companies of nations as numerous as the waters of the deep, and many nations will cover you.” Jonathan translated it in this manner, but according to its apparent meaning, first it was given into the hands of Nebuchadnezzar, and ultimately, the sea came up and inundated it.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:20:1)


**אֶת יוֹרְדֵי בוֹר.** עִם יוֹרְדֵי גֵּיהִנָּם:


**with those who descend into the Pit** with those who descend into Gehinnom.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:20:2)


**אֶל עַם עוֹלָם.** עִם שְׁאָר הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת אֲשֶׁר שָׁם מֵעוֹלָם:


**to a people of old** with the other nations who are there from long ago.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:20:3)


**כָּחֳרָבוֹת מֵעוֹלָם.** כֶּעָרִים אֲשֶׁר חָרְבוּ מֵאָז, כִּסְדוֹם וְחַבְרוֹתֶיהָ (בראשית יט:כד-כה):


**like the ruins of old** like the cities that were destroyed from times of yore, viz. Sodom and her sister cities.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:20:4)


**וְנָתַתִּי צְבִי בְּאֶרֶץ חַיִּים.** וְאֶתֵּן הַתִּפְאֶרֶת בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם:


**but I shall bestow beauty upon the land of the living** And I shall bestow beauty upon Jerusalem.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 26:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/26:21:1)


**בַּלָּהוֹת.** פֵּרְשׁוֹ יוֹנָתָן **בַּלָּהוֹת** ״כְּדְלָא הֲוֵית״, וְהַפּוֹתְרִים אוֹמְרִים לְשׁוֹן מְקוֹם מְרַקֵּד שְׂעִירִים (ישעיה יג:כא) וּמַזִּיקִין:


**a nonentity** [Heb. בַּלָהוֹת.] Jonathan explained בַּלָהוֹת [as] as if you did not exist, and the exegetes explain it as a word for a place where the satyrs and demons dance.



## Chapter 27


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:3:1)


**עַל מְבוֹאוֹת יָם.** עַל הַנָּמֵל, שֶׁהַסְּפִינוֹת בָּאוֹת עַד הַחוֹמָה וְהַשְּׁעָרִים; וְהַרְבֵּה כְּרַכִּין יוֹשְׁבִין עַל הַיָּם וְאֵינָהּ מְקוֹם נָמֵל, וְאֵין הַסְּפִינוֹת יְכוֹלוֹת לִקְרַב אֲלֵיהֶם:


**on the seaport** [Heb. מְבוֹאֹת יָם, lit. by the entrance of the sea.] By the port where the ships come up to the wall and the gates. Many large cities are situated by the sea, but they are not a [good] place for a port, and the ships cannot approach them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:3:2)


**מְבוֹאוֹת יָם.** פּוֹרְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


מְבוֹאֹת יָם is port in Old French, seaport.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:3:3)


**רוֹכֶלֶת הָעַמִּים.** כָּךְ הָיָה מִנְהָגָם: סוֹחֲרִים הַבָּאִים לָהּ, זֶה מִצָּפוֹן וְזֶה מִדָּרוֹם, לֹא הָיוּ רַשָּׁאִין לַעֲשׂוֹת סְחוֹרָה זֶה עִם זֶה, אֶלָּא יוֹשְׁבֵי הָעִיר לוֹקְחִין מִזֶּה וּמוֹכְרִין לָזֶה:


**trafficker of the peoples** So was their custom: the merchants who came there this one from the north, that one from the south were not allowed to conduct commerce with one another. Rather, the inhabitants of the city would purchase from this one and sell to that one.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:3:4)


**אַתְּ אָמַרְתְּ אֲנִי כְּלִילַת יֹפִי.** עַד עַכְשָׁיו הַכֹּל אוֹמְרִים עַל יְרוּשָׁלַיִם ״כְּלִילַת יֹפִי״ (איכה ב:טו), ״מְשׂוֹשׂ כָּל הָאָרֶץ״ (תהלים מח:ג). עַכְשָׁיו אַתְּ מִתְגָּאָה לוֹמַר אֲנִי כְּלִילַת הַיֹּפִי, סוֹף גְּמַר הַיֹּפִי, כָּלוּל בִּי:


**you said, I am the perfection of beauty** Until now, all [were] saying about Jerusalem (Lam. 2:15): “the perfection of beauty”; (ibid.) “the joy of all the earth.” Now you boast, saying, “I am the perfection of beauty,” the ultimate extreme of beauty is incorporated in me.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:4:1)


**בּוֹנַיִךְ.** ״אַרְדִּכְלַיִּיךְ״ (תרגום יונתן), לְשׁוֹן בִּנְיָן:


**your builders** [Heb. בֹּנַיִךּ,] your architects, from the word בִּנְיָן, building.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:5:1)


**בְּרוֹשִׁים מִשְּׂנִיר.** לְפִי שֶׁהָיְתָה בְּנוּיָה בַּיָּם וּבַיָּם אָבְדָה, הִמְשִׁילָהּ הַנָּבִיא לִסְפִינָה מְשֻׁבַּחַת שֶׁהִכְבִּידוּ מַשָּׂאָהּ בְּתוֹכָהּ וְטָבְעָה בְּרוּחַ קָדִים:


**junipers from Senir** Since it was built on the sea and was destroyed by the sea, the prophet compares it to a superior ship whose cargo in its hold was too heavy for it, and which sank by the east wind.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:5:2)


**מִשְּׂנִיר.** ״טוּר תַּלְגָּא״ (תרגום אונקלוס לדברים ג:ט):


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:5:3)


**אֶת כָּל לֻחֹתָיִם.** הַלְּוָחִים שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית הָאֳנִיָּה מֵהֶם:


**all the planks** the planks from which the ship was made.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:5:4)


**אֶרֶז מִלְּבָנוֹן.** שֵׁם יַעַר, שֶׁהָאֲרָזִים מְצוּיִין בּוֹ:


**cedars from Lebanon** the name of the forest where the cedars are prevalent.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:5:5)


**תּוֹרֶן.** הוּא הָעֵץ הַזָּקוּף וְגָבוֹהַּ הַתָּקוּעַ בַּסְּפִינָה, שֶׁבּוֹ תּוֹלִין הַמִּפְרָשׂ – שֶׁקּוֹרִין ׳בְּוַייל״א׳ בְּלַעַ״ז – וּבוֹ נְקָבִים בְּרֹאשׁוֹ, שֶׁהַחֶבֶל שֶׁמּוֹשְׁכִין בּוֹ הַסְּפִינָה נִכְנָס וְיוֹצֵא בּוֹ; מש״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a mast** [Heb. תֹּרֶן.] This is the tall, upright pole that is thrust into the ship, upon which they hang the sail, which is called voile in French. Also, there are holes at its top, through which is drawn the rope by which they pull the ship. [“Pole” is] mast in Old French.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:6:1)


**אַלּוֹנִים.** מִין עֵץ סְרָק, שֶׁקּוֹרִין קִישְׁנָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**oaks** [Heb. אַלוֹנִים,] a kind of tree that does not produce fruit, chesne in Old French.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:6:2)


**מְשׁוֹטָיִךְ.** הֵם שֶׁרְחָבִים בְּרֹאשָׁם, שֶׁמַּנְהִיגִין בָּהֶם הָאֳנִיָּה רִיימ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**your oars** [Heb. מִשּׁוֹטָיִם.] They are the boards that are wide at the end, with which they guide the ship, rames in French.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:6:3)


**קַרְשֵׁךְ.** הוּא הַקֶּרֶשׁ הָרָחָב שֶׁבְּרֹאשׁ הַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁל סְפִינָה, אֶל מוּל הַתֹּרֶן שֶׁבְּרֹאשָׁהּ, אֲשֶׁר בּוֹ מְהַפְּכִין אֶת הַסְּפִינָה לְכָל צְדָדֶיהָ וּמְכַוְּנִין אוֹתָהּ אֶל הַדֶּרֶךְ, וְרַב הַחוֹבֵל אוֹחֵז בּוֹ; וּבִלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה קַבְּרָנִיט (בבא בתרא צא.); וּבִלְשׁוֹן לַעַז גוֹבְרִיינָ״ל:


**your rudder** [Heb. קַרְשֵּׁך, lit. your board.] This is the wide board at the second end of the ship, opposite the mast at the other end, with which they steer the ship in all directions and direct it on its course, and the captain holds it. In the language of the Mishnah (B. B. 91a) קְבַרְנִיט, and in French gouvernail, rudder.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:6:4)


**עָשׂוּ שֵׁן בַּת אֲשֻׁרִים.** אִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹ לִהְיוֹת אֶלָּא שֶׁל עֵץ, וְזֶהוּ בַּת אֲשֻׁרִים, כְּאִלּוּ הוּא תֵּיבָה אַחַת, לְשׁוֹן ״תִּדְהָר וְתַאֲשׁוּר״ (ישעיהו מא:יט) שְׁנֵיהֶם תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״אַשְׁכְּרוּעִין״. וְאֶת קַבְרְנִיטָהּ שֶׁל צוֹר הִמְשִׁיל לְקֶרֶשׁ שֶׁל אַשְׁכְּרוּעַ מְצֻפֶּה בְּשֵׁן עַל יְדֵי מַסְמְרִים לְנוֹי וּלְחֹזֶק, כִּי כֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״דַּפִּין דְּאַשְׁכְּרוּעִין מְכַבְּשִׁין בְּשֵׁן דְּפִיל בֵּית חוּפָאָה לָא תִּיטְרוֹן״ כְּסוּיָה לָא יִפּוֹל, אֲבָל יוֹנָתָן פֵּירֵשׁ הַקַּבְרְנִיט הַזֶּה לְפִי עִנְיַן הָעִיר עַל קַרְשֵׁי שְׁעָרֶיהָ:


**they made of ivory inlaid in cypresses** [Heb. שֵּׁן בַּת-אֵשֻּׁרִים.] It is impossible for it to be of anything but wood, and this is בּתאשּׁרים, as if it were one word, an expression of (Isa 41: 19): “firs and cypresses (וּתְאַשּׁוּר).” Both of them, Jonathan rendered אֶשּׁכְּרוֹעִן. He compares the rudder of Tyre to a rudder of cypress inlaid with ivory [and] with nails for beauty and strength, for so did Jonathan render: boards of cypresses inlaid with ivory, בֵּיתחוֹפָאָה לְאִתְיָטְרוּן, so that its cover should not fall. However, Jonathan explained this rudder as applying to the city, concerning the boards of its gates.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:7:1)


**שֵׁשׁ בְּרִקְמָה.** בֶּגֶד שֵׁשׁ עָשׂוּי בְּצִיּוּרֵי רִקְמוֹת:


**Linen with embroidery** linen cloth made with pictures and embroideries.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:7:2)


**מִפְרָשֵׂךְ.** הוּא שֶׁקּוֹרִין בְּלָשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַז ׳סִיגְלָ״א׳, וּבִלְשׁוֹנֵינוּ ׳בְּוִויְילָ״א׳ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**your sail** [Heb. מִפְרָשֵּׂךּ,] that which is called voile in French, a sail.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:7:3)


**לִהְיוֹת לָךְ לְנֵס.** לְפָרְשׂוֹ עַל כְּלוֹנַס הָאֶרֶז וּלְהַגְבִּיהוֹ עַל הַתֹּרֶן; **נֵס** בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה (כלים כ,ג): כְּלוֹנַס, וּבְלַעַ״ז פוֹרְקָא:


**to be to you for a staff** to spread it on the pole of cedar and to raise it on the mast. נֵס is in the language of the Mishnah כְּלוֹנֵס, and in French perche, pole, rod.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:7:4)


**הָיָה מְכַסֵּךְ.** לְאֹהֶל וּלְגַג עַל פְּנֵי כָּל הָאֳנִיָּה:


**was your covering** as a tent and a roof over the entire surface of the ship.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:8:1)


**הָיוּ שָׁטִים לָךְ.** תּוֹפְסֵי מְשׁוֹטֶיךָ, לְהַנְהִיגֵךְ:


**were your oarsmen** [Heb. שָּׁטִים,] those who hold your oars to guide you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:8:2)


**חוֹבְלָיִךְ.** אוֹחֲזֵי הַקַּבְּרָנִיט (ראה פרושו לעיל, ו), לְכַוֵּן וּלְיַשֵּׁר הָאֳנִיָּה:


**your mariners** those who hold the rudder to direct and straighten the ship.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:9:1)


**זִקְנֵי גְבַל.** אוּמְנֵי הַבִּנְיָן, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וּבֹנֵי חִירוֹם וְהַגִּבְלִים״ (מלכים א ה:לב):


**The elders of Gebal** [who were] skillful builders, as it is said (I Kings 5:32): “and Hiram’s builders and the Gebalites.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:9:2)


**מַחֲזִיקֵי בִדְקֵךְ.** מְתַקְּנֵי בִּדְקֵךְ, שֶׁלֹּא יִכָּנְסוּ בָּהֶם הַמַּיִם. אפייטונ״ט טאקראוצ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**who repaired your cracks** [Heb. מַחַזִיקִי, lit. strengthen,] repaired your cracks so that no water should enter them, apiye[ce] tans ta crevaze in Old French, repairing your crack.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:9:3)


**כָּל אֳנִיּוֹת הַיָּם וּמַלָּחֵיהֶם.** הַסַּפָּנִים שֶׁלָּהֶם:


**all the ships of the sea and their mariners** [Heb. וּמַלָחֵיהֶם,] their sailors.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:9:4)


**הָיוּ בָךְ.** הָיוּ סְבִיבֵךְ, לָשׁוּט אֶל הַמְּדִינוֹת הָרְחוֹקוֹת לְהָבִיא הַסְּחוֹרָה לְתוֹכֵךְ:


**were within you** They were around you to sail to the distant countries to bring the merchandise into your midst.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:9:5)


**לַעֲרֹב מַעֲרָבֵךְ.** לְשׁוֹן עֲרוּבָה, הֵם כָּל צָרְכֵי הָאָדָם שֶׁהוּא חַי בָּהֶם, וּמָמוֹנוֹ הוּא עֲרוּבָתוֹ וְהַצָּלָתוֹ לְחַיָּיו, גרנטיא״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**to guarantee your necessities** [Heb. לַעֲרֹב מַעֲרָבֵךּ,] an expression of guaranteeing. These are all a person’s necessities, by which he lives; and his money is his guarantee and his salvation for his lifetime, garantie in French.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:11:1)


**בְּנֵי אַרְוַד וְחֵילֵךְ.** וּשְׁאָר חֵיל בְּנֵי אָדָם הַיּוֹשְׁבִים בְּתוֹכֵךְ:


**The children of Arvad and your army** [Heb. וְחֵילֵךּ,] the rest of the army of people who dwell within you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:11:2)


**וְגַמָּדִים.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״וְאַף קַפּוּטְקָאֵי״, שָׁמַעְתִּי מִשְּׁמוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי מְנַחֵם: עַל שֵׁם שֶׁיּוֹרְדִין בַּיָּם עַד הַתְּהוֹם מְשַׁעֲרֵי גּוֹמְדֵי אַמּוֹת עוֹמְקָהּ (ראה רשב״א בבא בתרא ק.), נִקְרְאוּ **גַמָּדִים** אַנְדוֹיִ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים, שֶׁהֵם נַנָּסִים וְנִכְנָסִין בְּמִדַּת אַמָּה:


**and Gammadim** Jonathan rendered: and also Cappadocians. I heard in the name of Rabbi Menachem that because they dive into the sea as far as the deep and estimate (גֹמְדֵי) the cubits of its depth, they are called Gammadim. Others explain that they are dwarfs and fit into the measure of a cubit.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:11:3)


**שִׁלְטֵיהֶם.** הֵם אַשְׁפּוֹת שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין בָּהֶם חִצִּים, כְּמוֹ ״הָבֵרוּ הַחִצִּים מִלְאוּ הַשְּׁלָטִים״ (ירמיה נא:יא); קויכרינ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**their quivers** [Heb. שִּׁלֻטֵיהֶם,] These are the quivers in which they would put arrows, like (Jer. 51:11): “Polish the arrows, fill the quivers (הַשְּׁלָטִים),” cuyvrin[e] in Old French, quivers.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:11:4)


**תִּלּוּ.** הַרְבֵּה תִּלֵּי תִּלִּים:


**they heaped** [Heb. תִּלוּ,] many heaps upon heaps (תִּלֵי תִּלִים).



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:12:1)


**תַּרְשִׁישׁ.** יָם שֶׁשְּׁמָהּ תַּרְשִׁישׁ:


**Tarshish** a sea named Tarshish.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:12:2)


**מֵרֹב כָּל הוֹן.** שֶׁהָיָה בְּתוֹכֵךְ, וּבְטוּחִים הַסּוֹחֲרִים שֶׁיִּמְצְאוּ בָךְ סְחוֹרָה הָרְאוּיָה לָהֶם:


**with the multitude of all wealth** that was in your midst. The merchants were confident that they would find in you the merchandise fit for them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:12:3)


**בְּדִיל.** אֶשְׁטִיי״ן בְּלַעַ״ז:


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:12:4)


**עִזְבוֹנָיִךְ.** חוֹזְקֵיךְ וְעוֹזְרָךְ, כְּמוֹ ״וַיַּעַזְבוּ אֶת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם עַד הַחוֹמָה״ (נחמיה ג:ח); וּמָמוֹנוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם הוּא חָזְקוֹ, הַמַּעֲמִידוֹ עַל רַגְלָיו:


**your aid** [Heb. עִזְבוֹנָיִךּ,] your strength and your aid, like (Neh. 3:8): “and they strengthened Jerusalem to the... wall,” and a person’s money is his strength. which sets him up on his feet.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:13:1)


**בְּנֶפֶשׁ אָדָם.** עֲבָדִים וּשְׁפָחוֹת לִמְכֹּר:


**with living people** male and female slaves to sell.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:15:1)


**יָדֵךְ.** מְקוֹמֵךְ:


**your place** [Heb. יָדִךּ, lit. your hand,] your place.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:15:2)


**קַרְנוֹת שֵׁן וְהָבְנִים.** קַרְנֵי יְעֵלִים וְעַצְמוֹת פִּיל, וְטַוָּסִים – עוֹפוֹת שֶׁשְּׁמָן פאו״ן בְּלַעַ״ז:


**horns, ivory, and peacocks** horns of ibexes, bones of elephants, and peacocks, birds called paon in French.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:15:3)


**הֵשִׁיבוּ אֶשְׁכָּרֵךְ.** הֵבִיאוּ לְךָ תְּשׁוּרָה:


**brought you as a gift** [Heb. הֵשִּׁיבוּ אֶשְּׁכָּרֵךּ,] brought your gift.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:16:1)


**אֲרָם סוֹחַרְתֵּךְ.** בָּאֲבָנִים טוֹבוֹת הָאֲמוּרוֹת בַּמִּקְרָא, וְ**אַרְגָּמָן וְרִקְמָה וּבוּץ**:


**Aram was your merchant** [dealing] with the precious stones mentioned in the verse, and “purple cloth, embroidery, and fine linen.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:16:2)


**מֵרֹב מַעֲשָׂיִךְ.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁהָיוּ יוֹדְעִים בִּנְשִׂיאֶיךָ וְשָׂרֶיךָ, שֶׁמַּרְאִין לָהֶם מַעֲשֵׂה נוֹי וְתִפְאֶרֶת לְתַכְשִׁיט:


**because of the multitude of your works** because they knew that your princes and dignitaries accumulate many works of beauty and glory for [use as] ornaments


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:16:3)


**בְּנֹפֶךְ.** אִיקַרְבּוּנְקְלָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**with carbuncle** [Heb. שְּׁנֹפֶךּ,] e c(h)arbuncle in Old French.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:17:1)


**בְּחִטֵּי מִנִּית.** שֵׁם מָקוֹם שֶׁחִטֶּיהָ יָפוֹת, כְּמוֹ ״עַד בּוֹאֲךָ מִנִּית״ (שופטים יא:לג); וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה: חִטִּין יָפִין וְגַסִּין הַנִּמְכָּרִים בְּמִנְיָן; וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״בְּחִטֵּי רִיחוּשׁ״, לְשׁוֹן קְבוּצַת מִנְיָנִים הַרְבֵּה, טְרוֹקָ״ס בְּלַעַ״ז, וְחֲבֵירוֹ בִּפְסִיקְתָּא (פסיקתא דרב כהנא כז:ט): מָה הַדַּס רָחוּשׁ בֶּעָלִין, אַף לֵאָה רְחוּשָׁה בְּבָנִים. וְלֹא יָדַעְתִּי מַהוּ. מִפִּי רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מָצָאתִי שֶׁמָּצָא בְּמִקְרָא תַּרְגּוּם יְרוּשַׁלְמִי **בְּחִטֵּי מִנִּית וּפַנַּג** ״דְּחוּשְׁלָא וְקַלְמָא״, שַׂעֲרֵי דְּחוּשְׁלָא, שֶׁבַּגְּמָרָא (יומא עט.) אָנוּ שׁוֹנִים: שְׂעוֹרִים קְלוּפִים. וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי: **מִנִּית** לְשׁוֹן מָנוֹת, מַאֲכָל מְזֻמָּן. וְאַף לְפִי נִקּוּד דָּגֵשׁ, דַּרְכּוֹ שֶׁל יוֹנָתָן לְתַרְגֵּם הַתֵּיבָה פַּעַם מִשְׁמָעָהּ פַּעַם מִדְרָשָׁהּ, כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם ״וְגַם שֵׁם הָעִיר הֲמוֹנָה״ (יחזקאל לט:טז) – אָנוּ נוֹקְדִים ״שֵׁם״, וְ״הֲמוֹנָה״ לֹא מַפִּיק הֵ״א, וְתִרְגֵּם: ״אַף לְתַמָּן יִתְרְמוּן קְטוּלֵי קַרְתָּא דְּסַגִּיאִין אִיתְרְגוּשְׁתְּהָא״:


**with wheat of Minnith** the name of a place that produced wheat of high quality, like (Jud. 11: 33): “until you come to Minnith.” Midrash Aggadah (source unknown) [explains it as] good, thick wheat kernels, which are sold by number. Jonathan renders: בִּחִטֵי רִיחוּשּׁ expression for a gathering of many numbers, troche in Old French, bundle, bunch. A similar word appears in Pesikta [d’Rav Kahana, p. 184b]: “Just as the myrtle is crowded (רָחוּשּׁ) with leaves, so was Leah crowded with sons.” But I do not know what it [wheat of Minnith] is. In the name of Rabbi Simon, I heard that he found in a Bible with a Jerusalem Targum בְּחִטֵי מִנִיתוּפַנַג rendered: בְּחוּשְּׁלָא וְקֲלְמָא. Now, שַּׁעֲרֵי דְחוּשְּׁלָא, which is found in the Talmud (Yoma 79a), is explained as: peeled barley. I say, however, that “Minnith” is an expression of portions (מָנוֹת), prepared food. Even according to its vocalization with a “dagesh,” Jonathan is wont to translate a word sometimes according to its apparent meaning and sometimes according to its midrashic meaning. So did he render (below 39:16): “And also the name of the city shall be Hamonah (הֲמוֹנָה).” Although we punctuate the name הֲמוֹנָה without a “mappiq hey” (הֱמוֹנָה), [with a “mappiq hey” (pronounced) הֲמוֹנָה would mean “her multitude” with a silent “hey” it means simply “a multitude.”] Jonathan paraphrases: And also there, the slain of the city whose multitude is great will be cast.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:17:2)


**וּפַנַּג.** רָאִיתִי בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסֵף הַכֹּהֵן (ראה יוסיפון עמוד 155 – 156): עֵץ אֲפַרְסְמוֹן הוּא **פַנַּג**, וְהָיוּ מְצוּיִין בִּירִיחוֹ; וְעַל שֵׁם רֵיחוֹ הַטּוֹב נִקְרֵאת ׳יְרִיחוֹ׳; ׳בַּאלְצְמָא׳ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**balsam trees** [Heb. פַנַג.] I saw in the book of Joseph the priest (Josiphon vol. 1 p.155 ch. 36 Flusser): The balsam tree is “pannag,” and they were found in Jericho, and because of the fragrant scent, it [the city] is called Jericho [יְרִיחוֹ from רֵיחַ, scent], balsme in Old French.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:18:1)


**בְּיֵין חֶלְבּוֹן.** יַיִן לָבָן מְבֻשָּׁל (תרגום יונתן). וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ עִם ״חֵלֶב חִטִּים״ (תהלים קמז:יד), לְשׁוֹן טוּב:


**with white wine** [Heb. בְּיֵין חֶלְבּוֹן,] cooked white wine, and Menachem (p. 89) connects it with (Ps. 147:14): “the best (חֵלֶב) of the wheat,” an expression of goodness


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:18:2)


**וְצֶמֶר צָחַר.** ״וְעַמַּר מִילַת כְּבִינָא״ (תרגום יונתן); בְּיוֹם שֶׁנּוֹלַד הַטָּלֶה וְטִהֲרָתוֹ אִמּוֹ בִּלְחִיכָתָהּ, עוֹשִׂין כְּסוּת וּמְכַבְּנִין אוֹתוֹ סְבִיבָיו בִּקְרָסִים, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא צַמְרוֹ נָקִי (שבת נד.):


**and white wool** [Heb. צָחֹר.] [Jonathan renders:] “fine white covered wool.” On the day the lamb is born, and its mother cleanses it by licking it, they make a cover and wrap it around it with clasps so that that its wool be clean.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:18:3)


**צָחַר.** לָבָן, כְּמוֹ ״אֲתוֹנוֹת צְחוֹרוֹת״ (שופטים ה:י):


צָחֹר means white, like (Jud. 5:10): “white (צִרוֹרוֹת) donkeys.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:19:1)


**וְדָן וְיָוָן.** מְדִינוֹת הֵם:


**Vedan and Javan** They are states.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:19:2)


**מֵאוּזָּל.** מַטְוֶה שֶׁל שִׁירָאִין (שמות לה:כה):


**spun silk** [Heb. מְאוּזָל,] spun silk thread.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:19:3)


**בַּרְזֶל עָשׁוֹת.** עָשׂוּי כַּעֲשָׁשִׁיּוֹת; מַשָׁ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, וְהוּא פַּרְזְלָא הִינְדְּוָאָה (עבודה זרה טז.):


**iron wrought into ingots** [Heb. עָשּׁוֹת,] made like ingots (עֲשָּׁשִּׁיוֹת) mas(s)es or mas(s)iz in Old French, ingots, massive, and that is iron of Cush.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:20:1)


**בְּבִגְדֵי חֹפֶשׁ לְרִכְבָּה.** ״בִּלְבוּשׁ דִּיקָר לְרְתִיכִין״ (תרגום יונתן):


**in precious raiments for chariots** [lit. in raiments of freedom for riding, translated according to the Targum as] in precious raiments for chariots.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:21:1)


**בְּכָרִים.** כְּבָשִׂים; וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״בְּתּוֹרִין״, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם ״מֵאָה אֶלֶף כָּרִים״ דְּמֵישַׁע מֶלֶךְ מוֹאָב (מלכים ב ג:ד):


**with lambs** [Heb. בְכָרִים,] lambs. Jonathan renders: with cattle, and so too did he render (II Kings 3: 4): “one hundred thousand bulls,” (כָּרִים) of Mesha, king of Moab.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:23:1)
