**אַשּׁוּר כִּלְמַד.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: מְדִינַת אַשּׁוּר וּמָדַי:


**Assyria and Media** [Heb. אַשּׁוּר כִּלְמַד.] Jonathan renders: the states of Assyria and Media.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:24:1)


**בְּמַכְלוּלִים.** בִּדְבָרִים שֶׁהֵם לְמַכְלוּל פרמונ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, וּמַה הֵם הַמַּכְלוּלִים? **גְּלוֹמֵי תְכֵלֶת וְרִקְמָה**, טַלִּיתוֹת שֶׁל תְּכֵלֶת מְרֻקָּמוֹת:


**with adornments** [Heb. בְּמַכְלֻלִים,] with things that are for adornment (מכלול), paramenz in Old French, adornments, jewelry, and what are the adornments? Wraps of embroidered blue cloth.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:24:2)


**וּבְגִנְזֵי בְרוֹמִים.** בְּתַכְשִׁיטֵי פְּאֵר, שֶׁגּוֹנְזִין אוֹתָן בְּאַרְגָּזִין שֶׁקּוֹרִין אשקרי״ן בְּלַעַ״ז, וְהֵם מְחוּפִּין עוֹר וּמְצוּיָּרִין לְנוֹי בְּמִינֵי מַסְמְרִין, שׁוּרוֹת שׁוּרוֹת וְצִיּוּרִין; וְהֵם בִּלְשׁוֹן אֲרַמִּי קַמְטְרִין, וּבִלְשׁוֹן עִבְרִי **בְּרוֹמִים**; וְהֵם **חֲבֻשִׁין** וַאֲסוּרִין בְּחַבְלֵי בּוּץ, לְנוֹי:


**and with treasures kept in chests** With beautiful adornments that are kept in chests called escrin in Old French, (ecrin in modern French). They are covered with leather and decorated for beauty with sorts of nails forming rows and pictures, and they are called קַמְטְרַיָא in Aramaic and בְּרוֹמִים in Hebrew, and they are tied and bound with linen ropes for beauty.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:24:3)


**וַאֲרוּזִים.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אֲעֵי הֲדַסִּים״ – אוֹתָן אֲרָגָזִים הָיוּ נְתוּנִין בְּמִגְדְּלֵי עֵץ הֲדַס; וּמְבִיאִין אוֹתָן לְתוֹךְ הַמִּגְדָּלִין, וְהַקְּמַטְרָאוֹת שֶׁבְּתוֹכָן וְהַתַּכְשִׁיטִין שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַקְּמַטְרָאוֹת, הַכֹּל יַחַד, לִסְחוֹרָה; וְכֹה פִּתְרוֹן הַלָּשׁוֹן בְּלָשׁוֹן קְצָרָה: **וּבְגִנְזֵי בְּרוֹמִים הַחֲבוּשִׁים בַּחֲבָלִים וַאֲרוּזִים** בְּתוֹךְ עֵץ אֶרֶז; שֶׁהַבְּרוֹמִים, הֵם הַקְּמַטְרָאוֹת, וְהָיוּ אֲסוּרִים בַּחֲבָלִים וּנְתוּנִים בְּמִגְדְּלֵי הֲדַס, שֶׁהִיא מִמִּינֵי אָרֶז; וּמַכְלוּלִים שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ הַבְּרוֹמִים קָרָא יוֹנָתָן: ״שׁוּפִין דְּוַרְדָּא וְאִימְלִין דְּזָהוֹרִיתָא״ – תַּכְשִׁיטִין הַצְּרִיכִין שְׁמִירָה נָאָה הֵן, ״מְחַתִּין בְּקַמְטְרְוָון״ – מוּנָחִים בְּקַמְטְרָאוֹת; וְהַמִּגְדָּלִים שֶׁבַּתַּרְגּוּם – כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָנוּ שׁוֹנִין (שבת טז,ה): ׳תֵּיבָה וּמִגְדָּל׳ שֵׁייפְרֵייטָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and [enclosed in] myrtle wood** Jonathan renders: myrtle wood. Those chests were placed in chests of myrtle wood, and they would bring them into the outer chests and the chest within them, and so too, the ornaments that were inside the chests; everything all together for merchandising. This is the meaning of the wording in brief: and with treasures [kept] in chests, tied with ropes and [enclosed in] cedar wood, for the בְּרוֹמִים are the chests, and they were tied with ropes and placed in chests of myrtle, which is a species of cedar. And the adornments that were inside the chests, Jonathan calls וְכוּשּׁפִּין דְאַרְזָא בְּאִימְלִין דִּי זָהְרָיִתָא adornments that require good watching (זְהִירוּת), [i.e.,] דְמֲחֲתִין בְּקוֹמְטְרִין that were placed in chests. And the word מִגְדָּל in the Targum is like what we learn: a box and a chest (מִגְדָּל) schafreite in Middle High German, clothes chest, cupboard. [In some editions] armaise in Old French.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:25:1)


**אֳנִיּוֹת תַּרְשִׁישׁ שָׁרוֹתַיִךְ.** הֵם הָיוּ לָךְ לַעֲרָבָה וּמִישׁוֹר; כְּשֵׁם שֶׁהַמִּישׁוֹר הוּא דֶּרֶךְ כְּבוּשָׁה לְהָבִיא בּוֹ סְחוֹרָה לָעִיר הַיּוֹשֶׁבֶת בַּיַּבָּשָׁה, הָיוּ לָךְ תַּחְתָּם **אֳנִיּוֹת תַּרְשִׁישׁ** מְסִלָּה לְהָבִיא **מַעֲרָבֵךְ** לְתוֹכֵךְ:


**Ships...your plains** [Heb. שָּׁרוֹתַיִךּ.] They were to you as a plain and a flat terrain. Just as the flat terrain is a paved road, upon which to bring merchandise to a city situated on dry land, the ships of Tarshish were for you instead of them (sic) as a highway for bringing your merchandise into your midst.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:25:2)


**שָׁרוֹתַיִךְ.** לְשׁוֹן שָׁרוֹן וּמִישׁוֹר, וְדֻגְמָתוֹ ״עֲלוּ בְשָׁרוֹתֶיהָ״ (ירמיה ה:י):


**your plains** [Heb. שָּׁרוֹתַיִךּ,] an expression for Sharon and flat terrain. A similar instance is (Jer. 5:10): “Ascend upon its rows בְּשָׁרוֹתֶיהָ.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:25:3)


**וַתִּמָּלְאִי, וַתִּכְבְּדִי מְאֹד.** הֵבִיאוּ לְתוֹכֵךְ מַשָּׂא רַב, וְהִכְבִּידוּ מַשָּׂאֵךְ יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי:


**and you were filled and you became very heavy** They brought a great burden into your midst and made your load too heavy.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:26:1)


**בְּמַיִם רַבִּים הֱבִיאוּךְ הַשָּׁטִים אוֹתָךְ.** כְּדֶרֶךְ הָאֳנִיּוֹת הַגְּדוֹלוֹת, שֶׁמַּפְלִיגִין אוֹתָן לְתוֹךְ פִּלְגֵי הַיָּם; וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁנִּתְמַלֵּאת וְכָבַדְתְּ, בָּא **רוּחַ הַקָּדִים וּשְׁבָרֵךְ בְּלֵב יַמִּים**. וּלְעִנְיַן דּוּגְמַת הָעִיר: מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁנִּתְמַלֵּאת עֹשֶׁר – גָּבַהּ לִבֵּךְ, וּבָא עָלֶיךָ פֻּרְעָנוּת:


**In the abundant waters, your oarsmen brought you** After the manner of the large ships, which they send into the midst of the sea. And since you are filled and are too heavy, the east wind will come and shatter you in the heart of the seas. As regards the comparison of the city: since you were filled with wealth, your heart became haughty, and retribution will befall you.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:28:1)


**מִגְרָשׁוֹת.** כְּמוֹ ״מִגְרְשֵׁי הֶעָרִים״ (במדבר לה:ב):


**the neighboring districts** [Heb. מִגְרשּׁוֹת,] like (Num. 35:2): “and open land (וּמִגְרָשּׁ) for the cities.”



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:29:1)


**אֶל הָאָרֶץ יַעֲמֹדוּ.** מַה לָּנוּ עוֹד בָּאֳנִיּוֹת? לְהֵיכָן נוֹלִיךְ סְחוֹרָה עוֹד?


**they will stand upon the land** What use have we anymore with ships? Where shall we take our wares from now on?



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:30:1)


**יִתְפַּלָּשׁוּ.** יִתְגַּלְגְּלוּ:


**they will wallow** [Heb. יִתְפַלָשּׁוּ,] they will roll.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:32:1)


**וְנָשְׂאוּ אֵלַיִךְ בְּנִיהֶם.** בְּנֶהִי שֶׁלָּהֶם:


**And they will lift up over you with their wailing** [Heb. בְּנִיהֶם,] with their wailing.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:32:2)


**כְּדֻמָה.** כָּךְ הָיָה שֵׁם רָאשֵׁי אֱדוֹם, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מַשָּׂא דּוּמָה״ (ישעיהו כא:יא); וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״לֵית דְּדָמֵי לָהּ״:


**“as Dummah”** [Heb. כְּדֻמָּה.] This was the name of the heads of Edom, as the matter is stated (Isa. 21: 11): “The harsh prophecy about Dumah (דּוּמָה).” Jonathan renders: There is none like (דָמֵי) as one of her likeness (כַּדוֹמָה).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:32:3)


**כְּדֻמָה.** כְּדֻגְמָתָהּ:



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:33:1)


**בְּצֵאת עִזְבוֹנַיִךְ וְגוֹ׳.** כָּל זֶה יְקוֹנְנוּ **עָלָיִךְ** בְּקִינָתָם:


**When your merchandise came out, etc.** All this they will lament over you in their lamentation.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:35:1)


**רָעֲמוּ פָנִים.** זְעוּמֵי פָנִים, וְכֵן ״בַּעֲבוּר הַרְעִימָהּ״ (שמואל א א:ו); וְכֵן כָּל תְּלוּנָה: ״תּוּרְעֲמָא״ (תרגום אונקלוס שמות טז:יב):


**their faces were angry** [Heb. רָעֲמוּ פָּנִים,] angry faces, and so (I Sam. 1:6): “in order to make her complain (הַרְעִימָהּ),” and so every expression of complaint is called תּוּרְעֲמָתָא.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 27:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/27:36:1)


**שָׁרְקוּ.** שׁוֹבְלִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז; כָּךְ שׁוֹרְקִין עַל דָּבָר שֶׁחָלַף וְהָלַךְ לוֹ:


**hissed** [Heb. שָּׁרְקוּ,] sibler in old French, to whistle. So do people whistle about a thing that has disappeared.



## Chapter 28


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:2:1)


**מוֹשַׁב אֱלֹהִים.** עָשָׂה לוֹ בָּאֲוִיר עַל הַיָּם בְּמַנְגְּנוֹן, כְּמִין שִׁבְעָה רְקִיעִים, וְיָשַׁב לוֹ עַל הָעֶלְיוֹן (תנחומא, בראשית ז, ילקוט שמעוני יחזקאל שסז):


**a seat of God** he made for himself in the air, over the sea, with a mechanism that was a replica of seven heavens, and he sat down on the highest one.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:3:1)


**הִנֵּה חָכָם אַתָּה מִדָּנִיֵּאל.** דָּנִיֵּאל, בָּא נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר לַעֲשׂוֹתוֹ אֱלוֹהַּ, ״וּלְדָנִיֵּאל סְגִיד וּמִנְחָה וְנִיחוֹחִין אָמַר לְנַסְּכָא לֵיהּ״ (דניאל ב:מו), וְלֹא קַבִּיל עֲלֵיהּ, וְאַתָּה עוֹשֶׂה עַצְמְךָ אֱלוֹהַּ:


**Behold, are you wiser than Daniel** [As for] Daniel Nebuchadnezzar came to make him a god (Dan. 2:46): “and prostrated himself before Daniel, and ordered that they should offer a meal offering and sacrifices of sweet odors to him,” but he did not accept it upon himself, yet you make yourself a God?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:3:2)


**כָּל סָתוּם לֹא עֲמָמוּךָ.** לֹא עָמוּם מִמְּךָ, קִנְתּוֹרִין הֵם שֶׁמְּקַנְתְּרִין, כְּלוֹמַר: שֶׁמָּא חָכָם אַתָּה כְּדָנִיֵּאל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ ״כָּל רָז לֹא אָנֵס לָךְ״ (דניאל ד:ו):


**that no secret is hidden from you** [Heb. לֹא עֲמָמוּךָ,] is not hidden from you. These are taunts [by] which he is taunting; that is to say: Perhaps you are as wise as Daniel, about whom it is stated: (Dan. 4: 6): “and no secret overwhelms you?”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:4:1)


**עָשִׂיתָ לְךָ חָיִל.** ״קָנִיתָ לְךָ נְכָסִין״ (תרגום יונתן):


**did you acquire wealth for yourself** [Heb. עָשִיתָ לְךָ חָיִל. lit. did you make.] Did you acquire property for yourself?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:4:2)


**וַתַּעַשׂ זָהָב וָכֶסֶף.** ״וּכְנַשְׁתָּא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and gather gold and silver** [Heb. וְתַעַשּׂ,] and gather.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:7:1)


**וְהֵרִיקוּ חַרְבוֹתָם.** כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ; וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (מכילתא בשלח שירה ז): מִשְׁכַּב זָכוּר, שֶׁנִּבְעַל כְּנָשִׁים. שְׁלֹשָׁה עָשׂוּ עַצְמָם אֱלוֹהַּ וְנִבְעֲלוּ כְּנָשִׁים: יוֹאָשׁ בֶּן אֲחַזְיָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְאֶת יוֹאָשׁ עָשׂוּ שְׁפָטִים״ (דברי הימים ב כד, כד), וְחִירָם, וּנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר (תנחומא, וארא ט):


**and they will draw their swords** [This is to be understood] according to its apparent meaning. [According to] Midrash Aggadah (see Mechilta to Exod. 15:9; Tan. Va’era 9), it means homosexuality, for he was used sexually as women are. Three pretended to be gods and were sexually molested like women: Joash the son of Ahaziah, as it is said (II Chron. 24:24): “and upon Joash they exacted judgment,” Hiram, and Nebuchadnezzar.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:7:2)


**יִפְעָתֶךָ.** הוֹפָעָתְךָ, לְשׁוֹן תֹּאַר פָּנִים; וְהַמִּדְרָשׁ דּוֹרְשׁוֹ יוֹפִי עִתֶּךָ:


**your brightness** [Heb. יִפְעָתֶךָ,] your brightness, an expression of the appearance of the countenance. The Medrash interprets it as “the beauty of your time (יְפֵה עִתֶּךָ) .”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:9:1)


**מְחַלְלֶיךָ.** כְּמוֹ **הֹרְגֶךָ**:


**your slayer** [Heb. מְחַלְלֶךָ,] like הוֹרְגֶךָ.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:12:1)


**אַתָּה חוֹתֵם תָּכְנִית וגו׳.** אַתָּה הוּא מָלֵא חָכְמָה לַחְתֹּם וְלִטְבֹּעַ כָּל תַּבְנִית וְצוּרָה:


**You are the one who engraves images, etc.** You are full of the wisdom to seal and to stamp every pattern and shape.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:12:2)


**תָּכְנִית.** פֵּיינְטוּרָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**images** [Heb. תָּכְנִית,] peinture in French, painting, engraving.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:13:1)


**בְּעֵדֶן גַּן אֱלֹהִים וְגוֹ׳.** :בִּסְגִי טָבוֹן וְתַפְנוּקִין אַתְּ מִתְפַּנִּיק כְּאִלּוּ בְּעֵדֶן גִּינְתָא דַה׳ אַתְּ שָׁרֵי, כָּל עוּתְרָא גֵּיוּתָא וִיקָרָא מִתְיַהֵב לָךְ, כָּל אַבְנִין טָבָן מְסַדְּרָן עַל לְבוּשָׁךְ״ (תרגום יונתן):


**In Eden, the garden of God** With much goodness and pleasure. You enjoy yourself as if you were dwelling in Eden, the garden of God. All wealth, pride, and honor are given to you: all precious stones are set on your raiment.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:13:2)


**מְסֻכָתֶךָ.** חֻפָּתְךָ, לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר לְבוּשׁ כִּסּוּיֶיךָ:


**your covering** Your canopy. Another explanation: Your raiment.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:13:3)


**מְלֶאכֶת תֻּפֶּיךָ וּנְקָבֶיךָ בָּךְ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנָּתַתִּי לְךָ הַכָּבוֹד הַזֶּה, יוֹדֵעַ הָיִיתִי שֶׁתִּתְגָּאֶה, וְעָשִׂיתִי בְךָ **מְלֶאכֶת** תֻּפִּים וּנְקָבִים, הַמּוֹצִיאִין רוּחַ בְּקוֹל זֶמֶר כְּתוֹף, וּבָם הָיָה לְךָ לְהִתְבּוֹנֵן:


**the work of your drums and your orifices** Although I gave you all this honor, I knew that you would become haughty, and I made in you the work of the perforated drums that let out wind with an instrumental sound like a drum, and these are what you should have contemplated about.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:14:1)


**אַתְּ כְּרוּב מִמְשַׁח הַסּוֹכֵךְ.** אַתְּ עוֹף מִדָּה, כְּלוֹמַר: הָעוֹף הַגָּדוֹל, **הַסּוֹכֵךְ** בִּכְנָפָיו סְכָךְ רַב, כְּלוֹמַר: מוֹשֵׁל מִמְשָׁל רַב. **מִמְשַׁח** לְשׁוֹן גֹּדֶל, כְּמוֹ ״אַנְשֵׁי מִדּוֹת״ (במדבר יג:לב), דִּמְתַרְגְּמִינָן: ״גַּבְרֵי דְמִשְׁחָן״:


**You are a cherub of great measure, that covers** You are a bird of measure, i.e., the huge bird that covers a large area with his wings; i.e., you rule over a large dominion. מִמְשַּׁח is a word for largeness, like (Num. 13:32): “men of stature (אַנְשֵּׁי מִדוֹת).” which is translated into Aramaic as גַבְרֵי דְמִשְּׂחָן.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:14:2)


**וּנְתַתִּיךָ בְּהַר קֹדֶשׁ אֱלֹהִים הָיִיתָ.** וְנָתַתִּי לְךָ מָקוֹם לִקְנוֹת לְךָ שֵׁם, בְּבִנְיַן הַר קָדְשִׁי, שֶׁסִּיַּעְתָּ אֶת שְׁלֹמֹה בַּעֲצֵי אֲרָזִים (מלכים א ה:כב-כד):


**and I gave that to you; you were on the mount of the sanctuary of God** and I gave you a place to acquire a name for yourself in the edifice of the mountain of My sanctuary, for you assisted Solomon with the cedar wood.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:14:3)


**בְּתוֹךְ אַבְנֵי אֵשׁ הִתְהַלָּכְתָּ.** קָנִיתָ לְךָ זִכָּרוֹן עִם מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁהֵם כְּמַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁרֵת:


**you walked among stones of fire** You acquired for yourself a memorial with the kings of Israel, who are like the ministering angels.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:15:1)


**נִמְצָא עַוְלָתָה בָּךְ.** בְּאָמְרְךָ **אֵל אֲנִי** (יחזקאל כח:ב):


**until wrongdoing was found in you** in your saying, “I am a god.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:16:1)


**מָלוּ.** כְּמוֹ מָלְאוּ:


**they filled** [Heb. מָלוּ, like מָלְאוּ,] they filled.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:16:2)


**וָאֲחַלֶּלְךָ מֵהַר אֱלֹהִים.** גָּרַמְתָּ שֶׁאֶמְחֶה אוֹתְךָ, שֶׁלֹּא תִצְדַּק עוֹד בְּבִנְיַן בֵּיתִי (ראה רש״י לעיל יד):


**and I shall cast you as profane** You caused me to eradicate you, so that you shall no longer be remembered concerning the building of My House.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:16:3)


**וָאַבֶּדְךָ.** כְּמוֹ וָאֲאַבֶּדְךָ:


**and I shall destroy you** [Heb. וָאַבֶּדְךָ,] like וָאֲאַבֶּדְךָ and I shall destroy you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:16:4)


**מִתּוֹךְ אַבְנֵי אֵשׁ.** שֶׁלֹּא תִּטּוֹל חֵלֶק עִם הַצַּדִּיקִים; וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ בְּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (בבא בתרא עה.) דָּרְשׁוּ הָעִנְיָן הַזֶּה בְּאָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן, וְכָךְ אָמַר הַנָּבִיא לְחִירָם: מָה נִתְגָּאִיתָ בְּרְקִיעֶיךָ? שֶׁמָּא בְּגַן עֵדֶן הָיִיתָ (יחזקאל כח:יג), כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה אָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן, **כָּל אֶבֶן יְקָרָה וְגוֹ׳**? כִּי כָּל הַחוּפּוֹת הָאֵלֶּה עָשִׂיתִי לְאָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן, וּפְחוּתָה שֶׁבְּכֻלָּן זָהָב, שֶׁהִיא מְנוּיָה אַחֲרוֹן (יחזקאל כח:יג). **מְלֶאכֶת תֻּפֶּיךָ וְגוֹ׳** – בְּזֹאת הִשְׁוֵיתִיךָ לוֹ, וְלֹא בְּאַחֶרֶת, וּבְךָ נִסְתַּכַּלְתִּי וּבָרָאתִי תּוּפִּים וּנְקָבִים בְּאָדָם הָרִאשׁוֹן (בבא בתרא עה.). **אַתְּ כְּרוּב מִמְשַׁח** – שֶׁמָּא אַתָּה כְּאוֹתוֹ **כְּרוּב** שֶׁהָיָה גָּדוֹל בְּ**מִמְשַׁח הַסּוֹכֵךְ** כֻּלּוֹ מִקְצֵה הַשָּׁמַיִם וְעַד קְצֵה הַשָּׁמַיִם? וְשֶׁמָּא **נְתַתִּיךָ** לִהְיוֹת בְּהַרְרֵי **קֹדֶשׁ**, כַּאֲשֶׁר נָתַתִּי לוֹ?


**from among the stones of fire** that you shall not take a share with the righteous. Our Rabbis in the Midrash Aggadah (B. B. 75a) interpreted this section as referring to Adam: So said the prophet to Hiram, “Why should you be proud of your sky? Were you in the Garden of Eden, as Adam was? **[13] every precious stone, etc.** For I made all these canopies for Adam, and the least of them was gold, which is counted last. **the work of your drums, etc.** With this, I made you equal to him, but not with anything else, and I pondered over you and created drums and orifices in Adam. **[14] Are you a cherub of great measure** Are you like that cherub that was great in measure, whose great wingspan covered from one end of heaven completely to the other end of heaven, and did I place you to be on the holy mountains as I placed him?



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:17:1)


**שִׁחַת חָכְמָתְךָ עַל יִפְעָתֶךָ.** שֶׁנִּתְגָּאֵיתָ בְּיִפְעָתֶךָ:


**you destroyed your wisdom with your brightness** for you were proud of your brightness.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:17:2)


**לְרַאֲוָה בָךְ.** לְהִסְתַּכֵּל בְּבָשְׁתְּךָ, **לְרַאֲוָה** כְּמוֹ לִרְאִיָּה:


**to gaze upon you** [Heb. לְרַאֲוָה בָּך] to gaze upon your shame; לְרַאֲוָה is like לִרְאִיָה, to see.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:18:1)


**חִלַּלְתָּ מִקְדָּשֶׁיךָ.** קְדֻשָּׁתְךָ:


**you profaned your sanctity** [Heb. מִקְדָשֶׁךָ,] your sanctity.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:18:2)


**אֵשׁ מִתּוֹכְךָ.** דִּבְרֵי גַאֲוָה שֶׁיָּצְאוּ מִפִּיךָ, לֵאמֹר **אֵל אָנִי** (יחזקאל כח:ב). לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר, כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״וְאַיְיתֵי עַמְמִיָּא דְּתַקִּיפִין כְּאֶשָּׁתָא מֵחוֹבֵי זְדוֹנָךְ״:


**fire out of your midst** Haughty words that came out of your mouth, saying, “I am a god.” Another explanation: [It is to be interpreted] according to the Targum: And I brought forth peoples as strong as fire because of your arrogant sins.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:19:1)


**שָׁמְמוּ.** תָּמְהוּ:


**will wander** [Heb. שָּׁמְמוּ.] will wonder.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:23:1)


**וְנִפְלַל חָלָל בְּתוֹכָהּ בְּחֶרֶב עָלֶיהָ מִסָּבִיב.** עַל אֲשֶׁר יִרְאוּ הַחֶרֶב מִסָּבִיב, בַּחוּץ, יִפְּלוּ יוֹשְׁבֵי הָעִיר וְיִהְיוּ בְּטוּחִים לִהְיוֹת חֲלָלִים בְּתוֹכָהּ:


**and they will judge themselves as slain in her midst by the sword [coming] upon her from all around** Because they will see the sword surrounding [them] from outside, the people of the city will assess [the situation] and be sure that they will be slain in her midst.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:23:2)


**וְנִפְלַל.** כְּמוֹ ״לֹא פִלַּלְתִּי״ (בראשית מח:יא): ״לָא סְבָרִית״, וּכְמוֹ ״וְאֹיְבֵינוּ פְּלִילִים״ (דברים לב:לא); יָדוּנוּ עַצְמָם לִהְיוֹת חֲלָלִים **בְּתוֹכָהּ**:


[Heb. וְנִפְלַל,] like (Gen. 48:11): “I did not think (פִלָּלְתִּי),” and like (Deut. 32:31): “and our enemies judge (פְּלִילִים).” They will judge themselves to be slain in her midst.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:24:1)


**וְלֹא יִהְיֶה עוֹד לְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ׳.** לְפִי שֶׁנִּבָּא עַל עַמּוֹן וּמוֹאָב וּפְלִשְׁתִּים וֶאֱדוֹם וְצוֹר וְצִידוֹן, שֶׁהֵם שְׁכֵינֵי אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁיֶּחְרְבוּ, וּמֵאַחַר שֶׁיֹּאבְדוּ כֻּלָּן **לֹא יִהְיֶה סִלּוֹן וְקוֹץ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל** בְּכָל **סְבִיבוֹתָם**, שֶׁיַּכְאִיבֵם וְשֶׁיָּרַע לָהֶם:


**And there will no longer be to the house of Israel, etc.** Since he prophesied about Ammon, Moab, the Philistines, Edom, Tyre, and Zidon, who are the neighbors of the land of Israel, that they would be destroyed. Since they all will be destroyed, there will not be a briar or a thorn to [the people of Israel, in all their surroundings, to pain or harm them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:24:2)


**סִלּוֹן.** מִין קַמְשׁוֹנִים וְחוֹחִים הוּא:


**briars** [Heb. סִילוֹן,] a species of thistles and thorns.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:24:3)


**מַמְאִיר.** עִנְיַן הַכְאָבָה, כְּמוֹ ״צָרַעַת מַמְאֶרֶת״ (ויקרא יג:נא), פוניינ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**pricking** [Heb. מַמְאִיר,] a matter of hurting, like (Lev. 13:51): “painful (מַמְאֶרֶת) zaarath,” poignante in French, stinging, pricking.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:24:4)


**הַשָּׁאטִים אוֹתָם.** הַבּוֹזִים אוֹתָם:


**who plunder them** [Heb. הַשָּׁאטִים,] who plunder them.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 28:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/28:25:1)


**לְעַבְדִּי לְיַעֲקֹב.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁנְּתָנָהּ לְיַעֲקֹב נַחֲלָה בְּלֹא מְצָרִים, ״וּפָרַצְתָּ יָמָּה וָקֵדְמָה צָפֹנָה וָנֶגְבָּה״ (בראשית כח:יד):


**to My servant, to Jacob** As it was given to Jacob, an inheritance without boundaries (Gen. 28: 14): “and you will spread out toward the west and the east, toward the north and the south.”



## Chapter 29


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:3:1)


**הַתַּנִּים הַגָּדוֹל.** לְפִי שֶׁכָּל גְּדֻלָּתָהּ שֶׁל מִצְרַיִם וְכָל שׂוֹבַע שֶׁבָּהּ, עַל יְדֵי יְאוֹרֵי נִילוּס הוּא, לְפִיכָךְ הַנָּבִיא מְמַשֵּׁל אֶת מַלְכָּהּ לְתַנִּין, וְאֶת עַמָּהּ לִדְגַת יְאוֹר:


**the great crocodile** Since all the greatness of Egypt and all its plenty comes though the Nile River, the prophet compares its king to a crocodile and its people to the fish of the river.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:3:2)


**לִי יְאוֹרִי.** אֵינִי צָרִיךְ לָעֶלְיוֹנִים, כִּי יֵשׁ לִי יְאוֹרִי, מַסְפִּיק כָּל צְרָכִי:


**My river is my own** I do not need the heavenly powers, for my river provides all my necessities


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:3:3)


**וַאֲנִי עֲשִׂיתִנִי.** בִּגְבוּרָתִי וּבְחָכְמָתִי הִגְדַּלְתִּי גְּדֻלָּתִי וּמֶמְשַׁלְתִּי:


**and I made myself** With my might and with my wisdom, I enhanced my greatness and my dominion.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:4:1)


**חַחִים.** כְּמִין טַבְּעוֹת בַּרְזֶל אֵיינְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**hooks** [Heb. חַתִים,] type of iron rings, ens in Old French.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:4:2)


**דְּגַת יְאוֹרֶיךָ בְּקַשְׂקְשֹׂתֶיךָ.** אִינְטִישָׁאִיקְרַאטִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז; כְּלוֹמַר: אֶתֵּן בְּלֵב כָּל גִּבּוֹרֶיךָ לָצֵאת עִמְּךָ לַמִּלְחָמָה, וְיִפְּלוּ כֻּלָּם:


**the fish of your rivers to fasten onto your scales** [Heb. בְּקַשְּׂקְשּׂתֶיךָ,] en tes e(s)chardes in Old French, to your scales. That is to say, I shall inspire all your mighty men to go forth with you to war so that they will all fall.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:5:1)


**וּנְטַשְׁתִּיךָ הַמִּדְבָּרָה.** זוֹ הִיא אָבְדָנָן שֶׁל דָּגִים, כֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּטְשׁוּ עַל פְּנֵי הַיַּבָּשָׁה מֵתִים. אי״י אטינדר״י טו״י בְּלַעַ״ז:


**And I will scatter you in the desert** [Heb. וּנְטַשְּׁתִּיךָ,] e etandre toy in Old French, and I shall spread you out. This is the perishing of fish: once they are spread out on the surface of the dry land, they die.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:6:1)


**מִשְׁעֶנֶת קָנֶה.** כַּמָּה פְּעָמִים סָמְכוּ עֲלֵיהֶם, בִּימֵי סַנְחֵרִיב (מלכים א יח:כא) וּבִימֵי נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר (ירמיה לז:ה-ח), וְלֹא הוֹעִיל לָהֶם, כְּקָנֶה זֶה, שֶׁהוּא רַךְ וְאֵינוֹ סוֹמֵךְ אֶת הַנִּשְׁעָן עָלָיו:


**a prop of reeds** Many times they relied on them: in the days of Sennacherib and in the days of Nebuchadnezzar, but it did not avail them, just like this reed, which is soft and does not support the one who leans on it.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:7:1)


**בְּתָפְשָׂם בְּךָ בְכַפְּךָ.** בַּכַּף קְרִי, כְּאָדָם הַמְהַלֵּךְ וְנִשְׁעָן עַל מַקְלוֹ:


**When they took hold of you with the hand** [Heb. בְּכַפְּךָ, lit. with your hand,] to be read בַּכַּף, with the hand, like a man who walks and supports himself on his staff.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:7:2)


**תָּרוֹץ.** תִּרְצֵץ:


**you splintered** [Heb. תֵּרוֹף] [like] תֵּרָצֵף.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:7:3)


**וּבָקַעְתָּ לָהֶם כָּל כָּתֵף.** כְּתֵפֵיהֶם, כְּאָדָם הַנִּסְמָךְ עַל **קָנֶה**, וְנִשְׁבָּר, וְהָאָדָם נוֹפֵל עָלָיו וְהַקְּרוּמִיּוֹת נִכְנָסוֹת לוֹ בִּכְתֵיפָיו:


**and pierced the whole of their shoulders** [lit. the whole of a shoulder,] i.e., their shoulders, like a man who leans on a reed, which breaks, and the man falls on it, and the shreds enter his shoulders.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:7:4)


**וְהַעֲמַדְתָּ לָהֶם כָּל מָתְנָיִם.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁתִּשָּׁבֵר, יִצְטָרְכוּ לְחַזֵּק אֶת מָתְנֵיהֶם וְלַעֲמֹד עֲלֵיהֶם; וַהֲרֵי אַתָּה כְּאָדָם שֶׁחֲבֵירוֹ נִשְׁעָן עָלָיו וְהוּא חַלָּשׁ, וְאוֹמֵר לוֹ: הִתְחַזֵּק עַל מָתְנֶיךָ, כִּי לֹא תִסְמֹךְ עוֹד עָלַי. וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְלָא תְהֵא לְהוֹן לְבֵית סוּמְכְוָון״. וְיֵשׁ מַחֲלִיפִין **וְהַעֲמַדְתָּ לָהֶם** לוֹ הִמְעַדְתָּ, וְאֵינִי רוֹאֶה אֶת דִּבְרֵיהֶן. וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ עִם מַעֲמַד רַגְלַיִם, כָּמוֹנִי:


**and you made their loins stand upright** [Heb. וְהַעֲמַדְתָּ.] Since you will break, they will be required to strengthen their loins and stand on them. You are like a man upon whom his friend was leaning, but he is weak and he says to him, “Strengthen yourself on your loins, for you can no longer lean on me.” So did Jonathan render: “and you will no longer be a support for them.” Some transpose וְהַעֲמַדְתָּ to וְהַמְעַדְתָּ, and you make all loins totter, but I do not care for their words. Moreover, Menachem (p. 134) associated it with the standing of the feet, just as I do.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:11:1)


**וְלֹא תֵשֵׁב אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה.** אַרְבָּעִים וּשְׁתַּיִם שְׁנֵי רְעָבוֹן נִגְזְרוּ בַּחֲלוֹם פַּרְעֹה, כְּנֶגֶד שְׁלֹשָׁה פְּעָמִים שֶׁנִּכְתַּב הַחֲלוֹם: שֶׁבַע פָּרוֹת וְשֶׁבַע שִׁבֳּלִים רָעוֹת רָאָה (בראשית מא:א-ז), וְסִפְּרוֹ לְיוֹסֵף (בראשית מא:יז-כד), הֲרֵי שְׁנֵי פְעָמִים, וְיוֹסֵף אָמַר לוֹ: ״שֶׁבַע הַפָּרוֹת הָרַקּוֹת וְהָרָעוֹת וְשֶׁבַע הַשִּׁבֳּלִים הָרֵקוֹת״ (בראשית מא:כז), הֲרֵי אַרְבָּעִים וּשְׁתַּיִם לָרָעָב; וְלֹא הָיָה לָהֶם אֶלָּא שְׁתַּיִם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״כִּי זֶה שְׁנָתַיִם הָרָעָב״ (בראשית מה:ו); וּמִשֶּׁיָּרַד יַעֲקֹב לְמִצְרַיִם פָּסַק הָרָעָב, שֶׁהֲרֵי בַּשָּׁנָה הַשְּׁלִישִׁית זָרְעוּ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְתֵן זֶרַע וְנִחְיֶה וְגוֹ׳״ (בראשית מז:יט); וְהָאַרְבָּעִים נִפְרְעוּ לָהֶם עַכְשָׁיו (בראשית רבה פט:ט):


**neither shall it be inhabited for forty years** Forty-two years of famine were decreed in Pharaoh’s dream, corresponding to the three times the dream is written. He saw seven bad cows and seven bad ears of grain (Gen. 41) and he told it to Joseph; hence we have [it mentioned] twice, and Joseph said to him, “The seven thin and bad cows and the seven empty ears,” totaling forty-two for the famine. But they had only two, as it is stated (ibid. 45:6): “For it is two years now that the famine has been on earth,” and when Jacob came down to Egypt, the famine ceased, for behold in the third year they sowed, as it is stated (ibid. 47:19): “and then you give us seed that we may live etc.,” and the forty years were paid to them now: “neither shall it be inhabited for forty years.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:11:2)


**תֵּשֵׁב אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה.** תֵּשֵׁב שְׁרָ״א אֲשִׁיגִיאָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


תֵּשֵּׁב means sera asijiee in Old French. will be settled, peopled.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:14:1)


**מְכוּרָתָם.** מְגוּרָתָם. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: כְּמוֹ תּוֹלְדוֹתָם, וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**their habitation** [Heb. מְכוּרָתָם] [like] מְגוּרָתָם, their habitation. Another interpretation: like תוֹלדוֹתָם, their birth, and this is how Menachem (p. 110) associated it.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:15:1)


**לְבִלְתִּי רְדוֹת בַּגּוֹיִם.** לְשׁוֹן מֶמְשָׁלָה, כְּמוֹ ״וּרְדוּ בִדְגַת הַיָּם״ (בראשית א:כח):


**So that they shall not domineer over the nations** [Heb. רְדוֹת,] an expression of ruling, like (Gen. 1:28): “and rule (וּרְדוּ) over the fish of the sea.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:16:1)


**וְלֹא יִהְיֶה.** מִצְרַיִם **לְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל עוֹד לְמִבְטָח הַמַּזְכִּיר** לָהֶם **עָוֹן**, שֶׁאָמַר לָהֶם הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: ״לֹא תֹסִיפוּ לִרְאוֹתָם עוֹד״ (שמות יד:יג):


**And it will no longer be** [i.e.,] Egypt [will no longer be] a source of confidence for the House of Israel, bringing iniquity into remembrance for them, since the Holy One, blessed be he, said to them (Exod. 14:13): “You shall no longer continue to see them.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:17:1)


**וַיְהִי בְּעֶשְׂרִים וְשֶׁבַע שָׁנָה.** לְמַלְכוּת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, אוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה נִתְּנָה מִצְרַיִם בְּיָדוֹ, כָּךְ שָׁנִינוּ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה כו); וְיֵשׁ בַּסֵּפֶר הַזֶּה הַרְבֵּה נְבוּאוֹת כְּתוּבוֹת מֻקְדָּם וּמְאֻחָר:


**And it came to pass in the twenty-seventh year** of the reign of Nebuchadnezzar. That year Egypt was delivered into his hand. This is what we learned in Seder Olam (ch. 26). In this book, there are many prophecies written in a non chronological order.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:18:1)


**הֶעֱבִיד אֶת חֵילוֹ.** עַל צוֹר עֲבוֹדָה גְדוֹלָה:


**made his army serve [a great work against Tyre]** [Heb. צוּר אֵל, usually to here means] against Tyre, [in] a great work.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:18:2)


**כָּל רֹאשׁ.** אַנְשֵׁי חֵילוֹתָיו:


**every head** the men of his forces


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:18:3)


**מֻקְרָח וְכָל כָּתֵף מְרוּטָה.** כְּדֶרֶךְ הַצָּרִים עַל הָעִיר יָמִים רַבִּים, וְטוֹרְחִים וִיגֵעִים בְּשֵׂאת מַשָּׂאוֹת עֵצִים וַאֲבָנִים. וְהוּא לָכַד אֶת צוֹר בְּעֶשְׂרִים וְשָׁלֹשׁ לְמַלְכוּתוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמָּצִינוּ בְּסֵדֶר עוֹלָם (סדר עולם רבה כו). **מֻקְרָח** קָאלְבּ״וּ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**became bald and every shoulder, etc.** As is the custom with those who besiege a city many days and toil and weary themselves with carrying burdens of wood and stones. He captured Tyre in the twenty third [year] of his reign, as we find in Seder Olam (ad loc.). מֻקְרָח means calvo in Italian, bald.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:18:4)


**מְרוּטָה.** פְּלִיאָ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**sore** [Heb. מְרוּטָה,] pelee in Old French, peeled.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:18:5)


**וְשָׂכָר לֹא הָיָה לוֹ.** לְאַחַר שֶׁשָּׁלְלוּ שְׁלָלָהּ, עָלָה הַיָּם וְשָׁטַף מֵהֶם, לְפִי שֶׁנִּגְזַר עָלֶיהָ וְעַל שְׁלָלָהּ לֵיאָבֵד בַּיָּם:


**yet he had no reward** After they had plundered its booty, the sea rose and washed it away from them, because it was decreed on it and on its booty to be lost in the sea.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:20:1)


**פְּעֻלָּתוֹ אֲשֶׁר עָבַד בָּהּ.** שְׂכַר פְּעֻלָּה אֲשֶׁר עָבַד בְּמִצְוָתִי בְּצוֹר, אֶתֵּן לוֹ אֶת אֶרֶץ מִצְרָיִם:


**For his labor that he undertook against it** [As] reward for the labor that he undertook against Tyre by My command, I shall give him the land of Egypt.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:20:2)


**אֲשֶׁר עָשׂוּ לִי.** מִצְרַיִם רָעָה, בְּמִשְׁעַנְתָּם שֶׁהִבְטִיחוּ אֶת עַמִּי, בְּעֶזְרָתָם, הֶבֶל (איכה ד:יז):


**because of what they did to Me** [Because of] the evil that Egypt [did to Me] with their prop, that they assured My people with their futile aid.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:21:1)


**בְּיוֹם הַהוּא אַצְמִיחַ קֶרֶן.** לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי וְלֹא מָצָאתִי פֵּירוּשׁ מִקְרָא זֶה מַה הוּא צֶמַח קַרְנָם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמַפֶּלֶת מִצְרַיִם, שֶׁהֲרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל גָּלוּ שְׁמוֹנֶה שָׁנִים לִפְנֵי מַפֶּלֶת מִצְרַיִם. וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי בְּיוֹם הַהוּא מוּסָב עַל פָּרָשָׁה שֶׁל מַעְלָה, **מִקֵּץ אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה אֲקַבֵּץ אֶת מִצְרַיִם**, וְאוֹתוֹ חֶשְׁבּוֹן כָּלָה בְּשָׁנָה שֶׁמָּלַךְ בֵּלְשַׁאצַּר, וּמָצִינוּ בְּדָנִיֵּאל שֶׁבְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה הִתְחִילוּ מַלְכֵי פָּרַס לְהִתְחַזֵּק וְנִגְזְרָה מַפָּלָה עַל בָּבֶל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דניאל ז:א) ״בִּשְׁנַת חֲדָא לְבֵלְשַׁאצַּר דָּנִיֵּאל חֵלֶם חֲזָה וְגוֹ׳ קַדְמִיתָא כְּאַרְיֵה הִיא בָּבֶל״, וּכְתִיב (שם) ״חָזֵה הֲוֵית עַד דִּי מְרִיטוּ גַפַּהּ וְגוֹמֵר וַאֲרוּ חֵיוָה אוֹחֲרִי תִנְיָנָא דָמְיָא לְדֹב״, הִיא פָּרַס, וּכְתִיב ״וְכֵן אָמְרִין לַהּ קוּמִי אֲכוּלִי בְּשַׂר שַׂגִּיא״, כְּלוֹמַר תִּפְשִׂי הַמְּלוּכָה, וּמַלְכוּת פָּרַס הִיא צְמִיחַת קֶרֶן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּכוֹרֶשׁ ״הוּא יִבְנֶה עִירִי וְגָלוּתִי יְשַׁלֵּחַ״ (ישעיהו מה:יג). וּמִנַּיִן שֶׁאַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה שֶׁל מִצְרַיִם כָּלוּ בְּאוֹתוֹ הַפֶּרֶק? מִצְרַיִם נִתְּנָה בְּיַד נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר בְּעֶשְׂרִים וָשֶׁבַע לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, בְּשָׁנָה שֶׁנֶּאֶמְרָה נְבוּאָה זוֹ לִיחֶזְקֵאל, הוֹסִיף אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה, הֲרֵי שִׁשִּׁים וְשֶׁבַע, צֵא מֵהֶם אַרְבָּעִים וְחָמֵשׁ לִנְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, וְעֶשְׂרִים וְשָׁלֹשׁ לְאֵוִיל מְרֹדַךְ, כִּדְאָמְרִינָן בְּמַסֶּכֶת מְגִלָּה (יא:), וְשָׁנָה אַחַת מֵהֶם עָלְתָה לָזֶה וְלָזֶה, כִּדְאָמְרִינָן הָתָם שָׁנִים מְקוּטָּעוֹת הָיוּ:


**On that day will I cause the horn of the House of Israel to blossom out** I have neither heard nor found the explanation of this verse. What is the blossoming of the horn of Israel in the downfall of Egypt? Was not Israel exiled eight years before the downfall of Egypt? [Therefore,] I say that “On that day” refers back to the above section, (verse 13): “At the end of forty years, I will gather the Egyptians.” That count ends in the year that Belshazzar assumed the throne, and we find in Daniel that in that year the kings of Persia began to gain strength, and downfall was decreed upon Babylon, as it is said (Dan. 7:1): “In the first year of Belshazzar,...Daniel saw a dream, etc.”; (verse 4) “The first one was like a lion” - that is Babylon. And it is written (ad loc.): “I saw until its wings were plucked off, etc. (verse 5) And behold another, second beast, resembling a bear” that is Persia. And it is written (ad loc.) “And thus it was said to it, ‘Devour much flesh.’” i.e., seize the kingdom. And the kingdom of Persia was the blossoming of the horn of Israel, as it is said regarding Cyrus (Isa. 45:13): “He shall build My city and free My exiles.” Now, how do we know that the forty years of Egypt ended at that time? [The proof is that] Egypt was given into the hands of Nebuchadnezzar in the twenty-seventh [year] of Nebuchadnezzar, in the year that this prophecy was said to Ezekiel. Add forty years, and you have sixty-seven. Deduct from them forty-five for Nebuchadnezzar and twenty-three for Evil- Merodach, as we say in Megillah (11b), one of these years counting for both [kings], as we say there: “they were incomplete years.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 29:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/29:21:2)


**וּלְךָ אֶתֵּן פִּתְחוֹן פֶּה בְּתוֹכָם.** לְךָ יְחֶזְקֵאל יְהֵא פִּתְחוֹן פֶּה כְּשֶׁיִּרְאוּ נְבוּאָתְךָ מִתְקַיֶּמֶת:


**and I will give you free speech in their midst** You, Ezekiel, will have free speech when they see your prophecy being fulfilled.

### Verse 2

**הָהּ.** כְּמוֹ אֲהָהּ; **הָהּ לַיּוֹם** הֶעָתִיד לָבֹא עַל אוֹתָן הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**Ha!** Like “Aha!” “Ha!” to the day that is destined to come upon those nations.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:3:1)


**עֵת גּוֹיִם.** ״עִידָּן תְּבַר עַמְמַיָּא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**the time of the nations** The time of the destruction of the nations.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:4:1)


**וְהָיְתָה חַלְחָלָה בְּכוּשׁ.** לְשׁוֹן רַעַד; כְּשֶׁיִּשְׁמְעוּ מַפֶּלֶת מִצְרַיִם, יֶחֶרְדוּ לְנַפְשָׁם, פֶּן יַעֲלֶה מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל עֲלֵיהֶם:


**and there will be quaking in Cush** [Heb. חַלְחָלָה,] expression of trembling. When they hear of Egypt’s downfall, they will fear for their lives: perhaps the king of Babylon will rise up against them.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:5:1)


**וְכָל הָעֶרֶב.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְכָל סוּמְכָוָותָא״, כָּל הָעוֹרְבִים עֲרוּבַת מִצְרַיִם; גרנסי״אה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and all who support her** [Heb. וְכָל הָעֶרֶב.]Jonathan renders: וְכָל סוּמְכָוָתָא, all those who guarantee the security of Egypt, garantie in French, guarantee.

**אֶרֶץ הַבְּרִית.** כּוֹרְתֵי בְרִית לְמִצְרַיִם, לַעֲזוֹר לָהּ:


**[and the people of] the allied land** Those who made a treaty with Egypt to aid her.

**בְּתִתִּי אֵשׁ.** חֲמָתִי כָּאֵשׁ:

**מַלְאָכִים מִלְּפָנַי.** גְּדוּדֵי גַּיָּסוֹת הוֹלְכִים בִּשְׁלִיחוּתִי, בְּשַׁיָּרוֹת רַבּוֹת, **לְהַחֲרִיד אֶת כּוּשׁ** הַיּוֹשֶׁבֶת **בֶּטַח**:


**emissaries...from before Me** Troops of armies going on My mission in many caravans to frighten Cush, which is dwelling securely.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:9:2)

**With legions** [Heb. בַּצִים,] like (Num. 24:24): “And legions (וְצִים) from the place of the Kittites,” and so too did Jonathan render it: with legions.

### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:21:1)


**אֶת זְרוֹעַ פַּרְעֹה מֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם שָׁבָרְתִּי.** כְּבָר פַּעַם אַחֶרֶת, שֶׁנָּתַתִּי חֵילוֹ בְּיַד נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית לִיהוֹיָקִים, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסֵפֶר יִרְמְיָה (ירמיהו מו:ב) ״לְמִצְרַיִם עַל חֵיל פַּרְעֹה נְכֹה מֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם אֲשֶׁר הָיָה בְּכַרְכְּמִישׁ עַל נְהַר פְּרָת אֲשֶׁר הִכָּה נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר וְגוֹמֵר״:


**I have broken the arm of Pharaoh, the king of Egypt** already another time, for I placed his army in the hands of Nebuchadnezzar in the fourth year of Jehoiakim, as it is said in the Book of Jeremiah (46:2): “Concerning Egypt, concerning the army of Pharaoh-neco, the king of Egypt, which was on the Euphrates in Carcemish, whom Nebuchadnezzar king smote, etc.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:21:2)


**וְהִנֵּה לֹא חֻבָּשָׁה.** מֵאוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם וָהָלְאָה, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסֵפֶר מְלָכִים בְּסוֹפוֹ (מלכים ב כד:ז): ״וְלֹא יָסַף עוֹד מֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם לָצֵאת מֵאַרְצוֹ כִּי לָקַח מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל מִנְּהַר פְּרָת עַד נַחַל מִצְרַיִם״, וּבְסִגְנוֹן זֶה נִתְנַבֵּא יִרְמְיָה גַּם הוּא עַל אוֹתָהּ הַמַּכָּה, בִּלְשׁוֹן רְפוּאָה אֵין לָהּ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (ירמיהו מו:יא): ״עֲלִי גִלְעָד וּקְחִי צֳרִי בְּתוּלַת בַּת מִצְרַיִם לַשָּׁוְא הִרְבֵּית רְפוּאוֹת תְּעָלָה אֵין לָךְ״:


**and behold, it was not bound** from that day on, as it is said at the end of the Book of Kings (II 24:7): “And the king of Egypt no longer went out of his land, for the king of Babylonia had taken from the river of Egypt,” and with this expression Jeremiah, too, prophesied concerning that blow, using the language “it has no cure,” as it is said (Jer. 46:11): “Go up to Gilead and take balm, O virgin daughter of Egypt; in vain you have increased medicines, you have no cure.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:21:3)


**לֹא חֻבָּשָׁה.** לְשׁוֹן קְשִׁירָה, שֶׁקּוֹשְׁרִין עֶצֶם הַנִּשְׁבָּר:


חֻבָּשָּׁה is an expression of binding, for they bind the broken bone.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:21:4)


**לָשׂוּם חִתּוּל.** מַעֲשֵׂה בְּגָדִים וּרְטִיָּה, כְּמוֹ ״וְהָחְתֵּל לֹא חֻתָּלְתְּ״ (יחזקאל טז:ד):


**to place a bandage** [Heb. חִתּוּל,] [a dressing] made of cloth and plaster, like (above 16: 44): “nor swaddled at all (והֳחְתֵּל לֹא חֻתָּלְתְּ).”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:21:5)


**לִתְפֹּשׂ בֶּחָרֶב.** שֶׁיְּהֵא לוֹ עוֹד כֹּחַ לְהִלָּחֵם:


**to grasp a sword** that he should have more strength to wage war.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:22:1)


**וְשָׁבַרְתִּי אֶת זְרֹעֹתָיו.** אֶשְׁבּוֹר אֶת הַשֵּׁנִית, וְתִהְיֶינָה שְׁתֵּיהֶן שְׁבוּרוֹת; כְּלוֹמַר: כְּבָר לָקַח מֶלֶךְ בָּבֶל מִצַּד הָאֶחָד, עוֹד אֶמְסוֹר לוֹ יֶתֶר הַמַּלְכוּת כֻּלּוֹ:


**and I shall break his arms** I shall break the second [arm], and both of them will be broken; i.e., the king of Babylon took from one side; I shall yet give him the entire kingdom.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:24:1)


**וְחִזַּקְתִּי.** לְשׁוֹן חוֹזֶק:


**And I shall strengthen** [Heb. וְחִזַּקְתִּי,] an expression of strength.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 30:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/30:25:1)


**וְהַחֲזַקְתִּי.** לְשׁוֹן אֲחִיזָה, שֶׁאוֹחֵז בְּיָדוֹ לִסְמִיכָה שֶׁלֹּא תִּפֹּל; וְסוֹפוֹ יוֹכִיחַ: **וּזְרֹעוֹת פַּרְעֹה תִּפֹּלְנָה**, שֶׁאֵינוֹ אוֹחֵז בָּהֶן:


**And I shall grasp** [Heb. וְהֲחֲזַּקְתִּי,] an expression of grasping, that one grasps his hand to support it so that it should not fall. The end of it proves it: “and the arms of Pharaoh will fall,” because He does not hold them.



## Chapter 31


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:2:1)


**אֶל מִי דָּמִיתָ בְגָדְלֶךָ.** בְּהִתְגַּדֶּלְךָ לְנֶגְדִּי, אֶל מִי הָיִיתָ סָבוּר לִדְמוֹת?


**Whom are you like in your greatness** In your haughtiness against Me, whom did you think to resemble?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:3:1)


**הִנֵּה אַשּׁוּר.** שֶׁהִפַּלְתִּי כְּבָר:


**Behold Assyria** which I have already cast down.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:3:2)


**אֶרֶז בַּלְּבָנוֹן.** הָיָה:


**a cedar in Lebanon** it was


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:3:3)


**וְחֹרֶשׁ מֵצַל.** יַעַר מְסַכֵּךְ צֵל גָּדוֹל:


**shady woods** A forest that covers, [making] a great shade.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:3:4)


**וּבֵין עֲבוֹתִים.** עֲצֵי רַעֲנָן:


**and among the interwoven branches** the green trees.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:3:5)


**צַמַּרְתּוֹ.** צִימָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**its lofty tops** [Heb. צַמַּרְתּוֹ,] cime in French, top.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:4:1)


**תְּעָלֹתֶיהָ.** פוֹשִׁי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**its canals** [Heb. תְּעָלתֶיהָ,] fosses in French, ditches, canals.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:4:2)


**אֶל כָּל עֲצֵי הַשָּׂדֶה.** לְהַשְׁקוֹתָם. וְהַדֻּגְמָא, כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ שֶׁל יוֹנָתָן: ״הָא אַתּוּרָאָה דַמְיָא לְאַרְזָא בְּלִבְנָן וְגוֹמֵר״, כִּבְעַמּוּד:


**to all the trees of the field** to water them. The symbol is as the Targum of Jonathan interprets it: Behold the Assyrians were like the cedars of Lebanon, etc., as above.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:4:3)


**מַיִם גִּדְּלוּהוּ תְּהוֹם רוֹמְמָתְהוּ.** ״בְּעַמְמַיָּא סַגִּי בְּסוּמְכְוָותָא תְּקוֹף יָת מַלְכַיָּא שַׁעֲבִיד לִתְחוֹת מַלְכוּתֵיהּ וְיָת שַׁלְטוֹנוֹהִי מַנִּי עַל כָּל מְדִינוֹת אַרְעָא״, וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה **וּבֵין עֲבוֹתִים הָיְתָה צַמַּרְתּוֹ** מֵהֵיכָן זָכָה לִגְדֻלָּה? לְפִי שֶׁנִּמְנַע מֵעֲצַת דּוֹר הַפְּלָגָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״מִן הָאָרֶץ הַהִיא יָצָא אַשּׁוּר״ (בראשית י:יא), כְּשֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ כָּל בָּאֵי הָעוֹלָם קְלִיעָה אַחַת לָבוֹא בְּעֵצָה לִמְרֹד בּוֹ, אָז הָיְתָה צַמַּרְתּוֹ נִרְאֵית לְעַצְמָהּ וְלֹא נִשְׁתַּלְשְׁלָה עִמָּהֶם. **מַיִם גִּדְּלוּהוּ תְּהוֹם רוֹמְמָתְהוּ.** וְעַל שֶׁשָּׁמַע לְקוֹל יוֹנָה בֶּן אֲמִתַּי, שֶׁיָּרַד לַתְּהוֹם וְשָׁבוּ אַנְשֵׁי נִינְוֵה מִן הֶחָמָס אֲשֶׁר בְּכַפֵּיהֶם (פסיקתא רבתי לג):


**Water nurtured it; the deep made it grow tall** [Jonathan paraphrases:] Among nations he is great; because of his supporters he is powerful; he subjugates kings under his kingdom, and he appoints rulers over every state of the earth. The Midrash Aggadah (Pesikta Rabbathi 34:11, Meir Ayin 33) [states]: “and among the interwoven branches was its lofty top.” Whence did he merit greatness? Because 1) he withdrew from the counsel of the generation of separation, as it is said (Gen. 10: 11): “Out of that land Asshur went forth. “When all those who came upon the world formed one league to enter counsel to rebel against Him, then his lofty top was visible by itself, and [he] did not link himself with them. “Water nurtured him; the [waters of the] deep raised him up” and because 2) he hearkened to the voice of Jonah the son of Amittai, who descended into the [waters of the] deep, and the people of Nineveh repented of the violence that was in their hands.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:5:1)


**סַרְעַפֹּתָיו.** עֲנָפָיו:


**its boughs** [Heb. סַרְעַפֹּתָיו,]


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:5:2)


**פֹארֹתָיו.** זְמוֹרוֹתָיו:


**its branches** [Heb. פֹארֹתָיו.]


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:5:3)


**מִמַּיִם רַבִּים בְּשַׁלְּחוֹ פֹּארוֹתָיו.** עַל יְדֵי מַיִם רַבִּים. **בְּשַׁלְּחוֹ פֹּארוֹתָיו.** כְּלוֹמַר: בְּגַדְּלוֹ פֹּארוֹתָיו. וְלָשׁוֹן שִׁלּוּחַ נוֹפֵל בָּהֶן, כְּמוֹ ״וַתְּשַׁלַּח פֹּארוֹת״ (יחזקאל יז:ו):


**because of abundant waters when it sent forth** its branches through the abundant waters. When it sent forth its branches, i.e., when it grew its branches, and the expression “sending” applies to them, as “and sent forth branches.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:6:1)


**בִּסְעַפֹּתָיו.** בַּעֲנָפָיו:


**In its boughs** [Heb. בִּסְעַפֹתָיו.]


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:6:2)


**קִנְּנוּ.** לְשׁוֹן קֵן:


**nested** [Heb. קִנְנוּ,] from the word קֵּן, nest.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:7:1)


**וַיִּיף.** לְשׁוֹן יֹפִי:


**And it became beautiful** [Heb. וַיִיף,] an expression of beauty (יֹפִי).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:7:2)


**דָּלִיּוֹתָיו.** אַף הֵם לְשׁוֹן עֲנָפִים הַנְּטוּיִם לְאֹרֶךְ לְכָל צַד, כְּמִין דָּלִיּוֹת הַכֶּרֶם, שישטרוי״ילאש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**its tendrils** [Heb. דָּלִיוֹתָיו.] They too are an expression used for branches: [those] that extend lengthwise in every direction, like the tendrils of a vineyard, ses treilles in French, its climbing vines.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:8:1)


**לֹא עֲמָמוּהוּ.** לֹא כִּהוּ תָּאֳרוֹ, כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא הָיָה בָּהֶם אֶחָד נָאֶה מִמֶּנּוּ:


**did not dim it** They did not dim its form; i.e., none among them surpassed it in beauty.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:8:2)


**בְּגַן אֱלֹהִים.** כְּלוֹמַר: בְּכָל הָעוֹלָם:


**in the garden of God** i.e., in the entire world.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:8:3)


**וְעַרְמֹנִים.** קַאשְׁטַנִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and chestnut trees** [Heb. ועֲרְמֹנִים,] chastaniyers, chastaneyrs in Old French [chataigniers in Modern French].



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:9:1)


**כָּל עֲצֵי עֵדֶן.** כְּלוֹמַר כָּל הַמְּלָכִים הַמְשׁוּבָּחִים:


**all the trees of Eden** i.e., all the praiseworthy kings.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:10:1)


**יַעַן אֲשֶׁר גָּבַהְתָּ בְּקוֹמָה.** כָּךְ אָמַרְתִּי לְאַשּׁוּר:


**Because you grew haughty with your height** So I said to Assyria.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:11:1)


**בְּיַד אֵל גּוֹיִם.** בְּיַד קָשֶׁה שֶׁבַּגּוֹיִם, בְּיַד נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר נְתַתִּיו; דְּאָמַר מַר (מגילה יא:): שָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה כָּבַשׁ נִינְוֵה:


**into the hands of the strongest of nations** into the hands of the harshest of nations; I gave him into the hands of Nebuchadnezzar, as the master said (Seder Olam, ch. 24; Meg. 11b; Arachin 12a): In his first year, he conquered Nineveh.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:11:2)


**עָשׂוֹ יַעֲשֶׂה לוֹ.** נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, כְּכָל רְצוֹנִי:


**that he execute upon him** [that] Nebuchadnezzar [execute upon him] in accordance with all My wishes.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:12:1)


**וַיִּטְּשֻׁהוּ.** וַיַּפִּילוּהוּ לָאָרֶץ, כְּמוֹ ״וַיִּטֹּשׁ עַל הַמַּחֲנֶה״ (במדבר יא:לא):


**cast it down** [Heb. וַיִטְשֻּׁהוּ,] and they cast it down to the earth, like (Num. 11:31): “and let them down (וַיִטשּׁ) upon the camp.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:13:1)


**עַל מַפַּלְתּוֹ יִשְׁכְּנוּ וְגוֹ׳.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״עַל מַפֶּלֶת קְטִילוֹהִי״:


**Upon its carcass...rested** as the Targum paraphrases: Upon the carcass of his slain.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:14:1)


**עֲצֵי מַיִם.** שֶׁהֵם רַכִּים וְחַלָּשִׁים, אֶלָּא שֶׁעֲנָפֵיהֶם רַכִּים וַאֲרֻכִּין מֵחֲמַת לִחְלוּחִית הַמַּיִם; אֵין לָהֶם לְדַמּוֹת עַצְמָם לְאַרְזֵי עֵדֶן. כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא הָיָה לוֹ לְהִתְגָּאוֹת עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל. **וְלֹא יַעַמְדוּ** עֲלֵיהֶם:


**water trees** which are tender and frail, but have numerous and long branches because of the moisture of the water, should not compare themselves to the cedars of Eden; i.e., he should not have been haughty over Israel and should not have stood over them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:14:2)


**אֶל בֵּין עֲבוֹתִים.** לֹא יִתְגָּאוּ לְהַשְׁווֹת גְּבוּרָתָם, שׁוֹתֶה מַיִם, **אֶל עֲצֵי עֲבוֹתִים**:


**among the interwoven branches** They should not haughtily compare their might - the trees that drink water to the interwoven trees.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:1)


**בְּיוֹם רִדְתּוֹ שְׁאוֹלָה.** כְּשֶׁהִשְׁפַּלְתִּי אֶת מֶלֶךְ אַשּׁוּר בְּיַד אוֹיְבוֹ:


**On the day he descended into the Grave** When I humbled the king of Assyria into the hands of his enemy.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:2)


**הֶאֱבַלְתִּי כִּסֵּתִי עָלָיו.** אֲבֵלִים רַבִּים, שֶׁהָיוּ סְמוּכִין עָלָיו:


**I caused mourning** [I caused] many mourners who relied on him [to mourn] over him.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:3)


**כִּסֵּיתִי עָלָיו אֶת תְּהוֹם.** כִּסֵּיתִי בִּשְׁבִילוֹ אֶת תְּהוֹם שֶׁהָיָה רָגִיל לְהַשְׁקוֹתוֹ וּלְגַדְּלוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה **תְּהוֹם רוֹמְמַתְהוּ**; אוֹתוֹ הַיּוֹם כִּסִּיתִיו, שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲלֶה וִיכַבֶּה אֶת תְּבֵעָרָתוֹ; כְּלוֹמַר: הֶחֱלַטְתִּי אֶת עוֹזְרָיו:


**I covered the deep on his account** I covered the deep because of him, for it was accustomed to water him and cause his growth, as Scripture stated above (verse 4): “the deep made it grow tall.” That day, I covered it so that it should not ascend and extinguish his fire. i.e., I weakened his aids.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:4)


**וָאֶמְנַע נַהֲרוֹתֶיהָ.** שֶׁהָיוּ הוֹלְכִין סְבִיבוֹת מַטָּעָהּ (יחזקאל לא:ד):


**I withdrew its rivers** which were flowing around its planting.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:5)


**וַיִּכָּלְאוּ מַיִם רַבִּים.** שֶׁהָיוּ מַאֲרִיכִין פְּאֹרוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה **וַתֶּאֱרַכְנָה פְאֹרוֹתָיו מִמַּיִם רַבִּים**:


**abundant waters stopped** [The waters] that used to make its branches grow long, as he said above (verse 5): “and its branches grew long because of abundant waters.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:6)


**וָאַקְדִּיר.** בִּשְׁבִילוֹ לְבָנוֹן:


**and I darkened** the Lebanon because of him.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:7)


**וָאַקְדִּיר.** לְשׁוֹן שַׁחֲרוּת:


[Heb. וָאַקְדִיר,] from an expression for blackness.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:8)


**וְכָל עֲצֵי הַשָּׂדֶה.** שֶׁהָיוּ תְּעָלוֹתָיו שְׁלוּחִין אֲלֵיהֶם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה (יחזקאל לא:ד):


**and all the trees of the field** to which his channels were sent, as stated above.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:15:9)


**עָלָיו עֻלְפֶּה.** בִּשְׁבִילוֹ פָּסַק לִחְלוּחָם, כְּאָדָם שֶׁמִּתְעַלֵּף וְרוּחוֹ פּוֹרַחַת; פשמי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**swooned over him** Because of him, their moisture ceased, as a person who swoons and whose spirit flies away [i.e., he loses consciousness]; pasmer in Old French, to faint, to swoon. [pamer in Modern French.]



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:16:1)


**וַיִּנָּחֲמוּ בְּאֶרֶץ תַּחְתִּית.** שְׁאָר יוֹרְדֵי גֵיהִנֹּם קִבְּלוּ תַּנְחוּמִים; אָמְרוּ: אַף זֶה כָּמוֹנוּ:


**and they were consoled in the nether part of the earth** The others who descended to Gehinnom received consolations. They said, “This one is like us also.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:17:1)


**וּזְרֹעוֹ יָשְׁבוּ בְצִלּוֹ.** וְעוֹזְרָיו אֲשֶׁר יָשְׁבוּ בְצִלּוֹ:


**and his allies, who dwelt in his shade** [Heb. וּזְרֹעוֹ, lit. and his arm,] and his helpers, who dwelt in his shade.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:18:1)


**אֶל מִי דָמִיתָ כָּכָה.** אַתָּה פַּרְעֹה, אֶל מִי מֵ**עֲצֵי עֵדֶן** דָּמִיתָ, שֶׁיְּהֵא כָּכָה גָּדוֹל כְּאַשּׁוּר **בְּכָבוֹד וּבְגֹדֶל**? וַהֲרֵי רָאִיתָ שֶׁהוֹרַדְתִּיו שְׁאוֹל; וְאַף אַתָּה: **וְהוּרַדְתָּ** עִם **עֲצֵי עֵדֶן** – עִם שְׁאָר מְלָכִים חֲזָקִים:


**To whom have you likened yourself so** You, Pharaoh, to which of the trees of Eden have you likened yourself, who is as great as Assyria in glory and in greatness? Behold you have seen that I lowered him into the Grave, and you too will be lowered with the trees of Eden, with the other strong monarchs.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 31:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/31:18:2)


**אֶל אֶרֶץ תַּחְתִּית.** גֵּיהִנֹּם:


**into the nether part of the earth** Gehinnom.



## Chapter 32


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:2:1)


**כְּפִיר גּוֹיִם נִדְמֵיתָ וְאַתָּה כַּתַּנִּים בַּיַּמִּים.** הָיָה לְךָ לִרְבֹּץ תּוֹךְ יְאוֹרְךָ כְּמִשְׁפַּט הַדָּגִים וְלֹא לָצֵאת אֶל הַיַּבָּשָׁה, וְנִתְגָּאִיתָ בְּלִבְּךָ וְנִדְמֵיתָ בְּעַצְמְךָ לִכְפִיר הַשַּׁלִּיט בַּיַּבָּשָׁה וְיָצָא לִטְרֹף טֶרֶף:


**You resembled a young lion among the nations, but you are like a crocodile in the seas** You should have lain in the midst of your rivers, as is the custom of the fish, and not gone out to the dry land; but you were haughty in your heart, and you compared yourself to a young lion, which dominates the dry land and goes forth to tear prey.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:2:2)


**וַתָּגַח בְּנַהֲרוֹתֶיךָ.** וְתֵצֵא חוּץ עִם נַהֲרוֹתֶיךָ; לְשׁוֹן ״גּוֹחִי מִבֶּטֶן״ (תהלים כב:י); ״כִּי יָגִיחַ יַרְדֵּן״ (איוב מ:כג); ״וְאֹרֵב יִשְׂרָאֵל מֵגִיחַ מִמְּקוֹמוֹ״ (שופטים כ:לג); לְשׁוֹן דָּבָר הַנּוֹגֵעַ וְיוֹצֵא מִבֵּית מִסְתָּרִים:


**and you went out with your rivers** [Heb. וַתָּגַח,] and you went out with your rivers, the same meaning as in (Ps. 22:10): “You drew me (גֹחִי) from the womb”; (Job 40:23), “he will draw (יָגִיחַ) the Jordan into his mouth”; Jud. 20:33), “And the liers in wait of Israel drew forth (מֵגִיחַ).” [This is] an expression for something flowing and going out of a hidden place.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:2:3)


**וַתִּדְלַח.** לְשׁוֹן עֲכִירָה:


**and you sullied** [Heb. וַתִּדְלַח,] an expression of making something murky.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:2:4)


**בְּרַגְלֶיךָ.** רַגְלַיִם לֹא הָיוּ לְךָ, וְעָשִׂיתָ לְךָ רַגְלַיִם כְּחַיַּת הָאָרֶץ לַעֲבֹר מֵימֵי אֲרָצוֹת וְלִרְפֹּס **נַהֲרוֹתָם**; וְהַדֻּגְמָא בְּתַרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וַאֲגַחְתָּא בְּמַשִׁירְיָיתָךְ וַאֲזַעְתָּא עַמְמַיָּא בְּסוּמְכְוָותָךְ וַאֲחְרִיבְתָּא מְדִינָתְהוֹן״:


**with your feet** You had no feet, but I made for you feet like the beasts of the earth, to cross the waters of the lands and to tread their rivers. The symbolism is like Targum Jonathan: and you waged war with your camps and you caused the peoples to quake with your supporters, and you destroyed their countries.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:3:1)


**בְּחֶרְמִי.** לְשׁוֹן מְצוּדָה; וְהַרְבֵּה יֵשׁ בַּמִּקְרָא: ״יְגֹרֵהוּ בְחֶרְמוֹ״ (חבקוק א:טו); ״מְצוֹדִים וַחֲרָמִים״ (קהלת ז:כו):


**with My net** [Heb. בְּחֶרְמִי,] a word for a net, and there are many in Scriptures, [e.g.,] (Hab. 1: 15): “he catches them in his net (בְּחֶרְמוֹ)”; (Ecc. 7:26), “snares and nets (וַחֲרָמִים).”



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:5:1)


**וּמִלֵּאתִי הַגֵּאָיוֹת רָמוּתֶךָ.** הַשְׁלָכָתֶיךָ; כְּמוֹ ״רָמָה בַיָּם״ (שמות טו:א), וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: רָמוּתֶךָ – לְשׁוֹן ׳רִמָּה׳, כְּמוֹ ״וַיָּרֻם תּוֹלָעִים וַיִּבְאַשׁ״ (שמות טז:כ):


**and I shall fill the valleys with what is cast off from you** [Heb. רָמוּתֶךָ] your casting, like (Exod. 15: 1): “He cast (רָמָה) into the sea.” Others say that רָמוּתֶךָ is meant as a form of the word for worm (רִמָה), like (ibid. 16:20): “and it became alive with worms (וַיָרֻם).”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:6:1)


**וְהִשְׁקֵיתִי אֶרֶץ צָפָתְךָ.** אֶרֶץ אֲשֶׁר אַתָּה צָף בָּהּ עַל פְּנֵי מֵימֵי יְאוֹרֶיךָ; כְּמוֹ ״צָפוּ מַיִם עַל רֹאשִׁי״ (איכה ג:נד); ״וַיָּצֶף הַבַּרְזֶל״ (מלכים ב ו:ו); אוֹתָהּ הָאָרֶץ אַשְׁקֶה **מִדָּמְךָ**:


**And I shall water...the land where you float** [Heb. צָפָתְךָ,] the land where you float on the surface of the water of your rivers-like (Lam. 3:54): “Water floated (צָפוּ) over my head”; (II Kings 6:6): “and the axe blade floated (וַיָצֶף)” - that land I will water with your blood.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:6:2)


**צָפָתְךָ.** טוֹנְפְרוֹט״מְנְט בְּלַעַ״ז, עַל הֶהָרִים הַגְּבוֹהִין שֶׁבָּהּ:


**even to the mountains** the highest [mountains] in it. צָפָתְךָ is ton flottement in French, your floating, swimming.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:6:3)


**וַאֲפִיקִים.** רָאוּיִ״רְשׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and the streams** [Heb. וַאֲפִיקִים,] riveyes in Old French, gullies, gulches; streams.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:7:1)


**וְכִסֵּיתִי בְכַבּוֹתְךָ שָׁמַיִם.** כְּשֶׁתִּכְבֶּה תַּבְעֵרָתְךָ, יַעֲלֶה הֶעָשָׁן וִיכַסֶּה הַשָּׁמַיִם וְיַחְשִׁיךְ הַמְּאוֹרוֹת; כְּלוֹמַר: כָּל שׁוֹמְעֵי שִׁמְעֲךָ יִתְאַבְּלוּ וְיִשְׁתּוֹמְמוּ, כִּי יִדְאַג אִישׁ לְנַפְשׁוֹ לֵאמֹר: גַּם עָלֵינוּ יַעֲמֹד הַמַּשְׁחִית:


**And with your smoke I will cover the heavens** [Heb. בְּכַבּוֹתְךָ, lit. with you extinguishing.] When your conflagration will be extinguished, the smoke will rise and cover the sky and darken the luminaries; i.e., all who hear the news of what happened to you will mourn and be astonished, because everyone will worry about himself, saying, “The destroyer will stand up over us as well.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:8:1)


**כָּל מְאוֹרֵי אוֹר בַּשָּׁמַיִם.** שָׂרִים שֶׁל מַעְלָה, מְלִיצֶיךָ:


**All the bright lights in the heavens** the heavenly princes, your intercessors.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:9:1)


**וְהִכְעַסְתִּי לֵב.** כְּלוֹמַר: יִרְגַּז וְיֶחֱרַד לִבָּם:


**And I will vex the heart** i.e., their heart will tremble and quake.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:9:2)


**בַּהֲבִיאִי שִׁבְרְךָ.** ״בְּאַיְתִיוֹתִי תְּבִירֵי קְרָבָךְ לְבֵינֵי עַמְמַיָּא״ (תרגום יונתן):


**when I bring your ruin** when I bring the ruin of your battles among the peoples.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:10:1)


**וַהֲשִׁמּוֹתִי.** לְשׁוֹן תִּמָּהוֹן:


**And I will cause...to be appalled** [Heb. וַהֲשִּׁוֹתִי,] an expression of bewilderment.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:10:2)


**בְּעוֹפְפִי חַרְבִּי עַל פְּנֵיהֶם.** לְשׁוֹן עוֹף פּוֹרֵחַ (תנחומא, בשלח יח): בְּשָׁלְחִי עָלֶיךָ חֵיל שְׁלוּחַי דֶּרֶךְ אַרְצָם:


**when I cause My sword to fly before their faces** [Heb. בְּעוֹפְפִי,] an expression of a flying bird, when I send upon you the army of My emissaries through their land.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:10:3)


**וְחָרְדוּ לִרְגָעִים.** לִשְׁבָרִים, פֶּן גַּם עֲלֵיהֶן יָבֹא הַשֶּׁבֶר. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: לְשׁוֹן ׳רֶגַע׳ מַמָּשׁ, כֵּן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**and they will quake at their ruin** [Heb. לִרְגָעִים,] to ruins, lest ruin come upon them as well. Another explanation: an expression of an actual moment (רֶגַע). In this sense, Menachem (p. 162) connects it.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:10:4)


**אִישׁ לְנַפְשׁוֹ.** כָּל אֶחָד עַל עַצְמוֹ, שׁוֹיְימָ״אִימָאשׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**everyone for himself** each one concerning himself, soi- meme in French, for himself.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:13:1)


**מֵעַל מַיִם.** שֶׁל שְׁאָר מְדִינוֹת, שֶׁהָיִיתָ עוֹכְרָם:


**from beside...waters** of other countries that you used to sully.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:14:1)


**אַשְׁקִיעַ מֵימֵיהֶם.** שֶׁלֹּא תְעַכְּרֵם רֶגֶל בְּהֵמָה, יִשְׁקַע הַטִּיט וְיִהְיוּ הַמַּיִם צְלוּלִין וּנְקִיִּים **כַּשֶּׁמֶן** הַמְזֻקָּק:


**I shall sink their waters** The foot of cattle will not make it murky, and the mud will sink, so that the water will be clear and clean like refined oil.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:15:1)


**אֶרֶץ מִמְּלוֹאָהּ.** אֶרֶץ שֶׁהִיא עַתָּה מִמְּלוֹאָהּ מִכָּל טוּב:


**a land that is full** [Heb. אֶרֶץ מִמְלוֹאָה, a land that is now full (מִמְוּלָאָה) of all goodness.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:18:1)


**נְהֵה.** בְּכֵה וְקוֹנֵן:


**lament** Weep and lament.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:18:2)


**וְהוֹרִידֵהוּ אוֹתָהּ וּבְנוֹת וגו׳.** הִתְנַבֵּא עָלָיו וְעַל כָּל מַכְחִישֵׁי הַנְּבוּאָה, שֶׁיֵּרְדוּ לִבְאֵר שַׁחַת; כָּאן הֶרְאָהוּ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לִיחֶזְקֵאל, שֶׁכָּל מַכְחִישֵׁי הַנְּבוּאָה יוֹרְדִין לְגֵיהִנֹּם (תנחומא, לך כ):


**and bring it down, it and the daughters, etc.** prophesy, about him and about all the heathens, that they will descend into the Pit of Destruction. Here the Holy One, blessed be He, showed Ezekiel that all the heathens descend to Gehinnom.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:19:1)


**מִמִּי נָעָמְתָּ.** כְּלוּם נָעַמְתָּ מִשְּׁאָר הָ**עֲרֵלִים**?! אַף אַתָּה **רְדָה** לַבּוֹר **וְהָשְׁכְּבָה** אִתָּם:


**Whom have you surpassed in pleasantness** Are you any better than the other uncircumcised ones? You too will descend into the Pit and lie with them.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:20:1)


**חֶרֶב נִתָּנָה.** לַחֶרֶב נִתְּנָה מִצְרַיִם:


**it was delivered to the sword** Egypt was delivered to the sword.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:21:1)


**יְדַבְּרוּ לוֹ.** יְדַבְּרוּ עָלָיו וְעַל עוֹזְרָיו:


**shall speak of him** [Heb. יְדַבְּרוּ לוֹ] shall speak of him and of his helpers.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:21:2)


**אֵילֵי גִבּוֹרִים מִתּוֹךְ שְׁאוֹל.** סָרֵס הַמִּקְרָא וְדָרְשֵׁהוּ **יְדַבְּרוּ לוֹ אֶת עֹזְרָיו**, עִם עוֹזְרָיו, כְּלוֹמַר: גַּם עַל עוֹזְרָיו אִתּוֹ יְדַבְּרוּ אֵילֵי גִבּוֹרִים שֶׁבִּשְׁאוֹל, וְכָכָה יֹאמְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם: **יָרְדוּ שָׁכְבוּ הָעֲרֵלִים וְגוֹ׳**. כָּל לוֹ וְלִי וְלָהֶם וְלְךָ הַסְּמוּכִין לְדִבּוּר, אֵינָם אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן עַל:


**the strongest of the mighty men...from the midst of the Grave** Transpose the verse and interpret it: They shall speak of him אֶתעֹזרָיו, with his helpers; i.e., concerning all his helpers with him, too, shall the strongest of the mighty men, who are in the Grave, speak, and they will say about them thus: **the uncircumcised... descended, etc.** Every לָהֶם, לִי, לוֹ, and לְךָ written next to דִבּוּר, speech, means nothing but “about.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:22:1)


**שָׁם אַשּׁוּר.** בְּגֵיהִנֹּם:


**There are Assyria** in Gehinnom.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:22:2)


**סְבִיבוֹתָיו.** בְּתוֹךְ שְׁאוֹל, קִבְרוֹתָיו מוּכָנִין, כִּי מִשָּׁם לֹא יֵצֵא לָנוּחַ בְּקִבְרוֹ:


**around it [are its graves]** In the midst of the Grave its graves are prepared, for from there it will not emerge to rest in a grave.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:23:1)


**בְּיַרְכְּתֵי בוֹר.** בְּמַעֲמַקֵּי בוֹר, בְּתוֹךְ עָמְקוֹ:
