**in the utmost part of the Pit** in the depths of the Pit; in the midst of its depth.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:23:2)


**וַיְהִי קְהָלָהּ.** בְּתוֹךְ שְׁאוֹל **סְבִיבוֹת קְבֻרָתָהּ**:


**and whose company was** in the midst of the Grave, “was around its grave.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:23:3)


**אֲשֶׁר נָתְנוּ חִתִּית.** אֲשֶׁר נָתְנוּ שֶׁבֶר **בְּאֶרֶץ** יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**who cast destruction** [Heb. חִתִית,] who cast destruction in the land of Israel.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:25:1)


**נָתְנוּ מִשְׁכָּב לָהּ.** לְ**עֵילָם**:


**they provided a bed for her** for Elam.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:26:1)


**מְחֻלְּלֵי.** כְּמוֹ הֲרוּגֵי:


**slain by the sword** [Heb. מְחֻלְלֵי,] like הֲרוּגֵי.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:27:1)


**וְלֹא יִשְׁכְּבוּ.** אֵלֶּה הַנִּזְכָּרִים, עִם **גִּבּוֹרִים** שֶׁמֵּתוּ מִיתַת עַצְמָם, כִּי **נוֹפְלִין/b> וּגְרוּעִים יִהְיוּ <b>מֵעֲרֵלִים אֲשֶׁר** מֵתוּ מִיתַת עַצְמָם וְ**יָרְדוּ בִּכְלֵי מִלְחַמְתָּם שְׁאוֹל**:


**But they will not lie** These who were mentioned [will not lie] with the mighty who died a natural death, for they will be inferior to and worse than the uncircumcised who died a natural death and descended with their weapons into the Grave.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:27:2)


**וַיִּתְּנוּ.** קוֹבְרֵיהֶם **אֶת חַרְבוֹתָם תַּחַת רָאשֵׁיהֶם** לְסִימָן, לְהוֹדִיעַ כִּי לֹא שָׁלְטָה בָּהֶם חֶרֶב:


**and they laid** i.e., those who buried them [laid]...**their swords under their heads** as a sign to make it known that the sword did not prevail over them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:27:3)


**וַתְּהִי עֲוֺנוֹתָם עַל עַצְמוֹתָם.** לֹא נֶחְסַר מֵעֲווֺנָם כְּלוּם, שֶׁלֹּא כִּפְּרָה עֲלֵיהֶם חֶרֶב הֲרִיגָה, שֶׁלֹּא נֶהֶרְגוּ; וּמָה הוּא הֶעָוֹן? **כִּי חִתִּית הַגִּבּוֹרִים** הָהֵם הָיְתָה **בְּאֶרֶץ חַיִּים**:


**and their iniquities were upon their bones** Nothing was lacking from their iniquity, for the slaying sword did not expiate it, because they were not slain. Now what is the iniquity? That the destruction wrought by these mighty men was in the land of the living.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:28:1)


**וְאַתָּה בְּתוֹךְ.** לְפַרְעֹה הוּא אוֹמֵר:


**And you - in the midst of the uncircumcised** He is addressing Pharaoh.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:28:2)


**אֶת חַלְלֵי חָרֶב.** וְלֹא עִם הַמֵּתִים מִיתַת עַצְמָם:


**with those slain by the sword** and not with those who die a natural death.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:30:1)


**בְּחִתִּיתָם מִגְּבוּרָתָם בּוֹשִׁים.** ״בְּאִתְבָּרוּתְהוֹן מִגְּבוּרַתְהוֹן בְּהָתוֹן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**ashamed when they are destroyed in spite of their might** [Jonathan renders:] When they are broken in spite of their might, they are ashamed.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 32:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/32:32:1)


**כִּי נָתַתִּי אֶת.** חִתִּיתִי **בְּאֶרֶץ חַיִּים**, כִּי אֶתֵּן אֲנִי אֶת מוֹרָאִי בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל (אדר״נ נוסח ב עמ׳ 122), וְלֹא תִנָּתֵן עוֹד חִתִּית אָדָם בָּהּ:


**For I shall cast My fear into the land of the living** For I shall cast My fear into the land of the living and the fear of man will no longer be cast therein.



## Chapter 33


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:2:1)


**לְצֹפֶה.** בָּאדִי״טָא בְּלַעַ״ז:


**as a lookout** [Heb. לְצֹפֶה,] badete in O.F., sentry, watchman.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:3:1)


**וְרָאָה אֶת הַחֶרֶב.** הֵמָּה הַגְּיָסוֹת:


**And he sees the army** [lit. the sword.] They are the armies.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:4:1)


**דָּמוֹ בְרֹאשׁוֹ יִהְיֶה.** עֲוֹן מִיתָתוֹ יִדָּרֵשׁ מִיַּד עַצְמוֹ, שֶׁפָּשַׁע בְּנַפְשׁוֹ:


**his blood will be on his own head** The iniquity of causing his death will be required of himself, because he was neglected his life.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:5:1)


**וְהוּא נִזְהָר נַפְשׁוֹ מִלֵּט.** אִם נִזְהָר, הָיָה נִמְלָט:


**for had he taken heed, he would have saved his life** Had he taken heed, he would have escaped.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:7:1)


**צוֹפֶה נְתַתִּיךָ.** כְּאוֹתוֹ הַצּוֹפֶה הַנִּיתָּן לְהַזְהִיר שֶׁלֹּא תֹּאבַד נֶפֶשׁ, כֵּן נְתַתִּיךָ **לְהַזְהִיר** אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**I have made you a lookout** Like that lookout, who is appointed to give warning so that no soul be lost, so have I appointed you to warn Israel.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:10:1)


**כֵּן אֲמַרְתֶּם.** לְפִיכָךְ אֵין אַתֶּם חֲפֵצִים לָשׁוּב, כִּסְבוּרִין אַתֶּם שֶׁלֹּא תוֹעִיל לָכֶם תְּשׁוּבָה:


**So have you spoken** Therefore, you do not wish to repent, because you believe that repentance will not avail you.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:12:1)


**לִחְיוֹת בָּהּ.** בְּצִדְקָתוֹ:


**live with it** with his righteousness.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:13:1)


**לֹא תִזָּכַרְנָה.** לְהַצִּילוֹ מִלֵּיעָנֵשׁ עַל עֲבֵירָה זוֹ:


**none... will be remembered** to save him from being punished for his sin.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:15:1)


**חֲבוֹל.** עֲבוֹט, שֶׁצִּוִּיתִי לַהֲשִׁיבוֹ כְּבֹא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ (דברים כד:יג):


**the pledge** the pledge that I commanded [one] to return at sunset.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:18:1)


**וּמֵת בָּהֶם.** בִּשְׁבִילָם יֵעָנֵשׁ:


**he will die because of them** He will be punished for them.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:22:1)


**וַיִּפְתַּח אֶת פִּי.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר בְּרֹאשׁ הַסֵּפֶר: ״וְנֶאֱלַמְתָּ וְלֹא תִהְיֶה לָהֶם לְאִישׁ מוֹכִיחַ״ (יחזקאל ג:כו), וְאַחַר כָּךְ אָמַר לוֹ: ״בְּיוֹם קַחְתִּי מֵהֶם אֶת מְעוּזָם״, ״יָבוֹא הַפָּלִיט אֵלֶיךָ״, ״בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא יִפָּתַח פִּיךָ אֶת הַפָּלִיט... וְלֹא תֵאָלֵם עוֹד״ (יחזקאל כד:כה-כז):


**and He opened my mouth** as He says in the beginning of the Book (3:26): “and you shall become mute, and you shall not be a reprover to them.” Afterward, He said to him (24:25ff.): “on the day I take from them their strength, etc., the fugitive will come to you, etc., on that day, your mouth will be opened with the fugitive, and you will speak and no longer be mute.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:22:2)


**עַד בּוֹא אֵלַי בַּבֹּקֶר.** כְּלוֹמַר: בְּעוֹד בּוֹאוֹ אֵלַי בַּבֹּקֶר, כְּמוֹ: לִפְנֵי בּוֹאוֹ אֵלַי בַּבֹּקֶר:


**before [his] coming to me in the morning** [Heb. עַד בּוֹא אֵלִי בַּבֹּקֶר,] i.e., when he had not yet (בְּעוֹד) come to me in the morning, like: before he came to me in the morning.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:24:1)


**אֶחָד הָיָה אַבְרָהָם.** זֶה אֶחָד מֵאַרְבָּעָה דְּבָרִים שֶׁהָיָה רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא דּוֹרֵשׁ וְאֵין רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן יוֹחַאי תַּלְמִידוֹ דּוֹרֵשׁ כְּמוֹתוֹ. וְכָךְ הָיָה רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דּוֹרְשׁוֹ (תוספתא סוטה ו:ז): אַבְרָהָם שֶׁלֹּא נִצְטַוָּה אֶלָּא עַל מִצְוָה אַחַת יָרַשׁ אֶת הָאָרֶץ, אָנוּ שֶׁנִּצְטַוִּינוּ עַל מִצְוֹת הַרְבֵּה כָּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁלָּנוּ נִתְּנָה הָאָרֶץ לְמוֹרָשָׁה; הוּא שֶׁהַנָּבִיא מֵשִׁיבָם:


**Abraham was one** This is one of four things that Rabbi Akiva interpreted [in one way] and which his disciple, Rabbi Shimon ben Yochai, did not interpret as he did. Rabbi Shimon interpreted it in this manner: Abraham, who was commanded with only one commandment [namely circumcision], inherited the land. We, then, who have been commanded with many commandments, should surely have the land given to us for an inheritance (Tosefta Sotah 6:7). This is what the prophet answers them.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:25:1)


**עַל הַדָּם תֹּאכֵלוּ וְגוֹ׳ וְהָאָרֶץ תִּירָשׁוּ.** בִּתְמִיהָּ? אִם נִצְטַוִּיתֶם, אֵין אַתֶּם שׁוֹמְרִים! **עַל הַדָּם תֹּאכֵלוּ**. אֶת מַאֲכָלֵי תַּפְנוּקִים אַתֶּם אוֹכְלִים עַל יְדֵי רְצִיחָה, שֶׁהוֹרְגִין אֶת בַּעֲלֵי מָמוֹן וְנוֹטְלִין אֶת מָמוֹנָם:


**You eat on the blood, etc., and you should inherit the land** This is a rhetorical question. If you were commanded [many commandments], you do not keep [them]. You eat on the blood: you eat delicacies through murder, for you slay those who have money and take away their money.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:26:1)


**עֲמַדְתֶּם עַל חַרְבְּכֶם.** כְּלוֹמַר: כָּל בִּטְחוֹנְכֶם נִתְחַזַּקְתֶּם עַל חַרְבְּכֶם:


**You stood on your sword** i.e., all your trust. You strengthened yourself on your sword.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:26:2)


**עֲשִׂיתֶן תּוֹעֵבָה.** מִשְׁכַּב זָכוּר (סוטה ו,ט):


**you committed abominations** homosexual acts.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:27:1)


**אֲשֶׁר בֶּחֳרָבוֹת.** בֶּעָרִים הַבְּצוּרוֹת, שֶׁסּוֹפָן לִהְיוֹת חֲרֵיבִים:


**those who are in the ruins** in the fortified cities that will ultimately become ruins.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:27:2)


**וַאֲשֶׁר בַּמְּצָדוֹת.** בַּמַּחֲבָא, לְהִטָּמֵן מִן הַחֶרֶב, אֶשְׁלַח בָּם אֶת הַדֶּבֶר:


**and those who are in the strongholds** in a hideout, to hide from the sword - I shall send the pestilence upon them.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:30:1)


**הַנִּדְבָּרִים בְּךָ.** מִתְלוֹצְצִים בְּךָ:


**who talk about you** who mock you.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:31:1)


**וְיֵשְׁבוּ לְפָנֶיךָ.** כְּאִלּוּ הֵם עַמִּי:


**and they will sit before you as My people** as if they were My people.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:31:2)


**עֲגָבִים.** שְׂחוֹק, כְּמוֹ ״עוּגָב״ (איוב כא:יב):


**jokes** [Heb. עֲגָבִים,] laughter, related to עוּגָב, a flute.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:31:3)


**בִּצְעָם.** גְּזֵלָתָם:


**their gain** their stolen goods.



### Verse 32

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:32:1)


**וְהִנְּךָ לָהֶם כְּשִׁיר עֲגָבִים.** ״וְהָא אַתְּ לְהוֹן כְּזָמַר אֲבוּבִין״ (תרגום יונתן):


**And you are to them as a song on the flute** And you are to them as a song on the flute.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:33:1)


**וּבְבֹאָהּ.** הָרָעָה אֲלֵיהֶם:


**And when it comes** the evil to them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 33:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/33:33:2)


**הִנֵּה בָאָה.** כִּי אֱמֶת מוּכֶנֶת הִיא לָבֹא, אָז יֵדְעוּ כִּי לֹא שְׂחוֹק הָיוּ דְּבָרֶיךָ, **כִּי נָבִיא** אֱמֶת **הָיָה בְתוֹכָם**:


**behold it is coming** for, indeed, it is ready to come; then they will know that your words were not a jest, but that a true prophet was in their midst.



## Chapter 34


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:2:1)


**עַל רוֹעֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** עַל פַּרְנָסֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**concerning the shepherds of Israel** concerning the leaders of Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:2:2)


**אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ רֹעִים אוֹתָם.** אֶת עַצְמָם הָיוּ מְעַנְּגִין, מִמָּמוֹן חֲבֵרֵיהֶם אֲשֶׁר תַּחְתֵּיהֶם:


**who were shepherding themselves** [Heb. אוֹתָם,] them. They enjoyed themselves with the money of their fellows who were under them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:2:3)


**הֲלֹא הַצֹּאן יִרְעוּ הָרֹעִים.** הֲלֹא מִשְׁפַּט הָרוֹעִים לִרְעוֹת אֶת הַצֹּאן, וְלֹא לִרְעוֹת אֶת עַצְמָן!


**Do not the shepherds shepherd the flocks** It is not the custom of shepherds to shepherd the flocks and not to shepherd themselves?



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:4:1)


**אֶת הַנַּחְלוֹת.** אֶת הַכְּחוּשׁוֹת מֵאֵין כֹּחַ; דוֹלְרוֹשִׁי״אָשׁ בְּלַעַ״ז; שֶׁצְּרִיכוֹת חִזּוּק, לִתְמֹךְ בָּהֶן בַּיָּד:


**The frail** [Heb. הַנַחְלוֹת,] those emaciated without strength, maigre in French, lean, which need strengthening, to support them with the hand.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:4:2)


**לֹא חֲבַשְׁתֶּם.** לִקְשֹׁר שִׁבְרֵי הָעֶצֶם זֶה עִם זֶה, כְּמִשְׁפַּט הָרוֹפְאִים:


**you did not bind** to bind the fragments of the bone, one with the other, as is the practice of the physicians.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:4:3)


**וְאֶת הַנִּדַּחַת.** שֶׁנִּדְּחֵית מִתּוֹךְ הָעֵדֶר אֶל הַחוּץ:


**those astray** who strayed from the midst of the flock, to the outside.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:4:4)


**רְדִיתֶם.** יִסַּרְתֶּם:


**you chastised** [Heb. רְדִיתֶם,] chastised.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:6:1)


**יִשְׁגּוּ צֹאנִי.** הוֹלְכוֹת טוֹעוֹת עַל פְּנֵי **כָּל הֶהָרִים**; כְּלוֹמַר: נַעֲשׂוּ הֶפְקֵר לַעֲשׂוֹת אִישׁ אִישׁ כִּרְצוֹנוֹ, וְלֹא הוֹכַחְתֶּם אוֹתָם וְלֹא שְׁפַטְתֶּם בֵּין אִישׁ לְרֵעֵהוּ:


**My flock strayed** They wander astray over the face of all the mountains; i.e., they are going free for anyone to do to them as he wishes, and you did not reprove them and did not judge between a man and his fellow.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:8:1)


**וַיִּרְעוּ הָרֹעִים אוֹתָם.** אֶת עַצְמָם:


**and the shepherds shepherded themselves** [Heb. אוֹתָם, lit. them.]



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:10:1)


**וְלֹא יִרְעוּ עוֹד הָרוֹעִים אוֹתָם.** אֶת עַצְמָם:


**and the shepherds will no longer shepherd themselves** [Heb. אוֹתָם,] themselves.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:11:1)


**וּבִקַּרְתִּים.** לְשׁוֹן בִּקּוּר חוֹלִים:


**and I shall seek them out** [Heb. וּבִקַרְתִּים,] in the sense of visiting (בִּיקוּר) the sick.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:12:1)


**נִפְרָשׁוֹת.** כְּשֶׁהֵן נְפוּצוֹת, וְהוּא הוֹלֵךְ בֵּינֵיהֶם וּמְאַסְּפָן יַחַד:


**separated** when they are scattered, and he walks among them and gathers them back together.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:12:2)


**בְּיוֹם עָנָן וַעֲרָפֶל.** כִּי כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ הַצֹּאן לְהִתְפַּזֵּר בְּיוֹם הַמְעֻנָּן:


**on a cloudy and dark day** for it is usual for the flocks to scatter on a cloudy day.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:16:1)


**וְאֶת הַשְּׁמֵנָה וְאֶת הַחֲזָקָה.** הַהוֹדֶפֶת וְדוֹחֶפֶת אֶת חַבְרוֹתֶיהָ:


**the fat and the strong I will destroy** who pushes and presses her fellows.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:17:1)


**בֵּין שֶׂה לָשֶׂה.** בֵּין בַּעֲלֵי אֶגְרוֹפִין (סנהדרין כא.) לַחֲלָשִׁים:


**between one lamb and another** between the strong, violent men and the weak ones.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:18:1)


**הַמְעַט מִכֶּם.** אֶת אֲשֶׁר הַחֲזָקוֹת שֶׁבָּכֶם רוֹעוֹת **הַמִּרְעֶה הַטּוֹב**? וְהַלְוַאי לֹא **תִּרְמְסוּ** הַמּוֹתָר **בְּרַגְלֵיכֶם**, וְתֹאכַלְנָה אוֹתוֹ שְׁאָר צֹאנִי; אֶלָּא שֶׁאַף מוֹתַר **מִרְעֵיכֶם תִּרְמְסוּ בְּרַגְלֵיכֶם**. וְלֹא דַּי לָכֶם אֲשֶׁר מַשְׁקֵה **מַיִם תִּשְׁתּוּ**, אֶלָּא שֶׁ**הַנּוֹתָרִים בְּרַגְלֵיכֶם תִּרְפֹּשׂוּן**:


**Is it little for you** that the strongest among you graze on the good pasture? If only you would not [also] tread the residues with your feet, and would let the rest of My flock eat them! But even the residue of your pasture you tread with your feet! And is it not enough for you that you drink the clear water, that you must tread the remainder with your feet?


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:18:2)


**וּמִשְׁקַע מַיִם.** הַמַּיִם הַצְּלוּלִים, שֶׁשָּׁקַע הַטִּיט, וְלֹא נֶעֶכְרוּ בְרַגְלַיִם. וְהַדּוּגְמָא כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״הַזְּעִיר לְכוֹן דְּפַרְנוֹס טָב אַתּוּן מִתְפַּרְנְסִין, וּמוֹתַר פַּרְנוֹסְכוֹן דָּיְשִׁין בְּרַגְלֵיכוֹן, וּמִשְׁתֵּי מְתַקֵּן אַתּוּן שָׁתָן, וְיָת מוֹתְרָא בְּרַגְלֵיכוֹן אַתּוּן דָּיְשִׁין״:


**the clear water** [Heb. וּמִשְּׁקַע מַיִם,] lit. sunken water, the clear water, whose mud sank. It was not made murky with [the movement of] the feet. The symbolic meaning is as stated by the Targum: Is it little for you that [you] feed yourself the best food, and your attendants must eat the residue of your food? And [that] you drink the best, and your attendants must drink the residue?



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:19:1)


**וְצֹאנִי מִרְמַס רַגְלֵיכֶם וְגוֹ׳.** ״וְעַמִּי מוֹתַר מֵיכַל שַׁמָּשֵׁיכוֹן אָכְלִין וּמוֹתַר מִשְׁתֵּי שַׁמָּשֵׁיכוֹן שָׁתָן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**And my flocks, etc.** And My people eat the residue of the food of your attendants and drink the residue of the drink of your attendants.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:20:1)


**שֶׂה בְרִיָה.** חֲזָקָה:


**a strong lamb** [Heb. בְרִיָה,] strong.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:20:2)


**רָזָה.** מֵיִיגְרָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**lean** [Heb. רָזָה,] maigre in French.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:23:1)


**עַבְדִּי דָוִד.** מֶלֶךְ מִזַּרְעוֹ:


**My servant David** A king [who will come] from his descendants.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:25:1)


**וַיִּשְׁנוּ בַּיְּעָרִים.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְיִתְעַתְּקוּן בְּחוּרְשָׁא״, וְיֵשׁ לְפוֹתְרוֹ בִּלְשׁוֹן שִׁינָה:


**and grow old in the forests** [Heb. וְיָשְּׁנוּ.] Jonathan renders: and they will age [from יָשָׁן] in the forest. It may also be interpreted as an expression of sleep (שִּׁינָה).



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:26:1)


**וְנָתַתִּי אוֹתָם וּסְבִיבוֹת גִּבְעָתִי.** תְּהֵא נְתִינָתָהּ בִּבְרָכָה; **וְנָתַתִּי** כְּמוֹ ״וְהִשְׁכַּנְתִּי״ (יחזקאל לב:ד):


**And I shall make them dwell around My hill** [Heb. וְנָתַתִּי.] Their dwelling shall be blissful. וְנָתַתִּי, and I shall place, is like וְהִשְּׁכַּנְתִי and I shall cause to dwell.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:29:1)


**מַטָּע לְשֵׁם.** קִיּוּם שֶׁיְּהֵא לְשֵׁם עוֹלָם:


**a plantation for renown** an existence that will obtain everlasting fame.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:29:2)


**אֲסֻפֵי רָעָב.** טְמוּנֵי רָעָב, מֵחֲמַת בּוֹשֶׁת רְעָבוֹן וְעוֹנִי:


**hidden because of hunger** hidden because of hunger, because of the disgrace of hunger and poverty.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 34:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/34:31:1)


**אָדָם אַתֶּם.** אֵינְכֶם כַּבְּהֵמוֹת בְּעֵינַי:


**you are man** You are not like animals in My eyes.



## Chapter 35


### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:5:1)


**וַתַּגֵּר.** וְתַשְׁפֵּל, כְּמוֹ ״וְהִגַּרְתִּי לַגַּיְא אֲבָנֶיהָ״ (מיכה א:ו); שֶׁהָיוּ מַסְגִּירִין אוֹתָן לְאוֹיְבֵיהֶן כְּשֶׁהָיוּ בּוֹרְחִין דֶּרֶךְ אַרְצָם, וְזוֹ הִיא שֶׁאָמַר ״אַל תַּעֲמֹד עַל הַפֶּרֶק״ (עובדיה א:יד):


**and you hurled** [Heb. וַתַּגֵר,] you cast down, as (Micah 1:6): “and I will cast (וְהִגַרְתִּי) its stones down into the valley,” for they would deliver them to their enemies as they fled through their land, and this is what Obadiah (1:14) says: “And you should not have stood by the gap [to cut off their fugitives, nor should you have delivered their survivors on the day of distress].”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:5:2)


**בְּעֵת אֵידָם.** בְּעֵת כְּשֶׁמְּסַרְתִּים בְּיַד אוֹיֵב:


**on the day of their misfortune** when I delivered them into the hands of the enemy.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:5:3)


**בְּעֵת עֲוֹן קֵץ.** כְּשֶׁנִּגְמַר קֵץ עֲוֹנָם וְנִתְמַלְּאָה סְאָתָם (על פי שוחר טוב י):


**at the time of the end of their iniquity** when the end of their iniquity culminated, and their measure was full.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:6:1)


**לְדָם אֶעֱשְׂךָ.** נוֹפֵל בַּחֶרֶב (שמואל ב ג:כט):


**I shall make you into blood** falling by the sword.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:6:2)


**דָּם שָׂנֵאתָ.** בָּזִיתָ אֶת הַבְּכוֹרָה, שֶׁעֲבוֹדָתִי הָיְתָה מְסוּרָה לָהֶם, וְשָׂנֵאתָ לִהְיוֹת מְלַכְלֵךְ עַצְמְךָ בְּדַם קָרְבַּן עֲבוֹדָתִי, וְלִכְלוּךְ **דָּם** הֲרוּגִים **יִרְדְּפֶךָ** (בראשית רבה סג:יג). וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: רוֹצְחִים אַתָּה יָרֵא כְּשָׁאָר כָּל אָדָם, וְרוֹצְחִים יִרְדְּפוּךָ; וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: **דָּם שָׂנֵאתָ** אָחִיךָ, שֶׁהוּא בְּשָׂרְךָ וְדָמְךָ:


**you hated blood** You despised the birthright, for its sacrificial service was handed over to them (sic), but “you hated” to filthy yourself with the blood of sacrificial offerings in worship of Me. And the filth of the blood of the slain will pursue you. Others say: You hated the blood of circumcision (Gen. Rabbah 63:13). Others say: You fear murderers just as anyone else, and murderers will pursue you. Others say: “You hated blood,” [meaning that you hated] your brother, who is your flesh and blood.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:9:1)


**וְעָרֶיךָ לֹא תָשׁוֹבְנָה.** לֹא כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לְמִצְרַיִם ״וְשַׁבְתִּי אֶת שְׁבוּת מִצְרָיִם״ (יחזקאל כט:יד); ״וְשַׁבְתִּי אֶת שְׁבוּת בְּנֵי עַמּוֹן״ (ירמיהו מט:ו). וְזוֹ הִיא שֶׁאָמַר מַלְאָכִי ״וְאֶת עֵשָׂו שָׂנֵאתִי וָאֶתֵּן אֶת הָרָיו שְׁמָמָה״ (מלאכי א:ג) – שֶׁעֲתִידִים לְשִׁמְמוֹת עוֹלָם:


**and your cities will not return** [Heb. תָשֹּׁבְנָה,] not as I said about Egypt (above 29:14): “And I will return the captives of Egypt”; (Jer. 49:6): “And afterward, I will return the captivity of the children of Ammon.” And this is what Malachi (1:3) said, “And I hated Esau, and I made his mountains desolate,” for I made them destined for everlasting desolations.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:10:1)


**שְׁנֵי הַגּוֹיִם.** יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיהוּדָה; וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (בראשית רבה סז:ח) דּוֹרְשׁוֹ כְּלַפֵּי שֶׁהָלַךְ עֵשָׂו אֶל יִשְׁמָעֵאל לְהִתְחַתֵּן בּוֹ, לְהַשִּׂיאוֹ שֶׁיְּעוֹרֵר עַל יִצְחָק עַל יְרֻשַּׁת אַבְרָהָם, וְיַהֲרֹג יִשְׁמָעֵאל אֶת יִצְחָק, וְאֶהְיֶה אֲנִי גּוֹאֵל הַדָּם וְאֶהֱרֹג אֶת יִשְׁמָעֵאל. וְזֶהוּ ״יִקְרְבוּ יְמֵי אֵבֶל אָבִי״ (בראשית כז:מא), וְאִירַשׁ אֲנִי אֶת יִצְחָק וְאֶת יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


**The two nations** Israel and Judah. The Midrash Aggadah of Rabbi Tanchuma (source unknown) explains it regarding [the incident in] which Esau went to Ishmael to become his son in law in order to persuade him to contest Isaac concerning the inheritance of Abraham, and [in order that] Ishmael would kill Isaac, and [he thought] “I will be the avenger of blood and kill Ishmael.” This is the meaning of (Gen. 27:41): “Let the days of mourning for my father draw near.” And I will inherit both Isaac and Ishmael.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:10:2)


**וַה׳ שָׁם הָיָה.** יוֹדֵעַ אֶת מַחְשְׁבוֹתֶיךָ:


**and the Lord was there** He knows your thoughts.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:11:1)


**וְעָשִׂיתִי כְּאַפְּךָ.** כְּאוֹתָהּ הָאַף שֶׁעָשִׂיתָ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, אֶעֱשֶׂה לְךָ:


**I shall commit [acts] like your [acts of] wrath** like the [acts of] wrath that you exerted against Israel, I will exert against you.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:11:2)


**וְנוֹדַעְתִּי בָם כַּאֲשֶׁר אֶשְׁפְּטֶךָ.** אָז יַכִּירוּ אֶת גְּבוּרָתִי:


**and I shall be known among them when I judge you** Then they will recognize My might.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:13:1)


**וְהַעְתַּרְתֶּם.** הִרְבֵּיתֶם, וְכֵן ״וַעֲתַר עֲנַן הַקְּטֹרֶת״ (יחזקאל ח:יא), לְשׁוֹן רִבּוּי:


**and you have multiplied your words** [Heb. וְהַעְתַּרְתֶּם,] you have increased. Similarly (above 8: 11): “and a thick (וַעֲתַר) cloud of incense,” an expression of increase.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 35:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/35:14:1)


**כִּשְׂמֹחַ כָּל הָאָרֶץ.** כְּשֶׁאֶטּוֹל אֲנִי אֶת מַלְכוּתִי, כִּי אָז תִּשְׂמַח הָאָרֶץ, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״ה׳ מָלָךְ תָּגֵל הָאָרֶץ״ (תהלים צז:א), אָז **שְׁמָמָה אֶעֱשֶׂה לָּךְ**:


**When the whole earth rejoices** when I claim My kingdom, for then the whole earth will rejoice, as the idea is stated (Ps. 97:1): “The Lord has reigned; the earth will exult.” Then I will make you a desolation.



## Chapter 36


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:2:1)


**הֶאָח.** שִׂמְחָה:


**“Aha!”** [An expression of] joy.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:2:2)


**וּבָמוֹת עוֹלָם.** אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁהוּא גָּבְהוֹ וְנוֹיוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם (שיר השירים רבה ד:יא):


**and “the high places of the world”** the land of Israel, which is the height and the beauty of the world.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:3:1)


**שַׁמּוֹת וְשָׁאֹף אֶתְכֶם מִסָּבִיב.** שְׁכֵינֵיכֶם הָיוּ מִשְׁתּוֹמְמִים וְתוֹמְהִין לִשְׁתּוֹק וְלִרְאוֹת בְּסוֹף חֻרְבַּנְכֶם, וְשׁוֹאֲפִים וְתָאֵבִים וּמְצַפִּים **לִהְיוֹתְכֶם מוֹרָשָׁה לִשְׁאֵרִית הַגּוֹיִם**:


**those around you were appalled and longed** [Your neighbors] were appalled and astounded [at the opportunity] to laugh and see the end of your destruction, and were longing, pining, and looking forward to your becoming an inheritance for the remnant of the nations.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:3:2)


**וַתֵּעֲלוּ עַל שְׂפַת לָשׁוֹן.** וְעַכְשָׁיו בְּחוּרְבַּנְכֶם הַכֹּל מְדַבְּרִים וּמְדוּבְּבִים בָּכֶם, לֵאמֹר: **הֶאָח**, הֲרֵי שָׁמֵמוּ:


**and you were brought up on the lips of every language** And now in your destruction, all talk and gossip (other editions: and hawk) about you, saying, “Aha! they have become desolate.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:3:3)


**וְדִבַּת עָם.** פַּארְלְמַנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and the gossip of every people** [Heb. וְדִבַּת,] parlemant in Old French, gossip, slander.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:5:1)


**לְמַעַן מִגְרָשָׁהּ לָבַז.** לְפִי שֶׁרָאוּ גֵּרוּשָׁהּ לָבַז, שֶׁהוּטְלָה לְבִזָּה:


**because her expulsion was for plunder** Because they saw her expulsion [as one] for plunder; that she was subjected to plunder.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:12:1)


**לְשַׁכְּלָם.** לְגָרֵשׁ יוֹשְׁבַיִךְ מִקִּרְבֵּךְ, הוּא קָרוּי שִׁכּוּל:


**to be bereaved of them** to drive your inhabitants from your midst. This is called שִּׁיכּוּל, bereavement.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:13:1)


**אוֹכֶלֶת אָדָם אָתְּ.** לְמוּדָה הִיא הָאָרֶץ הַזֹּאת לְהַשְׁחִית אֶת יוֹשְׁבֶיהָ: הָאֱמוֹרִיִּים כָּלוּ בָהּ וְהַיְּהוּדִים כָּלוּ בָהּ:


**You are a devourer of men** This land is accustomed to destroying its inhabitants. The Amorites perished therein, and the Jews perished therein.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:17:1)


**בֶּן אָדָם בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל יֹשְׁבִים עַל אַדְמָתָם וְגוֹ׳ כְּטֻמְאַת הַנִּדָּה.** הִמְשִׁילָהּ הַכָּתוּב לְנִדָּה, שֶׁבַּעְלָהּ מְצַפֶּה מָתַי תִּטְהַר וְתָאֵב לָשׁוּב אֵלֶיהָ:


**Son of man! The house of Israel, as long as they lived, etc., like the uncleanness of a woman in the period of her separation** Scripture likened them to a woman in the period of her separation, whose husband looks forward to when she will become clean, and longs to return to her.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:20:1)


**וַיָּבוֹא.** לְפִי שֶׁאָמַר ״בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל״ בְּרֹאשׁ הַפָּרָשָׁה (פס׳ יז), קָרָא כָּל הַפָּרָשָׁה בִּלְשׁוֹן יָחִיד. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה: הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בָּא עִמָּהֶם בַּגּוֹלָה, וְהִטָּה אֹזֶן מָה הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים הַשַּׁבָּאִין: הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ **עַם ה׳** וְלֹא הָיָה לוֹ יְכֹלֶת לְהַצִּילָן בְּמִדְרַשׁ אֵיכָה רַבָּתִי:


**And they entered** [Heb. וַיָבוֹא, lit. and he entered.] Since he says, “the house of Israel” at the beginning of the segment, He refers to them in the singular throughout the segment. According to Midrash Aggadah: the Holy One, blessed be He, came with them in exile and bent His ear [to hear] what the captors were saying. “Behold these are God’s people, and He had no power to save them.” In Midrash Lamentations Rabbah (Proem 15).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:20:2)


**וַיְחַלְּלוּ אֶת שֵׁם קָדְשִׁי.** הִשְׁפִּילוּ אֶת כְּבוֹדִי. וּמַהוּ הַחִלּוּל? בֶּאֱמֹר אוֹיְבֵיהֶם עֲלֵיהֶם: **עַם ה׳ אֵלֶּה וּמֵאַרְצוֹ יָצָאוּ**, וְלֹא הָיָה יְכֹלֶת בְּיָדוֹ לְהַצִּיל אֶת עַמּוֹ וְאֶת אַרְצוֹ:


**and they profaned My Holy Name** They lowered My honor. And what is the profanation? In that their enemies said of them, “These are the people of the Lord, and they have come out of His land, and He had no power to save His people and His land.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:22:1)


**לֹא לְמַעַנְכֶם אֲנִי עוֹשֶׂה.** הַתְּשׁוּעָה שֶׁאוֹשִׁיעֲכֶם:


**Not for your sake, etc.** is the salvation that I shall save you [by].



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:23:1)


**וְקִדַּשְׁתִּי אֶת שְׁמִי.** וּמַה הוּא הַקִּדּוּשׁ? **וְלָקַחְתִּי אֶתְכֶם מִן הַגּוֹיִם**:


**And I will sanctify My...Name** Now what is the sanctification? “I will take you from among the nations.”



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:25:1)


**מַיִם טְהוֹרִים.** אֲכַפֵּר עֲלֵיכֶם, וְאַעֲבִיר טֻמְאוֹתֵיכֶם בְּהַזָּיַת מֵי חַטָּאת, הַמַּעֲבִירִין טֻמְאַת הַמֵּת (במדבר ח:ז; יט):


**clean water** I will grant you atonement and remove your uncleanliness by sprinkling purification water, which removes [even the highest degree of defilement,] the defilement caused by a corpse.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:26:1)


**לֵב חָדָשׁ.** יֵצֶר שֶׁנִּתְחַדֵּשׁ לְטוֹבָה (אבות דרבי נתן ד):


**a new heart** An inclination that has been renewed for the better.



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:31:1)


**וּנְקֹטוֹתֶם בִּפְנֵיכֶם.** דְּמוֹנִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז; תִּמַּקּוּ בְּעַצְמִיכֶם, בְּבָשְׁתְּכֶם עַל הַגְּמוּל הָרָע אֲשֶׁר גְּמַלְתֶּם לְפָנַי, וַאֲנִי גּוֹמֵל לָכֶם טוֹבָה:


**and you will loathe yourselves in your own eyes** [Heb. וּנְקטֹתֶם,] demonir in Old French, to melt. You will melt in yourselves, from your shame over the evil recompense that you repaid Me whereas I recompense you with good.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:34:1)


**וְהָאָרֶץ הַנְּשַׁמָּה תֵּעָבֵד.** הָאָרֶץ **אֲשֶׁר הָיְתָה שְׁמָמָה** עַד הֵנָּה, תִּהְיֶה נֶעֱבֶדֶת וְנִזְרַעַת:


**And the desolate land** The land that was desolate until now will be tilled and sown.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:35:1)


**הָיְתָה כְּגַן עֵדֶן.** עַכְשָׁיו נֶהֶפְכָה לִהְיוֹת כְּגַן עֵדֶן:


**has become like the Garden of Eden** [Heb. הָיְתָה,] has now been transformed into being like the Garden of Eden.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:35:2)


**וְהֶעָרִים הַחֲרֵבוֹת.** זֶה יָמִים רַבִּים, עַכְשָׁיו **בְּצוּרוֹת יָשָׁבוּ**:


**and the cities that were destroyed** these many days, have now become settled as fortified cities.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:37:1)


**אִדָּרֵשׁ לְבֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל.** אֶתְפַּתֶּה לָהֶם בִּתְפִלָּתָם, בְּדָרְשָׁם אוֹתִי עַל **זֹאת**:


**I will...be inquired of by the house of Israel** I will be persuaded by them with their prayer when they entreat Me concerning this.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:37:2)


**אַרְבֶּה אוֹתָם. אָדָם, כַּצֹּאן** לָרֹב:


**I will multiply them** the men like sheep, they will be so many.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 36:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/36:38:1)


**כְּצֹאן קָדָשִׁים.** שֶׁהִיא בָּאָה לִירוּשָׁלַיִם בְּמוֹעֲדֵי פְּסָחִים. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר, כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״אַסְגִּינוּן בְּאִינָשָׁא וְאַצְלְחִינוּן בִּבְעִירָא״; כְּלוֹמַר: שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ עֲוֹנוֹתֵיהֶם נִזְכָּרִים עֲלֵיהֶן:


**Like the flocks appointed for the holy offerings** which come to Jerusalem at the times of the Passover sacrifices. Another explanation follows the Targum: I will multiply you with men, and I will cause them to prosper with cattle; i.e., their iniquities will not be remembered against them.



## Chapter 37


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:1:1)


**הָיְתָה עָלַי יַד ה׳ וַיּוֹצִיאֵנִי בְרוּחַ ה׳ וגו׳. יַד ה׳** – כָּל יַד ה׳ שֶׁבַּנְּבוּאָה לְשׁוֹן כְּפִיָּה הוּא, שֶׁהָיְתָה הָרוּחַ כּוֹפָה אוֹתוֹ לֵילֵךְ כְּמִשְׁתַּגֵּעַ אֶל מָקוֹם שֶׁהָרוּחַ חָפֵץ:


**The hand of the Lord came upon me and carried me out in the spirit of the Lord, etc.** Every “the hand of the Lord” in a prophecy is an expression of compulsion, meaning that the spirit would compel him to go as a madman to a place that the spirit desired.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:1:2)


**וְהִיא מְלֵאָה עֲצָמוֹת.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ שֶׁהָיוּ מִשֵּׁבֶט אֶפְרַיִם שֶׁיָּצְאוּ מִמִּצְרַיִם לִפְנֵי הַקֵּץ, וַהֲרָגוּם אַנְשֵׁי גַת הַנּוֹלָדִים בָּאָרֶץ (דברי הימים א ז:כא), כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים (סנהדרין צב:):


**and that was full of bones** Our Rabbis said (Sanh. 92b) that they were of the tribe of Ephraim, who left Egypt before the end [of the exile], and the people of Gath who were born in the land slew them, as is stated in (I) Chronicles (7: 20ff.)



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:2:1)


**וְהֶעֱבִירַנִי עֲלֵיהֶם סָבִיב.** לַבִּקְעָה; וְלֹא הִכְנִיסוֹ לְתוֹכָהּ, לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה כֹּהֵן:


**And He made me pass by them round about** the valley. But He did not bring him into its midst because he was a priest.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:3:1)


**הֲתִחְיֶינָה.** הֲנִרְאֶה בְּעֵינֶיךָ שֶׁיּוּכְלוּ לִחְיוֹת?


**can...become alive** Do you think that they can come to life?



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:6:1)


**וְנָתַתִּי עֲלֵיכֶם גִּידִים וְגוֹ׳.** וּבְסֵפֶר אִיּוֹב הוּא אוֹמֵר ״עוֹר וּבָשָׂר תַּלְבִּישֵׁנִי״ תְּחִלָּה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ ״וּבַעֲצָמוֹת וְגִידִים תְּסוֹכְכֵנִי״ (איוב י:יא)! אֶלָּא לְמָה הָיוּ אֵלּוּ דוֹמִים? לְאָדָם שֶׁפּוֹשֵׁט וְחוֹזֵר וְלוֹבֵשׁ, שֶׁמַּה שֶּׁפָּשַׁט אַחֲרוֹן לוֹבֵשׁ רִאשׁוֹן; אֲבָל בִּתְחִלַּת בְּרִיַּת הַוָּלָד, עוֹר וּבָשָׂר תְּחִלָּה וְאַחַר כָּךְ עֲצָמוֹת וְגִידִים (בראשית רבה יד:ה):


**And I will lay sinews upon you, etc.** But in the Book of Job it says (10:11): “You clothe me with skin and flesh” first, and afterwards, “and You cover me with bones and sinews.” But to what were these similar? To a man who undresses and then dresses [himself] again, in which case, what he took off last he puts on first, whereas at the beginning of the creation of the fetus, skin and flesh come first and afterwards bones and sinews.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:6:2)


**וְקָרַמְתִּי.** לְשׁוֹן קְרוּם, אִיקְרוֹטִיי״רְיָיא בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and cover you** [Heb. וְקָרַמְתִי,] from the word for skin (קְרוּם) ecro(s) ter in Old French, and I shall encrust (with skin).



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:7:1)


**וַיְהִי קוֹל. וְהִנֵּה רַעַשׁ** הָעֲצָמוֹת, שֶׁהָיוּ טוֹפְחִין זֶה לָזֶה:


**and there arose a noise** and behold the commotion of the bones, which were knocking one against the other.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:7:2)


**עֶצֶם אֶל עַצְמוֹ.** עַצְמוֹת כָּל אֶחָד; כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁנִּתְפַּזְּרוּ, הָיוּ קוֹפְצִין וּמִתְקָרְבִין כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד אֵצֶל מְקוֹם חִבּוּרוֹ:


**bone to its bone** The bones of each one, wherever they were scattered, were jumping and coming together, each one beside the place of its connection.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:9:1)


**מֵאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת.** כָּל מָקוֹם שֶׁהָלְכוּ נִשְׁמוֹתֵיהֶן לָשׁוּט, לְאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת הָעוֹלָם, מִשָּׁם יִתְקַבְּצוּ וְיָבֹאוּ:


**from four sides** Wherever their souls went to roam, to the four sides of the world, from there they will gather and come.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:9:2)


**וּפְחִי.** כְּמוֹ ״נוֹפֵחַ בָּאֵשׁ פֶּחָם״ (ישעיהו נד:טז):


**and breathe** [Heb. וּפְחִי,] like (Isa. 54:16): “who blows (נֹפֵחַ) on a charcoal fire.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:11:1)


**הָעֲצָמוֹת הָאֵלֶּה כָּל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל הֵמָּה.** רֶמֶז וְדֻגְמָא לְכָל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּצָרוֹתָם הֵמָּה, שֶׁהֲרֵי הֵם אוֹמְרִים: **יָבְשׁוּ עַצְמוֹתֵינוּ** בְּצָרוֹת, **אָבְדָה תִקְוָתֵנוּ**, וּמַה נּוֹחִיל לִתְשׁוּעָה עוֹד? דָּבָר אַחֵר (פרקי דרבי אליעזר לג): כֻּלָּם אֵלּוּ מִשֶּׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל הָיוּ, וּמִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֶחֱיִיתָם עַכְשָׁיו, הֵם אוֹמְרִים: **אָבְדָה תִקְוָתֵנוּ**, וְלֹא נִחְיֶה עוֹד שֵׁנִית כְּשֶׁיִּחְיוּ הַמֵּתִים:


**these bones are all the house of Israel** They are an intimation and an example of the entire house of Israel in their exile, for they say, “Our bones have become dried up from the troubles; our hope is lost, and what further hope can we have for salvation? Another explanation: All of these were of Israel, and because you revived them now, they say, “Our hope is lost, and we shall not come to life once again when the dead are resurrected.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:12:1)


**לָכֵן הִנָּבֵא.** שֶׁעָתִיד אֲנִי לְהַחֲיוֹתָם שֵׁנִית:


**Therefore, prophesy** that I am destined to resurrect you a second time.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:16:1)


**וְאַתָּה בֶן אָדָם קַח לְךָ עֵץ אֶחָד וגו׳. וּכְתוֹב עָלָיו לִיהוּדָה וְלִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל חֲבֵרָיו.** אַרְבַּע תֵּיבוֹת הַלָּלוּ כְּתוֹב עָלָיו, לֵאמֹר: עֵץ זֶה לִיהוּדָה וְלִשֵׁבֶט בִּנְיָמִין שֶׁנִּלְוָה עָלָיו:


**And you, son of man, take for yourself one stick and write upon it, ‘For Judah and for the children of Israel his companions’** These four words write upon it, to say that this stick is Judah’s and the tribe of Benjamin’s, who joined him.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:16:2)


**וְלָקַח עֵץ אֶחָד וּכְתֹב עָלָיו.** זֶה **לְיוֹסֵף עֵץ אֶפְרַיִם** וּשְׁאָר תִּשְׁעָה שְׁבָטִים שֶׁהָיוּ אַחֲרֵי יָרָבְעָם, שֶׁהָיָה מִשֵּׁבֶט אֶפְרַיִם (מלכים א יא:כו):


**and take one stick and write upon it** This is for Joseph: the stick of Ephraim and the remaining nine tribes, which followed Jeroboam, who was of the tribe of Ephraim.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:17:1)


**וְהָיוּ לַאֲחָדִים.** אֲנִי אֲחַבְּרֵם, לִשְׁנֵי הָעֵצִים, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ **עֵץ אֶחָד בְּיָדֶךָ**:


**and they shall be one** I shall join the two sticks, so that they will be one stick in your hand.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:19:1)


**הִנֵּה אֲנִי לוֹקֵחַ אֶת עֵץ וְגוֹ׳.** שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ עוֹד לִשְׁתֵּי מַמְלָכוֹת:


**Behold I will take the stick, etc.** that they shall no longer be two kingdoms.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 37:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/37:25:1)


**לְעַבְדִּי לְיַעֲקֹב.** כְּעֵין שֶׁנְּתַתִּיהָ לְיַעֲקֹב, בְּלִי מְצָרִים:


**to My servant, to Jacob** As I gave it to Jacob, without boundaries.



## Chapter 38


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:2:1)


**אֶל גּוֹג.** כָּךְ שֵׁם הַמֶּלֶךְ:


**toward Gog** So is the king’s name.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:2:2)


**הַמָּגוֹג.** שֵׁם הָאֻמָּה, כְּמָה דְאַתְּ אָמַר ״גֹּמֶר וּמָגוֹג״ (בראשית י):


**Magog** The name of the nation, as Scripture states (Gen. 10: 2): “Gomer and Magog.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:4:1)


**וְשׁוֹבַבְתִּיךָ.** אֶעֱשֶׂה אוֹתְךָ שׁוֹבָב, אַנְבוֹ״יִישִׁיר בְּלַעַ״ז. כָּל הַהוֹלֵךְ בִּשְׁרִירוּת לִבּוֹ נִקְרָא שׁוֹבָב, כְּמוֹ (ישעיהו נז:יז) ״וַיֵּלֶךְ שׁוֹבָב בְּדֶרֶךְ לִבּוֹ״:


**And I shall unbridle you** [Heb. וְשּׁוֹבַבְתִּיךָ.] I shall make you שׁוֹבָב a unbridled, backsliding, envoyzer in Old French, to unbridle. Whoever follows the dictates of his heart is called שּׁוֹבַב, as (Isa. 57: 17): “for he went rebelliously (שּׁוֹבֵב) in the way of his heart.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:4:2)


**וְנָתַתִּי חַחִים בִּלְחָיֶיךָ.** דֶּרֶךְ חַיָּה לְהוֹצִיאָהּ בְּחַחִים שֶׁבִּלְחָיֶיהָ, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינָהּ רוֹצָה לָצֵאת; גַּם אֲנִי אֶתֵּן בְּלִבְּךָ הִרְהוּרִין וְיֵצֶר, הַמּוֹשֵׁךְ אוֹתְךָ מֵאַרְצְךָ לָבֹא עַל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and I shall put hooks into your jaws** It is customary to lead out a beast with the hooks in its jaws because it does not want to go [voluntarily]. I, too, will put thoughts into your heart and an urge that will draw you from your land to come upon Israel.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:6:1)


**וְכָל אֲגַפֶּיהָ.** כָּל סְבִיבֶיהָ:


**and all its wings** And all those around it.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:7:1)


**הִכּוֹן וְהָכֵן לְךָ.** הִכּוֹן אוֹתְךָ, וְהָכֵן לְךָ חַיִל אֲחֵרִים:


**Be prepared and make ready for yourself** Prepare yourself, and make ready for yourself an army of others.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:7:2)


**הִכּוֹן.** הִזְדַּמֵּן:


**be prepared** [as translated,] be prepared.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:7:3)


**וְהָיִיתָ לָהֶם לְמִשְׁמָר.** דֶּרֶךְ הַמְּלָכִים לָתֵת לֵב עַל חֵילוֹתֵיהֶם הֵיאַךְ יֵצְאוּ, וּלְהַעֲמִיד מִשְׁמָרוֹת שֶׁלֹּא יָבֹא מַשְׁחִית עַל חֵילוֹ בַּלַּיְלָה:


**and you will be to them for a guardian** It is the custom of kings to pay attention, concerning their armies, to how they will march forth, and to appoint guards so that a destroyer should not come upon his army at night.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:8:1)


**מִיָּמִים רַבִּים תִּפָּקֵד.** מִיָּמִים רַבִּים שֶׁחָטָאתָ לִי, תִּפָּקֵד עַתָּה לְפָנַי, לִזְכּוֹר עֲוֹנוֹתֶיךָ וּלְהָשִׁיב גְּמוּלְךָ, וַאֲשִׂיאֲךָ **בְּאַחֲרִית הַשָּׁנִים** לָבוֹא **אֶל אֶרֶץ** יִשְׂרָאֵל הַ**מְּשׁוֹבֶבֶת מֵחֶרֶב**, שֶׁשָּׁבוּ יוֹשְׁבֶיהָ מִן הַגּוֹלָה, אֲשֶׁר גָּלוּ שָׁם עַל יְדֵי חֶרֶב הָאוֹיֵב:


**From many days past you will be remembered** From the many days past that you sinned against Me, you will now be remembered before Me, for [Me] to remember your iniquities and to pay you your recompense. And I shall entice you at the end of years into coming to the land of Israel, “returned from the sword” whose inhabitants returned from the exile wherein they had been exiled by the enemy’s sword.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:8:2)


**אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ לְחָרְבָּה תָּמִיד.** זֶה שָׁנִים רַבּוֹת; וְסָמוּךְ לְבוֹאֲךָ עֲלֵיהֶם, חָזְרוּ:


**which had been continually laid waste** these many years, and [only] shortly before your coming upon them, they returned.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:8:3)


**וְהִיא מֵעַמִּים הוּצָאָה.** וְהָיָה לְךָ לָשׂוּם עַל לֵב שֶׁ**הִיא מֵעַמִּים הוּצָאָה**, וְהַמּוֹצִיאָהּ מִן הָעַמִּים לֹא יַעַזְבֶנָּה בְּיָדְךָ:


**but it was liberated from the nations** But you should have taken into consideration that it liberated from the nations, and He Who liberated it from the nations will not leave it in your hands.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:9:1)


**כַּשּׁוֹאָה.** כְּחֹשֶׁךְ הַמְכַסֶּה אֶת הָאָרֶץ, **שׁוֹאָה** בְּרוּאִינְ״ה בְּלַעַ״ז:


**like mist** [Heb. כַּשּׁוֹאָה,] like darkness that covers the earth, שּׁוֹאָה is broine, bruine in Old French, fog, mist.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:10:1)


**יַעֲלוּ דְבָרִים.** ״יִסְקוּן הִרְהוּרִין״ (תרגום יונתן):


**words will enter** Thoughts will arise.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:10:2)


**מַחֲשֶׁבֶת רָעָה.** לְהִלָּחֵם כְּנֶגְדִּי:


**a thought of evil** to wage war against Me.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:11:1)


**עַל אֶרֶץ פְּרָזוֹת.** יוֹשְׁבֵי כְפָרִים מֵ**אֵין חוֹמָה**, שֶׁבּוֹטְחִים בְּמָעוּזָּם וְאֵינָם יְרֵאִים לְהִתְאַסֵּף אֶל עָרֵי מִבְצָר:


**upon a land of open cities** [Heb. פְּרָזוֹת,] dwellers in villages without a wall, who trust in their might and are not [so] afraid as to gather into fortified cities.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:11:2)


**פְּרָזוֹת.** כְּמוֹ ״מֵעָרֵי הַפְּרָזִי״ (דברים ג:ה), מֵ**אֵין חוֹמָה**:


פְּרָזוֹת is like (Deut. 3:5): “in addition to the very many open cities (עָרֵי הַפְרָזִי),” without a wall.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:11:3)


**אָבוֹא.** אֶל הַשּׁוֹקְטִים, וְאֶרְאֶה מָה הוּא מִבְטָחָם:


**I shall come** to the tranquil ones and see what they trust in.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:12:1)


**לְהָשִׁיב יָדְךָ עַל חֳרָבוֹת נוֹשָׁבוֹת.** לְהַשְׁחִית שֵׁנִית אֶת הֶעָרִים, שֶׁהָיוּ חֲרֵבוֹת עַד הֵנָּה, וְעַכְשָׁיו נִתְיַשְּׁבוּ:


**to return your hand upon the resettled ruins** To destroy a second time the cities that were in ruins until now, and now have been settled.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:12:2)


**וְאֶל עַם מְאֻסָּף.** מֵעַמִּים הָעוֹשִׂים עַתָּה מִקְנֶה וְקִנְיָן:


**and to a people gathered** from peoples, who now acquire livestock and possessions.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:12:3)


**עַל טַבּוּר הָאָרֶץ.** עַל גֹּבַהּ וְחֹזֶק הָאָרֶץ, כְּטַבּוּר זֶה, שֶׁהוּא אֶמְצָעִי שֶׁל אָדָם וּמְשֻׁפָּע מִכָּל צְדָדָיו:


**on the navel of the earth** In the height and the strength of the earth, like the navel, which is in the center of a person and slopes downward from all its sides.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:13:1)


**וְכָל כְּפִירֶיהָ.** סוֹחֲרִים חֲרִיפִין שֶׁבָּהּ, הָרְגִילִין לָצֵאת וְלָשׁוּט כִּכְפִירִים בְּכָל הַמְּדִינוֹת, וְיוֹדְעִין אֵיזֶה מָקוֹם הָעֹשֶׁר:


**and all its magnates** [Heb. כְּפִירֶיהָ,] shrewd merchants therein, who are accustomed to roam, like young lions (כְּפִירִים), in all the countries and who know where the place of riches is.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:13:2)


**יֹאמְרוּ לְךָ הֲלִשְׁלֹל שָׁלָל אַתָּה.** כְּלוֹמַר, מִי הִגִּיד לְךָ אֶת מְקוֹם הָעֹשֶׁר? הֲיָדַעְתָּ כִּי לְאֶרֶץ מְלֵאָה שָׁלָל וּבַז אַתָּה הוֹלֵךְ, וְשָׁם בֶּאֱמֶת תִּמְצָא הוֹן עָתֵק (משלי ח:יח):


**Are you coming to take spoil** i.e., who told you the place of the riches? Did you know [for a fact] that you are going to a land full of spoil and loot, and that there you will really find powerful wealth?



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:14:1)


**תֵּדָע.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״תֵּדַע פּוּרְעָנוּת גְּבוּרְתִּי״; כְּלוֹמַר: תֵּדַע וְתַכִּיר מִי הוּא מָעוּזָם וּמִבְטָחָם:


**you will know** Jonathan renders: you will know the retribution of My might, i.e., you will know and recognize Who is their might and their stronghold.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:17:1)


**הַאַתָּה הוּא.** בְּאוֹתָן הַיָּמִים יֹאמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא: **הַאַתָּה הוּא** אוֹתוֹ **אֲשֶׁר דִּבַּרְתִּי בְּיָמִים קַדְמוֹנִים בְּיַד עֲבָדַי נְבִיאֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל**, כְּגוֹן יְחֶזְקֵאל וּזְכַרְיָה, שֶׁאַף הוּא נִתְנַבֵּא עַל מִלְחֲמוֹת גּוֹג וּמָגוֹג: ״וְאָסַפְתִּי אֶת כָּל הַגּוֹיִם וְגוֹ׳״ (זכריה יד:ב). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (סנהדרין יז.): אֶלְדָּד וּמֵידָד הֵם נִתְנַבְּאוּ עָלָיו, וְזֶהוּ **שָׁנִים** הָאָמוּר כָּאן: אַל תִּיקְרֵי שָׁנִים אֶלָּא שְׁנַיִם (בפת״ח), שְׁנֵי נְבִיאִים נִתְנַבְּאוּ נְבוּאָה אַחַת בְּפֶרֶק אֶחָד (אֲבָל לֹא בְּסִגְנוֹן אֶחָד):


**Are you he** In those days, the Holy One, blessed be He, will say, “Are you the one about whom I spoke in ancient days through My servants, the prophets of Israel, e.g. Ezekiel and Zechariah?” For he [Zechariah] too prophesied about the wars of Gog and Magog (Zech. 14:2): “And I will gather all the nations, etc.” Our Sages said (Sanh. 17a): Eldad and Medad they [also] prophesied about it. That is the שָּׁנִים stated here. Do not read שָּׁנִים, years, but שְּׁנַיִם, two. Two prophets prophesied one prophecy at one time (though not in the same style of expression).



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:19:1)


**רַעַשׁ גָּדוֹל.** עַל יְדֵי קוֹלוֹת וּרְעָמִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר:


**a great noise** [Heb. רַעַשּׁ,] through noise and thunder, as he states.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:20:1)


**וְנָפְלוּ הַמַּדְרֵגוֹת.** אֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי שֶׁהֵם סְלָעִים הַזְּקוּפִים וּתְלוּיִים, וְנִרְאִים כְּנוֹפְלִים; וַאֲנִי אוֹמֵר: הֵם תִּלֵּי מִגְדָּלִים, שֶׁחוֹפְרִים סָבִיב וְזוֹרְקִים הֶעָפָר בָּאֶמְצַע לְהַגְבִּיהַּ הַתֵּל, וְעָשׂוּי מַדְרֵגוֹת מַדְרֵגוֹת – אֶשְׁקְלוֹנְ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז – כְּדֵי שֶׁיַּעֲמֹד הֶעָפָר, וּלְאַחַר שֶׁנִּכְבַּשׁ יָפֶה, נוֹטְלִים הַמַּלְבֵּנִים מַעֲמִידֵי הַמַּדְרֵגָה:


**and the cliffs shall fall** [Heb. הַמַּדְרִגוֹת.] I heard that they are perpendicular hanging rocks, which appear to be falling, but I say that they are mounds of the towers, for they dig around and cast up the earth in the center to raise the mound, and it is made into steps, eschelons, eschillons in Old French, steps, in order that the earth remain, and after it is pressed down well, they remove the forms that hold up the step.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:21:1)


**וְקָרָאתִי עָלָיו לְכָל הָרַי חֶרֶב.** לָבֹא חֶרֶב בָּהֶם עָלָיו, **וְקָרָאתִי** וְזִמַּנְתִּי. וּמַה הִיא הַחֶרֶב שֶׁאֶקְרָא עָלָיו? חַרְבָּם שֶׁל עַצְמָם, **חֶרֶב אִישׁ בְּאָחִיו**:


**And I will call the sword against him upon all My mountains** for the sword to come upon them, against him. “And I will call” means, I will prepare. And what is the sword that I will call against him? Their own sword: “every man’s sword shall be against his brother.”



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 38:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/38:22:1)


**וְאַבְנֵי אֶלְגָּבִישׁ.** אַבְנֵי בָּרָד הַמְּאִירוֹת כַּאֲבָנִים טוֹבוֹת שֶׁשְּׁמָם גָּבִישׁ, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״רָאמוֹת וְגָבִישׁ״ (איוב כח:יח); וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (ברכות נד:): אֶל גַּב אִישׁ – אַבְנֵי בָּרָד שֶׁהִתְחִילוּ לָרֶדֶת עַל מִצְרַיִם, וְעָמְדוּ בָּאֲוִיר עַל גַּב הָאִישׁ מֹשֶׁה, שֶׁהִתְפַּלֵּל שֶׁלֹּא יֵרְדוּ, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״לֹא נִתַּךְ אָרְצָה״ (שמות ט:לג) – לֹא הִגִּיעַ לָאָרֶץ:


**and great hailstones** Hailstones that glisten like precious stones named גָּבִישּׁ, crystal, as the matter is stated (Job 28:18): “Coral and crystal (וְגָּבִישּׁ).” Our Sages said (Ber. 54b): עַל גַב אִישּׁ: Hailstones that started to fall on Egypt, and stood in the air over the man (עַל גַב אִישּׁ), Moses, who prayed that they not fall, as the matter that is stated (Exod. 9:33), לֹא נִתַּךְ אַרְצָה “[it] did not reach the ground.”



## Chapter 39


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:2:1)


**וְשִׁשֵּׁאתִיךָ.** כְּמוֹ וְהִשֵּׁאתִיךָ עַל עַמִּי, כְּמוֹ ״לֹא יַשִּׁיא אוֹיֵב בּוֹ״ (תהלים פט:כג):


**and I will entice you** [Heb. וְשִּׁשֵּׁאתִיךָ,] like וְהִשֵּׁאתִיךָ, I will entice you upon My people, as in (Ps. 89: 23): “No enemy will entice (יַשִׂיא) him.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:7:1)


**וְלֹא אַחֵל אֶת שֵׁם קָדְשִׁי.** שִׁפְלוּתָם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל חִלּוּל שְׁמוֹ הוּא, בֶּאֱמוֹר לָהֶם ״עַם ה׳ אֵלֶּה״ (יחזקאל לו:כ) וְלֹא יָכוֹל לְהַצִּילָם:


**and I will no longer cause My Holy Name to be profaned** For Israel’s degradation is a profanation of His Name, “inasmuch as it is said of them, ‘these are the people of the Lord’” (above 36:20), and He is unable to save them.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:9:1)


**וְהִשִּׂיקוּ.** כְּמוֹ תַּנּוּר שֶׁהִשּׂיקוּהוּ בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה (שבת ג:):


**and heat up** [Heb. וְהִשִּׂיקוּ,] like (Orlah 3:5): “An oven that they heated up (שֶּׁהִטִיקוּהָ)” in the language of the Mishnah.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:9:2)


**בַּנֶּשֶׁק.** אַנָא״ר מוּרָא״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**with the weapons** [Heb. בַנֶשֶּׁק,] an armures in Old French, with weapons.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:11:1)


**אֶתֵּן לְגוֹג מְקוֹם שָׁם קֶבֶר.** מָקוֹם שֶׁיְּהֵא שָׁם קֶבֶר לָהֶם; לְפִי שֶׁהוּא מִזַּרְעוֹ שֶׁל יֶפֶת, שֶׁכִּסָּה אֶת עֶרְוַת אָבִיו, לְפִיכָךְ זָכָה לִקְבוּרָה (בראשית רבה לו:ו):


**I will give Gog a place there as a grave** A place where there will be a grave for them. Since he is of the seed of Japtheth, who covered his father’s nakedness, he therefore merited burial.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:11:2)


**גֵּי הָעֹבְרִים.** גֵּי שֶׁהֵם עוֹבְרִים אֶת יָם כִּנֶּרֶת לְהָבִיא מִשָּׁם פֵּירוֹת גִּינוֹסַר תָּמִיד:


**the valley of those who pass along** The valley where people pass the Sea of Kinnereth to bring from there Genossar fruits regularly.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:11:3)


**קִדְמַת הַיָּם.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״בְּמִדְנַח יָם גִּינוֹסַר״:


**the east side of the sea** Jonathan renders: on the east of the Sea of Gennasar.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:11:4)


**וְחֹסֶמֶת הִיא אֶת הָעֹבְרִים.** וְסוֹגֶרֶת הִיא אֶת הָעוֹבְרִים, כְּמוֹ ״לֹא תַחְסֹם״ (דברים כה:ד), שֶׁרֹב פְּגָרִים שֶׁיִּפְּלוּ שָׁם יַחְדְּלוּ אֶת הָעוֹבְרִים מֵעֲבֹר, לְפִיכָךְ יַעַבְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם וְיִקְבְּרוּ אוֹתָם; וּמְנַחֵם פֵּתְרוֹ בְּסִפְרוֹ: יַחְסְמוּ אֶת אַפָּם מִלְּהָרִיחַ רֵיחַ הַפְּגָרִים:


**and it will then stop those who pass along** [Heb. וְחֹסֶמֶת.] It closes off those who pass, like (Deut. 25: 4): “You shall not muzzle (לֹא תַחְסוֹם).” Because of the many corpses that will fall there, travelers will be prevented from passing through. Therefore, they will pass by them and bury them. Menachem (p. 91) interprets it in his book (Machbereth) to mean that they will close their nose so as not to smell the odor of the corpses.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:13:1)


**וְקָבְרוּ כָּל עַם הָאָרֶץ.** שֶׁמֵּתוּ שָׁם:


**They will bury all the people of the land** because they died there.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:13:2)


**וְהָיָה לָהֶם לְשֵׁם.** כָּל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת יְסַפְּרוּ אֶת שְׁמָם לְשֶׁבַח וּלְחֶסֶד: אֵין לְךָ כְּאוּמָּה זוֹ רַחֲמָנִים; יֵשׁ לְךָ אָדָם שֶׁקּוֹבֵר אֶת אוֹיְבוֹ שֶׁעָמַד עָלָיו לְהָרְגוֹ?


**and they will be renowned** All the nations will speak their name as praise and as a sign of kindness: “There are no nations as compassionate as this one; do you find a man who buries his enemy who rose up against him to kill him?”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:14:1)


**וְאַנְשֵׁי תָמִיד.** אֲנָשִׁים הַמּוּתְמָדִים לְכָךְ, **יַבְדִּילוּ** יִשְׂרָאֵל, כְּדֵי לַעֲבֹר **בָּאָרֶץ** לְלַקֵּט אֶת הַמְּפֻזָּרִים:


**Men of continuous employment** Men designed to continuously devote themselves to this. Israel will separate out in order to [be able to] pass through the land and gather the scattered [corpses].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:14:2)


**מְקַבְּרִים אֶת הָעוֹבְרִים.** מְקַבְּרִים עִם הָעוֹבְרִים אֶת הַפְּגָרִים הַחוֹסְמִין וּמְעַכְּבִין אֶת הַמַּעֲבָר, שֶׁהָעוֹבְרִין אֶת הַיָּם מְקַבְּרִים אוֹתָם לַהֲנָאָתָם. **אֶת הָעוֹבְרִים** עַם הָעוֹבְרִים; כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**burying with those who pass through** Burying with (Heb. אֶת) those who pass through; the corpses (אֶת הַנוֹתָרִים) that will block off and hinder the crossing. Those who crossed the ocean will bury them for their own benefit. **with those who pass through** Heb. אֶת הַעֹבְרִים, with those who pass through. So I heard, and so did Jonathan render.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:14:3)


**אֶת הַנּוֹתָרִים עַל פְּנֵי הָאָרֶץ.** רָחוֹק מִן הַמַּעֲבָר, שֶׁאֵין הָעוֹבְרִים אֶת הַיָּם מִתְעַסְּקִים בָּהֶם לְקָבְרָם, יִקְבְּרוּם אֵלֶּה הַמּוּתְמָדִים וּמוּבְדָּלִים לְכָךְ:


**those who are left on the surface of the land** far from the crossing. Those who cross the sea will not occupy themselves with burying them. Those designated to devote themselves to this purpose will bury them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:14:4)


**מִקְּצֵה שִׁבְעָה חֳדָשִׁים.** יִכְלוּ מַרְבִּית הַפְּגָרִים הַנִּרְאִים לְעֵינָם לְהִקָּבֵר, וּמֵאָז וָהָלְאָה **יַחְקֹרוּ** בָּאָרֶץ בִּמְקוֹם הַמִּסְתָּרִים, בַּחוֹחִים וּבֵין הַקּוֹצִים, וְיִקְבְּרוּ מַה שֶּׁיִּמְצָאוּ:


**at the end of seven months** the majority of the corpses visible to the eye will have been buried; from then on they will probe through the land in the hidden places, in the briars and among the thorns, and bury whomever find.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:15:1)


**וְעָבְרוּ הָעֹבְרִים בָּאָרֶץ.** הוֹלְכֵי דְרָכִים שֶׁיִּרְאוּ עֶצֶם אָדָם:


**And when they that pass through the land shall pass** Wayfarers who see a human bone


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:15:2)


**וּבָנָה אֶצְלוֹ צִיּוּן.** סִימָן שֶׁיֵּשׁ כָּאן עֶצֶם שֶׁל מֵת, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּפְרְשׁוּ מִמֶּנּוּ הוֹלְכֵי דְרָכִים וְעוֹשֵׂי טָהֳרוֹת, **עַד** בֹּא אַנְשֵׁי הַתָּמִיד הַמֻּבְדָּלִים לַחְקֹר **וְקָבְרוּ אוֹתָן**:


will construct a marker next to it as a sign that there is a bone of a dead body there, in order that wayfarers and those who prepare ritually pure food should stay away from it. **until** the men of continuous employment, designated to search, will come and bury it.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:16:1)


**וְגַם שֵׁם עִיר הֲמוֹנָה.** וְגַם הָעִיר שֶׁשְּׁמָהּ עִיר הוֹמִיָּה מֵרוֹב אָדָם, הִיא עִיר הַגְּדוֹלָה, גַּם הִיא – חֵיל יוֹשְׁבֶיהָ שָׁם יִקָּבְרוּ:


**And also the name of the city shall be Hamonah** And also the city whose name is “the city stirring with numerous people,” that is, the wicked city, the masses of its inhabitants will be buried there.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:16:2)


**וְטִהֲרוּ.** אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל בָּאָרֶץ אֶת טָהֳרוֹתֵיהֶם וּתְרוּמוֹתֵיהֶם:


**Thus they shall purify the land** [i.e.,] Israel will purify their ritually pure foods and their terumoth in the Land.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:16:3)


**הֲמוֹנָה.** לֹא מַפִּיק הֵ״א, לְפִי שֶׁהֵ״א אַחֲרוֹנָה מְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת שֵׁם דָּבָר; **הֲמוֹנָה** כְּמוֹ ״הוֹמִיָּה״ (ישעיה כב:ב):


**Hamonah** without a “mappiq hey” since the final “hey” serves as an adjective, for הֲמוֹנָה is like הוֹמִיָה, stirring.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:18:1)


**אֵילִים כָּרִים וְעַתּוּדִים פָּרִים.** מְלָכִים וְדוּכְּסִים שַׁלְטוֹנִין וְשָׂרִים:


**rams, lambs, he-goats, and bulls** kings and dukes, rulers, and princes.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:18:2)


**מְרִיאֵי בָשָׁן.** שׁוֹר שֶׁל פִּטָּם קָרוּי אַלמְרִ״י בִּלְשׁוֹן עֲרָבִי:


**fatlings of Bashan** [Heb. מְרִיאִי בָשָּׁן,] An ox given to be fattened is called al meri in Arabic.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:21:1)


**אֶת מִשְׁפָּטִי.** אֶת פּוּרְעָנוּתִי; יוּשְׁטִיצָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**My judgment** [Heb. מִשְׁפָּטִי,] My retribution, justize in Old French, judgment, punishment.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:23:1)


**וְיָדְעוּ הַגּוֹיִם כִּי בַעֲוֺנָם גָּלוּ בֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל.** כְּשֶׁיִּרְאוּ אֶת גְּבוּרָתִי, יַכִּירוּ כִּי לֹא מִקֹּצֶר יָדִי הָיָה שֶׁלֹּא הוֹשַׁעְתִּים, אֶלָּא **בַּעֲוֺנָם** הִסְתַּרְתִּי **פָּנַי מֵהֶם** וְגָלוּ:


**And the nations will know that for their iniquity they were exiled** [i.e.,] the Children of Israel. When they see My might, they will recognize that it was not because of inability that I did not save them but because of their iniquity that I hid My face from them, and they were exiled.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:25:1)


**וְקִנֵּאתִי לְשֵׁם קָדְשִׁי.** וְלָבַשְׁתִּי קִנְאַת תְּשׁוּעָה וְנָקָם, לְמַעַן שֵׁם קָדְשִׁי, שֶׁלֹּא יִתְחַלֵּל עוֹד:


**and I shall be zealous for My Holy Name** And I donned the zeal of salvation and vengeance for the sake of My Holy Name, that it no longer be profaned.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:25:2)


**וְקִנֵּאתִי לְשֵׁם קָדְשִׁי.** בִּשְׁבִיל שֵׁם קָדְשִׁי; כְּמוֹ ״הַמְקַנֵּא אַתָּה לִי״ (במדבר יא:כט):


**and I shall be zealous for My Holy Name** [Heb. לְשֵּׁם,] for the sake of My Holy Name, as in (Num. 11:29): “Are you jealous for my sake (הַמְקַנֵא אַתָּה לִי).”



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:26:1)


**וְנָשׂוּ אֶת כְּלִמָּתָם.** בְּהֵיטִיבִי לָהֶם וְלֹא אֶגְמְלֵם כְּרָעָתָם, אָז יִשְׂאוּ כְּלִמָּתָם וְיֵבוֹשׁוּ מִלְּהָרִים פָּנִים. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּירֵשׁ וְנָשׂוּ כְּמוֹ ״אַשְׁרֵי נְשׂוּי פֶּשַׁע״ (תהלים לב:א), לְשׁוֹן כַּפָּרָה:


**And they shall bear their disgrace** When I do good to them and I do not recompense them according to their evil, they will bear the disgrace and be ashamed to lift up their faces. Menahem, however, explained וְנָשּׂוּי like (Ps. 32:1): “Praiseworthy is he whose transgression is forgiven (נְשּׂוּי),” an expression of atonement.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 39:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/39:28:1)


**וְלֹא אוֹתִיר.** אֶחָד מֵהֶם בַּגּוֹלָה:


**And I shall no longer leave** one of them in exile.



## Chapter 40


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:1:1)


**בְּרֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה בֶּעָשׂוֹר לַחֹדֶשׁ.** אֵיזוֹהִי שָׁנָה שֶׁרֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁלָּהּ **בֶּעָשׂוֹר לַחֹדֶשׁ**? הֱוֵי אוֹמֵר: זֶה יוֹבֵל (ערכין יב.), שֶׁחָרַב הַבַּיִת בִּשְׁנַת שְׁלֹשִׁים וָשֵׁשׁ בַּיּוֹבֵל – שֶׁהִתְחִיל בִּשְׁמוֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה לְיֹאשִׁיָּהוּ – וּלְסוֹף אַרְבַּע עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה הָיָה רָאוּי הַיּוֹבֵל לִהְיוֹת, וְהִיא שְׁנַת **עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ** לְגָלוּת יְכָנְיָה (מלכים ב כד:טו); שֶׁגָּלָה בְּעֶשְׂרִים וָשֵׁשׁ בַּיּוֹבֵל: אַרְבַּע עֶשְׂרֵה שֶׁל יֹאשִׁיָּהוּ – מִשְּׁמוֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה עַד שְׁלֹשִׁים וְאַחַת (מלכים ב כג:א,ג), וִיהוֹיָקִים – אַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה (מלכים ב כג:לו):


**on Rosh Hashanah, on the tenth of the month** What “year” is it whose Rosh Hashanah falls on the tenth of the month? The answer is that this is the Jubilee year. The Temple had been destroyed in [3338] the thirty-sixth year of the [50-year] Jubilee [cycle] which had commenced in the eighteenth year of Josiah [3303]. Fourteen years later [3352], the [beginning of the next] Jubilee would have fallen, and this was the twenty-fifth year of the exile of Jeconiah, who had been exiled in [3327], the twenty-sixth year of the Jubilee [cycle] [consisting of] fourteen years of Josiah, from the eighteenth to the thirty-first year [3302-3316], and eleven years of Jehoiakim [3316-3327].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:1:2
