[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:1:2)


**יַד ה׳.** חֹזֶק תָּקְפּוֹ, לְהוֹלִיכֵנִי עַל כָּרְחִי:


**the hand of the Lord** The strength of His might, to lead me against my will.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:1:3)


**וַיָּבֵא אוֹתִי שָׁמָּה.** אֶל הָעִיר הַהֻכְּתָה, הִיא יְרוּשָׁלַיִם; אוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה הֶרְאָהוּ בִּנְיַן הַבַּיִת לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא:


**and He brought me there** to the city that was smitten - that is Jerusalem. That year He showed him the edifice of the future Temple.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:2:1)


**בְּמַרְאוֹת אֱלֹהִים הֱבִיאַנִי.** לֹא הוֹלִיכַנִי שָׁם, אֶלָּא הֶרְאַנִי כְּאִלּוּ אֲנִי שָׁם:


**In the visions of God He brought me** He did not take me there but made it appear to me as if I were there.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:2:2)


**אֶל הַר גָּבוֹהַּ מְאֹד.** שֶׁכֵּן הוּא עָתִיד לִהְיוֹת גָּבוֹהַּ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְנִשָּׂא מִגְּבָעוֹת״ (ישעיה ב:ב):


**on a very lofty mountain** For it is indeed destined to be lofty, as it is said (Isa. 2:2): “and it shall be raised above the hills.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:2:3)


**כְּמִבְנֵה עִיר.** כְּבִנְיַן עִיר:


**like the building of a city** [Heb. כְּמִבְנֵה עִיר.]


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:2:4)


**מִנֶּגֶב.** בִּדְרוֹמוֹ שֶׁל הַר:


**from the south** at the south of the mountain.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:3:1)


**כְּמַרְאֵה נְחֹשֶׁת.** כְּעֵין זִיו הַחַיּוֹת, וְנוֹצְצִים ״כְּעֵין נְחֹשֶׁת קָלָל״ (יחזקאל א:ז):


**like...copper** Like the shine of the Living Beings (above 1:7): “and they sparkled like the color of burnished copper.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:3:2)


**וּפְתִיל פִּשְׁתִּים בְּיָדוֹ.** לִמְדִידַת קַרְקַע אֵין (דָּבָר) יָפֶה מֵחֶבֶל פִּשְׁתִּים:


**with a linen cord in his hand** For measuring land, there is nothing better than linen cord.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:3:3)


**וּקְנֵה הַמִּדָּה.** לָמוֹד בּוֹ אֶת עֹבִי הַחוֹמָה וְאֹרֶךְ וְרֹחַב הַשְּׁעָרִים:


**and a measuring rod** with which to measure the thickness of the wall and the length and width of the gates.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:4:1)


**לְכֹל אֲשֶׁר אֲנִי מַרְאֶה אוֹתְךָ.** עִנְיַן הַבִּנְיָן:


**to all that I am showing you** the matter of the building.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:4:2)


**הוּבָאתָה הֵנָּה.** מִבָּבֶל:


**you have been brought here** from Babylon.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:5:1)


**וְהִנֵּה חוֹמָה מִחוּץ לַבַּיִת.** בִּנְיַן בַּיִת אֶחָד הֶרְאַנִי בִּצְפוֹן הָהָר וְהַחוֹמָה **מִחוּץ סָבִיב סָבִיב**; כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר (יחזקאל מה; מח), שֶׁהָעִיר בַּדָּרוֹם וְהַבַּיִת בַּצָּפוֹן:


**And behold a wall outside the House** He showed me the structure of one building on the north side of the mountain and a wall on the outside going all around. It is delineated as such at the end of the Book that the city is in the south and the House in the north.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:5:2)


**שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת בָּאַמָּה וָטֹפַח.** ״שִׁית אַמִּין בְּאַמְתָּא דְהִיא אַמְתָּא וּפְשַׁךְ״, כֵּן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן; זוֹ הִיא אַמָּה בֵינוֹנִית, בַּת שִׁשָּׁה (מנחות צח.), שֶׁהוּא אַמָּה וְטֹפַח בָּאַמָּה בַּת חֲמִשָּׁה:


**six cubits by a cubit and a handbreadth** “Six cubits of a cubit that is a cubit and a handbreadth.” So did Jonathan render it. This is a cubit of medium length which equals a cubit and a handbreadth using a cubit that equals five handbreadths.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:5:3)


**רֹחַב הַבִּנְיָן.** עֳבִי הַחוֹמָה:


**the width of the structure** the thickness of the wall.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:5:4)


**וְקוֹמָה קָנֶה אֶחָד.** וְזֶהוּ הִיא קוֹמָה חִיצוֹנָה, שֶׁהִיא סָבִיב הַר הַבַּיִת, וְהִיא הָיְתָה נְמוּכָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ (מדות ב:ד): כָּל הַכְּתָלִים שֶׁהָיוּ שָׁם הָיוּ גְבוֹהִים, חוּץ מִכּוֹתֶל מִזְרָחִי, שֶׁהַכֹּהֵן הַשּׂוֹרֵף אֶת הַפָּרָה עוֹמֵד בְּהַר הַמִּשְׁחָה וְכוּ׳:


**and the height one rod** And this was the outside wall, which surrounds the Temple Mount, and it was low, as we learned (in Middoth 2:4): All the walls that were there were high except for the eastern wall, for the priest who would burn the [Red] Cow would stand on the Mount of Olives [and would look directly at the entrance to the Temple proper at the time of the sprinkling of the blood].



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:6:1)


**וַיָּבֹא אֶל שַׁעַר אֲשֶׁר פָּנָיו דֶּרֶךְ הַקָּדִימָה.** נִכְנַס לְתוֹךְ הַהֶיקֵּף, וּבָא לוֹ לְשַׁעַר עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים הַמִּזְרָחִי; הִיא נִקְרֵאת חָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה בְּכָל הָעִנְיָן הַזֶּה (להלן יז), לְפִי שֶׁהוּא לִפְנֵי עֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And he came to the gate that faced eastward** he entered the enclosure and came to the eastern gate of the Women’s Court, which is called “the Outside Court” throughout this entire account, since it is in front of the Israelites’ Court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:6:2)


**וַיַּעַל בְּמַעֲלוֹתָו.** שֶׁשִּׁפּוּעַ הָהָר הוֹלֵךְ וְעוֹלֶה מִן הַחֵיל הַתַּחְתּוֹן עַד עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה מַעֲלוֹת, כְּאוֹתָהּ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ (מדות ב:ג): לִפְנִים מִמֶּנּוּ הַחֵיל – עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת; וּשְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה מַעֲלוֹת הָיוּ שָׁם:


**and he ascended its steps** For the incline of the mountain ascends gradually from the lower “hel” (an expanse and steps) up to the Women’s Court, twelve steps, in accordance with the Mishnah that we learned (Middoth 2:3): On its inner side, [i.e., on the inner side of the fence] was the “hel” of ten cubits’ [width], and twelve steps were there, etc.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:6:3)


**סַף הַשַּׁעַר.** הִיא מְזוּזַת הַשַּׁעַר:


**the post of the gate** This is the doorpost of the gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:6:4)


**קָנֶה אֶחָד רֹחַב.** שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת, וּמְכַסֶּה אֶת כָּל עֳבִי הַחוֹמָה, שֶׁהָיָה קָנֶה אֶחָד, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה (פס׳ ה):


**one rod the width** six cubits. It overlay the entire thickness of the wall, which was one rod, as stated above (verse 5).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:6:5)


**וְאֵת סַף אֶחָד.** מְזוּזָה הַשֵּׁנִית; אַחַת מִצָּפוֹן וְאַחַת מִדָּרוֹם:


**and the other post** The second sidepost; one was in the north and one was in the south.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:1)


**וְהַתָּא.** הוּא ״יָצִיעַ״ (מלכים א ו:ה) שֶׁקּוֹרִין אפנדי״ן בְּלַעַ״ז, וְהָיָה שְׁלֹשָׁה מִיָּמִין הַשַּׁעַר וּשְׁלֹשָׁה מִן הַשְּׂמֹאל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בָּעִנְיָן (להלן י). וְהַתָּאִים סְמוּכִין לְכוֹתֶל הַמִּזְרָחִי שֶׁל עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים מִבַּחוּץ, כְּלַפֵּי הַר הַבַּיִת. וּמֵהֵיכָן אֲנִי לָמֵד שֶׁהֵם מִבַּחוּץ? מִמַּה שֶׁכָּתוּב אַחַר כָּל הָעִנְיָן **וַיְבִיאֵנִי אֶל הֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה**, מִכָּאן אֲנִי לָמֵד, שֶׁכָּל הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה הָיָה חוּצָה לָהּ, שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא נִכְנַס לְתוֹכוֹ:


**And the cell** This was a projecting gallery, called appentis in French (see I Kings 6:5). There were three on the right of the gate and three on the left, as is stated in this section. The cells were near the eastern wall of the Women’s Court, on the outside, facing the Temple Mount. Now, from where do I learn that they were on the outside? From what is written after this entire account (verse 17): “And he brought me to the outer Court.” From here I learn that all that had been spoken of previously was outside it, for he had not yet entered it.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:2)


**וְהַתָּא קָנֶה אֶחָד אֹרֶךְ וְגוֹ׳.** חֲלָלוֹ שֶׁל כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת מְרֻבָּע:


**And the cell was one rod in length, etc.** The inner area of each one was six cubits square.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:3)


**וּבֵין הַתָּאִים חָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת.** כְּתָלִים הַמַּפְסִיקִים בֵּין הַתָּאִים עָבְיָם חָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וּבֵין תָּוְויָא כּוֹתְלָא חָמֵשׁ אַמִּין״; וְכֵן שָׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (ד, ז): כּוֹתֶל הַתָּא חָמֵשׁ:


**and between the cells five cubits** The walls separating the cells were five cubits thick, and so did Jonathan render: and between the cells was a wall of five cubits, and so we learned in Tractate Middoth (4:7): The wall of the cell was five [cubits.]


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:4)


**וְסַף הַשַּׁעַר מֵאֵצֶל אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר וגו׳.** שֶׁלִּפְנִים מִן הַשַּׁעַר הָיָה עָשׂוּי אוּלָם מְחֻבָּר לַשַּׁעַר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן (להלן ח), בּוֹלֵט לְתוֹךְ עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת, וּבְרֹאשׁוֹ דְּלָתוֹת וּמְזוּזוֹת, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר: **וְסַף הַשַּׁעַר** שֶׁהוּא **אֵצֶל אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר**:


**and the post of the gate that was beside the hall of the gate, etc.** [i.e.,] [extending] beyond the gate was a hall connected to the gate, as is delineated in this account, protruding ten cubits into the Women’s Court, and at its front end were doors and posts, and this is what he meant by saying: “and the post of the gate” which was “next to the hall of the gate.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:5)


**מֵהַבַּיִת.** כְּלוֹמַר: לְצַד פְּנִים:


**from inside** [Heb. מֵהַבָּיִת i.e.,] toward the inside.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:7:6)


**קָנֶה אֶחָד.** רָחְבּוֹ, לְכַסּוֹת עֳבִי חוֹמוֹת הָאוּלָם, אֶחָד מִיָּמִין וְאֶחָד מִשְּׂמֹאל, שֶׁעֳבִי הַחוֹמָה שֶׁל אוּלָם לְיָמִין וְלִשְׂמֹאל **קָנֶה אֶחָד**, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפָרֵשׁ וְהוֹלֵךְ:


**The width of one rod** covered the thickness of the wall of the hall, one on the right, [wall] and one on the left, for the thickness of [each] wall of the hall - to the [hallway’s] right and to [its] left - was one rod, as he explains further.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:8:1)


**וַיָּמָד אֶת אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר מֵהַבַּיִת.** לִפְנִים מִן הַשַּׁעַר:


**And he measured the hall of the gate from inside** inward of the gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:8:2)


**קָנֶה אֶחָד.** עֳבִי חוֹמוֹת הַיָּמִין וְהַשְּׂמֹאל, שֶׁהָאוּלָם נָכוֹן עֲלֵיהֶם:


**one rod** The thickness of the right and left walls upon which the hall was founded.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:9:1)


**וַיָּמָד אֶת אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר שְׁמֹנֶה אַמּוֹת.** מֶשֶׁךְ בְּלִיטָתוֹ לְתוֹךְ עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים:


**And he measured the hall of the gate eight cubits** the length of its protrusion into the Women’s Court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:9:2)


**וְאֵילָיו.** אֲשִׁישְׁלַטִינְ״ץ (בְּלַעַ״ז); כָּל אֵילִים הָאֲמוּרִים בָּעִנְיָן הֵם כְּמִין אִילָנוֹת עֲגוּלִין עֲשׂוּיִין מֵאַבְנֵי גָזִית, וְעוֹמְדִין בַּפֶּתַח, אֶחָד מִיָּמִין וְאֶחָד מִשְּׂמֹאל, בִּמְקוֹם סִפִּים בִּמְקוֹם מְזוּזוֹת; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן (ישעיהו ו:ד) ״וַיָּנֻעוּ אַמּוֹת הַסִּפִּים״ ״אֵילָנוֹת סְפִיָּא״; וּקְרוּיִין אֵילִים עַל שֵׁם עֲגוּלִין כָּאֵלָה וְכָאַלּוֹן:


**and its pillars** Meselins in Old French, posts, pillars. All the pillars mentioned in this account were sort of round trees made of hewn stone, standing in the entrance, one to the right and one to the left, in place of posts, in place of doorposts, and so did Jonathan render (Isa. 1:4): “And the doorposts (אַמוֹת הַסִפִּים) quaked”: אֵילְמַת סִפּיָא. And they are called אֵילִים because they were round like the terebinth and like the oak (וְכָאַלוֹן כָאֵלָה).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:9:3)


**וְאֵילָיו שְׁתַּיִם אַמּוֹת.** עֹבִי עִגּוּלָן; וְעוֹמְדִין בְּסוֹף חֲלַל הָאוּלָם דְּבוּקִים, זֶה לְכוֹתֶל יָמִין וְזֶה לְכוֹתֶל שְׂמֹאל; נִמְצָא בְּלִיטַת הָאוּלָם וְאֵילִים שֶׁלּוֹ עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת לְתוֹךְ הָעֲזָרָה לִפְנִים מִן הַשַּׁעַר:


**and its pillars two cubits** in diameter. They were standing at the end of the hall space, this one attached to the right wall and this one to the left wall. The result is that the [total] protrusion of the hall and its pillars was ten cubits beyond the gate, into the Court interior.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:9:4)


**וְאוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר מֵהַבָּיִת.** לְפִי שֶׁאוּלַמֵּי שַׁעֲרֵי עֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל הָיוּ בּוֹלְטִים וּמְשׁוּכִים לְצַד הַחוּץ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן (להלן לא), פֵּירֵשׁ בְּאוּלַמֵּי שַׁעֲרֵי עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים שֶׁהֵם הָיוּ מְשׁוּכִין לְצַד פְּנִים, וְשַׁעֲרֵי עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים מְכֻוָּנִים כְּנֶגֶד שַׁעֲרֵי עֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל; נִמְצָא אוּלָם כְּנֶגֶד אוּלָם, וּדְבָר נוֹי הוּא זֶה:


**and the hall of the gate from inside** Since the hall of the Israelites’ Court Gate protruded and extended outward, as is delineated in this account, he explains concerning the hall of the Women’s Court Gate that it [in contrast] extended inward. The Women’s Court Gates were built in a straight line with the gates of the Israelites’ Court, resulting in one hall facing another hall, which is a beautiful thing.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:10:1)


**וְתָאֵי הַשַּׁעַר.** הַזֶּה אֲשֶׁר **דֶּרֶךְ הַקָּדִים** שֶׁבְּכֹתֶל מִזְרָחִי שֶׁל עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים, **שְׁלֹשָׁה** הָיוּ **מִפֹּה וּשְׁלֹשָׁה** הָיוּ **מִפֹּה**, **שְׁלֹשָׁה** לְדָרוֹם **וּשְׁלֹשָׁה** לְצָפוֹן, סְמוּכִין לְכֹתֶל עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים, וּפְנֵיהֶם לְהַר הַבַּיִת. וּמִדַּת חַלְלוֹ וְכֹתֶל הַמַּפְסִיק בֵּין כָּל אֶחָד פֵּרֵשׁ לְמַעְלָה (פס׳ ז):


**And the cells of the gate** [i.e., of] this [gate] that faced eastward - which was in the eastern wall of the Women’s Court - three were from here and three were from here: three to the south and three to the north, next to the wall of the Women’s Court, and facing the Temple Mount. The measurements of each cell’s interior and the wall that intervened between one and another were explained above.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:10:2)


**וּמִדָּה אַחַת לָאֵילִים.** הָעֲשׂוּיִין לְסִפֵּי הָאוּלָם מִבִּפְנִים:


**and one measure for the pillars** made for the hall’s posts on the inside.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:10:3)


**מִפֹּה.** וּמִפֹּה מִיָּמִין וּמִשְּׂמֹאל, מִדָּתָן שְׁתַּיִם אַמּוֹת:


**from here and from here** of the right and of the left; their measurement was two cubits.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:11:1)


**רֹחַב פֶּתַח הַשַּׁעַר.** רֹחַב חֲלַל הַפֶּתַח; וְכֵן שָׁנִינוּ (מדות ב, ג): כָּל הַפְּתָחִים רָחְבָּן עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת:


**the width of the entrance of the gate** The width of the span of the entrance, and so we learned (Mid. 2:3): All the entrances...were ten cubits wide.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:11:2)


**אֹרֶךְ הַשַּׁעַר.** הוּא חֲלַל אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר מִן הַצָּפוֹן לַדָּרוֹם:


**the length of the gate** This is the span of the hall of the gate from north to south.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:11:3)


**שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמּוֹת.** עֶשֶׂר כְּנֶגֶד חֲלַל הַפֶּתַח, וְאַמָּה וָחֵצִי לְכָאן וְאַמָּה וָחֵצִי לְכָאן. וְאַל תִּתְמַהּ שֶׁאֵצֶל הַפֶּתַח קוֹרֵהוּ רֹחַב, וְאֵצֶל חֲלַל הָאוּלָם קוֹרֵהוּ אֹרֶךְ, שֶׁהַפֶּתַח גָּבְהוֹ הוּא אָרְכּוֹ וְהָרֹחַב מִסַּף אֶל סַף, וְהָאוּלָם לְפִי שֶׁמֶּשֶׁךְ בְּלִיטַת חֲלָלוֹ מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב פָּחוֹת מִמִּדַּת חֲלָלוֹ מִן הַצָּפוֹן לַדָּרוֹם שֶׁזּוֹ שְׁמוֹנֶה אַמּוֹת וְזוֹ שְׁלוֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה, קוֹרֵא אֶת הַמִּדָּה הַיְתֵרָה אֹרֶךְ,.וַהֲרֵי עֵדוּת לַדָּבָר בְּמַשְׁכְּנָא ״וְהָאוּלָם עַל פְּנֵי הֵיכַל הַבַּיִת עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה אָרְכּוֹ עַל פְּנֵי רֹחַב הַבַּיִת״ (מלכים א ו:ג), נִמְצָא מִן הַצָּפוֹן לַדָּרוֹם קָרוּי לְהֵיכָל רֹחַב וְלָאוּלָם קָרוּי אֹרֶךְ, לְפִי שֶׁהַהֵיכָל מִדָּתוֹ מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב יְתֵרָה עַל מִצָּפוֹן לַדָּרוֹם, וּבָאוּלָם חִלּוּף לְמִדַּת שֶׁהַמִּדָּה הַיְתֵרָה קָרוּי אֹרֶךְ:


**thirteen cubits** ten corresponding to the span of the entrance plus a cubit and a half on this side and a cubit and a half on the other side. Now, do not wonder why in respect to the entrance he calls it width and in respect to the span of the hall he calls it length, because in respect to the entrance, its height and its length and the width is from post to post, but the hall, since the extension of the protrusion of its east to west span is less than the measure of its north to south span, as this is eight cubits and that is thirteen, he calls the larger measure “length.” And here is evidence for the matter: in the Temple of [the Book of] Kings (I 6:3): “And the hall before the Temple of the House, twenty cubits [was] the length thereof, beyond the breadth of the House.” We thus find that north-to-south is called breadth as regards the Temple, yet, as regards the hall, it is called length, because the east-to-west measurement of the Temple exceeded [its measurement] from north to south, and with the hall, it was the opposite. [Hence,] you learn that the large measurement is [always] called length.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:12:1)


**וּגְבוּל לִפְנֵי הַתָּאוֹת אַמָּה אֶחַת וְגוֹ׳. הַתָּאוֹת**, שֶׁמִּכָּאן וּמִכָּאן לַשַּׁעַר הָיוּ מְשׁוּכִין מִן כְּנֶגֶד חֲלַל הָאוּלָם שֶׁלִּפְנִים אַמָּה לַצָּפוֹן וְאַמָּה לַדָּרוֹם, שֶׁכְּבָר פֵּרֵשׁ שֶׁהוּא מִבַּיִת מָשׁוּךְ מִכְּנֶגֶד חֲלַל הַשַּׁעַר אַמָּה וּמֶחֱצָה לַצָּפוֹן וְכֵן לַדָּרוֹם. וְעַכְשָׁיו מְפָרֵשׁ שֶׁהַתָּאִים שֶׁבַּחוּץ מְשׁוּכִים לַצָּפוֹן אַמָּה אַחַת יוֹתֵר מִן הָאוּלָם, וְכֵן לַדָּרוֹם אַמָּה אַחַת. נִמְצְאוּ מְשׁוּכִין מֵחֲלַל רֹחַב הַפֶּתַח שְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת וּמֶחֱצָה לְכָאן וּשְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת וּמֶחֱצָה לְכָאן. וְזֶהוּ לְשׁוֹן **וּגְבוּל**, אשומיי״ל בְּלַעַ״ז, מָקוֹם פָּנוּי. וְכוֹתֶל הַתָּא עוֹבִי חָמֵשׁ, נִמְצָא עוֹבִי כּוֹתֶל הַצְּפוֹנִי (צ״ל הדרומי צ״ה) שֶׁל הַתָּא הַצְּפוֹנִי וְעוֹבִי כּוֹתֶל הַצְּפוֹנִי שֶׁל אוּלָם כָּלִים זֶה כְּנֶגֶד זֶה, שֶׁכּוֹתֶל אוּלָם עוֹבְיוֹ שֵׁשׁ וְכוֹתֶל הַתָּא חָמֵשׁ, וְהוּא נִמְשָׁךְ אַמָּה אַחַת לְתוֹךְ כְּנֶגֶד עוֹבִי כּוֹתֶל הָאוּלָם, וְכֵן לַדָּרוֹם:


**And a border [area] was before the cells of one cubit, etc.** The cells on either side of the gate were inset [away] from being in line with the area of the inward-going hall, a cubit to the north and a cubit to the south. For he already made it clear that on the inside, it [the space] was extended beyond from the [the area] facing the space of the gate, a cubit and a half to the north and likewise to the south. Now he explains that the cells which were on the outside were recessed to the north, one cubit more than the hall, and likewise one cubit to the south. As a result, they were recessed from the area of the [gate’s] entrance’s width, two and a half cubits to this side and two and a half cubits to this side. This is [the meaning of] the word וּגְבוּל, somayl in Old French, border, limit, an empty place. The wall of the cell was five cubits thick, resulting in the thickness of the northern wall of the northern cell, and the thickness of the northern wall of the hall, ending opposite each other. For the wall of the hall was six cubits thick and the wall of the cell was five, and it was extended one cubit inward opposite the thickness of the wall of the hall, and likewise, to the south.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:13:1)


**וַיָּמָד אֶת הַשַּׁעַר מִגַּג הַתָּא לְגַגּוֹ.** מִגַּג הַתָּא שֶׁבִּצְפוֹן הַשַּׁעַר לְגַג הַתָּא שֶׁבִּדְרוֹם הַשַּׁעַר:


**And he measured the gate from the roof of the cell to its [other] roof** from the roof of the cell at the north of the gate to the roof of the cell at the south of the gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:13:2)


**עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת.** עֹבִי כּוֹתֶל הַתָּא חָמֵשׁ, וְכֵן עֹבִי כּוֹתֶל הַתָּא שֶׁמִּצַּד שֵׁנִי, הֲרֵי עֶשֶׂר; וּשְׁתֵּי אַמּוֹת וּמֶחֱצָה שֶׁתְּהֵא מָשׁוּךְ מֵחֲלַל הַפֶּתַח לְכָאן, וְכֵן לְכָאן, הֲרֵי חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה; וַחֲלַל הַפֶּתַח עֶשֶׂר אַמּוֹת, הֲרֵי עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ; וְכֻלָּן מִדַּת רֹחַב הַשַּׁעַר הֵן, כְּנֶגֶד שְׁלוֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמּוֹת שֶׁל חֲלַל הָאוּלָם מִבִּפְנִים, וּשְׁנֵי כְּתָלִים, קָנֶה מִכָּאן וְקָנֶה מִכָּאן, הֲרֵי עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ:


**twenty-five cubits** The thickness of the wall of the cell was five [cubits], as was the thickness of the wall of the cell that was on the second side, totaling ten [cubits]; and the two and a half cubits that it (some versions: the cell) was recessed from the space of the entrance to this side and likewise to that side, totaled fifteen; and the space of the entrance was ten cubits, making twenty-five. And all of these, which together make up the measurement of the width of the gate, corresponded to the thirteen cubits of the space of the inward going hall, plus the two walls, one rod from this side and one rod from that side, totaling twenty-five [cubits].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:13:3)


**פֶּתַח נֶגֶד פֶּתַח.** פֶּתַח הַתָּא שֶׁאֵצֶל הַשַּׁעַר בַּדָּרוֹם מְכֻוָּן כְּנֶגֶד פֶּתַח הַתָּא שֶׁאֵצֶל הַשַּׁעַר בַּצָּפוֹן, שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה לְכָל שֵׁשֶׁת הַתָּאִים הָאֵלֶּה פֶּתַח בַּכּוֹתֶל שֶׁבְּצַד הַחוּץ, אֶלָּא שֶׁלִּשְׁנֵי הַתָּאִים הַסְּמוּכִין לַשַּׁעַר הָיוּ לָהֶם פְּתָחִים פּוֹנִים אֶל שַׁעַר אֲוִיר שֶׁבֵּין שְׁנֵיהֶם, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר לְמַטָּה <b>וְאֶל אֵלִיהֶמָה לִפְנִימָה לַשַּׁעַר<b> – לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁפִּתְחֵיהֶם פּוֹנִים לַשַּׁעַר:


**an entrance opposite an entrance** The entrance of the cell that was next to the gate in the south was directed exactly opposite the entrance of the cell that was next to the gate in the north, for the six cells did not have an entrance in the outside wall, but the two cells nearest the gate had entrances facing the space between the two of them, and so he says below (verse 16): “and for their doorposts [facing] toward the ‘inward of the gate.’” This teaches us that their entrances faced the gate.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:14:1)


**וַיַּעַשׂ אֶת אֵילִים שִׁשִּׁים אַמָּה.** גָּבְהָן שֶׁל אֵילִים שֶׁל סִפֵּי הָאוּלָם, גְּבוֹהִים שִׁשִּׁים אַמָּה:


**And he made the pillars sixty cubits** The height of the pillars of the posts of the hall was sixty cubits.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:14:2)


**וְאֶל אֵיל הֶחָצֵר הַשַּׁעַר סָבִיב.** וְכֵן לְכָל אֵילֵי הֶחָצֵר שֶׁבְּכָל אוּלַמֵּי שְׁעָרִים סָבִיב סָבִיב, שֶׁאַף בַּצָּפוֹן וּבַדָּרוֹם הָיוּ לָהּ שְׁעָרִים עֲשׂוּיִין כְּתַבְנִית הַשַּׁעַר הַזֶּה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן (להלן כב, כד):


**and [likewise] to the pillars of the court [and of] the gates all around** And the same was for all the pillars that were in all the halls of the gates all around. For also in the north and in the south the gates were constructed like this gate, as is delineated in this account.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:15:1)


**וְעַל פְּנֵי הַשַּׁעַר הָאִיתוֹן.** גּוֹבְהוֹ שֶׁל הַשַּׁעַר הַזֶּה, וְהוּא קָרוּי שַׁעַר הָאִיתוֹן, לְפִי שֶׁהוּא מְשַׁמֵּשׁ כְּנִיסָה וִיצִיאָה לְכָל בָּאֵי הָעֲזָרָה (ירושלמי עירובין ה:א):


**And the height of the entrance gate** [Heb. וְעַל פְּנֵי הַשַּׁעַר,] lit. and on the face of the gate. The height of this gate. It is called שַּׁעַר הָאִיתוֹן because it serves as an entrance and exit for all who come into the Temple court. [The word] איתון is Aramaic for בִּיאָה, entry.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:15:2)


**אִיתוֹן.** תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל בִּיאָה:


**together with the height of the inner ball of the gate** together with the height of the hall of the gate, which is further in than the gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:15:3)


**עַל לִפְנֵי אֻלָם הַשַּׁעַר הַפְּנִימִי.** עִם גָּבְהוֹ שֶׁל אוּלַם הַשַּׁעַר שֶׁהוּא לִפְנִים מִן הַשַּׁעַר חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה, וּמִגֹּבַהּ שְׁאָר הַשְּׁעָרִים שֶׁבָּעִנְיָן שֶׁהֵם חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה אֲנִי לָמַד שֶׁעַל פְּנֵי הָאָמוּר כָּאן הוּא גֹּבַהּ (כְּלוֹמַר מִשַּׁעַר הַחִיצוֹן גָּבְהוֹ חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה עַל פְּנֵי כָּל הָאוּלָם שֶׁשַּׁעַר הַפְּנִימִי שֶׁל אוּלָם שֶׁאַף בְּסוֹפוֹ שֶׁל אוּלָם הוּא שַׁעַר כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ כְּבָר בְּפָסוּק ז׳ סא״א):


**fifty cubits** And from the height of the other gates [mentioned] in this account, which were fifty cubits, I learn that the עַל פְּנֵי stated here means the height. (i.e., the outer gate’s height was fifty cubits, “together with the height of the entire hall:” just like the inner gate of the hall. For also at the end of the hall is a[nother] gate, as was already made clear in verse 7.).



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:16:1)


**וְחַלּוֹנוֹת אֲטֻמוֹת אֶל הַתָּאִים.** הַתָּאִים הָאֵלֶּה לֹא הָיוּ לָהֶם פְּתָחִים אֶל הַחוּץ, אֶלָּא זֶה פָּתוּחַ לָזֶה; וְכֵן מָצִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (מדות ד,ג) בַּתָּאִים שֶׁסְּבִיבוֹת הַהֵיכָל: שְׁלוֹשָׁה פְּתָחִים לְכָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד, אֶחָד לַתָּא מִן הַיָּמִין, וְאֶחָד לַתָּא מִן הַשְּׂמֹאל, וְאֶחָד לַתָּא שֶׁעַל גַּבָּיו. וְלַתָּאִים שֶׁבֶּחָצֵר, שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ אֶלָּא שְׁלוֹשָׁה תָּאִים זֶה אֵצֶל זֶה, וְלֹא הָיוּ לָהֶם תָּאִים עַל גַּבֵּיהֶם, הָיוּ לָאֶמְצָעִי שְׁנֵי פְּתָחִים, אֶחָד לַתָּא מִן הַיָּמִין וְאֶחָד לַתָּא מִן הַשְּׂמֹאל; וְלַחִיצוֹן שֶׁל צַד הַשַּׁעַר הָיוּ שְׁנֵי פְּתָחִים, אֶחָד פָּתוּחַ לָאֶמְצָעִי וְאֶחָד לְצַד הַשַּׁעַר; וְכֵן לַתָּא הַחִיצוֹן שֶׁל כָּתֵף הַשֵּׁנִי מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה לַשַּׁעַר כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב לְמַעְלָה **פֶּתַח נֶגֶד פֶּתַח**. וְהָיוּ לָהֶם חַלּוֹנוֹת שְׁקוּפִים אֲטוּמִים (מלכים א ו:ד): פְּתוּחִים לְצַד הַחִיצוֹן, וּסְתוּמִים לְצַד פְּנִים; כְּלוֹמַר: קְצָרִים לְצַד פְּנִים וּרְחָבִים לְצַד הַחוּץ אֶל הַמִּזְרָח. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּתֵר שְׁקוּפִים – לְשׁוֹן הַשְׁקָפָה, כְּמוֹ ״מִי זֹאת הַנִּשְׁקָפָה״ (שיר השירים ו:י); ״כִּי הִשְׁקִיף מִמְּרוֹם קָדְשׁוֹ״ (תהלים קב:כ); וְכֵן ״אֶל הַמַּשְׁקוֹף״ (שמות יב:כב) גַּם כֵּן פֵּתֵר לְשׁוֹן שְׁקָפָה:


**And narrowing windows [were made] for the cells** These cells did not have doors facing the outside, but one open to the other; and so we find in Tractate Middoth (4:3) regarding the cells around the Temple: “Each one had three entrances, one to the cell on the right, one to the cell on the left, and one to the cell above.” But as for the cells in the Court which were only three cells, one beside the other, having no cells above them the middle ones had two entrances: one to the cell on the right and one to the cell on the left; and the outer one, [standing] at the side of the gate, had two entrances: one open to the middle one and one to the side of the gate, and it was likewise with the outer cell of the other second side, on both sides of the gate, as is written above (verse 13): “an entrance opposite an entrance.” Now these [cells] had windows “שְּׁקֻפִים אֲטֻמִים both open and shut” - [I Kings 6:4] - open to the outside and “shut” to the inside, meaning narrow on the inside and wide on the outside to the east. Menahem (p. 180) interpreted שְּׁקֻפִים as being related to the word for looking (הַשְּׁקָפָה), as in (Song 6: 10) “Who is this who looks forth (הַנִשְּׁקָפָה)”; (Ps. 102:20) “For He has looked down (הִשְּׁקִיף) from His holy height.” He interprets [the word “lintel” in] (Exod. 12:22) “to the lintel (אֶל הַמַשְּׁקוֹף)” also, as being related to the word for [over]looking.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:16:2)


**וְאֶל אֵילֵיהֶמָה לִפְנִימָה לַשַּׁעַר.** וְאֶל כִּתְפוֹת פִּתְחֵיהֶם שֶׁל שְׁנֵי תָאִים הַסְּמוּכִים לַשַּׁעַר מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה, שֶׁהָיָה פִּתְחָן אֶל תּוֹךְ אֲוִיר הַשַּׁעַר, פֶּתַח הַתָּא הַצְּפוֹנִי מִצָּפוֹן לַשַּׁעַר וּפֶתַח הַתָּא הַדְּרוֹמִי מִדָּרוֹם לַשַּׁעַר. וְכִתְפוֹת הַפְּתָחִים שֶׁמִּזֶּה וּמִזֶּה לְפֶתַח הַתָּא הֵם הָאֵילִים, וְהָיוּ בָהֶם חַלּוֹנוֹת פְּתוּחִים לַאֲוִיר הֶפְסֵק רֹחַב הַשַּׁעַר, שֶׁהָיָה מַפְסִיק בֵּין הַתָּאִים. וְאוֹתוֹ אֲוִיר קוֹרֵא **לִפְנִימָה לַשַּׁעַר**, שֶׁמִּי שֶׁנִּכְנָס לְבֵין אֲוִיר בְּלִיטַת הַתָּאִים שֶׁמִּכָּאן וּמִכָּאן נִרְאֶה שֶׁהוּא נִכְנָס לַשַּׁעַר:


**and for their doorposts [facing] toward the “inward of the gate”** [i.e.], and to the edges of the entrances of the two cells nearest the gate on this side and that side. For their entrances were [facing] towards the inner area of the gate: the entrance of the northern cell on the north of the gate, and the entrance of the southern cell on the south of the gate. The edges of the entrances on each side of the entrance of the cell are the “posts.” And they had windows open to the intervening space - having the width of the gate - which intervened between the cells. Now, he calls that space “inward of the gate,” for when someone enters the area between the protrusions of the cells of here and there, it appears as though he has entered the gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:16:3)


**וְכֵן לָאֵילַמּוֹת.** וְכֵן הָיוּ חַלּוֹנוֹת לָאֵילַמּוֹת שֶׁל כָּל שַׁעַר וְשַׁעַר:


**and likewise for the halls** And so did the halls of every gate have windows.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:16:4)


**וְחַלּוֹנוֹת סָבִיב סָבִיב לִפְנִימָה.** בַּחוֹמָה, לְצַד הָעֲזָרָה מִבִּפְנִים:


**and windows all around [facing] inward** in the wall, on the Court’s side, on the inside.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:16:5)


**וְאֶל אַיִל תִּמֹּרִים.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״כּוֹתַרְתָּא״ – כּוֹתָרוֹת; בְּרֹאשׁ הָאַיִל הָיָה עָשׂוּי כְּמִין כּוֹתֶרֶת, דּוֹמֶה לְדֶקֶל, כִּי בְּמִשְׁכְּנָא דִּשְׁלֹמֹה (מלכים א ו:כט) הֵן מְתֻרְגָּמוֹת ״צוּרַת דִּיקְלִין״ (תרגום יונתן). פומיל״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and to the posts were palm like crowns** Jonathan renders: כּוֹתַרְתָּא, crowns at the top of the pillar. They were made like crowns, [each] resembling a palm tree, for in [the account about] Solomon’s Temple (I Kings 6:29) they are translated by the Targum, צוּרַת דִּקְלִין (shapes of palm trees), pomels, crowns.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:17:1)


**וְרִצְפָה.** פְּלַנְקִי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז. **רִצְפָּה** אוֹמֵר אֲנִי שֶׁהוּא עֲלִיָּה סָבִיב, כְּאוֹתָהּ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ חֲלָקָה הָיְתָה בָרִאשׁוֹנָה וְהִקִּיפוּהָ גְּזוּזְטְרָא (סוכה נא:), וְעַל אוֹתָהּ הָעֲלִיָּה הָיוּ הַלְּשָׁכוֹת, קַנְבְּרָא״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז. וּמִמַּה שֶׁאָמוּר בָּעִנְיָן **וְהָרִצְפָה אֶל כְּתֵף הַשְּׁעָרִים** וְתַחְתֵּיהֶם כְּנֶגֶד גּוֹבַהּ הַשְּׁעָרִים, אָנוּ לְמֵדִין שֶׁהָרִצְפָּה הַזֹּאת עֲלִיָּה הִיא:


**and a balcony** [Heb. וְרִצְפָּה,] planchez in Old French, flooring, paving: and I say that it was a surrounding balcony, like the one of which we learned (Middoth 2:5): “Originally it had been quite bare, but they surrounded it with a balcony” and on that balcony were the chambers, chanbres in Old French. And from that which is stated in this account “And the balcony was to the side of the gates,” and its base was on line with the height of the gates - we learn that this flooring was a balcony.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:18:1)


**וְהָרִצְפָּה אֶל כֶּתֶף הַשְּׁעָרִים.** גֹּבַהּ שַׁעֲרֵי הֶחָצֵר הָיָה מַפְסִיק הָעֲלִיָּה, שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה מְסַבֶּבֶת כָּל הֶחָצֵר, וְהִיא לְכִתְפוֹת הַשַּׁעַר מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה, כְּנֶגֶד גֹּבַהּ הַשְּׁעָרִים:


**And the balcony was to the side of the gates** The top of the gates of the Court separated the balcony, so that it did not go around the entire Court, and [thus the balcony] was to the sides of the gate from this side and from that side, parallel to the top of the gates.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:18:2)


**הָרִצְפָּה הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה.** נִשְׁתַּנֵּית תֵּיבָה זוֹ מִכָּל תַּחְתּוֹנָה שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא, שֶׁבְּכָל מָקוֹם הַטַּעַם בְּנוּ״ן, וְזֶה בְּתִי״ו (בנסחנו הטעם בנו״ן, ובפסוק הבא בתי״ו); לָמַדְנוּ, שֶׁאֵין תַּחְתּוֹנָה זוֹ רִצְפָּה שֶׁתַּחַת אַחֶרֶת, אֶלָּא רִצְפָּה אַחַת הִיא, וְתַחְתִּיתָהּ שֶׁל רִצְפָּה הָיָה לְעֻמַּת גֹּבַהּ הַשַּׁעַר. וְה״א שֶׁל ״תַּחְתּוֹנָה״ כְּה״א שֶׁל ״בֵּיתָה״ (תהלים סח:ז), וְכְה״א שֶׁל ״עֶזְרָתָה״ (תהלים מד:כז) וְשֶׁל ״חֶלְאָתָה״ (יחזקאל כד:ו), שֶׁהֵן יְתֵרוֹת:


**the base of the balcony** [Heb. הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה.] This word is different from every תַּחְתּוֹנָה in the Scriptures, for everywhere the accent is on the “nun,” whereas here, it is on the “tav” [so that the word is essentially תַּחְתּוֹן]. We learn that this תַּחְתּוֹנָה was not a balcony that is under another one but was one balcony, and the base of this balcony paralleled the top of the gate. The “hey” of תַּחְתּוֹנָה is like the “hey” of בֵּיתָה and like the “hey” of עֶזְרָתָה, and of חֶלְאָתָה, each of which is superfluous.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:19:1)


**וַיָּמָד רֹחַב.** חֲלַל עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים:


**And he measured the width** the area of the Women’s Court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:19:2)


**מִלִּפְנֵי הַשַּׁעַר הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה.** זֶה הַשַּׁעַר הַמְפֹרָשׁ לְמַעְלָה (פס׳ טו):


**in front of the lower gate** This is the gate delineated above.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:19:3)


**לִפְנֵי הֶחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִי.** עַד עֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**in front of the Inner Court** up to the Israelites’ Court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:19:4)


**הַקָּדִים וְהַצָּפוֹן.** לִשְׁתֵּי רוּחוֹתֶיהָ חֲלָלָהּ **מֵאָה אַמָּה**:


**to the east and to the north** to two of its directions its space was one hundred cubits.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:20:1)


**מָדַד אָרְכּוֹ וְרָחְבּוֹ.** כְּמִדַּת שַׁעַר הַקָּדִים; כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן (להלן מ:כא):


**he measured its length and its width** [to be] like the measurement of the eastern gate. This is delineated in the section.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:21:1)


**חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה אָרְכּוֹ.** גָּבְהוֹ:


**fifty cubits its length** its height.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:21:2)


**וְרֹחַב חָמֵשׁ וְעֶשְׂרִים.** מִגַּג הַתָּא לְגַגּוֹ:


**And twenty five cubits its width** from the roof of one cell to the roof of the other cell.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:22:1)


**וְאֵילַמָּיו לִפְנֵיהֶם.** וְאוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר לִפְנֵי הַמַּעֲלוֹת; קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּכָּנֵס לָאוּלָם הָיָה עוֹלֶה בַּמַּעֲלוֹת:


**and its hall was beyond them** The hall of the gate was beyond the steps; before he would enter the hall, he would ascend the steps.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:23:1)


**וְשַׁעַר לֶחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִי.** לְעֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And the gate of the Inner Court** of the Israelites’ Court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:23:2)


**נֶגֶד הַשַּׁעַר.** הַחִיצוֹנִים מְכֻוָּנִין:


**opposite the gate** directly opposite the Outer [Gate].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:23:3)


**וַיָּמָד מִשַּׁעַר.** הַתַּחְתּוֹן עַד הָעֶלְיוֹן **מֵאָה אַמָּה**, הוּא רֹחַב חֲלַל עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים:


**and he measured from gate** [i.e., from the] Lower [Gate] until the Upper [Gate] one hundred cubits. That is the width of the space of the Women’s Court.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:24:1)


**וַיּוֹלִיכֵנִי דֶּרֶךְ הַדָּרוֹם.** לֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה:


**And he led me by way of the south** of the Outer Court.



### Verse 25

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:25:1)


**כְּהַחֲלּוֹנוֹת הָאֵלֶּה.** דָּרַשׁ רַבִּי תַּנְחוּמָא (תנחומא, בהעלותך ה): ה״א זוֹ יְתֵרָה לְשׁוֹן כֵּהָה חַלּוֹנוֹת, כֵּהוֹת – רְחָבוֹת מִבַּחוּץ וּקְצָרוֹת מִבִּפְנִים; לוֹמַר: לֹא לְאוֹרָה אֲנִי צָרִיךְ; שֶׁכָּל חַלּוֹנוֹת הָעֲשׂוּיוֹת לְהַכְנִיס אוֹרָה, קְצָרוֹת מִבַּחוּץ וּמַרְחִיבוֹת מִבִּפְנִים:


**were like these windows** [Heb. כְּהַחַלנוֹת.] Rabbi Tanhuma expounded (Buber, Behaalothecha p. 48): This “hey” is superfluous, to indicate the term כֵּהָה, dark. Dark windows, wide on the outside and narrow on the inside, as if to say, “I do not need the light,” for all windows made to bring in light are narrow on the outside and wide on the inside.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:26:1)


**עוֹלוֹתָיו.** כְּמוֹ **מַעֲלוֹתָיו**:


**its steps** [Heb. עֹלוֹתָו,] like מַעֲלוֹתָו, its steps.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:26:2)


**אֶל אֵילָיו.** עַל רֹאשׁ הָאַיִל הָיָה דֶּקֶל מְצֻיָּר:


**on its posts** Atop the post was pictured a palm tree.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:28:1)


**וַיָּמָד אֶת הַשַּׁעַר הַדָּרוֹם.** שֶׁל חָצֵר הַפְּנִימִי:


**and he measured the Southern Gate** of the Inner Court.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:30:1)


**וְאֵלַמּוֹת.** אַרְבּוֹ״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**And arcades** [Heb. וְאֵלַמּוֹת,] arboz in Old French.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:30:2)


**סָבִיב סָבִיב.** הַכּוֹתֶל הָיָה עָשׂוּי אוּלַמִּים אוּלַמִּים, נִכְנָסִים בְּעָבְיוֹ; וְאֵלַמּוֹת הַלָּלוּ הָיוּ בִּפְנִים (ס״א לְמַעְלָה מֵהַתָּאִים כִּי הַתָּאִים הָיוּ מִבִּפְנִים בֶּחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִי, שֶׁהֲרֵי אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר הָיָה בּוֹלֵט אֶל הַחִיצוֹנָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בָּעִנְיָן, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁהָיוּ הַתָּאִים וְהָאוּלָם פּוֹנִים לְמָקוֹם אֶחָד לִהְיוֹת פִּתְחֵי הַתָּאִים נִפְתָּחִים לְתוֹךְ הָאוּלָם, שֶׁהֲרֵי הִשְׁוָה הָעִנְיָן כָּל מִדּוֹת הַשְּׁעָרִים הַחִיצוֹנִים וְהַפְּנִימִים, וְלֹא חָלַק בֵּינֵיהֶם אֶלָּא בְּלִיטַת הָאוּלָמוֹת) (כִּי הַתָּאִים הָיוּ מִבַּחוּץ, וְשִׁשָּׁה תָּאִים וְכָתְלֵיהֶם וַחֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה אַמּוֹת שֶׁל רֹחַב הַשַּׁעַר עוֹלִים לִשְׁמוֹנִים וְאַחַת (לתשעים ואחת כצ״ל צ״ה) אַמָּה, וְכָל הֶחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִי אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא מֵאָה עַל מֵאָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן סא״א):


**round about** The wall was made of many halls running through its thickness. These halls were inside [(absent in other editions:) above the cells. For the cells were inside, in the Inner Court, for the hall of the gate protruded into the outer [court], as is included in the account, and it is impossible for [both] the cells and the hall to have been facing the same area so that the entrances of the cells [too] could have opened to the inside of the hall, for the account equates all the measurements of the outer gates with those of the inner ones, and it distinguishes between them only as regards the protrusion of the halls.] (For the cells were outside, and six cells and their walls and the fifteen cubits of the width of the gate total eighty-one cubits, whereas the entire Inner Court is only one hundred by one hundred, as is specified in the account. Absent in other editions).



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:31:1)


**וְאֵילַמָּיו אֶל חָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה.** אוּלַמּוֹ שֶׁל שַׁעַר הַפְּנִימִי לֹא הָיָה לְצַד פְּנִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיוּ אוּלַמּוֹת שֶׁל שַׁעַר הַחִיצוֹנָה, אֶלָּא מִבַּחוּץ הָיָה בּוֹלֵט:


**And its halls to the Outer Court** The halls of the Inner Gate were not toward the inner side as were the halls of the Outer Gate, but were protruding outward.



### Verse 36

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:36:1)


**אֹרֶךְ חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה.** גָּבְהוֹ חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה:


**fifty cubits long** Its height was fifty cubits.



### Verse 37

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:37:1)


**וְאֵילָיו לֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה.** אֶשִׁישְׁפְּיַאַרְ״קָאשׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, וְאוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר קָרוּי אֵילִים כָּאן:


**And its pillars were toward the Outer Court** Et ses portiques in French, and its porticoes, and the hall of the gate is called אֵילִים here.



### Verse 38

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:38:1)


**וְלִשְׁכָּה.** הָיְתָה אֵצֶל שַׁעַר הַצְּפוֹנִי בְּעֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And a chamber** was beside the Northern Gate in the Israelites’ Court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:38:2)


**וּפִתְחָהּ.** שֶׁל לִשְׁכָּה אֶל אֵילֵי הַשַּׁעַר לְצַד הַשַּׁעַר:


**and its entrance** [The entrance] of the chamber faced the posts of the gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:38:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:38:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:38:3)


**בְּאֵילִים הַשְּׁעָרִים.** יֵשׁ בַּפָּרָשָׁה פְּסוּקִים קוֹרִין לְכִתְפוֹת הַשַּׁעַר אֵילִים; מיינשלי״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**in [line with] the pillars of the gates** In this chapter, there are verses that call the sides of the gates אֵילִים, meselins in Old French, posts, pillars.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:38:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:38:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:38:4)


**שָׁם יָדִיחוּ אֶת הָעֹלָה.** הָעוֹלָה הַנִּשְׁחֶטֶת בַּצָּפוֹן (זבחים מז:):


**there they would rinse the burnt offering** the burnt offering, that was slaughtered in the north.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:38:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:38:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:38:5)


**יָדִיחוּ אֶת הָעוֹלָה.** יִרְחֲצוּ אֶת הַקְּרָבַיִם:


**they would rinse the burnt offering** they would wash the innards.



### Verse 39

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:39:1)


**וּבְאוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר.** הַצְּפוֹנִי:


**And in the hall of the gate** [i.e., of] the Northern [Gate].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:39:2)


**שְׁנַיִם שֻׁלְחָנוֹת.** בְּמִזְרָחוֹ, וּשְׁתַּיִם בְּמַעֲרָבוֹ; וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן הָיוּ מְשׁוּכִין אֶל תּוֹךְ הֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה, קְדֻשָּׁתָן כְּלִפְנִים:


**two tables** in its east and two in its west, and although they extended into the Outer Court area, their sanctity was as [great as] those things of the Inner [Court area].



### Verse 40

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:40:1)


**וְאֶל הַכָּתֵף מִחוּצָה.** וְאֶל עֵבֶר פֶּתַח הָאוּלָם, מִחוּצָה לַחֲלַל הָאוּלָם, לְתוֹךְ חֲלָלוֹ שֶׁל פֶּתַח (סא״א שֶׁל צַד) עֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And to the outer side** and to the entrance side of the hall, which was outside the hall interior, leading into the entrance area of (some editions: the side of) the Israelites’ Court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:40:2)


**לַעוֹלֶה לְפֶתַח הַשַּׁעַר הַצָּפוֹנָה.** כְּשֶׁנִּכְנָס לְתוֹךְ הַשַּׁעַר וְעוֹלֶה בַּמַּעֲלוֹת פֶּתַח הָעֲזָרָה:


**for the one who ascends to the entrance of the Northern Gate** When one enters within the gate and ascends the steps of the entrance of the Court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:40:3)


**שְׁנַיִם.** שֻׁלְחָנוֹת לְכָתֵף הַפֶּתַח, לְמִזְרָח (וְכֵן) לְמַעֲרָב:


**two tables** to the side of the entrance, to the east or to the west.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:40:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:40:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:40:4)


**וְאֶל הַכָּתֵף הָאַחֶרֶת.** אֲשֶׁר לְפֶתַח הָאוּלָם **שְׁנַיִם שֻׁלְחָנוֹת**:


**and to the other side** of the entrance of the hall, were two tables.



### Verse 42

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:42:1)


**אֲלֵיהֶם וְיַנִּיחוּ אֶת הַכֵּלִים.** עֲלֵיהֶם מַנִּיחִין הַסַּכִּינִין וְהַמִּזְרָקוֹת שֶׁמְּקַבְּלִין בָּהֶן אֶת הַדָּם, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מוּכָנִים לַשּׁוֹחֲטִים שָׁם קָדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים בַּצָּפוֹן:


**upon them they would lay the implements** Upon them they lay the knives and the basins in which the blood is received to be ready for [use by] those who slaughter the holiest sacrifices [Kodshei Kodoshim] in the north.



### Verse 43

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:43:1)


**וְהַשְּׁפַתַּיִם טֹפַח אֶחָד.** אוּנְקְלִיּוֹת שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל קְבוּעִין בְּעַמּוּדֵי נַנָּסִין שֶׁבְּבֵית הַמִּטְבָּחַיִם בַּצָּפוֹן, לִתְלוֹת וּלְהַפְשִׁיט בָּהֶם אֶת הַקֳּדָשִׁים; הוּא שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בִּפְסָחִים (משנה פסחים ה, ט): אוּנְקְלִיּוֹת שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל הָיוּ קְבוּעִין בָּעַמּוּדִים וּבַכְּתָלִים, שֶׁבָּהֶם תּוֹלִין וּמַפְשִׁיטִין. וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְעוּנְקְלִין נָפְקִין פְּשַׁךְ חַד״:


**And the hooks one handbreadth** iron hooks attached to short pillars in the slaughtering area in the north upon which the hallowed animals were hung and flayed, as we learned in Pes. (5:9): Iron hooks were attached to the pillars and to the walls upon which they would hang and flay [the sacrifices]. Jonathan renders likewise: And hooks extending one handbreadth.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:43:2)


**מוּכָנִים בַּבַּיִת.** קְבוּעִים בְּתוֹךְ חֲלַל הָעֲזָרָה, **בַּבַּיִת** ״לְגַיּוֹ״:


**attached on the inside** attached within the interior of the Court. בַּבַּיִת means inside.



### Verse 44

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:44:1)


**וּמִחוּצָה לַשַּׁעַר הַפְּנִימִי.** וּמִחוּץ לַחֲלַל אוּלָם **הַשַּׁעַר** לְתוֹךְ אֲוִיר **חָצֵר הַפְּנִימִי**, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בַּמִּקְרָא בְּסוֹפוֹ:


**And outside the Inner Gate** outside the gate’s hall space, within the Inner Court’s space, as explained at the end of this verse.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:44:2)


**לִשְׁכוֹת שָׁרִים.** לִשְׁכּוֹת לְוִיִּם מְשׁוֹרְרִים:


**the chambers of the choristers** the chambers of the Levites who sing.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:44:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:44:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:44:3)


**וּפְנֵיהֶם דֶּרֶךְ הַדָּרוֹם.** פִּתְחָם לְצַד דָּרוֹם:


**and they faced southward** their entrance[s] were towards the south.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:44:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:44:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:44:4)


**אֶחָד אֶל כֶּתֶף שַׁעַר הַקָּדִים.** לִשְׁכָּה אַחַת אֶל כֶּתֶף שַׁעַר הַקָּדִים:


**one to the side of the Eastern Gate** One chamber was to the side of the Eastern Gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:44:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:44:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:44:5)


**פְּנֵי דֶרֶךְ הַצָּפוֹן.** וּפִתְחָהּ לְצַד צָפוֹן:


**facing northward** Its entrance was toward the north.



### Verse 45

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:45:1)


**לַכֹּהֲנִים.** מְשָׁרְתֵי הַבַּיִת; כִּמְדֻמֶּה הֵם הַלְוִיִּם הַמְשׁוֹרְרִים:


**for the priests** who served [in] the Temple. These are apparently the Levite choristers.



### Verse 47

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:47:1)


**וַיָּמָד אֶת הֶחָצֵר.** חֲלַל עֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל, לִפְנֵי הָאוּלָם וְהַהֵיכָל:


**And he measured the Court** the interior of the Israelites’ Court [situated] before the Hall and the Temple proper.



### Verse 48

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:48:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:48:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:48:1)


**אֶל אוּלָם הַבַּיִת.** אוּלָם הַהֵיכָל:


**to the Hall of the Temple** the Hall of the Heichal.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:48:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:48:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:48:2)


**וַיָּמָד אֵל אוּלָם.** כְּמוֹ: אֵיל אוּלָם, עֳבִי כִּתְפוֹת הַפֶּתַח (הָרוֹאֶה אֶת הַמִּזְרָח סא״א), מָדַד מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב (ואנ״י הכות״ב מצאת״י בפירושי״ם אחרי״ם כ״מו שהעתק״תי):


**and he measured the pillars of the hall** [Heb. אֵל אֻלָם,] like אֶל אֻלָם, the pillars of the hall; the thickness of the sides of the entrance (that look toward the east absent in some editions) he measured from east to west. (And I, the copyist, found in other commentaries as I copied.)


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:48:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:48:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:48:3)


(**וְרֹחַב הַשַּׁעַר שְׁלֹשָׁה אַמּוֹת מִפֹּה וְגוֹ׳.** הוּא עֹבִי הָאַיִל הַצְּפוֹנִי לְתוֹךְ חֲלַל הַפֶּתַח לְמַעֲרָב, וְכֵן עֹבִי הָאַיִל הַדְּרוֹמִי לְתוֹךְ חֲלַל הָאוּלָם לְמַעֲרָב, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁכָּךְ שָׁנִינוּ (מידות ד,ז): כֹּתֶל אוּלָם חָמֵשׁ וְאוּלָם אַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה, שֶׁלֶּעָתִיד לָבֹא אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא שָׁלֹשׁ, אֶלָּא שֶׁבְּאוּלָם בַּיִת שֵׁנִי וְכוּ׳ סא״א):


(**and the width of the gate, three cubits from here, etc.** That was the width of the northern pillar [going] into the hall space to the west. So too was the thickness of the southern pillar which went inside the Hall on the west. And although we learned that (Mid. 4:7): “The wall of the Hall was five [cubits] and the Hall was eleven,” that of the future Temple will only be three, but in the Hall of the Second Temple etc. - absent in some editions.)


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:48:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:48:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:48:4)


**חָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת.** וְאַף שֶׁל בַּיִת שֵׁנִי הָיָה כֵּן, שֶׁכֵּן שָׁנִינוּ (מידות ד,ז): כּוֹתֶל אוּלָם חָמֵשׁ וְאוּלָם אַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה; אֶלָּא שֶׁבְּאוּלָם בַּיִת שֵׁנִי לֹא הָיוּ כְּתֵפוֹת לַפֶּתַח לַצָּפוֹן וְלַדָּרוֹם, שֶׁהָיָה רֹחַב הַפֶּתַח עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה כְּרֹחַב הָאוּלָם, וְכָאן כָּתוּב **וְרֹחַב הַשַּׁעַר שָׁלֹשׁ אַמּוֹת מִפֹּה וְשָׁלֹשׁ אַמּוֹת מִפֹּה**; וְאֵינִ״י יוֹדֵ״עַ לְצַדֵּ״ד פֵּירוּשׁ״וֹ אֶלָּא לְרֹחַב כְּתֵפוֹת הַפֶּתַח: שָׁלוֹשׁ אַמּוֹת סוֹתְמִין חֲלַל הַפֶּתַח מִכָּאן וְשָׁלוֹשׁ אַמּוֹת מִכָּאן:


**five cubits** That of the Second Temple was the same, for so we learned (Mid. 4:7): “The wall of the Hall was five [cubits] and the Hall was eleven [cubits],” except that there were no [front wall] sides to the entrance on the north and south in the Hall of the Second Temple. The width of the entrance was twenty cubits, just as the width of the Hall, and here it says, “and the width of the gate: three cubits over here and three cubits over there,” (and I do not know how to reconcile it) except [to say] that the width of the front sides of the entrance was three cubits closing in on the space of the entrance here and three cubits there.



### Verse 49

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:49:1)


**אֹרֶךְ הָאוּלָם עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה.** כְּנֶגֶד רֹחַב הַהֵיכָל:


**The length of the Hall was twenty cubits** corresponding to the width of the Temple.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:49:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:49:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:49:2)


**וְרֹחַב עַשְׁתֵּי עֶשְׂרֵה.** מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב:


**and the width was eleven cubits** from east to west.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:49:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:49:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:49:3)


**וּבַמַּעֲלוֹת אֲשֶׁר יַעֲלוּ אֵלָיו.** וּבַמַּעֲלוֹת הָיָה הַדֶּרֶךְ אֲשֶׁר בָּהֶם יַעֲלוּ אֵלָיו, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ (משנה מידות ג, ו): בֵּין הָאוּלָם וְלַמִּזְבֵּחַ עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁתַּיִם אַמָּה, וּשְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה מַעֲלוֹת הָיוּ שָׁם, רוּם מַעֲלָה חֲצִי אַמָּה:


**and with the steps upon which they would ascend to it** And they ascended to it by way of the steps, as we learned (Mid. 3:6): “Between the Hall and the altar were twenty-two cubits, and twelve steps were there, the height of each step being one half cubit.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 40:49:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:49:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/40:49:4)


**וְעַמּוּדִים אֶל הָאֵילִים.** תַּחַת יָכִין וּבֹעַז שֶׁהָיוּ בְּמִקְדָּשׁ רִאשׁוֹן (מלכים א ז:כא):


**and pillars to the posts** instead of Jachin and Boaz (I Kings 7:21) which were in the First Temple.



## Chapter 41


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:1:1)


**שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת רֹחַב.** הוּא עֳבִי כּוֹתֶל הַהֵיכָל שֶׁבֵּין הַהֵיכָל לָאוּלָם, (מִמִּזְרָח לְמַעֲרָב):


**six cubits wide** That is the width of the wall of the Heichal, between the Heichal and the hall (from east to west).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:1:2)


**רֹחַב הָאֹהֶל.** רֹחַב הָאוּלָם; שֶׁהוּא בְּרָחְבּוֹ שֶׁל אוּלָם וּבְאָרְכּוֹ שֶׁל הֵיכָל. בְּפֵרוּשִׁ״ים אֲחֵרִ״ים **רֹחַב הָאֹהֶל** אָהֳלוֹ שֶׁל פֶּתַח:


**the width of the tent** the width of the Hall, for it was in the width of the Hall and in the length of the Heichal. (In other commentaries:) The width of the tent - the tent of the entrance.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:2:1)


**וְכִתְפוֹת הַפֶּתַח חָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת.** שֶׁהַהֵיכָל רָחְבּוֹ עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה וְרֹחַב הַפֶּתַח עֶשֶׂר, נִמְצָא כִּתְפוֹתָיו **מִפֹּה וּמִפֹּה** שֶׁל חָמֵשׁ חָמֵשׁ:


**and the sides of the entrance were five cubits** for the Heichal was twenty cubits wide and the width of the entrance was ten. Its sides from here and there are found to be five cubits each.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:3:1)


**וּבָא לִפְנִימָה.** אֶל הַמְּחִיצָה הַמַּפְסֶקֶת בֵּין הַהֵיכָל לְקֹדֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁים:


**And he came to the interior** to the wall that separated the Heichal from the Holy of Holies.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:3:2)


**וַיָּמָד אֵיל הַפֶּתַח.** עָבְיוֹ **שְׁתַּיִם אַמּוֹת**; אֵין זֶה שָׁוֶה לְאַמָּה טְרַקְסִין (ראה רש״י יומא נא:):


**and measured the pillar of the entrance** Its thickness was two cubits. This was not equal to the cubit for the partition.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:3:3)


**וְהַפֶּתַח שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת.** אֵינִי יָכוֹל לְפָרְשׁוֹ אֶלָּא לְגָבְהוֹ:


**and the entrance six cubits** I cannot explain this except as a reference to its height.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:4:1)


**אֶל פְּנֵי הַהֵיכָל.** עַל פְּנֵי רָחְבּוֹ שֶׁל הֵיכָל:


**to the face of the Temple** on the face of the width of the Temple.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:5:1)


**קִיר הַבַּיִת.** כּוֹתֶל מַעֲרָבִי:


**And he measured the wall** the western wall.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:5:2)


**וְרֹחַב הַצֵּלָע.** אַפְּנְדִי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, הַתָּא שֶׁל אַחֲרָיו אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת חֲלָלוֹ, וְשֶׁל בַּיִת שֵׁנִי הָיָה שֵׁשׁ (מדות ד:ז); וְעַל כָּרְחֲךָ בַּיִת זֶה לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּבַיִת שֵׁנִי לֹא הָיְתָה חוֹמָה מַפְסֶקֶת לְבֵית קָדְשֵׁי הַקֳּדָשִׁים (יומא נא:):


**and the width of the cell** [Heb. הַצֵלָע,] apendiz in Old French. The interior of the cell behind it was four amos in measurement, and in the Second Temple it was six (amos). And perforce this Temple is the one of the future, because there was no wall separating the Holy of Holies in the Second Temple.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:6:1)


**צֵלָע אֶל צֵלָע.** תָּא אֶל תָּא וְתָא עַל תָּא:


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:6:2)


**שָׁלוֹשׁ וּשְׁלֹשִׁים פְּעָמִים.** וּבְבַיִת שֵׁנִי הָיוּ שְׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁמוֹנֶה: חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה בַּצָּפוֹן וְחָמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה בַּדָּרוֹם, חֲמִשָּׁה עַל חֲמִשָּׁה וַחֲמִשָּׁה עַל גַּבֵּיהֶם וּשְׁמוֹנֶה בַּמַּעֲרָב, הוּא צֶלַע הוּא יַצִּיעַ הוּא תָּא. וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי שֶׁהַתָּאִים שֶׁבַּצָּפוֹן וּבַדָּרוֹם כָּךְ סַדְּרָן: שֶׁכָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד אָרְכוּ שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה אַמָּה, הֲרֵי שִׁשִּׁים אַמָּה לַחֲמִשָּׁה תָּאִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה כְּתָלִים שֶׁל חָמֵשׁ חָמֵשׁ אַמָּה, הֲרֵי שְׁמוֹנִים וְחָמֵשׁ, וְרֹחַב חָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת שֶׁל הַמֻּנָּח, פִּרְנַסְתָּ תִּשְׁעִים אַמָּה שֶׁל אֹרֶךְ הַכֹּתֶל, וְכֵן לְדָרוֹם; וְשֶׁבְּמַעֲרָב אֶחָד עַל גַּב אֶחָד, וּשְׁלִישִׁי עַל גַּבֵּיהֶן וְאָרְכָּן עַל גַּבֵּיהֶם עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה כְּנֶגֶד רֹחַב בֵּית קָדְשֵׁי הַקֳּדָשִׁים, הֲרֵי אַחַת עֶשְׂרֵה בְּכָל סֵדֶר, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״חַד עַסְרִי בַּסִּדְרָא״ (ס״א לא גורסים פעמים שלוש פעמים אחת עשר על גב אחת עשר על גביהן וכן ת״י חד עסרי בסדרא ובבית שני וכו׳ עד הוא תא, ותו לא):


**thirty-three times** But in the Second Temple, there were thirty-eight: fifteen in the north, and fifteen in the south - five upon five, and five atop them - and eight in the west. [The term] צֵלָע is synonymous with יָצִיעַ and with תָּא. I say that the cells in the north and the south were arranged as follows: each one was twelve cubits long, totaling sixty cubits for five cells, and five walls of five cubits each, totaling 85. The width of the cleared space was five cubits. Thus you have accounted for ninety cubits of the length of the wall, and so to the south. Those in the west were one on top of another, and a third atop them, and their length across their top was twenty cubits, paralleling the width of the Holy of Holies, making eleven in each row. So did Jonathan render: eleven in a row. (**thirty-three times** eleven upon eleven, and eleven atop them. So did Jonathan render: eleven in a row, but in the Second Temple etc.... and with תָּא, and no more.)


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:6:3)


**וּבָאוֹת בַּקִּיר אֲשֶׁר לַבַּיִת לַצְּלָעוֹת.** קוֹרוֹת עֲלִיּוֹת הַיְּצִיעִים; הָיוּ רָאשֵׁיהֶם שֶׁלְּצַד מְחִיצַּת הַתָּאִים הַחִיצוֹנָה בָּאוֹת וְנוֹקְבוֹת בַּקִּיר בְּצִדּוֹ הַפְּנִימִי, וְזֶהוּ **אֲשֶׁר לַבַּיִת לַצְּלָעוֹת** – בְּצִדּוֹ אֲשֶׁר לְתוֹךְ הַתָּאִים, וְזֶהוּ **לִהְיוֹת אֲחוּזִים בְּקִיר הַבַּיִת** – קְבוּעִים בְּחָזְקָה פּוֹפְרִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and they rested upon the inside of the wall** The beams of the cells of the top story - their ends on the side toward the cells’ outer wall would rest upon the wall, on its inner side, and puncture it. This is the meaning of “the inside of the wall of the cells” into the side that was toward the interior of the cells; and this is the meaning of “that they might hold therein,” - be fastened securely, po[r]pris in Old French, firmly held.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:6:4)


**וְלֹא יִהְיוּ אֲחוּזִים בְּקִיר הַבַּיִת.** רָאשֵׁיהֶם שֶׁל צַד קִיר הַהֵיכָל הָיוּ בָּאוֹת וְנוֹקְבוֹת עַל כְּנִיסוֹת, שֶׁהָיָה קִיר הַהֵיכָל בּוֹלֵט לְתוֹךְ הַתָּאִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בְּבִנְיַן שְׁלֹמֹה (מלכים א ו:ו) ״כִּי מִגְרָעוֹת נָתַן לַבַּיִת סָבִיב חוּצָה״, ״הַיָּצִיעַ הַתַּחְתֹּנָה חָמֵשׁ בָּאַמָּה רֹחַב״, חֲלָלָהּ וַעֲלִיָּתָהּ שֵׁשׁ, שֶׁהָיָה כּוֹתֶל הַהֵיכָל כָּנוּס מִלְמַעְלָה אַמָּה, לְפִיכָךְ חֲלַל הַתִּיכוֹנָה שֵׁשׁ אַמָּה רְחָבָה, וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁית שֶׁבַע בָּאַמָּה רְחָבָה, וְהִיא הָיְתָה עֲלִיָּה שֶׁל תִּיכוֹנָה, שֶׁהָיָה כּוֹתֶל הַהֵיכָל כָּנוּס לְתוֹכָהּ אַמָּה מִלְמַעְלָה, וְאַף אֵלּוּ הָיוּ כְּמוֹתָן; וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר: **וְלֹא יִהְיוּ אֲחוּזִים בְּקִיר הַבַּיִת.** לֹא הָיוּ נוֹקְבִים בְּקִיר הַהֵיכָל, כִּשְׁאָר רָאשֵׁי הַקּוֹרוֹת נְמוּכִים הַקְּבוּעִים בְּכוֹתֶל גָּבוֹהַּ, שֶׁנּוֹקְבִים הַכּוֹתֶל וְתוֹקְעִים רָאשֵׁי הַקּוֹרוֹת בַּנְּקָבִים:


**that they should not be held in the wall of the House** Their ends on the side toward the wall of the Heichal rested on, and penetrated the recesses, i.e., the wall of the Heichal protruded into the interior of the cells, as it is stated regarding Solomon’s [Temple] building (I Kings 6:6): “for he made rebatements in [the wall of] the House round about on the outside.” The width of the interior of the nethermost chamber was five cubits, and its upper story was six, for the wall of the Heichal was recessed from above one cubit; therefore the interior of the nethermost one was five cubits wide and the third chamber was seven cubits wide and this was atop the middle one, which had the wall of the Heichal protruding into it one cubit from above and these too were like them. And this is what is meant by “and they would nor be held in the wall of the House”; they did not penetrate into the wall of the Heichal as do ends of other low beams which are fastened onto a high wall, i.e., [builders usually] make holes in the wall and thrust the beams into the holes.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:7:1)


**וְרָחֲבָה.** וְהַצְּלָעוֹת הָיוּ הוֹלְכִין וּמַרְחִיבִין לְמַעְלָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי: הָאֶמְצָעִית רְחָבָה מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה אַמָּה, וְכֵן **הָעֶלְיוֹנָה** מִן הָאֶמְצָעִית – אַמָּה:


**And it became wider** And the cells constantly widened as they went upward, as I explained: the middle one was a cubit wider than the lowest one, and the uppermost [one] was a cubit [wider] than the middle one.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:7:2)


**וְנָסְבָה לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה.** וּמוּסַבָּה מְסִיבָּה, שֶׁקּוֹרִין וִי״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז, שֶׁל בִּנְיַן אֲבָנִים הוּא כְּמִין עַמּוּד וּמַעֲלוֹת עוֹלוֹת בּוֹ, וְהָעוֹלֶה בָּהֶם דּוֹמֶה לוֹ כְּהוֹלֵךְ וּמַקִּיף סְבִיבוֹת עַמּוּד אֲבָנִים; וּבִלְשׁוֹן אַשְׁכְּנַז קָרוּי שְׁוִוינְדְ״ל שְׁטִיי״ן. הוּא שֶׁאָמוּר בְּבִנְיַן שְׁלֹמֹה ״וּבְלוּלִּים יַעֲלוּ אֶל הַתִּיכֹנָה״ (מלכים א ו:ח), אַף הוּא מְתֻרְגָּם ״וּבִמְסִיבָתָא״, כְּמוֹ **וְנָסְבָה לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה**. וְכֵן מְסִיבָּה עוֹלָה לְמַעְלָה עַד גַּג הַתָּא הָעֶלְיוֹן, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא מְפָרֵשׁ וְהוֹלֵךְ, שֶׁהַמְּסִיבָּה עוֹלָה מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹן לָאֶמְצָעִי וּמִן הָאֶמְצָעִי לָעֶלְיוֹן:


**and it wound higher and higher** [Heb. וְנָסְבָה,] and it was surrounded by a spiral staircase, fiz in Old French. This is a stone structure similar to a pillar, with steps winding around it. It seems to the person who is ascending upon it that he is encircling a stone pillar. In German it is called a wendelstein. This is what is [meant when it is] stated regarding Solomon’s Temple (I Kings 6:8): “and upon winding stairs (לוּלִים) they went up into the middle [chamber].” That too is translated [into Aramaic] as וּבִמְסִיבָתָא, like וְנָסְבָה לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה and it wound higher and higher. The spiral staircase continued to wind higher and higher up to the roof of the uppermost cell, as he proceeds to explain, [so] that the spiral staircase ascended from the nethermost cell to the middle one and from the middle one to the uppermost one.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:7:3)


**כִּי מוּסַב הַבַּיִת.** לַצְּלָעוֹת:


**for the encompassing of the House** to the cells.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:7:4)


**לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה סָבִיב סָבִיב לַבַּיִת עַל כֵּן רֹחַב לַבַּיִת לְמָעְלָה.** עַל כֵּן שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ **וְרָחֲבָה** לְמַעְלָה לְמַעְלָה, רֹחַב בַּיִת תּוֹךְ הַצְּלָעוֹת לְמַעְלָה, **הָעֶלְיוֹנָה** רְחָבָה מִכֻּלָּן:


**went upward round about the House: therefore, the widest part of the House was above** Therefore, we said, “And it became wider and it wound higher and higher.” The widest part of the House within the cells was above. The uppermost [cell] was wider than all of them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:7:5)


**וְכֵן הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְכֵן בִּמְסִיבְתָא סָלְקִין מִן אַרְעִיתָא לְעִילִיתָא בְּאוֹרַח מְצִיעֲתָא״. וְכֵן יַעֲלוּ עַל יְדֵי מְסִיבָּה מִן הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה אֶל הָעֶלְיוֹנָה דֶּרֶךְ הַתִּיכוֹנָה:


**and so, [from] the nethermost** Jonathan rendered: “And so, with the spiral staircase they would ascend from the nethermost to the uppermost through the middle one.” And so they would ascend with the spiral staircase from the nethermost one to the uppermost one by way of the middle one.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:8:1)


**וָרָאִיתִי לַבַּיִת גֹּבַהּ.** לֹא פֵּרַשׁ שִׁעוּרוֹ, אֲבָל בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת שָׁנִינוּ (מדות ד,ו): עַל רוּם מֵאָה אַמָּה:


**And I saw that the House had a height** He does not delineate its measurement, but we learn in Tractate Middoth (4:6): “with a height of one hundred cubits.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:8:2)


**מוּסְדוֹת הַצְּלָעוֹת מְלוֹ הַקָּנֶה.** יְסוֹד הַצְּלָעוֹת הָיָה מָלֵא כַּקָּנֶה:


**the foundations of the cells were the full length of a rod** The foundation of the cells was the full length of a rod.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:8:3)


**שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת אַצִּילָה.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״רְוַח״; יְסוֹד מְחִיצַת הַצֶּלַע בְּתוֹךְ הָאָרֶץ הָיָה רָחָב שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת, אֲבָל לְמַעְלָה מִן הָאָרֶץ הָיָה רָחְבּוֹ חָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן, בַּמִּקְרָא שֶׁל אַחֲרָיו:


**six cubits was its span** [Heb. אַצִילָה.] Jonathan renders: span. [The part] of the foundation of the stone wall within the earth was six cubits wide, but [the part] above the earth was five cubits wide, as is delineated in this section in the following verse.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:9:1)


**רֹחַב הַקִּיר אֲשֶׁר לַצֵּלָע אֶל הַחוּץ.** מְחִצַּת הַתָּאִים הַסְּמוּכָה לֶחָצֵר, הוּא מְחִצַּת מַעֲרָבִית:


**The width of the wall of the cell to the outside** The wall of the cells adjoining the Court; i.e., the western wall.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:9:2)


**חָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת.** וְכֵן שָׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (ד, ז) שֶׁאוֹתוֹ כּוֹתֶל הוּא חָמֵשׁ:


**was five cubits** And we also learned in Tractate Middoth (4:7), that the wall was five cubits [wide].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:9:3)


**וַאֲשֶׁר מֻנָּח.** וּמָקוֹם פָּנוּי הָיָה מֻנָּח בְּמִקְצוֹעוֹת הַצָּפוֹן וְהַדָּרוֹם, שֶׁאֵצֶל הַמִּזְרָח, שֶׁאֵין הַתָּאִים מַקִּיפִין אֶת כָּל הַבַּיִת, וּלְאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם פָּנוּי הָיוּ פִּתְחֵי הַתָּאִים שֶׁבַּמִּקְצוֹעוֹת מִזְרָחִיִּים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בָּעִנְיָן **וּפֶתַח הַצֵּלָע לַמֻּנָּח**, כִּי לֹא הָיָה לָהֶם פְּתָחִים לַתָּאִים לֹא לְצַד הֶחָצֵר וְלֹא לְצַד הַהֵיכָל, אֶלָּא לְאוֹתָן שֶׁבְּמִקְצוֹעַ מִזְרָחִית צְפוֹנִית וּבְמִזְרָחִית דְּרוֹמִית הָיָה הַפֶּתַח בַּכּוֹתֶל רָחְבּוֹ פָּתוּחַ לְאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם הַמֻּנָּח, וּבְאוֹתוֹ פֶּתַח נִכְנָסִין לוֹ וּמִמֶּנּוּ לַתָּא הַשֵּׁנִי, וּמִן הַשֵּׁנִי אֶל הַשְּׁלִישִׁי, וְכֵן סָבִיב, שֶׁכֵּן שָׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (מדות ד:ג): שְׁלֹשָׁה פְתָחִים לְכָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד, אֶחָד לַתָּא מִן הַיָּמִין וְאֶחָד לַתָּא מִן הַשְּׂמֹאל וְאֶחָד לַתָּא שֶׁעַל גַּבָּיו. וְאַף יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם כֵּן **וַאֲשֶׁר מֻנָּח** – ״וַאֲתַר שְׁבִיק״. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: **וַאֲשֶׁר מֻנָּח** כְּלוֹמַר וְכֵן אוֹתָן (שֶׁל צַד פְּנִים) מִן הַצְּלָעוֹת לְצַד בַּיִת מֻנָּח – מָקוֹם פָּנוּי הָיָה מֻנָּח בֵּין הַתָּאִים וּבֵין הַבַּיִת, וּבְאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם הָיוּ פִּתְחֵי הַתָּאִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן **וּפֶתַח הַצֵּלָע לַמֻּנָּח**, וְרָחְבּוֹ הָיָה חָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר: **וְרֹחַב מְקוֹם הַמֻּנָּח חָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת**, כִּדְפֵּרִישִׁית לְעֵיל, שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ הַתָּאִים סְמוּכִים לַבַּיִת כְּלָל, וַאֲוִיר יִהְיֶה בֵּינֵיהֶם וְהַבַּיִת חָמֵשׁ אַמּוֹת. וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וַאֲתַר שְׁבִיק״:


**and what was left clear** And a clear area was left at the northern and southern corners that adjoined the east and the west. For the cells did not encompass the entire House, and the entrances of the cells in the eastern corners led to that cleared area - as is stated in this section: “And the entrance of the cell to the clear space”. The cells had no entrances either to the side of the Court or to the side of the Heichal, but to those in the northeastern [corner] and the southeastern corner, had an the entrance in the wall which opened to the cleared off area. And by that entrance they would enter it, and from it, into the second cell, and from the second into the third, and so on all around, as we learned in Tractate Middoth (4:3): “and an area left over.” Another explanation: **left clear** Those on the inside of the cells that were toward the side of the House was left clear; i.e., a clear area was left over between the cells and the House, and in the area were the cells’ entrances, as is delineated in this section: “And the entrance of the cell was toward the clear place,” and its width was five cubits, as [Scripture] says: “and the width of the clear area was five cubits,” as I explained above, [i.e.,] that the cells will not be close to the House at all, and there will be a space of five cubits between them and the House. And so did Jonathan render: and an area left over.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:9:4)


**בֵּית צְלָעוֹת אֲשֶׁר לַבָּיִת.** נֶגֶד אֲוִיר רֹחַב הַצְּלָעוֹת בְּקֶרֶן הַזָּוִית. **סָבִיב סָבִיב** שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּ**מְקוֹם הַמֻּנָּח** אֵינוֹ בְּאַרְבַּע פִּנּוֹת כְּמַשְׁמָעוֹ, אֶלָּא כְּלוֹמַר: מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן, דָּרוֹם וְצָפוֹן, זָוִית דְּרוֹמִית מִזְרָחִית, וְזָוִית צְפוֹנִית מִזְרָחִית:


**for the structure of cells that belonged to the House** Opposite the space of the width of the cells in the corner. The words “round about,” written about the clear area, are not referring to [all] four corners, but mean “here and there,” i.e., south and north: the southeastern and the northeastern corners.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 41:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/41:10:1)
