**וְגָדֵר אֲשֶׁר לַחוּץ וְגוֹ׳.** הַמַּפְסִיק בֵּין הַלְּשָׁכוֹת וְהֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה שֶׁל צַד מִזְרָח:


**And the outer wall, etc.** that intervenes between the chambers and the Outer Court of the eastern side.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:7:2)


**אָרְכּוֹ חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה.** מִן הַצָּפוֹן לַדָּרוֹם:


**its length being fifty cubits** from north to south.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:8:1)


**כִּי אֹרֶךְ הַלְּשָׁכוֹת.** מִן הַצָּפוֹן לַדָּרוֹם **חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה**:


**For the length of the chambers** from north to south, was fifty cubits.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:8:2)


**וְהִנֵּה עַל פְּנֵי הַהֵיכָל.** מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב, כְּנֶגֶד הָאוּלָם וְהַהֵיכָל וּבֵית הַכַּפֹּרֶת וְהַתָּאִים **מֵאָה אַמָּה**:


**and behold, before the Temple** from east to west, paralleling the Hall and the Temple and House of the Ark Cover, [i.e., the Holy of Holies] and the cells, were one hundred cubits.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:9:1)


**וּמִתַּחַת הַלְּשָׁכוֹת הָאֵלֶּה.** אֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ אִם בָּא לְלַמֵּד שֶׁהָיָה לָהֶם מְחִילוֹת מִתַּחְתֵּיהֶם, אוֹ בָּא לְלַמֵּד שֶׁהָיוּ לְשָׁכוֹת בְּמִזְרַח הֶחָצֵר, וּלְפִי שֶׁהָהָר הוֹלֵךְ וּמְשַׁפֵּעַ לְצַד הַמִּזְרָח קוֹרֵא לוֹ **וּמִתַּחַת הַלְּשָׁכוֹת הָאֵלֶּה**, שֶׁאֵלּוּ בְּגָבְהוֹ וְאֵלּוּ בְּשִׁפּוּלוֹ; וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר: וּבְשִׁפּוּלוֹ שֶׁל חָצֵר אֶל **הַמֵּבוֹא אֲשֶׁר מֵהַקָּדִים בְּבֹאוֹ לָהֵנָּה מֵהֶחָצֵר הַחִיצֹנָה**:


**And below these chambers** I do not know whether this comes to teach that they had tunnels beneath them, or whether it comes to teach that there were chambers in the east of the Court, and [that] since the mountain slopes [downward] to the east, he calls it “and below these chambers.” And he is saying this: And at the lowest point of the Court, to the approach that was on the east as one comes to them from the outer Court.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:10:1)


**בְּרֹחַב גֶּדֶר הֶחָצֵר וְגוֹ׳.** הַבָּא מִן **הַקָּדִים** שֶׁל **חָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה**, וְסוֹבֵב אֶל הַצָּפוֹן דֶּרֶךְ מִקְצוֹעַ צְפוֹנִית מִזְרָחִית שֶׁל חוֹמוֹת הַפְּנִימִית מִבַּחוּץ, לָבֹא לְאוֹתָן הַלְּשָׁכוֹת שֶׁבַּצָּפוֹן, מוֹצֵא עַל פְּנֵי גֶדֶר הֶחָצֵר כּוֹתֶל מִזְרָח שֶׁל חָצֵר הַפְּנִימִית שֶׁהִיא מַעֲרָבִית לַחִיצוֹנִית, כְּנֶגֶד הַגִּזְרָה וְהַבִּנְיָן שֶׁבַּפְּנִימִית **לְשָׁכוֹת** סְמוּכוֹת לְאוֹתוֹ הַכּוֹתֶל וְעוֹמְדִים בַּחִיצוֹנָה. מָצָאתִי:


**Along the width of the wall of the Court, etc.** One who comes from the east of the Outer Court and veers to the north by way of the outside of the northeastern corner of the Inner [Court’s] walls, to come to those chambers in the north, will find, facing the wall of the [Outer] Court, the eastern wall of the Inner Court, which is west of the Outer [Court] opposite the fortress and the building in the Inner [Court]. **chambers** adjacent to that wall, and standing in the Outer (Court).



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:11:1)


**וְדֶרֶךְ לִפְנֵיהֶם.** לִפְנֵי לְשָׁכוֹת הַלָּלוּ יֵשׁ דֶּרֶךְ רֹחַב אֲוִיר בֶּחָצֵר (הָחִיצוֹנָה) חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה כְּמַרְאֵה דֶּרֶךְ הַלְּשָׁכוֹת אֲשֶׁר בְּצַד הַצָּפוֹן הַכְּתוּבוֹת לְמַעְלָה (פסוק ב):


**And the way before them** there was a road before these chambers whose width [was the same as] the space of the Court: fifty cubits. Its appearance was the same as that of the pathway to the chambers that were on the northern side, mentioned above (verse 4).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:11:2)


**כְּאָרְכָּן.** שֶׁל לִשְׁכוֹת הַצָּפוֹן, אָרְכָּן שֶׁל אֵלּוּ, וְכֵן רָחְבָּן:


**as was their length** [The length] of the northern chambers was the length of these, and the same was so of their width.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:11:3)


**וְכָל מוֹצָאֵיהֶן.** כְּמוֹצָא לִשְׁכוֹת הַצָּפוֹן:


**and all their exits** were like the exit of the northern chambers.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:11:4)


**וּכְמִשְׁפְּטֵיהֶן וּכְפִתְחֵיהֶן.** שֶׁל לִשְׁכוֹת הַצָּפוֹן:


**and as their measurements and as their entrances** [i.e., the measurements and the entrances] of the northern chambers.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:12:1)


**וּכְפִתְחֵי הַלְּשָׁכוֹת אֲשֶׁר דֶּרֶךְ הַדָּרוֹם.** פִּתְחֵי לִשְׁכוֹת הַקָּדִים, שֶׁאַף בַּדָּרוֹם הָיוּ לְשָׁכוֹת מֻבְדָּלוֹת מִן הַתָּאִים הַדְּרוֹמִיִּים עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה ״וּבֵין הַלְּשָׁכוֹת רֹחַב עֶשְׂרִים אַמָּה סָבִיב לַבַּיִת סָבִיב סָבִיב״ (יחזקאל מא:י):


**And as the entrances of the chambers that are toward the way of the south** the eastern entrances of the chambers. For in the south, too, there were chambers separated from the southern cells by twenty cubits (as it is written) above (41:10): “And between the chambers was a [space] twenty cubits wide surrounding the House, round about.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:12:2)


**פֶּתַח בְּרֹאשׁ דָּרֶךְ.** פֶּתַח הָיָה לָהֶם לִלְשָׁכוֹת הַקָּדִים בְּרֹאשׁ דֶּרֶךְ, וְהַדֶּרֶךְ הָיָה **בִּפְנֵי הַגְּדֶרֶת הַגִּינָה**, תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״דּוּכַן לֵיוָאֵי״; וְלָשׁוֹן **גְּדֶרֶת הַגִּינָה** – בִּנְיַן גֶּדֶר אֲבָנִים עָשׂוּי בְּמַעֲלוֹת, שֶׁהָיוּ נוֹגְנִים וְשָׁרִים עָלָיו:


**an entrance at the beginning of the pathway** The eastern chambers had an entrance at the beginning of the pathway, and the pathway was before the wall of the musicians. Jonathan renders: the platform of the Levites. The expression הַגְּדֶרֶת הֲגִינָה means a stone wall structure made like steps upon which the musicians and the choristers stood.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:12:3)


**דֶּרֶךְ הַקָּדִים.** שֶׁהַדּוּכָן הָיָה בַּמִּזְרָח, כְּדִכְתִיב בְּדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים בְּפָרָשַׁת וַתִּשְׁלַם (דברי הימים ב ה:יב): ״וְהַלְוִיִּם הַמְשׁוֹרְרִים לְכֻלָּם לְאָסָף לְהֵימָן לִידוּתוּן וְגוֹ׳ עוֹמְדִים מִזְרָח לַמִּזְבֵּחַ״:


**the way of the east** For the platform was in the east, as it is written in (II Chron. 5:12) in the chapter commencing: “was completed”: “And the Levites who sang, all of them - Asaph, Heman, Jeduthun, etc., stood east of the altar.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:14:1)


**בְּבֹאָם הַכֹּהֲנִים וְגוֹ׳.** בְּבוֹאָם לְאוֹתָן לְשָׁכוֹת לֶאֱכֹל:


**When the priests arrive, etc.** Once they enter those chambers to eat.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:14:2)


**וְלֹא יֵצְאוּ.** מֵהֶן שֶׁהֵן קֹדֶשׁ:


**they shall not go out** of them, which are holy.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:14:3)


**אֶל הֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה.** בְּבִגְדֵי קֹדֶשׁ שֶׁעֲלֵיהֶם:


**into the Outer Court** with the holy raiment that is upon them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:14:4)


**וְשָׁם יַנִּיחוּ בִגְדֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳.** וְקָרְבוּ אֶל אֲשֶׁר לָעָם – וְיִגְּשׁוּ לִיגַּע בְּבִגְדֵי הָעָם אִם יִרְצוּ; אֲבָל בְּבִגְדֵי כְּהֻנָּה לֹא יִגְּעוּ אֶל הָעָם, שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶן מַעֲלוֹת טָהֳרָה, וּבִגְדֵי הַחוֹל מִדְרָס לָהֶם (משנה חגיגה ב,ז):


**but shall there leave their garments, etc., and they will approach those of the people** And they may approach to touch the peoples’ garments if they wish, but they must not touch the [garments of] people with the priestly raiment, for they have higher degrees of ritual purity, and ordinary garments have midras contamination on them.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:15:1)


**וּמְדָדוֹ סָבִיב סָבִיב.** לְכָל הֶיקֵּף הַר הַבַּיִת מִבַּחוּץ:


**and he measured it round about** the entire outer perimeter of the Temple Mount.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:20:1)


**לְאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת מָדְדוּ.** שְׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים עַל שְׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים אַמָּה, שֶׁהַקָּנֶה שֵׁשׁ אַמּוֹת (יחזקאל מ:ה); וְזֶהוּ שֶׁיִּסֵּד הַקְּלִירִי (בקדשתא דיום שני של סכות): ״דָּוִד יִנְטֶה קַו שְׁלֹשֶׁת אַלְפֵי אַמּוֹת קְצוּבָה עַל שְׁלֹשֶׁת אַלְפֵי אַמּוֹת״, וְהִיא שְׁלֹשִׁים וְשִׁשָּׁה כַּמִּדָּה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה – שֶׁהָיָה הַר הַבַּיִת חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת אַמָּה עַל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת אַמָּה (מידות ב:א) – חֲלוֹק שְׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים עַל שְׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים לִרְצוּעוֹת שֶׁל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת אַמּוֹת שְׁתִי וָעֵרֶב, וְתִמְצָא שָׁם שְׁלֹשִׁים וְשִׁשָּׁה רְבָעִים שֶׁל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת עַל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת; וְעַל כֵּן אָמַר הַפַּיְּטָן (שם בקדשתא): ״שְׁלֹשִׁים וְשִׁשָּׁה כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיְתָה מִתְכַּפֶּלֶת״, מָצָאתִי:


**On four sides he measured it** Three thousand cubits by three thousand [cubits], for the rod was six cubits long. This is what the Kalir asserted, “The Beloved will stretch out a line of three thousand cubits limited by three thousand cubits.” This is thirty-six times the original dimensions - for the Temple Mount was [originally] five hundred cubits by five hundred cubits; arrange 3,000 times 3,000 as strips of 500 cubits laid vertically and horizontally [forming a solid square], and you will have thirty-six squares of 500 cubits by 500 cubits. [3,000 x 3,000 = 9,000,000. 500 x 500 = 250,000. 9, 000,000 divided by 250,000 = 36.] Therefore, the poet states: “And thirty-six as it was multiplied.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:20:2)


**חוֹמָה לוֹ.** לְהַר הַבַּיִת:


**On four sides he measured it: its wall all around** [i.e., the wall] of the Temple Mount.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:20:3)


**סָבִיב.** חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת שְׁתִי וָעֵרֶב, זוֹ חוֹמַת הַחֵיל הַמַּקֶּפֶת כָּל הָהָר:


**around...five hundred** in length and in width. This was the wall that encompassed the entire mountain.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 42:20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/42:20:4)


**לְהַבְדִּיל.** אֶת הָעָם בֵּין **קֹדֶשׁ** שֶׁלִּפְנִים **לְחֹל** שֶׁבַּחוּץ. מָצָאתִי גְּמַר כָּל מִדָּה (סא״א):


**to separate** the people between the holy which was inside, and the profane which was outside. I found the completion of every measure. (This does not appear in other editions.)



## Chapter 43


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:1:1)


**וַיּוֹלִיכֵנִי.** חָזַר וְהִכְנִיסַנִי לְשַׁעַר הַקָּדִים, וְרָאִיתִי הַכָּבוֹד יוֹרֵד לָעֲזָרָה:


**And he led me** He again led me into the Eastern Gate, and I saw the Glory descending to the Court.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:3:1)


**וּכְמַרְאֵה הַמַּרְאֶה.** תֵּשַׁע מַרְאוֹת כְּתוּבִין בַּמִּקְרָא זֶה, וּמִתּוֹכָן הָיוּ רוֹאִין הַנְּבִיאִים, חוּץ מִמֹּשֶׁה רַבֵּינוּ שֶׁהָיָה רוֹאֶה מִתּוֹךְ מַרְאֶה אַחַת (ויקרא רבה א:יד), לְפִיכָךְ אִיסְפַּקְלַרְיָא שֶׁלּוֹ מְאִירָה (יבמות מט, ב) וְרוֹאֶה מַמָּשׁ; וְזֶהוּ שֶׁיִּסֵּד הַפַּיְּיט (שם בסלוק): ״מַחֲזוֹת הַמְצֻחְצָחוֹת בְּתֵשַׁע מַחֲזוֹת״:


**And it was like the appearance of the vision, etc.** [Heb. וּכְמַרְאֵה הַמַרְאֶה.] Nine visions [or “mirrors”] (מַרְאוֹת) are written here through which the prophets perceived. Moses, however, was an exception, for he saw through one vision. Therefore, his mirror reflected clearly and he saw reality more closely. And this is [what is behind] the words of the liturgical poet. The mirrors polished with nine visions.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:3:2)


**בְּבוֹאִי לְשַׁחֵת אֶת הָעִיר.** בְּפָרָשַׁת וְהִתְוִיתָ תָּו, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר שָׁם ״קִרְבוּ פְּקֻדּוֹת הָעִיר״ (יחזקאל ט:א), וְשָׁם (יחזקאל י) פֵּרַשׁ אֶת דּוּגְמַת מֶרְכָּבָה:


**when I came to destroy the city** in the section (9:4) of “and you shall mark a sign,” where it says (verse 1): “Bring near those appointed over the city,” and delineates the form of the Celestial Chariot.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:6:1)


**מְדַבֵּר אֵלַי.** ״מִתְמַלֵּל עִמִּי״ (תרגום יונתן), כָּבוֹד הוּא לוֹמַר כְּלַפֵּי מַעְלָה **מְדַבֵּר**, כְּמוֹ מִתְדַּבֵּר, כְּלוֹמַר: בֵּינוֹ לְבֵין כְּבוֹדוֹ מְדַבֵּר, וְהַקּוֹל בָּא אֵלַי:


**speaking to me** [Heb. מִדַּבֵּר,] speaking to Himself with me. It is respectful to say concerning Heaven מִדַּבֵּר, equivalent to מִתְדַּבֵּר ; i.e., He spoke between Himself and His glory, and the voice came to me.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:7:1)


**וּבְפִגְרֵי מַלְכֵיהֶם.** שֶׁהָיוּ בָתֵּיהֶם סְמוּכִין (לְבֵית הַקְּבָרוֹת), כְּמוֹ שֶׁמָּצִינוּ בַּמְּלָכִים בִּשְׁלֹמֹה (מלכים א י:ה) וּבַעֲתַלְיָהוּ (מלכים ב יא:טו-טז), וּפְעָמִים שֶׁהָיוּ קוֹבְרִין אוֹתָם בְּגַן בֵּיתָם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא מְפָרֵשׁ וְאָמַר **בְּתִתָּם סִפָּם אֶת סִפִּי**. מָצָאתִי:


**and with the corpses of their kings** whose houses were near the Temple, as we find with Solomon and with Athaliah. Sometimes they would bury them in the gardens of their houses, as He explains and says, “... by placing their threshold with My threshold.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:8:1)


**וָאֲכַל.** כְּמוֹ ״וָאֲכַלֶה״ (יחזקאל כא:ג) – וְכִלִּיתִי:


**and I destroyed** [Heb. וָאַכַל,] like וָאֲכַלֶה, [equivalent to] וְכִלִיתִי, and I destroyed.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:9:1)


**עַתָּה.** עַל כָּרְחָם **יְרַחֲקוּ אֶת זְנוּתָם**, שֶׁהִבְדַּלְתִּי לִי הֶיקֵּף גְּבוּל גָּדוֹל סָבִיב:


**Now** perforce, they will distance [from Me] their harlotry, for I have put up, as a separation for Myself, an encompassment of a large border all around.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:10:1)


**אַתָּה בֶן אָדָם הַגֵּד אֶת בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת הַבַּיִת וְיִכָּלְמוּ מֵעֲוֺנוֹתֵיהֶם.** בְּהַרְאוֹתִי לָמוֹ חַסְדִּי שֶׁאֵינִי מוֹאֲסָם בַּעֲוֹנָם, מָצָאתִי:


**You, Son of man, describe etc., that they may be ashamed of their iniquities** when I show them My loving-kindness that I do not reject them for their iniquity.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:10:2)


**וּמָדְדוּ אֶת תָּכְנִית.** וּמָדְדוּ עַל יָדֶיךָ, שֶׁאַתָּה מוֹרֶה אֶת תֹּכֶן חֶשְׁבּוֹן הַבִּנְיָן:


**let them measure its plan** Let them take measurements through you, for you will show them the measurements of the building plan.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:1)


**וְאִם נִכְלְמוּ.** שֶׁיִּתְבַּיְּשׁוּ וְיִכְבְּשׁוּ פְנֵיהֶם **מִכֹּל אֲשֶׁר עָשׂוּ**, אָז **הוֹדַע אוֹתָם** אֶת מַה שֶּׁאֲנִי עָתִיד לַעֲשׂוֹת לָהֶם:


**And if they are ashamed** i.e., [if] they will be embarrassed and hide their faces because of all they did, then let them know what I am destined to do for them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:2)


**צוּרַת הַבַּיִת.** וּגְבוּל הַבִּנְיָן, הֵיאַךְ הוּא מִלְּפָנָיו וְעַד אַחֲרָיו סְדוּרִים לְאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹתָיו:


**the form of the House** the perimeter of the House, from front to back, arranged on [all] four sides.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:3)


**וְתְכוּנָתוֹ.** חֶשְׁבּוֹן תָּאָיו וְלִשְׁכּוֹתָיו:


**and its scheme** the number of its cells and its chambers.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:4)


**וְכָל צוּרוֹתָיו.** כְּרוּבִים וְתִימוֹרִים וְאֵילִים וְאֵילַמִּים, מָצָאתִי:


**and all its forms** the cherubim, palm trees, posts, and halls.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:5)


**וְכָל צוּרוֹתָיו.** בַּתְרָא. לֹא פֹּרַשׁ:


I found: “and all its forms” is unclear.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:11:6)


**וְיִשְׁמְרוּ.** יִלְמְדוּ עִנְיְנֵי הַמִּדּוֹת מִפִּיךָ, שֶׁיֵּדְעוּ לַעֲשׂוֹתָם לְעֵת קֵץ. מָצָאתִי, רְאוּיָה הָיְתָה בִּיאָה שְׁנִיָּה שֶׁל עֶזְרָא כְּבִיאָה רִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁל יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לָבֹא בִּזְרוֹעַ וּבְנֵס, כִּדְדַרְשִׁינַן ״עַד יַעֲבֹר״ (שמות טו:טז), וּבִנְיָן זֶה מֵאָז הָיָה רָאוּי לָהֶם כְּשֶׁעָלוּ מִן הַגּוֹלָה לִגְאֻלַּת עוֹלָם, אֶלָּא שֶׁגָּרַם הַחֵטְא שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה תְּשׁוּבָתָם הוֹגֶנֶת עַל מְנָת שֶׁלֹּא לַחֲטֹא, וְיָצְאוּ בִּרְשׁוּת כּוֹרֶשׁ וּבָנוּ לְעַצְמָן. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים בְּבָבֶל נִכְשְׁלוּ בְּנָכְרִיּוֹת (עזרא ט:א-ב):


**so that they keep** They will learn the matters of the measurements from your mouth so that they will know how to do them at the time of the end. I found [the following]: The second aliyah [to the Holy Land] through Ezra was merited to be like the first entry through Joshua, to come about by force and through a miracle, as expounded (Ber. 4a, Exod. 15:16): “until... pass.” This Building would then have been fit for them as of then, when they emerged from exile, to an everlasting redemption. But [their] sin caused [this not to happen] for their repentance was not suitable, [i.e.,] they did not resolve to stop sinning. [Therefore,] they emerged to freedom [only] through the sanction of Cyrus and his son. Some say that in Babylon they stumbled regarding gentile women.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה מִדּוֹת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** שֶׁמְּפָרֵשׁ לְמַטָּה: **אַרְבַּע עֶשְׂרֵה אֹרֶךְ בְּאַרְבַּע עֶשְׂרֵה רֹחַב**:


**And these are the measurements of the altar** as he explains further (verse 17): “fourteen [cubits in] length by fourteen [cubits in] breadth.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:2)


**בָּאַמּוֹת אַמָּה אַמָּה וָטֹפַח.** בְּאַמָּה בַּת שִׁשָּׁה, שֶׁהִיא אַמָּה וָטֹפַח בְּאַמָּה בַּת חֲמִשָּׁה:


**in cubits; the cubit, one cubit and a handbreadth** with a cubit of six [handbreadths], which is a cubit and a handbreadth, [judging] by a cubit of five [handbreadths].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:3)


**וְחֵיק הָאַמָּה.** אֲבָל הַיְסוֹד שֶׁלּוֹ נִמְדָּד בָּאַמָּה שֶׁל חוֹל, שֶׁהִיא בַּת חֲמִשָּׁה; **חֵיק** לְשׁוֹן חִיקּוּי וּקְבִיעוּת, וְהוּא הַיְסוֹד; **הָאַמָּה** אַמַּת חוֹל, וְלֹא אַמָּה וָטֹפַח, שֶׁהִיא אַמָּה בַּת שִׁשָּׁה; וְכֵן שָׁנִינוּ בִּמְנָחוֹת (מנחות צז.-צז:), שֶׁהַיְסוֹד וְהַסּוֹבֵב וְהַקְּרָנוֹת נִמְדָּדִין בְּאַמָּה בַּת חֲמִשָּׁה:


**but the base one [ordinary] cubit** but its base is measured with an ordinary cubit, which equals five [handbreadths]. The word חֵיק is related to חֵקוּי, permanence, it refers to the one cubit. The cubit is the ordinary cubit, not a cubit and a handbreadth, which is the cubit of six [handbreadths]. So too did we learn in Menahoth (97b) that the base, the circuit, and the horns are measured with a cubit of five [handbreadths].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:4)


**וְחֵיק הָאַמָּה.** זֶה הַיְסוֹד:


**but the base one [ordinary] cubit** This is the yesod.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:5)


**וְאַמָּה רֹחַב.** זֶה הַסּוֹבֵב:


**and such a cubit also for the breadth** This is the circuit.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:6)


**וּגְבוּלָהּ אֶל שְׂפָתָהּ סָבִיב זֶרֶת הָאֶחָד.** אֵלּוּ הַקְּרָנוֹת; זֶרֶת אֶחָד מֵאֶמְצָעִיתוֹ מוֹדְדוֹ זֶרֶת לְכָל רוּחַ, הֲרֵי אַמָּה עַל אַמָּה:


**and [likewise on] its border, [going] one span to its edge all around** These are the horns, [each of which measured] one span from its center; and they measured a span to each side, totaling a cubit by a cubit.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:7)


**וְזֶה גַּב הַמִּזְבֵּחַ.** וּבְאַמָּה זוֹ הָיָה נִמְדָּד גַּב מִזְבַּח הַזָּהָב ״אַמָּה אָרְכּוֹ וְאַמָּה רָחְבּוֹ רָבוּעַ״ (שמות לז:כה), כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּעֵירוּבִין, וּבִמְנָחוֹת (דף צז:) מְפֹרָשׁ יוֹתֵר לְפִי הַחֶשְׁבּוֹן: חֵיק הָאַמָּה בְּגוֹבְהָהּ עָלָה אַמָּה וְאַמָּה רֹחַב, כָּנְסָה כְּנִיסַת הַסּוֹבֵב אַמָּה בַּת חֲמִשָּׁה, אֲבָל עֲלִיָּתוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ עָלָה חָמֵשׁ וְכָנַס אַמָּה אֵינוֹ בְּאַמָּה בַּת חֲמִשָּׁה:


**and equally so the very top of the altar** And with this cubit the top of the golden altar was measured [to be] a cubit in length and a cubit in breadth [forming a] square. So it is explained in Erubin (4a). In Menahoth (97b) it is explained more fully in terms of the computation: The base was a cubit in height. It extended upward a cubit, and a cubit was its breadth. The recess of the circuit was recessed a cubit of five [handbreadths], but its height, of which we learned (Middoth 3:1): “It extended upward 5 [cubits] and was recessed a cubit” was not [measured] with a cubit of five [handbreadths].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:13:8)


**וּגְבוּלָהּ אֶל שְׂפָתָהּ.** הַקְּרָנוֹת, בֵּין לְרָחְבָּן בֵּין לְגָבְהָן, בְּאַמָּה בַּת חֲמִשָּׁה:


**and [likewise on] its border, [going] one span to its edge** The horns, both in their breadth and in their height, were [measured] with a cubit of five [handbreadths].



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:1)


**וּמֵחֵיק הָאָרֶץ.** מִן הַיְסוֹד, מִקַּרְקַע הָאָרֶץ:


**And from the base on the ground** [Heb. וּמֵחֵיק הָאָרֶץ. From the “yesod,” from the floor of the earth.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:2)


**עַד הָעֲזָרָה הַתַּחְתּוֹנָה.** עַד גַּב מַלְבֵּן הָרִאשׁוֹן:


**to the lower ledge** [Heb. הָעֲזָרָה, lit. court.] Until the top of the first frame.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:3)


**שְׁתַּיִם אַמּוֹת.** וְלֹא הָיָה כֵן בְּבֵית עוֹלָמִים, שֶׁשָּׁם שָׁנִינוּ (זבחים נד.): מֵבִיא מַלְבֵּן שֶׁל שְׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם עַל שְׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם, וְגָבְהוֹ אַמָּה; וְכָאן גָּבְהוֹ **שְׁתַּיִם**:


**two cubits** It was not the same in the [Second] Temple, for there, we learned (Zeb. 54a): “They bring a frame of 32 [cubits] by 32 [cubits], whose height is a cubit,” whereas here its height is two cubits.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:4)


**וְרֹחַב אַמָּה אֶחָת.** כְּשֶׁהִנִּיחַ מַלְבֵּן הַשֵּׁנִי עָלָיו, הָיָה מַלְבֵּן הַשֵּׁנִי שְׁלֹשִׁים עַל שְׁלֹשִׁים (זבחים נד.), נִמְצָאת כְּנִיסָה לוֹ סָבִיב סָבִיב **רֹחַב אַמָּה** וְהוּא הַיְסוֹד, הֲרֵי עָלָה שְׁתַּיִם וְכָנַס אַמָּה וְהוּא הַיְסוֹד; וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם **עֲזָרָה**: ״מְסַתְּמְתָא״, לְשׁוֹן רִצְפָּה, שֶׁהָיָה מְמַלֵּא אֶת הַמַּלְבֵּן חֲלוּקֵי אֲבָנִים מִזֶּפֶת וְסִיד וְקוּנְיָא וּמְמַחֶה וְשׁוֹפֵךְ וּמַחְלִיקוֹ לְמַעְלָה (זבחים נד.), וַהֲרֵי הוּא כְּמִין רִצְפָּה:


**and the breadth one cubit** when one places the second frame upon it. The second frame was thirty by thirty, the result being that it [the bottom, 32 by 32 cubit frame] had a recess around it a cubit wide which was the base. Jonathan renders: עֲזָרָה as מסְתַּמְתָא, a word for floor, [and it was called so] because one would fill the frame with smooth stones, pitch, lime, and molten lead, mix it and pour it out and smooth it out on top, and behold, it would be a kind of floor.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:5)


**וּמֵהָעֲזָרָה הַקְּטַנָּה עַד הָעֲזָרָה הַגְּדוֹלָה אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת.** מִגַּג מַלְבֵּן הַתַּחְתּוֹן עַד גַּג מַלְבֵּן שֶׁעָלָיו, אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת גֹּבַהּ:


**and from the smaller ledge to the greater ledge four cubits** From the top of the lower frame until the top of the frame that was upon it was a height of four cubits.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:14:6)


**וְרֹחַב הָאַמָּה.** הוּא כְּנִיסַת הַסּוֹבֵב, עָלָה אַרְבָּעָה וְכָנַס אַמָּה, זֶהוּ סוֹבֵב. וְהַתַּחְתּוֹן קוֹרֵא **קְטַנָּה** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין גָּבְהוֹ אֶלָּא שְׁתַּיִם, וְהָעֶלְיוֹן גָּבְהוֹ **אַרְבַּע**:


**and the breadth, one cubit** This is the amount of the recess of the circuit. It went up four cubits and went in one cubit. [In the second Temple, it went up five cubits and went in one.] And this is the “circuit.” The lower one is called smaller because it is only two [cubits] high, whereas the upper one is four.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:15:1)


**וְהַהַרְאֵל.** הוּא גֹּבַהּ גַּג הַמִּזְבֵּחַ מִן הַסּוֹבֵב וּלְמַעְלָה; וּבְבַיִת עוֹלָמִים לֹא הָיָה אֶלָּא שָׁלֹשׁ (זבחים ס.); וְיוֹנָתָן אַף הוּא תִּרְגֵּם כָּאן: ״וּמַדְבְּחָא אַרְבַּע אַמִּין״:


**And the altar hearth** The height of the top of the altar, from the circuit and above; and in the [Second] Temple it was only three [cubits]. Jonathan, too, renders: And the altar was four cubits.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:16:1)


**וְהָאֲרִיאֵל שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה אֹרֶךְ.** מְקוֹם הַמַּעֲרָכָה הָיָה עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע עַל עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע (מדות ג:א), וְזֶהוּ **שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה בִּשְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה**:


**And the altar hearth shall be twelve [cubits in] length** The [entire] place for the woodpile was twenty-four [cubits] by twenty-four [cubits] and this [section] was twelve by twelve.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:16:2)


**אֶל אַרְבַּעַת רְבָעָיו.** שֶׁמֵּאֶמְצָעִיתוֹ הוּא מוֹדֵד שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה לְכָל רוּחַ, הֲרֵי עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע עַל עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע. כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בִּזְבָחִים (נט:). וְכֵן שָׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (ג:א): הַמִּזְבֵּחַ הָיָה שְׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם עַל שְׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁתַּיִם, עָלָה אַמָּה וְכָנַס אַמָּה, זֶה הַיְסוֹד, נִמְצָא שְׁלֹשִׁים עַל שְׁלֹשִׁים. עָלָה חָמֵשׁ וְכָנַס אַמָּה, זֶה הַסּוֹבֵב, נִמְצָא עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁמוֹנֶה עַל עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁמוֹנֶה. מְקוֹם הַקְּרָנוֹת אַמָּה מִזֶּה וְאַמָּה מִזֶּה, נִמְצָא עֶשְׂרִים וְשֵׁשׁ עַל עֶשְׂרִים וְשֵׁשׁ. מְקוֹם הִלּוּךְ רַגְלֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים אַמָּה מִזֶּה וְאַמָּה מִזֶּה, נִמְצָא עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע עַל עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע מְקוֹם הַמַּעֲרָכָה:


**to [each of] its four sides** [i.e.,] he measured twelve [cubits] from its center to every side. Behold [it was] 24 [cubits] by 24 [cubits]. So is it clear from Zebahim (61b), and so have we learned in Tractate Middoth (3:1): The altar [of the second Temple] was 32 by 32 [cubits]. It extended upward a cubit and receded a cubitforming the baseleaving it 30 by 30 [cubits]. It extended upward five [cubits] and receded a cubitthis is the circuitleaving 28 by 28 [cubits]. The area for the horns was a cubit here and a cubit there, leaving 26 by 26 [cubits]. The area for the priests to walk was a cubit here and a cubit there, leaving 24 by 24 [cubits]the place for the woodpiles.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:17:1)


**וְהָעֲזָרָה אַרְבַּע עֶשְׂרֵה אֹרֶךְ.** גַּג הַמִּזְבֵּחַ עִם מְקוֹם הַקְּרָנוֹת וְעִם מְקוֹם רַגְלֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁמֹנֶה עַל עֶשְׂרִים וּשְׁמֹנֶה, וְאַרְבַּע עֶשְׂרֵה הָאֲמוּרִים כָּאן – מֵאֶמְצָעִיתוֹ מוֹדֵד, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְפֹרָשׁ: **אֶל אַרְבַּעַת רְבָעֶיהָ**:


**And the ledge, fourteen [cubits in] length** The top of the altar, including the area for the horns and including the area for the feet of the priests, was 28 by 28 [cubits], and the 14 mentioned here were measured from its center, as is explained by “to its four sides.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:17:2)


**וְהַגְּבוּל סָבִיב אוֹתָהּ.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״וְגַדְנְפָא״, וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּהוּ עַל הַקְּרָנוֹת (זבחים סב:):


**and the boundary around it** Jonathan renders: and the rim, but our Sages explained it as referring to the horns.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:17:3)


**חֲצִי הָאַמָּה.** מֵאֶמְצָעִיתוֹ מוֹדֵד; נִמְצָא אַמָּה עַל אַמָּה:


**half a cubit** He measures from its center; hence it was a cubit by a cubit.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:17:4)


**וְהַחֵיק לָהּ אַמָּה.** זוֹ כְּנִיסַת הַיְסוֹד:


**and its base a cubit** This is the recession of the base.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:17:5)


**וּמַעֲלֹתֵהוּ פְּנוֹת קָדִים.** וְכֶבֶשׁ שֶׁעוֹלִים בּוֹ הָיָה בַּדָּרוֹם, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהֵא הָעוֹלֶה בּוֹ פּוֹנֶה לַמִּזְרָח; שֶׁכָּל הַפְּנִיּוֹת הָיוּ דֶּרֶךְ יָמִין; וּכְשֶׁאַתָּה נוֹתֵן הַכֶּבֶשׁ בַּדָּרוֹם, הָעוֹלֶה בּוֹ וּפוֹנֶה דֶּרֶךְ יָמִין פּוֹנֶה לַמִּזְרָח:


**and its ascents will look towards the east** The ramp upon which they ascended was in the south in order that the one who ascended it would face east, for all the [priests’] turns were made toward the right, and when you place the ramp in the south, the one who ascends it and turns right faces east.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:19:1)


**הַכֹּהֲנִים הַלְוִיִּם.** אֲשֶׁר מִשֵּׁבֶט לֵוִי:


**the priests, the Levites** [i.e.,] who are of the tribe of Levi.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:19:2)


**מִזֶּרַע צָדוֹק.** לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל שֶׁשִּׁמֵּשׁ רִאשׁוֹן בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ בִּימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה (מלכים א ב:כז, לה), נִקְרְאוּ עַל שְׁמוֹ:


**who are of the seed of Zadok** Since he was the High Priest who was the first to serve in the Temple, in the days of Solomon, they are [all] called by his name.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:20:1)


**עַל אַרְבַּע קַרְנוֹתָיו.** שֶׁל מִזְבֵּחַ:


**on its four horns** [i.e., the four horns] of the altar.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:20:2)


**פִּנּוֹת הָעֲזָרָה.** גַּג הַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


**corners of the uppermost ledge** the top of the altar.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:20:3)


**וְכִפַּרְתָּהוּ.** תְּקַנַּח אוֹתוֹ מִדֶּרֶךְ חוֹל שֶׁבּוֹ, לְהַכְנִיסוֹ בִּקְדֻשָּׁה, לִהְיוֹתוֹ רָאוּי לְכַפֵּר עָלָיו מִזֶּה וּלְהַלָּן:


**and make it fit for atonement** You shall “wipe” it of its ordinariness to initiate it into sanctity, so that it will befit for [providing] atonement from then on.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:21:1)


**בְּמִפְקַד הַבַּיִת.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״בַּאֲתַר דַּחֲזִי לְבֵיתָא״, כְּלוֹמַר: בְּמָקוֹם קָדוֹשׁ; וְלָשׁוֹן **בְּמִפְקַד הַבַּיִת** – מָקוֹם שֶׁהַבַּיִת כָּלֶה, כְּמוֹ ״לֹא נִפְקַד מִמֶּנּוּ אִישׁ״ (במדבר לא:מט). וְהוּא פַּר הַמִּלּוּאִים, כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁעָשׂוּ בַּמִּשְׁכָּן (ויקרא ח:ב), וְנִשְׂרַף אוֹתוֹ פַּר וְלֹא נֶאֱכַל; וְכֵן דָּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בִּמְנָחוֹת (מה.): מִלּוּאִים הִקְרִיבוּ בִּימֵי עֶזְרָא כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהִקְרִיבוּ בִּימֵי מֹשֶׁה:


**at the end of the House** [Heb. בְּמִפְקַד.] Jonathan renders: in a place where he sees the House, i.e., in a holy place. בְּמִפְקַד הַבַּיִת means a place where the House terminates, as in (Num. 32:49): “and not one man of us is terminated (נִפְקַד).” This [bull] is the bull of investiture, [used] in the manner that they [used it] in the Tabernacle, and that bull was burnt and not eaten. And our Sages expounded so in Menahoth (45a): They sacrificed investiture offerings in Ezra’s days just as they sacrificed in Moses’ days.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:22:1)


**תַּקְרִיב שְׂעִיר עִזִּים.** זֶה לֹא הָיָה בַּמִּשְׁכָּן; וּבַמִּלּוּאִים שֶׁל לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא – כָּךְ הֵם עַל פִּי הַדִּבּוּר:


**you shall offer a he-goat** This was not [done] in the Tabernacle [of Moses’ time], but the investiture of the future will be [done] so by Divine ordinance.

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:23:1

**מֵחַטֵּא.** מִלְּטַהֵר:


**the purification** [Heb. מֵחַטֵא,] from purifying.

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:25:1)


**תְּמִימִים יַעֲשׂוּ.** לְעוֹלָה:


**without blemish shall they make** (the burnt offering).

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 43:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/43:27:1)


**וְרָצִאתִי.** לְשׁוֹן רָצוֹן:


**and I will accept you with satisfaction** [Heb. וְרָצִאתִי,] from רָצוֹן will.



## Chapter 44


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:2:1)


**סָגוּר יִהְיֶה.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ הֶעֱמִידוּ הַמִּקְרָא הַזֶּה בְּפִשְׁפָּשׁ הַדְּרוֹמִי שֶׁהָיָה לְשַׁעַר הַהֵיכָל; פִּשְׁפָּשׁ – פֶּתַח קָטָן. כָּךְ שָׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (ד, ב): שְׁנֵי פִשְׁפָּשִׁין הָיוּ לוֹ לַשַּׁעַר הַגָּדוֹל, אֶחָד בְּדָרוֹם וְאֶחָד בְּצָפוֹן; שֶׁבְּדָרוֹם – לֹא נִכְנַס בּוֹ אָדָם מֵעוֹלָם, עָלָיו מְפֹרָשׁ עַל יְדֵי יְחֶזְקֵאל: **הַשַּׁעַר הַזֶּה יִהְיֶה סָגוּר**:


**shall be closed** Our Rabbis interpreted this verse as referring to the southern wicket, for the gate of the Heichal had a wicket, a small entrance. So we learned in Tractate Middoth (4:2): The Great Gate had two wickets, one in the south and one in the north. No one ever entered the one in the south, of which Ezekiel says, “This gate shall be closed.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:2:2)


**בָּא בוֹ.** לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא:


**comes through it** in the future.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:1)


**אֶת הַנָּשִׂיא נָשִׂיא הוּא וְגוֹ׳.** אֶת הַנָּשִׂיא אֲנִי נוֹתֵן רְשׁוּת, נָשִׂיא הוּא וְחָשׁוּב, וְאֵין דַּרְכּוֹ לֵישֵׁב וְלֶאֱכוֹל עִם שְׁאָר הַכֹּהֲנִים בַּלְּשָׁכוֹת:


**The prince [I permit]; he is a prince, etc.** The prince I give permission. He is a prince, and is esteemed, and it is not fitting for him to sit and eat with the rest of the priests in the chambers.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:2)


**הוּא יֵשֶׁב בּוֹ לֶאֱכֹל לֶחֶם.** בְּתוֹךְ חֲלָלוֹ:


**he shall sit therein** in its space.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:3)


**מִדֶּרֶךְ אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר יָבוֹא.** כְּשֶׁהוּא נִכְנָס לְתוֹךְ הֶחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִית כְּדֵי לִיכָּנֵס לְשַׁעַר קָטָן זֶה לֶאֱכוֹל בּוֹ, דֶּרֶךְ **אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר** הָעֲזָרָה שֶׁבְּמִזְרָח יִכָּנֵס לְתוֹךְ הָעֲזָרָה, וּבוֹ בַּדֶּרֶךְ יָשׁוּב כְּשֶׁיָּשׁוּב **יֵצֵא** בְּאוֹתוֹ הַדֶּרֶךְ שֶׁנִּכְנַס. וּלְפִי שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר לְמַטָּה (יחזקאל מו:ט) בְּעִנְיָן ״וּבְבֹא עַם הָאָרֶץ לִפְנֵי ה׳ בַּמּוֹעֲדִים הַבָּא דֶּרֶךְ וְגוֹ׳״, שֶׁצִּוָּה לַעֲשׂוֹת הָעֲזָרָה קַפַּנְדַּרְיָא – לְפִי שֶׁאוֹתָהּ בִּיאָה לִרְאִיַּת פָּנִים וְצָרִיךְ לְהֵרָאוֹת יָפֶה, הֻצְרַךְ לוֹמַר כָּאן בְּבִיאוֹת שֶׁל כָּל יְמוֹת הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲשֶׂנָּה קַפַּנְדַּרְיָא (ברכות ט,ה), לְפִי שֶׁאֵין בִּיאָה זוֹ לִרְאִיַּת פָּנִים כְּמוֹ שֶׁל רְגָלִים; וְכֵן הֵעִיד עוֹד לְמַטָּה בָּעִנְיָן פְּעָמִים רַבּוֹת:


**by way of the hall of the gate he shall come** When he enters the Inner Court in order to enter this small gate to eat therein, he shall enter by way of the hall of the Court’s eastern gate and leave the same way. Now since it states below (46:9), concerning: “But when the people of the land come before the Lord on the Festivals, he who enters by way of, etc.” Scripture commanded making the Court a shortcut since that entry was [made] for the sake of appearing [before God]. He must therefore be well seen [i.e., throughout the entire Court]. It was necessary to specify here concerning visits during the rest of the year that he should not make it a shortcut because this visit is not for the sake of appearing, as is that of the Pilgrimage Festivals. This matter is corroborated many more times in this account.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:4)


מָצָאתִי. **אֶת הַנָּשִׂיא.** כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל **נָשִׂיא הוּא**, וּמֵחֲשִׁיבוּתוֹ יְהֵא רַשַּׁאי **לֶאֱכוֹל** בָּשָׂר וְ**לֶחֶם** קָדָשִׁים **בּוֹ** אוֹתוֹ שַׁעַר שֶׁפּוֹתְחִין לוֹ בִּשְׁעַת אֲכִילָתוֹ:


**The prince** The High Priest is a prince, and because of his importance he will be permitted to eat the meat and bread of hallowed status in that gate, which is opened for him at the time of his eating.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:5)


**מִדֶּרֶךְ אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר יָבוֹא.** דֶּרֶךְ אוּלָם שַׁעַר מִזְרָחִי יִכָּנֵס לָעֲזָרָה, וְיָבוֹא עַד אוֹתוֹ פִּשְׁפָּשׁ **לֶאֱכוֹל** בּוֹ לַחְמוֹ:


**by way of the hall of the gate he shall come** By way of the hall of the Eastern Gate he shall enter the Court and come up to that wicket to eat his bread therein.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:3:6)


**וּמִדַּרְכּוֹ יֵצֵא.** שֶׁאֵין זֶה רָאוּי לְקַפַּנְדַּרְיָא כִּשְׁאָר שְׁעָרִים, וְכָל הַיּוֹם שֶׁלֹּא בִּשְׁעַת אֲכִילַת הַנָּשִׂיא יִהְיֶה סָגוּר:


**and by the same way he shall leave** For this is not fit for a shortcut, as are the rest of the gates. Throughout the day, when not during the time of the prince’s eating, it shall be closed.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:7:1)


**בְּנֵי נֵכָר.** שֶׁנִּתְנַכְּרוּ מַעֲשָׂיו לְאָבִיו שֶׁבַּשָּׁמַיִם, זֶהוּ מְשׁוּמָּד; וְהֵם **עַרְלֵי לֵב** (זבחים כב:):


**aliens** [lit. sons of an alien.] [Sons of] one whose deeds have become alienated from his Father in Heaven; this is an apostate who worships idols. They are the ones “of uncircumcised heart.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:7:2)


**וְעַרְלֵי בָשָׂר.** כֹּהֵן שֶׁמֵּתוּ אֶחָיו מֵחֲמַת מִילָה:


**and of uncircumcised flesh** [a] priest whose brothers died because of circumcision.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:8:1)


**וַתְּשִׂימוּן לְשֹׁמְרֵי מִשְׁמַרְתִּי, בְּמִקְדָּשִׁי לָכֶם.** תְּשִׂימוּן לָכֶם, לְדַעְתְּכֶם, בְּמִקְדָּשִׁי אֲנָשִׁים רוֹצְחִים שֶׁאֵינָם רְאוּיִין לִהְיוֹת **שׁוֹמְרֵי מִשְׁמַרְתִּי**:


**And you appointed keepers of My watch in My Sanctuary** You knowingly appointed in My Sanctuary, people who are not fit to be keepers of My watch.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:10:1)


**כִּי אִם הַלְוִיִּם אֲשֶׁר רָחֲקוּ וְגוֹ׳.** כִּי אִם כֹּה תַּעֲשׂוּ לָהֶם, הַלְוִיִּם **אֲשֶׁר רָחֲקוּ מֵעָלַי** וְנַעֲשׂוּ מְשׁוּמָּדִים לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, וְעַכְשָׁיו חָזְרוּ בָהֶם:


**Only the Levites who distanced themselves** Only thus shall you do to the Levites who distanced [themselves] from Me and became idolatrous priests and have now repented...


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:10:2)


**וְנָשְׂאוּ עֲוֹנָם.** מִגֶּשֶׁת עוֹד אֶל מִזְבְּחִי, אֶל פְּקֻדּוֹת שֶׁל שַׁעֲרֵי הַבַּיִת, פְּקֻדּוֹת קוֹמַנְדִישָׁ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז, שׁוֹמְרִים וְשׁוֹטְרִים יִהְיוּ:


**and they shall bear their iniquity** from ever approaching My altar again, but they may be appointed to the gates of the House (comandises in Old French, orders). They shall be watchmen and gatekeepers.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:11:1)


**הֵמָּה יִשְׁחֲטוּ אֶת הָעוֹלָה.** עֲבוֹדָה הַכְּשֵׁרָה בְּזָרִים, וּבַעֲבָדִים וּבְנָשִׁים (זבחים לא:):


**they shall slaughter the burnt offering** a service that is valid [if performed] by non priests, slaves, or women.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:12:1)


**לְמִכְשׁוֹל עָוֹן.** כּוֹמְרִים לַעֲבוֹדָה זָרָה, לְהַמְשִׁיכָם לְכָךְ:


**as a stumbling block of iniquity** priests of idolatry, attracting them to it.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:15:1)


**וְהַכֹּהֲנִים הַלְוִיִּם.** מִשֵּׁבֶט לֵוִי:


**But the priests, the Levites** [i.e., the priests, who are] of the tribe of Levi.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:15:2)


**בְּנֵי צָדוֹק.** לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל שֶׁשִּׁמֵּשׁ רִאשׁוֹן בַּמִּקְדָּשׁ בִּימֵי שְׁלֹמֹה, נִקְרְאוּ עַל שְׁמוֹ:


**the sons of Zadok** Since he was the High Priest who served as the first one in the Sanctuary of Solomon’s days, they are called by his name.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:15:3)


**בִּתְעוֹת.** קְנַאיִנְשַׁאַרְ״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**when...went astray** [Heb. בִּתְעוֹת,] quand ils erraient, in Fr., when they strayed.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:17:1)


**אֶל שַׁעֲרֵי הֶחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִית.** לִפְנַי וְלִפְנִים בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים:


**when they enter the gates of the Inner Court** the Inner Sanctum on the Day of Atonement.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:17:2)


**וְלֹא יַעֲלֶה עֲלֵיהֶם צֶמֶר.** תְּכֵלֶת שֶׁהָיָה בַּמְּעִיל וּבָאַבְנֵט (שמות לט:כד, כט) לֹא יִלְבָּשֶׁנּוּ בְּיוֹם הַכִּפּוּרִים בַּעֲבוֹדַת פְּנִים:


**and no wool shall be upon them** They shall not wear the blue wool that was in the robe and girdle on the Day of Atonement during the service in the Inner Court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:17:3)


**וָבָיְתָה.** וְלִפְנִים:



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:18:1)


**פַּאֲרֵי.** קַאפִּיֵיל״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**hats** [Heb. פַאֲרֵי.] c(h)apelas in O.F., hats, head dress.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:18:2)


**לֹא יַחְגְּרוּ בַּיָּזַע.** שָׁנִינוּ בִּבְרַיְתָא: אֵין חוֹגְרִין בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁמְּזִיעִין, לֹא לְמַעְלָה מֵאֲצִילֵיהֶם וְלֹא לְמַטָּה מִמָּתְנֵיהֶם, שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם זֵיעָה (זבחים יח:). אַנְלַאשׁוֹיִי״רָא בְּלַעַ״ז. לָשׁוֹן אַחֵר: אָסְרָה הַתּוֹרָה לַכֹּהֲנִים מַלְבּוּשֵׁי הַצֶּמֶר, כִּי הַצֶּמֶר מֵזִיעַ הַגּוּף וּמֵזִיעַ אֶת הַבָּשָׂר:


**they shall not gird themselves in a place that sweats** We learned in a baraitha (Zeb. 18b, 19a): They do not gird themselves in a place where they sweat, neither above their elbows nor below their loins, which is a place of sweat, en la suor in O.F., on (whatever causes) sweat. Another explanation: The Torah prohibited the priests from wearing woolen raiment because wool causes the body to sweat (not found in some editions).



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:19:1)


**אֶל הֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה.** לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה מְדַבֵּר בַּהֵיכָל לִפְנַי וְלִפְנִים וּקְרָאָם ״חָצֵר הַפְּנִימִית״ (יחזקאל מד:יז), וְאֶצְלָם יֵשׁ לוֹ לִקְרוֹת עֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל חָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה, לְכָךְ הֻצְרַךְ לִכְפּוֹל שְׁתֵּי פְּעָמִים, לוֹמַר שֶׁעַל עֶזְרַת נָשִׁים הוּא אוֹמֵר, מָקוֹם שֶׁכָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל נִכְנָסִין:


**into the Outer Court, into the Outer Court** Since he was speaking of the Heichal and the Inner Sanctum, and he called them the “Inner Court,” and in relation to them, he should call the Israelites’ Court an “Outer Court,” he therefore had to double it twice to say that he is speaking of the Men’s Court, the area that all Israel enter.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:19:2)


**וְהִנִּיחוּ אוֹתָם בְּלִשְׁכוֹת הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר מֹשֶׁה רַבֵּינוּ עָלָיו הַשָּׁלוֹם (ויקרא טז:כג) ״וּפָשַׁט אֶת בִּגְדֵי הַבַּד אֲשֶׁר לָבַשׁ וְהִנִּיחָם שָׁם״:


**and place them in chambers belonging to the Sanctuary** as stated by our master Moses, may he rest in peace (Lev. 16:23): “after that, he shall take off the linen garments which he had put on, etc., and he shall leave them there.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:19:3)


**וְלֹא יְקַדְּשׁוּ אֶת הָעָם.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְלָא יִתְעָרְבוּן עִם עַמָּא בִּלְבוּשֵׁיהוֹן״, כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא יִגְּעוּ אֶל הָעָם **בְּבִגְדֵיהֶם** הַקְּדוֹשִׁים, שֶׁאֵין בִּגְדֵי חוֹל טְהוֹרִין אֵצֶל בִּגְדֵי הַקֹּדֶשׁ (ראה רש״י יחזקאל מב:יד):


**and they shall not sanctify the people** [Heb. וְלֹא יְקַדְשׁוּ אֶתהָעָם, lit. they shall not sanctify the people.] Jonathan renders: and they shall not mingle with the people in their garments, [i.e.,] they shall not touch the people with their holy garments, for ordinary garments are not ritually clean as regards [contaminating] holy garments.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:20:1)


**וְרֹאשָׁם לֹא יְגַלֵּחוּ.** לְהַעֲבִיר אֶת כָּל הַשֵּׂעָר:


**And [the hair of] their heads they are not to shave** to remove all the hair.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:20:2)


**וּפֶרַע לֹא יְשַׁלֵּחוּ.** גִּדּוּל שֵׂעָר לֹא יַרְבּוּ:


**but also not to let it grow wild** They may not let their hair grow very long.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:20:3)


**כָּסוֹם יִכְסְמוּ.** כַּכֻּסֶּמֶת הַזּוֹ, שֶׁסְּדוּרִין בַּשִּׁבֹּלֶת רֹאשׁוֹ שֶׁל זֶה לְצַד עִקָּרוֹ שֶׁל זֶה; כָּךְ שָׁמַעְתִּי מִשְּׁמוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי מְנַחֵם זַצַ״ל. וְיִתָּכֵן לְפָרְשׁוֹ לְשׁוֹן מִדַּת דָּבָר הַשָּׁוֶה, לֹא גְזִיזַת רֹאשׁ וְלֹא גִּדּוּל שֵׂעָר, אֶלָּא בְּמִדָּה שָׁוָה; אמולי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**they must be careful to trim** [Heb. כָּסוּם יִכְסְמוּ, (to cut the hair so that it appears)] like spelt (כֻּסֶמֶת), which is arranged on the ear [with] the end of one beside the root of another. So I heard in the name of Rabbi Menahem of blessed memory. It is possible to explain it as an expression for a measure of a medium thing, neither shearing [all the hair of] the head nor letting the hair grow long, but a medium amount, amo(d)ler in O.F., to cut to medium length.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:21:1)


**בְּבוֹאָם אֶל הֶחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִית.** אֶל הַהֵיכָל:


**when they come into the Inner Court** to the Heichal.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:22:1)


**כִּי אִם בְּתוּלוֹת.** יִקְּחוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים הַגְּדוֹלִים, אֲבָל יֵשׁ מִן הַכֹּהֲנִים אֲשֶׁר יִקְּחוּ אֶת הָאַלְמָנָה, כְּגוֹן הֶדְיוֹטִין, וְזֶהוּ **מִכֹּהֵן יִקָּחוּ** – יֵשׁ מִן הַכֹּהֵן שֶׁמּוּתָּרִין בְּאַלְמָנָה, מָצָאתִי:


**but...virgins** may the High Priests take. But there are some priests who may take a widow, namely, the ordinary ones, and this is the meaning of “some of the priests may marry”; there are some priests who are permitted to marry a widow.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:22:2)


**אֲשֶׁר תִּהְיֶה אַלְמָנָה.** גְּמוּרָה, פְּרָט לִגְרוּשָׁה וַחֲלוּצָה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁפְּנוּיָה הִיא, אֲסוּרָה אַף לְהֶדְיוֹט (קדושין עח.):


**who is only a widow** A real [widow], excluding a divorcee and a woman upon whom the rite of chalitzah was performed; although she is unmarried, she is forbidden even for an ordinary [priest].



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:26:1)


**וְאַחֲרֵי טָהֳרָתוֹ.** אַחַר פְּרִישָׁתוֹ מִן הַמֵּת; כָּךְ שְׁנוּיָה בְּמוֹעֵד קָטָן (מועד קטן טו:):


**And after his purification** and after he has separated from the corpse. So was it taught in Moed Katan (15b).



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:27:1)


**וּבְיוֹם בֹּאוֹ.** תְּחִלָּה **אֶל הַקֹּדֶשׁ** לְהִתְחַנֵּךְ לַעֲבוֹדָה. **יַקְרִיב חַטָּאתוֹ** זוֹ עֲשִׂירִית הָאֵיפָה שֶׁלּוֹ; כָּךְ שְׁנוּיָה בְּמוֹעֵד קָטָן (טו, ב – טז, א), שֶׁכֹּהֵן הֶדְיוֹט טָעוּן עֲשִׂירִית הָאֵיפָה בְּיוֹם חִנּוּכוֹ, כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״זֶה קָרְבַּן אַהֲרֹן וּבָנָיו וְגוֹ׳״ (ויקרא ו:יג), וּכְמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מְנָחוֹת (עח, א):


**And on the day that he enters** into the Sanctuary for the first time to initiate himself into the service, he shall offer up his sin-offering; this is his one tenth of an ephah [of flour]. In Moed Katan (16a) the following is taught: the regular priest requires one tenth of an ephah on the day of his initiation, as it is stated (Lev. 6:13): “This is the offering of Aaron and his sons, etc.” and as is explained in Tractate Menachoth (51b).



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:28:1)


**וְהָיְתָה לָהֶם.** הַכְּהֻנָּה **לְנַחֲלָה**:


**It shall be to them** the priesthood, for an inheritance.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:29:1)


**חֵרֶם.** לְשׁוֹן קֹדֶשׁ; וְכֵן כָּל לְשׁוֹן חֶרְמָה שֶׁהוּא עַל הַקֹּדֶשׁ (ויקרא כז:כח):


**and everything that is holy** [Heb. חֶרֶם,] an expression of sanctity, and so is every expression of חֶרֶם [when used] in the context of hallowed things.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:30:1)


**לְהָנִיחַ.** אַפּוֹשִׁי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז, כְּמוֹ ״וַיָּנַח בְּכָל גְּבוּל מִצְרָיִם״ (שמות י:יד):


**to bring enduring blessings into your home** [Heb. לְהָנִיחַ,] aposer on O.F., (to cause) to rest, settle, as in (Exod. 10:14): “and it rested (וֳיָנָח) throughout all the borders of Egypt.”



### Verse 31

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 44:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/44:31:1)


**כָּל נְבֵלָה וּטְרֵפָה וְגוֹ׳.** לְפִי שֶׁהֻתְּרָה לָהֶם מְלִיקַת חַטַּאת הָעוֹף, שֶׁהִיא נְבֵלָה וּטְרֵפָה, הֻצְרַךְ לְהַזְהִירָם עַל שְׁאָר נְבֵלוֹת וּטְרֵפוֹת; כָּךְ פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (מנחות מה.):


**Anything that has died of itself or is fatally wounded, etc.** Since nipping the neck of the bird sin- offering was permitted, which is [tantamount to] an animal that died of itself or was fatally wounded [since it is not the normal method of slaughter], he had to warn them concerning [eating] other creatures that died of themselves or were fatally wounded. So our Sages explain.



## Chapter 45


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:1:1)


**וּבְהַפִּילְכֶם אֶת הָאָרֶץ בְּנַחֲלָה.** שֶׁעֲתִידִים לַחֲלוֹק אֶת אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לִשְׁנֵים עָשָׂר רְצוּעוֹת; לֹא כַּחֲלוּקָה הָרִאשׁוֹנָה שֶׁהָיְתָה לָרַב כְּפִי מִנְיָנוֹ וְלַמְּעַט כְּפִי פְּקוּדָיו (במדבר כו:נד), וְהָיוּ שְׁנֵי שְׁבָטִים אוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה בִּרְצוּעָה אַחַת, עַכְשָׁיו הַחֲלָקִים שָׁוִים וְכְּשׁוּרוֹת הַכֶּרֶם, מִפְּאַת יָם עַד פְּאַת קָדִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר (יחזקאל מח):


**And when you divide the land by lot** For they are destined to divide the land of Israel into twelve strips, not like the original division, in which the large [tribe] had [land] according to its number and the small [tribe] according to its number, and there were two or three tribes on one strip. Now the portions are equal and they are like rows in a vineyard, from the western side to the eastern side, as delineated at the end of the Book.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:1:2)


**תְּרוּמָה לַה׳.** לִבְנוֹת בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ הַזֶּה בּוֹ:


**an offering to the Lord** in which to build this Temple.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:2:1)


**יִהְיֶה מִזֶּה.** מִן הַתְּרוּמָה הַזּוֹ יִהְיֶה לְצֹרֶךְ **הַקֹּדֶשׁ** לְהַר הַבַּיִת **חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת** קָנִים, וְהַמּוֹתָר לְבָתִּים לַכֹּהֲנִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר (יחזקאל מח:ח-כא):


**From this shall be** From this offering, there shall be for the needs of the Sanctuary: five hundred rods for the Temple Mount and the rest shall be for houses for the priests, as is delineated at the end of the Book.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:3:1)


**וּמִן הַמִּדָּה הַזֹּאת.** בְּקָנֶה הַמִּדָּה שֶׁנִּמְדַּד בּוֹ רִבּוּעַ הַר הַבַּיִת חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת עַל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בָּעִנְיָן שֶׁל מַעְלָה (יחזקאל מב:כ): ״לְאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת מְדָדוֹ חוֹמָה לוֹ סָבִיב״, ״חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת קָנִים וְגוֹ׳״ (יחזקאל מב:טז-יט):


**And with this measurement** [lit. from this measurement.] With the measuring rod by which the 500 by 500 square of the Temple Mount was measured, as is stated above (42:20): “To four sides he measured it; its wall all around, five hundred rods, etc.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:3:2)


**תָּמוֹד אֹרֶךְ חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים אֶלֶף.** קָנִים, **וְרֹחַב עֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים** קָנִים; וּלְפִי שֶׁלֹּא פֵּרֵשׁ בַּפָּסוּק רִאשׁוֹן מַה הֵן **חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים אֶלֶף**, אִם קָנִים אִם אַמּוֹת, הֻצְרַךְ לוֹמַר שֶׁ**חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים** מִדּוֹת – בִּקְנֵה הַמִּדָּה שֶׁנִּמְדְּדוּ בָּהּ חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת עַל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת שֶׁל הַר הַבַּיִת נִמְדָּדִין אֲלָפִים הַלָּלוּ לַתְּרוּמָה:


**you shall measure the length** of 25,000 rods and the width of 10,000 rods. Because he did not explain in the first verse what the 25,000 are, whether rods or cubits, he had to say, concerning the 25,000 measures that they were measured with the measuring rod by which the five hundred by five hundred of the Temple Mount were measured.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:4:1)


**קֹדֶשׁ מִן הָאָרֶץ.** סָרֵס אֶת הַמִּקְרָא: מוֹתַר הַקֹּדֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר מִן הָאָרֶץ **הוּא יִהְיֶה לַכֹּהֲנִים מְשָׁרְתֵי הַמִּקְדָּשׁ הַקְּרֵבִים וְגוֹ**׳; **קֹדֶשׁ מִן הָאָרֶץ** הִיא הַתְּרוּמָה הַזֹּאת:


**It is the holy portion of the land** Rearrange the verse: “The remainder of the holy portion, which is from that land, shall be for the priests, the ministers of the Sanctuary, who come near, etc. The holy portion of the land is this offering.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:4:2)


**לַכֹּהֲנִים מְשָׁרְתֵי הַמִּקְדָּשׁ יִהְיֶה.** הָעוֹדֵף עַל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת שֶׁל הַר הַבַּיִת: שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר אֶלֶף וּמָאתַיִם וַחֲמִשִּׁים לַמִּזְרָח, וּכְנֶגְדָּן לַמַּעֲרָב, וְהַמִּקְדָּשׁ בָּאֶמְצַע, וְאַרְבַּעַת אֲלָפִים וּשְׁבַע מֵאוֹת וַחֲמִשִּׁים לַצָּפוֹן, וּכְנֶגְדָּן לַדָּרוֹם:


**for the priests, the ministers of the Sanctuary** The remainder over the 500 of the Temple Mount; 12,250 to the east and correspondingly to the westthe Sanctuary [being] in the center 4,750 to the north, and correspondingly to the south.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:4:3)


**וְהָיָה לָהֶם מָקוֹם לְבָתִּים.** הָעוֹדֵף הַזֶּה שֶׁסָּבִיב הַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**and it shall be for them a place for houses** this remainder, which surrounds the Sanctuary.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:4:4)


**וּמִקְדָּשׁ לַמִּקְדָּשׁ.** וַחֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת עַל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת הָאֶמְצָעִיִּים (יחזקאל מה:ב) יִהְיוּ מְקֻדָּשִׁים לְצֹרֶךְ הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, אִישִׁיינְטִיג״יאָה אַלְשִׁיינְטוּאִיירָא בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and the hallowed part shall be for the Sanctuary** And the middle five hundred by five hundred shall be hallowed for the Sanctuary, e sentije al sentuere in O. F., and consecrated for the Sanctuary.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:5:1)


**וַחֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים אֶלֶף.** קָנִים **אֹרֶךְ וַעֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים רֹחַב** – תָּרִימוּ רְצוּעָה אַחֶרֶת, אֵצֶל זוֹ בִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל זוֹ, לְצֹרֶךְ הַלְוִיִּם; כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר, שֶׁהִיא בַּדָּרוֹם (יחזקאל מח:י-יג). מצאתי, **עֶשְׂרִים לְשָׁכֹת** יִהְיֶה לַלְוִיִּם בְּהֶיקֵּף הַמִּקְדָּשׁ, לִשְׁמֹר הַבַּיִת וּלְנוֹי, וּמוֹתַר הָרְצוּעוֹת יַעֲשׂוּ לְצָרְכֵיהֶם, תוספות:


**And twenty-five thousand** rods in length, and ten thousand in width, you shall separate as another strip beside this one, south of this one, for the Levites. It is explained at the end of the Book that it is in the south. Twenty chambers shall be for the Levites in the perimeter of the Sanctuary in order to guard the House and to provide beauty, and the remainder of the strip shall be used for their own needs.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:6:1)


**וַאֲחֻזַּת הָעִיר.** בְּהֶיקֵּף הָעִיר, **אֲחֻזַּת** מוֹשַׁב חוֹל שֶׁלָּהּ, לִבְנוֹת בָּהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל בָּתִּים:


**And, [for] the property of the city** The environs of the city; its properties meant for ordinary dwelling, in which the Israelites may build houses.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:6:2)


**תִּתְּנוּ חֲמֵשֶׁת אֲלָפִים רֹחַב.** בִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל שְׁנִיָּה, וְאֹרֶךְ כְּמִדַּת שְׁתֵּי הָרְצוּעוֹת; נִמְצֵאת כָּל הַתְּרוּמָה מְרֻבַּעַת: עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה עַל עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֲלָפִים:


**you shall give a width of five thousand** in the south of the second one, and a length equal to the measurement of the two strips. It is found that the entire offering is square, twenty-five [thousand] by twenty-five thousand.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:6:3)


**לְעֻמַּת תְּרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** כְּלוֹמַר: בְּמִדַּת אֹרֶךְ רְצוּעוֹת תְּרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ:


**corresponding to the offering of the holy portion** in the measurement of the length of the strips of the offering of the holy portion.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:6:4)


**לְכָל בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל יִהְיֶה.** אוֹתָהּ רְצוּעָה שְׁלִישִׁית תִּהְיֶה מוֹשָׁב לַזָּרִים:


**for the entire House of Israel it shall be** That third strip shall be the dwelling place for non priests.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:7:1)


**וְלַנָּשִׂיא מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה לִתְרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ וְלַאֲחֻזַּת הָעִיר.** בְּסוֹף הַפָּרָשָׁה הוּא חוֹלֵק אֶת אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל מִמִּזְרָחָהּ לְמַעַרְבָהּ לִשְׁלוֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה רְצוּעוֹת, שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה לְמִנְיָן הַשְּׁבָטִים, וְכָל אַחַת עֶשְׂרִים וְחָמֵשׁ אֶלֶף קָנִים רֹחַב וְאָרְכָּן כְּאֹרֶךְ כָּל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, וּרְצוּעָה אַחַת לִדְרוֹמָהּ, אַרְכָּה מִן הַגְּבוּל מִזְרָח וְעַד גְּבוּל מַעֲרָב, וְרָחְבָּהּ עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֲלָפִים קָנִים, כִּשְׁאַר כָּל חֵלֶק וְחֵלֶק. וּמֵאוֹתָהּ רְצוּעָה הוּא תּוֹרֵם בְּאֶמְצָעִיתָהּ שָׁלוֹשׁ רְצוּעוֹת הַלָּלוּ הָאֲמוּרוֹת לְמַעְלָה, שֶׁהֵם עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֲלָפִים עַל עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֲלָפִים, וְהָעוֹדֵף עֲלֵיהֶם לַמִּזְרָח עַד סוֹף הַגְּבוּל הָאָרֶץ, וְכֵן לַמַּעֲרָב, יִהְיֶה לַנָּשִׂיא מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה לַמִּזְרָח וּלְמַעֲרַב:


**And for the prince, on either side of the offering of the holy portion and of the possession of the city** At the end of the section, he divides the land of Israel from east to west into thirteen strips: twelve as the number of the tribes each one twenty-five thousand rods wide, and its length equaling the length of all the land of Israel and one strip as an offering whose length is from the eastern border to the western border, and whose width is twenty-five thousand rods, just as each of the other portions. And from that strip he separated in its center the three strips stated above, which [all together] are twenty- five thousand by twenty-five thousand. And the remainder to the east until the end of the border of the land, and to the west, as well, shall be for the prince from either side to the east and to the west.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:7:2)


**אֶל פְּנֵי תְרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ וְאֶל פְּנֵי אֲחֻזַּת הָעִיר.** כְּנֶגֶד כָּל רֹחַב שְׁלֹשָׁה הָרְצוּעוֹת הַמֻּבְדָּלוֹת לִתְרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ שֶׁל רְצוּעוֹת הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם וַאֲחֻזַּת הָעִיר:


**alongside the offering of the holy portion and alongside the possession of the city** opposite the entire breadth of the three strips separated for the offering of the holy portion of the strips of the priests, the Levites, and the property of the city.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:7:3)


**מִפְּאַת יָם יָמָּה.** מִמַּעֲרַב תְּרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ וְהָעִיר עַד מַעֲרַב הַגְּבוּל:


**from the western side, westward** from the west of the offering of the holy portion and the city until the west of the boundary.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:7:4)


**וּמִפְּאַת קֵדְמָה קָדִימָה.** וּמִמִּזְרָח הַתְּרוּמָה לְמֶצֶר מִמִּזְרָח, **לְעֻמַּת אַחַד הַחֲלָקִים** שֶׁל שְׁבָטִים הַמְפֹרָשִׁין בְּסוֹף הַסֵּפֶר (יחזקאל מח:כא), שֶׁהֵם **מִגְּבוּל יָם** שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, עַד **גְּבוּל** קָדִים:


**and from the eastern side, east ward** And from the east of the offering to the eastern boundary, opposite one of the portions of the tribes delineated at the end of the Book, which are from the western boundary of the land of Israel, until the eastern boundary.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:8:1)


**לָאָרֶץ יִהְיֶה לוֹ לַאֲחֻזָּה.** תַּרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן: ״דָּא אַרְעָא תְּהֵא רַבָּא לְאַחֲסָנְתָא״:


**In the land he shall have it for a possession** [Heb. לָאָרֶץ Jonathan renders: This land shall be for the prince as a possession.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:8:2)


**וְלֹא יוֹנוּ.** לְשׁוֹן אוֹנָאַת מָמוֹן, שֶׁגּוֹזְלִין אֶת נַחֲלָתָם:


**shall no longer defraud** [Heb. יוֹנוּ,] an expression for monetary fraud; they take away their inheritance forcibly for they rob their inherited property



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:9:1)


**הָרִימוּ גְרֻשׁוֹתֵיכֶם.** סַלְּקוּ אֶת שֶׁאַתֶּם מְגָרְשִׁים אֶת עַמִּי מִנַּחֲלָתָם:


**take away your evictions** Take away [your practice] of evicting My people from their inherited property.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:10:1)


**וְאֵיפַת.** לְמִדַּת הַיָּבֵשׁ:


**ephah** of the dry measure.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:10:2)


**וּבַת.** לְמִדַּת הַלַּח:


**bath** of the liquid measure.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:11:1)


**הָאֵיפָה וְהַבַּת.** לְשׁוֹן מִדָּה, חֹמֶר וְכוֹר (יחזקאל מה:יד) מוֹיִ״י בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the homer** [A measure known further as] kor, moy(d) or muy(d) in Old French, a measure.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:11:2)


**תֹּכֶן אֶחָד.** לְשׁוֹן מִנְיָן, כְּמוֹ ״וְתֹכֶן לְבֵנִים תִּתֵּנוּ״ (שמות ה:יח); מִדָּה אַחַת: זוֹ עֲשִׂירִית לְחֹמֶר שֶׁל יָבֵשׁ, שֶׁהוּא שְׁלֹשִׁים סְאִין, וְזוֹ עֲשִׂירִית לְחֹמֶר הַלַּח:


**one volume** [Heb. תֹּכן,] a word denoting number, like (Exod. 5:18): “and a quota (וְתֹכֶן) of bricks you must deliver.” One measure is equivalent to one tenth of a “homer” of dry measure, which equals thirty “se’ah,” and which is a tenth of a “homer” of liquid measure. “Ephah” and “bath” are words for [units of] measurement.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:11:3)


**לָשֵׂאת.** כְּמוֹ לָקַחַת, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לְמֵיסַב״. **לָשֵׂאת מַעְשַׂר הַחֹמֶר** תִּהְיֶה **הַבַּת**, וְכֵן **עֲשִׂירִית לַחֹמֶר** יָבֵשׁ:


**shall contain** [Heb. לָשֵּׂאת,] similar to לָקַחַת, to take, and so too did Jonathan render it: לְמֵיסַב. A tenth part of a “homer” shall be a “bath,” and a tenth part of the dry “homer,” shall be an “ephah.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:11:4)


**הָאֵיפָה אֶל הַחֹמֶר יִהְיֶה מַתְכֻּנְתּוֹ.** סְכוּם חֶשְׁבּוֹן הַבַּת וְהָאֵיפָה לְפִי גֹּדֶל הַחֹמֶר יִהְיֶה, מָצָאתִי, אִם יוֹסִיפוּ בַּחֹמֶר יוֹסִיפוּ בַּבַּת, אִם יִפְחֲתוּהוּ מִזֶּה יִפְחֲתוּהוּ מִזֶּה, שֶׁיְּכַוֵּן מַעְשְׂרוֹ לוֹ:


**according to the homer shall be its volume** The total amount of [the volume of] the “bath” and the “ephah” shall be according to the size of the “homer.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:12:1)


**וְהַשֶּׁקֶל עֶשְׂרִים גֵּרָה.** ״עֶשְׂרִים מָעִין״ (תרגום יונתן):


**And the shekel is twenty gerah** Twenty “ma’ah.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:12:2)


**עֶשְׂרִים שְׁקָלִים חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים שְׁקָלִים עֲשָׂרָה וַחֲמִשָּׁה שֶׁקֶל.** הֲרֵי שִׁשִּׁים שְׁקָלִים:


**twenty shekels, twenty-five shekels, and fifteen shekels** totaling sixty shekels.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:12:3)


**הַמָּנֶה יִהְיֶה לָכֶם.** לוּצוֹנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז; וּמְנַחֵם חִבְּרוֹ לְשׁוֹן מִנְיָן. וַהֲרֵי יֵשׁ כָּאן מָאתַיִם וְאַרְבָּעִים זוּז; מִכָּאן שֶׁהָיָה מָנֶה שֶׁל קֹדֶשׁ כָּפוּל, וְהוֹסִיפוּ עָלָיו בִּימֵי יְחֶזְקֵאל שְׁתוּת – הֲרֵי מָאתַיִם וְאַרְבָּעִים (בבא בתרא צ.-צ:). וּכְשֶׁחִלְּקוֹ הַכָּתוּב לִשְׁלֹשָׁה, וְלֹא כָתַב סְתָם שִׁשִּׁים שְׁקָלִים יִהְיֶה לָכֶם הַמָּנֶה – צִוָּה לַעֲשׂוֹת מִמֶּנּוּ מִשְׁקָל שְׁלִישִׁיתוֹ וּמִשְׁקָל רְבִיעִיתוֹ וּמִשְׁקָל מָנֶה שֶׁל חוֹל שֶׁהָיָה מִתְּחִלָּה; וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״תְּלָתוּת מַנְיָא עֶסְרִין סִלְעִין, מָנֵי כַסְפָּא עֶסְרִין וַחֲמֵשׁ סִלְעִין, רַבְעוּת מַנְיָא חֲמֵשׁ עֶסְרֵי סִלְעִין, כּוּלְהוֹן שִׁתִּין מָנִין, וּמַנָּא רַבָּא – קוּדְשָׁא יְהֵא לְכוֹן״:


**shall the maneh be to you** Le zent in O. F., the 100 (zuz weight). Menahem, however, connected it to the word מִנְיָן, a number (p. 118). We have here 240 “zuz,” [four zuz to a shekel]. From here we derive that the “maneh” of the Sanctuary was double, and they added a sixth to it in Ezekiel’s time, totaling 240 [zuz] (Men. 77a). When Scripture divided it into three parts and did not write simply, “sixty shekels shall the maneh be for you,” it commanded to make from it a weight one third of it, and a weight equaling a fourth of it, and a weight of the ordinary “maneh” as it was originally. So too did Jonathan paraphrase: a third of the “maneh” shall be twenty “selaim” of silver; a “maneh” of silver twenty-five “selaim”; a fourth of a “maneh”fifteen “selaim”; altogether, sixty “selaim”; and the great “maneh” of the Sanctuary shall be for you.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:13:1)


**שִׁשִּׁית הָאֵיפָה מֵחֹמֶר הַחִטִּים.** הֲרֵי אֶחָד מִשִּׁשִּׁים; הַפּוֹחֵת לֹא יִפְחוֹת מִכָּאן, וְזֶה הוּא שֶׁאָמְרוּ (משנה תרומות ד, ג): עַיִן רָעָה אֶחָד מִשִּׁשִּׁים:


**a sixth of an ephah from a homer of wheat** This amounts to one out of sixty. Whoever wishes to give little shall not give less than this, and this is what they said (Ter. 4:3): “A stingy person gives one out of sixty.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:13:2)


**וְשִׁשִּׁיתֶם.** וּתְחַלְּקוּ אֶחָד מִשִּׁשָּׁה בָּאֵיפָה לִתְרוּמַת חֹמֶר הַשְּׂעוֹרִים:


**and you shall separate a sixth** And you shall separate a sixth of an “ephah” for the “terumah” of a “homer” of barley.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:14:1)


**וְחֹק הַשֶּׁמֶן.** לְמַעֲשֵׂר:


**And the rule of the oil** regarding tithes.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:14:2)


**הַבַּת הַשֶּׁמֶן וְגוֹ׳.** הַבַּת שֶׁהִיא מִדַּת הַשֶּׁמֶן, זוֹ הִיא **מַעְשַׂר הַבַּת** יְהִי **מִן הַכֹּר**. מָצָאתִי הָעִשּׂוּר שֶׁהַבַּת מֵעֲשֶׂרֶת מִן הַכּוּר יִהְיֶה. כֵּיצַד (**מַעְשַׂר הַבַּת מִן הַכֹּר** עֲשִׂירִית שֶׁל בַּת עַל הַכּוּר הוּא, די״ץ מזור״ש בְּלַעַ״ז; תֵּבָה זוֹ מְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת עַל עַצְמָהּ וְעַל אֲחֵרִים, כְּמוֹ ״עֲבוֹדַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ״ (במדבר ז:ט) וַ״עֲבוֹדַת בְּנֵי מְרָרִי״ (במדבר ד:לג) סא״א):


**the bath, [which is a measure of] oil, etc.** The “bath,” which is a measure of oil this is its tithe: the “bath” will be from a “kor.” I found [the following]: The tenth that the “bath” represents as a tithe shall be from a “kor.” How so? The tithe of a “bath” is from a “kor.” Dix measures in French, ten measures. This word is used for itself and for others, like (Num. 7:9): “the service of the Sanctuary,” (ibid. 4:33): “the service of [the families of] the sons of Merari.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:14:3)


**עֲשֶׂרֶת הַבַּתִּים.** יִהְיֶה לָכֶם הַ**חֹמֶר**, וְאָז יִתָּכֵן לִטֹּל הֵימֶנּוּ בַּת אַחַת לְמַעֲשֵׂר:


**ten baths** shall equal a “homer” for you. Then it will be possible to take from it one “bath” as a tithe.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:14:4)


**כִּי עֲשֶׂרֶת הַבַּתִּים חֹמֶר.** לְפִי שֶׁהַחֹמֶר יִהְיֶה לָכֶם עֶשֶׂר בַתִּים, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״חַד מִן עַסְרָא הֲוָיא בֵּיתָא בְּכוֹרָא, אֲרֵי עֲסַר בֵּיתִין כּוֹרָא״:


**for ten baths are a homer** because the “homer” will consist of ten “baths” for you. So too did Jonathan render it: one out of ten is the “bath” in relation to the “kor” for ten “baths” are a “kor”.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:15:1)


**וְשֶׂה אַחַת מִן הַצֹּאן.** מְיֻחָד שֶׁבְּצֹאנוֹ, וְכֵן אָמַר מֹשֶׁה ״מִבְחַר נִדְרֵיכֶם״ (דברים יב:יא). לומיי״לור בְּלַעַ״ז:
