**And one lamb from the flocks** A special one of his flocks, and so too said Moses (Deut. 12: 11): “and all the choice of your pledges, le meilleur in Fr., the best.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:15:2)


**מִן הַמָּאתַיִם מִמַּשְׁקֵה יִשְׂרָאֵל.** דָּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (פסחים מח.) לְעִנְיַן הַנְּסָכִים, מִמּוֹתַר שְׁתֵּי מָאוֹת שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּיְּרוּ בַּבּוֹר שֶׁנָּפַל לְתוֹכוֹ יַיִן שֶׁל עָרְלָה אוֹ שֶׁל כִּלְאֵי הַכֶּרֶם; מִכָּאן לְעָרְלָה וְכִלְאֵי הַכֶּרֶם שֶׁבְּטֵלִין בְּמָאתַיִם:


**out of two hundred, from Israel’s banquet** Our Rabbis expounded (Pes. 48a) this as regarding libations [coming] from a multiplicity of two hundred [times as much of the original wine] as remained in the pit after the wine of “orlah” or of “mingled species in the vineyard” fell into it. From here it is derived that “orlah” and “mingled species in the vineyard” are nullified in two hundred [times as much].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:15:3)


**מִמַּשְׁקֵה יִשְׂרָאֵל.** מִן הַמֻּתָּר לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, כָּל קָרְבְּנוֹתֵיהֶם יִהְיוּ מַשְׁקֶה הָרָאוּי לְיִשְׂרָאֵל. כָּל עִקַּר הַסְּעוּדָה קְרוּיָה עַל שֵׁם הַמַּשְׁקֶה, כְּלוֹמַר: הָאֲכִילָה עִם הַשְּׁתִיָּה תְּהֵא מִן הַמֻּתָּר לְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


**from Israel’s banquet** from what is permissible for Israel. All your sacrifices shall be drink that is fit for Israel. The main part of the feast is called by the name of the drink; i.e., the food and also the drink shall be from that which is permitted for Israel.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:16:1)


**אֶל הַתְּרוּמָה הַזֹּאת.** הָאֲמוּרָה לְמַעְלָה (פסוק יג ואילך), לְדַעַת כָּל עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ תִּהְיֶה:


**to give this oblation** mentioned above; it will be [given] with the knowledge of all the people of the land.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:17:1)


**וְעַל הַנָּשִׂיא.** אוֹמֵר אֲנִי, שֶׁהַנָּשִׂיא הַזֶּה – בְּכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל מְדַבֵּר, וְכֵן כָּל הַנָּשִׂיא שֶׁבָּעִנְיָן; וְשָׁמַעְתִּי מִשְּׁמוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי מְנַחֵם, שֶׁבְּמֶלֶךְ מְדַבֵּר:


**on the prince** I say that this “prince” as well as every [mention of] “the prince” in this section means the High Priest; but I heard in the name of Rabbi Menahem that it means the king.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:18:1)


**כֹּה אָמַר אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהִים בָּרִאשׁוֹן בְּאֶחָד לַחֹדֶשׁ תִּקַּח פַּר בֶּן בָּקָר תָּמִים.** הוּא פַּר הַמִּלּוּאִים הָאָמוּר בְּרֹאשׁ הָעִנְיָן (יחזקאל מג:יח-כז); וְלִמֵּד כָּאן שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַמִּלּוּאִים בְּאֶחָד בְּנִיסָן:


**So says the Lord God: In the first month, on the first of the month, you shall take a young bull [and] without blemish** This is the bull of investiture mentioned at the beginning of this section (43:18 27), and he teaches [us] here that the investiture should be on the first of Nissan.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:18:2)


**וְחִטֵּאתָ.** וְטִהַרְתָּ:


**and you shall purify** [as translated,] and you shall purify.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:20:1)


**וְכֵן תַּעֲשֶׂה בְּשִׁבְעָה בַחֹדֶשׁ.** יֵשׁ לוֹמַר: **וְכֵן תַּעֲשֶׂה** כָּל שִׁבְעָה, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר לְמַעְלָה ״שִׁבְעַת יָמִים יְכַפְּרוּ עַל הַמִּזְבֵּחַ״ (יחזקאל מג:כו). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ כָּךְ פֵּרְשׁוּהוּ בִּמְנָחוֹת (מנחות מה.): **וְכֵן תַּעֲשֶׂה** פַּר שֶׁאֵינוֹ נֶאֱכָל, **בְּשִׁבְעָה** שְׁבָטִים, שֶׁחִדְּשׁוּ דָבָר וְהוֹרוּ בֵּית דִּין שֶׁלָּהֶן שֶׁחֵלֶב מֻתָּר, וְעָשׂוּ שִׁבְעָה שְׁבָטִים, שֶׁהֵם רֹב צִבּוּר, עַל פִּיהֶם – שֶׁמְּבִיאִין פַּר הֶעְלֵם דָּבָר:


**And so shall you do on seven [days] in the month** It may be said that [this means]: And so shall you do all seven, and so too he says above (43:26): “For seven days they shall effect atonement for the altar.” But our Rabbis explained it in Menahoth (45a) in the following manner: And so shall you make [as a sacrifice] a bull that is not to be eaten in [the event of] “seven” tribes who “interpreted the Torah in a new way (שֶּׁחְדֹּשּׁוּ)” and whose tribunal issued a decision that fat is permissible, and seven tribes, (which are the majority of the nation), who acted on their word. They must bring a bull for communal error.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:20:2)


**מֵאִישׁ שׁוֹגֶה וּמִפֶּתִי וגו׳.** הֲרֵי זֶה מִקְרָא מְסֹרָס: **וְכִפַּרְתֶּם אֶת הַבַּיִת מֵאִישׁ שׁוֹגֶה וּמִפֶּתִי**. אַחַר שִׁבְעַת יְמֵי הַמִּלּוּאִים, שֶׁיְּהֵא הַמִּזְבֵּחַ מְחֻנָּךְ, יָבִיאוּ חַטָּאוֹתָם וַאֲשָׁמוֹתָם וְיִתְכַּפְּרוּ; שֶׁבִּזְמַן שֶׁיִּשְׂרָאֵל מְכֻפָּרִים, הַבַּיִת מְכֻפָּר:


**because of mistaken and simple-minded men** This is a transposed verse: And you shall expiate the House from mistaken and simple-minded men. After the seven days of investiture, whenceforth the altar will be dedicated, they shall bring their sin-offerings and their guilt-offerings, and they will attain atonement, for at the time that Israel attains atonement, the House attains atonement.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:21:1)


**שְׁבֻעוֹת יָמִים.** עַל שֵׁם שֶׁמַּתְחִילִין מִמֶּנּוּ לִסְפֹּר שִׁבְעָה שָׁבוּעוֹת, מָצָאתִי:


**seven days** [Lit. weeks of days,] because they commence from it to count seven weeks.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:21:2)


**מַצּוֹת יֵאָכֵל.** מַצּוֹת יֵאָכְלוּ בּוֹ בֶּחָג:


**unleavened bread shall be eaten** and unleavened bread shall be eaten on that festival.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:22:1)


**וְעָשָׂה הַנָּשִׂיא בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא וגו׳.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (שבת יג:) שֶׁבִּקְּשׁוּ לִגְנֹז סֵפֶר יְחֶזְקֵאל, שֶׁהָיוּ דְבָרָיו סוֹתְרִין דִּבְרֵי תוֹרָה, בְּרַם זָכוּר לְטוֹב חֲנַנְיָה בֶּן חִזְקִיָּהוּ בֶּן גָּרוֹן שֶׁיָּשַׁב בַּעֲלִיָּה וּדְרָשׁוֹ. וּבַעֲוֹנִינוּ נֶעְלַם מִמֶּנּוּ מַה שֶּׁדָּרַשׁ בַּקָּרְבָּנוֹת הָאֵלֶּה, פַּר בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן לָמָּה; וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי: שֶׁמָּא בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן שֶׁל פֶּסַח רִאשׁוֹן, שֶׁנִּתְחַנֵּךְ בּוֹ הַבַּיִת עוֹמֵד, וּפַר זֶה בָּא תַּחַת עֵגֶל בֶּן בָּקָר שֶׁהִקְרִיב אַהֲרֹן בַּשְּׁמִינִי לַמִּלּוּאִים (ויקרא ט:ב); וּמַגִּיד, שֶׁאִם לֹא הִקְרִיבוֹ בַּשְּׁמִינִי לַמִּלּוּאִים, יַקְרִיבֶנּוּ בְּאַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר בְּנִיסָן כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְחַנֵּךְ לָעֲבוֹדָה קֹדֶם יוֹם טוֹב, לְפִי שֶׁעָלָיו קָרְבָּנוֹת וְעוֹלַת הַמּוֹעֵד לַעֲשׂוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה **וְעַל הַנָּשִׂיא יִהְיֶה הָעוֹלוֹת וְהַמִּנְחָה וְהַנֶּסֶךְ בֶּחַגִּים וְגוֹ׳**:


**And the prince shall make on that day, etc.** Our Rabbis (Hag. 13a) said that they sought to suppress the Book of Ezekiel for his words contradicted the words of the Torah. Indeed, Hananiah the son of Hezekiah the son of Gurion is remembered for good, for he sat in his attic and expounded on it. But because of our iniquities, what he expounded on these sacrificeswhy a bull is brought on the fourteenth day of Nissanhas been lost to us. I say that perhaps he is dealing with the fourteenth of Nissan of the first Passover in which the fully erected House will be dedicated, and this bull will be brought in lieu of the calf born of cattle that Aaron offered up on the eighth of investiture (Lev. 9:2). [Scripture] tells us that if he will not have offered it up on the eighth day of investiture, he should offer it up on the fourteenth of Nissan in order that he should be initiated for the service before the Festival, for it is incumbent upon him [to bring] the sacrifices and the burnt offering of the appointed time, as is stated above (v. 17): “And the burnt offerings and the meal-offerings and the libations on the festivals...shall devolve on the prince, etc.”



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:23:1)


**שִׁבְעַת פָּרִים וְשִׁבְעַת אֵילִים תְּמִימִים לַיּוֹם.** וְהַתּוֹרָה אָמְרָה ״פָּרִים בְּנֵי בָקָר שְׁנַיִם וְאַיִל אֶחָד״ (במדבר כח:יא)! אֵין לִי לְהַעֲמִיד מִקְרָא זֶה אֶלָּא שִׁבְעָה פָּרִים וְשִׁבְעָה אֵילִים לְשִׁבְעַת הַיָּמִים, פַּר לַיּוֹם וְאַיִל לַיּוֹם, וּבָא לְלַמֵּד שֶׁאֵין הַפָּרִים מְעַכְּבִין זֶה אֶת זֶה, וְלֹא הָאֵילִים מְעַכְּבִין זֶה אֶת זֶה. וְכֵן שָׁנִינוּ בִּמְנָחוֹת (מה.), אַךְ לֹא הֵבִיאוּ מִן הַמִּקְרָא הַזֶּה רְאָיָה, אֶלָּא מִמִּקְרָא שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר לְמַטָּה ״וּבְיוֹם הַחֹדֶשׁ פַּר בֶּן בָּקָר תְּמִימִים וְשֵׁשֶׁת כְּבָשִׂים וְאַיִל״ (יחזקאל מו:ו); וְשֶׁמָּא אַף זֶה בָּא לְלַמֵּד כֵּן; וְכֵן פֵּרוּשׁוֹ: **שִׁבְעָה פָּרִים וְשִׁבְעָה אֵילִים לַיּוֹם**. צֵרוּף דְּבַר יוֹם בְּיוֹמוֹ עוֹלִים שִׁבְעָה פָּרִים לְשִׁבְעַת הַיָּמִים:


**seven bulls and seven rams without blemish daily** But the Torah states (Num. 28:19): “two young bulls, one ram.” We can explain this verse only as meaning seven bulls and seven rams for the seven days, a bull daily and a ram daily, and it comes to teach us that the bulls do not render each other invalid and the rams do not render each other invalid. [I.e., if one bull is missing, the other one may be brought.] So we learned in Menahoth (45a); however, they did not bring proof from this verse but from the verse below (46:6): “But on the New Moon: a young bull from those without blemish, and six lambs and a ram.” But perhaps this too comes to teach the same thing, and this is its meaning: seven bulls and seven rams daily. By adding the sacrifices of each day on its day, they add up to seven bulls for the seven days.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:23:2)


**וְחַטָּאת שְׂעִיר עִזִּים לַיּוֹם.** שְׂעִירֵי הָרְגָלִים:


**and a sin-offering, a he-goat daily** the he-goats of the pilgrimage festivals.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:24:1)


**וּמִנְחָה אֵיפָה לַפָּר.** מִנְחַת הַנְּסָכִים אֵיפָה לַפָּר, אֵינִי יוֹדֵעַ מַה הוּא, שֶׁהֲרֵי אָמְרָה תּוֹרָה ״שְׁלֹשָׁה עֶשְׂרוֹנִים״ (במדבר כח:כ), וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר: אֵיפַת קֶמַח לְהוֹצִיא סֹלֶת עִשָּׂרוֹן מִן הַסְּאָה, שֶׁהָאֵיפָה שָׁלֹשׁ סְאִין. וְ**אֵיפָה לַפָּר** לִמֶּדְךָ שֶׁאִם לֹא מָצָא סֹלֶת מְנֻפָּה כָּל כָּךְ, יָבִיא מִשֶּׁל עִשָּׂרוֹן לַסְּאָה:


**And a meal-offering...an ephah for a bull** the meal-offering of the libations, an “ephah” for a bull. Now I do not know what this means, for the Torah stated (28:20): “three tenths for the bull.” It is possible that it means an “ephah” of flour from which we extract a tenth of fine flour from a “se’ah”, for the “ephah” is three “se’ahs”.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:24:2)


**וְאֵיפָה לָאַיִל.** אַף זוֹ קֶמַח, לְהוֹצִיא מִמֶּנָּה שְׁנֵי עֶשְׂרוֹנִים סֹלֶת מְנֻפָּה כָּל צָרְכָּהּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ (מנחות עו:): שְׁתֵּי הַלֶּחֶם – שְׁתֵּי עֶשְׂרוֹנִים מִשָּׁלֹשׁ סְאִין:


**and an ephah for a ram** This too is flour from which we extract two tenths of fine flour sifted thoroughly, as we learned (Men. 6:6): The two loaves were two tenths from three “se’ahs”. [The requirement of] an “ephah” for the bull teaches that if he did not find fine sifted flour that yielded that much, he may bring from [flour that yields] a tenth to a “se’ah”.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 45:24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/45:24:3)


**הִין לָאֵיפָה.** לֹא יָדַעְתִּי לָמָּה. וְיֵשׁ לוֹמַר: לֹא שֶׁיַּקְרִיב הַהִין כֻּלּוֹ, אֶלָּא שְׁנָתוֹת הָיוּ בַּהִין (משנה מנחות ט:), וְיַקְרִיב שֶׁמֶן לְפִי הַסֹּלֶת כְּמִשְׁפַּט הַזֶּבַח, לַפָּר כְּמִשְׁפָּטוֹ וְלָאַיִל כְּמִשְׁפָּטוֹ, לְפִי שְׁנָתוֹת שֶׁל הִין:


**and for each ephah one hin of oil** I do not know why. We may say that it does not mean that he must sacrifice the entire “hin,” but that there were notches in the “hin” and he would sacrifice oil according to the fine flour, according to the sacrificial laws for a bull according to its requirement and for a ram according to its requirement, according to the notches of the “hin”.



## Chapter 46


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:1:1)


**שַׁעַר הֶחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִית הַפּוֹנֶה קָדִים וְגוֹ׳.** שָׁנוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (ד, ב): שְׁנֵי פִשְׁפְּשִׁין הָיוּ לוֹ לְשַׁעַר הַהֵיכָל, אֶחָד בַּדָּרוֹם וְאֶחָד בַּצָּפוֹן; שֶׁבַּדָּרוֹם – עָלָיו מְפֹרָשׁ בַּקַּבָּלָה (יחזקאל מד:ב) ״אִישׁ לֹא יָבֹא בוֹ וְהָיָה סָגוּר״:


**The gate of the Inner Court that faces toward the east, etc.** Our Rabbis learned in Tractate Middoth (4:2): The gate of the Heichal had two wickets, one in the south and one in the north. Concerning the one in the south it is explained in the post Mosaic Scriptures (above 44:2): “and no man shall come through it... and it shall be closed.”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:2:1)


**דֶּרֶךְ אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר מִחוּץ.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה ״וְאֵילַמָּיו אֶל הֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה״ (יחזקאל מ:לא). **דֶּרֶךְ אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר** – שֶׁל עֲזָרָה; כְּלוֹמַר: יִכָּנֵס בְּ**דֶרֶךְ שַׁעַר** מִזְרָחִי, שֶׁהוּא מְשַׁמֵּשׁ כְּנִיסָה וִיצִיאָה, וּבָא לוֹ לְאוֹתוֹ הַפִּשְׁפֵּשׁ:


**by way of the vestibule of the gate without** as he states above (40:31): “And its halls were to the Outer Court.” By way of the vestibule of the Gate of the Court, i.e., he shall enter by way of the Eastern Gate, which serves for entry and exit, and come to that wicket.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:2:2)


**וְעָמַד עַל מְזוּזַת הַשַּׁעַר.** אוֹתוֹ שַׁעַר הַקָּטָן, הוּא הַפִּשְׁפָּשׁ:


**and he shall stand at the doorpost of the gate** That small gate is the wicket.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:2:3)


**אֶת עוֹלָתוֹ וְאֶת שְׁלָמָיו.** עוֹלַת רְאִיָּה וְשַׁלְמֵי חֲגִיגָה, וּמִקְרָא זֶה מוּסָב עַל יוֹם טוֹב, כָּךְ מָצָאתִי בְּפֵירוּשִׁים מְדֻיָּקִים:


**his burnt offering and his peace offering** The burnt offering for appearing in the Temple and the peace offering for celebrating the festivals; this verse refers to the festivals.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:2:4)


**וְעָמַד עַל מְזוּזַת הַשַּׁעַר.** שַׁעַר הַפְּנִימִית, הִיא אֲשֶׁר בֵּאֵר – הוּא הַפִּשְׁפָּשׁ, כְּדַאֲמַר מַר (משנה תענית ד:א): אִיפְשָׁר קָרְבָּנוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם קָרֵב וְאֵינוֹ עוֹמֵד עַל גַּבָּיו?


**and he shall stand at the doorposts of the gate** The inner gate; this is the wicket, as the master stated (Taanith 4:2): “Is it possible for a person’s sacrifice to be offered up when he is not standing over it?”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:2:5)


**וְלֹא יִסָּגֵר עַד הָעָרֶב.** לָמָּה לֹא יִסָּגֵר?


**shall not be closed until the evening** Now why should it not be closed?



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:3:1)


**וְהִשְׁתַּחֲווּ עַם הָאָרֶץ.** כָּל הַיּוֹם, וְגַם כָּל הַבָּא, וְלָעֶרֶב יִסְגְּרוּהוּ:


**And the people of the land shall prostrate themselves** all day, and whoever comes, too, and in the evening they shall close it.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:4:1)


**בְּיוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת שִׁשָּׁה כְבָשִׂים.** לֹא יָדַעְתִּי לָמָּה, שֶׁהֲרֵי אָמְרָה תּוֹרָה ״שְׁנֵי כְבָשִׂים״ (במדבר כח:ט)?! וּבְיוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת מַשְׁמַע שַׁבַּת בְּרֵאשִׁית וּמַשְׁמַע יוֹם טוֹב. וְאוֹמֵר אֲנִי, שַׁבָּת זוֹ אֵינָהּ שַׁבַּת בְּרֵאשִׁית אֶלָּא יוֹם טוֹב, שֶׁטָּעוּן שִׁבְעָה כְבָשִׂים וּשְׁנֵי אֵילִים (במדבר כח:יט); וּבָא וְלִמֶּדְךָ שֶׁאֵין מְעַכְּבִין זֶה אֶת זֶה, וְאִם לֹא מָצָא שִׁבְעָה יָבִיא שִׁשָּׁה, וְאִם לֹא מָצָא שְׁנֵי אֵילִים יָבִיא אֶחָד; כְּמוֹ שֶׁדָּרְשׁוּ חֲכָמִים לְעִנְיַן רֹאשׁ חֹדֶשׁ:


**On the Sabbath day shall be six lambs** I do not know why, for the Torah said (Num. 28:9): “two lambs,” and “on the Sabbath Day” means either the Sabbath commemorating the Creation or a festival. I, therefore, say that this Sabbath is not the Sabbath commemorating the Creation, but a festival that requires seven lambs and two rams. Scripture comes and teaches you that [the absence of one] does not render the other one invalid, and if he does not find seven, he should bring six, and if he does not find two rams, he should bring one, as our Rabbis expounded regarding the New Moon.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:5:1)


**מַתַּת יָדוֹ.** לִמֵּד שֶׁאֵין הַמְּנָחוֹת מְעַכְּבוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ:


**as he is able to give** This teaches that the meal-offerings do not render each other invalid [in each other’s absence].



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:6:1)


**וּבְיוֹם הַחֹדֶשׁ פַּר בֶּן בָּקָר תְּמִימִים.** מִקְרָא זֶה דָּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בִּמְנָחוֹת (מה.): **פַּר** מַה לָמַדְנוּ? לְפִי שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בַּתּוֹרָה ״וּבְרָאשֵׁי חָדְשֵׁיכֶם... פָּרִים בְּנֵי בָקָר שְׁנַיִם״ (במדבר כח:יא), מִנַּיִן שֶׁאִם לֹא מָצָא שְׁנַיִם יָבִיא אֶחָד? לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: **פַּר**;


**But on the New Moon: a young bull without blemish** Our Rabbis expounded upon this verse in Menahoth (45a): Why does it say, “a young bull”? Since it is stated in the Torah (Num. 28:11): “And at the beginnings of your months, etc., two young bulls,” how do you know that if he did not find two, he should bring one? Because it is stated: “a bull.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:6:2)


**וְשֵׁשֶׁת כְּבָשִׂים.** מַה לָּמַדְנוּ? לְפִי שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בַּתּוֹרָה ״שִׁבְעָה״ (במדבר כח:יא), מִנַּיִן שֶׁאִם לֹא מָצָא שִׁבְעָה יָבִיא שִׁשָּׁה? לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: **וְשֵׁשֶׁת כְּבָשִׂים**. וּמִנַּיִן אֲפִילוּ אֶחָד? תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר **וְלַכְּבָשִׂים כַּאֲשֶׁר תַּשִּׂיג יָדוֹ**. מָצָאתִי:


**and six lambs** Why is it necessary to state this? Since it is said in the Torah, “seven,” how do you know that if he did not find seven, he should bring six? Because it is stated: “and six lambs.” And how do you know [that he should bring] even one? Because the Torah says, “but for the lambs as much as he can afford.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:8:1)


**וּבְבוֹא הַנָּשִׂיא.** בְּיוֹם הַחֹדֶשׁ וּבְשַׁבָּת בְּרֵאשִׁית, שֶׁאֵין יִשְׂרָאֵל מְצֻוִּים עַל הָרְאִיָּה, וְהוּא בָּא לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת:


**And whenever the prince goes in** on the New Moon and on the Sabbath of Creation, when Israel is not commanded to appear at the Temple, and he comes in to prostrate himself.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:8:2)


**דֶּרֶךְ אוּלָם הַשַּׁעַר יָבוֹא וּבְדַרְכּוֹ יֵצֵא.** בְּאוֹתוֹ שַׁעַר עַצְמוֹ יֵצֵא, וְאֵינוֹ מְצֻוֶּה לַעֲשׂוֹת הָעֲזָרָה קַפַּנְדַּרְיָא. אֲבָל בַּמּוֹעֲדִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בָּהֶם (דברים טז:טז) ״יֵרָאֶה כָל זְכוּרְךָ״, זָקוּק הוּא לַעֲשׂוֹת קַפַּנְדַּרְיָא כִּשְׁאָר הָעָם, הַיְינוּ דִּכְתִיב **בְּתוֹכָם בְּבוֹאָם יָבוֹא וּבְצֵאתָם יֵצֵאוּ**:


**he shall go in by way of the vestibule of the gate, and by the same way shall he go out** Through the very same gate he shall go out, and he is not commanded to make the Court a short-cut. But on the festivals, concerning which it is stated (Deut. 16:16): “shall all your males appear,” he is required to make it a short-cut like the rest of the people. That is what is written (verse 10): “goes in among them when they go in, and when they go out, they [the prince and people together] go out.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:9:1)


**וּבְבוֹא עַם הָאָרֶץ דֶּרֶךְ שַׁעַר צָפוֹן וְגוֹ׳ כִּי נִכְחוֹ יֵצֵא.** מִצְוָה עֲלֵיהֶם שֶׁיִּתְרָאוּ בְּתוֹךְ הָעֲזָרָה יָפֶה יָפֶה:


**But when the people of the land come, etc., by way of the north gate, etc., but he shall go out by that which is opposite it** It is incumbent upon them to be seen in full view in the Court.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:10:1)


**וְהַנָּשִׂיא.** גַּם הוּא כְּשֶׁנִּכְנָס לָעֲזָרָה לָבֹא בְּפִשְׁפַּשׁ הַהֵיכָל הַדְּרוֹמִי לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת, גַּם הוּא מְצֻוֶּה עָלָיו לַעֲשׂוֹת הָעֲזָרָה קַפַּנְדַּרְיָא, וְיָבֹא דֶּרֶךְ שַׁעַר צָפוֹן וְיֵצֵא דֶּרֶךְ שַׁעַר נֶגֶב עִם שְׁאָר עַם הָאָרֶץ, וְנִכְנָס וְיֵצֵא נִכְחוֹ דֶּרֶךְ שַׁעַר הָאַחֵר. וְזֶהוּ **בְּתוֹכָם בְּבוֹאָם יָבוֹא וּבְצֵאתָם יֵצֵאוּ** – כֻּלָּם, הַנָּשִׂיא עִם שְׁאָר הָעָם. וְלֹא יָבֹא דֶּרֶךְ שַׁעַר הַמִּזְרָחִי כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהוּא בָּא בְּיוֹם הַחֹדֶשׁ וּבְיוֹם שַׁבָּת בְּרֵאשִׁית, שֶׁהַשַּׁעַר הַמִּזְרָחִי אֵין נִכְחוֹ שַׁעַר בַּמַּעֲרָב:


**The prince** When he enters the Temple Court through the southern wicket of the Heichal to prostrate himself, he too must make the Temple Court a short-cut. He shall enter by way of the northern gate and leave by way of the southern gate with the rest of the people of the land. This is the meaning of “enters in their midst when they go in, and when they go out, they go out”all of them, the prince with the rest of the people. And he shall not enter by way of the eastern gate as he regularly does on the New Moon and on the Sabbath of Creation, for the eastern gate has no gate opposite it in the west.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:12:1)


**וְכִי יַעֲשֶׂה הַנָּשִׂיא נְדָבָה.** בְּשֵׁשֶׁת יְמֵי הַמַּעֲשֶׂה:


**And when the prince brings a freewill offering** on the six working days.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:12:2)


**וּפָתַח לוֹ אֶת הַשַּׁעַר וְגוֹ׳.** לֹא נכְנַס בּוֹ לַהֵיכָל, אֶלָּא עוֹמֵד שָׁם וְהַכֹּהֲנִים יַעֲשׂוּ אֶת עוֹלָתוֹ וְאֶת שְׁלָמָיו, וְהִשְׁתַּחֲוָה וְיָצָא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: **כַּאֲשֶׁר יַעֲשֶׂה בְּיוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת**. וּמַה אוֹמֵר בְּיוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת? **וְעָמַד עַל מְזוּזַת הַשַּׁעַר וְעָשׂוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים אֶת עוֹלָתוֹ וְגוֹ׳**:


**one shall then open for him the gate, etc.** Not to enter the Heichal through it, but he shall stand there, and the priests shall make his burnt offering and his peace-offering, and he shall prostrate himself and leave, as it is said: “as he would do on the Sabbath day.” Now what is stated regarding the Sabbath day (here)? (Verse 2) “And he shall stand at the doorpost of the gate, and the priests shall offer his burnt offering, etc.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:12:3)


**אַחֲרֵי צֵאתוֹ.** וְאֵינוֹ אוֹמֵר כָּאן **וְהַשַּׁעַר לֹא יִסָּגֵר עַד הָעָרֶב** כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בְּיוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת; שֶׁבְּיוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת הוּא אוֹמֵר **וְהִשְׁתַּחֲווּ עַם הָאָרֶץ פֶּתַח הַשַּׁעַר הַהוּא**, לְפִיכָךְ מַנִּיחוֹ פָּתוּחַ, אֲבָל בְּחוֹל אֵין דַּרְכָּם לָבֹא לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת, שֶׁכָּל אֶחָד עָסוּק בִּמְלַאכְתּוֹ, לְפִיכָךְ **וְסָגַר אֶת הַשַּׁעַר אַחֲרֵי צֵאתוֹ**:


**after he has gone out** He does not say here: “but the gate shall not be closed until the evening,” as he says regarding the Sabbath day. For regarding the Sabbath day it says (verse 3): “And the people of the land shall prostrate themselves at the entrance of that gate.” Therefore, it is left open. But on weekdays it is not customary for them to come to prostrate themselves, for everyone is occupied with work; therefore, “after he has gone out, one shall close the gate.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:13:1)


**וְכֶבֶשׂ בֶּן שְׁנָתוֹ.** עוֹלַת הַתָּמִיד:


**And a lamb of the first year** the daily sacrifice.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:14:1)


**שִׁשִּׁית הָאֵיפָה.** יְרוּשַׁלְמִית, שֶׁהִיא חֹמֶשׁ שֶׁל מִדְבָּרִית (מנחות ז.), וְהֵן שְׁנֵי עֶשְׂרוֹנִים, הָאֶחָד לְמִנְחַת הַתָּמִיד וְאֶחָד לַחֲבִיתִין. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחֲבִיתִין קְרֵבִין לַחֲצָאִין, מֵבִיא הוּא בַּבֹּקֶר עִשָּׂרוֹן שָׁלֵם וְחוֹצֵהוּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בִּמְנָחוֹת (נ:):


**a sixth of an ephah** of the [post-Exodus] Jerusalemite measure, which is a fifth of the [Mosaic] “measure of the desert,” equaling two tenth parts, one for the daily meal-offering and one for the pancakes. And although the pancakes were offered up by halves, he brings a complete tenth part in the morning and divides it in half, as we learned in Menahoth (4:5)


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:14:2)


**לָרֹס אֶת הַסֹּלֶת.** אִילְפַּרְ״סִיר בְּלַעַ״ז, לְ״אַ אִימְ״לְלִיר בְּלַעַ״ז. לָרֹס אֶת הַסֹּלֶת – לְכַתֵּת וְלִבְלוֹל בּוֹ אֶת הַסֹּלֶת, לְשׁוֹן רְסִיס, כְּמוֹ ״וְהִכָּה הַבַּיִת הַגָּדוֹל רְסִיסִים״ (עמוס ו:יא), וּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה: אִם הָיָה עָבֶה אוֹ מְרֻסָּס (שבת ח:ה):


**to stir [with] the fine flour** [Heb. לָרֹם,] asperger in French, to moisten, besprinkle. Manuscripts read: ameller in Old French, to mix. לָרֹם אֶתהַסֹלֶת, to crush and mix with it the fine flour, a form of word for crushing (רִסוּם), as in (Amos 6: 11): “and he shall smite the great house into splinters (רְסִיסִים),” and in the language of the Mishnah (Shab. 8:6): If it was thick or cracked (מְרֻסָם).



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:17:1)


**שְׁנַת הַדְּרוֹר.** שְׁנַת הַיּוֹבֵל (ויקרא כה:י):


**the year of liberty** the Jubilee year.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:17:2)


**וְשָׁבַת.** כְּמוֹ וְשָׁבָה:


**and then it comes back** [Heb. וְשָּׁבָת], like וְשָּׁבָה.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:17:3)


**אַךְ נַחֲלָתוֹ בָּנָיו לָהֶם תִּהְיֶה.** כְּמוֹ: אַךְ לְבָנָיו תִּהְיֶה נַחֲלָתוֹ, וְלֹא תִנָּתֵן לִצְמִיתוּת לְאַחֵר (ויקרא כה:ל):


**only to his sons shall his inheritance belong** [Only his inheritance has sons, to them it shall be.] This is like: only to his sons shall his inheritance belong, and it shall not be given permanently to anyone else.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:19:1)


**וַיְבִיאֵנִי בַמָּבוֹא.** דֶּרֶךְ הָאַמָּה שֶׁבְּזָוִית בֵּית הַחִילְפוֹת, שֶׁנִּכְנָסִין בּוֹ לַאֲוִיר עֶשְׂרִים שֶׁבֵּין הַלְּשָׁכוֹת שֶׁבַּצָּפוֹן לְבֵין תָּאֵי הַהֵיכָל (ראה רש״י יחזקאל מב:ד):


**Now he brought me through the entry** by way of the cubit at the corner of the depository of the knives, by which they enter the space of twenty [cubits] that is between the chambers in the north and the cells of the Heichal.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:19:2)


**אֲשֶׁר עַל כְּתֶף הַשַּׁעַר.** שַׁעַר הַצְּפוֹנִי. **כְּתֶף הַשַּׁעַר**, אֲשֶׁר מִן הַשַּׁעַר לְמַעֲרָב:


**that was on the side of the gate** the northern gate; the side of the gate that is west of the gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:19:3)


**הַפֹּנוֹת צָפוֹנָה.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהֶם פְּתָחִים לְצַד חֲצַר הַחִיצוֹנָה לְצָפוֹן, וּפְתָחִים הָיוּ לָהֶם לַאֲוִיר הָעֶשְׂרִים:


**which face northward** Which have entrances to the side of the Outer Court to the north; and they had entrances to the space of twenty [cubits].


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:19:4)


**בַּיַּרְכָתַיִם יָמָּה.** בְּסוֹף הַלְּשָׁכוֹת לְמַעֲרָב:


**at the end toward the west** at the end of the chambers to the west.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:20:1)


**לְבִלְתִּי הוֹצִיא.** כְּמוֹ לְהוֹצִיא, שֶׁקָּדְשֵׁי קָדָשִׁים נִפְסָלִין בְּיוֹצֵא (שבועות טו.):


**so as not to bring [them] out** [Heb. הוֹצִיא,] like לְהוֹצִיא, for the “most holy sacrifices” become disqualified by being taken out.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:20:2)


**לְקַדֵּשׁ אֶת הָעָם.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לְאִתְעָרְבָא עִם עַמָּא״:


**to mingle with the people** [Heb. לְקַדֵשׁ אֶתהָעָם lit. to sanctify the people.] Jonathan renders: to mingle with the people.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:21:1)


**מִקְצוֹעַ.** אַנְגְלָ״אשׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**corners** [Heb. מִקְצוֹעֵי,] angles in French, corners.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:22:1)


**חֲצֵרוֹת קְטֻרוֹת.** שָׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (מדות ב,ה): אֵין קְטוּרוֹת אֶלָּא שֶׁאֵינָן מְקוֹרוֹת; וְשָׁם מְפָרֵשׁ מָה הָיוּ מְשַׁמְּשׁוֹת:


**open enclosures** We learned in Tractate Middoth (2:5): קְטֻרוֹת means that they had no roofs, and there it is explained what they were used for.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:22:2)


**מְהֻקְצָעוֹת.** בְּזָוִיּוֹת מִקְצוֹעוֹת הֶחָצֵר:


**in the corners** in the corners of the Court.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:23:1)


**וְטוּר סָבִיב בָּהֶם.** שׁוּרַת נִדְבָּךְ חוֹמַת אֲבָנִים בּוֹלֵט מִן הַחוֹמָה סָמוּךְ לָאָרֶץ, וּבוֹ נְקָבִים, מְקוֹם שְׁפִיתַת קְדֵרוֹת; וּמִתַּחַת הַטּוּר חָלָל לְהַסִּיק בּוֹ אוּר מִתַּחְתָּיו, וְהַקְּדֵרוֹת מִלְמַעְלָה:


**And a row around in them** A row of masonry, a wall of stones protruding from the wall, near the ground, in which there were holes, a place to stand pots. Below the row [of masonry] was a space in which to kindle a fire and the pots [would be heated] above it.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:23:2)


**וּמְבַשְּׁלוֹת עָשׂוּי.** הוּא הֶחָלָל מִתַּחַת שְׁפִיתַת הַקְּדֵרוֹת:


**and a place for cooking was made** that is the space below the placing of the pots.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 46:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/46:24:1)


**אֶת זֶבַח הָעָם.** חָזֶה וְשׁוֹק שֶׁל שְׁלָמִים (ויקרא ז:ל-לד), שֶׁאֵינָן נִפְסָלִין בְּצֵאתוֹ חוּץ לְעֶזְרַת יִשְׂרָאֵל כְּמוֹ הַחַטָּאת וְהָאָשָׁם (זבחים ה,ג-ז):


**the people’s sacrifices** The breast and the thigh of the peace offering, which do not become unfit by going out of the Israelites’ Court as do the sin-offering and the guilt-offering.



## Chapter 47


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:1:1)


**וַיְשִׁיבֵנִי.** לְתוֹךְ הֶחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִית:


**Then he brought me back** into the Inner Court.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:1:2)


**אֶל פֶּתַח הַבַּיִת וְהַמַּיִם יֹרְדִים מִתַּחַת מִכֶּתֶף הַבַּיִת הַיְמָנִית.** נוֹבְעִין מִתַּחַת הַמִּפְתָּן, שֶׁהוּא בְּאֶמְצַע הַמִּזְרָח, וּמְצַדְּדִין בַּאֲלַכְסוֹן לְצַד הַיָּמִין, וְיוֹצְאִים מֵעֲזָרָה בִּדְרוֹם הַמִּזְבֵּחַ, וְיוֹצְאִין חוּץ לָעִיר. בְּמַסֶּכֶת יוֹמָא שָׁנִינוּ: אָמַר רַבִּי פִּנְחָס מִשּׁוּם רַב הוּנָא צִפּוֹרָאָה: מַעְיָן הַיּוֹצֵא מִבֵּית קָדְשֵׁי הַקֳּדָשִׁים בִּתְחִלָּה דּוֹמֶה לְקַרְנֵי סְלָעִים, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִגִּיעַ לְפֶתַח הַהֵיכָל נַעֲשָׂה כְּחוּט שֶׁל שְׁתִי, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִגִּיעַ לְפֶתַח הָאוּלָם נַעֲשָׂה כְּחוּט שֶׁל עֵרֶב, כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִגִּיעַ לְפֶתַח הָעֲזָרָה נַעֲשָׂה כְּפִי פַּךְ קָטָן (יומא עז:):


**To the entrance of the House.** And the water was descending from under the right side of the House, flowing from under the threshold, which was in the center of the east[ern side] and [going] diagonally toward the right, and going out of the Court at the south of the altar, and proceeding out of the city. In Tractate Yoma we learned (77b): Said Rabbi Phinehas in the name of Rab Huna of Sepphoris: The fountain emanating from the Holy of Holies at first resembles the points of rocks. As soon as it reaches the entrance of the Hall, it becomes like a woof thread. When it reaches the entrance of the Heichal, it becomes like a warp thread. When it reaches the entrance of the Court, it becomes like the mouth of a small jug.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:2:1)


**וַיּוֹצִיאֵנִי.** מִן הֶחָצֵר הַפְּנִימִית אֶל הֶחָצֵר הַחִיצוֹנָה, בְּשַׁעַר הַצָּפוֹן:


**And he took me out** of the Inner Court to the Outer Court through the northern gate.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:2:2)


**וַיְסִבֵּנִי.** סָבִיב חוֹמַת הַפְּנִימִית בְּתוֹךְ הַחִיצוֹנָה:


**and led me around** around the inner wall within the outer one.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:2:3)


**אֶל שַׁעַר.** הַחִיצוֹנָה הַפּוֹנֶה קָדִים:


**to the...Gate** The outer [gate] that faces eastward.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:2:4)


**מַיִם מְפַכִּים.** בויאונ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, רְחָבִים כְּרֹחַב פִּי הַפַּךְ. כָּךְ פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ כִּדְלְעֵיל (יומא עז:):


**water was trickling** [Heb. מְפַכִּים,] buient in O.F., dripping, trickling, as wide as the width of the mouth of a jug. So did our Sages explain it, as above.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:3:1)


**וַיָּמָד אֶלֶף בָּאַמָּה.** חוּץ לַחוֹמָה; וְכָל אוֹתָן אֶלֶף אַמּוֹת לֹא גָּבַר הַנַּחַל לִהְיוֹת עָמוֹק אֶלָּא עַד אָפְסַיִם, הֵם הַקַּרְסוּלִּים (ירושלמי שקלים ו:ב), שֶׁקּוֹרִין קביליי״ש בְּלַעַ״ז:


**he measured one thousand cubits** outside the wall. For those thousand cubits, the stream did not swell to the extent of being deep up to the אֳפְסָיִם ; the ankles, called chevilles in Fr.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:4:1)


**וַיָּמָד אֶלֶף.** אֲחֵרוֹת, וְהֶעֱמִיקוּ עַד בִּרְכַּיִם, וְעוֹד אֶלֶף – וְהֶעֱמִיקוּ עַד **מָתְנַיִם**, מִכָּאן וָאֵילָךְ: **נַחַל אֲשֶׁר לֹא אוּכַל לַעֲבֹר**; מִכָּאן שֶׁאָסוּר לִיכָּנֵס בַּמַּיִם מִן הַמָּתְנַיִם וּלְמַעְלָה, שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁטְפוּהוּ הַמַּיִם (יומא עז:). מִשֶּׁבָּא לְ**מֵי מָתְנָיִם** יָצָא עַל שְׂפַת הַנָּהָר וּמָדַד עוֹד אֶלֶף לְפָנָיו, **נַחַל אֲשֶׁר לֹא** יַעֲבֹר, וְרַגְלָיו בַּיַּבָּשָׁה עוֹמְדִין; הַיְינוּ דִּכְתִיב **וַיְשִׁיבֵנִי עַל שְׂפַת הַנָּחַל** – כְּלוֹמַר: אֵצֶל שְׂפַת הַנַּחַל שֶׁשָּׁם הָיָה עוֹמֵד:


**And he measured one thousand** another [one thousand cubits], and they deepened up to the knees, and another thousand, and they deepened up to the loins. From here on, it was a stream that I could not cross. From here we learn that one should not enter water from the loins and higher, lest the water over power him. As soon as he came to the water reaching the loins, he went out upon the bank of the river and measured another thousand before him, a stream that cannot be crossed, and his feet were standing on dry land. This is what is written (v. 6): “and he brought me back [upon] the bank of the stream,” i.e., near the bank of the stream that is where he had been standing.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:5:1)


**גָאוּ.** כְּמוֹ גָדְלוּ:


**for the water was so high** [Heb. גָאוּ,lit. became haughty,] like גָדְלוּ, became large.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:5:2)


**מֵי שָׂחוּ.** נוֹאִירֵינְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז, מַיִם שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לָשׂוּט בָּהֶן וְלֹא לְעָבְרָם בָּרֶגֶל שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁטְפוּהוּ; שָׂחוּ, שַׁיִּיט, כְּמוֹ ״כַּאֲשֶׁר יְפָרֵשׂ הַשּׂוֹחֶה לִשְׂחוֹת״ (ישעיהו כה:יא):


**water for swimming** [Heb. שָׂחוּ,] noer in O.F., to swim, water that one must swim in and not cross by foot lest it overpower him. שָׂחוּ means swimming, like (Isa. 25:11): “as the swimmer spreads out [his hands] to swim הַשׂחֶה לִשְׂחוֹת.”



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:6:1)


**הֲרָאִיתָ בֶן אָדָם.** כַּמָּה מִתְגַּבֵּר הַנַּחַל הַזֶּה?


**Have you seen, Son of man** how much this stream has swelled?



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:7:1)


**בְּשׁוּבֵנִי וְהִנֵּה אֶל שְׂפַת הַנַּחַל וגו׳.** בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁהוֹלִיכֵנִי וְהֵשִׁיבַנִי, צָמְחוּ עַל שְׂפַת הַנַּחַל **עֵץ רַב מְאֹד מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה** .**עֵץ** אאביר״ץ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**and behold, upon the bank of the stream, etc.** While he led me and brought me back, a very great profusion of trees had grown on the banks of the stream on each side. **profusion of trees** [Heb. עֵץ,] abres; erbeiz in Old French, trees, vegetation.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:1)


**אֶל הַגְּלִילָה.** לָאמַרְקָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**to the...frontier** [Heb. הֳגְלִילָה,] la marche, frontier (province).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:2)


**הַקַּדְמוֹנָה.** הַמִּזְרָחִית. שָׁנוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בְּתוֹסֶפְתָּא שֶׁל סֻכָּה (סוכה ג:ט): לְהֵיכָן יוֹצְאִין? לְיָמָּהּ שֶׁל טְבֶרְיָא וּלְיָמָּהּ שֶׁל סְדוֹם וְלַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל, לְרַפּוֹת אֶת מֵימֵיהֶם הַמְּלוּחִים וּלְמַתְּקָן:


**the eastern** [Heb. הַקַּדְמוֹנָה,] the eastern. Our Rabbis taught in the Tosefta of Succah (3:3): Where do they flow? To the Sea of Tiberias (Kinnereth), the Sea of Sodom (Dead Sea), and to the Great Sea, to heal their salty waters and to sweeten them.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:3)


**וְיָרְדוּ עַל הָעֲרָבָה.** זוֹ יַמָּהּ שֶׁל טְבֶרְיָא:


[“Will descend upon the plain”] this is the Sea of Tiberias.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:4)


**וּבָאוּ הַיָּמָּה.** זוֹ יָמָּה שֶׁל סְדוֹם:


[“And come to the sea”] this is the Sea of Sodom.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:5)


**אֶל הַיָּמָּה הַמּוּצָאִים.** זוֹ יָמָּה שֶׁל אוֹקְיָנוֹס (תנחומא, חיי שרה ג), שֶׁהוּא מוּצָא מִן הַיִּשּׁוּב לְהַקִּיף אֶת הָעוֹלָם:


**to the sea that is brought out** This is the אוֹקיַינוּס ocean, which is brought out from civilization to encompass the world.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:8:6)


**וְנִרְפְּאוּ.** הַמַּיִם מִמְּלִיחָתָן. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (ירושלמי שקלים ו, ב): לָמָּה קוֹרֵא אוֹתָם מוּצָאִים? עַל שֵׁם שְׁנֵי פְּעָמִים שֶׁיָּצְאוּ:


**And [it will come] to heal** the waters of their saltiness. The Midrash Aggadah [says] Yerushalmi Shekalim (6:2): Why does he call them הַמוּצָאִים (in the plural)? Because of the two times that they went out.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:9:1)


**אֶל כָּל אֲשֶׁר יָבוֹא שָׁם נַחֲלַיִם.** הַנַּחַל הַזֶּה הַמִּתְחַלֵּק לְכַמָּה נְחָלִים, מְמַתֵּק אֶת הַיַּמִּים שֶׁמִּתְעָרֵב בְּתוֹכָם, וְדָגָתָם הַנִּשְׁרֶצֶת בָּם חַיִּים וּמְתוּקִים:


**wherever the two streams will go** This stream, which divides into many streams, sweetens the seas into which it mingles, and their fish, which swarm in them, are alive and sweet.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:10:1)


**וְהָיָה עָמְדוּ עָלָיו דַּוָּגִים וְגוֹ׳ לַחֲרָמִים.** לִמְכַמֹּרֶת:


**And it will be [a place] beside which fishermen will stand etc., nets** [Heb. לַחֲרָמִים,] for nets


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:10:2)


**לְמִינָה תִּהְיֶה דְגָתָם.** לֹא מַפִּיק הֵ״א, וּפֵירוּשׁוֹ: לְמִינִים הַרְבֵּה תִּהְיֶה דְגָתָם:


**their fish will be of many kinds** לְמִינָה.] This does not have a “mappik hey,” and it means that its fish will be of many kinds of many kinds.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:10:3)


**לְמִינָה.** לְשׁוֹן רוֹב מִינִים, כְּמוֹ ״וְהַדָּגָה אֲשֶׁר בַּיְאוֹר״ (שמות ז:יח); ״כָּל אַנְחָתָהּ הִשְׁבַּתִּי״ (ישעיה כא:ב):


**of many kinds** [Heb. לְמִינָה,] a form of the word to denote a profusion of kinds, as in (Exod. 7:21): “And the fish וְהֲדָּגָה in the river”; (Isa. 21:2): “All sighs (אָנְחָתָה) have I brought to an end.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:11:1)


**בִּצֹּאתָיו.** שֶׁל יָם, כְּמוֹ ״הֲיִגְאֶה גֹמֶא בְּלֹא בִצָּה וְגוֹ׳״ (איוב ח:יא); מָרֵישְׁ״ק בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Its marshes** [The marshes] of the sea, as in (Job 8:11): “Can papyrus shoot up without a marsh (בִצָה)?” This is maresc in Old French, a marsh.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:11:2)


**וּגְבָאָיו.** כְּמוֹ ״עֲשֵׂה הַנַּחַל הַזֶּה גֵבִים גֵּבִים״ (מלכים ב ג:טז). הַבִּצִּים הֵם הַבִּצְעִים שֶׁסְּבִיבוֹתָיו, וְהַגֵּבִים הֵם נְקָעִים שֶׁהַמַּיִם נֶאֱסָפִים בָּהֶם:


**and its pools** [Heb. וּגְבָאָיו,] like (II Kings 3:16): “This valley will be full of pools.” The בִּצִים are the marshes around it, and the גֵבִים are ditches in which water is gathered.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:11:3)


**וְלֹא יֵרָפְאוּ.** לֵהָפֵךְ לִמְתִיקָה, לָמָּה? כִּי **לְמֶלַח נִתָּנוּ**:


**will not be healed** to be turned sweet. Why? Because “they will be set aside for salt [mines].”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:11:4)


**וְלֹא יֵרָפְאוּ.** הַוָּי״ו הַזּוֹ טְפֵלָה וִיתֵרָה:


וְלא יִרָפְאוּ This “vav” is subordinate and superfluous.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:12:1)


**לָחֳדָשָׁיו יְבַכֵּר.** מִדֵּי חֹדֶשׁ בְּחָדְשׁוֹ יִבְשַׁל פֵּירוֹתָיו:


**month after month its fruits will ripen** From month to month its fruits will ripen.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:12:2)


**לִתְרוּפָה.** פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ ז״ל (סנהדרין ק.): לְהַתִּיר פֶּה אִלְּמִים וּפֶה עֲקָרוֹת. וּמַשְׁמָעוֹ לְשׁוֹן רְפוּאָה, כְּמוֹ ״וַיְרַפּאוּ אֶת שֶׁבֶר עַמִּי עַל נְקַלָּה״ (ירמיה ו:יד), וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**for a cure** [Heb. לִתְרוּפָה.] Our Sages of blessed memory explained: to unlock the mouth (לְהָתִיר פֶה) of the dumb and the mouth [of the womb] of the barren (Men. 98a), but its apparent meaning is a word for healing, as in (Jer. 6:14): “And they healed (וַיְרַפְאוּ) the breach of My people easily.” And so did Menahem connect it.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:13:1)


**גֵּה גְבוּל.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״דֵּין תְּחוּמָא״, כְּמוֹ זֶה גְּבוּל וְכֵן ״וּנְתַתִּיךָ לְבַג לְגוֹיִם״ (לעיל כה:ז) – כְּמוֹ לָבַז לְגוֹיִם; וְיֵשׁ עוֹד לִפְתֹּר: **גֵּה** – לְשׁוֹן גַּיְא:


**This is the border** [Heb. גֵּה גְבוּל.] Jonathan renders: This is the border, just like זֶה גְבוּל ; and so too (above 25:7): “and I have given you for plunder (לבג) to the nations,” is like לְבַז. It may also be interpreted as a form of the word for valley (גַיְא).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:13:2)


**יוֹסֵף חֲבָלִים.** יוֹסֵף יִטּוֹל שְׁנֵי חֲלָקִים, אֶחָד לִמְנַשֶּׁה וְאֶחָד לְאֶפְרַיִם:


**Joseph portions** Joseph shall take two portions, one for Manasseh and one for Ephraim.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:14:1)


**אִישׁ כְּאָחִיו.** לֹא כְּהָרִאשׁוֹנָה, אִישׁ לְפִי פְקוּדָיו (במדבר כו:נד), אֶלָּא עַכְשָׁיו כָּל הַשְּׁבָטִים שָׁוִין. וּכְמִין שׁוּרַת הַכֶּרֶם, כָּל חֵלֶק וְחֵלֶק מִן הַגְּבוּל הַמִּזְרָחִי עַד יָם אוֹקְיָנוֹס לַמַּעֲרָב, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן (יחזקאל מח), וְאֵין שְׁנֵי שְׁבָטִים בִּרְצוּעָה אַחַת:


**as one another** and not like the first one (Num. 26:54): “to each according to his numbered ones.” Now, instead, all the tribes will be equal, and every portion will be like a row in a vineyard from the eastern border until the ocean to the west, as is explained in the account, and no two tribes will be in strip.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:15:1)


**וְזֶה גְּבוּל הָאָרֶץ.** תְּחוּמֵי מֵצְרֵי אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And this is the border of the land** the boundaries of the Land of Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:15:2)


**לִפְאַת צָפוֹנָה.** רוּחַ צְפוֹנִית:


**to the northern side** [Heb. לִפְאַת צָפוֹנָה,] the northern direction.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:15:3)


**מִן הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל.** שֶׁהוּא גְּבוּל לְמַעֲרָב:


**from the Great Sea** which is the western border.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:15:4)


**הַדֶּרֶךְ חֶתְלוֹן.** בָּא לוֹ לְצַד מִזְרָח אֶל חֶתְלוֹן וְאֶל **צְדָדָה**, וְאֶל **חֲמָת וּבֵרוֹתָה** וְ**סִבְרַיִם** – כָּל הָעֲיָרוֹת הַלָּלוּ בְּמֶצֶר צָפוֹן:


**the way to Hethlon** It comes to the east to Hethlon, to Zedad, to Hamath, Berothah, and Sibraim. All these cities are on the northern border.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:16:1)


**חֲצַר הַתִּיכוֹן.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״בְּרִיכַת עַגְבָאֵי״:


**Hazer-hatticon** Jonathan renders: the pond of the Agebeans.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:16:2)


**אֲשֶׁר אֶל גְּבוּל חוֹרָן. גְּבוּל דַּמֶּשֶׂק** בַּמִּזְרָח סָמוּךְ לַמִּקְצוֹעַ:


**which is by the border of Hauran** The border of Damascus is in the east, near the corner.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:17:1)


**וְהָיָה גְבוּל מִן הַיָּם חֲצַר עֵינוֹן.** וְהָיָה גְבוּל רוּחַ **צָפוֹן** מִן הַיָּם עַד חֲצַר עֵינוֹן:


**And the border shall be from the sea to Hazer-enon** The northern border shall be from the sea until Hazer-enon.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:17:2)


**גְּבוּל דַּמֶּשֶׂק.** שֶׁהוּא בִּגְבוּל דַּמֶּשֶׂק; שֶׁחֲצַר עֵינָן בְּמִקְצוֹעַ צְפוֹנִית מִזְרָחִית, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בַּגְּבוּלִין שֶׁכָּתַב מֹשֶׁה רַבֵּינוּ עָלָיו הַשָּׁלוֹם ״וְהָיוּ תוֹצְאֹתָיו חֲצַר עֵינָן וְגוֹ׳ וְהִתְאַוִּיתֶם לָכֶם לִגְבוּל קֵדְמָה מֵחֲצַר עֵינָן וְגוֹ׳״ (במדבר לד:י). גְּבוּל דַּמֶּשֶׂק בְּמִקְצוֹעַ צְפוֹנִית מִזְרָחִית אֵצֶל חֲצַר עֵינָן, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה חֲצַר עֵינוּן וּגְבוּל דַּמֶּשֶׂק. וּגְבוּל חֲמָת בְּמִקְצוֹעַ צְפוֹנִית מַעֲרָבִית, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״זֶה יִהְיֶה לָכֶם גְּבוּל צָפוֹן מִן הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל תְּתָאוּ לָכֶם הֹר הָהָר מֵהֹר הָהָר תְּתָאוּ לְבֹא חֲמָת וּמִשָּׁם צְדָדָה״ (שם):


**the border of Damascus** [i.e.,] which is on the border of Damascus, i.e., Hazer-enon is on the northeastern corner, as is explicated in the [description of] the boundaries which Moses, our master, may he rest in peace, wrote (Num. 34:9-10f.): “and its ends shall be Hazer -as your eastern border [the territory] from Hazer-enan, etc.” The border of Damascus is in the northeastern corner beside Hazer-enan, as is stated above: “Hazer-enan” and “the border of Damascus.” The border of Hamath is at the northwestern corner, as is stated (ibid. v. 7f.): “And this shall be your northern border: from the Great Sea you shall draw a line extending to the road [leading] to Hamath, and from there to Zedad.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:17:3)


**וְצָפוֹן צָפוֹנָה וּגְבוּל חֲמָת.** כְּלוֹמַר: כָּל מֵצַר הַצָּפוֹן הַמְפֹרָשׁ בַּתּוֹרָה (במדבר לד:ז-ח) וּגְבוּל חֲמָת, שֶׁאַף הִיא בְּמֵצַר הַצָּפוֹן, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וְזֶה יִהְיֶה לָכֶם גְּבוּל צָפוֹן מִן הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל תְּתָאוּ לָכֶם הֹר הָהָר. מֵהֹר הָהָר תְּתָאוּ לְבֹא חֲמָת וְהָיוּ תּוֹצְאוֹת הַגְּבוּל צְדָדָה״ (במדבר לד:ז-ח), הִיא צְדָדָה הָאָמוּר כָּאן, **הַדֶּרֶךְ חֶתְלוֹן לָבוֹא צְדָדָה**:


**and in the north northward is the border of Hamath** i.e., the entire northern border delineated in the Torah, and the border of Hamath-which is also on the northern border as is stated (ibid. verse 7f.): “And this shall be your northern border: from the Great Sea you shall draw a line extending to Mount Hor. From Mount Hor you shall draw a line extending to the road [leading] to Hamath, and the ends of the border shall be toward Zedad.” That is the Zedad mentioned here: “the way to Hethlon to the road [leading] to Zedad.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:17:4)


**וְאֵת פְּאַת צָפוֹן.** וַהֲרֵי לְךָ פְּאַת צָפוֹן:


**this is the northern side** [Lit. and the northern side.] And here you have the northern side.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:18:1)


**וּמִבֵּין הַגִּלְעָד וּמִבֵּין אֶרֶץ יִשְׂראֵל.** מְצַר מִזְרָחִי שֶׁל אֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן, וְאֵין עֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן בְּתוֹךְ מְצָרִים הַלָּלוּ, כִּי אִם אֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן לְבַדָּהּ. וְכֵן בַּגְּבוּלִין הַמְמוּצָרִים בַּתּוֹרָה (במדבר לד) אֵין עֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן בְּתוֹכָם. וְכֵן פֵּרוּשׁ הַמִּקְרָא הַזֶּה: וּמְצַר מִזְרָחִי לְאֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן אֲשֶׁר בֵּין חַוְרָן וְדַמֶּשֶׂק, הָעוֹמְדִין לָהּ בְּמִקְצוֹעַ צְפוֹנִית מִזְרָחִית, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה בָּעִנְיָן, וּבֵין הַגִּלְעָד וְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲשֶׁר בְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן מִזְרָחָה:


**between Gilead and the land of Israel** The eastern border of the land of Canaan. Trans-Jordan is not included within these borders; only the land of Canaan. Similarly, the borders demarcated in the Torah do not include Trans Jordan. And this is the meaning of this verse: And from the eastern border of the land of Canaan, which is between Hauran and Damascus which are situated on its northeastern corner, as is stated above in this section, and between Gilead and the land of Israel that is on the eastern side of the Jordan.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:18:2)


**הַיַּרְדֵּן מִגְּבוּל עַל הַיָּם הַקַּדְמוֹנִי תָּמֹדּוּ.** הַיַּרְדֵּן הוּא מֵצַר מִזְרָחִי שֶׁלָּהּ, מִגְּבוּל מִקְצוֹעַ הַצָּפוֹן, הוּא חֲצַר עֵינָן הַמְפֹרָשׁ לְמַעְלָה (פס׳ יז), עַד הַיָּם הַקַּדְמוֹנִי – הוּא יָם הַמֶּלַח, שֶׁהוּא בְּמִקְצוֹעַ דְּרוֹמִית מִזְרָחִית, **תָּמֹדּוּ** אֹרֶךְ מֵצַר מִזְרָחִי. וְאַף בַּתּוֹרָה כָּךְ מְפֹרָשׁ בִּגְבוּל קֵדְמָה: ״וְהָיוּ תוֹצְאֹתָיו יָם הַמֶּלַח״ (במדבר לד:יב) – בְּמִקְצוֹעַ דְּרוֹמִית:


**is the Jordan; from the border by the eastern sea shall you measure** The Jordan is its eastern border from the boundary of the northern corner—that is the Hazer-enan delineated above, until the eastern sea, the Dead Sea, which is in the south-eastern corner. This shall you measure as the length of the eastern border. In the Torah, too, the eastern boundary is delineated in this manner (Num. 34:12): “and its ends shall be the Salt Sea” in the southern corner.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:18:3)


**וְאֵת פְּאַת קָדִימָה.** כְּלוֹמַר: וַהֲרֵי לְךָ פְּאַת קָדִים:


**this is the eastern side** As if to say, [as translated,] this is the eastern side.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:19:1)


**מִתָּמָר.** מִירִיחוֹ, שֶׁהִיא ״עִיר הַתְּמָרִים״ (דברים לד:ג); וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן:


**from Tamar** From Jericho, which is the city of date palms, (Deut. 33:3), and so did Jonathan render it.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:19:2)


**עַד מֵי מְרִיבַת קָדֵשׁ.** הוּא מִדְבַּר צִין (במדבר כז:יד):


**until the water of Meriboth Kadesh** that is the desert of Zin. (Num 27:14)


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:19:3)


**נַחְלָה אֶל הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל.** וּמִשָּׁם הוֹלֵךְ הַגְּבוּל עַד נַחַל מִצְרַיִם הַנּוֹפֵל בַּיָּם הַגָּדוֹל בְּמִקְצוֹעַ דְּרוֹמִית מַעֲרָבִית, וְאַף מֹשֶׁה כָּךְ נָתַן גְּבוּל נֶגֶב: ״מִמִּדְבַּר צִין עַל יְדֵי אֱדוֹם״ (במדבר לד:ג), וּמוֹנֶה וְהוֹלֵךְ עַד ״מֵעַצְמוֹן נַחְלָה מִצְרַיִם וְהָיוּ תוֹצְאוֹתָיו הַיָּמָּה״ (במדבבר לד:ה). וְ**נַחְלָה** הָאָמוּר כָּאן כְּמוֹ לְנַחְלָה, וּמָצָאתִי מְתֻרְגָּם ״אַחְסָנָא״, וְאִם לֹא שֶׁהַטַּעַם לְמַטָּה וְרָאִיתִי ״נַחְלָה מִצְרַיִם״ (במדבר לד:) שֶׁהַטַּעַם לְמַעְלָה, וּמָסֹרֶת עָלָיו לֵית בְּטַעְמָא, הָיִיתִי אוֹמֵר שִׁבּוּשׁ הוּא וְלֹא תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן כֵּן, כִּי אִם קוֹרְאִים טוֹעִים:


**to the stream [that falls] into the Great Sea** And from there the border proceeds until the stream of Egypt, which falls into the Great Sea in the southwestern corner. Moses, too, delineated the southern boundary in this way, from the desert of Zin alongside Edom, and he proceeds and counts until (ibid. 34:5): “from Atzmon to the stream of Egypt, and its ends shall be at the sea.” [Accordingly,] נַחֲלָה stated here is like לַנַחַל, to the stream. I found it, however, translated [by Jonathan] as אַחֲסָנָא, an inheritance, and were it not for the fact that the accent is on the last syllable, and [that] the Masorah states that there is no other instance in which the accent is on the first syllable, I would have said that it is an error, and that Jonathan did not translate it that way, only erring readers.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:20:1)


**וּפְאַת יָם.** וּמֵצַר מַעֲרָבִי:


**And the western side** the western border.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:20:2)


**הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל מִגְּבוּל עַד נֹכַח לְבוֹא חֲמָת.** מִמִּקְצוֹעַ דְּרוֹמִית הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה, וְהוּא נַחַל מִצְרַיִם:


**the Great Sea from the border until opposite the road [leading] to Hamath** from the southern corner, which is the stream of Egypt,


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 47:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/47:20:3)


**עַד נֹכַח לְבוֹא חֲמָת.** עַד מִקְצוֹעַ צְפוֹנִית מַעֲרָבִית, הוּא הַר הָהָר, שֶׁהוּא נֹכַח לְבֹא חֲמָת; כִּי חֲמָת בְּמִקְצוֹעַ צְפוֹנִית מַעֲרָבִית אֵצֶל הַר הָהָר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב ״מִן הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל תְּתָאוּ לָכֶם הַר הָהָר. מֵהַר הָהָר תְּתָאוּ לְבֹא חֲמָת״ (במדבר לד:ז-ח). מָצָאתִי:


**until opposite the way [leading] to Hamath**, until the northwestern corner, which is Mount Hor, opposite the road [leading] to Hamath, for Hamath is in the northwest corner alongside Mount Hor, as is written (Num. 34:7f.): “From the Great Sea you shall draw a line extending to Mount Hor. From Mount Hor you shall draw a line extending to the road [leading] to Hamath.”

**גָּר הַגֵּר אִתּוֹ.** שֶׁנִּתְגַּיֵּר בַּגּוֹלָה בְּתוֹךְ אוֹתוֹ שֵׁבֶט:
