## Chapter 48


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:1:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה שְׁמוֹת הַשְּׁבָטִים.** סֵדֶר חֲלָקִים שֶׁיְּחַלְּקוּהָ בֵּינֵיהֶם, מָקוֹם כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד:


**And these are the names of the tribes** The arrangement of the portions they divide among themselves; the place of each one.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:1:2)


**מִקְּצֵה צָפוֹנָה.** דָּן יִטּוֹל מֵצַר צְפוֹנִי **מֵחֲצַר עֵינָן** שֶׁבְּמִקְצוֹעַ מִזְרָח, שֶׁהִיא עַד **גְּבוּל דַּמֶּשֶׂק** (שֶׁבְּמִקְצוֹעַ מַעֲרַב צָפוֹן, סא״א):


**From the northern end** Dan will receive the northern border from Hazer-enan, which is at the eastern corner, until the border of Damascus (in the northwestern corner).


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:1:3)


**וְהָיוּ לוֹ פְּאַת קָדִים הַיָּם.** כְּלוֹמַר מִפְּאַת קָדִים וְעַד יָם דָּן אֶחָד, הֲרֵי פֵּרַשׁ הָאֹרֶךְ וְלֹא פֵּרַשׁ הָרֹחַב. וּבִבְרַיְתָא דְסִפְרִי בְּפָרָשַׁת הַאֲזִינוּ מַה תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר **יְהוּדָה אֶחָד**, דָּן אֶחָד, **אָשֵׁר אֶחָד**, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁעֲתִידִין יִשְׂרָאֵל לִיטֹּל אֹרֶךְ מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב בְּרֹחַב עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֶלֶף קָנִים שֶׁהֵם שִׁבְעִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה מִילִין. וּמֵהֵיכָן לָמְדוּ הַשִּׁיעוּר הַזֶּה? מִן הַתְּרוּמָה שֶׁהִיא כְּאֶחָד מִן הַחֲלָקִים הַלָּלוּ כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בָּעִנְיָן. מָצָאתִי שֶׁכָּךְ כָּתוּב **וְאֹרֶךְ כְּאַחַד הַחֲלָקִים**, וְהוּא רֹחַב עֶשְׂרִים חֲמֵשׁ אֶלֶף קָנִים, וְאֹרֶךְ מִפְּאַת יָם עַד פְּאַת קָדִים עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֶלֶף קָנִים, מֵאָה וַחֲמִשִּׁים אֶלֶף אַמָּה, שֶׁהַקָּנֶה שֵׁשׁ אַמָּה. וְכָל אַלְפַּיִם אַמָּה מִיל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָנוּ לְמֵדִין בִּמְסֶכֶת יוֹמָא אֵצֶל עֶשֶׂר סֻכּוֹת שֶׁמִּירוּשָׁלַיִם וְעַד צוּק, הֲרֵי מֵאָה וַחֲמִשִּׁים אֶלֶף אַמָּה עוֹלִין לְשִׁבְעִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה מִיל. וְזֶהוּ שֶׁיִּסֵּד רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בְּפִיּוּט כִּי אֶקַּח מוֹעֵד (בסלוק דיום שני של סכות) ״לְעֵת חִלּוּק אַרְצוֹתֶיהָ, כְּשׁוּרַת הַכֶּרֶם לְחוּצוֹתֶיהָ, בְּאֹרֶךְ מִפְּאַת קָדִים לִקְצוֹתֶיהָ, עַד יָם אוֹקְיָנוֹס לְמִצְוֹתֶיהָ, בְּרֹחַב שִׁבְעִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה מִיל חֲרִיצוֹתֶיהָ״ (ויש גורסין שִׁבְעִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה מִיל לִשְׁלוֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה רְצוּעוֹת, בֵּין רְצוּעוֹת הַשְּׁבָטִים וּרְצוּעוֹת הַקֹּדֶשׁ, יַעֲלוּ לִתְשַׁע מֵאוֹת שִׁבְעִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה מִילִין; וּלְמִנַּיִן אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת פַּרְסָה עַל אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת פַּרְסָה שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל עוֹלִין הַמִּילִין לְשֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה מֵאוֹת לִסְכוּם הַקָּלִירִי, שֶׁאָמַר אַרְבַּעַת מִילִין הִיא פַּרְסָה. וְשֶׁמָּא מִילִין שֶׁל חֶשְׁבּוֹן הָרְצוּעוֹת גְּדוֹלִים הֵם, אֵין נִכְנָסִין אַרְבָּעָה מֵהֶם בַּפַּרְסָה. וְאָכֵן בָּא לְמַנּוֹת שְׁנֵי מִילִין לְמַחֲצִית (וּמֶחֱצָה) בְּפַרְסָה יַעֲלוּ כְּאַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת פַּרְסָה לְאֶלֶף מִילִין, וְחֶשְׁבּוֹן מִילִין שֶׁל רְצוּעָה אֵין עוֹלִין אֶלָּא תְשַׁע מֵאוֹת שִׁבְעִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה מִילִין, עֲדַיִן חָסֵר הַמִּנְיָן עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה מִילִין, לֹא כִּוֵּן מִנְיַן מִילִין הָרְצוּעוֹת שֶׁל שְׁמוֹנִים וְתֵשַׁע לְמִנְיַן אַרְבַּע מֵאוֹת פַּרְסָה. וּמִנַּיִן לָמַד רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אֹרֶךְ הַשּׁוּרוֹת עַד יָם אוֹקְיָנוֹס שֶׁהוּא בְּסוֹף עוֹלָם? וּמִקְרָא מֵעִיד תְּחוּם מַעֲרָב עַד יָם הַגָּדוֹל שֶׁלִּפְנִים מִיָּם אוֹקְיָנוֹס הַרְבֵּה):


**and he shall have the eastern side to the sea** i.e., from the eastern side until the sea, Dan [shall have] one portion. Now, he delineated the length but not the width. In the Baraitha of Sifrei in the portion of Ha’azinu (Deut. 32:12), [it says]: “What is the reason for stating, ‘Judah: one portion; Dan: one portion; Asher: one portion?’ It is to teach us that Israel are destined to receive the length, from east to west, measuring 25,000 rods in width, which equal 75 mil.” Now from where did they learn this measurement? From the offering, which is like one of these portions, as is explained above (45:1,6). I found that the following is written: “the length is like one of the portions, and it has a width of 25,000 rods, and a length, from the western side to the eastern side, of twenty-five thousand rods.” [Now, 15, 000 rods] equal 150,000 cubits, for a rod equals six cubits, and every 2,000 cubits equals one mil as we find in Tractate Yoma (67a) regarding the ten sukkot [booths] from Jerusalem to the precipice- behold 150,000 cubits amount to 75 mils. This is what Rabbi Eliezer [Hakalir] established in the liturgical poem, entitled “When I take an appointed time” (the last poem for the second day of Succoth): “At the time of the division of her lands, like a row of a vineyard abroad, in the length from the eastern side to its ends, until the ocean are its commandments, with a width of seventy-five mils are its limits.” (Some editions read: Seventy-five mils to thirteen strips, between the strips of the tribes and the strips of the Sanctuary will amount to 975 mils. Now, according to the figure of 400 parasangs by 400 parasangs for the land of Israel, the mils amount to 1600 [not 975] according to the figuring of the Kalir, who said that four mils equal a parasang. Perhaps the mils of the computation by strips are larger, four of them not fitting into a parasang, and he therefore came to count two and a half mils to a parasang, by which 400 parasangs will equal 1,000 mils. However, the computation of the mils of a strip equal only 975 mils, still leaving the total 25 mils short. The sum of the liturgical poet’s mils of strips does not equal the sum of four hundred parasangs. And whence did Rabbi Eliezer learn that the length of the strips extended to the ocean, which is at the end of the world, whereas Scripture testifies that the western border is [only] until the Great Sea [the Mediterranean], which is quite a distance inland from the ocean?)



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:2:1)


**וְעַל גְּבוּל דָּן.** וְאֵצֶל גְּבוּל דָּן בִּדְרוֹמוֹ:


**And alongside the border of Dan** and alongside the border of Dan to its south.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:8:1)


**תִּהְיֶה הַתְּרוּמָה וְגוֹ׳.** וְאִם תֹּאמַר: הֵיאַךְ יִתָּכֵן שֶׁתְּהֵא יְרוּשָׁלַיִם בִּמְקוֹמָהּ, וַהֲלֹא הָיְתָה יְרוּשָׁלַיִם בְּמֵיצַר צְפוֹנִי שֶׁל יְהוּדָה שֶׁנָּטַל דְּרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן, וְכָאן הוּא בְּאֶמְצַע הַשְּׁבָטִים? אֵין חֲלוּקָה זוֹ דּוֹמָה לַחֲלוּקַת יְהוֹשֻׁעַ, שֶׁזּוֹ כְּשׁוּרַת הַכֶּרֶם וְכָל הַחֲלָקִים שָׁוִין, וַחֲלוּקַת יְהוֹשֻׁעַ הָיָה שֵׁבֶט יְהוּדָה חֵלֶק גָּדוֹל, וְאַף כִּי (חֶלְקוֹ הָיָה) רַב מִמֶּנּוּ עַד שֶׁנָּטַל שִׁמְעוֹן בְּגוֹרָלוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (יהושע יט:ט) ״מֵחֶבֶל בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה נַחֲלַת בְּנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן״. לְפִיכָךְ יֵשׁ דַּי עַתָּה בְּרֹחַב חֵלֶק שֶׁנָּטַל יְהוּדָה בִּימֵי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לִיטֹּל בּוֹ חֵלֶק עַכְשָׁיו חֲמִשָּׁה שְׁבָטִים הַנּוֹתָרִים, לְפִי שֶׁהָאֹרֶךְ גָּדוֹל מְאֹד **מִפְּאַת קָדִים עַד** אוֹקְיָינוֹס, וִירוּשָׁלַיִם בִּמְקוֹמָהּ:


**shall be the offering etc.,** Now, if you ask, how is it possible that Jerusalem should be in its place? Was not Jerusalem on the northern border of Judah, which received the south of the land of Canaan? And here it is in the middle of the tribes! [I will answer you that] this division is not like Joshua’s division, for this one is like the rows of a vineyard and all the portions are equal, whereas [in] Joshua’s division, the tribe of Judah had such a large portion that [its territory was] too much for it until Simeon took part of it as is said (Josh. 19:9): “Out of the lot of the children of Judah was the inheritance of the children of Simeon.” Therefore, the width of the portion that Judah received in the days of Joshua suffices now for the five remaining tribes to take a share of it, because its length was very great, from the eastern side to the ocean, and Jerusalem is located there.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:8:2)


**וְהָיָה הַמִּקְדָּשׁ בְּתוֹכוֹ.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפָרֵשׁ וְהוֹלֵךְ:


**and the Sanctuary shall be in its midst** as he proceeds to explain.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:9:1)


**הַתְּרוּמָה, אֲשֶׁר תָּרִימוּ לָה׳.** מִן הַחֵלֶק הַזֶּה שֶׁהֻפְרַשׁ אֵצֶל יְהוּדָה בִּדְרוֹמוֹ, אֹרֶךְ מִמִּזְרָח עַד הַיָּם, וְרֹחַב עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֶלֶף קָנִים, מִמֶּנָּה תִּהְיֶה תְּרוּמָה לָה׳ לַמִּקְדָּשׁ וּמָקוֹם לְבָתִּים לְכֹהֲנִים, אֹרֶךְ עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֲלָפִים קָנִים מִמִּזְרָח לְמַעֲרָב, וְרֹחַב עֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים מִן הַצָּפוֹן לְדָרוֹם:


**The offering that you shall separate for the Lord** From this portion that was separated alongside Judah on their south, whose length is from the east to the sea and whose width is 25,000 rodsfrom it shall be the offering for the Lord for the Sanctuary and a place for houses for the priests, the length 25, 000 rods from east to west and the width 10,000 north to south.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:1)


**וְלְאֵלֶּה תִּהְיֶה תְרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ לַכֹּהֲנִים.** וְאֵלֶּה הָעֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים רֹחַב וְעֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֶלֶף אֹרֶךְ יִהְיֶה לַכֹּהֲנִים בַּצָּפוֹן בְּתוֹךְ רְצוּעוֹת הַתְּרוּמָה הַגְּדוֹלָה אֵצֶל דְּרוֹמוֹ שֶׁל יְהוּדָה:


**And these shall be the offering of the Sanctuary for the priests** [Heb. וּלְאַלָה.] And these 10,000 in width and 25,000 in length shall be for the priests in the north in the midst of the strips of the great offering alongside the south of Judah.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:2)


**צָפוֹנָה חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים אָלֶף.** הוּא הָאֹרֶךְ מִמִּזְרָח לְמַעֲרָב:


**to the north 25,000** That is the length from east to west.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:3)


**וְיָמָּה רֹחַב עֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים.** מִצַּד מַעֲרָבִי רֹחַב עֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים מִן הַצָּפוֹן לַדָּרוֹם:


**and to the west the width is 10,000** The western border is 10,000 wide from north to south.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:4)


**וְקָדִימָה רֹחַב עֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים.** וְרוּחַ מִזְרָחִית עֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים מִן הַצָּפוֹן לַדָּרוֹם:


**and to the east the width is 10,000** The eastern side is 10,000 from north to south.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:5)


**וְנֶגְבָּה אֹרֶךְ חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים אָלֶף.** מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב:


**and to the south the length is 25, 000** from east to west.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:10:6)


**וְהָיָה מִקְדַּשׁ ה׳ בְּתוֹכוֹ.** הוּא הַר הַבַּיִת חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת קָנִים עַל חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת קָנִים, יִהְיֶה בְּאֶמְצַע רְצוּעָה זוֹ, וְכָל סְבִיבוֹתָיו לַכֹּהֲנִים שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר אֲלָפִים וּמָאתַיִם וַחֲמִשִּׁים לְצַד מִזְרָח וְכֵן לְצַד מַעֲרָב, וּבָרֹחַב אַרְבַּעַת אֲלָפִים וּשְׁבַע מֵאוֹת וַחֲמִשִּׁים לַצָּפוֹן וְכֵן לַדָּרוֹם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה בָּעִנְיָן ״יִהְיֶה מִדָּה אֶל הַקֹּדֶשׁ חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת בַּחֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת מְרֻבָּע״:


**and the Sanctuary of the Lord shall be in its midst** This is the Temple Mount. Five hundred rods by 500 rods shall be in the center of this strip, and all its surrounding area shall belong to the priests: 12,250 to the east and likewise to the west and in the width, 4,750 to the north and likewise to the south, as stated above (45:2) “of this, there shall be for the Sanctuary five hundred by five hundred, square.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:11:1)


**לַכֹּהֲנִים הַמְקֻדָּשׁ.** הַחֵלֶק הַזֶּה הַמְקֻדָּשׁ יִהְיֶה לַכֹּהֲנִים:


**The hallowed part shall be for the priests** This part, which is hallowed, shall be for the priests.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:12:1)


**תְּרוּמִיָּה.** מְפֹרֶשֶׁת, תְּרוּמָה הִיא שֵׁם הַפָּרָשָׁה. תְּרוּמִיָּה מְפֹרֶשֶׁת, **וְהָיְתָה לָהֶם** הָרְצוּעָה הַזֹּאת מְפֹרֶשֶׁת **מִתְּרוּמַת הָאָרֶץ**, מִן הַתְּרוּמָה הַגְּדוֹלָה שֶׁהוּתְרְמָה מִן הָאָרֶץ, אֹרֶךְ מֵאוֹקְיָנוֹס עַד גְּבוּל מִזְרָח בְּרֹחַב עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֲלָפִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה (יחזקאל מח:ח), מִמֶּנּוּ יַבְדִּילוּ רְצוּעָה זוֹ בְּאֶמְצַע אָרְכָּהּ לַכֹּהֲנִים וְלַמִּקְדָּשׁ:


**separated** [Heb. תְּרוּמִיָה,] separated. תְּרוּמָה is the noun, separation. תְּרוּמִיָה means separated. And this strip shall be separated for them, from the offering of the land, from the great separation that was separated from the land, whose length is from the ocean to the eastern border by the width of 25,000, as is stated above. From it, they shall set apart this strip, at the middle of its length, for the priests and for the Sanctuary.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:12:2)


**קֹדֶשׁ קָדָשִׁים.** שֶׁכָּל הַתְּרוּמָה הַגְּדוֹלָה קֹדֶשׁ לַכֹּהֲנִים וְלַלְוִיִּם וְלַנָּשִׂיא וְלַאֲחֻזַּת הָעִיר וּלְעוֹבְדֵי הָעִיר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ וְהוֹלֵךְ; וְזוֹ עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּה אֶלֶף אֹרֶךְ וַעֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים רֹחַב בְּצָפוֹנוֹ, הַמְּפֹרָשֶׁת מִמֶּנָּה לַכֹּהֲנִים וְלַמִּקְדָּשׁ, תִּהְיֶה **קֹדֶשׁ קָדָשִׁים**:


**holy of holies** for the entire great separation is holy for the priests, the Levites, the prince, the possession of the city, and those who serve the city, as he proceeds to explain. This 25,000 in length and 10,000 in width in its north which is separated from it [the total holy area] shall be “holy of holies” for the priests and the Sanctuary.”


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:12:3)


**אֶל גְּבוּל הַלְוִיִּם.** אֵצֶל רְצוּעוֹת הַלְוִיִּם:


**alongside the border of the Levites** [Heb. אֶל-גְבוּל הַלְוִים, lit., to the border of the Levites,] alongside the strips of the Levites.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:13:1)


**וְהַלְוִיִּם לְעֻמַּת גְּבוּל הַכֹּהֲנִים.** אֵצֶל רְצוּעוֹת הַכֹּהֲנִים, כְּמִדַּת הַכֹּהֲנִים כְּנֶגֶד כָּל אָרְכָּהּ:


**And the Levites: opposite the border of the priests** Alongside the strips of the priests, like the measurement of the priests, parallel to its entire length.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:13:2)


**כָּל אֹרֶךְ.** כָּל הָרְצוּעוֹת אָרְכָּן **חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים אֶלֶף**:


**every length** The length of all the strips is 25,000.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:14:1)


**וְלֹא יָמֵר.** וְלֹא יַחֲלִיפֵם בְּקַרְקַע אַחֵר:


**or exchange** And he shall not exchange it for any other land.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:15:1)


**וַחֲמֵשֶׁת אֲלָפִים הַנּוֹתָר בָּרֹחַב.** רְצוּעוֹת הַתְּרוּמָה הַגְּדוֹלָה שֶׁהָיְתָה עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֲלָפִים רֹחַב וְאֹרֶךְ כְּאַחַת הַחֲלָקִים מִפְּאַת קָדִים עַד פְּאַת יָם, וּמִמֶּנָּה חָזַר וְתָרַם שְׁתֵּי רְצוּעוֹת בְּאֶמְצָעִיתָהּ אֹרֶךְ עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֶלֶף וְרַחְבָּן עֶשְׂרִים אֶלֶף, וַעֲדַיִן נִשְׁאַר בָּרְחָבָה חֲמֵשֶׁת אֲלָפִים, אוֹתָם חֲמֵשֶׁת אֲלָפִים רֹחַב יִהְיוּ לְמוֹשַׁב הָעִיר, אֶרְכֹּן עַל פְּנֵי אֹרֶךְ עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֶלֶף שֶׁל תְּרוּמַת הַלְּוִיִּים:


**And the 5,000 that remain in the width** of the strips of the great separation which was 25,000 [in] width and its length was the same as that of any portions from the eastern side to the western side. From that he set apart two strips from its center, 25,000 long and 20,000 wide; and 5,000 were left over in its width. Those 5,000 of the width shall be for the residential area of the city, their length facing the 25,000 length of the offering for the Levites.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:15:2)


**חֹל הוּא.** וְאֵינָן קֹדֶשׁ כִּתְרוּמַת אוֹתָהּ רְצוּעָה שֶׁל הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם. חֹל הוּא אֶצְלָם, וְיִהְיוּ **לָעִיר לְמוֹשָׁב** יִשְׂרָאֵל **וּלְמִגְרָשׁ** סְבִיב **הָעִיר**:


**is non-holy** [These 5,000 rods of land] are not holy like the “offering” of the strips of the priests and the Levites. It is non-holy as compared to them, and they shall be for the city for dwellings of Israelites and for an open space around the city.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:15:3)


**בְּתוֹכָהּ.** בְּאֶמְצַע אוֹתָהּ רְצוּעָה:


**it its midst** in the middle of that strip.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:16:1)


**וְאֵלֶּה מִדּוֹתֶיהָ.** שֶׁל עִיר:


**And these are its measurements** [i.e., the measurements] of the city.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:16:2)


**פְּאַת צָפוֹן וְגוֹ׳.** מְרֻבַּעַת **אַרְבַּעַת אֲלָפִים** וַ**חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת** קָנִים עַל אַרְבַּעַת אֲלָפִים וַחֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת קָנִים, נִשְׁאֲרוּ בָרֹחַב חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת, הֵם יִהְיֶה לָהֶם לְמִגְרָשׁ, מָאתַיִם וַחֲמִשִּׁים לַצָּפוֹן מָאתַיִם וַחֲמִשִּׁים קָנִים לַדָּרוֹם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בַּמִּקְרָא שֶׁלְּמַטָּה, וְכֵן לַמִּזְרָח וּמַעֲרָב, הֲרֵי חֲמֵשֶׁת אֲלָפִים עַל חֲמֵשֶׁת אֲלָפִים בְּאֶמְצַע הָרְצוּעָה, הֲרֵי כָּל רָחְבָּהּ מְמֻלָּא, וּבְאֹרֶךְ הָרְצוּעָה הַזֹּאת הַשְּׁלִישִׁית נִשְׁאֲרוּ עֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים לַמִּזְרָח וַעֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים לַמַּעֲרָב, עֲלֵיהֶם הוּא מְפָרֵשׁ וְאוֹמֵר וְהַנּוֹתָר בָּאֹרֶךְ לְעֻמַּת תְּרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ עֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים קָדִימָה וַעֲשֶׂרֶת אֲלָפִים יָמָּה:


**The north side, etc.** 4,500 rods by 4,500 rods square. Five hundred remain in the width, which will provide them with an open space, 250 to the north and 250 rods to the south, as is stated in the following verse, and likewise to the east and west. Thus we have 5,000 by 5,000 in the middle of the strip, thereby filling its entire width. In the length of this third strip, 10,000 are left to the east and 10,000 are left to the west. Concerning them, Scripture states (verse 18): “And the remainder in length, opposite the offering of the Sanctuary: 10,000 to the east and 10,000 to the west.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:17:1)


**וְהָיָה מִגְרָשׁ לָעִיר.** כָּל מִגְרְשֵׁי עִיר שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא מָקוֹם פָּנוּי, לֹא בָתִּים וְלֹא שָׂדֶה:


**The city shall have an open space** All the ‘migrashim’ of the cities in Scripture are an open space with neither houses or fields.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:18:1)


**וְהָיְתָה תְבוּאָתָהּ לְלֶחֶם לְעוֹבְדֵי הָעִיר.** הֵם הַגִּבְעוֹנִים הַנְּתוּנִים ״חוֹטְבֵי עֵצִים וְשׁוֹאֲבֵי מַיִם״ (יהושע ט:כז). כֵּן שָׁמַעְתִּי מִשְּׁמוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי מְנַחֵם זַצַ״ל:


**and its grain shall be for food for those who serve the city** These are the Gibeonites, who were made wood-cutters and water-drawers (Josh. 9:27). So I heard in the name of Rabbi Menahem of blessed memory.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:18:2)


**לְעוֹבְדֵי הָעִיר.** אִישָׁארְבַנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


לְעוֹבדֵי הָעִיר, “as servants” in Old French: to the workers.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:19:1)


**יַעַבְדוּהוּ מִכָּל שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** שֶׁנָּתְנוּ לָהֶם חֵלֶק בָּאָרֶץ:


**of all the tribes of Israel shall serve him** for they gave them this as a portion of the land.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:20:1)


**כָּל הַתְּרוּמָה.** הַמְקֻדֶּשֶׁת, הַמּוּתְרֶמֶת מִתּוֹךְ הַתְּרוּמָה הַגְּדוֹלָה, **חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים** אֲלָפִים קָנִים עַל **חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים** אֲלָפִים קָנִים:


**The entire offering** that is hallowed, that is separated from the great separation, amounts to 25,000 by 25,000 rods.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:20:2)


**רְבִיעִית.** מְרֻבַּעַת, אֶל **תְּרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ** – לַכֹּהֲנִים וְלַלְוִיִּם:


**a square** [Heb. רְבִיעִית,] a square, alongside the offering for the Sanctuary, for the priests and the Levites, and the possession of the city.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:20:3)


**וְאֶל אֲחֻזַּת הָעִיר.** אֲחֻזַּת פּוֹרְפְּרִי״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the possession** [Heb. אַחֻזַת,] porpris in Old French; precincts.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:1)


**וְהַנּוֹתָר לַנָּשִׂיא וְגוֹ׳.** וְהַנּוֹתָר בְּאֹרֶךְ הַתְּרוּמָה הַגְּדוֹלָה:


**And the remainder shall be for the prince, etc.** and the remainder of the length of the great separation.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:2)


**מִזֶּה וּמִזֶּה.** לְמִזְרָח וּלְמַעֲרָב שֶׁל **תְּרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ וְלַאֲחֻזַּת הָעִיר**:


**on either side** to the east and to the west of the offering of the Sanctuary and the possession of the city.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:3)


**אֶל פְּנֵי חֲמִשָּׁה וְעֶשְׂרִים אֶלֶף.** עַל רֹחַב עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֲלָפִים **תְּרוּמָה עַד גְּבוּל קָדִימָה** שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְגוֹ׳:


**alongside 25,000 of the offering** alongside the 25,000 width of the offering extending to the eastern border of the entire land of Israel.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:4)


**וְיָמָּה עַל פְּנֵי וְגוֹ׳.** וְכֵן לְמַעֲרָב, עַל פְּנֵי שְׁלֹשֶׁת הָרְצוּעוֹת שֶׁל כֹּהֲנִים וּלְוִיִּם, אֲחֻזַּת הָעִיר בְּרֹחַב עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֲלָפִים:


**and to the west, alongside, etc.** and likewise to the west, facing the three strips of the priests, the Levites, and the city’s possession, in the width of 25,000.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:5)


**עַל גְּבוּל יָמָּה.** כְּמוֹ: עַד גְּבוּל יָמָּה, עַד אוֹקְיָינוֹס:


**the western border** [Heb. עַל-גְבוּל יָמָּה,] like עַד גְבוּל יָמָּה, until the ocean.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:6)


**לְעֻמַּת חֲלָקִים.** כְּנֶגֶד אֹרֶךְ חֶלְקֵי הַשְּׁבָטִים:


**opposite the portions** opposite the length of the tribes’ portions.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:7)


**לַנָּשִׂיא.** יִהְיֶה:


**shall belong to the prince** [lit. to the prince,] it shall be.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:8)


**וְהָיְתָה תְרוּמַת הַקֹּדֶשׁ.** הִיא רְצוּעַת הַכֹּהֲנִים:


**and the offering of the Sanctuary... shall be** i.e., the strip of the priests.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:21:9)


**וּמִקְדַּשׁ הַבַּיִת בְּתוֹכָהּ.** הַכֹּל בְּאֶמְצַע נַחֲלַת הַנָּשִׂיא:


**and the Sanctuary of the House shall be in its midst** Everything is in the middle of the inheritance of the prince.



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:22:1)


**וּמֵאֲחֻזַּת הַלְוִיִּם וּמֵאֲחֻזַּת הָעִיר.** גַּם הוּא **בְּתוֹךְ אֲשֶׁר לַנָּשִׂיא יִהְיֶה**:


**Both the possession of the Levites and the possession of the city** That, too, shall be in the midst of what belongs to the prince.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:22:2)


**בֵּין גְּבוּל יְהוּדָה וּבֵין גְּבוּל בִּנְיָמִין.** כָּל רֹחַב הָרְצוּעָה אֲשֶׁר בֵּין שְׁנֵי הַשְּׁבָטִים הָאֵלֶּה, יְהוּדָה מִצָּפוֹן וּבִנְיָמִין מִן הַדָּרוֹם, **יִהְיֶה לַנָּשִׂיא**. וְהִיא הָרְצוּעָה שֶׁהוּתְרְמָה רִאשׁוֹן, רֹחַב עֶשְׂרִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה אֲלָפִים בְּאֹרֶךְ אַחַר חֶלְקֵי הַשְּׁבָטִים (יחזקאל מה:א-ח):


**between the border of Judah and the border of Benjamin** The entire width of the strip that is between these two tribes, Judah on the north and Benjamin on the south, shall belong to the prince, and that is the strip that was separated first, a width of 25,000, with a length [that follows] the portions of the tribes.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:23:1)


**וְיֶתֶר הַשְּׁבָטִים.** יִטְּלוּ בִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל אוֹתָהּ רְצוּעָה, **בִּנְיָמִין** אֶצְלָהּ, וְאֶצְלוֹ **שִׁמְעוֹן**, וְאֶצְלוֹ **יִשָּׂשכָר**, וְאֶצְלוֹ **זְבוּלֻן**, וְאֶצְלוֹ **גָּד**. נִמְצָא גָּד בַּמֵּצַר דְּרוֹמִי שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And the rest of the tribes** shall receive [portions] south of that strip, Benjamin alongside it, and beside him Simeon, beside him Issachar, beside him Zebulun, and beside him Gad. Gad is therefore at the southern boundary of the land of Israel.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:28:1)


**וְעַל גְּבוּל גָּד.** מְצַר דְּרוֹמִי שֶׁלּוֹ:


**And alongside the border of Gad** its southern boundary.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:28:2)


**מִתָּמָר מֵי מְרִיבַת קָדֵשׁ נַחֲלָה.** מִירִיחוֹ ״עִיר הַתְּמָרִים״ (דברים לד:ג) בְּמִקְצוֹעַ הַמִּזְרָח, וּבָא לַמַּעֲרָב לְמֵי מְרִיבַת קָדֵשׁ, וּבָא וְהוֹלֵךְ עַד נַחַל מִצְרַיִם, הַשּׁוֹפֵךְ לַ**יָּם הַגָּדוֹל** בְּמִקְצוֹעַ דְּרוֹמִית מַעֲרָבִית:


**from Tamar to the waters of Meribath-kadesh, to the brook** [It starts] from Jericho, the city of date palms (הַתְּמָרִים), in the eastern corner, and comes to the west to the waters of Meribath-kadesh, and proceeds until the brook of Egypt, which empties into the Great Sea in the southwestern corner.



### Verse 30

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:30:1)


**תּוֹצְאוֹת הָעִיר.** שְׁעָרֶיהָ לְאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹתֶיהָ:


**The exits of the city** the gates of its four sides.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:30:2)


**מִדָּה.** הוּא קְנֵה הַמִּדָּה, **חֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת וְאַרְבַּעַת אֲלָפִים** קָנִים:


**measures** that is of the measuring rod: 4,500 rods.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:35:1)


**סָבִיב שְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר אָלֶף.** הֶקֵּף עִיר שְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר אֶלֶף קָנִים; כָּךְ עוֹלֶה חֶשְׁבּוֹן רִבּוּעָהּ:


**All around, it amounts to 18,000** The perimeter of the city is 18,000 rods. This is the sum total of its four sides.


###### Rashi on Ezekiel 48:35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Ezekiel/r/48:35:2)


**וְשֵׁם הָעִיר מִיּוֹם ה׳ שָׁמָּה.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְשְׁמָא דְקַרְתָּא דְמִתְפָּרַשׁ מִיּוֹמָא דִּי יַשְׁרֵי ה׳ שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ תַּמָּן״, כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא יִקָּרֵא לָהּ שֵׁם אַחֵר, אֶלָּא שֵׁם הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁהָיָה לָהּ מִיּוֹם שֶׁעָקַד אַבְרָהָם אֶת יִצְחָק בְּנוֹ שָׁם (בראשית כב:ב), שֶׁקָּרָא לוֹ ״יִרְאֶה״ (בראשית כב:יד), וְהִיא הָיָה שְׁמָהּ שָׁלֵם (בראשית יד:יח) – הֲרֵי יְרוּשָׁלַיִם (בראשית רבה נו:טז). וְיֵשׁ עוֹד לִפְתֹּר: **וְשֵׁם הָעִיר** יִהְיֶה לֶעָתִיד; **מִיּוֹם** בִּנְיָנָהּ וּלְהַלָּן יִקְרְאוּ לָהּ **ה׳ שָׁמָּה**. סְלִיק סֵפֶר יְחֶזְקֵאל:


**and the name of the city from that day will be “The Lord is There.”** Jonathan renders: “And the name of the city had been declared as of that day that the Lord caused His Shechinah to rest there.” It shall not be called by a name other than the original name that it had since the day that Abraham bound Isaac his son there, for he called it Yireh, and its name had been Shalem, thus forming “Jerusalem” (Gen. Rabbah 56:16). It may also be interpreted: and the name of the city in the future, from when it is built and thenceforth, shall be “The Lord is There.”
